Gautamadharmasūtra
Maskarin        Haradatta
sarvadharmasvarūpāya sarvakāryalayāya ca |
praṇipatyādidevāya vāsudevāya bhaktitaḥ |
gautamīyacchalenedaṃ sarvasmṛtinibandhanam ||
kriyate ‘dya mayā bhāṣyaṃ sūnunā vāmanasya tu ||
ko vāsyābhisaṃbandhaḥ | ayam ucyate – santi caturdaśa vidyāsthānāni | tad yathā –
aṅgāni vedāś catvāro mīmāṃsā nyāyavistaraḥ |
purāṇaṃ dharmaśāstraṃ ca vidyā hy etāś caturdaśa || iti | teṣāṃ madhye parigaṇitam idaṃ dharmaśāstram ārabhyata ity abhisaṃbandhaḥ | atha vā – puruṣasya sukhaduḥkhopabhogārthaiḥ śarīrendriyaviṣayaiḥ saṃyogaviyogarūpo janmajarāmaraṇaprabandhaḥ saṃsāraḥ | sa ca dharmādharmanimitto nānyanimittaḥ | tayoś ca dharmādharmayor iṣṭāniṣṭopabhogahetutvād upādānaparivarjanārhau tau bhavataḥ | tad ubhayam aparijñāne na saṃbhavatīti dharmādharma-svarūpāvabodhanārthaṃ gautamena śāstraṃ praṇītam iti | prayojanam api cāsyaitad eva, dharmādharmopādānaparivarjanayoḥ puruṣārthatvād iti | abhidheyam aṣṭakādīni, yathā śrautāny agniṣṭomādīny avaśyakartavyāni tathāṣṭakādīny apīti |
namo rudrāya yad dharmaśāstraṃ gautamanirmitam |
kriyate haradattena tasya vṛttir mitākṣarā ||
tatra sarvakāryāṇāṃ kāraṇataḥ pravṛttidarśanāt pūrvaṃ kāraṇaviśeṣapratipādanārtham āha |
vedo dharmamūlam || 1.1 ||
nanu vidhipratiṣedharūpatvāc chāstrasya “upanayanaṃ brāhmaṇasyāṣṭame” ityādy eva vaktavyam, na tv anuvādarūpatvād “vedo dharmamūlam” ityādi, niṣprayojanatvād anuvādasyeti | na cānena vedasya prāmāṇyaṃ pratipādyate, tasya svata eva siddheḥ | anyathā yena prāmāṇyaṃ pratipādyate tasyāpy anyena prāmāṇyaṃ tasyāpy anyenety anavasthā syād iti |
ucyate – vedasya dharmamūlatvānuvādaḥ taddvāreṇa smṛtiśīlayor api dharmamūlatvapratipādanārthaḥ | katham iti ced, ucyate – vedavacanād uccaritāt tatpadārthavidāṃ viśiṣṭe ‘rthe yā pratipattir niḥsaṃdigdhā jāyate, na ca kālāntare deśāntare puruṣāntare ‘vasthāntare vā viparyeti sā dharmamūlam iti | yac ca niḥsaṃdigdham aviparītaṃ ca jñānam utpadyate tasya prāmāṇyam adhyavasituṃ śakyate | na ca vedajanitapratyayasya kāraṇadoṣakṛtaṃ mithyātvaṃ, kartur anabhyupagamāt | kartṛgato hi doṣaḥ śabdajanitasya pratyayasya mithyātvam āpādayati | evam uttarārtho ‘nuvādaḥ |
idānīṃ sūtraṃ vivrīyate – tatra vedo mantrabrāhmaṇākhyo grantharāśiḥ | vid jñāne, jñāyate ‘nena dharmādisvarūpam iti vedaḥ | yady api smṛtyāder etat tulyatvam, tathāpi parivrājakādivad rūḍhatvād adoṣaḥ | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
śrutiś tu vedo vijñeyo dharmaśāstraṃ tu vai smṛtiḥ | iti | [MDh 2.10] | sa ca ṛgvedādibhedena bhidyate | jātyapekṣayaikavacanam | dharmaḥ karma ācāra ityarthaḥ, apūrvākhyo vā | mūlaṃ kāraṇaṃ jñāpakaṃ, pramāṇam ity arthaḥ | tatra vidhipratiṣedhātmako hi vedo dharmādyavabodhakatvaṃ pratipadyate, na tu mantrārthavādarūpa iti ced, ucyate — arthavādāder api vidhyekavākyatvenopakārakatvāt pāraṃparyeṇa dharmamūlatvam aviruddham | arthavādas tāvat stutinindādvāreṇa vidhipratiṣedharūpatvaṃ pratipadyate, mantras tv anuṣṭhānakāle vihitārthasmārakatvāt | tataḥ sarvaprakāra eva vedo dharmamūlam iti |
kecin mantrārthavādamūlam eva pratijānate, yathā “dhanvann iva prapā asi” [RV 10.4.1] ity atra prapā kartavyetyādi |
anye tv āhuḥ — adhyayanamātrād eva dharmasiddhiḥ | yathāha |
ā haiva sa nakhāgrebhyaḥ paramaṃ tapyate tapaḥ |
yaḥ sragvy api dvijo ‘dhīte svādhyāyaṃ śaktito 'nvaham || iti | ataḥ pāṭha eva dharmamūlam iti |
tad ayuktaṃ, karmaphalaśruteḥ ānarthakyaprasaṅgāt, mīmāṃsakair adhyayanaphalasya nirastatvāc ca | atha vā vedavācyā kriyā vedaśabdenocyate, sa dharmasya mūlam iti |
śyenādiṣu na bhavatīti cet,
ucyate — tatrāpi vairivadhāt prāṇadhanaputradārādirakṣaṇadvāreṇa dharmo ‘sty eveti | tataś cāvagamyate — “yad idaṃ putrapaśugrāmādidṛṣṭaṃ, yac ca sargādyadṛṣṭaṃ, tat sarvaṃ dharmahetukam” iti |
dharmaḥ pañcaprakāraḥ — varṇadharma āśramadharmo guṇadharmo varṇāśramadharmo nimittadharma iti | varṇadharmo ‘dhyayanādiḥ, āśramadharmaḥ samidādhānādiḥ, guṇadharmo ‘bhiṣekādiguṇayuktasya rakṣaṇādiḥ, varṇāśramadharmo brāhmaṇasya pālāśo daṇḍa ityādiḥ, nimittadharmaḥ prāyaścittādiḥ | mūlaṃ kāraṇam | tathā cāha — “vedād dharmo hi nirbabhau” [MDh 2.10] iti | sūtrārambhaprayojanaṃ ca — yathaiva vedo dharmamūlaṃ tathā smṛtiśīle apīti || 1.1 ||
karmajanyo ‘bhyudayaniḥśreyasahetur apūrvākhya ātmaguṇo dharmaḥ | tasya mūlaṃ pramāṇam | vedo mantrabrāhmaṇātmakaḥ | jātāv ekavacanam | catvāro vedā ṛgyajuḥsāmātmakās ta eva dharme pramāṇam | na yogipratyakṣaṃ nānumānaṃ nārthāpattir na śākyādyāgamaḥ | tena tanmūlā evopanayanādayo dharmā vakṣyante na caityavandanakeśolluñcanādaya iti | dharmagrahaṇam upalakṣaṇam | adharmasyāpi pratiṣedhātmako veda eva mūlam | niṣedhavidhayo hi brahmahatyādau viṣaye pravṛttaṃ nivartayanti | na ca rāgadveṣādinā viṣaye pravṛttas tato nivartayituṃ śakyaḥ | yady asau viṣayo ‘nuṣṭhitaḥ pratyavāyahetur na syād iti niṣadhavidhir eva pratyavāyahetutāṃ gamayati || 1.1 ||
na kevalaṃ veda eva dharmamūlaṃ, kiṃ tarhi
atha yatra pratyakṣo vedo mūlabhūto nopapadyate tatra katham |
tadvidāṃ ca smṛtiśīle || 1.2 ||
vedavidāṃ ca ye smṛtiśīle te ‘pi dharmamūle bhavataḥ | te ‘pi vedāvirodhinī, tadvidām ity ārambhāt | mūlaśabdaś ceha dvābhyām abhisaṃbadhyamāno dvivacanānto draṣṭavyaḥ | tacchabdena ṣaḍaṅgo vedo lakṣyate | smṛtir upanibandhanaṃ manu-yama-vasiṣṭha-bhṛgv-aṅgiro-bṛhaspaty-uśano-bhāradvāja-gautama-āpastamba-saṃvarta-vyāsa-śatātapa-śaṅkha-likhita-hārīta-bodhāyana-yājñavalkya-pracetādibhiḥ kṛtam | śīlam anupanibaddhaḥ samācāraḥ kautukamaṅgalādiḥ bahutvāt pratideśaṃ bhidyamānatvāc cānupanibaddhaḥ | cakāro ‘nuktasamuccayārthaḥ | yathāha manuḥ |
vedo 'khilo dharmamūlaṃ smṛtiśīle ca tadvidām |
ācāraś caiva sādhūnāṃ ātmanas tuṣṭir eva ca || iti | [MDh 2.6] | saṃdhyoparamaṇakāle triḥ pradakṣiṇādiḥ śiṣṭācāraḥ | vedavidāṃ sakalānuṣṭhānaparāṇāṃ nirdoṣatvena yad vijñānam utpadyate, sātmatuṣṭiḥ | vakṣyati “yac cātmavanto vṛddhāḥ” [GDh 9.62] ityādi |
kecid varṇayanti — aṣṭakādivad eṣāṃ kautukamaṅgalādīnām anupanibandho ‘karaṇe pratyavāyābhāvāt | ācārātmatuṣṭī api śīla evāntarbhūte iti na pṛthag upanyaste | cakāro ‘py uktasamuccayārtha eveti || 1.2 ||
tadvidāṃ vedavidāṃ manvādīnāṃ yā smṛtis tatpraṇītaṃ dharmaśāstraṃ yac ca teṣāṃ śīlam anuṣṭhānaṃ te smṛtiśīle asmadādīnāṃ pramāṇam | na ca teṣām anuṣṭhānaṃ nirmūlaṃ saṃbhavati | saṃbhavati ca vaidikānām utsannapāṭhe vedānubhava iti | teṣāṃ tu tadānīṃ vidyamānatvena saṃpradāyāvicchedāc ca vaidikānuṣṭhānaṃ vedamūlam eva | yathāha āpastambaḥ: “teṣām utsannāḥ pāṭhāḥ prayogād anumīyante” iti || 1.2 ||
idānīṃ pūrvapakṣīkaroti vedavidāṃ śīlaṃ dharmamūlaṃ na bhavati yataḥ |
yadi śīlaṃ pramāṇam atiprasaṅgaḥ syāt | katham | katakabharadvājau vyatyasya bhārye jagmatuḥ; vasiṣṭhaś caṇḍālīm akṣamālām; prajāpatiḥ svāṃ duhitaram; rāmeṇa pitṛvacanād avicāreṇa mātuḥ śiraś chinnam – ityādisāhasam api pramāṇam syāt | nety āha –
dṛṣṭo dharmavyatikramaḥ || 1.3 ||
dṛṣṭo dharmavyatikramaḥ sāhasaṃ ca mahatām || 1.3 ||
dharmamūlatvaṃ na prāpnotīti śeṣaḥ | yathā prajāpatiḥ svāṃ duhitaram abhyadhyāyat, yathendrasyāhalyāgamanādiḥ, yathā vyāsabhīṣmādīnām anāśramāvasthānam || 1.3 ||
mahatām etādṛśaṃ sāhasam api dharmavyatikrama eva dṛṣṭo na tu dharmaḥ, rāgadveṣanibandhanatvāt || 1.3 |s
kiṃ ca |
sāhasaṃ ca mahatām || 1.4 ||
atrāpi dharmamūlatvaṃ na prāpnotīti śeṣaḥ, caśabdopādānāt | sahaḥśabdena balam ucyate | yathā ca nāradaḥ: “saho balam ihocyate” [NSm 14.1] iti | tena śāstraṃ lokasaṃvyavahāraṃ cānavekṣya yat kriyate tat sāhasam | mahatāṃ lokavikhyātānām ity arthaḥ | yathā rāmasya tāṭakādistrīvadhaḥ, jāmadagnyasya mātuḥ śiraśchedaḥ, vasiṣṭhasya jalapraveśaḥ ityādi | tena ayuktaṃ śīlasya dharmamūlatvam iti |
kathaṃ punar dharmavyatikramasāhasayoḥ bhedaḥ — viṣayābhilāṣeṇa yad ayuktam ācaryate, sa dharmavyatikramaḥ, krodhādyabhibhavena yat kriyate tat sāhasam || 1.4 ||
atrottaram āha |
na ca teṣām evaṃvidhaṃ dṛṣṭam ity etāvatāsmadādīnām api prasaṅgaḥ | kutaḥ |
na tu dṛṣṭārthe ‘varadaurbalyāt || 1.5 ||
avaradaurbalyāt || 1.4 ||
tuśabdaḥ pakṣanivṛttyarthaḥ | dṛṣṭārtho dṛṣṭaprayojanaḥ, tasmin dṛṣṭaprayojane śīlaṃ dharmamūlaṃ na bhavati | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “agṛhyamāṇakāraṇo dharmaḥ” [VaDh 1.7] iti | naitad vivacanaṃ, dṛṣṭārthe dharmavyatikramasāhase iti | tasmin gṛhyamāṇe “īd udet” [Pāṇ 1.1.11] ityādinā pragṛhyasaṃjñāyāṃ satyāṃ “dṛṣṭārthe avaradaurbalyāt” iti pāṭhaḥ prāpnoti | avaradaurbalyāt na varaḥ avaro nikṛṣṭaḥ dveṣādyabhibhūtaḥ aparamārthajñāna ity arthaḥ | tasya daurbalyāt dharmādharmaparijñānāśakter ity arthaḥ | etac cānena jñāpitaṃ bhavati — mahatām api tadvidāṃ kadācid abhibhavo 'stīti, śarīravataḥ priyāpriyayor avaśyaṃbhāvitvāt | tasmād yāvad eteṣāṃ rāgādidoṣeṇābhibhavaḥ, tāvat teṣām ācāro ‘pi na grāhyaḥ | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “śiṣṭaḥ punar akāmātmā” [VaDh 1.6]  iti | atha vā – avaraśabdenedānīṃtanāḥ kaliyugapuruṣā ucyante, teṣāṃ daurbalyāt asāmarthyāt || 1.5 ||
avareṣām asmadādīnāṃ durbalatvāt | tathā ca śrūyate |
teṣāṃ tejoviśeṣeṇa pratyavāyo na vidyate |
tad anvīkṣya prayuñjānaḥ sīdaty avarako janaḥ || iti || 1.4 ||
evaṃ smṛtipramāṇam uktvā adhunā ekārthopanipatitānāṃ vākyānāṃ parasparavirodhe sati kiṃ kravyam ity ata āha |
atha yatra dve viruddhe tulyabale pramāṇe upanipatataḥ | yathā “atirātre ṣoḍaśinaṃ gṛhṇāti, nātirātre ṣoḍaśinaṃ gṛhṇāti | udite juhoty anudite juhoti” iti śrutiḥ | “nityam abhojyaṃ keśakīṭāvapannam” [GDh 17.8–9] iti gautamaḥ |
pakṣijagdhaṃ gavāghrātam avadhūtam avakṣatam |
keśakīṭāvapannaṃ ca mṛtprakṣepeṇa śudhyati || [MDh 5.125] iti manuḥ | tatra kiṃ kartavyam |
tulyabalavirodhe vikalpaḥ || 1.6 || — || 1.5 ||
tulyapramāṇaprāpitaḥ padārthas tulyabalaḥ | tayoḥ parasparavirodhe sati vikalpo veditavyaḥ, ayaṃ vāyaṃ veti | nobhayaparityāgaḥ, ubhayoḥ pramāṇaprāpitatvāt | nānyataraparigrahaḥ, ekasmin gṛhyamāṇe dvitīyasyānarthakyaprasaṅgaḥ syād iti | kim udāharaṇam — śrutau tāvat “udite hotavyam, anudite hotavyam” ityevamādi | smṛtāv api, “nityam abhojyaṃ keśakīṭāvapannam” [GDh 17.8–9] iti gautamaḥ |
pakṣijagdhaṃ gavāghrātam avadhūtam avakṣutam |
dūṣitaṃ keśakīṭaiś ca mṛtprakṣepeṇa śudhyati || [MDh 5.125] iti manuḥ |
nanu cātra sāmarthyād eva vikalpaprasiddheḥ anarthakam idaṃ sūtram, samuccayas tāvad atra na saṃbhavatīti | na hy anudite hutvā punar udite hotuṃ śakyate, guṇabhūtakālavaśena pradhānasya homasyāvṛtter anyāyyatvāt | pradhānavaśavartino hi guṇāḥ, na guṇavaśavarti pradhānam iti | na cānāvṛttam ubhayor kālayor hetuṃ śakyate | evaṃ smṛtāv api śuddhiparityāgayor naiva samuccayaḥ sambhavatīti, parasparavirodhād iti | na cobhayaparityāgo yukta ity uktam | tenātra vikalpa eva prāpnotīti nārtho ‘neneti |
evaṃ tarhi nyāyaprāptānuvādo ‘yaṃ viśeṣārthaḥ | ko viśeṣaḥ? tulyabalayor eva virodhe vikalpaḥ, atulyabalayos tu bādha eveti | tena śrutismṛtyor virodhe smṛtir eva bādhyate | śrutau hi pratyakṣaḥ śabdaḥ smṛtāv ānumānikaḥ | na cānumānaṃ pratyakṣavirodhe pramāṇībhavati | evaṃ smṛtyācāravirodhe ca mātuladuhitṛpariṇayanādyācāro bādhyata iti, tatra hy ācārāt smṛtyanumānam iti | virodhe vikalpavacanāt, avirodhe tu tulyapramāṇaśiṣṭānāṃ samuccayaḥ yathā ācamanādīnāṃ śrauteṣu karmasv iti |
nanu ca yadi vedaliṅgaprabhavatvābhyupa-gama eva smṛteḥ śrutyā sahātulyabalatvam upapādyate, tadā hi mantrārthavādavākyeṣv anyapareṣv api yāni smārtadharmamūlabhūtāni liṅgāni “kumārā viśikhā iva” [RV 6.75.27] ity evamādīni teṣāṃ pratyakṣavedavākyavirodhe sati virodhivākyānumānavirodhāt durbalatvaṃ prasajyate | yadā tūtsannaśākhāpūrvikā smṛtir abhyupagamyate – yathāha āpastambaḥ “brāhmaṇoktā vidhayas teṣām utsannāḥ pāṭhāḥ prayogād anumīyante” [ĀpDh 1.12.10] iti tadobhayoś ca vaidikatvāt tulyabalataiva prasajyate | na hy anumitasya vedavākyasya pratyakṣasya ca balābalaviśeṣo ‘sti pratyakṣānumānaparicchinnāgnivad iti | syād etat yatra smṛtivacane vedavirodho ‘sti tan naiva vedavākyānumāpakaṃ bhavati, kiṃ tarhi bhrāntyādiprabhavam evaitad iti |
tac ca na yuktaṃ, dharmaśāstrapraṇetur anāptatvābhyupagame aviśvāsaprasaṅgāt | yadāpi vedavad avicchinnapāraṃparyeṇāgatā smṛtir abhyupagamyate, tadāpi tulyabalatvam upapadyata iti tatrāpi tan naivopapadyate | yas tāvan mātuladuhitṛpariṇayanādikaḥ samācāra upanyastaḥ sa kāmādyabhibhave sati duṣṭācāratvāc chiṣṭācāra eva nopapadyate | yas tv avigānenoktalakṣaṇaiḥ śiṣṭair ācaryate tasya vedamūlatvāviśeṣāt smṛtyā saha tulyabalatvam eva prāpnoti | nibandhanānibandhanayos tv anyad eva prayojanam upadiṣṭam | na ca samācārasya smṛtivākyāntaritarūpasya vedavākyānumānasaṃnikarṣakṛtaṃ balābalatvaṃ sidhyati |
atrocyate — atulyabalatvaṃ tāvat vedādīnāṃ sūtrārambhasāmarthyād evāvagamyate | yadā hi tulyabalāny etāni syuḥ, tadā virodhe vikalpaḥ sārmathyād eva siddhaḥ, kim anena sūtreṇeti tulyabalagrahaṇaṃ cānarthakaṃ syād iti | asmin pakṣe vedād durbalataratvaṃ smṛter upapadyate | tatra liṅgaprabhavatvam upapadyate ity uktam | tenāyam eva pakṣo yuktaḥ | yas tu svatantrapakṣo ‘bhihitaḥ, sa pakṣaḥ “tadvidāṃ ca smṛtiśīle” [GDh 1.2] ity etasmin sati durupapāda eveti | tasmād vedaliṅgaprabhavatvam eva smṛtīnāṃ nirdoṣataram iti |
nanu caivam api nopapadyate, kvacit smṛtyā śruter bādhadarśanāt | yathā sāmānyena pravṛtto vaidikakarmādhikāraḥ smṛtyā upanītādipuruṣaviśeṣa evādhikriyate | tathā saṃskāraikadeśenāpy ātmaguṇayoge sati viśiṣṭaphalābhidhānāt | tathā “gām ālabheta” iti śruticodito ‘pi govadhaḥ smārtapratiṣedhabalīyastvān na kriyate, tathāgnihotrādikaṃ coditajīvanopādhikaṃ karma dravyasādhyam api na smārtacauryādipratiṣaddhenāpi dravyam ārjayitvā tatkriyate | ataḥ “śrutyā smṛtir bādhyate” ity ayuktam uktam iti |
ucyate — virodhe sata pramāṇānāṃ balābalacintā yujyate | yatra tu virodhābhāvaḥ tatra durbalo ‘pi naiva bādhyata iti | yas tāvad upanītasya karmādhikāraḥ saḥ śrutyā naiva virudhyate, upanayanasya vedagrahaṇārthatvāt, tasya ca karmārthatvāt, na cāpūrvasaṃyogam antareṇa pradhānasiddhiḥ | yac coktaṃ “govadhas tu śruticodito ‘pi na kriyate” iti, tad api na kevalaṃ smṛtyā, kiṃ tu mantravarṇāc ca “mā gāḥ pramāpayāśvān pramāpaya” ityādi | ataḥ smṛtyā śrutir naiva bādhyate |
kecid utsannaśākhāpūrvikāṃ smṛtim āhuḥ |
tadayuktam | tatra vicāryate — kiṃ smaraṇāt pūrvam eva naṣṭāḥ śākhāḥ utottarakālam iti | yādi pūrvaṃ tatra smaraṇaṃ nopapadyate, vinaṣṭatvāt, pūrvavijñānasadṛśatvāt smṛteḥ | yady uttarakālam, tadotsannaśākhāpūrvakatvaṃ vaktuṃ na yujyate, tadānīm upalabhyamānatvāt | āpastamboktasyāpy ayam abhiprāyaḥ — utsannāḥ kvacit kvacid deśe na sarvatreti “teṣām utsannāḥ pāṭhāḥ prayogād anumīyante” [ĀpDh 1.12.10] ity atrāpi vyākhyā kartavyā || 1.6 ||
tulyapramāṇaprāpitayor evaṃjātīyakayor arthayor vikalpaḥ | tad vedaṃ vety anyatarasvīkāraḥ | na samuccayo ‘saṃbhavāt | prakarṣabodhane tu śrutismṛtivirodhe smṛtyartho nādaraṇīyaḥ, atulyabalatvāt | ata eva jābālir āha |
śrutismṛtivirodhe tu śrutir eva garīyasī |
avirodhe sadā kāryaṃ smārtaṃ vaidikavat sadā || iti || 1.5 ||
evam iyaṃ dharmapramāṇaparibhāṣoktā | idānīṃ smṛtyā yat pramīyate tad abhidhānārtham āha |
athedānīṃ darmān vakṣyann upanayanapūrvakatvāt teṣām upanayanaṃ tāvad āha |
upanayanaṃ brāhmaṇasyāṣṭame || 1.7 || — || 1.6 ||
vedasya agner ācāryasya yamaniyamānāṃ vā samīpe nayanam upanayanam | tad aṣṭame varṣe, varṣa-gaṇanāsāmarthyāt | nityo ‘yam upanayanakālaḥ, kāmyāpatkalpayor ūrdhvam abhidhānāt | kṣatriya-vaiśyayor ūrdhvam abhidhānāt śūdrasyaikajātitvād arthasiddhasyāpi brāhmaṇagrahaṇasya jātinimittatva-prasiddhyartham ārambhaḥ | na ca dvijatvavat brāhmaṇatvaṃ kriyata iti | evaṃ ca surāpasya brāhmaṇasya “surāpasya brāhmaṇasyoṣṇām āsiṃceyuḥ” [GDh 23.1] ityevamādayo brāhmaṇoddeśena vidhīyamānā anupanītasyāpi bhavanti | yad vā brāhmaṇagrahaṇena vinā trayāṇām apy etan nityaṃ syāt, kāmyaṃ tu brāhmaṇasya navame pañcame vā syāt [GDh 1.8], itarayos tv ekādaśa-dvādaśayoḥ syātām [GDh 1.13], kāmyānantaram abhidhānāt | pūrvasaṃskārābhāve ‘pi upanayanenādhi-kriyate, na tv anena vinoparitanair iti garbhādhānādim anuktvā upanayanasyaivehārambhaḥ || 1.7 ||
upanayanānantarabhāvini brāhmaṇatve ‘tra [brāhmaṇagrahaṇam] | brāhmaṇagrahaṇaṃ tu brāhmaṇasya sata evopanayanaṃ na tūpanayanādisaṃskarajanyabrāhmaṇyam iti jñāpanartham | kiṃ ca “brāhmaṇo na hantavyaḥ,” “brāhmaṇo na surāṃ pibet” iti niṣedhaśrutir anupanītaviṣaye(yā) na syāt | brāhmaṇasyāṣṭamaṃ varṣaṃ mukhyam upanayanakālaḥ | prathamabhāvino garbhādhānādīn saṃskārān ullaṅghyopanayanaṃ vyācakṣāṇas tasya prādhānyaṃ darśayati | tena daivānupapattyā garbhādhānāder akaraṇe ‘py upanayanaṃ bhavati | tasyākaraṇe tu vivāhādiṣv anadhikāra iti siddham || 1.6 ||
navame pañcame vā kāmyam || 1.8 || — || 1.7 ||
kāmāya hitaṃ kāmyaṃ tadutpādanasamartham | pañcame navama iti vācye kramabhedaḥ anayor antarā kṣatriyavaiśyayoḥ kāmyakālaparigrahārthaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
rājño balārthinaḥ ṣaṣṭhe vaiśyasyārthārthino ‘ṣṭame || [MDh 2.37] | visamāsaḥ kāmyabhedajñāpanārthaḥ | tathā ca smṛtyantaram: “navame tv āyuṣmākaṃ pañcame brahmavarcasakāmam” iti | vāśabdo vikalpārthaḥ | sa icchāto draṣṭavyaḥ || 1.8 |||
kāmanimittaṃ kāmyam | tan navame pañcame vā bhavati | “navame tejaskāmam” [ĀpDh 1.1.23] ity āpastambaḥ |
brahmavarcasakāmasya kāryo viprasya pañcame | [MDh 2.37] | iti manuḥ || 1.7 ||
garbhādiḥ saṃkhyā varṣāṇām || 1.9 || — || 1.8 ||
garbhasahacaritā daśa māsā garbha ity ucyate | tatprabhṛti varṣagaṇanā kartavyā, na tu janmata ārabhya | tataś ca “upanayanaṃ brāhmaṇasya” [GDh 1.7] ity evamādau “garbhāṣṭame” ity evamādi yojyam
|| 1.9 ||
varṣāṇāṃ saṃkhyā garbhādir eva bhavati, na jananādiḥ || 1.8 ||
tad dvitīyaṃ janma || 1.10 || — || 1.9 ||
tat upanayanaṃ dvitīyaṃ janma | tena saṃskṛto dvijo bhavatīty arthaḥ | “dvijātīnām adhyayanam” [GDh 10.1] ity evamādau vyavahārārthaṃ dvijasaṃjñā-karaṇam | tadgrahaṇaṃ nityakāmyopanayana-saṃgrahaṇārtham || 1.10 ||
tad upanayanaṃ dvitīyaṃ janma | atrāsya mātā sāvitrī pitā tv ācāryaḥ | tena dvijanmatvasiddhiḥ || 1.1.9 ||
tad yasmāt sa ācāryaḥ || 1.11 || — || 1.10 ||
tadgrahaṇaṃ nityakāmyopanayanasaṃgrahaṇārtham | “ācāryaḥ śreṣṭho gurūṇām” [GDh 2.57] ityādau vyavahārārthaṃ saṃjñākaraṇam || 1.11 ||
tad upanayanaṃ pitur abhāve yasmāt puruṣād bhavati sa ācāryaḥ || 1.10 ||
vedānuvacanāc ca || 1.12 || — || 1.11 ||
vedānuvacanaṃ samastavedānuvacanam | adṛṣṭārtham ekadeśādhyāpanena guruḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
alpaṃ vā bahu vā yasya śrutasyopakaroti yaḥ |
tam apīha guruṃ vidyāt śrutopakriyayā tayā || iti |
[MDh 3.22] | dṛṣṭārthaṃ ced upādhyāyaḥ | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “yas tv ekadeśaṃ sa upādhyāyaḥ” [VaDh 3.22] iti | cakārāt aṅgavyākhyātāpi | tathā ca yāskaḥ: “ācārya ācārān ācinoti, arthān ācinoti, buddhiṃ vā” [Nirukta 1.4] iti |
kecic cakāraṃ pūrvasūtreṇa samuccayārthaṃ vyācakṣate | tenobhayakaraṇād evācāryaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
upanīya tu yaḥ śiṣyaṃ vedam adhyāpayed dvijaḥ |
sakalpaṃ sarahasyaṃ ca tam ācāryaṃ pracakṣate || [MDh 2.140] iti || 1.12 ||
anuvacanam adhyāpanam | atra manuḥ |
upanīya tu yaḥ śiṣyaṃ vedam adhyapayed dvijaḥ |
sakalpaṃ sarahasyaṃ ca tam ācāryaṃ pracakṣate ||
[MDh 2.140] iti || 1.11 ||
ekādaśadvādaśayoḥ kṣatriyavaiśyayoḥ || 1.13 || — || 1.12 ||
nityo ‘yaṃ kālaḥ, kāmyāpatkalpayor ūrdhvam abhidhānāt || 1.13 ||
nityo ‘yam anayoḥ kalpaḥ | kāmyas tu manunā darśitaḥ | rājño balārthinaḥ ṣaṣṭhe vaiśyasyārthārthino ‘ṣṭame | [MDh 2.37] | iti || 1.12 ||
yadā tu nityakāmyayoḥ kenacid durbhikṣādinā nimittenātikramaḥ syāt tadānīm āpatkalpam āha |
ā ṣoḍaśād brāhmaṇasyāpatitā sāvitrī || 1.14 || — || 1.13 ||
āṅ atrābhividhyarthaḥ, saha ṣoḍasena varṣeṇeti, “dvyadhikāyāḥ” [GDh 1.16] iti liṅgāt, “ṣoḍaśe sarvakāmam” iti smṛtyantaradarśanāc ca | apatitā anapagatā | sāvitrīśabdena tatsādhanam upanayanam ucyate | upanayanasyādhikṛto bhavatīty arthaḥ | anupanītasyāpi jāter avināśārthaṃ brāhmaṇagrahaṇam | evaṃ ca pitrādiṣv anupanīteṣv api prāyaścittenopanayane ‘dhikriyate | tathāca āpastambaḥ: “yasya pitā pitāmahaḥ prapitāmahaḥ” [ĀpDh 1.1.32] ityādi || 1.14 ||
abhividhāv ākāraḥ | ā ṣoḍaśād varṣād brāhmaṇasya sāvitry apatitā apracyutā | sāvitrīśabdena tadupadeśanimittam upanayanaṃ lakṣyate | tad upanayanasya kālaḥ ity arthaḥ || 1.1.13 ||
dvāvaṃśate rājanyasya || 1.15 ||
dvāviṃśate rājanyasya dvyadhikāyā vaiśyasya || 1.14 ||
atrāpy āṅ anuvartate varṣasahitam | rājanyaśabdaprayogo rājatvaprasiddhyarthaḥ, tasyopanītasyaiva rājyādhikāra iti || 1.15 ||
ubhayatrāpy āṅ anuvartate | pūraṇapratyayasya lopo draṣṭavyaḥ | ā dvādiṃśād varṣād rājanyasya ā caturviṃśād vaiśyasyāpatitā sāvitrī || 1.14 ||
dvyadhikāyā vaiśyasya || 1.16 ||
dvābhyām adhikābhyāṃ sahitā yā dvāviṃśatiḥ sā dvyadhikāḥ, caturviṃśāter ity arthaḥ | caturviṃśater iti vaktavye evamabhidhānaṃ pūrvatrāṅo ‘bhividhijñāpanārtham || 1.16 ||
ukta upanayanakālaḥ | idānīṃ tatsādhanābhidhitsayāha |
mauñjījyāmaurvīsautryo mekhalāḥ krameṇa || 1.17 || — || 1.15 ||
muñjavikārā mauñjī | mūrvā prasiddhā, tadvikārā maurvī | jyā cāsau maurvī ceti samānādhikaraṇasamāsaḥ | tataś ca dhanuṣo ‘panīya grāhyā | sūtraṃ śaṇasūtram | ekaguṇā maurvī, itare trivṛtau | yathāha manuḥ |
mauñjī trivṛt samā ślakṣṇā kāryā viprasya mekhalā |
kṣatriyasya tu maurvī jyā vaiśyasya śaṇatāntavī || iti | [MDh 2.42] | jyāmaurvyor dvitvavyāvṛttyarthaṃ kramagrahaṇam |
kecid abhāve vaikalpikārtham iti varṇayanti | tathā ca manuḥ |
muñjābhāve tu kartavyā kuśāśmantakabalbajaiḥ |
trivṛtā granthinaikena tribhiḥ pañcabhir eva vā || iti | [MDh 2.43] nyāyasiddheḥ kramagrahaṇasyānyārthatvād iti || 1.17 ||
muñjo darbhaviśeṣas tadvikāro mauñjī | mūrvā araṇyauṣadhiviśeṣaḥ | (saralīti draviḍabhāṣāyām) | tadvikāro maurvīḥ | jyā cāsau maurvī ceti karmadhārayaḥ | jyāśabdena dhanuṣo grāhyeti yāvat | sautrī sūtravikāraḥ | etā varṇakrameṇa mekhalā bhavanti || 1.15 ||
kṛṣṇarurubastājināni || 1.18 || — || 1.16 ||
ajinaśabdaḥ pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyate | kṛṣṇaḥ kṛṣṇamṛgaḥ, ruruḥ pṛṣanmṛgaḥ, bastaḥ prasiddhaḥ | etāni krameṇottarīyāṇi | tathā ca smṛtyantaram: “ajinam evottarīyaṃ dhārayeyuḥ” iti || 1.18 ||
kṛṣṇaḥ kṛṣṇasāraḥ | rurur bindumān mṛgaḥ | bastaś chāgaḥ | eteṣām ajināny uttarīyāṇi krameṇa, “ajinaṃ tv evottaraṃ dhārayet” (ĀpDh 1.3.10) ity āpastambīye darśanāt || 1.16 ||
vāsāṃsi śāṇakṣaumacīrakutapāḥ sarveṣām || 1.19 || — || 1.17 ||
śaṇavikāraḥ śāṇaḥ | kṣumā atasī, tadvikāraḥ kṣaumaḥ | darbhādipariniṣpannaṃ cīram | kutapaḥ pārvatīyacchāgaromaniṣpannaḥ | etāny adhovasanāni | viṣamasaṃkhyatvād eva sarveṣāṃ siddheḥ smṛtyantare “śāṇaṃ kṣaumaṃ vāso brāhmaṇasya” iti | tad āśritya yad yathāsaṃkhyaṃ tannivṛttyarthaṃ sarveṣāṃgrahaṇaṃ, adhikārārthaṃ vā || 1.19 ||
śaṇavikāraḥ śāṇaḥ | kṣumā atasī tadvikāraḥ kṣaumam | śvetapaṭṭa ity anye | darbhādinimitaṃ cīram | ūrṇānirmitaḥ kambalaḥ kutapaḥ | catvāry etāni vāsāṃsi sarveṣām || 1.17 ||
kārpāsaṃ cāvikṛtam || 1.20 ||
kārpāsaṃ vāvikṛtam || 1.18 ||
sarveṣām ity anuvartate, vāsa iti ca | avikṛtam arāgasaṃbandham | svabhāvataḥ śuklam evety arthaḥ | pūrveṣām apy avikṛtatvajñāpanārthaś caśabdaḥ || 1.20 ||
avikṛtaṃ kārpāsaṃ vāsaḥ sarveṣām | kusumbhādirāgadravyair varṇāntarakalpanaṃ vikṛtis tadrahitam || 1.18 ||
anumānāny āha |
kāṣāyam apy eke || 1.21 || — || 1.19 ||
rāgasaṃbandham api kecid icchanti | apiśabdo nindārthaḥ | ataḥ pūrveṣām abhāve draṣṭavyam || 1.21 ||
eke tv ācāryāḥ kaṣāyeṇa raktam api dhāryaṃ manyante || 1.19 ||
idānīṃ kāṣāyapakṣe niyamārtham āha |
tatrāpi niyamaḥ |
vārkṣaṃ brāhmaṇasya || 1.22 ||
vārkṣaṃ brāhmaṇasya māñjiṣṭhahāridre itarayoḥ || 1.20 ||
vṛṣakaṣāyakṛtaṃ vārkṣam, tad eva brāhmaṇasya vāsaḥ || 1.22 ||
vṛkṣakaṣāyeṇa raktaṃ vārkṣam | tad brāhmaṇasya | mañjiṣṭhayā raktaṃ māñjiṣṭham | haridrayā raktaṃ hāridram | te itarayoḥ | kṣatriyavaiśyayor iti yāvat || 1.20 ||
māñjiṣṭhahāridre itarayoḥ || 1.23 ||
mañjiṣṭhayā vikṛtaṃ māñjiṣṭhaṃ kṣatriyasya | haridrayā vikṛtaṃ vaiśyasya || 1.23 ||
bailvapālāśau brāhmaṇadaṇḍau || 1.24 || — || 1.21 ||
bailvaḥ pālāśo vā brāhmaṇasya daṇḍaḥ | yady api dvandvanirdeśaiḥ, tathāpi “yajñiyo vā sarveṣām” [GDh 1.26] ity ekavacananirdeśāt, “pratigṛhyepsitaṃ daṇḍam” [MDh 2.48] iti smṛtyantaradarśanāc ca ekasyaiva dhāraṇaṃ yuktam || 1.24 ||
bailvaḥ pālāśo vā brāhmaṇasya daṇḍo na punaḥ samuccitau || 1.21 ||
āśvatthapailavau śeṣe || 1.25 || — || 1.22 ||
aśvatthaḥ prasiddhaḥ, pīluḥ kalkiḥ, śeṣe kṣatriyavaiśyaviṣaye | tatrāpi kṣatriyasyāśvatthaḥ, vaiśyasya pailavaḥ || 1.25 ||
pīlur vṛkṣaviśeṣaḥ | utā iti prasiddhaḥ | śeṣe kṣatriyavaiśyaviṣaye || 1.22 ||
yajñiyo vā sarveṣām || 1.26 || — || 1.23 ||
yajñārho vā syāt vibhītakabilvakayāvakādivarjito vṛkṣaviśeṣa ity arthaḥ | vāśabdo ‘bhāvavikalpārthaḥ, ataḥ pūrveṣām abhāve | anyathāpārtho vāśabdaḥ | sarveṣāṃgrahaṇam anantarayor eva mā bhūd iti || 1.26 ||
sarveṣām uktālābhe yajñiyo yajñiyavṛkṣo vā daṇḍaḥ syāt || 1.23 ||
idānīṃ daṇḍaguṇam āha |
apīḍitā yūpavakrāḥ saśalkāḥ || 1.27 || — 1.24 ||
apīḍitā vallīveṣṭanakīṭāgnyādibhir anupahatā ity arthaḥ | yūpavakrāḥ yūpavan natāgrā ity arthaḥ | saśalkāḥ satvacaḥ | asamāsāt sarva evaṃrūpāḥ, na tu yathāsaṃkhyam | tathā cāha manuḥ |
ṛjavas te tu sarve syur avraṇāḥ saumyadarśanāḥ |
anudvegakarā nṝṇāṃ satvaco ‘nagnidūṣitāḥ ||
[MDh 2.47] iti || 1.27 ||
apīḍitāḥ kīṭādibhir adūṣitāḥ | yūpavakrā yūpavad agre vakrāḥ | saśalkāḥ śalkāḥ [tvacaḥ?] satvacaḥ | evaṃvidhā daṇḍāḥ sarveṣām || 1.24 ||
mūrdhalalāṭanāsāgrapramāṇāḥ || 1.28 || — || 1.25 ||
pramāṇaśabdaḥ pratyekaṃ saṃbadhyate | mūrdhapramāṇo brahmaṇasya, lalāṭapramāṇaḥ kṣatriyasya, nāsāgrapramāṇo vaiśyasya | tathā ca manuḥ |
keśāntiko brāhmaṇasya daṇḍaḥ kāryaḥ pramāṇataḥ |
lalāṭasaṃmito rājñaḥ syāt tu nāsāntiko viśaḥ ||
[MDh 2.46] iti || 1.28 ||
yathāsaṃkhyam atreṣyate | mūrdhapramāṇo brāhmaṇasya daṇdaḥ | lalāṭāvadhiḥ kṣatriyasya | nāsāvadhir vaiśyasyeti || 1.25 ||
idānīṃ brahmacāriveṣam āha |
muṇḍajaṭilaśikhājaṭāś ca || 1.29 || — || 1.26 ||
muṇḍo muṇḍitaśiraskaḥ | jaṭilaḥ sakeśaḥ | śikhā eva jaṭā yasya sa śikhājaṭaḥ | evaṃ muṇḍaśabdena samastamuṇḍanam ucyate | cakāro vārthaḥ pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyate — muṇḍo vā jaṭilo vā śikhājaṭo veti | brāhmaṇakṣatriyavaiśyābhiprāyaṃ bahuvacanam | yathākramanivṛttyarthaṃ veti noktam |
tathā ca manuḥ |
muṇḍo vā jaṭilo vā syād atha vā syāc chikhājaṭaḥ | [MDh 2.219] iti || 1.29 ||
atra na yathāsaṃkhyam | muṇḍā luptasarvakeśāḥ | jaṭilāḥ keśadhāriṇaḥ | jaṭā keśasaṃhatiḥ | śikhāmātraiva jaṭā yeṣāṃ te śikhājaṭāḥ | sarveṣām ayaṃ sāmānyadharmaḥ | chandogāpekṣayā muṇḍaśabdagrahaṇam || 1.26 ||
idānīṃ śaucavato 'dhyayanādhikārāt śaucārthaṃ pūrvaṃ dravyaśuddhir ucyate |
dravyahasta ucchiṣṭo nidhāyācāmet
|| 1.30 ||
dravyahastaś ced ucchiṣṭo ‘nidhāyācāmet
|| 1.27 ||
brāhmaṇabhojanādau pravṛtto dravyahastaḥ pūrvam anucchiṣṭaḥ kenacit kāraṇenocchiṣṭasparśād aprayato bhavati, tadā taddravyaṃ bhūmau nidhāya sthāpayitvācāmet | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ |
pracarann abhyavahāryeṣu ucchiṣṭaṃ yadi saṃspṛśet |
bhūmau nidhāya tad dravyam ācamya pracaret punaḥ || iti | [VaDh 3.43] | puruṣaśaucavivakṣāyāṃ dravyahastagrahaṇam anarthakam | ato dravyasya nidhānamātrād eva śuddhiḥ, puruṣasyācamanāt | yat tu dravyasya smṛtyantare prokṣaṇam uktam: “bhūmau nikṣipya prokṣaṇād dravyaṃ śuddhyati” iti tad abhyavahāryapakvadravyaviṣayam eva draṣṭavyam |
kathaṃ punas tulyasaṃhite sūtre viśeṣo ‘vagamyate — nidhāyācamanaṃ na punar anidhāyeti |
ucyate – yady anidhāyācamanam iṣṭaṃ syāt, tato “dravyahasta ucchiṣṭa ācāmet” ity etāvad eva vaktavyaṃ syāt, kiṃ nidhānena kriyata iti, tataḥ sūtrātirekān nidhānapakṣa evābhīṣṭa ity avagamyate | yat tu manunoktaṃ |
ucchiṣṭena tu saṃspṛṣṭo dravyahastaḥ kathaṃcana |
anidhāyaiva tad dravyam ācāntaḥ śucitām iyāt || [MDh 5.143] iti tad anabhyavahāryadravyaviṣayaṃ draṣṭavyam | evaṃ cābhyavahāryadravyasyāpakvasya kadalīphalādeḥ nidhānamātrād eva śuddhiḥ | pakvasya bhūmau nikṣipya prokṣaṇāc chuddhiḥ | anabhyavahāryadravyasya vastrāder anidhāyācamanāc chuddhiḥ || 1.30 ||
mūtrapurīṣayoḥ karma bhojanādi cocchiṣṭatvanimittam | dravyahastaḥ sann ucchiṣṭaś cet tad dravyam anidhāyācāmet | ucchiṣṭaḥ san dravyahastaś ced dravyaṃ nidhāyācāmet | tathā ca manuḥ |
ucchiṣṭena tu saṃspṛṣṭo dravyahastaḥ kathaṃcana |
anidhāyaiva tad dravyam ācāntaḥ śucitām iyāt || iti | [MDh 5.143]
kiṃ ca bhakṣyabhojyādidravyaviṣaye tad dravyaṃ nidhyaiva mūtrapurīṣayoḥ karma kṛtvā punas tat pātraṃ nidhāyācāmet | vastradaṇḍādiviṣaye tv anidhāyaivācāmet || 1.27 ||
idānīṃ dravyaśuddhim āha |
atha dravyaśuddhir ucyate |
dravyaśuddhiḥ parimārjanapradāhatakṣaṇanirṇejanāni taijasamārttikadāravatāntavānām || 1.31 || — 1.28 ||
taijasādīnāṃ dravyāṇāṃ parimārjanādinā krameṇa śuddhiḥ | dravyatve siddhe teṣāṃ dravyavacanaṃ svakāryaniṣpādanasāmarthyārtham | tataś ca bhinnabhāṇḍādīnāṃ na śaucam | tejasā hetubhūtena yāni kriyante tāni taijasāni sauvarṇādīni | teṣāṃ taijasānām ucchiṣṭaliptānām | kuta etat? mūtrādyupahatānāṃ smṛtyantare śuddhyantaravidhānāt | tathā ca auśanasaṃ: “taijasānāṃ mūtrapurīṣaretaḥkuṇapopahatānāṃ āvartanam ullekhanaṃ bhasmanā trisaptakṛtvaḥ parimārjanam | ataijasānām evaṃbhūtānām utsargaḥ” iti | tathā anyaśarīramaladūṣite kaṇvavacanam ārabdham: “śleṣmādyupahatānām ekaviṃśatiparimārjanāc chuddhiḥ” iti | tathācāspṛśyochiṣṭānām auśanasaṃ vacanaṃ saṃgatam ārabdham: “anulomaśūdraśva-kākopahatānām ullekhanaṃ pratilomeṣu spṛśyopahatānām ekaviṃśatiparimarjanam aspṛśyopahatānām āvartanam” iti | tathā māṃsarudhiropahatānāṃ śaṅkhavacanam ārabdhaṃ: “mṛdariṣṭakeṅgudabilvasarṣapakalkagomūtragomayādīni śaucadravyāṇi” iti | tathā “rajasvalāduṣṭānām ekadinaṃ pañcagavye nikṣipya ekaviṃśatiparimārjanāc chuddhiḥ” iti paiṭhīnasivacanam | evam anyeṣv api smṛtyantareṣu malāntaradūṣiteṣu taijaseṣu śuddhyantarāṇy āmnātāni: “ato ‘trocchiṣṭaliptānām eva kalpayituṃ nyāyyam” iti | teṣām ucchiṣṭaliptānāṃ parimārjanaṃ bhasmanā paritaḥ sarvataḥ | mārttikānāṃ mṛnmayānām aspṛśyopahatānām | kutaḥ? smṛtyantare viśeṣadarśanāt | tathā uśanaso vacanaṃ: “mṛnmayānām ucchiṣṭaliptānām apamārjanam ucchaṣṭaspṛṣṭānāṃ prokṣaṇam” iti | tathā vasiṣṭhavacanam |
madyamūtrapurīṣaiś ca śleṣmapūyāśruśoṇitaiḥ |
saṃspṛṣṭaṃ naiva śuddhyeta punaḥpākena mṛnmayam | [VaDh 3.59] iti madyādidūṣitānāṃ tyāga uktaḥ | ato ‘tra caṇḍālādyupahatānām eva kalpayituṃ nyāyyam iti | pradāhaḥ prakarṣeṇa dāhaḥ pūrvasvabhāvotpādanam ity arthaḥ | dāravāṇāṃ āsanaśayanādīnām amedhyādyupahatānāṃ takṣaṇaṃ tanūkaraṇaṃ yāvanmātram upahatam | aspṛśyaspṛṣṭānāṃ prokṣaṇam | kutaḥ? “prokṣaṇaṃ saṃhatānām” iti manuvacanāt [MDh 5.115] | prakṣālanaśakyānāṃ prakṣālanam | tāntavānāṃ tantumayānāṃ kārpāsādi-vikārāṇām aspṛśyadūṣitānām | maladūṣite smṛtyantaraṃ draṣṭavyam: “dhāvanaṃ tanmātracchedanam utsargaḥ” iti | “celādīnāṃ malādidūṣitānām” iti paiṭhīnasivacanam | tathā “śrīphalair aṃśupaṭṭānām” [MDh 5.120] ityevamādīni manunoktāni draṣṭavyāni, aspṛśyadūṣitānāṃ bahūnāṃ vastrāṇāṃ prokṣaṇam “bahūnāṃ dhānyavāsasām” [MDh 5.118] iti manunā prokṣaṇenaiva śuddhividhānāt | ato ‘lpānām aspṛśyadūṣitānāṃ nirṇejanam || 1.31 ||
taijasādīnāṃ dravyāṇāṃ yathākramaṃ parimārjanādiśuddhayaḥ | taijasaṃ kāṃśyādi | mārtikaṃ mṛnmayādi | dāravaṃ dārumayādi | tāntavaṃ tantumayādi | teṣāṃ krameṇa parimārjanam | tatra bhasmanā kāṃsyasya, śakṛtā sauvarṇarājatayoḥ, āmlena tāmrasya | idam ucchiṣṭaliptānām | “taijasānām evaṃbhūtānāṃ bhasmādibhiḥ” iti kaṇvaḥ | rajasvalācaṇḍālādispṛṣṭānām ekadinaṃ pañcagavyaṃ nikṣipyaikaviṃśatikṛtvo mārjanāc chuddhiḥ | mārtikānāṃ pradāhaḥ | prakṛṣṭo dāho varṇāntarāpattir yathā syāt tathāvidho dāhaḥ śodhanam | idaṃ sparśopahatānām | atra vasiṣṭhaḥ |
madyamūtrapurīṣais tu śleṣmapūyāśruśoṇitaiḥ |
saṃspṛṣṭaṃ naiva śudhyeta punardāhena mṛnmayam || iti | [VaDh 3.59] | dāravāṇāṃ takṣaṇāc chuddhiḥ | idam amedhyādivāsitānām | anyatra prokṣaṇaprakṣālanādi | tāntavānāṃ nirṇejanāc chuddhiḥ | idaṃ sparśadūṣitānām | malādidūṣitānāṃ dhāvanaṃ tanmātracchedanaṃ vā | sparśadūṣitānāṃ bahūnāṃ prokṣaṇāc chuddhir iti || 1.28 ||
taijasavad upalamaṇiśaṅkhaśuktīnām
|| 1.32 ||

|| 1.29 ||
taijasavac chuddhir upalādīnām | atideśaḥ smṛtyantaroktaśuddhyapekṣārthaḥ | upalānāṃ maṇīnāṃ tāvac chrūyate |
nirlepaṃ kāñcanaṃ bhāṇḍaṃ adbhir eva viśuddhyati |
abjam aśmamayaṃ caiva rājataṃ cānupaskṛtam || [MDh 5.112]
śaṅkhaśuktimaṇīnāṃ ca gomūtreṇodakena vā ||
iti manuvacanam | “payasā śaṅkhaśuktīnām’ iti paiṭhīnasivacanam || 1.32 ||
upalādīnāṃ taijasavac chuddhiḥ parimārjanam iti || 1.29 ||
dāruvad asthibhūmyoḥ || 1.33 || — || 1.30 ||
asthi hastidantādisaṃvyavahāryadravyāṇi | bhūmiḥ gṛhādiḥ dāruvad iti prakṛtidravyeṇātideśaṃ bruvan etat jñāpayati – yat vikārasya śaucam ucyate prakṛtidravyasyāpi tad eva bhavatīti | itarathā dāravavad iti vaktavyam āsīd iti | asthibhūmyoḥ smṛtyantaropadiṣṭāpekṣārtho ‘tideśaḥ | tathā ca smṛtyantaram | “aspṛśyaspṛṣṭānāṃ bhūmyasthnāṃ prokṣaṇāc chuddhiḥ” iti kaṇvavacanam || 1.33 ||
asthi hastidantādi | bhūmir gṛhādi | tayor dāruvac chuddhis takṣaṇam iti | dāravavad iti vaktavye dāruvad iti nirdeśād vikārasya yā śuddhir vikāriṇo ‘pi saiva śuddhir ity uktam || 1.30 ||
āvapanaṃ ca bhūmeḥ || 1.34 || — || 1.31 ||
bhūmigrahaṇam asthinivṛttyartham | cakārāt smṛtyantaroktāny api draṣṭavyāni | tathā coktaṃ vasiṣṭhena |
khananād dahanād gharṣāt gobhir ākramaṇād api |
caturbhiḥ śuddhyate bhūmiḥ pañcamāc copalepanāt || iti | [VaDh 3.57]
tathā ca manuḥ |
saṃmārjanena sekena dāhenollekhanena ca |
gavāṃ ca parivāsena bhūmiḥ śuddhyati pañcabhiḥ || iti | [MDh 5.124] | yathālepaṃ vikalpena samuccayena vā śaucaṃ kartavyam || 1.34 ||
āvapanam anyata ānīya pūraṇam adhikā śuddhir bhūmeḥ | atra vasiṣṭhaḥ |
khananād dahanād adbhir gobhir ākramaṇena ca |
caturbhir śudhyate bhūmiḥ pañcamāt tūpalepanāt || [VaDh 3.57] iti || 1.31 ||
celavad rajjuvidalacarmaṇām || 1.35 || — || 1.32 ||
H reads “cailavad”
rajjuḥ darbhādiniṣpannaḥ | vidalaḥ vaṃśamayūra-pakṣādiniṣpannaḥ carma vyāghracarmādi teṣām aspṛśyaspṛṣṭānāṃ celavat vastravac chaucam | carmaśabdena prakṛtigrahaṇena vikārasyāpi grahaṇam | rajjvādīnāṃ prokṣaṇasyāpi praveśārtham atideśaḥ | ato ‘spṛśyaspṛṣṭānāṃ prokṣaṇam api kartavyam | tathā ca paiṭhanasiḥ: “'rajjuvidalacarmaṇām aspṛśyaspṛṣṭānāṃ prokṣaṇāc chuddhiḥ” iti || 1.35 ||
vidalaṃ vetraveṇuvidalādinirmitam | picchanirmitam apy anye | rajjvādīnāṃ trayāṇāṃ cailavad vastravac chuddhir nirṇejanam iti | paiṭhīnasis tu: “rajjuvidalacarmaṇām aspṛśyaspṛṣṭānāṃ prokṣaṇāc chuddhiḥ” iti || 1.32 ||
utsargo vātyantopahatānām || 1.36 || — 1.33 ||
rajjvādīnāṃ tyāgo vā | vyavasthitavikalpo ‘yam | ato ‘tyantopahatānāṃ tyāgaḥ | itareṣāṃ śaucam iti || 1.36 ||
idaṃ vāsiṣṭhena samānaviṣayaṃ “madyamūtrapurīṣaiḥ” [VaDh 3.59] ityādinā | vāśabdaḥ pakṣavyāvṛttau || 1.33 ||
idānīṃ “dravyahasta ucchiṣṭo nidhāyācāmet” ityuktaṃ, tadācamanavidhitsayāha |
prāṅmukha udaṅmukho vā śaucam ārabheta || 1.37 || — || 1.34 ||
prāṅmukha udaṅmukho vetīcchāto vikalpaḥ |
nanu ca śaucagrahaṇam anarthakam, uttaratrācāmed iti vakṣyati, tenaivārthasiddhatvād iti |
ucyate – anyad api śaucāṅgam etad digabhimukha eva kuryād iti | evaṃ ca pādaprakṣālanādikam api taddigabhimukha eva kuryād iti | ārabheteti samāhitārtfham, ato yāvad ācamanaparisamāptiḥ tāvantaṃ kālam ananyacitto bhavet || 1.37 ||
icchāto vikalpa ārabheteti vacanāt pādaprakṣālana-prabhṛtidiṅniyamaḥ | āpastambas tu “pratyakpādāva-nejanam” [ĀpDh 1.31.2] ity āha | śaucagrahaṇam ācamana eva mā bhūn mūtrapurīṣādiśauce 'pi diṅniyamajñāpanārtham || 1.34 ||
śucau deśa āsīno dakṣiṇaṃ bāhuṃ jānvantarā kṛtvā yajñopavīty ā maṇibandhāt pāṇī prakṣālya vāgyato hṛdayaspṛśas triś catur vāpa ācāmet || 1.38 || — || 1.35 ||
śucau deśe sthāne anupahate | evaṃ ca pādaprakṣālanam apy arthasiddhaṃ mā bhūt, aśuddhipādasaṃbandhād deśasyāpy aśucibhāva iti | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “prakṣālya pādau pāṇī cā maṇibandhanāt” [VaDh 3.26] iti | āsīna eva, nānyāvasthaḥ | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “vrajan tiṣṭhan śayānaḥ praṇato vā nācāmet” [VaDh 3.30] iti | dākṣiṇaṃ bāhuṃ jānvor madhye kṛtvā | yajñopavītīti | yadi kadācit ayajñopavītī bhūtvāpy āste tathāpy ācamane avaśyaṃ yajñopavītinā bhavitavyam iti | tathā ca āpaspambaḥ: “upāsane gurūṇāṃ vṛddhānām atithīnāṃ home japyakarmaṇi bhojana ācamane svādhyāye ca yajñopavīti syāt” [ĀpDh 1.15.1] iti | ācamanāṅgatvenānyayajñopavītotpādanārthaṃ vā | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ |
snātakānāṃ tu nityaṃ syād antarvāsas tathottaram |
yajñopavīte dve yaṣṭiḥ sodakaś ca kamaṇḍaluḥ || iti | [VaDh 12.14]
maṇir yasmin deśe badhyate sa maṇibandhaḥ | tasmād ā maṇibandhanāt pāṇī prakṣālya vāgyatas tūṣṇīṃ hṛdayaspṛśo hṛdayaṃgamāḥ | apāṃ parimāṇārtham idam | māṣanimajjanamātrās tāvatyo bhavanti | tathā cāha uśanā: “māṣanimajjanamātrā hṛdayaṃgamā bhavanti” iti | triś caturvety atra vikalpo vyavasthitaḥ – yadā “āpaḥ punantu” ityādimantram uktvācamyate tadā catuḥ anyatra trir iti | abgrahaṇaṃ gomūtrādi-nivṛttyartham, teṣāṃ śuddhihetutvasāmānyād grahaṇaṃ mā bhūd iti | uṣṇabudbudaphena-kaṣāyādivarjanārthaṃ vā | pibed iti vaktavye ācāmed iti gurusūtrakaraṇaṃ vidhyantaropasaṃgrahārtham | tatra tu uśanāha: “dakṣiṇaṃ hastatalaṃ gokarṇākāraṃ vinyasya brāhmeṇa tīrthenāśabdaṃ pibed ṛgyajuḥsāmavedān dhyāyan” iti || 1.38 ||
idam ekaṃ vākyam | ācamanakāle śucau deśe ‘nupahata āsīna ity upalakṣaṇam āsīnas tiṣṭhan prahvo veti | jānvantarā jānunor madhye dakṣiṇabāhuṃ kṛtvā | dakṣiṇaṃ bāhum ity uktatvād vāmahastasya nāvaśyaṃbhāvaḥ | yajñopavītīti pūrvaṃ svasthānastham api yathāsthānaniveśanārtham | atha vottarīyavinyāsārtham | tathā ca āpastambaḥ: “upāsane gurūṇāṃ vṛddhānām atithīnāṃ home japyakarmaṇi bhojana ācamane svādhyāye ca yajñopavītī syāt” | “api vā sūtram evopavītārthaḥ” [ĀpDh 1.15.1; 2.4.22] iti | ā maṇibandhād yasmin deśe maṇir badhyata ā tasmāt pāṇī prakṣālya | vāgyataḥ śabdam akurvan | hṛdayaspṛśaḥ parimāṇārtham idaṃ yāvatyaḥ pītā hṛdayaṃ spṛśanti yāsu māṣo majjati tāvatīr apa ācāmet triś catur vā | yatra mantravad ācamanaṃ vihitaṃ tatra tena saha catuḥ | anyatra trir iti vikalpaḥ || 1.35 ||
dviḥ parimṛjyāt || 1.39 ||
dviḥ parimṛjyate || 1.36 ||
prakṛtāv oṣṭhau, atrānuktatvāt, sṛtyantaradarśanāc ca, brāhmeṇaiva tīrthena | parity upasargo vidhyantaropasaṃgrahārthaḥ | tatra tūktaṃ kaṇvena: “atharvavedetihāsapurāṇāni dhyāyan brāhmeṇa tīrthenoṣṭhayoḥ salomapradeśaṃ mṛjyāt” iti || 1.39 ||

trir ācāmed apaḥ pūrvaṃ dvir unmṛjyāt tato mukham |
khāni copaspṛśed adbhir ātmānaṃ hṛdayaṃ śiraḥ || iti | [MDh 2.60] | upopasargo vidhyantaropasaṃ-grahārthaḥ | tatra uśanā: “aṅguṣṭhopakaniṣṭhikābhyāṃ nayane candrādityau dhyātvā tata upaspṛśya tarjanyaṅguṣṭhābhyāṃ nāsikāvakāśau prāṇān dhyātvā tata upaspṛśya kaniṣṭhikāṅguṣṭhābhyāṃ karṇau diśo dhyātvā tata upaspṛśyāṅguṣṭhena kevalena hṛdayam upaspṛśed ātmānaṃ dhyāyan” iti || 1.41 ||
śīrṣe bhavāni śīrṣaṇyāni | śirobhavānīti yāvat | khāni indriyāṇi | tāny upaspṛśet | atra cakāraḥ pratīndriyopasparśanārthaḥ || 1.38 ||
mūrdhani ca dadyāt || 1.42 || — || 1.39 ||
mūrdhani ceti vaktavye dadyād iti grahaṇaṃ sarvāṅgulyartham | tathā ca smṛtyantaram: “sarvāṅgulibhiḥ śriyaṃ dhyātvā mūrdhani prakṣipet” iti | cakārāt savye ca pāṇau | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “mūrdhani ca dadyāt savye pāṇau” [VaDh 3.29] iti | kṣatriyavaiśyayor apy etad evācamanaṃ viśeṣānabhidhānāt “strīśūdrau ca sakṛt sakṛt” [MDh 5.139] iti smṛtyantarābhidhānāt strīśūdrayoś ca sakṛt pānāt sakṛd oṣṭhasparśanāc ca śuddhir draṣṭavyā atrānuktāpi || 1.42 ||
cakārān nābhau mūrdhani ca sarvābhir aṅgulībhir upaspṛśed ity arthaḥ || 1.39 ||
na kevalam ucchiṣṭasparśa evācamanaṃ, anyad apy ācamananimittam idānīm āha |
suptvā bhuktvā kṣutvā ca punaḥ || 1.43 || — 1.40 ||
punargrahaṇaṃ “ādāv ante cācamanaṃ kuryād” ityevamartham | cakārāt snātvā ruditvā ca | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “suptvā bhuktvā kṣutvā snātvā pītvā ruditvā cācāntaḥ punar ācāmet” [VaDh 3.38] iti | tatra kṣutasyāniyatakālatvāt ādāv aśakyam ācamanam iti cet, tatrāpi nityaprāyatyārtha ārambhaḥ | yathāha āpastambaḥ: “muhūrtam api śaktiviṣaye nāprayatas tiṣṭhet” [ĀpDh 1.15.8] iti | svāpādau prayatasyāpy ācamanam ārambhasāmarthyāt | atas tatrādāv ante cācamanaṃ kuryāt |
tatra bhojane kecid ādāv api dvirācamanam icchanti | tathā ca āpastambaḥ: “bhokṣyamāṇas tu prayato dvir ācāmet” [ĀpDh 1.16.9] iti |
anye svāpādāv anta eva dvir ācamanam icchanti yathāha uśanā: “suptvā bhuktvā kṣutvā snātvā pītvā ruditvā cācāntaḥ punar ācāmed dviḥ” iti |
evaṃ ca bhojane ādāv ante ca dvirācamanaṃ svāpādāv ekam ante ca dviḥ | kṣutasyāniyatatvād anta eva dvir iti siddhāntaḥ || 1.43 ||
svāpādinimitte punar dvir ācāmed iti yāvat || 1.40 ||

saty api jihvābhimarśane yāval lepāḥ svasthānān na cyavante tāvan nāśucitvam iti || 1.42 ||
cyuteṣv āsrāvavad vidyān nigirann eva tacchuciḥ || 1.46 || — || 1.43 ||
cyuteṣu āsye yaj jalaṃ tad āsrāve tadvad vijānīyāt | tannigiran śucir eva bhavati | ācamanaśaṅkā nāstīty arthaḥ | evaṃ cāsrāvam api nigiran śucir eva bhavati | kutaḥ? cyuteṣu nigiran śucir iti vaktavye “āsrāvavad vidyād” ityārambhāt | gautamas tu dantaśliṣṭānāṃ sati jihvābhimarśane aśucir bhavatīti | anyamataṃ tu prākcyuter iti | so ‘yaṃ vyavasthitavikalpaḥ, karmānuṣṭhānādhikṛtasyācamanam anyasya neti || 1.46 ||
āsrāva āsyajalam | nigaraṇam antaḥpraveśanam | cyuteṣu nigirann eva tacchucir iti vaktavya āsrāvavad vidyād iti vacanam āsrāve ca nigaraṇād eva śucir iti sūcanārtham || 1.43 ||
yathocchiṣṭāvayavo bahirbhūtaḥ aśucitvam āpādayati tadvad āsrāvasyāpi prāpta āha |
na mukhyā vipruṣa ucchiṣṭaṃ kurvanti na ced aṅge nipatanti || 1.47 || — 1.44 ||
mukhe bhavā mukhyāḥ, vipruṣa āsrāvāvayavāḥ, te mantrayamāṇasya vinirgatā nāprāyatyaṃ janayanti yady aṅge stanodarādau na nipatanti | niśabdo niścayārthaḥ | ato niścayena yady aṅge patanti tadānīm evocchiṣṭaṃ janayanti nāśaṅkāmātreṇeti | evaṃ cocchiṣṭāvayavo yatra kutracin nipatann ucchiṣṭaṃ janayati, āsrāvo ‘ṅga eveti siddhāntaḥ || 1.47 ||
mukhe bhavā mukhyāḥ | vipruṣa āsrāvabindavaḥ | bhūmyādiṣu patitā nocchiṣṭatāṃ nayanti
|| 1.44 ||
sarvaśaucārtham idam adhunocyate |
lepagandhāpakarṣaṇe śaucam amedhyaliptasya || 1.48 ||
lepagandhāpakarṣaṇaṃ śaucam
amedhyasya || 1.45 ||
lepagandhayor apanayane kṛte, amedhyaliptasya amedhyasaṃbaddhasya śucitvaṃ bhavati | amedhyaśabdena dvādaśamalā ucyante | yathāha manuḥ |
vasā śuklam asṛṅ majjā mūtraviḍ ghrāṇakarṇaviṭ |
śleṣmāśru dūṣikā svedo dvādaśaite nṛṇāṃ malāḥ || [MDh 5.135] | anabhivyaktagandhānāṃ śleṣmādīnāṃ lepamātradarśanāt lepagrahaṇam | mūtrādīnāṃ lepānupalabdher gandhamātropalabdheś ca gandhagrahaṇam | śaucādhikāre punaḥ śaucagrahaṇaṃ taijasādīnām api lepagandhāpanayanād eva śaucam ity evamartham || 1.48 ||
vasā śukram asṛṅ majjā mūtraviṭkarṇaviṇnakhāḥ |
śleṣmāśru dūṣikā svedo dvādaśaite nṛṇāṃ malāḥ || [MDh 3.135]
iti manuḥ | etat sarvam amedhyaśabdena vivakṣitam | asya yāvatā gandho lepaś cāpakṛṣyate ‘panīyate tāvatā śaucam iti | tatra yasya malasya gandhamātraṃ tasya tadapakarṣaṇam | yasya gandho lepaś ca tasya tadubhayāpakarṣaṇam || 1.45 ||
tat kena kartavyami ty ata āha |
tad adbhiḥ pūrvaṃ mṛdā ca || 1.49 || — || 1.46 ||
tac chaucam adbhiḥ pūrvaṃ tato mṛdā | cakārāt tata ubhayena | tadgrahaṇaṃ annalepasyāpi cūrṇādinā apakarṣaṇārtham | tad adbhir mṛdā ceti vaktavye pūrvagrahaṇaṃ yatra nātīva lepo lakṣyate tatrādbhir eva śaucam ity evamarthaṃ | yathāha manuḥ |
nirlepaṃ kāñcanaṃ bhāṇḍam adbhir eva viśudhyati |
abjam aśmamayaṃ caiva rājataṃ cānupaskṛtam || iti | [MDh 5.112]
atha vā yatra yatra śaucam uktaṃ tatra tatrādbhir eva śaucaṃ prathamaṃ kāryam ity evamartham | yathā mṛdā adbhir iti samuccīyate, tathā bhasmādibhir api samuccayārtho visamāsaḥ | taisajādīnāṃ dravyāṇām uktatvāt, idaṃ śaucaṃ śarīrāvayavasyāmedhyaliptasya draṣṭavyam || 1.49 ||
tat pūrvaṃ gandhavan malāpakarṣaṇam adbhir lepagandhavan malāpakarṣaṇaṃ mṛdā cādbhiś ceti | idaṃ hastapādāder amedhyaliptasya śaucam, taijasādiṣu viśeṣasya pūrvam uktatvāt
|| 1.46 ||
mūtrapurīṣasrehuvisraṃsanābhyavahārasaṃyogeṣu ca || 1.50 || — || 1.47 ||
[H sneha for srehu]
cakāraḥ ācamanānukarṣaṇārthaḥ | visraṃsanaṃ nirasanaṃ, tat mūtrapūrīṣasrehūṇāṃ pratyekaṃ saṃbadhyate | abhyavahārasaṃyogo ‘bhyavahāryadravyopayogaḥ | tasyānamedhyatvāt lepagandhāpakarṣaṇārtham abhidhānam, ācamanasya ‘suptvā bhuktvā’ ityanena siddhatvāt lepagandhāpakarṣaṇaṃ ca mṛtsalilaprakṣālanena kuryāt | evaṃ ca ‘tad adbhiḥ pūrvam’ ity atra yad uktaṃ cūrṇādinā annalepāpakarṣaṇaṃ tenāśakyam idaṃ draṣṭavyam |
athavā —abhyavahārasaṃyogasyāpi ācamanārtha evopanyāsaḥ | ‘suptvā bhuktvā’ ity anenaiva siddham anūdyate kramārtham | katham? – tadgrahaṇenānnalepāpakarṣaṇam uktaṃ tadapakarṣaṇaṃ pūrvaṃ kṛtvā tata ācāmed ity evamartham, anyathā saṃdehaḥ syād iti | tathā ca āpastambaḥ: “mūtraṃ kṛtvā pūrīṣaṃ vā mūtrapurīṣalepān annalepān ucchiṣṭalepān retasaś ca ye lepās tān prakṣālya pādau cācamya prayato bhavati” [ĀpDh 1.15.23] iti | mūtrapurīṣau prasiddhau | srehū retaḥ | mūtrādīnām ācamanārtham upanyāsaḥ, lepagandhāpakarṣasya ‘lepagandhāpakarṣaṇe śaucam’ ity anenaiva siddhatvāt || 1.50 ||
cakāraḥ pūrvoktasamuccaye | sneho retaḥ | mūtrapurīṣasnehānāṃ visraṃsanaṃ nirasanam | abhyavahāram avyavahāryadravyaṃ tena saṃyogaḥ | eṣu nimitteṣu pūrvavan mṛdā cādbhiḥ śaucam iti
|| 1.47 ||
yatra cāmnāyo vidadhyāt || 1.51 || — 1.48 ||
tatra tad eva bhavatīti śeṣaḥ | āmnāyo vedaḥ, sa yatra yajñaviṣaye sphyādīnāṃ prakṣālanamātrād eva śuddhiṃ vidadhyāt tatra tad eva, na dārusāmānyena takṣaṇādi | atulyabalatvād evedaṃ siddham iti cet, śrutyuktasya laukike ananupraveśanārtha upanyāsaḥ, yathā darvyā yāgaviṣaye śrutyā prakṣālanena śuddhir uktā, tasyā laukike vyāpṛtāyā api darvīsāmānyena śrutyuktā mā bhūt smṛtyuktām eva kuryād ity evamartham upanyāsaḥ |
atha vā — āmnāyaśabdena manur ucyate | sa yatra śaucaṃ vidadhyāt tatra tad eva, yathā “yatīnāṃ tu caturguṇam” [MDh 5.137] ityādi | taduktatvād evāsaṃdeha iti cet, ucyate — yasmin viṣaye na kiṃcid ucyate tatra smṛtyantaroktaṃ bhavati | yatra lepagandhāpakarṣaṇādi kiṃcid ucyate tatra smṛtyantareṇa vikalpaśaṅkā bhavatīti tannirāsārtham uktaṃ ‘yatra cāmnāyo vidadhyāt’ iti | evaṃ ca lepagandhāpakarṣaṇe kṛte ‘pi “dviguṇaṃ brahmacāriṇaḥ” [MDh 5.137] ityevamādy āśrayaṇīyam || 1.51 ||
yatra viṣaye yac chaucam āmnāyo vidadhyāt tatra tad eva bhavati | yathā camasānām ucchiṣṭaliptānāṃ mārjālīyādbhiḥ prakṣālanam iti || 1.48 ||
idānīṃ kṛtaśaucasya brahmacāriṇo ‘dhyayanārthaṃ gurūpasadanavidhim āha |
atha gurūpasadanavidhiḥ |
pāṇinā savyam upasaṃgṛhyānaṅguṣṭham adhīhi bho ity āmantrayeta gurum || 1.52 ||
pāṇinā savyam upasaṃgṛhyānaṅguṣṭham adhīhi bho ity āmantrayed guruṃ tatracakṣur-manāḥ prāṇopasparśaṇam darbhaiḥ || 1.49 ||
pāṇinā dakṣiṇena savyaṃ pāṇim anaṅguṣṭham aṅguṣṭhavarjitaṃ gṛhītvā adhīhi bho iti prārthayeta gurum adhyāpakam | pāṇigrahaṇaṃ pādanivṛttyartham | pāṇiprakaraṇāt savyam api pāṇim eveti gamyate | upasaṃgrahaṇaṃ aṅgulipradeśe iti gamyate, anaṅguṣṭham ity uktatvāt
|| 1.52 ||
pāṇinā svena dakṣiṇena | savyam iti viśeṣanād dakṣiṇeneti gamyate | guroḥ savyaṃ pādam anaṅguṣṭham aṅguṣṭhavarjaṃ gṛhītvā, adhīhi bho iti gurum āmantrayet | tatra gurau manaścakṣuṣī ca nidhāyāvahitaḥ syād iti | prāṇāḥ śīrṣaṇyānīndiryāṇi | teṣām ātmīyānām ācamanoktakrameṇa darbhair upasparśanaṃ kartavyaṃ māṇavakena || 1.49 ||

tatra cakṣurmanāḥ || 1.53 ||

prāṇopasparśanaṃ darbhaiḥ || 1.54 ||

prāṇāyāmās trayaḥ pañcadaśamātrāḥ || 1.55 || — || 1.50 ||
kāryā iti śeṣaḥ | prāṇāyāmaḥ prāṇanirodhaḥ, nirucchvāsenāvasthānaṃ, te trayaḥ kāryāḥ | pratyekaṃ pañcadaśamātrāḥ | mātrā nāma madhyamāṅguṣṭhaśabdena loke prasiddhā || 1.55 ||
kāryā iti śeṣaḥ | jānupārśvataḥ parimṛjya truṭim ekāṃ kuryāt saikā mātrā | tāḥ pañcadaśa pūryante yāvatā kālena tāvantaṃ kālaṃ prāṇavāyuṃ dhārayet sa ekaḥ prāṇāyāmaḥ | te trayaḥ kāryāḥ | manuḥ |

oṃkāro ‘nyatrāpi || 1.62 || — || 1.58 ||
anuvacanānantaram oṃkāraś codāhartavyaḥ anyatrāpi | atra kramaḥ | prātar utthāya pādopasaṃgrahaṇam, tadāhūya upaviśet darbheṣu, tataḥ prāṇopasparśanaṃ darbhaiḥ, tataḥ prāṇāyāmān kṛtvā mahāvyāhṛtīḥ pañca uktvā, añjalinā ‘adhīhi bhoḥ’ iti gurum āmantrya, oṃkārapūrvikā vyāhṛtīḥ sāvitrīṃ coktvā praṇavapūrvam adhīyīta | prathamādhyayana evāyaṃ vidhiḥ | kutaḥ? sāvitrī cānuvacanaṃ tato ‘dhīyīteti vaktavye ādito brahmaṇa ādāna ityārambhāt | pratidivasam oṃkāram evoktvā | kutaḥ? oṃkāro ‘nyatrety uktatvāt | anyatra prathamādhyayanād anyatra | apiśabdaḥ prathamādhyayane ‘pi praṇavapraveśanārthaḥ | kecid anyatrāpīty anenāṅgādau praṇavam icchanti || 1.62 ||
sāvitryanuvacanād anyatrāpy oṃkāro vaktavyaḥ | pratyaham adhyayanakāla ity arthaḥ || 1.58 ||
antarāgamane punar upasadanam || 1.63 || — || 1.59 ||
yady adhīyatām antarā madhye kaścid gacchet tataḥ punar api pādopasaṃgrahaṇādipraṇavoccāraṇānto yo vidhis taṃ kuryāt tasya prāyaścittārtham | aprakaraṇe ‘pi prāyaścittam ānantaryeṇānuṣṭhānārtham | mānuṣāṇāṃ gamana etat, itareṣām ūrdhvam abhidhānāt | atrānadhyāyaśravaṇābhāvāt prāyaścattānantaram adhīyīta | prathamādhyayanāpekṣayā punargrahaṇam, anyatropasadanābhāvāt | ato ‘nyatra prāyaścittārtham evopasadanaṃ kartavyaṃ tadanantaram adhyayanaṃ kartavyam | tathāha āpastambaḥ: “adhīhi bho ity uktvādhīyītādhyāpayed vā” [ĀpDh 1.10.15–16] iti || 1.63 ||
guroḥ śiṣyasya ca madhye gamanam antarāgamanam | yasya kasyāpy antarāgamane punar upasadanaṃ kartavyam | “pāṇinā savyam” [GDh 1.49] ityādy “oṃkāro ‘nyatrāpi” [GDh 1.58] ity antam upasadanam || 1.59 ||

śvanakulasarpamaṇḍūkamārjārāṇāṃ tryaham upavāso vipravāsaś ca || 1.64 || — || 1.60 ||
ete prasiddhāḥ | śvādīnāṃ pratyekam antarāgamane tryahaṃ bhaktatyāgaḥ prāyaścittaṃ, vipravāso guruṇā virahitasyāvasthānam ekāham | evaṃ ca guruviprayogopadeśād eva ekāham anadhyāyasiddhiḥ | kuta ekāhopalabdhir iti cet, visamāsāt | anyathā tryaham upavāsavipravāsau cety avakṣyat | ata ekāham evānadhyāyaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
paśumaṇḍūkamārjāraśvasarpanakuleṣu ca |
antarā gamane vidyād anadhyāyam aharniśam || iti | [MDh 4.126] | evaṃ cātrāpi śvagrahaṇaṃ catuṣpadāṃ paśūnām upalakṣaṇam | catuṣpādgrahaṇena gṛhyamāṇatvāt mārjārasya punar upanyāsaḥ prāyaścittagauravārthaḥ | yathaha uśanā: “mārjārāntarāgamane ghṛtaṃ prāśya tryaham upavaset” iti | upasadanānukarṣaṇārthaś cakāraḥ | ato ‘dhyayanakāle tat kṛtvādhīyīta | kecid upavāsam adhyayanatyāgam āhuḥ | tad yuktam ayuktaṃ veti vicāryam || 1.64 ||
śvādīnām antarāgamane tryaham upavāso vipravāsaś ca kartavyaḥ | vipravāsa ācāryakulād anyatra vāsaḥ | manus tu |
paśumaṇḍūkamārjāraśvasarpanakuleṣu ca |
antarāgamane vidyād anadhyāyam aharniśam || iti | [MDh 4.126] | tad dhāraṇādhyayanaviṣayam | gautamīyaṃ tu grahaṇādhyayanaviṣayam || 1.60 ||
kiṃ ca |
prāṇāyāmā ghṛtaprāśanaṃ cetareṣām || 1.65 || — || 1.61 ||
itareṣāṃ kākādīnāṃ prāṇāyāmās trayaḥ, bahuvacana-śravaṇāt | ghṛtaprāśanaṃ kāyāplavanamātram | upasadanānukarṣaṇārthaḥ cakāraḥ | tatas trīn prāṇāyāmān dhārayitvā ghṛtaṃ prāśyopasadanaṃ ca kṛtvādhīyīta, anadhyāyāśravaṇāt || 1.65 ||
itareṣāṃ śvādivyatiriktānāṃ paśvādīnām antarāgamane prāṇāyāmās trayaḥ kāryā ghṛtaprāśanaṃ ca kāryam | etat sarvaṃ śiṣyasya prāyaścittaṃ na guroḥ, | ubhayor ity apare || 1.61 ||
śmaśānābhyadhyayane caivam || 1.66 || — || 1.62 ||
āpastambena “sarvataḥ śamyāprāsān nādhīyīta” [ĀpDh 1.9.6] ity uktam | tasmāt tatsamīpe ‘dhyayanaṃ śmaśānādhyayanam | abhyupasargo buddhipūrvasūcanārthaḥ | tato ‘buddhipūrve anyat prāyaścittaṃ laghutaraṃ draṣṭavyam | tathā cāha uśanā: “anabhisaṃdhyā śmaśānābhyadhyayane ‘dhīhi bho ity uktvādhīyīta” iti | cakārād anantaroktasya prāṇāyāmādeḥ sarvasya prāptau evaṃśabdena anantaroktasyopasadanasyaiva prāptiḥ kriyate | tarhy evaṃśabda eva vaktavya iti cet, na, ubhayasakāśa evāyam artho labhyate | kathaṃ? evaṃśabde caśabde vā vaktavye ubhayapāṭhasya prayojanāntaraṃ kalpayituṃ nyāyyaṃ, na hy ekasyaiveti | ata upasadanam eva kartavyam | tathā ca smṛtyantaraṃ: “śmaśānasamīpe ‘dhītya prāṇān dhārayet prāṇopasparśanam abhivādanaṃ sāvitryanuvācanam” ityādi | dviruktir adhyāyaparisamāptyarthā | uktam idaṃ sarvaṃ prāyaścittaṃ śiṣyasyaiva, tadarthatvād adhyanaprabhṛteḥ | kiṃ ca sarvatra sopasadanam eva prāyaścittaṃ, upasadanaṃ tv abhivādanādi, tad abhivādanam ācāryasya na saṃbhavatīti || 1.66 ||
abhir uparibhāve śmaśānasyopary adhyayane caivaṃ prāyaścittam | prāṇāyāmā ghṛtaprāśanaṃ ceti | dviruktir adhyāpanaparisamāptyarthā || 1.62 ||
atha dvitīyo ‘dhyāyaḥ
dvijānām uktam upanayanam | teṣām upanayanāt prāg api kaiścid varṇadharmair adhikāra iṣyata iti tadartham āha |
upanītaprasaṅgenānupanītadharmā ucyante |
prāg upanayanāt kāmacāravādabhakṣaḥ
|| 2.1 ||
prāg upanayanāt kāmacāraḥ kāmavādaḥ kāmabhakṣaḥ || 2.1 ||
nanu ca prāg evopanayanād etad vaktavyaṃ, tatra prāg upanayanād ity etan na vaktavyaṃ bhavati |
ucyate | satyam etat | kiṃ tu upanayanasyādāv abhidhāne prayojanam uktam | nānena vidhīyate, arthaprāptatvāt | na ca niyamyate, avaśyakartavyābhāvāt, “upanayanādir niyamaḥ” [GDh 2.10] iti vakṣyamāṇatvāt pākṣikatvābhāvāc ca | nāpi parisaṃkhyā, nivartyābhāvāt | na hy asyākāmacāritvādikaṃ prāptam | ataḥ idaṃ sūtram anuvādakaṃ prāyaścittarājadaṇḍābhāvajñāpanārthaṃ, tayoḥ sāmānyenopadiśṭatvāt |
nanu ca rājadaṇḍaprāyaścittāni ca vihitākaraṇe niṣiddhasevane vā vidhīyante | na cāsya vidhipratiṣedhābhyām adhikāraḥ | na cāsya vidhiḥ pratiṣedho vā śrūyata iti | na tv asyāhutāttvādi [GDh 2.2] vidhāyiṣyate pratiṣidhyate vā, yad vyatikrame ubhayaṃ bhaviṣyatīti, pitrādīnām upadeśāt | ata idaṃ sūtram anarthakam iti |
atrocyate | yad vihitākaraṇe pratiṣiddhasevane vā prāyaścittam uktaṃ rājadaṇḍo vā tad ubhayam asyāpi prāpnoty eva | yady apy asyaivoddeśena vidhiḥ pratiṣedho vā na śrūyate, tathāpi yat tu sāmānyenoktaṃ “ahiṃsā satyam asteyam” [MDh 10.63] ityādi, tad varṇadharmatvād asyāpi prāpnoti | tadvyatikrame rājadaṇḍaprāyaścitte api bhavataḥ | ataḥ tannivṛttyartham anuvādasvarūpaṃ sūtram ārabdham |
“upanayanādir niyamaḥ” [GDh 2.10] ity anena sūtreṇa upanayanād ārabhya yo vidhir ucyate tadvyatikrame eva prāyaścittaṃ nānyavyatikrame iti vakṣyamāṇatvāt | tenaivāsyāpi varṇadharmātikrame prāyaścittābhāvo jñātuṃ śakyate, rājadaṇḍābhāvo ‘pi | tathāpi nityopanayanakālātītasya bhinnabuddheḥ śaktasya ekādaśavarṣād ūrdhvaṃ ṣoḍaśavarṣāt prāk prāyaścittārdhapraveśanārthaṃ, tato ‘rvāk pañcavarṣād ūrdhvaṃ pitrādīnāṃ prāyaścittapraveśārthaṃ, tato ‘rvāg aparādhābhāvajñāpanārthaṃ ca sūtram ārabdham | tathā ca bhārgavīyam |
aśītir yasya varṣāṇi bālo vāpy ūnaṣoḍaśaḥ |
prāyaścittārdham arhanti striyo vyādhita eva ca ||
ūnaikādaśavarṣasya pañcavarṣāt parasya ca |
caret suhṛd guraś caiva prāyaścittaṃ viśuddhaye ||
ato bālatarasyātha nāparādho ‘sti kutracit |
rājadaṇḍaś ca tasyātaḥ prāyaścittaṃ ca neṣyate || iti |
evaṃ copanayanāt prāg yady api sāmānyena kāmacāritvādy uktaṃ, tathāpi pañcavarṣāt prāg evātyantakāmacāritā, tata ūrdhvaṃ varṇadharmeṣu niyoktavyaḥ pitrādibhiḥ, tadvyatikrame bālasya sataḥ pitrādīnāṃ prāyaścittasya śravaṇāt | alam atiprasaṅgena |
idānīṃ sūtraṃ vivriyate — upanayanāt prāk kāmacāra icchāgatiḥ | tasmāc caṇḍālasparśanādau prokṣaṇamātraṃ vakṣyati “avokṣaṇebhyaḥ” [GDh 2.6] iti | tad api pañcavarṣāt prāg evety uktam | ata ūrdhvaṃ caṇḍālādisparśane snāpayitavyaḥ | ekādaśavarṣād ūrdhvaṃ svayam eva snāyāt | evaṃ sarvatra | kāmavādaḥ aślīlādy api vadati | kāmabhakṣaḥ paryuṣitādy api bhakṣayati | pañcavarṣād ūrdhvaṃ varṇadharmāvirodhīni kārayitavyāni | upanayanāt prāg ity āpatkalpopanayanasyāpi grahaṇam | kutaḥ? brahmacārīti liṅgāt | nityakālāt prāk brahmacaryasya skhalanāsambhavād eva siddhatvāt, brahmacaryarakṣaṇopadeśo ‘narthakaḥ syād iti | kāmacārasyodāharaṇaṃ svayam eva vakṣyati “yathopapādamūtrapurīṣo bhavati” [GDh 2.4] iti | ato ‘tyantabālāvasthāyām api brahmahatyāder yatnato nivārayitavyaḥ | kāmavāde abhiśaṃsādau kāmabhakṣaṇe surāpānādau cātyantabālyāvasthāyām api yatnato nivārayitavyaḥ, kāmacāra ity anenaiva siddhe vādabhakṣaṇayoḥ punar upanyāsāt | upanayanād iti vaktavye prāggrahaṇaṃ niyamārthaṃ, upanayanāt prāg eva kāmacāritvād iti | asati niyame “uttareṣāṃ caitad avirodhi” [GDh 3.10] ity anena hutaśiṣṭa-bhakṣaṇavarjanādi yatīnām api prāpnotīti || 2.1 ||
ā ṣoḍaśād brāhmaṇasyety āpatkalpopanayanaviṣayam | kāmacāra icchācaraṇam | apaṇyāny api vikrīṇīyāc chvavṛttyāpi jīved iti | kāmavādo ‘ślīlānṛtādivacanam | kāmabhakṣo laśunaparyuṣitānnādibhakṣaṇaṃ catuḥ pañcakṛtvo vā bhojanam ity etāvad yasya sa tathoktaḥ | na tu brahmahatyāsurāpānādyatiprasaṅgaḥ || 2.1 ||
idānīṃ kāmavādabhakṣaṇasyāpavādam āha |
ahutāt || 2.2 ||
hutaṃ hutaśiṣṭaṃ carupuroḍāśādi, na vaiśvadevaśiṣṭaṃ, tasya sarvārthatvāt | tathāha āpastambaḥ: “sarvān vaiśvadeve bhāginaḥ kurvīta” [ĀpDh 2.9.5] iti | ahutāt kārya iti śeṣaḥ | arthaprāptasya pratiṣedho ‘yaṃ pitrādīnām upadeśaḥ, bālasya vijñānābhāvāt | ekādaśavarṣād ūrdhvaṃ tasyaivety uktam || 2.2 ||
hutaśeṣaṃ puroḍāśādi | tad attīti hutāt | tadviparīto ‘hutāt | anupanīto hutaṃ nādyād iti || 2.2 ||
brahmacārī || 2.3 ||
brahmacaryarakṣaṇaṃ kartavyam || 2.3 ||
kāmacārāder ayam apavādaḥ | ā ṣoḍaśād ity uktatvāt strīṣu prasaṅgayogyatāsti | ato brahmacārī jitendriyaḥ syād iti | tathā ca smṛtyantare: “prāyaścittaṃ viluptam avakīrṇivratena śuddham upanayen na ūrdhvaṃ vrātyāvakīrṇivratābhyām” iti || 2.3 ||
idānīṃ kāmacārasyodāharaṇam āha |
yathopapādamūtrapurīṣo bhavati || 2.4 ||
yathāpariveṣṭitaśirasaḥ kṛṣṭādāv api mūtrapurīṣāv utpadyete tiṣṭhato vā tathā tatraiva kuryād ity arthaḥ
|| 2.4 ||
mūtrapurīṣe yathopapadyete yasya sa tathoktaḥ prāṅmukhādir api kuryāt | “na bhūmāv anantardhāya” (GDh 9.38) ityādisthānaniyamo ‘pi nāsti || 2.4 ||
kiṃ ca |
nāsyācamanakalpo vidyate || 2.5 ||
pañcavarṣād ūrdhvam asyaikādaśavarṣāt prāk ācamanakalpaḥ ācamanavidhir na vidyate | kalpaprati-ṣedhāt ācamanamātram astīty avagamyate | tatra “śūdreṇa hi samas tāvad yāvad vedena jāyate” [MDh 2.172cd] iti smṛtyantare śūdreṇa tulyadharmaśravaṇāt, ācamanaṃ śūdravad draṣṭavyam | ūnaikādaśavarṣasyeti kutaḥ? asyety ārambhāt, pañcavarṣat prāg doṣābhāvaśravaṇāt ekādaśavarṣād ūrdhvaṃ varṇadharme ‘dhikṛtatvāc ca | ekādaśavarṣād ūrdhvam ācamanakalpo ‘pi tasya vidyata eveti gamyate || 2.5 ||
kalpaniṣedhād ācamanam anujñātaṃ strīśūdravat
|| 2.5 ||
idānīm atibālasyāha |
anyatrāpamārjanapradhāvanāvokṣaṇebhyaḥ || 2.6 ||
asya na vidyata iti vartate | asya ūnapañcavarṣasya apamārjanādibhyaḥ anyatra kiṃcid api śaucaprakāraḥ na vidyata ity arthaḥ | apamārjanaṃ bhuktocchiṣṭasya sodakena pāṇinā taducchiṣṭāpanayanam | pradhāvanaṃ prakṣālanaṃ mūtroccārādau | avokṣaṇaṃ caṇḍālādisparśane prokṣaṇam | prāptyabhāvāt “nāsyācamanakalpo vidyate anyatrāpamārjanapradhāvanāvokṣaṇebhyaḥ” iti pratiṣedhadvayasyānārambhaḥ prāpnotīti cet, naiṣa doṣaḥ, “nāsyācamanakalpo vidyatate” ityanenonaṣoḍaśavarṣasyācamanakalpo vidhīyate ūnaikādaśavarṣasya pratiṣedhadvāreṇa | “anyatrāpamārjanapradhāvanāvokṣaṇebhyaḥ” ity anena punaḥ ūnapañcavarṣasyāpamārjanādi vidhīyate anyaśaucapratiṣedhadvāreṇeti || 2.6 ||
apamārjanādīni varjayitvā, ācamanakalpo nāsti | apamārjanādikam astīti yāvat | yady apy apamārjanādīny ācamanakalpe nāntarbhavanti tathāpi paryudāsamukhena tāni vidhīyante | atramārjanaṃ (atrāpamārjanaṃ) sodakena pāṇinā parimārjanam ucchiṣṭādiliptasya | pradhāvanam amedhyādiliptasyādbhir mṛdā ca kṣālanam | avokṣaṇaṃ rajasvalādispṛṣṭasya | idam atyantabālaviṣayam | ṣaḍvarṣād ūrdhvaṃ snānam icchanti | asyānupanītasyaitāvad uttamātra (?) kāmacārādivyatikrame prāyaścittam asti | tatra smṛtyantare |
aśītir yasya varṣāṇi bālo vāpy ūnaṣoḍaśaḥ |
prāyaścittārdham arhanti striyo vyādhita eva ca ||
ūnaikādaśavarṣasya pañcavarṣāt parasya ca |
cared guruḥ suhṛc caiva prāyaścittaṃ viśuddhaye ||
ato bālatarasyāsya nāparādho na pātakam |
rājadaṇḍaś ca tasyātaḥ prāyaścittaṃ ca neṣyate ||
iti || 2.6 ||
kiṃ ca |
na tadupasparśanād aśaucam || 2.7 ||
tadgrahaṇenākṛtaśauco gṛhyate | akṛtaśaucasparśanāt tena vā spṛṣṭasyāśucitvaṃ na bhavati | atibālaviṣayam evaitat |
tarhy asya śaucavidhānam anarthakam | kutaḥ? dvayam eva prayojanaṃ śaucavidhānasya dṛśyate itarasparśayogyārtham anuṣṭhānārthaṃ ca | asya tu itarasparśayogyārthaṃ na bhavati, akṛtaśaucasyāpi sparśayogyatvāt, “na tadupasparśanād aśaucam” ity anena | anuṣṭhānārthaṃ ca na bhavati, asyānuṣṭhānābhāvād iti |
nānarthakam, rakṣārthatvāc chaucavidhānasyeti | tathā ca smṛtyantaram: “bālasyāpañcamād varṣād rakṣārthaṃ śaucaṃ kuryāt” iti |
akṛtaśaucasyāsyaiva sparśane doṣābhāvajñāpanārthaḥ upaśabdaprayogaḥ | evaṃ cānyeṣām ucchiṣṭakṛtamūtrapurīṣāṇāṃ sparśane ācamanaṃ kartavyam | tatsparśane na doṣaḥ śavaspṛṣṭisūtre parigaṇitatvāt | tathā smṛtyantaram |
aśuciṃ spṛśate yas tu eka eva hi duṣyate |
taṃ spṛṣṭvānyo na duṣyeta sarvadravyeṣv ayaṃ vidhiḥ || iti |
caṇḍālasparśane tu śavaspṛṣṭisūtre vakṣyati [Gsh 14.22] | “tam upaspṛśet” iti vaktavye “na tadupasparśanād aśaucam” iti gurusūtrakaraṇaṃ “prāg upanayanād” [GDh 2.1] ity atra niyamasyāpi bādhanārtham | tataś copanayanād ūrdhvam apy ucchiṣṭakṛtamūtra-purīṣāṇāṃ sparśane ācamanaṃ kartavyam || 2.7 ||
tadupasparśanāt tasyākṛtopanayanasyodakyādispṛṣṭasyāpy upasparśanād aśaucaṃ na syāt | spṛṣṭāspṛṣṭir upasparśanam | tena snānaṃ na kartavyam | bhuktocchiṣṭasya kṛtamūtrapurīṣa(sya) sparśanād api nācamanam | idam api ṣaḍvarṣāt prāg eva | kimarthaṃ tarhi tasya śaucaṃ vihitam | na tāvad anuṣṭhānārthaṃ nāpi sparśayogyatārtham, akṛtaśaucasyāpi sparśayogyatvāt | rakṣaṇārtham iti brūmaḥ | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
bālasya pañcamād varṣād rakṣārthaṃ śaucam ācaret | iti || 2.7 ||
kiṃ ca |
na tv evainam agnihavanabaligrahaṇayor niyuñjyāt || 2.8 ||
H reads “-haraṇayor” for “-grahaṇaayor”
enam iti sarvasyānupanītasyopasaṃgrahārtham | agnau yat kriyate agnihotrahomādi tat agnihavanam | vaiśvadevottarakālaṃ digdevatābhyo yo balir dīyate tad baliharaṇam | tuśabdo viśeṣavācī, aṣṭakādau brāhmaṇapādaprakṣālanādāv api na niyuñjyāt, viśeṣato ‘gnihavanabaliharaṇayor iti | kāmacāritvādy atra kutracin mantram adhītyāgnihotrādau yadi svayam eva pravartate tadā taṃ dṛṣṭvānumantāpi na syād ity evamartha evaśabdaḥ | upanītasya niyogārtham idaṃ vacanaṃ, anyatra prāptyabhāvāt pratiniṣedhasya | evaṃ ca “vidvān āgnihotraṃ juhuyāt” iti yady api śrutyā bahuśrutasyaivādhikāraḥ, tathāpi tadasambhave alpavidyam apy upanītaṃ niyuñjyāt | tathā ca manuḥ |
naiva kanyā na yuvatir nālpavidyo na bāliśaḥ |
hotā syād agnihotrasya nārto nāsaṃskṛtas tathā || iti | [MDh 11.36] | tatrālpavidyapratiṣedhād eva pratiṣedhe siddhe nāsaṃskṛta iti pratiṣedho ‘nyasya viduṣo ‘bhāve ‘lpavidyasyāpi saṃskṛtasyābhyanujñānārtham iti pratipāditam | “prāg upanayanād” [GDh 2.1] ityādi yad uktaṃ tat strīṇām api ā vivāhād draṣṭavyam | kutaḥ?
vaivāhiko vidhiḥ strīṇām aupanāyanikaḥ smṛtaḥ | [MDh 2.67]  iti smṛtyantaradarśanāt | evaṃ cāgnihavanabaliharaṇayoḥ kanyāyā api niyogapratiṣedhaḥ siddhaḥ | ato vivāhottarakālaṃ niyogaḥ kartavyaḥ | tatra “na strī juhuyāt” iti śrutyāgnihotre pratiṣiddhatvāt aupāsanādau draṣṭavyam | tathā ca smṛtyantaram: “patny aupāsanaṃ juhuyād vaiśvadevaṃ ca” ityādi | na yuvatir iti pratiṣedhaś ca paradāraviṣayatvād adoṣaḥ || 2.8 ||
enam anupanītam agnihavana aupāsanahomādau baliharaṇe vaiśvadevādau na niyuñjyān na niyuñjīteti yāvat | tuśabdād uktād anyatrāpi samantrake karmaṇi na niyuñjīteti | evakāro ‘vadhāraṇe | atha āśvalāyanaḥ: “pāṇigrahaṇādi gṛhyaṃ paricaret svayaṃ patny api vā putraḥ kumāry antevāsī vā” iti | chandogāś ca patnī juhuyād iti patnīkumāryādyanujñāte ‘smin pakṣe na tv evainam ity arthaḥ || 2.8 ||
kiṃ ca |
na brahmābhivyāhārayed anyatra svadhāninayanāt || 2.9 ||
evam ity anuvartate | brahmaśabdena vedā ucyante | tasmād enaṃ nādhyāpayet | upanītasyopākaraṇād ūrdhvaṃ “adhīyīta chandāṃsi” ity anena vedādhyayanavidhānād evānupanītasya pratiṣedhe siddhe, vedasya punaḥ pratiṣedhaḥ veda eva nādhyāpayitavyaḥ iti niyamārthaḥ | tataś cāṅgādhyāpane na doṣaḥ | kāmacāritvād yena kenacit prakāreṇa gṛhītaṃ mantram api noccārayet | kutaḥ? adhyāpayayed ity anārabhyābhivyāhārayed ity ārambhāt | ‘anyatra svadhāninayanāt,’ svadhāninayanaṃ varjayitvā | amuṣmai svadhety uktvā yat piṇḍodakādi pretebhyo dīyate tat svadhāninayanam | tad api pretakarmaṇaḥ samastasyāpy upalakṣaṇam | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “anyatrodakakarmasvadhā-pitṛsaṃyuktebhyaḥ” [VaDh 2.7] iti | evaṃ ca putre ‘nupanīte ‘pi sati sapiṇḍādibhiḥ pretakriyā na kartavyā, śāstracodanānarthakyaprasaṅgo mā bhūd iti || 2.9 ||
brahma vedaḥ | enam anupanītaṃ brahma nābhivyāhārayen noccārayet | kim aviśeṣeṇeti | neti āha | anyatra svadhāninayanāt | pitryasya sarvasya karmaṇa upalakṣaṇam | “anyatrodakakarmasvadhāpitṛ-saṃyuktebhyaḥ” (VaDh 2.7) iti vāsiṣṭhe darśanāt | agṛhītākṣaraḥ putraḥ pitroḥ saṃskāram arhatītyādi ca | anyasyāsaṃbhave sarvaṃ pitryaṃ karma tadānīṃ mantrān grāhayitvāsau kārayitavyaḥ || 2.9 ||
anupanītasyoktvedānīm upanītasyāha |
upanayanādir niyamaḥ || 2.10 ||
upanayanād ārabhya yo vidhir uktaḥ sa niyamasaṃjño bhavati | evaṃ cāsyākaraṇe prāyaścittaṃ na pūrvasya | ato vā ārabhya niyamaḥ vratadhāraṇaṃ yo vakṣyate ‘gnīndhanādiḥ sa niyamaḥ upanayanādiḥ kartavyaḥ
|| 2.10 ||
agnīndhanādir yo niyamo vakṣyate sa upanayanādir eva | anupanītādhikāreṇa vicchinnatvād upanītādhikārārtham idam || 2.10 ||
kiṃ ca |
uktaṃ brahmacaryam || 2.11 ||
uktagrahaṇaṃ prāg upanayanān niyama evaitad iti | tataś ca tatskhalane ‘nupanītasyāpi avakīrṇiprāyaścittenaivādhikāro draṣṭavyaḥ | tathā ca smṛtyantaraṃ: “viplutam avakīrṇivratena śuddham upanayed ūnasaptadaśavarṣaṃ, ata ūrdhvaṃ vrātyāvakīrṇivratābhyām” iti | brahmacāriṇas tu strīprekṣaṇādipratiṣedhād arthasiddham iti cet, na, paśvādaṣv api sambhavāt || 2.11 ||
anupanītasya yad uktaṃ brahmacaryaṃ tad upanītasyāpi samānam | nanu ca “strīprekṣaṇālambhane” [GDh 2.16] iti neṣedho vakṣyate | tathāpi smaraṇakīrtanādiniṣedhārtham idam || 2.11 ||
kiṃ ca |
agnīndhanabhaikṣacaraṇe || 2.12 ||
agnīndhanaṃ samiddhomaḥ | itarat prasiddham | te pratidinaṃ kartavye | sahanirdeśo ‘nantarānuṣṭhā-nārthaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
pratigṛhyepsitaṃ daṇḍam upasthāya ca bhāskaram |
pradakṣiṇaṃ parītyāgniṃ cared bhaikṣaṃ yathāvidhi || iti | [MDh 2.48] | tatrāpi ‘pradakṣiṇaṃ parītyāgnim’ ity anena samiddhoma ukta ity uktam | “sāyaṃ prātas tv annam abhipūjitam anindan bhuñjīta” [GDh 9.59] iti dvirbhojanaśravaṇāt sāyaṃ prātaḥ dvir agnīndhanam api draṣṭavyam | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
dūrād āhṛtya samidhaḥ saṃnidadhyād vihāyasi |
sāyaṃ prātaś ca juhuyāt tābhir agnim atandritaḥ || iti | [MDh 2.186] | kecit samiddhomaṃ sāyam evecchanti
|| 2.12 ||
agnīndhanaṃ samiddhomaḥ | bhikṣāṇāṃ samūho bhaikṣam | tadarthaṃ pratigṛhaṃ caraṇaṃ bhaikṣacaraṇam | te pratyahaṃ kartavye | tatra manuḥ |
dūrād āhṛtya samidhaḥ saṃnidadhyād vihāyasi |
sāyaṃ prātaś ca juhuyāt tābhir agnim atandritaḥ ||
akṛtvā bhaikṣacaraṇam asamidhya ca pāvakam |
anāturaḥ saptarātram avakīrṇivrataṃ caret || iti | [MDh 2.186–87]
āpastambas tu: “sāyam evāgnipūjety eke” iti [ĀpDh 1.4.17] || 2.12 ||
kiṃ ca |
satyavacanam || 2.13 ||
sarvatra kartavyakriyādhyāhartavyā | vacanagrahaṇam asaṃdehārtham, satyam apām upasparśanam ity evaṃ saṃdehaḥ syād iti | pṛthaggrahaṇaṃ āturasyāpy anuṣṭhānārtham | tataś ca pūrvayos tan na | tathā ca manuḥ |
akṛtvā bhaikṣacaraṇam asamidhya ca pāvakam |
anāturaḥ saptarātram avakīrṇivrataṃ caret || [MDh 2.187] iti || 2.13 ||
upanītena satyam eva vaktavyam || 2.13 ||
kiṃ ca |
apām upasparśanam || 2.14 ||
abgrahaṇaṃ bhasmādīnām upalakṣaṇam | tathā ca bhṛguḥ |
snānāni pañca puṇyāni kīrtitāni maharṣibhiḥ |
āgneyaṃ vāruṇaṃ brāhmaṃ vāyavyaṃ divyam eva ca ||
āgneyaṃ bhasmanā snānam avagāhaṃ tu vāruṇam |
āpo hiṣṭheti ca brāhmaṃ vāyavyaṃ gorajaḥ smṛtam ||
yat tu syāt sātapaṃ varṣaṃ divyaṃ snānaṃ tad ucyate |
aśakto vāruṇe snāne āgneyādi samācaret || iti |
snānam iti vaktavye evam abhidhānaṃ smṛtyantaroktasnānavidher upasaṃgrahaṇārtham | evaṃ ca smṛtyantaroktaṃ draṣṭavyam | upaśabdo ‘vijñātajalavarjanārthaḥ | agnīndhanabhaikṣacaraṇayoḥ pṛthaggrahaṇaṃ bhuktasya nityasnānapratiṣedhārthaṃ, na naimittikasya | ato naimittikaṃ kartavyam | satyavacanasya pṛthaggrahaṇam āturasya naimittikasyāpi pratiṣedhārtham | upaśabdo ‘rdharātrasnānapratiṣedhārthaṃ iti kecid vadanti | yad uktam atra tat sarvaṃ manunāpy uktaṃ |
na snānam ācared bhuktvā nāturo na mahāniśi |
na vāsobhiḥ sahājasraṃ nāvijñāte jalāśaye || [MDh 4.129] iti || 2.14 ||
upasparśanam snānam | tad apy ahar ahaḥ kartavyam || 2.14 ||
eke godānādi || 2.15 ||
godānaṃ nāma chandogānāṃ dvitīyaṃ vratam, anyeṣām api ṣoḍaśe varṣe dhārayitavyam | teṣām api ṣoḍaśe godānadhāraṇaṃ kecid icchacanti | tata ārabhyaike snānam icchanti | godānāt prāk nityasnānaṃ na kuryāt, naimittikam eva kartavyam | tata ūrdhvaṃ nityasnānam apīty eke ācāryā icchanti | eka iti vacanāt na tu gautamaḥ || 2.15 ||
godānaṃ nāma ṣoḍaśe varṣe kartavyaṃ vratam | tad vrateṣu dvitīyam | chandogānām eke ācāryā godānādi snānam icchanti na tataḥ prāg dīkṣitavad asyāpi brahmacaryadīkṣāniyuktatvāt | nityasnānasyāyaṃ pratiṣedhaḥ | naimittikaṃ tu kartavyam, tatra daṇḍavad āplavanam “nāpsu ślāghamānaḥ snāyāt” ity āpastambasmaraṇāt [ĀpDh 1.2.30] || 2.15 ||
bahiḥsaṃdhyatvaṃ ca || 2.16 ||
eke grahaṇānuvartanārthaś cakāraḥ | bahirgrāmāt saṃdhyopāsanam eke icchanti | na tu gautamaḥ | etad uktaṃ bhavati | grāmād bahir eva saṃdhyopāsanaṃ kartavyam ity ekeṣāṃ mataṃ, bahir abhyantare vā yatra kutracic chuddhadeśe kartavyam iti gautamamatam || 2.16 ||
sāyaṃprātar dve saṃdhye yasya grāmād bahir bhavataḥ sa bahiḥsaṃdhyas tasya bhāvaḥ | grāmād bahir eva saṃdhyopāsanaṃ kartavyam iti || 2.16 ||
idānīṃ prasaṅgāt tat saṃdhyopāsanaṃ kadā kathaṃ ca kartavyam ity ata āha |
tat kadā kathām cety āha |
tiṣṭhet pūrvām āsītottarāṃ sajyotiṣyā jyotiṣo darśanād vāgyataḥ || 2.17 ||
vāgyata iti sthānāsanayoḥ pratyekaṃ saṃbadhyate | yasyām āditya udeti sā pūrvā saṃdhyā | pūrvasūtrakhaṇḍāt saṃdhyāśabdo ‘nuvartate | yasyām ādityo ‘stam eti sottarā saṃdhyā | sthānāsane atra guṇabhūte | upāsanam atra pradhānaṃ karma | ato yady api tiṣṭhed ity uktaṃ, tathāpi tiṣṭhann upāsīta, āsīna upāsīteti draṣṭavyam | tathā ca manuḥ |
pūrvāṃ saṃdhyāṃ japan tiṣṭhen naiśam eno vyapohati |
paścimāṃ tu samāsīno malaṃ hanti divā kṛtam || iti | [MDh 2.101] sajyotiḥ kālaḥ, pūrvasyāṃ nakṣatrajyotiḥ | yaḥ kālo nakṣatrajyotiṣā saha vartata sa sajyotiḥ | tasmin sajyotiṣi kāle ārambhaḥ kartavyaḥ | itarasyāṃ tathā ādityajyotiḥ | tata ā jyotiṣo darśanāt –pūrvasyām ādityadarśanāt uttarasyāṃ nakṣatradarśanāt | ā jyotiṣa iti vaktavye darśanagrahaṇaṃ samyagartham | tathā ca manuḥ |
pūrvāṃ saṃdhyāṃ japaṃs tiṣṭhet sāvitrīm ārkadarśanāt |
paścimāṃ tu samāsīta samyagṛkṣavibhāvanāt || iti | [MDh 2.102] tasminn uparamaḥ kartavya ity arthaḥ | atrāṅ abhividhyarthaḥ, dīrghasaṃdhyatvasyābhīṣṭatvāt | tathā ca manuḥ |
ṛṣayo dīrghasaṃdhyatvād dīrgham āyur avāpnuyuḥ |
prajñāṃ yaśaś ca kīrtiṃ ca brahmavarcasam eva ca || iti | [MDh 4.94]
nanu ca “uttareṣāṃ caitad avirodhi” [GDh 3.10] ity anena snātakasyāgnimata etan na prāpyate, tasyāgnihavanenāsya kālasyāvaruddhatvād iti |
ucyate – yas tāvat smārtaḥ sāyaṃ prātar homaḥ tatra kālabhedād avirodha eva | kathaṃ? sāyaṃśabdena rātrir ucyate, prātaḥśabdena ca divasaḥ, uditahomādivākyavad atra tathāvidhasyābhāvāt saṃdhyopāsanānantaram eva homasyānupraveśaḥ syād iti | athāpi keṣāṃcit saṃdhyākāla eva homa uktaḥ, tatrāpi dīrghasaṃdhyatvasyābhīṣṭatvād dhomānantaram api saṃdhyopāsanasyāvakāśo ‘stīti | evaṃ śraute uditahomādiviṣaye ‘py avirodha eveti |
“tiṣṭhan pūrvāṃ saṃdhyām āsīna uttarām upāsīta” iti vaktavye evamabhidhānaṃ smṛtyantaroktasyāpi vidher grahaṇārtham | tathāha uśanā:
sāyaṃ prātar udakaṃ gatvā pūto bhūtvā pavitreṇa mārjayitvā āpohiṣṭhīyābhis tisṛbhir abliṅgābhiḥ vāmadevyena ca, tataḥ śucau deśe darbheṣv āsīno darbhān dhārayamāṇaḥ pratyaṅmukho vāgyata ātmānaṃ dhyātvā tataḥ sarecakapūrakakumbhakādīn trīn prāṇāyāmān dhārayitvā ṛṣichandodevatādhyānapūrvakaṃ bhūrbhuvaḥsvarityādikāṃ sāvitrīṃ sahasrakṛtva āvartayec chatakṛtvo vā daśāvarām athottiṣṭhann agniyamabrahmabhyaḥ pratyagdakṣiṇodīcyāsu namaskṛtvā triḥ pradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā punar api pratīcīṃ diśaṃ namaskuryāt | evam eva prātaḥ prāṅmukhas tiṣṭhan mitrādibhyo namaskuryāt |
iti || 2.17 ||
prātaḥsaṃdhyāṃ tiṣṭhet sāyaṃsaṃdhyām āsīta | atyantasaṃyoge dvitīyā | sthānāsanayor uprakramopasaṃhārau kathayati – sajyotiṣi kāle samārabhya ā jyotirantaradarśanāt | prātar nakṣatrajyotir ārabhya ā sūryajyotirdarśanāt sāyam ādityajyotir ārabhya ā nakṣatradarśanād iti | tāvantaṃ kālaṃ vāgyataś ca syāt | tathā ca manuḥ |
pūrvāṃ saṃdhyāṃ japaṃs tiṣṭhet sāvitrīm ārkadarśanāt |
paścimāṃ tu samāsīta samyag ṛkṣavibhāvanāt || iti [MDh 2.101] || 2.17 ||
nādityam īkṣeta || 2.18 ||
yadi saṃdhyākālayor evāyaṃ pratiṣedhaḥ syāt tadānīm ekam eva sūtraṃ kriyeta “tiṣṭhet pūrvām āsītottarāṃ sajyotiṣyā jyotiṣo darśanād vāgyato nādityam īkṣeta” iti | yadi sarvadā syāt tadānīṃ “varjayen madhumāṃsa” [GDh 2.19] ityādāv ādityekṣaṇam api paṭhet | ubhayathākaraṇād anyābhiprāyeṇedam uktam iti jñātuṃ śakyate | abhiprāyaś ca smṛtyantarokto draṣṭavyaḥ | yathā cāha uśanā: “nekṣetādityaṃ saṃdhyāgataṃ madhyaṃdinagataṃ rāhugrastaṃ vāristham” iti || 2.18 ||
brahmacāriṇo ‘yaṃ sadādityadarśane pratiṣedhaḥ | snātakasya tu māna(vam) |
nekṣetodyantam ādityaṃ nāstaṃ yāntaṃ kadācana |
noparaktaṃ na vāristhaṃ na madhyaṃ nabhaso gatam || iti [MDh 4.37] || 2.18 ||
varjayen madhumāṃsagandhamālyadivā-svapnāñjanābhyañjanayānopānacchatrakāma-krodhalobhamohavādavādanasnānadanta-dhāvanaharṣanṛttagītaparivādabhayāni
|| 2.19 ||
varjayen madhumāṃsagandhamālyadivā-svāpābhyañjanayānopānacchatrakāmakrodha-lobhamohavādavādanasnānadantadhāvana-harṣanṛtyagītaparivādabhayāni || 2.19 ||
varjayed iti pratyekaṃ saṃbadhyate | madhu mākṣikam | tasyābuddhipūrvabhakṣaṇe na doṣaḥ, “akāmopanataṃ madhu vājasaneyake na duṣyati” [VaDh 23.13] iti smṛtyantaradarśanāt | māṃsaṃ gṛhasthasya apratiṣiddham api | tatrāpy āturasyauṣadhārthaṃ guror ucchiṣṭasya māṃsasya bhakṣaṇe na doṣaḥ, “sa ced vyādhīyīta kāmaṃ guror ucchiṣṭaṃ bhaiṣajyārthe sarvaṃ prāśnīyāt” [VaDh 23.9] iti smṛtyantaradarśanāt | evaṃ cānāturasya śrāddhe ‘pi varjanīyam | tathā ca manuḥ |
vratavad devadaivatye pritryai karmaṇy atharṣivat |
kāmam abhyarthito ‘śnīyād vratam asya na lupyate || [MDh 2.189] ityādi | gandhaṃ candanādi | tasyāpi sukhārthasya pratiṣedhaḥ, na devatāśiṣṭasya, “devatāśiṣṭam eva gṛhṇīyād brahmacārī” iti smṛtyantaradarśanāt | mālāyā api tathaiva | divāsvapno divā nidrāsevanaṃ | tad apy āturasya na doṣaḥ | añjanam akṣṇoḥ | atrāpy upabhogārthasya pratiṣedhaḥ, nauṣadhārthasya | abhyañjanaṃ tailena gātrābhyaṅgaḥ | kecid gandhatailasyāyaṃ pratiṣedha iti vadanti | tasmin pakṣe itarasya na doṣaḥ | gandhapratiśedhenaivāyaṃ pratiṣedhaḥ siddha iti cen, na, viśeṣata eva lokaprasiddher na gandhasāmānyasya grahaṇaṃ bhavatīti | yānaṃ śibikādi | tad apy anāturasyaiva | upānacchatre prasiddhe | mayūrapatrakṛtasyādoṣaḥ | kāmaḥ strīgato bhogābhilāṣaḥ | krodhaḥ parasyāniṣṭacintanam | lobhaḥ anyāyena paradravyādyapaharaṇam | moho ‘jñānam | vādo bahupralāpaḥ | vādanaṃ bherītāḍanādi | snānaṃ śirasnānādi yenopabhogaḥ, vihitatvād itarasya | dantadhāvanaṃ prasiddham | tāmbūlapatreṇāpi tan na kartavyaṃ “paṅkadanto bhaved vratī” iti smṛtyantaradarśanāt | harṣaḥ prītiḥ | evaṃ ca taddhetūnāṃ nāṭakādīnāṃ prekṣaṇasyāpi pratiṣedhaḥ siddhaḥ, taddarśane prītyutpatteḥ nivārāyitum aśakyatvāt | nṛttaṃ gātravikṣepaḥ | gītaṃ gāndharvasaṃbandhavākyasyābhidhānam | parivādaḥ paradoṣasaṃkīrtanam | bhayaṃ parasya bhīṣaṇaṃ, itarasya parihartum aśakyatvāt || 2.19 ||
madhvādīni varjayet | madhu mākṣikam | māṃsaṃ mṛgādeḥ | gandhaś candanādiḥ | mālyāni puṣpāṇi | divāsvapno divānidrā | añjanam akṣṇoḥ | abhyañjanaṃ tailābhyaṅgaḥ | yānaṃ śakaṭādi | upanacchatre prasiddhe | kāmaḥ strīsaṅgaḥ | krodhaḥ kopaḥ | lobho dravyābhilāṣaḥ | moho vivekaśūnyatā | vado bahujalpaḥ | vādanaṃ vīṇādīnām | snānaṃ sukhārtham uṣṇatoyādinā kaṇṭhād adhaḥ prakṣālanam | dantadhāvanaṃ dantamalāpakarṣaṇam | harṣo ‘bhimatalābhāc cittodrekaḥ | nṛtyagīte prasiddhe | privādaḥ paradoṣakathanam | bhayaṃ bhayahetuḥ kāntārapraveśādiḥ | idaṃ harṣe ‘pi draṣṭavyam || 2.19 ||
kiṃ ca |
gurudarśane kaṇṭhaprāvṛtāvasakthikāpāśrayaṇapādaprasāraṇāni || 2.20 ||
gurur ācāryādi | kutaḥ? yady ācāryasyaiva grahaṇaṃ tadānīm ācāryadarśana iti vaktavyam | gurugrahaṇaṃ sarveṣāṃ gurusthānīyānām apy upalakṣaṇam | tathā ca smṛtyantaram: “mānyānāṃ sarveṣāṃ sakāśe saṃkucitas tiṣṭhet” iti | kaṇṭhaprāvaraṇaṃ kaṇṭhe vastraprakṣepaḥ | avasakthikāṃ jānvor upari parikarabandhaḥ | śeṣaṃ prasiddham | paravaśād dhy etāni kriyanta iti pratiṣedhaḥ || 2.20 ||
guravaḥ pitrācāryādayaḥ | teṣāṃ darśanayogye deśe kaṇṭhaprāvṛttādīni varjayet | kaṇṭhaprāvṛtaṃ kaṇṭhaprāvaraṇaṃ vastrādinā | avasakthikā ūrau pādam āropyāvasthānam | apāśrayaṇaṃ kuḍyastambhādyāśrityāsanam | pādaprasāraṇaṃ prasiddham | gurujanasakāśe vinayasaṃkocena tiṣṭhed ity arthaḥ || 2.20 ||
kiṃ ca |
niṣṭhīvitahasitavijṛmbhitāvasphoṭanāni || 2.21 ||
gurusamīpe ‘nyasamīpe ‘pi varjanārthaṃ pṛthagabhidhānam | niṣṭhīvanaṃ prasiddham | hasanaṃ paravaśe ‘pi yatnato nivārayitavyam | tathā ca āpastambaḥ: “na smayeta yadi smayetāpipṛhya smayeteti brāhmaṇaṃ” [ĀpDh 2.7.6–7] iti | vijṛmbhaṇaṃ prasiddham | avasphoṭhanaṃ saśabdasaṃdhipramokaḥ || 2.21 ||
varjayed iti | niṣṭhīvitaṃ kaṇṭhac chleṣmaṇaḥ saśabdaṃ bahir nirasanam | hasitaṃ hāsaḥ | vijṛmbhitaṃ jṛmbhikā | avasphoṭanam aṅgulīnāṃ saśabdam upamardanam || 2.21 ||
kiṃ ca |
strīprekṣaṇālambhane maithunaśaṅkāyām || 2.22 ||
varjayed iti vartate | prekṣaṇaṃ prakarṣeṇekṣaṇaṃ avayavanirūpaṇam, na rūpamātradarśanam, tasya nimiṣato vārayitum aśakyatvāt | ālambanaṃ sparśanam | sparśana iti vaktavye evam abhidhānam abhisaṃdhi-sparśanavarjanārtham | evaṃ ca pramādān na doṣaḥ | abhisaṃdhyā tv andhakārādāv api sparśanaṃ na kartavyam | tad ubhayaṃ striyāṃ na kuryāt | yasyāṃ maithunaśaṅkānyeṣāṃ bhavati tasyām eva, nānyasyām || 2.22 ||
strīṇāṃ prekṣaṇam avayavaśo nirūpaṇaṃ na yādṛcchikaṃ darśanam | ālambhanaṃ sparśanam | te ‘pi varjayet | maithunaśaṅkāyām iti vacanād bālavṛddhāturāsu svayaṃ ca tathāvidhasya na doṣaḥ
|| 2.22 ||
dyūtaṃ hīnasevām adattādānaṃ hiṃsām || 2.23 ||
varjayed ity eva | dyūtam akṣādinā | hīnasevā mūtrapurīṣāpamārjanādinā sevā | evaṃ ca guror apy ananurūpā sevā na kartavyā | adattādānaṃ ananujñātasyotsṛṣṭasyāpi grahaṇam | hiṃsā prāṇivadhaḥ prāṇipīḍā vā | visamāsān na kārayen nānumanyetāpi || 2.23 ||
dyūtaṃ varjayed iti | dvividhaṃ [dyūtam] pāṇyaprāṇibhedāt | prāṇidyūtaṃ meṣayuddhādi | aprāṇidyūtam akṣakrīḍādi | hīnasevāṃ hīnasya sevām adhojātiprabhṛteḥ | hīnā cāsau sevā ca śaucādijalāharaṇam | adattādānaṃ kenāpy adattasyotsṛṣṭasyāpy asvāmikasyādānam | hiṃsā prāṇipīḍā || 2.23 ||
kiṃ ca |
ācāryatatputrastrīdīkṣitanāmāni || 2.24 ||
varjayed iti prastutam | ācāryasya tatputrasya ca tadbhāryāyāś ca dīkṣitasya ca saṃjñāṃ pitrādikṛtāṃ noccārayet || 2.24 ||
ācāryasya tatputrasya tatstriyā dīkṣitasya nāmāni varjayet | parokṣe ‘py aupādhikanāmagrahaṇaṃ kartavyam iti || 2.24 ||
kiṃ ca |
madhumāṃsādyetatparyantaṃ varjayed iti kriyānvayo ‘syāpi sūtrasya |
śuktā vācaḥ || 2.25 ||
śuklavāco madyaṃ nityaṃ brāhmaṇaḥ || 2.25 ||
śuktāḥ niṣṭhurāḥ parasyodvegakāriṇyaḥ | sarveṣāṃ varjayet || 2.25 ||

madyaṃ nityaṃ brāhmaṇaḥ || 2.26 ||
varjayed iti prakṛtam | madyaṃ guḍamadhupiṣṭakṛtaṃ anyad api yan madakaraṃ yat sarvaṃ brāhmaṇo nityaṃ prāg apy upanayanād varjayet | evaṃ kṣatriyavaiśyayoḥ prāg upanayanād ūrdhvaṃ ca brahmacaryāt paiṣṭīvarjitāyāḥ surāyā apratiṣedhaḥ, smṛtyantaradarśanāt | yathāha manuḥ |
gauḍī mādhvī ca paiṣṭī ca vijñeyā trividhā surā |

nāmagotre guroḥ samānato nirdiśet || 2.29 || — 2.28 ||
gurugrahaṇam ācāryādīnām apy upalakṣaṇam | samānataḥ samyag ānataḥ prahvaḥ | taṃ dhyātvā namaskāraṃ kṛtvety arthaḥ | evaṃ ca tatrāpi “ācāryatatputraḥ” [GDh 2.24] ity asminn ācāryagrahaṇaṃ gurvādīnām apy upalakṣaṇam, teṣām api nāmadheyoccāraṇasya pratiṣiddhatvāt | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
ācāryaṃ caiva tatputraṃ tadbhāryāṃ dīkṣitaṃ gurum |
pitaraṃ ca pitṛvyaṃ ca mātaraṃ mātulaṃ tathā ||
hitaiṣiṇaṃ ca vidvāṃsaṃ śvaśuraṃ patim eva ca |
na brūyān nāmato vidvān mātuś ca bhaginīṃ tathā ||
yadi brūyāt tu taṃ dhyātvā namaskṛtvā vaded atha || iti || 2.29 ||
