arcite śreyasi caivam || 2.30 || — || 2.29 ||
arcito mānyo vidvān ity arthaḥ | śreyān hitaiṣī vayasā ca vṛddha iti draṣṭavyam | cakārād evaitasmin siddhe evaṃśabdaḥ samastātideśārthaḥ | tataś ca gurudarśane yad abhivādanādi tad atrāpi draṣṭavyam || 2.30 ||
arcito loke pūjitaḥ | śreyān vidyādibhir adhikaḥ | tayor apy evam eva samyag ānata iti | atra smṛtyantaram |
ācāryaṃ caiva tatputraṃ tadbhāryāṃ dīkṣitaṃ gurum |
pitaraṃ vā pitṛvyaṃ ca mātulaṃ mātaraṃ tathā ||
hitaiṣiṇaṃ ca vidvāṃsaṃ śvaśuraṃ patim eva ca |
na brūyān namato vidvān mātuś ca bhaginīṃ tathā ||
arcite śreyasi cety evaṃśabdo yac ca yāvac ca gurāv uktaṃ tat sarvam atidiśati | tena śayyāsanādikam api tayoḥ saṃnidhau nīcaṃ bhavatīti || 2.29 ||
śayyāsanasthānāni vihāya pratiśravaṇam || 2.31 || — || 2.30 ||
yadā gurur ājñāṃ dadyāt tadā — śayanaṃ khaṭvādi, āsanaṃ phalakādi, sthānaṃ yatra sthitaḥ — etāni vihāya tyaktvā pratiśravaṇaṃ prativacanaṃ dadyād ity arthaḥ || 2.31 ||
gurāv ājñāpayati sati pratiśravaṇaṃ prativacanaṃ kurvañ śayyāsanasthānāni vihāyābhigacchan kuryāt || 2.30 ||
abhikramaṇaṃ vacanād adṛṣṭena || 2.32 || — || 2.31 ||
adṛṣṭena guruṇā ājñāyāṃ dīyamānāyām abhikramaṇaṃ tadabhimukhena gamanaṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ || 2.32 ||
yadi bahiḥsthito gurur apaśyann eva śiṣyaṃ bravīti tadā śiṣyeṇābhikramaṇam upasarpaṇaṃ kartavyaṃ na punar adṛṣṭo ‘smīty anādaraḥ kartavyaḥ || 2.31 ||
adhaḥsthānāsanatiryagvātasevāyāṃ gurudarśane cottiṣṭhet || 2.33 ||
adhaḥsthānāsanas tiryagvātasevāyāṃ gurudarśane cottiṣṭhet || 2.32 ||
yadā guruḥ adho nīcaiḥ tiṣṭhet āsīta vā, tiryagvātasevā mūtrapurīṣotsargaḥ, tatra guruṃ dṛṣṭvā uttiṣṭhet | cakārād anyamukho bhūtvā | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
kūpasthitaṃ guruṃ dṛṣṭvā taṭākasthaṃ tathaiva ca |
tiṣṭhed anyamukho bhūtvā śiṣyo dāsas tathaiva ca || iti |
atha vā — cakārād apagamanam ucyate | evaṃ sūtrārthaś ca tadānīṃ draṣṭavyaḥ | adhaḥsthānāsana-tiryagvātasevāyām uttiṣṭhet apayāyāc ca krameṇeti | tatrādhaḥsthānāsane dṛṣṭvottiṣṭhet | tiryagvātasevāyām apayāyād iti kramaḥ || 2.33 ||
yadā gurur nīcaiḥ sthānam āsanaṃ cādhitiṣṭhati svayam uccaiḥ sthānāsanasthas tadā guruṃ dṛṣṭvottiṣṭhet | tiryagvātasevāyāṃ mūtrapurīṣotsargādau ca guruṃ dṛṣtvottiṣṭhet | cakāraḥ pūrvāpekṣayā samuccayārthaḥ || 2.32 ||
gacchantam anuvrajet || 2.34 || — || 2.33 ||
gacchantaṃ dṛṣṭvānuvrajet | cakāro ‘nuvartate | tena anuktam api samuccīyate āsīnaṃ dṛṣṭvā uttiṣṭhet śayānaṃ dṛṣṭvāsīteti | tathā ca smṛtyantaram: “gacchantam anugacched āsīnaṃ cottiṣṭhec chayānaṃ cāsīna upāsīta” [VaDh 7.12] iti || 2.34 ||
gacchantaṃ gurum anugacchet || 2.33 ||
karma vijñāpyākhyāya || 2.35 || — || 2.34 ||
yad asya karma kartavyaṃ tat karomīty ācāryasya vijñāpya tat kṛtvā kṛtam ity ākhyāya tiṣṭhet | vākyasyāparipūrṇatvāt vijñāpyety atra kṛtvā ity adhyāhartavyam | ākhyāyety atrāpi tiṣṭhed iti kriyā ca, “praviśa piṇḍīm” [Patañjali I: 111, 23] itivat || 2.35 ||
yat kiṃcid asya śiṣyasya kartavyaṃ tasya niṣkṛtir idaṃ kariṣyāmīty ācāryāya vijñāpya yac cācāryaupayikam udakumbhaharaṇādi tat svayam eva jñātvā kṛtvā ca tasmai kṛtam ity ākhyāya vartitavyam ity arthaḥ

yuktaḥ priyahitayoḥ || 2.37 || — || 2.36 ||
syād iti śeṣaḥ | yuktas tatparaḥ | priyahitayoḥ samuccitayoḥ | hitasya cāpriyasyākartavyatvāt | priyaṃ prītikaraṃ, hitaṃ āyatikṣamam | yat priyam āyatikṣamaṃ ca tad eva kuryād ity arthaḥ || 2.37 ||
ācāryasya yat priyaṃ hitaṃ ca tatra yuktas tatparaḥ syāt | priyaṃ tatkālaprītikaram | hitaṃ kālāntare --- tatkaram (?) || 2.36 ||
tadbhāryāputreṣu caivam || 2.38 || — 2.37 ||
tasya yau savarṇau sadṛśau bhāryāputrau tayor api priyahitayor yuktaḥ syāt | cakārāt kaṇṭhaprāvṛtādy api | kutaḥ savarṇayor eva grahaṇam iti cet, bahuvacanasya pūjārthatvāt || 2.38 ||
tasyācāryasya bhāryāputrāś ca teṣu caivam ācāryavad vartitavyam || 2.37 ||
idānīṃ tasyāpavādam āha |
asyāpavādaḥ |
nocchiṣṭāśanasnāpanaprasādhanapādaprakṣālanonmardanopasaṃgrahaṇāni
|| 2.39 || — || 2.38 ||
tadbhāryāputrayor etāni na kuryāt | priyahitatvāt prāptaṃ pratiṣiddhyate | anayor eva pratiṣedhāt guror etāni kartavyāny eva | tathā ca āpastambaḥ: “proṣito bhaikṣād agnau kṛtvā bhuñjīta bhaikṣaṃ haviṣā saṃstutaṃ tatrācāryo devatārthe āhavanīyārthe ca taṃ bhojayitvā yad ucchiṣṭaṃ prāśnāti havirucchaṣṭam eva tat” [ĀpDh 1.3.42–1.4.2] ityādi | tathā ca vasiṣṭho ‘pi: “ucchiṣṭam aguror abhojyam” [VaDh 14.20] ityādi | prasādhanaṃ alaṃkāraḥ | śeṣaṃ prasiddham || 2.39 ||
ucchiṣṭāśanaṃ bhuktaśeṣāśanam | snāpanaṃ snānīyādibhiḥ śiro’ṅgamardanapūrvakam abhiṣekaḥ | prasādhanam alaṃkaraṇam | pādaprakṣālanaṃ prasiddham | unmardanam abhyaṅgaśarīrasaṃvāhanādi | upasaṃgrahaṇaṃ “vyatyastapāṇinā” [MDh 2.72] ityādi pūrvoktam | etāni guror bhāryāputreṣu ca na kartavyāni | ata evācārye kartavyānīti siddham || 2.38 ||
upasaṃgrahaṇasyedānīṃ pratiprasavam āha |
athopasaṃgrahaṇasya pratprasavaḥ |
viproṣyopasaṃgrahaṇaṃ gurubhāryāṇām || 2.40 || — 2.39 ||
viproṣya pratyāgatena gurubhāryāṇām upasaṃ-grahaṇaṃ kāryam – nānyadā, nānyasyāḥ || 2.40 ||
viproṣya pravāsaṃ gatvā pratyāgatena gurubhāryāṇām upasaṃgrahaṇaṃ kāryam || 2.39 ||
tatrāpi tu |
tatrāpi
naike yuvatīnāṃ vyavahāraprāptena || 2.41 || — || 2.40 ||
vyavahārasamarthena pūrṇaṣoḍaśavarṣeṇety arthaḥ | yuvatīnāṃ taruṇīnāṃ viproṣyāpi na kāryam ity eke manyante, na tu gautamaḥ | āśaṅkanīyānāśaṅkanīyāpekṣayā draṣṭavyaḥ || 2.41 ||
eke tv ācāryā yuvatīnāṃ gurubhāryāṇāṃ vyavahāraprāptena ṣoḍavarṣaprāyeṇa śiṣyeṇa viproṣyāpy upasaṃgrahaṇaṃ na kāryam iti manyante || 2.40 ||
uktam “agnīndhanabhaikṣacaraṇe” [GDh 2.12] iti | tatrāgnīndhanaṃ gṛhyebhya eva pṛthak pṛthag upalabhyata iti, bhaikṣacaraṇavidhim āha |
agnīndhanabhaikṣacaraṇa ity uktam | tatrāgnīndhanasya pratigṛhyaṃ vyavasthitatvāt sādhāraṇabhaikṣacaraṇe vidhim āha |
sārvavarṇikaṃ bhaikṣacaraṇam abhiśastapatitavarjam || 2.42 ||
sārvavarṇikabhaikṣyacaraṇam abhiśastapatitavarjam || 2.41 ||
kim artham idaṃ sūtram ārabhyate? bhaikṣacaraṇaṃ tāvad anena na vidhīyate “agnīndhanabhaikṣacaraṇe” [GDh 2.12] ity anena vihitatvāt, “abhiśastapativarjanam” ca “praśastānāṃ svakarmasu” [GDh 17.1] ity anenoktatvād iti |
atraika āhuḥ — śūdraprāpaṇārtho ‘yam ārambhaḥ, “praśastānāṃ svakarmasu” [GDh 17.1] ityanenāprāptatvād iti |
na caitad yuktaṃ, “vṛttiś cen nāntareṇa śūdrāt” [GDh 17.5] ity anenāpadi śūdrābhyanujñānasiddheḥ | na cānāpady api brahmacāriṇaḥ śūdrābhyanujñānam anena kalpayituṃ yuktam, smṛtyantare atyantapratiṣiddhatvāt,
śūdrānnarasapuṣṭāṅgo yo ‘dhīyāno ‘pi nityaśaḥ |
juhvann api japan vāpi gatim ūrdhvāṃ na vindati || iti | [VaDh 6.28] | tasmād anāpady eva brahmacāriṇaḥ apraśastadvijātiprāpaṇārtho ‘yam ārambhaḥ | apraśastānām aviśeṣeṇa prāptau satyām “abhiśastapatitavarjam” iti yujyate ca pratiṣedhaḥ | itarathā śūdraprāpaṇe sati abhiśastādīnāṃ prāptyabhāvād eva pratiṣedho na yujyata iti na ca śakyate vaktuṃ śūdraviṣaya evāyam apavāda iti | yatra hi dvijātayaḥ praśastā adhikriyante tatra śūdro ‘pi praśasta eveti itarasya prasaṅga eva nāstīti |
tena prathamakalpas tāvat brahmacāriṇaḥ praśastebhya eva bhaikṣacaraṇaṃ, tadalābhe apraśastebhyaḥ, tadalābhe ācāryādibhyaḥ, sarvābhāve āpadi śūdrād iti sthitam |
sarvavarṇān arhatīti sārvavarṇikaṃ, sarveṣāṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ | sarvagrahaṇaṃ kṣatriyavaiśyaprāpaṇārtham, anyathā brāhmaṇasyaiva pratigrahādhikārāt tasyaiva syād iti | varṇagrahaṇaṃ śūdrasyāpi prāpaṇārtham asati varṇagrahaṇe sarvagrahaṇaṃ tayor eva syād iti tadarthaṃ varṇagrahaṇaṃ kṛtam | tena śūdrasyāprāptau tatprāpaṇārthaṃ varṇagrahaṇam | tataś ca śūdrasyāpi vṛttihīnasya bhikṣācaraṇe na doṣaḥ | bhikṣāsamūho bhaikṣam | tataś ca bahubhyo gṛhebhya āhartavyam | abhiśasta upapātakadoṣeṇābhiśasto gṛhyate, pātakadoṣeṇābhiśastasya patitagrahaṇenaiva siddhatvāt | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “brāhmaṇam anṛtenābhiśasya patanīyenopapatanīyena vā” [VaDh 23.39] iti |
kecit sarveṣu varṇeṣu bhavaṃ sārvavarṇikam iti vigrahaṃ kurvanti | teṣāṃ sarvaśabdasya prayojanaṃ na vidmaḥ | apraśastadvijātiprāpaṇārthaṃ cen na, sūtrārambhasyāpi tasyaiva prayojanatvāt | śūdraprāpaṇārthatve ‘py ukta eva parihāraḥ | tasmāt tad bhūsurair vicāraṇīyam || 2.42 ||
sarveṣu varṇeṣu bhavaṃ sārvavarṇikam | abhiśastān patitāṃś ca varjayitvā sarveṣu varṇeṣu bhaikṣyaṃ caritavyam | abhiśastā upapātakinaḥ || 2.41 ||
ādimadhyānteṣu bhavacchabdaḥ prayojyo varṇānupūrvyeṇa || 2.43 || — || 2.42 ||
dvipadasya “bhikṣāṃ dehi” iti śabdasya prārthanāyāṃ dehītyasyāpi ca sārmathyāt prāptasyādau brāhmaṇena bhavacchabdaḥ prayoktavyaḥ, madhye kṣatriyeṇa, ante vaiśyena | bhavacchabdasya prātipadikatvāt kevalasya ca prayogābhāvāt strīṇāṃ sakāśe tadāmantritavibhaktyantaḥ prayoktavyaḥ, “bhavati bhikṣāṃ dehi” iti | puruṣasakāśe “bhavan bhikṣāṃ dehi” iti | pretyupasargāt smṛtyantaroktam api draṣṭavyam | tad api “kṣakārahikārau noccair vaktavyau” iti | yathāha gṛhyasmṛtiḥ:
brāhmaṇo bhikṣeta ‘bhavati bhikṣāṃ dehi’ iti, bhavanmadhyayā rājanyo ‘bhikṣāṃ bhavati dehi’ iti, bhavadantyayā vaiśyo ‘bhikṣāṃ dehi bhavati’ | kṣāṃ ca hiṃ ca na vardhayet || iti |
varṇagrahaṇaṃ śūdrasya mantraprāpaṇārtham, tataś ca trayāṇām eva varṇānām anyatamo mantro draṣṭavyaḥ | anyo vā racanāviśeṣaḥ kartavyaḥ | "vaiśyavac chūdravṛttiḥ" iti smṛtyantaradarśanāt, vaiśyokto vā draṣṭavyaḥ | aniyatavṛttivijñāpanārthaṃ sākṣān mantro noktaḥ | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: "aniyatā ca vṛttir aniyatakeśaveṣāḥ” ityādi | ekasyaiva vikalpo mā bhūd iti anupūrvagrahaṇam || 2.43 ||
bhikṣāṃ dehīti padadvayasyādimadhyānteṣu varṇakrameṇa bhavacchabdaḥ saṃbuddhyantaḥ prayoktavyaḥ | strīṣu strīliṅgaḥ | brāhmaṇasya bhavan bhikṣāṃ dehi | brahmaṇyāṃ bhavati bhikṣāṃ dehi | kṣatriyasya bhikṣāṃ bhavan dehi | bhikṣāṃ bhavati dehi | vaiśyasya bhikṣāṃ dehi bhavan | bhikṣāṃ dehi bhavati || 2.42 ||
ācāryajñātigurusveṣv alābhe ‘nyatra || 2.44 || — || 2.43 ||
ācārya uktaḥ | jñātiḥ sapiṇḍaḥ, gurur mātulādiḥ, svam ātmīyam | anyatrālābhe eṣu bhaikṣaṃ prārthanīyam | kramas tu pratipāditaḥ || 2.44 ||
ācārya uktaḥ | jñātiḥ pitṛvyādiḥ sapiṇḍaḥ | gurur mātulādiḥ | svam ātmīyagrahaṇam | anyatra bhikṣāyā abhāve, ācāryādigṛheṣu bhaikṣyaṃ caritavyam || 2.43 ||
atrāpi |
teṣāṃ pūrvaṃ pūrvaṃ pariharet || 2.45 || — || 2.44 ||
teṣāṃ yo yaḥ prathamanirdiṣṭaḥ tatra tatra pariharet | anyatrālābhe pūrvaṃ pūrvaṃ parihṛtyāpi teṣām eva sakāśe bhaikṣaṃ paricaritavyam | nopādhyāyagṛhād ity evaṃ niyamārthaṃ teṣāṃ grahaṇam | tataś ca sarvatrālābhe upādhyāyagṛhād ity eva draṣṭavyam | tathā ca smṛtyantaram: “alābhe ‘nyatropādhyāyagṛhād api” iti || 2.45 ||
teṣām ācāryādīnāṃ yo yaḥ prathamanirdiṣṭas taṃ taṃ pariharet | anyatrālābhe svagṛhe, tatrālābhe guruṣu, tatrālābhe jñātiṣu, tatrālābha ācāryagṛha iti || 2.44 ||
nivedya gurave ‘nujñāto bhuñjīta || 2.46 || — || 2.45 ||
“idaṃ bhaikṣam” iti gurave kathayitvā tato “bhuṃkṣva” iti tenānujñāto bhuñjīta | evaṃ ca nivedanaṃ bhaikṣasaṃskārārtham, anujñāta iti vacanāt | yadi gurur gṛhṇīyāt tadānyad āhartavyam || 2.46 ||
idam ānītaṃ bhaikṣyam iti gurave nivedya tadanujñāto bhuñjīta | yadi guruḥ svayaṃ gṛhṇīyāt tato ‘nyad āharet || 2.45 ||
asaṃnidhau tadbhāryāputrasabrahmacārisadbhyaḥ || 2.47 || — 2.46 ||
H in some mss. reads: -brahmacāribhyaḥ
bhāryāputrayoḥ samānajātīyatvajñāpanārtham | sabrahmacārī samānacaraṇaḥ, san sādhuḥ prakṛṣṭaguṇaḥ || 2.47 ||
ācāryāsaṃnidhāne tadbhāryādibhyo yathāsaṃbhavaṃ nivedya tair anujñāto bhuñjīta || 2.46 ||
tat kathaṃ bhuñjītety ata āha |
vāgyatas tṛpyann alolupyamānaḥ saṃnidhāyodakam || 2.48 || — || 2.47 ||
vāgyatas tūṣṇīm, tṛpyan saṃtoṣaṃ kurvan, pūrvam asaṃtuṣṭo 'py annadarśanamātrād eva hṛṣyed ity arthaḥ | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
dṛṣṭvā hṛṣyet prasīdec ca pratinandecc a sarvaśaḥ | iti | [MDh 2.54] | alolupyamāno viśeṣaspṛhāṃ pariharann atibhojanaṃ varjayed ity arthaḥ | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
anāyuṣyam anārogyarm asvagyaṃ cātibhojanam |
apuṇyaṃ lokavidviṣṭaṃ tasmāt tat parivarjayet || iti | [MDh 2.57]
atha vā – bhojanavidhim aparivarjayann ity arthaḥ | tathā ca smṛtyantare bhojanavidhir draṣṭavyaḥ | samyaṅ nidhāyodakaṃ prāśyety arthaḥ | samupasargaḥ smṛtyantaroktamārgopasaṃgrahārthaḥ | tathāha uśanā: “satyaṃ tvartenetyādinodakaṃ pariṣicyāmṛtopastaraṇam ityādinā prāśayet” iti |
atha vā – udakam iti kamaṇḍalur ucyate | tat saṃnidhāya | tataś ca brahmacāriṇo ‘pi kamaṇḍaludhāraṇaṃ kartavyam | tathaiva manur api mekhalādibhiḥ saha pratipattividhānāt brahmacāriṇaḥ kamaṇḍaludhāraṇaṃ jñāpayati:
mekhalām ajinaṃ daṇḍam upavītaṃ kamaṇḍalum |
apsu prāsya vinaṣṭāni gṛhṇītānyāni mantravat || iti | [MDh 2.64] | evaṃ ca tata evācamanaṃ kartavyam, vacanasāmarthyāt | ucchiṣṭasparśane na doṣaḥ | smṛtyantaroktaṃ vā draṣṭavyam | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
apsu pāṇau ca kāṣṭhe ca kalpitaḥ pāvakaḥ śuciḥ |
tasmād udakapāṇibhyāṃ parimṛjyāt kamaṇḍalum || iti | [VaDh 12.15] | kecit samupasargād evaitam arthaṃ samarthayanti || 2.48 ||
yāvadbhukti vācaṃyamaḥ | tṛpyann annadarśanena hṛṣyan | alolupyamāno ‘tispṛhām akurvan | saṃnidhāyāntarbhāvitaṇyarthaḥ | saṃnidhāpyeti | udakam udakabhājanam iti || 2.47 ||
idānīṃ yadi brahmacārī guroḥ samyag vinaye nāvatiṣṭheta tadānīṃ kiṃ kartavyam ity ata āha|
śiṣyaśāsanaprakāram āha |
śiṣyaśiṣṭir avadhena || 2.49 || — || 2.48 ||
śiṣyāśiṣṭiḥ śiṣyaśāsanam, avadhena vadhaṃ muktvā, nirbhartsanādinā kartavyam ity arthaḥ | adhikārād eva siddhe śiṣyagrahaṇam anyasyāpi śāsanīyasya bhāryā-putrāder ayam eva dharma iti jñāpanārtham || 2.49 ||
vadhas tāḍanam | atāḍyatā guruṇā bhartsanādibhiḥ śiṣyaḥ śāsyaḥ || 2.48 ||
aśaktau rajjuveṇuvidalābhyāṃ tanubhyām || 2.50 || — || 2.49 ||
nirbhartsanādinā śāsanāśaktau rajjvā veṇuvidalena vā | sahanirdiṣṭayor api tulyārthatvāt vikalpaḥ | tenāpi tanunā śāsyaḥ || 2.50 ||
yadi bhartsanādibhiḥ śāsitum aśakyas tato rajjvā tanvā, tanunā veṇuvidalena veti | dvaṃdvanirdiṣṭayor api vikalpo “rajjvā veṇudalena vā” [MDh 8.229] iti mānave darśanāt | tābhyāṃ durbalābhyāṃ tāḍayitvāpi śāsanīyaḥ || 2.49 ||
anyena ghnan rājñā śāsyaḥ || 2.51 || — || 2.50 ||
anyena hastapādādinā krodhena ghnan rājñā daṇḍyaḥ | gauravārthaṃ rājagrahaṇam || 2.51 ||
hastādinā krodhavaśena tāḍayan rājñā śāsya ācāryaḥ | evaṃ śiṣyasya gurukule vāsa uktaḥ || 2.50 ||
atha kiyantaṃ kālam evaṃ vartetety āha |
kimantaṃ kālam ity ata āha |
dvādaśa varṣāṇy ekavede brahmacaryaṃ caret || 2.52 || — || 2.51 ||
ekavedam adhyeṣyamāṇo dvādaśa varṣāṇy abhihitarūpaṃ brahmacaryaṃ caret kuryāt | brahmacaryaśabdena yamaniyamakalāpa ucyate, brahmārthatvāt || 2.52 ||
yady apy ekaikasya vedasya bahvyaḥ śākhāḥ | ekaviṃśatidhā bahvṛca ekaśataṃ yajuḥśākhāḥ sahasravartmā sāmavedo navadhātharvaṇo veda iti | tathāpi tatra tatra vede pūrvair adhyayanānuṣṭhānā-bhyāṃ parigṛhītā yavatī śākhā tāvaty atra vedaśabdena vivakṣitā | ya ekaṃ vedam adhīte sa dvādaśa varṣāṇi gurukule brahmacaryaṃ caret || 2.51 ||
prati dvādaśa vā || 2.53 ||
pratidvādaśa vā sarveṣu || 2.52 ||
prativedaṃ vā dvādaśa varṣāṇi brahmacaryaṃ caret
|| 2.53 ||
yas tu caturo vedān adhyetuṃ śaktaḥ sa pratidvādaśa prativede dvādaśa varṣāṇīty arthaḥ | yathāha āpastambaḥ: “upetasyācāryakule brahmacārivāso ‘ṣṭācatvāriṃśad vatsarāṇi” [ĀpDh 1.2.11–12] iti
|| 2.52 ||
sarveṣu grahaṇāntaṃ vā || 2.54 ||
grahaṇāntaṃ vā || 2.53 ||
sarveṣu vedeṣu yāvataiva kālenādhyayanam abhinirvartayet tāvantam eva kālaṃ brahmacaryaṃ caret tasya tadarthatvāt | sarvagrahaṇaṃ yady ekam adhīyīta yadi vā dvau yadi vā sarvān ity evamartham | tathā ca manuḥ |
vedān adhītya vedau vā vedaṃ vāpi yathākramam | iti | [MDh 3.2] so ‘yam uktāvadher ūrdvam arvāg vāpavādaḥ | “niyamenādhītaṃ vīryavad bhavati” iti niyamoktiḥ || 2.54 ||
yāvatā kālenaiko vedo dvau trayaś caturo vā grahītuṃ śakyās tāvantaṃ kālam iti || 2.53 ||
vidyānte gurur arthena nimantryaḥ || 2.55 || — || 2.54 ||
vidyāsamāptau, na tu vratānām, gurur ācāryaḥ arthena prayojanena nimantryaḥ, praṣṭavyaḥ “kiṃ gurvarthe karavāṇi” iti || 2.55 ||
vidyāsamāptau gurur arthena prayojanena nimantryaḥ praṣṭavyaḥ | guro, idaṃ dhanam āharāṇīti || 2.54 ||
tataḥ |
kṛtvānujñātasya vā snānam || 2.56 || — || 2.55 ||
kṛtvā datvety arthaḥ, gurūpadiṣṭam artham | tena vā “alaṃ gurudakṣiṇayā” ity anujñātasya snānaṃ samāvṛttiḥ || 2.56 ||
tata āharety ācāryoktaṃ kṛtvā snānaṃ kartavyam | vatsa tvadguṇair evāham asmi toṣito dhanenālam iti tenānujñātasya vā, snānaṃ samāvartanaṃ kartavyam iti || 2.55 ||
idānīṃ pitrādyanekagurusaṃnidhāne kaḥ prathamaḥ pūjya ity ata āha |
ācāryaḥ śreṣṭho gurūṇām || 2.57 ||
ācāryaḥ śreṣṭho gurūṇāṃ mātety eke mātety eke || 2.56 ||
ācārya uktalakṣaṇaḥ, sa śreṣṭhaḥ pradhāno gurūṇām anyeṣāṃ pitrādīnām | tataś ca “saṃnipāte parasya” [GDh 6.4] ity atra paraśabdenāyam evocyate || 2.57 ||
gurūṇāṃ pitrādīnāṃ madhya uktalakṣaṇa ācāryaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ | sa hi vidyātas taṃ janayati tac chreṣṭhaṃ janma | tenānekagurusamavāye sa eva prathamaṃ pūjyaḥ | eke tv ācāryā mātā śreṣṭheti manyante | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ |
upādhyāyād daśācārya ācāryāṇāṃ śataṃ pitā |
pitur daśaguṇaṃ mātā gauraveṇātiricyate || [VaDh 13.48] āpastambo ‘pi: “mātā putratvasya bhūyāṃsi karmāṇy ārabhate tasyāṃ śuśrūṣā nityā patitāyām api” [ĀpDh 1.28.9] | dviruktir adhyāyaparisamāptyarthā
|| 2.56 ||
mātety eke mātety eke || 2.58 ||
eke mātā śreṣṭheti manyante | itikaraṇāt pitety apare, anyathā mātaika ity eva siddhatvāt | kevalāt pitur ācāryaḥ pradhānaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
kāmān mātā pitā cainaṃ yad utpādayato mithaḥ |
saṃbhūtiṃ tasya tāṃ vidyāt yad yonāv abhijāyate ||
ācāryas tv asya yāṃ jātiṃ vidhivad vedapāragaḥ |
utpādayati sāvitryā sā satyā sājarāmarā || iti | [MDh 2.147–48] | yadi pitaivācāryo bhavati tadā pitā viśiṣyate | mātā sarveṣāṃ śreṣṭhā | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ |
upādhyāyad daśācārya ācāryāt tu śataṃ pitā |
sahasraṃ tu pitur mātā gauraveṇātiricyate | iti || [VaDh 13.48] atra pitṛśabdena pitaivācāryo yo bhavati sa eva gṛhyata iti || 2.58 ||
atha tṛtīyo ‘dhyāyaḥ
evaṃ kṛtaniyamasya kṛtavidyasya ca |
tasyāśramavikalpam eke bruvate || 3.1 ||
tasyākhaṇḍitabrahmacaryasya | tathā ca manuḥ |
vedān adhītya vedau vā vedaṃ vāpi yathākramam |
aviplutabrahmacaryo gṛhasthāśramam āvaset || iti | [MDh 3.2] | evaṃ ca viplutasyopapātakaprāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam | śūdraparyudāsārthaṃ vā tadgrahaṇam, āśramadharmābhidhānaṃ mā bhūt tasyāpīti | vīpsārthaṃ vā tasya tasyeti, brāhmaṇasya kṣatriyasya vaiśyasya ceti | tadgrahaṇam antareṇāpy adhikārād evaitat siddham iti cen na, smṛtyantarābhāsāśaṅkā-nivṛttyarthatvāt | evaṃ hi smṛtyantaraṃ pratibhāti:
eṣa vo ‘bhihito dharmo brāhmaṇasya caturvidhaḥ | [MDh 6.97] iti, tatra brāhmaṇasyaivāyaṃ caturvidho dharma itarayor ekavidha ity āśaṅkā syād iti | tarhi tat katham iti cet, tatra brāhmaṇagrahaṇaṃ pradarśanārtham | tat kathaṃ gamyata iti cet, “trayāṇām eva varṇānāṃ catvāra āśramāḥ” iti smṛtyantaradarśanāt | tathā ca āpastambaḥ: “sarveṣām upanayanaprabhṛti samānam ācāryakule vāsaḥ | sarveṣām anūtsargo vidyāyāḥ | buddhvā karmāṇi yat kāmayet tad ārabheta” [ĀpDh 2.21.3–5] iti |
eke bruvate: “tulyaphalatvād āśramavikalpaḥ | dvividhaṃ hi phalam abhyudayarūpaṃ niḥśreyasarūpaṃ ceti | tad anyatamenaiva labhyate” iti | tathā ca śrutiḥ: “trayo dharmaskandhā yajño ‘dhyayanaṃ dānam iti prathamas tapa eva dvitīyas tṛtīyam ācāryakule vasanam etān āruhya brahmalokaṃ gacchati brahmasaṃstho ‘mṛtatvameti” [ChUp 2.23.1] iti | arthas tu trayo dharmasyāśrayāḥ, yajñādhyayanaṃ dānam iti gṛhasthanirdeśaḥ, tapa eveti vānaprasthaparivrājakayor dharmaḥ, ācāryakulavasanaṃ brahmacāriṇaḥ | etān āruhyeti brahmacāryāśramaṃ gṛhasthāśramaṃ vānaprasthāśramaṃ parivrājakāśramaṃ vā samyag āsthāyety arthaḥ | yas teṣāṃ brahmasaṃstho brahmaniṣṭho jñānī amṛtatvaṃ mokṣam eti gacchatīti | tataś ca sarveṣām api brahmalokaprāptipratipādanāt, tulyaphalataivoktā | tathā jābālaśrutāv api tulyaphalataiva śrūyate: “brahmacaryaṃ samāpya gṛhī bhavet | gṛhī bhūtvā vanī bhavet | vanī bhūtvā pravrajet | yadi vetarathā brahmacaryād eva pravrajet gṛhād vā vanād vā brahmalokaṃ gacchati” [JābUp 4 ] iti | manur apy āha |
sarve ‘pi kramaśas tv ete yathāśāstraṃ niṣevitāḥ |
yathoktakāriṇaṃ vipraṃ nayanti paramāṃ gatim || iti | [MDh 6.88] | atrāpiśabdād eko ‘pi draṣṭavyaḥ | āpastambo py āha: “catvāra āśramā gārhasthyam ācāryakule vasanaṃ maunaṃ vānapraśthyām iti | teṣu sarveṣu yathopadeśam avyagraṃ vartamānaḥ kṣemaṃ gacchati” [ĀpDh 2.21.1–2] iti | tena tulyaphalatvād vikalpa iti |
nanu tulyatve sati brahmacaryeṇaivobhayavidhaphalaprāpter itarāśramavidhānānarthakyaprasaṅgaḥ iti |
ucyate – yo yasyānuṣṭhāne samarthaḥ tasya tadāśramapratipattyartham itarāśramavidhānam iti | janmāntaravāsanayā vā iṣṭāśramapratipattyartham | śrutir api: “taṃ vidyākarmaṇī samanvārabhete pūrvaprajñā ca” [BṛĀrUp 4.4.2] iti | yāvajjīvaśrutir api gṛhasthasyārthinaḥ śaktiviṣaye draṣṭavyā | evaṃ gṛhīte vyutthānaśrutir apy upapadyate: “etaṃ vai tam ātmānaṃ viditvā putreṣaṇāyāś ca vitteṣaṇāyāś ca lokeṣaṇāyāś ca vyutthāyātha bhikṣācaryaṃ caranti” [BṛĀrUp 3.5.1] iti | itarathā hi bhikṣācaryam iti saṃnyāsopadeśo nopapadyata iti | jāyamānaśrutyāpi yajñādīnām avaśyakartavyatā pratipādyate gṛhasthasya, na tu jāyamānasyaiva, ṛṇatrayasaṃbandhasyātyantā-saṃbhavāt “brahmacaryād eva pravrajet” [JābUp 4] iti śrutiviruddhatvāt, “so ‘ta eva brahmacaryāt pravrajati” [ĀpDh 2.21.8] “yam icchet tam āvaset” [VaDh 7.3] ityādismṛter aviruddhatvāc ca, arthavādānāṃ ca kāryaśeṣatvāt | tataś ca padārtho ‘py evaṃ grahītavyaḥ | gṛhastho jāyamānaḥ pūrvavāsanayā gārhasthyaṃ praviṣṭaḥ | brāhmaṇagrahaṇaṃ kṣatriyavaiśyayor upalakṣaṇam | tribhiḥ ṛṇaiḥ saṃbadhyate: yajñena devebhya ṛṇī bhavati, ato yajñaḥ kartavyaḥ | prajayā pitṛbhyaḥ, ataḥ prajotpādanaṃ kartavyam | brahmacaryeṇa ṛṣibhyaḥ, ataḥ parvādau brahmacaryarakṣaṇaṃ kartavyam iti | evaṃ gṛhīte sarvatra śrutismṛtyor avirodho bhavati | ekegrahaṇān na tu gautamaḥ, tasya tu samuccayo mataḥ | katham avagamyate “aikāśramyaṃ tv ācāryāḥ” [GDh 3.36] iti bādhapakṣasyāpi paramatatvena nirdeśāt, “prāguttamāt traya āśramiṇaḥ” [GDh 28.49] iti ca liṅgāt, prāguttamāśramāt trayasyābhāvāt | vikalpapakṣe gautamas tāvad evaṃ manyate |
yajñena devān āpnoti vairājaṃ tapasā punaḥ |
saṃnyāsād brahmaṇaḥ sthānaṃ vairāgyāt prakṛtau layam ||
iti bhinnaphalatvenopadeśāt, jābālaśrutāv apy “anyatamam āruhya” ity anuktvā “etān āruhya” iti samuccayābhiprāyeṇoktatvāt, brahmalokaprāpter api samuccayaphalatvenopadiṣṭatvāt, jābālaśrutāv api prādhānyena pūrvaṃ samuccayapakṣasyaivābhi-hitatvāt, “sarve ‘pi kramaśaḥ” [MDh 6.88] ity asmin manuvākye ‘pi śabdasya samuccayārthatvāt, āpastambavākye ‘pi “teṣu sarveṣu” [ĀpDh 2.21.2] iti bahuvacanāt, naikasminn eva vartamānaḥ kiṃ tu sarveṣv eva vartamāna ity abhiprāyeṇoktatvāt, ṛṇatrayaśruter api yathārtha evopapadyamānatvāt, “pūrvaprajñā ca” iti śruter api vidyābhiprāyeṇopa-padyamānatvāt, yāvajjīvaśruter api “gṛhī vanaṃ praviśed yadi gṛham eva kāmayeta tadā yāvajjīvam agnihotraṃ juhuyāt” iti jābālaśrutivākye anityatvadarśanāt, asmin pakṣe vyutthānaśruter api sutarām upapadyamānatvāt, “caturtham āyuṣo bhāgaṃ” [MDh 4.1] ityanenāpi manunā samuccaya-pakṣam evāśrityoktatvāt, samuccayapakṣa eva śreyān iti | ata eva vikalpabādhapakṣau paramatatvenopa-nyastau | evaṃ ca “yam icchet tam āvaset” [VaDh 7.3] ityādivākyāni vikalpācāryadarśanenoktānīti draṣṭavyāni | asamartho yaḥ samuccayānuṣṭhāne andhapaṅgutvādi-doṣeṇa niḥsvatvādinā vā tena vikalpapakṣo ‘py āśrayaṇīyaḥ ity abhiprāyeṇa bruvata ity arthasiddhasyāpy upanyāsaḥ |
atha vā yo ‘dhyātmajñānī tena vikalpapakṣa āśrayaṇīyaḥ, itareṇa samuccayapakṣa iti | kutaḥ? anuṣṭhānasya vijñānotpattiḥ prayojanam | tasminn utpanne yatra kvāpy āśrame sthitasya puruṣārthaḥ sidhyatīti | āśramagrahaṇaṃ saṃvyavahārārthaṃ “varṇān āśramāṃś ca” [GDh 11.9] ityādau || 3.1 ||
tasyaivam adhītavedasya brahmacāriṇo vakṣyamāṇāś catvāra āśramā vikalpyanta ity eka ācāryā bruvate | anye tu samuccīyanta iti | tatra āpastambaḥ: “teṣu sarveṣu yathopadeśam avyagro vartamānaḥ kṣemaṃ gacchati” [ĀpDh 2.21.2] iti | “buddhvā karmāṇi yat kāmayeta tad ārabheta” [ĀpDh 2.21.5] iti ca | tathā ca brahmacaryāśramam uktvā “ata eva brahmacaryavān pravrajati” [= ĀpDh 2.21.8] iti baudhāyanaḥ | manunā tu samuccayo darśitaḥ |
ṛṇāni trīṇy apākṛtya mano mokṣe niveśayet |
anapākṛtya mokṣaṃ tu vrajamānaḥ pataty adhaḥ || [MDh 6.35]
sarve ‘pi kramaśas tv ete yathāśāstraṃ niveṣitāḥ |
yathoktakāriṇaṃ vipraṃ nayanti paramāṃ gatim || [MDh 6.88] iti || 3.1 ||
ke punas ta āśramā ity ata āha |
ke punas ta āśramāḥ |
brahmacārī gṛhastho bhikṣur vaikhānasaḥ || 3.2 ||
brahmacaryaṃ caratīti, brahmaṇa eva vā viṣaye niḥspṛhaś caratīti, brahmacārī | gṛhaṃ patnī, tatsaṃyogād gṛhasthaḥ | bhikṣāśīlatvād bhikṣuḥ | vikhanasā proktaṃ śāstraṃ vaikhānasam, tadvidhinā vartata iti vaikhānasaḥ | visamāsa eṣām eva pṛthaktvajñāpanārthaḥ | yathāha uśanā: “dvau brahmacāriṇāv upakurvāṇo naiṣṭhikaś ceti, dvau vaikhānasau sapatnīko vipatnīkaś ceti, dvau saṃnyāsinau bhikṣusaṃnyāsī vedasaṃnyāsī ceti, bahudhā gṛhasthaḥ śālīnayāyāvarādibhedena” ityādi |
nanu ca smṛtyantare vaikhānasam abhidhāya bhikṣur ity uktaḥ | iha kim arthaṃ kramabheda iti |
ucyate – “prāguttamās traya āśramiṇaḥ” [GDh 28.49] ity atra vaikhānasavarjanārthaḥ kramabhedaḥ | tasya grāmapraveśanapratiṣedhād eva siddham iti cet, na, arthinām araṇyagamanasyāpi saṃbhavāt | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
cāturvaidyo vikalpī ca nairukto dharmapāṭhakaḥ |
brahmacārī gṛhī bhikṣuḥ parṣad eṣā daśāvarā || iti |
kecit tu |
prajāpatyāṃ nirupyeṣṭiṃ sarvavedasadakṣiṇām |
ātmany agnīn samāropya brāhmaṇaḥ pravrajet gṛhāt || iti smṛtyantaram avalambya gārhasthyād eva saṃnyāsaḥ kartavya ity evamarthaḥ kramabheda iti varṇayanti | tat pūrvasūtreṇaiva pratipāditaṃ uta neti bhūsurair vicāraṇīyam || 3.2 ||
yady apy asau pūrvam api brahmacaryāśrama uktas tathāpi prapitsitanaiṣṭhikabrahmacāritvam atra vivakṣitam | bhikṣuḥ saṃnyāsī | vaikhānaso vānaprasthaḥ | vaikhānasaproktena mārgeṇa vartata iti | tena sa āśramaḥ prādhānyena darśitaḥ | śāstrāntareṣu vaikhānasas tṛtīyo bhikṣus caturtha āśramaḥ | iha tu kramabhedaḥ “prāguktās traya āśramiṇaḥ” [GDh 28.49] ity atra vaikhānasavarjanārthaḥ || 3.2 ||
teṣāṃ gṛhastho yonir aprajanatvād itareṣām || 3.3 ||
caturṇām apy āśramāṇāṃ gṛhasthaḥ kāraṇam, aprajanatvād itareṣāṃ brahmacāryādīnām | vaikhānasārthā hetūktiḥ, brahmacāriṇo brahmacaryarakṣaṇād eva siddhatvāt, bhikṣor apy ūrdhvaretastvavidhānāt | evaṃ ca vaikhānasasya sapatnīkatvapakṣe ‘pi brahmacaryarakṣaṇaṃ kartavyam | adhikārād eva siddhe teṣāṃgrahaṇaṃ gṛhasthotpannānām eva teṣāṃ dharme ādhikāra ity evamartham | tathā ca śātātapaḥ |
caṇḍālāḥ pratyavasitāḥ parivrājakatāpasāḥ |
teṣāṃ jātāny apatyāni caṇḍālaiḥ saha vāsayet || iti |
“teṣāṃ gṛhastha eva yonir” ity ukte evakārād evetareṣāṃ brahmacaryarakṣaṇe siddhe evam abhidhānam eṣāṃ punar nivṛttir mā bhūd iti | yadi te gārhasthyaṃ prati nivṛttiṃ bhaveyuḥ tadā te caṇḍālā bhavanti | kutaḥ? udāhṛtaśātātapavacanāt | tatra – caṇḍālā bhavanti | pratyavasitāḥ pratinivṛttāḥ ye parivrājakatāpasāḥ naiṣṭhikavaikhānasāḥ tasmāt teṣāṃ jātāny apatyāni caṇḍālaiḥ saha vāsayed rājā grāmo vety arthaḥ || 3.3 ||
teṣāṃ caturṣv apy āśrameṣu vartamānānāṃ gṛhastho yonir upasatti[utpatti]sthānam | gṛhasthenaivotpāditāś caturbhir āśramair adhikriyante | gṛhasthavyatiriktāśramasthānāṃ prajotpādanasya niṣiddhatvāt | tatra śātātapaḥ |
caṇḍālāḥ pratyavasitāḥ parivrājakatāpasāḥ |
teṣāṃ jātāny apatyāni caṇḍālaiḥ saha vāsayet ||
iti || 3.3 ||
idānīm āśramadharmeṣu krameṇa vaktavyeṣu prāpteṣu sarvāśramasāmyād upakurvāṇasya pūrvam uktvā naiṣṭhikasya krameṇa prāptaṃ vaktum āha |
idānīm āśramadharmān vakṣyan prathamanirdiṣṭasya brahmacāriṇa āha |
tatroktaṃ brahmacāriṇaḥ || 3.4 ||
tatropanayanaprakareṇa yad agnīndhanādy uktaṃ tad asyāpi bhavatīty atideśaḥ |
nanu ca naiṣṭikasyaivedaṃ nopakurvāṇasyeti kathaṃ jñāyata iti |
ucyate – upakurvāṇasyoktatvāt, gṛhasthabhikṣuvaikhānasānāṃ vakṣyamāṇatvāt, naiṣṭikasyaiva bhavatīti |
tarhi brahmacārigrahaṇaṃ kim artham iti cet, yenopakurvāṇavratam anuṣṭhitaṃ tasyaiva naiṣṭhikatvasyādhikāra ity evamartham || 3.4 ||
tatra teṣāṃ madhye brahmacāriṇo naiṣṭhikasya yad upakurvānasya “upanayanādir niyamaḥ” [GDh 2.10] ity ārabhyoktam tad evāsyāpīty uktaṃ bhavati || 3.4 ||
atideśāt upakurvāṇavratam eva naiṣṭhikasyāpy anuṣṭhātavyam ity uktvā idānīm asya viśeṣavidhim āha |
tatra viśeṣaḥ |
ācāryādhīnatvam āntam || 3.5 ||
ācāryapāratantryaṃ taccittānuvidhāyitvaṃ ā maraṇāt kartavyam || 3.5 ||
āntam ā dehapātam | ācāryakula eva tacchuśrūṣayā varteta || 3.5 ||
kiṃ ca |
guroḥ karmaśeṣeṇa japet || 3.6 ||
guruśuśrūṣātiriktakāle japaṃ kuryāt | evaṃ ca japa evāsya viśiṣṭaṃ dharmasādhanam iti | ācārya iti prakṛte gurugrahaṇaṃ mātāpitror api śuśrūṣāprāpaṇārtham | tathā ca manuḥ |
teṣāṃ trayāṇāṃ śuśrūṣā paramaṃ tapa ucyate |
na tair anabhyanujñāto dharmam anyaṃ samācaret || [MDh 2.229] iti || 3.6 ||
ācārye prakṛte guruśabdaḥ pitror api grahaṇārthaḥ | tataś cācāryaṃ pitarau ca śuśrūṣamāṇas tadvyatirikte kāle japed vedam adhīyīta | na tu svādhīno bhavet
|| 3.6 ||
gurvabhāve tadapatye vṛttiḥ || 3.7 ||
gurvabhāve tadapatyavṛttis tadabhāve vṛddhe sabrahmacāriṇy agnau vā || 3.7 ||
tadabhāve guruputrābhāve vidyādibhir vṛddhe, tada-bhāve sabrahmacāriṇi samānacaraṇe, tadabhāve agnau | agneḥ śuśrūṣā jyotīrūpasyāgner dhyānam || 3.8 ||
ācārye yā vṛttir abhihitā sā tadabhāve tatputre, tatputrā-bhāve vṛddhe vidyayā vayasā vādhike, vṛddhābhāve sabrahmacāriṇi, sabrahmacāryabhāve ‘gnau vā kartavyā | samidādhānādibhir agnau vṛttiḥ || 3.7 ||
tadabhāve vṛddhe sabrahmacāriṇy agnau
vā || 3.8 ||
tadabhāve guruputrābhāve vidyādibhir vṛddhe, tad-abhāve sabrahmacāriṇi samānacaraṇe, tadabhāve agnau | agneḥ śuśrūṣā jyotīrūpasyāgner dhyānam || 3.8 ||
kiṃ punar asyāśramasya phalam ity ata āha |
evaṃvṛtto brahmalokam avāpnoti jitendriyaḥ || 3.9 || — || 3.8 ||
evaṃ yathopadeśaṃ vartamāno brahmalokaṃ brahmaṇaḥ sthānam avāpnoti, sa cej jitendriyo bhavati | brahmacaryarakṣaṇopadeśāt upasthendriyasyoktatvāt idaṃ cakṣurādiviṣayaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha manuḥ |
śrutvā spṛṣṭvā ca dṛṣṭvā ca bhuktvā ghrātvā ca yo naraḥ |
na hṛṣyati glāyati vā sa vijñeyo jitendriyaḥ || iti | [MDh 2.98] uttareṣām apy etad eva pradhānam ity evamarthaṃ phalāvasāne vidhānam || 3.9 ||
spaṣṭo ‘rthaḥ | jitendriyatvaṃ manunā darśitam |
śrutvā spṛṣṭvā ca dṛṣṭvā ca bhuktvā ghrātvā ca yo naraḥ |
na hṛṣyati glāyati vā sa vijñeyo jitendriyaḥ || MDh 2.98] || 3.8 ||
idānīm anyeṣām api sāmānyadharmam āha |
uttareṣāṃ caitad avirodhi || 3.10 || — || 3.9 ||
yad brahmacāriṇa uktaṃ tad uttareṣām api bhavati, yad aviruddhaṃ dyūtādivarjanam | yad virudhyate tad vyāvartate, yathāgnikāryaṃ pravrajitasya, gurukulavāso vaikhānasasya, gṛhasthasya brahmacaryarakṣaṇam | caśabdaḥ uttarasya yad vakṣyamāṇaṃ tad apy aviruddhaṃ pūrveṣām api bhavatīti jñāpayati, yathā “na mlecchāśucyadhārmikaiḥ saha sambhāṣeta” [GDh 9.17] ityādi || 3.10 ||
uttareṣām apy āśramāṇām asmin vṛtte yad aviruddhaṃ tat samānam | yathā dyūtādivarjanam | viruddhaṃ yathā – agnikāryaṃ pravrajitasya, gurukulavāso vaikhānasasya, brahmacaryaṃ gṛhasthasyety aparā vṛttiḥ | uttareṣāṃ cāśramāṇāṃ dharmajātam etasya draṣṭavyam | kim aviśeṣeṇa | na | etad avirodhi | etad āśramadharmāvirodhi “na mlecchāśucyadhārmikaiḥ saha saṃbhāṣeta” [GDh 9.16] ity evamādy asyāpi bhavati || 3.9 |||
bahuvaktavyatvāt kramaprāptam api gṛhastham ullaṅghya bhikṣor dharmān āha |
anicayo bhikṣuḥ || 3.11 || — || 3.10 ||
idānīṃ kramaprāptasya gṛhasthasya dharmā vaktavyāḥ, tān vilaṅghya kimarthaṃ bhikṣor abhidhīyata iti cet, atrocyate-– bahuvaktavyo gṛhasthaḥ | tasyābhidhīyamānāḥ dharmāḥ aviruddhāḥ kecit kathaṃ nāma pūrveṣāṃ syur iti tadarthaṃ vyutkrameṇābhidhānam | tarhi vaikhānasasya vaktavya iti cet, upanyāsakrameṇoktatvād adoṣaḥ |
ato bhikṣos tāvad ucyate | anicayaśabdenaupacayikadravyasya tyāga ucyate, na nityadravyasya śikhāyajñopavītādeḥ |
nanu ca nityasyaiva tyāgo yuktaḥ | upavītaśabdena nyāsaviśeṣo ‘bhidhīyate –
uddhṛte dakṣiṇe pāṇāv upavīty ucyate budhaiḥ | iti | [MDh 2.63]
yajñārtham upavītaṃ yajñopavītam | asya yajñādhikārābhāvāt |
na kuṇḍyāṃ nodake saṅgo na cele na tripuṣkare |
nāgāre nāsane nānne yasya syān mokṣavit tu saḥ || [VaDh 10.23] iti kuṇḍyādīnāṃ tyāgopadeśāt | jābālaśrutāv api: “nakhāni nikṛtya yajñopavītaṃ visṛjya” iti yajñopavītasya tyāgopadeśāc ceti |
ucyate – prathamaṃ tāvad yad uktaṃ yajñādhikārābhāvāt iti tatra yāgaśabdo devapūjanādāv api vartate | devapūjanaṃ tv asya dhyānena paramātmapūjanām asty eva | kutaḥ?
araṇyanityasya jitendriyasya
sarvātmakaprītinivartakasya |
adhyātmacintāgatamānasasya
dhruvā hy anāvṛttir upekṣakasya || [VaDh 10.17] iti smṛtyantaradarśanāt, ātmayāga evādhikārāt | tathā ca śrūyate śrutau: “dharmādharmau havir manaḥ sruvaḥ prāṇo ‘dhvaryur indriyāṇy udgātāraḥ śabdādayo hotā etai ṛtvigbhis tena sruveṇa tad dhavir ahar ahar yatinā paramātmany agnau hotavyam” iti | ato yāgādhikārasadbhāvād adoṣaḥ | yac coktam: “na kuṇḍyāṃ nodake saṅgaḥ” [VaDh 10.23] iti tatrāpi kuṇḍikādīnāṃ vaicitryavarjanārthaḥ saṅgasyaiva parityāgaḥ, na kuṇḍikādeḥ | kutaḥ? tatraiva “yajñopavīty udakakamaṇḍaluhastaḥ” [VaDh 10.31] iti vakṣyamāṇatvāt | tatra “sarvāśramāṇāṃ dharmiṣṭhaṃ” [VaDh 10.30] iti sarvāśramādhikārāt sāmānyatvena vartamāno ‘pi viśeṣadharmeṇa bādhyata iti cet, na, yadi sarvāśramādhikāras tatreṣyate | tatra prakaraṇe vākyārthavākyoccāraṇaghaṭanārthaṃ “yajñopavīty-udakakamaṇḍaluhastatvam” iti bhāvapratyayenaivā-vakṣyat “paiśunamatsarābhimānāhaṃkārānārjavātma-stavaparagarhadambhalobhakrodhamohavivarjanaṃ sarvāśramāṇāṃ dharmiṣṭham” [VaDh 10.30] iti pūrvam uktvā tataś cābhāvapratyayena viśeṣoddeśena “yajñopavīty udakakamaṇḍaluhastaḥ” [VaDh 10.31] iti nirdeśaḥ sarvāśramādhikāranivṛttyartha iti jñātuṃ śakyate | tatas tatra sarvāśramādhikārābhāvāt pūrvāparavirodhaprasaṅgo mā bhūd iti “na kuṇḍyāṃ nodake saṅgaḥ” [VaDh 10.23] iti tatrāpi saṅgamātrasyaiva tyāgaḥ kriyata iti grahītuṃ śakyata eveti | tadartha eva saṅgaśabdasyāpi nirdeśaḥ | tasmāt tenāpi na kuṇḍyādīnāṃ tyāgaḥ | yac coktam – “nakhāni nikṛtya yajñopavītaṃ visṛjya” iti tatrāpi prathamaṃ saṃnyāsāśramapraveśakāle purāṇasya yajñopavītasya tyāgamātraṃ kriyate, na tadānīṃ gṛhyamāṇasya pratiṣedhaḥ | kutaḥ? “nakhāni nikṛtya purāṇaṃ vastraṃ yajñopavītaṃ kamaṇḍaluṃ tyaktvā navāni gṛhītvāśramaṃ praviśet” iti smṛtyantaraśravaṇāt | tasmād aupacayikataddravyasya tyāgaḥ |
kiṃ ca – nityadravyatyāge bhikṣor ācamanābhāvaś ca prāpnoti, “ācamane yajñopavīti” [ĀpDh 1.15.1] ityārambhāt, tadabhāve ācamanasyāpy abhāvaḥ prāpnotīti |
“muṇḍaḥ śikhī vā” [GDh 3.22] iti muṇḍapakṣe ācamanāṅgaśikhāvarjitasyāpi ācamanaṃ yuṣmābhir abhyupagamyate, tadvad asmābhir apīti cet –
na, tatra vacanād adoṣaḥ “muṇḍaḥ śikhī vā” [GDh 3.22] iti | yuṣmākaṃ tu yajñopavītaṃ tyaktavyam iti vacanābhāvād doṣa eveti |
“bhikṣur anicayaḥ” iti vaktavye dharmāsyādāv abhidhānaṃ nityasyāgnihotradārāder api tyāgārtham | evaṃ ca gārhasthyād eva yaḥ pravrajet, tena śrautasya ca smārtasya cāgneḥ dāraputraprapañcayajñādi-sahitasya tyāgaḥ kartavyaḥ | vaikhānasād eva yaḥ pravrajet, tatrāpi sapatnīkapakṣe prāptasya dārasya śrāmaṇakāgnisahitasya vihitasya pañcayajñāder api tyāgaḥ kartavyaḥ |
kecin naiṣṭhikasyāpy anicayatvavidhānārtham ādāv abhidhānam iti varṇayanti |
tad ayuktaṃ, “guroḥ karmaśeṣeṇa japet” [GDh 3.6] iti japasyaiva pradhānatvenopadiṣṭatvāt, tenaivāgniparigrahāder apy abhāvo jñātuṃ śakyata eveti || 3.11 ||
nicayo dravyasaṃgrahas tadrahitaḥ syāt || 3.10 ||
ūrdhvaretāḥ || 3.12 || — || 3.11 ||
“uttareṣāṃ caitad avirodhi” [GDh 3.10] ity anena brahmacāriṇaḥ uktena brahmacaryarakṣaṇenāsyāpi siddher ūrdhvaretograhaṇaṃ yathā yathā retasaḥ kṣayo bhavati tathā tathopāyaḥ kartavya ity evamartham | upāyaś cālpānnābhyavahārādi | yathā cāha bhagavān vāsudevaḥ |
viṣayā vinivartante nirāhārasya dehinaḥ |
rasavarjaṃ raso ‘py asya paraṃ dṛṣṭvā nivartate || iti | [BhG 2.59] tathālpānnābhyavahārābhiprāyeṇa smṛtyantare ‘py uktam “aṣṭau grāsā muner bhakṣaḥ” [BDh 2.18.13] ityādi | evaṃ ca brahmacāriṇaḥ saṃpūrṇabhojane na doṣaḥ | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
anaḍvān brahmacārī ca āhitāgniś ca te trayaḥ |
bhuñjānā eva sidhyanti naiṣāṃ siddhir anaśnatām || [VaDh 6.21] iti || 3.12 ||
“uttareṣāṃ caitad avirodhi” [GDh 3.9] iti jitendriyatve siddhe ‘pi punar ūrdhvaretā iti retasaḥ srotobhaṅgo yathā bhavet tathā prayatetety evamartham || 3.11 ||
dhruvaśīlo varṣāsu || 3.13 || — || 3.12 ||
varṣāsv iti bahuvacanāt grāmād grāmāntaraṃ caturṣu māseṣu na gacched ity arthaḥ | ekatrāpi vasan na caṃkramaṇaprayāsaḥ syād iti śīlagrahaṇam | anenaivāsyāniketanatvaṃ sidhyati | tathā ca smṛtyantaram: “grāmānte devagṛhe śūnyāgāre vṛkṣamūle vā” [VaDh 10.13] iti | śīlagrahaṇaṃ ca kurvann etat jñāpayati, nāvaśyam evaṃ bhavet | tataś ca durbhikṣarāṣṭrabhraṃśādau gacched apīti || 3.13 ||
varṣāśabdo nityaṃ bahuvacanāntaḥ | varṣartau sati dhruvaśīlaḥ syād ekatra tiṣṭhed iti || 3.12 ||
bhikṣārthī grāmam iyāt || 3.14 || — || 3.13 ||
bhikṣā bhikṣyamāṇatvād annaṃ vāsaḥ kamaṇḍalvādiḥ, tadarthaṃ grāmaṃ gacchet | arthāc cheṣaṃ kālam araṇye devāyatanādau tiṣṭhed iti siddham || 3.14 ||
bhikṣākāla eva grāmaṃ praviśet | śeṣakālaṃ devālayādau vṛkṣamūleṣu vā vaset || 3.13 ||
brahmacāribhikṣākāle prāpta āha |
jaghanyam anivṛttaṃ caret || 3.15 || — || 3.14 ||
jaghanyaṃ bhojanakriyātaḥ paścād ity arthaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
vidhūme sannamusale vyaṅgāre bhuktavajjane |
vṛtte śarāvasaṃpāte bhikṣāṃ nityaṃ yatiś caret || iti | [MDh 6.56] anivṛttaṃ yasmin gṛhe pratyākhyātaṃ tasmin dine tad grahaṃ punar na praviśed ity arthaḥ | cared iti smṛtyantaropasaṃgrahaṇārtham | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “vidhūme sannamusale ekaśāṭhīparihito ‘jinena vā” [VaDh 10.8] iti || 3.15 ||
bhikṣākāle yad gṛham anupapattyā vilambitaṃ na tad bhūyas tadahaḥ praviśet | tatra manuḥ |
vidhūme sannamusale vyaṅgāre bhuktavajjane |

nivṛttāśīḥ || 3.16 || — || 3.15 ||
bhikṣādānābhivādanādāv āśiṣām aprayoktety arthaḥ | “hiṃsānugrahayor anārambhī” [GDh 3.25] ity anenaivaital labhyam iti cet, na, kriyārūpatvād anugrahasya, vāgātmakatvād āśiṣaḥ || 3.16 ||
adhikabhikṣālābhārthaṃ gṛheṣv āśīrvādaparo na syāt || 3.15 ||
vākcakṣuḥkarmasaṃyataḥ || 3.17 || — || 3.16 ||
brahmacāriprakaraṇe “vāgbāhūdarasaṃyataḥ” [GDh 2.28] ity anenaiva siddhe atiśayārthaṃ punarvacanam | ato vāksaṃyamo maunam, anyatra svādhyāyajapapathipraśnadharmayoga-praśnebhyaḥ,
dharmayogaṃ pathipraśnaṃ svādhyāyaṃ ca tathaiva ca |
bhikṣārthaṃ dehivacanaṃ na nindati yater api ||
iti smṛtyantaravacanāt | cakṣuḥsaṃyamaḥ, ātmano vyādher apīkṣaṇaṃ na kartavyam | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “upekṣakaḥ sarvabhūtānām” [VaDh 10.29] iti | karmasaṃyamaḥ yāvadvihitānuṣṭhānamātraṃ, kāmye pravṛttir mā bhūd iti || 3.17 ||
vāksaṃyamo maunam | cakṣuḥsaṃyamaḥ pādavikṣepapradeśād anyatra cakṣuṣor apravartanam | karmasaṃyamo bhikṣoś coditakarmānatikramaḥ | atra vāksaṃyamavirodhe tu smṛtyantaram |
dharmayogaṃ pathipraśnaṃ svādhyāyaṃ ca tathaiva ca |
bhikṣārthaṃ dehivacanaṃ na nindati yater api || iti || 3.16 ||
idānīm “anicayo bhikṣuḥ” [GDh 3.11] ityanena sarva-syaupacayikadravyasya parityāge sati pratiprasavaḥ kriyate |
kaupīnācchādanārthaṃ vāso bibhṛyāt || 3.18 ||
kaupīnācchādanārthe vāso bibhṛyāt || 3.17 ||
kaupīnām iti nagnatocyate | tadāvaraṇamātraṃ vāsaḥ paridadhyāt | evaṃ ca praśastadvijātibhyas tāvanmātrapratigrahe na doṣa | tataś ca,
abhayaṃ sarvabhūtebhyo datvā yas tu nivartate |
hanti jātān ajātāṃś ca pratigṛhṇāti yaḥ sadā ||
iti smṛtyantare yad doṣasaṃkīrtanaṃ tad asmād anyatreti draṣṭavyam || 3.18 ||
kaupīnam iti guhyapradeśasya nāma | tad ācchādyate yāvatā tāvad eva vāso bibhṛyāt | adhikaṃ tu prāvaraṇādi na bibhṛyāt || 3.17 ||
prahīṇam eke nirṇijya || 3.19 || — || 3.18 ||
eke manyante pratigrahadoṣaparihārārthaṃ prahīṇaṃ yad rathyādau tyaktaṃ tat prakṣālya paridadhyāt nirṇijyeti pratyātmikaśaucaprāpaṇārtham || 3.19 ||
eke manyante tad api kaupīnācchādanaṃ prahīṇaṃ jīrṇaṃ tathānyais tyaktaṃ prakṣālya bibhṛyāt || 3.18 ||
nāviprayuktam oṣadhivanaspatīnām aṅgam upādadīta || 3.20 || — || 3.19 ||
oṣadhayaḥ phalapākāntāḥ | apuṣpeṇa ye phalavantas te vanaspatayaḥ | ubhayagrahaṇena sarve sthāvarā api gṛhyante | teṣām aviprayuktaṃ tato ‘napagataṃ śākhāpatrādi na gṛhṇīyāt | “hiṃsānugrahayor anārambhī” [GDh 3.25] ity anena siddham iti cet, tatra jaṅgamavadha eva hiṃseti kecid vadanti | tadabhiprāyeṇoktatvād adoṣaḥ | upaśabdāt prayojayitāpi na syāt || 3.20 ||
vṛkṣalatādīnām aṅgaṃ phalapatrādyaviprayuktaṃ tato ‘pracyutaṃ nopādadīta na gṛhṇīyāt | svayaṃ patitaṃ tu gṛhṇīyāt || 3.19 ||
dhruvaśīlatvopadeśād varṣābhyo ‘nyatra kāmacāre prāpte āha|
na dvitīyām apartu rātriṃ grāme vaset || 3.21 || — || 3.20 ||
ṛtuśabdena varṣā evocyante | apagate ṛtau dvitīyāṃ rātriṃ grāme na vaset | ekasmin grāme dinadvayaṃ na vased ity arthaḥ | grāmagrahaṇād araṇye na doṣaḥ | apartugrahaṇād varṣāsu kadācit dvitīyām api vasato na doṣaḥ | varṣāśabdena hemantagrīṣmāv ucyete | tataś ca tasmin varṣākāle ekatraiva vased anyatra na vased ity arthasiddham || 3.21 ||
yatra varṣartau dhruvaśīlatoktā tam ṛtuṃ varjayitvā ṛtvantareṣu yatraikāṃ rātrim uṣitas tatra grāme na dvitīyāṃ vaset | grāmaikarātraḥ syād iti || 3.20 ||
muṇḍaḥ śikhī vā || 3.22 || — || 3.21 ||
jaṭilapratiṣedhārtha ārambhaḥ | icchāto vikalpaḥ
|| 3.22 ||
sarvān eva keśān saha śikayā vāpayet | śikhāvarjaṃ vāpayed vā | muṇḍaḥ śikhī veti vikalpenaikadaṇḍatridaṇḍagrahaṇavikalpo ‘py uktaḥ | atra śrutismṛtī |
agner iva śikhā nānyā yasya jñānamayī śikhā |
sa śikhīty ucyate vidvān netare keśadhāriṇaḥ || iti | [Brahma Up 87]
saśikhaṃ vapanaṃ kṛtvā bahiḥsūtraṃ tyajed budhaḥ | [Brahma Up 86]

śikhī yajñopavītī ca yad vā samyak prabodhitaḥ |
tridaṇḍagrahaṇaṃ kṛtvā bhikṣudharmaṃ samācaret || 3.21 ||
varjayed bījavadham || 3.23 || — || 3.22 ||

|| 3.23 ||

samo bhūteṣu || 3.24 ||
samo bhūteṣu hiṃsānugrahayoḥ || 3.23 ||
sarvaprāṇiṣu madhyasthaḥ syāt ātmaparapakṣavibhāga-varjaka ity arthaḥ || 3.24 ||
hiṃsāyām anugrahe ca bhūteṣu samo yo hinasti yo vānugṛhṇāti tatra tatra nirvikāra iti || 3.23 ||
hiṃsānugrahayor anārambhī || 3.25 ||
anārambhī || 3.24 ||
brahmacāriprakaraṇe “hiṃsām” [GDh 2.23] ity anenaiva siddhe atiśayārtham idaṃ punarvacanam | tataś cājñāte ‘pi prāṇivadhe ahar ahas tatparihārārthaṃ prāyaścittaṃ kartavyam | tathā cāha uśanā: “ajñātavadhaśuddhyarthaṃ rātrāv ahas trīn prāṇāyāmān dhārayet” iti | anugrahaśabdena sneha ucyate | tam api na kuryād ity arthaḥ || 3.25 ||
aihikaṃ pāratrikaṃ ca na kaṃcid ārambhaṃ kuryāt | yathāha āpastambaḥ: “aniho ‘namutraś caret” [ĀpDh 2.21.10] iti || 3.24 ||
bhikṣor uktvā idānīṃ vaikhānasadharmam āha |
atha vaikhānasasyāha |
vaikhānaso vane mūlaphalāśī tapaḥśīlaḥ || 3.26 || — || 3.25 ||
vaikhānasaśabdaḥ kṛtanirvacanaḥ | so ‘raṇye vasan mūlādyaśanaśīlaḥ syāt | na saṃskṛtam annam aśnīyād ity arthaḥ | tapaḥśīlaḥ śarīrapariśoṣaṇaśīlaḥ | evaṃ ca mūlaphalādy api svalpam evāśnīyāt | smṛtyantara-darśanāt ṣoḍaśakṛtva eva “vānaprasthasya ṣoḍaśa” [VaDh 6.20] iti || 3.26 ||
vaikhānaso vānaprastho vane vasan mūlāni phalāni ca pakvāni vāśnīyān na punar odanam | tapaḥ kāyapariśoṣaṇam | tataś ca mūlaphalāny api svalpāny evāśnīyād iti || 3.25 ||
śrāmaṇakenāgnim ādhāya || 3.27 || — || 3.26 ||
śrāmaṇako nāma vaikhānasānāṃ śāstram | tenāgnim ādhāya gṛhītvā sāyaṃ prātar homaṃ kuryād iti śeṣaḥ
|| 3.27 ||
śrāvaṇakaṃ nāma vaikhānasaṃ śāstram | taduktena prakāreṇāgnim ādhāya sāyaṃ prātar juhuyād iti śeṣaḥ
|| 3.26 ||
agrāmyabhojī || 3.28 || — || 3.27 ||
mūlaphalam api grāmyaṃ na bhakṣayet || 3.28 ||
phalamūlāny api grāmyāṇi na bhuñjīta || 3.27 ||
devapitṛmanuṣyabhūtarṣipūjakaḥ || 3.29 || — || 3.28 ||
grāmyānnasādhanāḥ gṛhasthasya pañca mahāyajñāḥ | asya cāgrāmyāhārapratiṣedhāt tannivṛttir mā bhūd ity ārambhaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
āraṇyair vividhair medhyaiḥ śākamūlaphalena vā |
etān eva mahāyajñān nirvaped vidhipūrvakam || [MDh 6.5] || 3.29 ||
vanyair eva phalamūlair ahar ahaḥ pañca mahāyajñān kuryāt | atra manuḥ |
āraṇyair vividhair medhyaiḥ śākamūlaphalena vā |
etān eva mahāyajñān nirvaped vidhipūrvakam || iti [MDh 6.5] || 3.28 ||
sarvātithiḥ pratiṣiddhavarjam || 3.30 || — || 3.29 ||
yāvad asya āśramaṃ gacchati tat sarvam asyātithir eva | “brāhmaṇasyānatithir abrāhmaṇaḥ” [GDh 5.43] ityādivarṇaniyamaḥ saṃkhyāniyamo vā nāstīty arthaḥ | pratiṣiddhāḥ stenādayaḥ pratilomajāś ca, tadvarjam
|| 3.30 ||
ya enam upāgacchanti te sarve ‘syātithayaḥ | na punar “brāhmaṇasyānatithir abrāḥmaṇaḥ” [GDh 5.39] ity ayaṃ niyamo ‘sti | tatrāpi stenapatitādīn varjayet pratiṣiddhavarjam || 3.29 ||
vaiṣkam apy upayuñjīta || 3.31 || — || 3.30 ||
viṣkair vyāghrādibhir hatasya paśor māṃsaṃ vaiṣkam | tad upayuñjīta | apiśabdo garhāyām | tataś cāpady evopayuñjīta || 3.31 ||
viṣkā duṣṭamṛgā vyāghrādayaḥ, tair hataṃ māṃsaṃ vaiṣkam | tad apy upayuñjīta | apiśabdo gauṇārthaḥ | phalamūlādyabhāve tad api bhakṣyam iti | tatrāpi “pañca pañcanakhā bhakṣyāḥ” ity etad vātiriktaṃ varjayitvā | “pratiṣiddhavarjam” iti padaṃ kākākṣinyāyenobhayatra saṃbadhyate || 3.30 ||
na phālakṛṣṭam adhitiṣṭhet || 3.32 || — 3.31 ||
araṇye ‘pi phālakṛṣṭapradeśaṃ nākrāmeta, āvāsaṃ tatra na kuryād ity arthaḥ | ākrāmed iti siddhe adhitiṣṭhed iti vacanaṃ pramādād ākramato doṣābhāvajñāpanārtham | kṛṣṭam iti siddhe phālagrahaṇaṃ kṣetrasamīpe vāsapratiṣedhārtham || 3.32 ||
araṇye vasan halena kṛṣṭaṃ pradeśaṃ nādhivaset
|| 3.31 ||
grāmaṃ ca na praviśet || 3.33 || — || 3.32 ||
aphālakṛṣṭenāpi mārgeṇa janavāsaṃ na praviśet | caśabdād grāmāntaram api || 3.33 ||
vane vasato ‘pi yādṛcchikopagrāmapraveśo niṣiddhaḥ
|| 3.32 ||
jaṭilaś cīrājinavāsāḥ || 3.34 || — || 3.33 ||
jaṭilaḥ keśī | jaṭila eva syād iti niyamārtha ārambhaḥ | cīraṃ darbhādiniṣpannam | idam adhovāso draṣṭavyam | ajinaṃ carma | uttarīyam idam | tathā ca smṛtyantaram: “cīravāsā ajinottarīyaś ca” iti || 3.34 ||
jaṭilaḥ keśaśmaśrulomanakhadhārī | cīraṃ darbhādinirmitaṃ vāsaḥ, ajinam uttarīyam, tathā ca smṛtyantare vyavasthādarśanāt || 3.33 ||
nātisaṃvatsaraṃ bhuñjīta || 3.35 || — || 3.34 ||
mūlaphalam api gatasaṃvatsaraṃ na bhuñjīta | tathā ca manuḥ: “tyajed āśvayuje māsi” [MDh 6.15] ityādi | evaṃ ca saṃvatsaramātranicayaḥ siddhaḥ || 3.35 ||
saṃvatsaram atikrāntam atisaṃvatsaram, tad āraṇyam api nāśnīyāt | atra manuḥ |
tyajed āśvajuye māsi hy utpannaṃ pūrvasaṃcitam |
jīrṇāni caiva vāsāṃsi puṣpamūlaphalāni ca || iti [MDh 6.15] || 3.34 ||
vaikhānasasyoktvā idānīm ekīyamatenāśramabādhapakṣaṃ vaktukāma āha |
uktā āśramās teṣāṃ vikalpasamuccayāv api darśitau | teṣāṃ prādhānyaṃ darśayati |
aikāśramyaṃ tv ācāryāḥ pratyakṣavidhānāt gārhasthasya gārhasthasya
|| 3.36 || — || 3.35 ||
ekāśrame bhavam aikāśramyam | tuśabdo ‘vadhāraṇe | gārhasthasyeti karmaṇi ṣaṣṭhī | gārhasthyam evācāryā icchanti | kutaḥ? pratyakṣavidhānāt upalabdhaśrutividhānād ity arthaḥ | yady api gṛhastho bhaved ity evaṃ na vihitam, tathāpy agnihotrādigṛhasthasādhyakriyāvidhānāt tasyaiva vidhānaṃ draṣṭavyam || 3.36 ||
tuśabdo viśeṣavācī | sarveṣu vedaśāstretihāsapurāṇeṣu gṛhasthadharmā evāgnihotrādayaḥ prācuryeṇa vidhīyante | tataḥ sarva evācāryā gārhasth[y]asyaikāśramyaṃ prādhānyaṃ manyante | tatrāśaktānām itarāśramadharmā vidhīyante | pratyakṣavidhānād itarāśramāṇāṃ pratyakṣeṇopajīvyatvāt | dviruktir vyākhyātā || 3.35 ||
atha caturtho ‘dhyāyaḥ
brahmacāryādīnāṃ dharmā uktāḥ, sāṃprathaṃ gṛhasthasya dharmā vaktavyā ity ata āha |
gṛhasthadharmā ucyante |
gṛhasthaḥ sadṛśīṃ bhāryāṃ vindetānanyapūrvāṃ yavīsasīm || 4.1 ||
gṛhastha iti bhāvisaṃjñayā vyapadeśaḥ, snātakasya yāvat pāṇigrahaṇaṃ tāvad aviruddhasya gṛhasthadharmasya praveśārtham | sadṛśī jātyā na kulataḥ |
vidyā praṇaṣṭā punar abhyupaiti
jātipraṇāśe tv iha sarvanāśaḥ ||
kulāpadeśena yathābhipūjyā
tasmāt kulīnāṃ striyam udvaheta ||
iti smṛtyantare utkṛṣṭakulāyā evādhigamaśravaṇāt | bhāryāṃ bharaṇīyāṃ lakṣaṇayuktām ity arthaḥ | vindeta yācetety arthaḥ | ananyapūrvām anyasya vācāpy adattām ity arthaḥ | yavīyasīm ātmano hīnavayasīm ity arthaḥ || 4.1 ||
gṛhastha iti bhāvisaṃjñāvyapadeśaḥ | atha vā gṛhasthasya ye dharmās te vivāhāt prāg api snātakasyāpi samā iti darśanārthaṃ ca | jātyā kulena ca sadṛśīm | anyasmai vācāpy adattām | avaravayasīm | evaṃbhūtāṃ bhāryāṃ vindeta udvahet || 4.1 ||
asyāpavādaḥ
asamānapravarair vivāhaḥ || 4.2 ||
samānapravaratvaṃ samānārṣeyatā, tadabhāvaḥ asamānapravaratvam, tair asmānapravarair vivāhaḥ kartavyaḥ | asamānaparvarām iti vaktavye evam abhidhānaṃ yadi mātṛpakṣataḥ sagotrā bhavati tadānīm api doṣābhāvajñāpanārtham | evaṃ ca pitṛpakṣata eva sagotrāpratiṣedhaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
asapiṇḍā ca yā mātur asagotrā ca yā pituḥ |
[MDh 3.5] ityādi || 4.2 ||
samāna ekaḥ pravaro yeṣāṃ taiḥ saha na vivāhaḥ | tad yathā haritakutsapiṅgaśaṅkhadarbhahaimakabhavānām āṅgirasāmbarīṣayauvanāś ceti | hārītaḥ kautsīṃ nodvahed ityādipravaraprapañca āpastambīye draṣṭavyaḥ || 4.2 ||
ūrdhvaṃ saptamāt pitṛbandhubhyaḥ || 4.3 ||
ūrdhvaṃ saptamāt pitṛbandhubhyo bījinaś ca mātṛbandhubhyaḥ pañcamāt || 4.3 ||
pitṛbāndhavāḥ pitur mātulabhaginyādayaḥ, tebhyaḥ saptamād ūrdhvaṃ vivāhaḥ | aṣṭamāt prabhṛti vā vivāhaḥ kartavya ity arthaḥ || 4.3 ||
pitaram ārabhya tadbandhuvarge gaṇyamāne saptamāc chirasa ūrdhvaṃ jātāṃ kanyakām udvahet | mātaram ārabhya tadbandhuvarge gaṇyamāne pañcamāc chirasa ūrdhvaṃ jātām udvahet | bījinaś ca saptamād ūrdhvam iti cakārāt sidhyati | yathā kṣetrī badhnyo rugṇo vā devaraṃ prārthayate mama kṣetre putram utpādayeti | yad vā saṃtānakṣaye vidhavāṃ guravo niyuñjate, dṛṣṭaṃ vicitravīryakṣetre satyavatīvākyād vyāso dhṛtarāṣṭrādīn utpāditavān iti | yathāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
aputreṇa parakṣetre niyogotpāditaḥ sutaḥ |
ubhayor apy asau rikthī piṇḍadātā ca dharmataḥ || iti | [YDh 2.127] tadviṣayam etad “bījinaś ca” iti || 4.3 ||
bījinaś ca || 4.4 ||
ūrdhvaṃ saptamād ity anukarṣaṇārthaś cakāraḥ | bījaṃ yat parakṣetre uptaṃ tasya jātamātrād eva | anenā-samānapravaratvena prāptau satyāṃ pratiṣedhaḥ || 4.4 ||
mātṛbandhubhyaḥ pañcamāt || 4.5 ||
ūrdhvaṃ vindeteti vartate | mātṛbāndhavāḥ mātulamātṛṣvasrādayaḥ | tatra pañcamād ūrdhvaṃ vivāhaḥ kartavyaḥ || 4.5 ||
idānīṃ vivāhavidhim āha |
atha vivāhabhedāḥ |
brāhmo vidyācāritrabandhuśīlasaṃpannāya dadyād ācchādyālaṃkṛtām || 4.6 || — || 4.4 ||
brāhma iti vivāhasaṃjñā | vidyā trayī | cāritraṃ coditakarmānuṣṭhānam | bandhuḥ praśastābhijanaḥ | śīlaṃ dharma evābhimukhatvam | saṃpannaśabdaḥ pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyate | ācchādya vastrayugalena varam, alaṃkṛtāṃ bhūṣaṇādibhiḥ, dadyād udakapūrveṇa | ācchādya alaṃkṛtām ity uttarasminn api vivāhatraye draṣṭavyam || 4.6 ||
vidyā vedavidyā | cāritraṃ coditakarmānuṣṭhānam | bandhavo jñātayo mātulādayaś ca | śīlaṃ vihiteṣu śraddhā | etair guṇaiḥ saṃpannāya vastrayugulenācchādya yathāvibhavam alaṃkṛtāṃ kanyāṃ dadyāt | evaṃvidhasya vivāhasya brāhmasaṃjñā || 4.4 ||
saṃyogamantraḥ prājāpatye saha dharmaś caryatām iti || 4.7 || — || 4.5 ||
prājāpatya iti vivāhasaṃjñā | tasmin prājāpatye vivāhe saṃyogārtho mantraḥ saṃyogamantraḥ, tasmin taṃ pradānakāle uktvā dadyāt | ko ‘sau mantraḥ | “saha dharmaś caryatām” iti || 4.7 ||
prājāpatyasaṃjñake vivāhe “saha dharmaś caryatām” iti pradānamantraḥ | yady api brāhmādiṣv api sahadharmacaryā bhavati tathāpy āntād anayā sahā dharmaś caritavyaḥ | nāśramāntaraṃ praveṣṭavyaṃ nāpi stryantaram upayantavyam iti mantreṇa samayaḥ kriyate | eṣa brāhmādeḥ prājāpatyasya viśeṣaḥ | ācchādyālaṃkṛtām iti samānam || 4.5 ||
ārṣe gomithunaṃ kanyāvate dadyāṭ || 4.8 || — || 4.9 ||
ārṣa iti vivāhasaṃjñā | tasmin purvaṃ vareṇa gomi-thune datte paścād ācchādyālaṃkṛtāṃ dadyāṭ || 4.8 ||
ārṣasaṃjñake vivāhe gomithunaṃ strīpūṃrūpaṃ kanyāvate dadyād varas tadbandhur vā kaścit | ācchādyālaṃkṛtām iti samānam || 4.6 ||
antarvedy ṛtvije dānaṃ daivaḥ || 4.9 ||
antarvedy ṛtvije dānaṃ daivo ‘laṃkṛtya || 4.7 ||
antarvedīti prakrāntayajñagrahaṇārtham | “ṛtvikśva-śura” [GDh 5.28] ityādau kriyottarakālam api ṛtvija iti vyavahāradarśanāt | nityadakṣiṇāto ‘dhikam iti jñāpa-nārthaṃ dānagrahaṇam | daiva iti vivāhasaṃjñā || 4.9 ||
antarvedi, vedyā [vedyāṃ?] dakṣiṇākāla ṛtvije karma kurvate yad alaṃkṛtya kanyāyā dānaṃ sa daivo vivāhaḥ | ācchādyālaṃkṛtām iti | prakṛte punar alaṃkṛtyeti vacanaṃ varasyāpy aṅgulīyakādibhir alaṃkārārtham || 4.7 ||
alaṃkṛtyecchantyāḥ svayaṃ saṃyogo gāndharvaḥ || 4.10 ||
icchantyāḥ svayaṃ saṃyogo gāndharvaḥ
|| 4.8 ||
alaṃkṛtya svayam evātmānam, vakṣyati – “utsṛjya pitryān alaṃkārān” [GDh 18.21] iti tadviṣayam etad iti | kāmayamānā svayam eva vareṇa saṃyujyeta, sa gāndharvo vivāhaḥ || 4.10 ||
icchantyā vadhvā icchato varasya saṃyogo gāndharvo vivāhaḥ | svayam iti vacanād varecchā gamyate || 4.8 ||
vittenānatiḥ strīmatām āsuraḥ || 4.11 || — || 4.9 ||
yatra dhanadānenānamanaṃ kanyāvatāṃ kriyate, sa āsuro nāma vivāhaḥ | kanyāvatām iti vaktavye strīgrahaṇaṃ kanyādānaphalanivṛttyartham || 4.11 ||
yatra strīmatāṃ kanyāvatāṃ pitrādīnāṃ vittena dhanapradānenānatir ārjanaṃ kriyate sa āsuro vivāhaḥ | atra yājñavalkyaḥ: “āsuro dravaṇādānād” [YDh 1.61] iti | manuś ca |
jñātibhyo draviṇaṃ datvā kanyāyai ca svaśaktitaḥ |
kanyāpradānaṃ svāc chandyād āsuro dharma ucyate || [MDh 3.31]
strīmatām iti vacanān na kevalaṃ kanyāyai dhanapradānam āsuratvanibandhanam | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
yāsāṃ nādadate śulkaṃ jñātayo na sa vikrayaḥ |
arhaṇaṃ tat kumārīṇām ānṛśaṃsyān na kevalam || iti || 4.9 ||
prasahyādānād rākṣasaḥ || 4.12 || — || 4.10 ||

anulomāḥ anantaraikāntaradvyantarāsu jātāḥ savarṇāmbaṣṭhograniṣādadauṣyantapāraśavāḥ || 4.16 ||

anulomāḥ varṇānupūrvyeṇotpannāḥ | brāhmaṇa-syānantarā kṣatriyā, ekāntarā vaiśyā, dvyantarā śūdrā, kṣatriyasyānantarā vaiśyā, ekāntarā śūdrā, tasya dvyantarā nāsti | vaiśyasyānantarā śūdrā, ekāntarā tasya nāsti | tataś ca brāhmaṇenānantarāyāṃ jātaḥ savarṇaḥ | kṣatriyeṇānantarāyāṃ jāta ambaṣṭhaḥ | vaiśyenānanta-rāyāṃ jāta ugraḥ | brāhmaṇenaikāntarāyāṃ jātaḥ niṣādaḥ | kṣatriyeṇaikāntarāyāṃ jāto dauṣyantaḥ | brāhmaṇena dvyantarāyāṃ jātaḥ pāraśavaḥ || 4.16 ||
brāhmaṇasyānantarā kṣatriyā tasyāṃ jātaḥ savarṇaḥ | kṣatriyasya vaiśyā tasyāṃ tasmād ambaṣṭhaḥ | vaiśya-syānantarā śūdrā tasyāṃ tasmād ugrah | brāhmaṇasyai-kantarā vaiśyā tasyāṃ tasmān niṣādaḥ | kṣatriyasyai-kantarā śūdrā tasyāṃ tasmād dauṣmantaḥ | brāhma-ṇasya dvyantarā śūdrā tasyāṃ tasmāt pāraśavaḥ | pra-pañco jātinirṇayasya smṛtyantare draṣṭavyaḥ || 4.14 ||
prātilomyena jātān āha |

varṇaśabdenotpādakabījam ucyate | yo varṇa utpādakaḥ tadvarṇaḥ saṃbhaved ity arthaḥ | saptamena yugaparivartanena | antaraśabdena kṣetram ity ucyate | tataś ca kramo draṣṭavyaḥ, utkarṣeṇotpādakavarṇatvam apakarṣeṇa kṣetravarṇatvam iti | tataḥ strītaḥ utkarṣo mantavyaḥ | puruṣād apakarṣaś ca | tad yathā — brāhmaṇena kṣatriyāyāṃ mūrdhāvasiktā jāyate | sāpi mūrdhāvasiktā tatrotkṛṣṭabījaguṇā brāhmaṇenaiva duhitaram utakṛṣṭabījaguṇāṃ janayati, sāpi brāhmaṇenaivety evaṃ saptame yuge brāhmaṇy eva bhavatīty evam utkarṣaḥ | tathā brāhmaṇena kṣatriyāyāṃ mūrdhāvasikto jāyate | sa kṣatriyayā saṃbadhyamānaḥ pūrvavat kṣatriya eva bhavatīty apakarṣaḥ | evaṃ śūdrāpatyādāv api sarvatra draṣṭavyam | tathā ca manuḥ |
śūdrāyāṃ brāhmaṇāj jātaḥ śreyasā cet prajāyate |
aśreyāṃś chreyasīṃ jātiṃ prāpnoty ā saptamād yugāt ||
śūdro brāhmaṇatām eti brāhmaṇaś caiti śūdratām |
kṣatriyāj jātam evaṃ ca vidyād vaiśyāt tathaiva ca || [MDh 10.64–65]  iti || 4.22 ||
manyanta iti vākyaśeṣaḥ | teṣām eva savarṇādīnām anulomajātānām utkarṣeṇa pitṛdvārā saptamapuruṣād utkṛṣṭavarṇāntaraprāptir bhavati | apakarṣeṇa mātṛdvārā pañcamapuruṣād apakṛṣṭavarṇāntaraprāptir bhavati | tad yathā – brāhmaṇenoḍhāyāṃ kṣatriyāyām utpāditā savarṇā sāpi brāhmaṇenoḍhā tasyām utpāditā cety evam ā saptamyāḥ saptamī tu brāhmaṇenoḍhā yad apatyaṃ sūte tad brāhmaṇajātīyam eva bhavati | evaṃ brāhmaṇena kṣatriyāyām utpāditaḥ putraḥ savarṇaḥ so ‘pi kṣatriyām udvāhya putram utpādayati so ‘pi kṣatriyām ity evam ā pañcamāt pañcamas tu kṣatriyāyāṃ yad apatyam utpādayati tat kṣatriyajātīyam eva bhavati | vikalpasyaivaṃ cārthaḥ | tatrāpi varṇāntaragamane vṛttasvādhyāyabāhulye sati pañcamenotkṛṣṭaṃ bhavati | hīnavṛttyā pañcamenāpakṛṣṭaṃ ca bhavatīti | evaṃ kṣatriyasya vaiśyāyāṃ vaiśyasya śūdrāyām api draṣṭavyam || 4.18 ||
pañcamenācāryāḥ || 4.23 ||
pañcamenaiva yugaparivartanena varṇāntaragamanaṃ bhavatīti kecid ācāryā varṇayanti | idam api vṛttāpekṣayā draṣṭavyam || 4.23 ||
sṛṣṭyantarajānāṃ ca || 4.24 || — || 4.19 ||
sṛṣṭiś catvārā varṇāḥ | tadantaraṃ mūrdhāvasiktādiḥ | tebhyo jātāḥ sṛṣṭyantarajāḥ | varṇāntaragamanam utkarṣāpakarṣābhyāṃ saptamena pañcamenācāryā ity anukarṣaṇārthaś cakāraḥ | tataś ca tatrāpy anulomapratilomān kalpayitvā utpādakakṣetra-jātyantaragamaṇaṃ draṣṭavyam || 4.24 ||
cāturvarṇyam antareṇa cānulomajātānāṃ savarṇāmbaṣṭhādīnām apy utkarṣāpakarṣābhyām anyonyavarṇāntaragamanaṃ bhavati | tad yathā – savarṇenoḍhāyām ambaṣṭhyām utpāditā duhitā punaḥ savarṇenohyate | tasyām apy utpāditā savarṇenety ā saptamāt saptamī tu savarṇenoḍhā yad apatyaṃ sa eva bhavati | evaṃ saivāmbaṣṭhenoḍhāyāṃ duhitaraṃ sūte sāpy ambaṣṭheneti saptamī tv ambaṣṭhenoḍhā yad apatyam utpādayati so ‘mbaṣṭha eva bhavati | evam ambaṣṭhayor api draṣṭavyam || 4.19 ||
pratilomāt tu dharmahīnaḥ || 4.25 || — || 4.20 ||
H reads: pratilomās
tuśabdo viśeṣārthaḥ, sṛṣṭer api pratilomāj jātaḥ sūtādiḥ dharmahīnaḥ tadantare pratilomāj jāto viśeṣata eveti | dharmaśabdenopanayanaṃ draṣṭavyam | evaṃ ca anulomānām upanayanaṃ siddham | vedādhyayane tu pratiṣedho draṣṭavyaḥ | kutaḥ – “anulomānām upanayanam eva na vedādhyayanaṃ na pañcayajñaṃ naupāsanaṃ nāgnihotraṃ na samitkāryaṃ na saṃdhyopāsanam upanayanam api tūṣṇīm eva” ityādismṛtyantaradarśanāt |
kecic cakāraṃ na paṭhanti | teṣām uttarasūtrāt “śūdrāyāṃ ca” [GDh 4.26] ity ataś cakāram adhyāhṛtya yojanīyam “sṛṣṭyantarajānām” [GDh 4.24] ity asmin sūtre |
apare “sṛṣṭyantarajānāṃ pratilomāt tu dharmahīnaḥ” ity ekam evedaṃ sūtram iti vyācakṣate |
sarvatra vyākhyānaṃ tulyam eva || 4.25 ||
pratilomāj jātāḥ sūtādayo dharmahīnā upanayanādi-dharmahīnāḥ | tatra sūtasyaikasyopanayanamātraṃ śāstrāntare ‘ṅgīkṛtam || 4.20 ||
śūdrāyāṃ ca || 4.26 || — || 4.21 ||
anulomānām api śūdrāyāṃ jāta upanayanādirahitaḥ | eṣa ca savarṇāmbaṣṭhaniṣādānām evopanayanam | tathā ca smṛtyantaram: “savarṇam upanīya dharnurvedaṃ śikṣayet, ambaṣṭham upanīya vaidyaśāstram, niṣādam upanīya hastisikṣāṃ śikṣayet, anyeṣām upanayanā-bhāvaḥ” iti | kecid ambaṣṭhāyāṃ savarṇāj jātam upanīya nṛttaṃ śikṣayed iti varṇayanti | ugrādayo vṛttiviśeṣeṇa jñātavyāḥ | ugro hantā vyālamṛgādīnām, dauṣyanto matsyaghātakaḥ, pāraśavo bhadrakālī-pūjakaḥ, sūto hayaśikṣakaḥ, māgadho vaṇikpathasevī, paṭṭakṛd āyogavaḥ, kṣattā cākrikaḥ, sa eva lavaṇavikretā, vaidehako ‘japālaḥ ityādi || 4.26 ||
ānulomyenāpi śūdrāyām utpannaḥ pāraśavādir dharmahīnaḥ | evaṃ ca savarṇādīnām anulomānāṃ siddho dharmāṅgīkāraḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
svajātijātyantarajāḥ ṣaṭ sutā dvijadharmiṇaḥ |

asamānāyāṃ ca śūdrāt patitavṛttiḥ || 4.27 ||

antyaḥ pāpiṣṭhaḥ || 4.28 || — || 4.23 ||

tatrānulomānām upanayanavidhānāt, anyāyenotpannānāṃ tadayogyatvāt yady api “sadṛśīṃ bhāryāṃ vindeta” [GDh 4.1] ity atra samānajātiṣv eva coditaḥ, tathāpi prathamaniviṣṭasya nikṛṣṭajātiṣv api krameṇa vivāho ‘stīti jñāpayati | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “tisro brāhmaṇasya bhāryā varṇānupūrvyeṇa dve rājanyasya ekaikā vaiśyaśūdrayoḥ” [VaDh 1.24] iti | “pratilomaśūdrābhyāṃ jāto dharmahīnaḥ” iti vaktavye “śūdrāyāṃ ca” [GDh 4.26] iti pṛthak sūtrakaraṇāt śūdrāvivāhasyāpi kathaṃcid yogyatāṃ darśayati | tataś ca prathamam uttamajātiṣūtpannaputrasya kāminaḥ śūdrāvivāho ‘pi mantravarjo draṣṭavyaḥ | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “śūdrām apy eke mantravarjam” [VaDh 1.25] iti | tatrāpiśabdasya nindārthatvād utpannaputrasyeti tatrārthaḥ | “pratilomāt tu dharmahīnaḥ” [GDh 4.25] ity anena nikṛṣṭasyottamāvivāhābhāvaṃ darśayati | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ |
channotpannās tu ye kecit prātilomyaguṇāśritāḥ |
