H reads daśa parān
brāhmavivāhenotpanno brāhmaḥ putraḥ | atikrāntān daśa bhaviṣyāṃś ca daśa punāti | upanyāsakramaṃ vihāya vyutkrameṇa phalābhidhānaṃ kanyādātur api pratiphalabhūyastvadarśanārtham, yathā jābālaśrutiḥ: “yaḥ saṃpannāya putrīṃ dadyāt so ‘gniṣṭomaphalam avāpnoti” iti | tathā kauṣītakabrāhmaṇam api: “tasya putrā dāyam upayanti suhṛdaḥ sādhukṛtyā dviṣantaḥ pāpakṛtyām” iti || 4.33 ||
brāhmavivāhenoḍhā brāhmī tasyāṃ jātaḥ putraḥ pitrādīn daśa pūrvān daśa parān bhaviṣyataḥ putrādīṃś ca daśātmānaṃ caikaviṃśaṃ punāti | tasmād brāhmo vivāhaḥ praśastatamaḥ || 4.27 ||
atha pañcamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
apatyotpādanārthaṃ karmārthaṃ ca bhāryāpariṇayanam uktam | tatra pūrvam apatyotpādanavidhim āha |
garbhādhānakālaṃ prastauti |
ṛtāv upeyāt || 5.1 ||
ṛtur garbhagrahaṇasāmarthyena strīgatenopalakṣitaḥ kālaḥ ṣoṣaśāhorātraḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
ṛtuḥ svābhāvikaḥ strīṇāṃ rātrayaḥ ṣoḍaśa smṛtāḥ |
caturbhir itaraiḥ sārdham ahobhiḥ sadvigarhitaiḥ || iti | [MDh 3.46] | tasmin upeyād upagacchet, prakṛtāṃ bhāryām, praharṣayan na tu paśuvat, upopasargayogāt |
nāyaṃ vidhiḥ, kāmata evārthaprāptatvāt | nāpi niyamaḥ | ko ‘sau niyamaḥ? pakṣe prāptasya punarvacanaṃ niyamaḥ, icchātaḥ pravṛttiḥ, ṛtau gamane cāgamane ca prāpte ṛtāv upeyād eveti | asmin pakṣe gṛhīte anṛtau na vidhir na pratiṣedha iti pravṛttir eva bhavati marīcādivat | marīcaṃ bhakṣayen na bhakṣayed iti na kvacid dharmaśāstre śrūyate | tathāpi śiṣṭā bhakṣayanti, tadvad atrāpi pravṛttir eva bhavatīti “sarvatra vā” [GDh 5.2] ity asyānārambhaḥ prāpnoti, agamane prāyaścittaṃ ca |
nanu cobhayam apīṣyate, “yatra tu prītyupalabdhitaḥ pravṛttiḥ na tatra śāstram asti, tadanuvartamāno narakāya rādhyati” itīcchātaḥ pravṛttau narakapātaśravaṇāt, tannivāraṇārthaḥ “sarvatra vā” [GDh 5.2] ity asyārambhaḥ | smṛtyantare agamane prāyaścittaṃ śrūyate |
ṛtusnātāṃ tu yo bhāryāṃ saṃnidhau nopagacchati | [=BDh 4.1.18]
ghorāyāṃ bhrūṇahatyāyāṃ yujyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || iti |
naitad evam | “yatra tu prītyupalabdhitaḥ pravṛttiḥ” ityādes tāvad ayam arthaḥ | yatra yasminn anuṣṭhāne prītyupalabdhitaḥ pravṛttiḥ phalaṃ jñātvā tatprītyā pravṛttiḥ, phalābhisaṃdhinā pravṛttir ity arthaḥ | tasyāṃ pravṛttau na śāstram asti atyantaṃ na phalatīty arthaḥ | tadanuvartamānaḥ phalābhisaṃdhinānu-ṣṭhānaṃ kurvan narakāya rādhyati narakaṃ gacchatīti | saṃdhyopāsanādeḥ phalābhisaṃdhityāgenānuṣṭhā-nārtham idaṃ vacanam | tathā ca bhagavān vāsuvevaḥ “mā karmaphalahetur bhūḥ” [BhG 2.47] ityādi | yadi sarvatra prītipravṛttinivāraṇaṃ syāt tadānīṃ marīcādīnām api śāstraṃ vaktavyam, śāstreṇa vinā narakapatanaṃ bhavatīti | tasmāt “sarvatra vā” [GDh 5.2] ity etad api śāstreṇānugrahārthaṃ na vaktavyam | yac coktam “ṛtusnātāṃ tu yo bhāryām” [BDh 4.1.18] ityādi, tad api ṛtusnātāyā bhāryāyā ātmadarśanārthaṃ vacanam | kutaḥ? jābāliśrutidarśanāt | evaṃ ca śrūyate: “ṛtusnātā bhāryā yaṃ pūrvaṃ paśyet tādṛśaṃ putraṃ janayati | tasmāt saṃnidhau bhartaiva prathamam ātmānaṃ darśayet” iti | tataś cāgamane prāyaścittābhāvāt niyamena doṣābhāvaḥ prāpnoti | prāptasya punarvacanaṃ niyama iti yeṣāṃ pakṣaḥ, teṣāṃ niyamāśaṅkāpi nopapadyate, ṛtugamanasyaiva prāptyabhāvāt, icchātaḥ pravṛttiḥ sarvatra bhavatīti |
tasmāt parisaṃkhyārtham idaṃ sūtram | kā ca parisaṃkhyā? tatra cānyatra ca prāptau vacanam | icchātaḥ pravṛtteḥ ṛtāv anṛtau ca prāptau satyām ṛtāv eva gamanaṃ kartavyam iti | idam apy anarthakam “sarvatra vā” ity ārambhād iti cet, na, vyavasthita-vikalpārthatvāt | vyavasthā caiva draṣṛṭavyā – yadi putrārthaiva pravṛttiḥ tadānīm ṛtāv evopeyāt | ratyarthā cet “sarvatra vā” it | ṛṇatrayaśrutyā putrotpādanārtham anuṣṭhānārthaṃ ca bhāryāgrahaṇaṃ na ratyarthm iti cen, na “ratiputraphalā nārī” iti smṛtyantaradarśanāt, “yā syād anaticāreṇa ratiḥ sā dharmasaṃśritā” iti [VaDh 12.23] vāsiṣṭhadarśanāt, “svadāratuṣṭa eva bhavet” ity auśanasadarśanāt, “api nāryo vijaniṣya-māṇāḥ patibhiḥ saha śayīran” iti vājasaneyaśruti-darśanāt, “virakto bhikṣur bhaved itaro gārhasthyaṃ praviśet” iti ca smṛtyantaradarśanāt,
kāmatas tu pravṛttānām imā syuḥ kramaśo ‘varāḥ | [MDh 3.12] iti manudarśanāt, ṛṇatrayaśrutyāpi vaikalpikācāryapakṣa evānṛtau brahmacaryopadeśāt | samuccayapakṣe tu yajñaprajābhyām ity udāharaṇatvenopapadyamānatvāt, gārhasthya-codanāyāḥ smṛtivirodho mā bhūd iti | tasmād anaṣṭarāgasya ratyartham api dāragrahaṇam asty eva | tataś ca yadi jāyāpatī viraktau bhavataḥ gamanaṃ ca putrārtham eva tadānīm ṛtāv evopeyāt | saraktayoḥ “sarvatra vā” ity upapadyata iti | tarhi ṛtāv upagamanam akurvato doṣābhāvaḥ prāpnoti, sa doṣa iti cen, na asmābhir api doṣābhāvasyaiveṣyamāṇatvāt | evam api putrārthina anṛtau gamanaṃ kurvataḥ prāyaścittaṃ prāpnoti, sa doṣa iti cen, na tasyaiva parisaṃkhyāphalatvāt | yeṣāṃ śāstraprāptasya punarvacanaṃ parisaṃkhyeti teṣām apy upapadyate | tad yathā – “jātaputro ‘gnīn ādadhīta,” “tribhir ṛṇavān jāyate” iti śruteḥ, “punanti sādhavaḥ putrāḥ” iti ca smṛteḥ | putrotpādanārthaṃ garbhagrahaṇasāmarthyād ṛtāv eva gamane prāpte anutpannaputrasya punar idaṃ vacanam anṛtukālīnagamanapratiṣedhaparaṃ vijñāyata iti | tasmāt parisaṃkhyaiveyaṃ yady upeyād ṛtāv eveti |
nanu yadi putrārthaṃ gamanaṃ tadā utpannaputrasya na prāpnoti iti |
ucyate – bahuputrasyābhīṣṭatvāt | yathāha śaṅkhaḥ |
putrapautrapratiṣṭhasya bahvapatyasya jīvataḥ |
ityādi | tathā ca śrutiḥ: “daśāsyāṃ putrān ādhehi patim ekādaśaṃ kṛdhi” iti | tasmāt parisaṃkhyārthaṃ sūtram iti sthitam || 5.1 ||
rajodarśanād ārabhya ṣoḍaśāhorātrā ṛtuḥ strīṇāṃ garbhagrahaṇakālas tatropagacched bhāryām | tatra āpastambīye viśeṣaḥ: “caturthīprabhṛty ā ṣoḍaśīm uttarām uttarāṃ yugmāṃ prajāniḥśreyasam ṛtugamanam ity upadiśanti” | mānavaṃ tu |
ṛtuḥ svābhāvikaḥ strīṇāṃ rātrayaḥ ṣoḍhaśa smṛtāḥ |
caturbhir etaiḥ sārdham ahobhiḥ sadvigarhitaiḥ ||
tāsām ādyāś catasras tu nindyā ekādaśī tu yā |
trayodaśī ca śeṣās tu praśastā daśa rātrayaḥ || [MDh 3.46–47]
amāvāsyām aṣṭamīṃ ca paurṇamāsīṃ caturdaśīm |
brahamacārī bhaven nityam apy ṛtau snātako dvijaḥ || [MDh 4.128] iti | yājñavalkyas tu |
evaṃ gacchan striyaṃ kṣāmāṃ maghāṃ mūlaṃ ca varjayet | [YDh 1.80]
yugmāsu putrā jāyante striyo ‘yugmāsu rātriṣu | [MDh 3.48] iti ca | tad iha ṣoḍaśasu rātriṣv āditas tisraḥ sarvathā varjyāḥ | itarāsu gacched iti sarvasmṛticoditaniṣedān parityajya gacchann utkṛṣṭaṃ putraṃ janayati | dveṣādinā ṛtāv anupayan pratyaveyād iti | tathā ca devalaḥ |
yaḥ svadārān ṛtusnātān svasthaḥ san nopagacchati |
bhrūṇahatyām avāpnoti garbhaṃ prāptaṃ vināśya saḥ || iti | smṛtyantaraṃ ca |
ṛtusnātāṃ tu yo bhāryāṃ saṃnidhau nopagacchati |
tasyā rajasi taṃ māsaṃ pitaras tasya śerate || iti |
ayaṃ tu rāgataḥ prāptatve saty apy akaraṇe pratyavāya-śravaṇād dvividhaś ca bhavati | ṛtāv evopeyad eveti kasyacin matena niyamaś ca bhavati || 5.1 ||
ācāryas tu parisaṃkhyānaṃ ca pratipādayati |
sarvatra vā pratiṣiddhavarjam || 5.2 ||
parvādīni pratiṣiddhāni | yathāha manuḥ |
amāvāsyām aṣṭamīṃ ca paurṇamāsīṃ caturdaśīm |
brahmacārī bhaven nityam apy ṛtau snātako dvijaḥ || iti | [MDh 4.128] vāśabdo vyavasthitavikalpārthaḥ |
anye tu vyācakṣate – “ṛtāv upeyād” [GDh 5.1] ity anena ṛtāv eva sakṛd gamanaṃ codyate | “sarvatra vā” ity anena sarvasmin kāle sakṛd gamanam iti |
tad ayuktam “ṛtāv upeyād dvir ekato vā anṛtau kāmataḥ” iti śrutidarśanāt || 5.2 ||
sarveṣu vā kāleṣūpeyād ṛtāv anṛtau ca pratiṣiddhadivasān varjayitvā |
yathākāmī bhaved vāpi strīṇāṃ varam anusmaran | iti | [YDh 1.81] varaś ca – kāmam ā vijanitoḥ saṃbhavāmeti | yad apy ātmano jitendriyatve saty api “dharmadārāḥ sarvadā rakṣaṇīyāḥ” iti smaryate | “apramattā rakṣata tantum enaṃ mā vaḥ kṣetre parabījāni vāpsuḥ” [VaDh 17.9] iti || 5.2 ||
atha pañca mahāyajñāḥ |
devapitṛmanuṣyarṣibhūtapūjakaḥ || 5.3 ||
ete pañca mahāyajñāḥ | tatra devapūjakaśabdena vaiśvadevam ucyate | pitṛpūjaka iti pitrarthaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ bhojayen nityam iti | tathā ca manuḥ |
ekam apy āśayed vipraṃ pitrarthaṃ pāñcayajñike |
na caivātrāśayet kiṃcid vaiśvadevaṃ prati dvijam || iti | [MDh 3.83] | annāsaṃbhave payo dadyāt, tadasaṃbhave mūlam, tadasaṃbhave phalam, sarvāsaṃbhave udakam | tathāha manuḥ |
dadyād ahar ahaḥ śrāddham annādyenodakena vā |
payomūlaphalair vāpi pitṛbhya prītim āvahan || iti | [MDh 3.82] | tatrāpi sarvāsaṃbhave udakam iti draṣṭavyam | manuṣyapūjaka iti atithipūjanam ucyate | tatrāpy annāsaṃbhave āsanārthaṃ tṛṇaṃ dadyāt, tadasaṃbhave saṃveśanārthaṃ bhūmim, śītāpano-dārtham agnim, paṅkāpakarṣaṇārtham udakam, madhurāṃ ca vāṇīm | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “tṛṇabhūmy-agnyudakavāksūnṛtānasūyāḥ satāṃ geheṣu nocchidyante kadācana” [VaDh 13.61] iti | svayam api ca vakṣyati atithipūjanam | taccheṣaḥ vasiṣṭhoktaḥ draṣṭavyaḥ | bhūtapūjaka iti vaiśvadevottarakāla-digdevatābhyo baliharaṇam | ṛṣipūjaka iti nityasvādhyāyaḥ | agniparigrahaṇāt prāg eṣām abhidhānam anagnikasyāpi laukike ‘gnau prāptyartham | tataś ca śūdrasya pākayajñapakṣe ‘pi laukike ‘gnau draṣṭavyam | tathā avakīrṇihomaś ca || 5.3 ||
atra pūjakaśabdo devādiṣu pratyekaṃ saṃbadhyate | dvandvāntyaṃ śrūyamāṇaṃ pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyate | gṛhastho nityaṃ devādipūjakaḥ syāt | tatra devapūjā vaiśvadevasakalahomādyagnikāryaṃ ca | pitṛpūjāṃ manur āha |
ekam apy āśayed vipraṃ pitrarthe pāñcayājñike |
na caivātrāśayet kiṃcid vaiśvadevaṃ prati dvijam || [MDh 3.83] iti |
dadyād ahar ahaḥ śrāddham annādyenodakena ca |
payomūlaphalair vāpi pitṛbhyaḥ prītim āvahan || [MDh 3.82] iti ca | manuṣyapūjā atithipūjā | bhūtapūjā baliharaṇam | ṛṣipūjā brahmayajñaḥ | ṛṣiyajño brahmayajña iti paryāyaḥ || 5.3 ||
te ca pratipādyante | atra kramo na vivakṣitaḥ | brahmayajñas tu |
nityasvādhyāyaḥ || 5.4 ||
nityasvādhyāya iti brahmayajño ‘bhidhīyate | pūrvam ṛṣipūjaka ity anenābhihita eva itarānuṣṭhānāśaktāv api sa nityaṃ viguṇo ‘pi kartavyaḥ | pūrvoktenaiva prakāreṇa ṛṣiyajña iti vaktavye smṛtyantaroktasya vidheḥ prāpaṇārtham evam abhidhānam | tathā cāha uśanā: “atha brahmayajñe darbheṣv āsīno darbhān dhārayamāṇaḥ prāṅmukha udaṅmukho vā praṇava-vyāhṛtisāvitrīpūrvaṃ vedānām ekadeśaṃ paṭhet vedānām ādiṃ vā ṛgyajuḥsāmnām eka vā trir vā praṇavam aitihāsikaṃ ślokaṃ vā samāhitaḥ” ityādi
|| 5.4 ||
bahuvrīhir ayam | tatprakāraś ca “brahmayajñena yakṣyamāṇa” ity ārabhya taittirīyake ‘bhihitaḥ | atra nityaśabdād brahmayajñavyatiriktakāle ‘pi yathāvasaram adhīyīteti darśitam || 5.4 ||
pitṛyajñas tu |
pitṛbhyaś codakadānam || 5.5 ||
pitṛbhyaś codakadānaṃ yathotsāham
anyat|| 5.5 ||
cakārād devatānām ṛṣīṇāṃ ca | udakam iti siddhe dānagrahaṇaṃ tarpaṇapūrvatvajñāpanārtham | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
devatānām ṛṣīṇāṃ ca tarpayitvā samāhitaḥ |
paścād dadyāt pitṝṇāṃ ca svadhāpūrvaṃ tilaiḥ saha || iti | nityaṃ pitṛbhya uadkadānacodanayaiva ahar ahaḥ snānam apy arthāt siddham, tatpūrvakatvād udakadānasya | tathā ca manuḥ |
nityaṃ snātvā śuciḥ kuryād devarṣipitṛtarpaṇam | iti | [MDh 2.176] | udakadānaṃ dvividham, yad apsu dīyate yad ākāśe ceti |
nanu ca bhūmyām eva codyate, nāpsu, yathāha bhṛguḥ |
nodakeṣu na pātreṣu na kruddho naikapāṇinā |
nopatiṣṭhati tat toyaṃ yad bhūmyāṃ na pradīyate || iti | tathā bṛhaspatir api |
udake nodakaṃ dadyāt pitṛbhyas tu kadācana |
uttīrya tu śucau deśe kuryād udakatarpaṇam || iti |
naitad evam – smṛtyantare viruddhābhidhānāt, viṣayavibhāgakathanāc ca | yathā cāha uśanā |
āpo devagaṇāḥ sarve āpaḥ pitṛgaṇāḥ smṛtāḥ |
tasmād apsu jalaṃ deyaṃ pitṝṇāṃ dattam akṣayam || iti | tatra kātyāyano viṣayavibhāgam āha |
devānāṃ ca pitṝṇāṃ ca jale dadyāj jalāñjalim |
asaṃskṛtapramītānāṃ sthale dadyāj jalāñjalim || iti | tarpaṇādyudakadānāntam ubhābhyāṃ hastābhyāṃ draṣṭavyam | udakadāne tāvad adoṣaḥ, jalāñjalim iti vacanāt | tarpaṇe tu nopapadyate, naikapāṇinety asya viruddhavākyaśravaṇāt | tathā ca bhṛguḥ |
ubhābhyām api hastābhyām udakaṃ tarpayan dvijaḥ |
sa mūḍho narakaṃ yāti kālasūtram avākchirāḥ || iti |
naiṣa doṣaḥ, vibhāgavākyaśravaṇāt | tathā cāha bṛhaspatiḥ |
śrāddhe secanakālaś cet ekapāṇiṃ pradāpayet |
tarpaṇe tūbhayaṃ kuryād eṣa dharmaḥ sanātanaḥ || iti | tasmād ekam apsv eva codyate | ākāśe yac codyate tad aparam | yathāha bhṛguḥ |
nābhimātre jale sthitvā cintayann ūrdhvamānasaḥ |
āgacchantu me pitara imaṃ gṛhṇantv apo’ñjalim ||
tris trir añjalim ākāśe uccair uccataraṃ budhaḥ |
uktvā coktvā kṣiped vāri vāgyato dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ ||
pitṝṇāṃ sthānam ākāśaṃ dakṣiṇā dik tathaiva ca |
tasmāt tathā sadā dattaṃ pitṝṇāṃ syāt tad akṣayam || iti | pitṝṇāṃ yad dīyate tat sarvaṃ tilair darbhaiś ca saha draṣṭavyam, yathāha bhṛguḥ |
tiladarbhayutaṃ vāri svadhayā yat pradīyate |
tat sarvam amṛtaṃ bhūtvā pitṝṇām akṣayaṃ bhavet || iti | tasmād eva pitṛbhya udakadānaṃ kartavyam
|| 5.5 ||
pitṛbhyo nityam udakaṃ dadyāt | anyad bhojanaphalamūlādi yathotsāhaṃ yathāśakti dadyāt | atra cakārān nityaṃ devarṣīṇām api tarpaṇaṃ kartavyam iti darśitam | tatra manur brahmacāriṇaṃ prakṛtyāha |
nityaṃ snatvā śuciḥ kuryād devarṣipitṛtarpaṇam || iti | [MDh 2.176] kātyāyanaḥ |
devatānāṃ pitṝṇāṃ ca jale dadyāj jalāñjalim |
asaṃskṛtapramītānāṃ sthale dadyāj jalāñjalim || iti |
bhṛguḥ |
nodakeṣu na pātreṣu na kruddho naikapāṇinā |
nopatiṣṭhati tat toyaṃ yad bhūmyāṃ na pradīyate ||
uśanā |
āpo devagaṇāḥ sarva āpaḥ pitṛgaṇāḥ smṛtāḥ |
tasmād apsu jalaṃ deyaṃ pitṝṇāṃ dattam akṣayam || iti | bhṛguḥ prakārāntaram āha |
nābhimātre jale sthitvā cintayann ūrdhvamānasaḥ |
āgacchantu me pitara imaṃ gṛhṇantv apo ‘ñjalim ||
tris trir añjalim ākāśam uccair uccataraṃ budhaḥ |
uktvā coktvā kṣipan vāri vāgyato dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ || iti || 5.5 ||
yathotsāham anyat || 5.6 ||
brahmayajñodakadānābhyāṃ yad anyad avaśiṣṭaṃ gṛhasthadharmarūpaṃ tad yathāśakti kuryāt, utsāhavacanāt | tataś ca mukhyāsaṃbhave amukhyenāpi kartavyam, udāhṛtaṃ ca “payomūlaphalair vāpi” [MDh 3.82] ityādi || 5.6 ||
devayajñasyāgnikāryamūlatvād agniparigrahakālaṃ tāvad āha |
bhāryādir agnir dāyādir vā || 5.7 || — 5.6 ||
bhāryāśabdena vivāho labyate, agniśabdenaupāsanam, vaivāhikam agnim aupāsanarūpeṇa dhārayed ity arthaḥ |
kecid evaṃ vyācakṣate – jātakāgner eva pāraṃparyāgatasya vivāhottarakālam aupāsanarūpeṇa dhāraṇārtham abhidhānam iti |
tad ayuktam – “vivāhaṃ kurvan laukikam agnim ādhāya” it gṛhyasmṛteḥ | yadi jātakāgnir evāgacchati tadānīṃ tatra laukikagrahaṇaṃ na karoti | tataś cāśucitvāt jātakāgnis tyaktavya eva |
yadā vā asya bhrātṛbhiḥ pitrā vā saha vibhāgaḥ tatra vāgniparigrahaṇaṃ kuryāt | vivāhottarakālam api dravyāpekṣayā vikalpaḥ, karmaṇāṃ dravyasādhyatvāt | yo hi dravyavān sa vivāhakāle parigṛhṇīyāt niḥsvo dāyakāla iti |
kecid vivāhāt prāg api dāyādikam agniṃ parigṛhṇīyād iti vyācakṣate |
tad ayuktam, “vidhuraḥ punar niviṣṭaḥ punar agniṃ ca gṛhṇīyāt abhāryasyāgnyabhāvāt” iti smṛtyantaradarśanāt |
katham avagamyate smārtasyaiva dhāraṇaṃ na śrautasyeti | “jātaputro ‘gnīn ādadhīta” iti śruteḥ, tatrāpy agnīn iti bahuvacanaprayogāt || 5.7 ||
bhāryāśabdena vivāho lakṣyate | yasminn agnau bhāryohyate tam agnim ārabhya vā yasminn ahani pitṛbhrātrādibhir dāyavibhāgaḥ kriyate tad ahar ārabhya vā sāyam upakramam agniṃ paricaret | dāyavibhāgāt pūrvaṃ pitur jyeṣṭhabhrātur vā karmaṇy upajīvato na pratyavāyaḥ || 5.6 ||
tasmin gṛhyāṇi || 5.8 ||
tasmin gṛhyāṇi karmāṇi || 5.7 ||
tasminn evaupāsane gṛhyāṇi karmāṇi puṃsavanādīni kuryāt | grahaṇapakṣe idam | agṛhītaś cel laukike ‘gnāv eva draṣṭavyāni || 5.8 ||
tasminn evaṃparigṛhīte ‘gnau gṛhye manuṣyabhūtāni puṃsavanādīni karmāṇi kartavyānīti || 5.7 ||
devapitṛmanuṣyayajñāḥ svādhyāyaś ca || 5.9 ||
devapitṛmanuṣyayajñāḥ svādhyāyaś ca balikarma || 5.8 ||
devayajño vaiśvadevaḥ | pitrarthaṃ brāhmaṇabhojanaṃ pitṛyajñaḥ | manuṣyayajño ‘tithipūjanam | eteṣām agnisaṃbandho ‘pi vaiśvadevasya prasiddhaḥ, pitṛyajñasya tadbhojanārthaḥ pākaḥ, manuṣyayajñasyāpy atithibhojanārthaḥ pākaḥ, svādhyāyaśabdena brahmayajña ucyate | tasyaikīyamata evāgnisaṃbandha iti visamāsaḥ | tathā cāha uśanā: “udakasamīpe atha brahmayajñaḥ” ity ārabhya brahmayajñam uktvā “agnisamīpe ity ekeṣām” iti | eṣāṃ gṛhyoktakarmatvād eva siddham iti cet, na, āśaṅkānivṛttyarthatvāt punar abhidhānasya | kāśaṅketi cet, devayajñādīnāṃ pūrvaṃ laukike ‘gnau vidhānāt tatraiva teṣām adhikāraḥ, naupāsane | “tasmin gṛhyāṇi” [GDh 5.8] ity anenāpi tadvarjitānāṃ gṛhyakarmaṇām eva grahaṇam, teṣāṃ pūrvaṃ pṛthagabhidhānād iti kasyacid āśaṅkā syād iti | evaṃ ca teṣām ubhayatrādhikāraḥ | tataś cāgnim ato ‘pi laukike ‘gnau kṛte na doṣaḥ | tataś ca bādarāyaṇasūtram: “agnim ato ‘pi vacanena niṣiddhāḥ pañcayajñāḥ āhitāgneḥ” iti || 5.9 ||
tasminn ity anuvartate | tatra devayajñasyāgnisaṃ-bandhaḥ prasiddhaḥ | pitṛmanuṣyabhūtayajñānāṃ tu tadartham annam asmin gṛhyāgnau pacyata iti | brahmayajñasyāgnisaṃbandha uśanasā pakṣe darśitaḥ: “agnisamīpa ity ekeṣām” iti | aparigṛhītāgner api pañca-mahāyajñavidhānād ete laukike ‘gnau kartavyāḥ || 5.8 ||
balikarma || 5.10 ||
anena bhūtayajña uktaḥ | āśaṅkānivṛttyartham evāsyābhidhānam | cakāraḥ pūrvasūtrād adhikriyate tasminn evety anukarṣaṇārthaḥ | vaiśvadevānantaram eva kartavyaṃ na pṛthag ity evamarthaṃ pṛthak punaḥ karaṇam | tathā ca gṛhyakāraḥ: “pūrvaṃ devānāṃ hutvā paścād baliṃ haret” iti | pūrvavad bhūtayajña iti vaktavye evam abhidhānaṃ vaiśvadevāt pṛthagghaviḥsūcanārtham | tataś ca siddhasyānnasya vaiśvadevārthaṃ bhūtayajñārthaṃ caikadeśaṃ gṛhṇīyāt | tathā ca jaiminiḥ: “aśanasya balī vardhayitvā” iti dvivacanaṃ sāyaṃ prātaḥ kartavyārtham | karmagrahaṇaṃ vaiśvadevasyāpi sāyaṃ prātaḥ kartavyārtham | tathā ca gṛhyasmṛtiḥ: “sāyaṃ prātar vaiśvadevaṃ baliharaṇaṃ ca” iti, “atha sāyaṃ prātaḥ siddhasya haviṣyasya juhuyāt” ityādi || 5.10 ||
anuktagṛhyakārāṇām api sāmānyapraveśanārtham idānīṃ vaiśvadevam āha |
atha devayajñavaiśvadevaprayogam āha |
agnāv agnir dhanvantarir viśve devā prajāpatiḥ sviṣṭakṛd iti homāḥ || 5.11 || — 5.9 ||
homagrahaṇād eva labhyamānasyāgneḥ susamiddhe ‘gnau homārthaṃ punarvacanam | homā iti bahuvacanam ekāhutipratiṣedhārtham | homagrahaṇaṃ ca svāhākārapradarśanārtham | itikaraṇaṃ caturthyupasaṃgrahaṇārtham | visamāso mantrāntaraprasiddhyarthaḥ | tataś cāgnaye svāhā, dhanvatantraye svāhā, viśvebhyo devebhyaḥ svāhā, prajāpataye svāhā | sviṣṭakṛcchabdo ‘gnyupapadaś caturthyanto draṣṭavyaḥ, agnaye sviṣṭakṛte svāheti | etāvān vaiśvadevaḥ || 5.11 ||
atra “juhoticodanāḥ svāhākāraprad(h)ānāḥ” ity āpastambasmaraṇād dhomaśabdena svāhākāra uktaḥ | agnyādibhiḥ svāhākārāntair ganau juhuyāt | sviṣṭakṛc cāgnyupapado draṣṭavyaḥ | homatvād evāgnisiddhāv agnāv iti vacanaṃ baliharaṇavad bhūmau na kartavyam iti vacanārthm | tasmād dhomadharmaḥ svāhākāro baliharaṇeṣv api bhavati | tathā ca āśvalāyanaḥ: “svāhety atha baliharaṇam” iti | āpastambīyānāṃ svāhākārāntā eva mantrāḥ paṭhitāḥ || 5.9 ||
idānīṃ bhūtayajñavidhim āha |
atha bhūtayajñabaliharaṇam āha |
digdevatābhyaś ca yathāsvam || 5.12 || — 5.10 ||
pūrvaṃ digdevatābhyo baliṃ haret | indrāgniyamaniṛti-varuṇavāyusomeśānānāṃ prācyādipradakṣiṇam, ūrdhvaṃ viṣṇuḥ, atho ‘nantaḥ indrāya nama iti mantro ‘tra draṣṭavyaḥ | caśabdāt tatpuruṣebhyaś ca | tataś cendrapuruṣebhyo nama ityādi draṣṭavyam | yathāsvaṃ yathāsthānam ity arthaḥ || 5.12 ||
yasyā devatāyā yā dik tasyāṃ diśi tasyai devatāyai balir hartavyaḥ | indrāya svāhetīśānaparyantaṃ prāgādi pradakṣiṇaṃ kartavyam || 5.10 ||
dvārṣu marudbhyaḥ || 5.13 ||
dvārṣu mahadbhyaḥ || 5.11 ||
bahuvacanāt sarveṣu | marudbhyo nama iti draṣṭavyaḥ | atra tatpuruṣacodanā nāsti, cakārābhāvāt || 5.13 ||
gṛhasya yāvatyo dvāras tāsu mahadbhyaḥ svāheti baliharaṇam || 5.11 ||
gṛhadevatābhyaḥ praviśya || 5.14 || — || 5.12 ||
praviśyeti vacanāt pūrvaṃ gṛhād bahir draṣṭavyam | tataś ca gṛhād bahir ekasmin deśe gomayena maṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā digdevatābhyo baliharaṇaṃ kṛtvā tato bāhyāntareṣu ca dvāreṣu baliharaṇaṃ kṛtvābhyantaram eva praviśya gṛhadevatābhyo baliṃ haret | tāḥ śryādayaḥ, yathāha uśanā: “śayanasya śiraḥsthāne śriyai nama iti dadyāt pādasthāne bhadrakālyai nama iti, koṣṭhāgāre dhanapataye nama iti, stambhanilaye rudrāya nama iti, goṣṭhe mitrāya nama iti, ulūkhalamusalayor varuṇāya nama iti, kūpe ahaye budhnyāya nama iti, peṣaṇyām agnisamīpe caitābhyo devatābhyo nama iti dadyāt” iti || 5.14 ||
antaḥ praviśya gṛhadevatābhyaḥ svāheti baliharaṇam | praviśyeti vacanād dvārabahiṣṭhena na kartavyam
|| 5.12 ||
brahmaṇe madhye || 5.15 || — || 5.13 ||
gṛhamadhye brahmaṇe nama iti dadyāt || 5.15 ||
gṛhasya madhye brahmaṇe svāheti balir hartavyaḥ | digdevatābhyaś ceti cakārāt pṛthivī vāyuḥ prajāpatir viśve devā iti sūtrakāroktadevatābhyaś ca brahmaṇo ‘nantaraṃ balir hartavyaḥ || 5.13 ||
adbhya udakumbhe || 5.16 ||
adbhyo nama ity udakumbhe dadyāt || 5.16 ||
ākāśāyety antarikṣe || 5.17 ||
ākāśāyety antarkṣe balir utkṣepyaḥ || 5.14 ||
ākāśāya nama ity antarikṣe ‘ṅkaṇapradeśe dadyāt | itiśabdaḥ prakāravācī | tataś ca mṛtyave nama ity annaśālāyām, ajāyaikapade nama iti phalakeṣu ceti draṣṭavyaḥ || 5.17 ||
viśvebhyaś caiva devebhyo balir ākāśa utkṣipet |
divācarebhyo bhūtebhyo naktaṃcāribhya eva ca || [MDh 3.90] iti manuḥ || 5.14 ||
naktaṃcarebhyaś ca sāyam || 5.18 || — || 5.15 ||
sāyaṃ cet phalakābaliharaṇād ūrdhvam ākāśe naktaṃ-carebhyo nama iti dadyāt | cakāraḥ pūrvasamuccayā-rthaḥ, itarathā nakṃcarebhya eva syād iti || 5.18 ||
sāyaṃ baliharaṇe ‘yaṃ viśeṣaḥ | naktaṃcarebhyaḥ svāheti balir hartavyaḥ | cakārāt pūrvokābhyaś ca bhavati || 5.15 ||
svasti vācya bhikṣādānam appūrvam || 5.19 || — 5.16 ||
svastīti bhikṣukaṃ vācayitvā tata udakadānapūrvam asmai bhikṣādānaṃ kartavyam | bhikṣādāne striyā adhikārāt tasyā eva ca yācitatvād atas tām eva kārayed gṛhasthaḥ | kecid vyācakṣate vaiśvadevānantaram eva vidhānād agrabhaikṣasyāyaṃ vidhir na sāmānyasyeti | teṣāṃ pakṣe svastivācanaṃ puruṣeṇaiva kārayitavyam iti draṣṭavyam || 5.19 ||
baliharaṇānantaraṃ bhikṣādānaṃ kartavyam | svasty astv iti svastivacanam uktvā bhikṣor haste pūrvam apo datvā ceti | parivrājake viśeṣaḥ |
yatihaste jalaṃ datvā bhaikṣaṃ datvā punar jalam |
bhaikṣaṃ parvatamātraṃ syāt tajjalaṃ sāgaropamam || iti | etad bhikṣādānam atithipūjābhāve manuṣyayajñaḥ syāt | ete pañca mahāyajñā brahmayajñavarjāḥ kartavyāḥ sāyaṃ prātaś ca | kasmāt | “atha sāyaṃ prātaḥ siddhasya haviṣyasya juhuyāt” ity āśvalāyanasmarṇāt | “sāyaṃ prātar bhūtam ity ukta” ityādi kātyāyanasūtravacanāc ca || 5.16 ||
dadātiṣu caivaṃ dharmyeṣu || 5.20 || — || 5.17 ||
dadātiśabdena hiraṇyādidānam ucyate | dharmyagrahaṇān na dṛṣṭārthaṃ mitrādibhyo dānam | evam eva udakapūrvaṃ dadyāt | cakārād eva siddhe evaṃśabdaḥ samastaprāpaṇārthaḥ | tataś ca yathā bhikṣādānaṃ strīkṛtam api bhartur anujñayā pramāṇaṃ bhavati, tadvad dhiraṇyādidānam api pramāṇaṃ bhavati | tathā ca nāradaḥ |
strīkṛtāny apramāṇāni kāryāṇy āhur anāpadi |
viśeṣato gṛhakṣetradānadharmān na vikrayāḥ ||
etāny eva pramāṇāni bhartā yady anumanyate || [NSm 1.22–23] ityādi | bhikṣādānam api bhartur anujñayā vinā na kāryam | tathā ca manuḥ |
bālayā vā yuvatyā vā vṛddhayā vāpi yoṣitā |
na svātantryeṇa kartavyaṃ kāryaṃ kiṃcid gṛheṣv api || [MDh 5.147] iti || 5.20 ||
dadātayo dānāni yāni dānāni dharmyāni na bhayādi-nimittāni teṣu caivam appūrvadānam iti || 5.17 ||
dānaprasaṅgāt phalaviśeṣam āha |
samadviguṇasāhasrānantāni phalāny abrāhmaṇabrāhmaṇaśrotriya-vedapāragebhyaḥ || 5.21 ||
samadviguṇasāhasrān ānantyāni phalāny abrāhmaṇabrāhmaṇaśrotriyaveda-pāragebhyaḥ || 5.18 ||
pātraviśeṣāt phalaviśeṣapratipādanārtho ‘yam ārambhaḥ | samaṃ īṣatphalam ity arthaḥ | abrāhmaṇāya dattaṃ hiraṇyādi bhaikṣaṃ vā | abrāhmaṇaśabdena upanītamātra ucyate | tathā cāha uśanā: “yena vedā anadhītās tadvratāni ca nānuṣṭhitāny abrāhmaṇa eva saḥ” iti | abrāhmaṇaśabdaṃ brāhmaṇa eva prayuktavān | dviguṇaṃ bhavati brāhmaṇāya dattam | brāhmaṇaśabdenānuṣṭhitavedavrato anadhītaveda ucyate | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
yaś ca kāṣṭhamayo hastī yaś ca carmamayo mṛgaḥ |
yaś ca vipro ‘nadhīyānas trayas te nāmadhārakāḥ || [VaDh 3.11] śrotriyāya dattaṃ sahasraguṇaṃ bhavati | śrotriyaśabdena ekaśākhādhyāyī ucyate | yathāha baudhāyanaḥ: “ekāṃ śākhām adhītya śrotriyo bhavati” [=ĀpDh 2.6.4] iti | anantaṃ vedapāragāya dattam | vedapāragaśabdena sāṅgavedādhyāyy ucyate | yathāha kaṇvaḥ: “yena sāṅgo vedo ‘dhītaḥ sa vedapārago bhavati” iti | eṣāṃ pūrvaṃ phalābhidhānāt | evaṃ jātiviśeṣād api draṣṭavyam | yathāha prajāpatiḥ |
śūdre samaphalaṃ dānaṃ vaiśye ca dviguṇaṃ bhavet |
kṣatriye triguṇaṃ prāhur amitaṃ brāhmaṇe bhavet ||
iti || 5.21 ||
abrāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyādiḥ brāhmaṇo jātimātram | śrotriyo ‘dhītavedaḥ | sāṅgaṃ sakalpaṃ sarahasyaṃ cādhītavedo vedapāragaḥ | ebhyo dattaṃ yathākramaṃ samadviguṇasāhasram ānantyaṃ ca phalaṃ dadāti | tathā ca manuḥ |
samam abrāhmaṇe dānaṃ dviguṇaṃ brāhmaṇabruve |
śrotriye śatasāhasram anantaṃ vedapārage || [MDh 7.85] iti || 5.18 ||
evaṃ phalaviśeṣāpekṣayā dānaphalam uktvā idānīṃ tatprasaṅgenānyam api
dānaprakāram āha |
dānaprasaṅgād yatrāvaśyadeyam adāne ca pratyavāyas taṃ viṣayam āha |
gurvarthaniveśauṣadhārthavṛttikṣīṇayakṣymāṇādhyayanādhvasaṃyogavaiśvajiteṣu dravyasaṃvibhāgo bahirvedi || 5.22 || — || 5.19 ||
gurugrahaṇaṃ mātṛpitṛbhṛtyānāṃ copalakṣaṇam | yathāha manuḥ |
sāntānikaṃ yakṣyamāṇam adhvagaṃ sarvavedasam |
gurvarthaṃ pitṛbhṛtyārthaṃ svādhyāyārthyupa-tāpinau || [MDh 11.1] ityādi | niveśanārthaṃ ca | evaṃ yācitvāpi niveśaḥ kartavyaḥ | idam api samuccayapakṣe draṣṭavyam | oṣadhārtham arthagrahaṇe prastute punar arthagrahaṇam ebhyo vidyāviśeṣato ‘rthasyāpi gurulāghavajñāpanārtham, niḥsvebhya eva dātavyam ity evamarthaṃ vā | yathāha manuḥ |
niḥsvebhyo deyam etebhyo dānaṃ vidyāviśeṣataḥ | [MDh 11.2] ityādi | vṛttikṣīṇaḥ āpannaḥ | yakṣyamāṇaḥ nityayajñārtham idaṃ vacanam | tataś ca “alaṃ somāya, nāsomayājī, sa somaṃ pātum arhati” ity evamādīni smṛtyantaravākyāni kāmyayajñe draṣṭavyāni | yācitvāpi yakṣyamāṇatvān nityayajñasya | adhyayanārthaṃ vedādhyayanārtham, adhvasayogaḥ adhvani vartamānaḥ, kṣīṇaḥ pathyadānārthaṃ ca saṃyogagrahaṇāt | vaiśvajito viśvajitā yeneṣṭam | ebhyaḥ saṃvibhāgaḥ kartavyaḥ hiraṇyāder api, dravyagrahaṇāt | niyamena kartavyam, samupasargaprayogāt | vaiśvajitasya ā saṃvatsarāt yācñāpratiṣedhāt aprārthayamānāyaiva deyam | itarebhyo ‘prārthayamānebhyo vā prārthayamānebhyo vā dadyāt | dravyavibhāgaś ca yathātmanaḥ kuṭumbavyasanaṃ na bhavati tathā kartavyaḥ, vyupasargaprayogāt | tataś caikadeśo dātavya ity arthaḥ | bahirvedigrahaṇād antarvedy anyebhyo ‘pi | evaṃ ca niyamena kartavyatvāt tad akurvato na kevalaṃ phalābhāva eva | kiṃ tarhi | pratyavāyaś ca bhavati | tarhi tat kurvato ‘pi tatpratyavāyābhāva eva prayojanaṃ na phalārtham iti cet, na, vidyāviśeṣata iti liṅgāt pātraviśeṣāt phalaviśeṣo ‘stīti jñāpayati | tathā ca vyāsaḥ |
guruniveśauṣadhārthaṃ svādhyāyārthaṃ ca bhārata |
adhvany api pravṛttasya yāgārthaṃ ca tathaiva ca ||
kṛtavaiśvajitasyāpi vṛttikṣīṇasya pārthiva |
datvā vidyāviśeṣeṇa svargaloke mahīyate ||
ityādi | tataś ca saṃdhyopāsanādivad draṣṭavyam | yathā saṃdhyopāsanam akurvataḥ pratyavāyaḥ, kurvataḥ pratyavāyābhāvaś ca phalaprāptiś ceti || 5.22 ||
yajñe dakṣiṇākāle sadasyebhyo yad dānaṃ tad antarvedi | tato ‘nyatra bahirvedi | dravyasaṃvibhāgo hiraṇyāder dānam | tadgurvādiviṣaye ‘vaśyaṃ kartavyam | adhītavedasya dakṣiṇārthaṃ gurvartham | niveśauṣadhārthaṃ niveśo vivāhaḥ | sa ca prathamas tadartham | auṣadhārthaṃ rugṇasya bheṣajārtham ¦ vṛttyā tadahar jīvanena hīno vṛttikṣīṇaḥ | yakṣyamāṇo yajñaṃ kariṣyan | adhyayanādhvasaṃyogaḥ | adhyayanena saṃyogo yasya so ‘dhyayanasaṃyogaḥ | adhvanā saṃyogo yasya so ‘dhvasaṃyogaḥ | bahuvrīhiḥ | vaiśvajitaḥ kṛtaviśvajidyāgaḥ | sarvasvadānena nirdravyaḥ | etair yācito ‘vaśyaṃ yathāśakti hiraṇyādi dadyāt | adadat pratyaveyād iti | bahirvedigrahaṇena sadīkṣitaviṣayam idam antarvedy anyebhyo ‘pi deyam || 5.19 ||
bhikṣamāṇeṣu kṛtānnam itareṣu || 5.23 || — || 5.20 ||
uktavyatirikteṣu bhikṣamāneṣu kṛtānnaṃ dadyāt | “itarebhyaḥ” iti kecit paṭhanti, teṣām apy ayam evārthaḥ |
nanu ca “svasti vācya bhikṣādānam” [GDh 5.19] ity anenaiva siddhatvād etan na vaktavyam iti |
ucyate – itikartavyatāmātraṃ tat | idaṃ punar niyamena bhikṣā dātavyety evamartham iti | evaṃ cet idam api saṃdhyopāsanādivad draṣṭavyam, aviśeṣeṇoktatvāt | dānaṃ ca bahirvedy antarvedi ca | kṛtānnagrahaṇāt taṇḍulāder aniyamaḥ | “bhaikṣam itareṣu” ity vaktavye, “bhikṣamāṇeṣu” iti vacanāt “bhavati bhikṣāṃ dehi” ityādibhir mantrair prārthayamāneṣv eva niyamaḥ | atha vā bhikṣārhāṇām evāyaṃ niyama iti | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
avratā hy anadhīyānā yatra bhaikṣacarā dvijāḥ |
taṃ grāmaṃ daṇḍayed rājā corabhaktaprado hi saḥ || iti | [VaDh 3.4] tatra bhikṣādāne yater haste ‘py udakadānaṃ kartavyam | yathāha bhṛguḥ |
yatihaste jalaṃ dadyād bhaikṣaṃ dadyāt punar jalam |
bhaikṣaṃ parvatamātraṃ syāt taj jalaṃ sāgaropamam || || iti || 2.23 ||
itareṣūktavyatirikteṣu bhikṣamāṇeṣu kṛtānnaṃ pakvānnam avaśyaṃ deyam | dravyāder adāne na doṣaḥ | kṛtānnaviṣaye ‘pi vasiṣṭhaḥ |
avratā hy anadhīyānā yatra bhaikṣacarā dvijāḥ |
taṃ grāmaṃ daṇḍayed rājā coradaṇḍavrato hi saḥ || [VaDh 3.4] iti || 5.20 ||
dānāpavādam adhunocyate |
atha dānāpavādaḥ |

adharmasaṃyuktaḥ gurvādyartham uddiśya ya prārthayate na ca karoti tasmai pratiśrutam api na dadyāt | apiśabdāt saṃakalpitam api || 5.24 ||
dāsyāmīti pratiśrutyāpy adharmasaṃyuktaviṣaye na dadyāt | yadi tena dravyeṇādharmasaṃyuktaṃ veśyāgamanādy asau kariṣyatīti vijānīyat || adharmasaṃyukta iti vacanād anyatra pratiśrutam adadat pratyaveyād iti darśayati || 5.21 ||
adharmasaṃyuktatvaṃ na yasya kasyacid vacanāt pratipattavyam ity āha |
pratiśravaṇaviṣaye viśeṣam āha |

anṛtāny apātakāni || 5.25 || — || 5.22 ||
krodhaharṣabhayarogalobhāviṣṭānāṃ bālādīnāṃ ca vacanenārdham asaṃyuktatvaṃ nādhyavaseyam, apra-māṇatvāt teṣāṃ vacanasya | vākyagrahaṇam anyatrāpy eṣāṃ vacanasyāprāmāṇyaprasiddhyartham || 5.25 ||
kruddhādivākyāny anṛtāny ayathārthāny apy apātakāni na pāpaṃ janayanti | kruddhaḥ krodhāviṣṭaḥ | hṛṣṭo harvāviṣṭaḥ | bhīto bhayāviṣṭaḥ | eteṣāṃ guṇāntarair āviṣṭatvād vākyam apramāṇam | tasmāt pratiśrutyādāne ‘pi teṣām adoṣaḥ || 5.22 ||

jaghanyāṃś ca || 5.26 || — || 5.23 ||
ātmana prathamam etān bhojayet | atithir vakṣyamāṇaḥ | kumāro bālaḥ | vyādhito rogārtaḥ | suvāsinyo garbhayuktā duhitaraḥ | sthavirā vṛddhāḥ | jaghanyāḥ paricārakāḥ | caśabdād dīnānāthādayaḥ upasaṃgṛhyante | visamāsaḥ kramaniyamārthaḥ | tataś ca yathānugrāhyam etān bhojayet || 5.26 ||
atithir vakṣyamāṇaḥ | kumārā bālāḥ | vyādhitaḥ saṃjātavyādhiḥ | garbhiṇyaḥ prasiddhāḥ | svavāsinyo duhitaro bhaginyaś ca | sthavirā vṛddhāḥ | jaghanyāḥ paricārakādayaḥ | etān ātmanaḥ pūrvaṃ bhojayet, paścāt svayaṃ bhuñjīta | jaghanyānāṃ pṛthakpadatvaṃ teṣām ānantaryasūcanārtham || 5.23 ||
ācāryapitṛsakhīnāṃ tu nivedya vacanakriyā || 5.27 || — || 5.24 ||
yo hy etasmin kāle ācāryādīnāṃ kaścid gṛham āgacchet tataḥ siddham annaṃ nivedya tasmai taduktasya vacanasya karaṇaṃ yat te bruvate tat kuryād ity arthaḥ | tuśabdo niyamārthaḥ, tad eva kuryān na kvacid api svatantreṇa bhavitavyam iti | vacanakriyety uktatvād yadi te na kiṃcid vadanti tadānīṃ svatantreṇa bhavitavyam || 5.27 ||
yadi bhojanakāla ācāryādya āgaccheyus tadā siddham annaṃ tebhyo nivedya tadannakriyā tadicchātaḥ kartavyāḥ | na teṣu saṃnihiteṣu svatantro bhaved ity arthaḥ || 5.24 ||
ṛtvigācāryaśvaśurapitṛvyamātulānām upasthāne madhuparkaḥ || 5.28 || — 5.25 ||
ṛtvigādīnāṃ tu gṛhāgamane sati madhuparkeṇa pūjā kartavyā prathamam, upasthānagrahaṇān na dvitīyādau | madhuparkavidhiś ca gṛhye draṣṭavyaḥ || 5.28 ||
ṛtvigādiṣu gṛham āgateṣu madhuparko deyaḥ || 5.25 ||
saṃvatsare punaḥ || 5.29 || — || 5.26 ||

yajñavivāhayor arvāk || 5.30 || — || 5.27 ||
arvāg api saṃvatsarād yajñe vivāhe cāgatānāṃ madhuparko deyaḥ | yajñe ṛtvijāṃ śrutita eva madhuparkadānasya siddhatvāt śvaśurādyartha ārambhaḥ || 5.30 ||
saṃvatsarād arvāg api yajñavivāhayor āgatebhyas tebhyo madhuparko deyaḥ | madhuparkavidhir gṛhyokto draṣṭavyaḥ || 5.27 ||

aśrotriyasyāsanodake || 5.32 || — || 5.29 ||

śrotriyasya tu pādyam arghyam annaviśeṣāṃś ca prakārayet || 5.33 || — 5.30 ||
brāhmaṇasya śrotriyasya | kuta etat? punaḥ śrotriyagrahaṇāt | vaiśyārthaṃ cet, na, “vaiśyo madhuparkeṇa na mānyaḥ tasmāt taṃ bhojayed eva” iti smṛtyantaradarśanāt | tuśabdo viśeṣārthaḥ | sa yadi vṛttasaṃpanno bhavati tasyopasthāne pādaprakṣālanārthaṃ pūjārthaṃ codakaṃ deyam | pādyam arghyam iti visamāsān madhuparkaś ca annaviśeṣāḥ mudgodanādibhedabhinnāḥ | cakārād upadaṃśādiviśeṣāś ca | propasargād ghṛtabahulam | tathā ca śrutiḥ; “brāhmaṇāyābhyāgatāya sarpiṣmad annaṃ pacati” iti || 5.33 ||
tuśabdo na brāhmaṇaṃ vyāvartayati | śrotriyasya brāhmaṇasyātitheḥ pādyaṃ pādodakam | arghyaṃ phalopahāratāmbūlādi | annaviśeṣāḥ pāyasāpūpādayas tāṃś ca prakarṣeṇa kārayet samarthaḥ || 5.30 ||

tuśabdād ubhayaviparīte | tataś ca yadi vidvān asādhuvṛtto bhavati tasyāpi “nityaṃ vā saṃskāraviśiṣṭaḥ” [GDh 5.34] ity evaṃ draṣṭavyam | na tṛṇodakabhūmimātram eva deyam || 5.36 ||

svāgatam antataḥ || 5.37 ||

śayyāsanāvasathānuvrajyopāsanāni sadṛkchreyasoḥ samāni || 5.39 || — || 5.34 ||
H reads “samānāni”
ātmanā tulyasya cādhikasya cātitheḥ śayyādīny ātmanulyāni kartavyāni | śayyā khaṭvādi, āsanaṃ phalakādi, āvasatham apavarakādi, anuvrajyā anugamanam, upāsanaṃ namaskārādi | anuvrajyopāsanayor ātmatulyatvāsaṃbhavād ātmanas tulyasya cādhikasya cānuvrajyopāsanaṃ ca kartavyam ity evamartha upadeśaḥ | śayyāsanādi tv ātmanaḥ sāmyena kartavyam | ātmana eva sāmyaṃ na parasparayoḥ, uttamasya saṃbhāvanīyatvāt | tathā ca vakṣyati: “samāsamābhyāṃ viṣamasame pūjātaḥ | anarcitaṃ ca” [GDh 17.18–19] iti | tataś ca sadṛkśreyasor ātmāpekṣayā tulyāni parasparāpekṣayā atulyānīti draṣṭavyam || 5.39 ||
yo ‘tithir vidyāvṛttādinātmanā sadṛśo yaś ca śreyāṃs tayor dvayor apy ātmanā tulyāni śayyāsanādīni deyāni | āvasatho gṛhe sthānaviśeṣaḥ | anuvrajyopāsanayor ātmany asaṃbhavāt tulyatvaṃ na saṃbhavati te api kārye ity arthaḥ || 5.34 ||

pādopasaṃgrahaṇaṃ samavāye ‘nvaham || 6.1 ||
pādayor upaśleṣaṇam upasargadvayaprayogāt smṛtyantaropadiṣṭena vidhinā kartavyam | yathāha manuḥ: “vyatyastapāṇinā kāryam upasaṃgrahaṇam” [MDh 2.72] ityādi | samavāye saṃgame | anvahaṃ pratidinam | kartavyam iti śeṣaḥ | vakṣyamāṇānāṃ saṃgame iti draṣṭavyam || 6.1 ||
vakṣyamāṇānāṃ mātrādīnāṃ samavāye saṃgame pratidinaṃ pādopasaṃgrahaṇaṃ kāryam | “vyatyastapāṇinā kāryam” [MDh 2.72] iti pūrvoktaprakāreṇa || 6.1 ||
abhigamya tu viproṣya || 6.2 ||
viprakṛṣṭadeśāntarapratyāgatenābhimukhyena tadgṛhaṃ gatvā kartavyam | tuśabda ādaropasaṃgrahārthaḥ | tataś ca na yādṛcchikadarśanam udīkṣeta || 6.2 ||

mātāpitarau prasiddhau | tadbandhavaḥ mātulamātāmahapitṛvyapitāmahādayaḥ | pūrvajāḥ ātmano bhrātaro jyeṣṭhā | vidyāguravaḥ ye aṅgādhyāpayitāraḥ, pūrvaṃ vedādhyāpayitur uktatvāt | tadguravaḥ teṣāṃ pitrādīnāṃ ye vidyāguravaḥ | cakārād gurusakhyādīnām api | eṣāṃ samavāye anvaham abhigamya ca viproṣya | pūrvajādīnāṃ visamāsaḥ tacchvaśurādigurūpasaṃgrahaṇārthaḥ || 6.3 ||
mātāpitarau prasiddhau | tadbandhavo mātulamātṛṣvasṛpitṛvyapitṛṣavasrādayaḥ | pūrvajā jyeṣṭhabhrātaraḥ | vidyāgurava ācāryopādhyāyādayaḥ | tadgurava [pr-?]ācāryādayaḥ | tadviṣayaṃ pūrvasūtradvayam iti || 6.3 ||

mātrādīnāṃ yugapatsaṃnipāte samāgame parasyotkṛṣṭasya prathamam upasaṃgrahaṇaṃ kāryam | “ācāryaḥ śreṣṭho gurūṇāṃ mātety eke” [GDh 2.56] ity utkarṣaḥ pūrvoktaḥ | āpastambena tu: “ācāryaprācāryasaṃnipāte prācār[yā]yopasaṃgṛhyopasaṃjighṛkṣed ācāryam” [ĀpDh 1.8.19] ityādinopasaṃgrahaṇam uktam || 6.4 ||
abhivādanavidhim āha |
svanāma procyāham ayam ity abhivādo jñasamavāye || 6.5 ||
svanāma pitrādikṛtam | tat prakarṣeṇoccārya tadanantaram ahaṃśabdaṃ coktvā ayam iti pratyakṣopadeśaḥ, tadartho ‘smiśabdaḥ prayoktavyaḥ | itiśabdaḥ prakāravācī | ante, guror nāmnaḥ sthāne prakṛtatvāt bhośabdaś ca | “devadattanāmāham asmi bho” iti prayojyaḥ | asminn upasaṃgrahaṇakāle evam abhivādaḥ | ābhimukhyenoccāraṇaṃ kartavyam ity arthaḥ | jñasamavāye pratyabhivādanavidhijñasamavāya ity arthaḥ | pratyabhivādanavidhiś ca smṛtyantare draṣṭavyaḥ | yathāha manuḥ |
āyuṣmān bhava saumyeti vācyo vipro ‘bhivādane |
akāraś cāsya nāmno ‘nte vācyaḥ pūrvākṣaraḥ plutaḥ || iti | [MDh 2.125] tathā ca vasiṣṭho ‘pi: “āmantrite svaro ‘ntyaḥ plavate saṃdhyakṣaram apragṛhyam āyāvbhāvaṃ cāpadyate, tathā bhobhāv iti” [VaDh 13.46] | āyuṣmān bhava saumya viṣṇumitrā a āyuṣmān bhava saumya dāśarathāya, ityevamādayaḥ prayogāḥ pratyabhivādanavidhau draṣṭavyāḥ | ajñasamavāye tu upasaṃgrahaṇaṃ na kartavyam | evam abhivādanaṃ ca | kiṃ tatra kartavyam iti cet – abhivādaye ity etāvad eva vaktavyam | abhivādanam eva kuryāt, yathāha āpastambaḥ: “dakṣiṇaṃ bāhuṃ śrotrasamaṃ prasārya brāhmaṇo ‘bhivādayīta, uraḥsamaṃ rājanyo, madhyasamaṃ vaiśyo nīcaiḥ śūdraḥ prāñjaliṃ plāvanaṃ ca” [ĀpDh 1.5.16–17] ityādi | evaṃ ca “vyatyastapāṇinā” [MDh 2.72] ityādir upasaṃgrahaṇa-vidhiḥ āpastambokto ‘bhivādanavidhir ity avagantavyaḥ || 6.5 ||
yaḥ pratyabhivādanābhijñas tena saṃgame svanāma procya vyāvahārikaṃ prasiddhaṃ nāma procyāham ayam iti prakarṣeṇoccair uktvābhivādaḥ kāryaḥ | abhivādo ‘bhivādanaṃ ṇyantāder ac | evaṃ cārthajñānāṃ jñātavarasamavāye ‘bhivādanakrameṇāyam aham iti svanāma guhyaṃ procyābhivādanaṃ kāryam | hīnavyatirikābhivādyaviṣayam | tad yathā – “abhivādaye haradattaśarmā nāmāham asmi bho” iti | tatra pratyabhivādanavidhir manunā darśitaḥ |
āyuṣmān bhava saumyeti vācyo vipro ‘bhivādane |
akāraś cāsya nāmno ‘nte vācyaḥ pūrvākṣaraḥ plutaḥ || [MDh 2.125] asyārthaḥ | vipraśabdena brāhmaṇaviṣayam idam | abhivādayitā vipra āyuṣmān bhava saumyeti vācyaḥ | asya nāmno ‘nte pūrvākṣarapluto ‘kāraś ca vācyaḥ | pūrvākṣarapluta ity akārasya viśeṣaṇam | yasmāt pūrvo yo ‘ntyaḥ sa pluto vācyaḥ | tena vyañjanavyavadhāne ‘pi bhavati | “āyuṣmān bhava saumya haradattā3a” | vyañjanavyavadhānena yathā “āyuṣmān bhava saumyāgni3da” | iti prayogaḥ | vasiṣṭhas tu saṃdhyakṣare viśeṣam āha: “āmantrite yo ‘ntyaḥ svaraḥ sa plavate saṃdhyakṣaram apragṛhyam āhuḥ” [VaDh 13.46] [i] ā u bhāvaṃ cāpadyata iti | “eco ‘pragṛhyasyādūrād ghūte pūrvasyārdhasyād uttarasyedutau” iti vaiyākaraṇaḥ | tatrānte ‘kāre prayukte tayor yvāv aci saṃhitāyām iti yakāravakārau | āyuṣmān bhava saumya pinākapāṇā3yeti viṣṇā3veti ca prayogaḥ | ajñasamavāya iti pakṣe nāyam abhivādanaprakāraḥ | tatra smṛtyantaram |
avidvāṃsaḥ pratyabhivāde nāmno ye na plutiṃ viduḥ |
kāmaṃ teṣu tu viproṣya strīṣv ivāyam ahaṃ vadet || iti | yathā strīṣūktaprakāraṃ vinā tādātmikena deśabhāṣādinā yena kenāpi śabdenābhivādanaṃ tadvat teṣv api bhavati | abhivādanam iti sāmānyopalakṣaṇam, prakāravarjitasya stryādiprayuktasyāpy abhidhānāt | abhivādanaprakāre tv āpastambaḥ: “dakṣiṇaṃ bāhuṃ śrotrasamaṃ prasārya brāhmaṇo ‘bhivādayīta uraḥsamaṃ rājanyo madhyamaṃ vaiśyo nīcaiḥ śūdraḥ prāñjaliḥ” [ĀpDh 1.5.16] iti || 6.5 ||
strīpuṃyoge ‘bhivādane ‘niyamam eke || 6.6 ||
jāyāpativiṣaye jāyayā bhartur abhivādane kartavye prāpta ity arthaḥ | yady api bhartā pratyabhivādanajñaḥ, tathāpy aniyamam eke icchanti | aham ayam ity evamādiyamo nāsti, yena kenacit prakāreṇa kartavyam iti | na tu gautamaḥ, sa tu aham ayam ity evamādinā niyamenaiva kartavyam iti | vaidagdhyāpekṣayā vikalpaḥ || 6.6 ||
strīpuṃyoge jāyāpatisamavāye ‘bhivādataḥ sārvavibhaktikas tasiḥ | abhivādane prāpte ‘niyamam eke manyante | yady api bhartā pratyabhivādajñas tathāpi tadabhivādane bhāryāyā niyamaṃ necchanti | abhivādaye ‘ham iyam ityādikramo niyamas taṃ necchanti | sāmānyābhivādanamātram eva | evaṃ ca bhāryayā bhartur ahar ahar namaskāraḥ kāryaḥ | eka iti vacanād gautamasya pakṣe niyama eva || 6.6 ||
nāviproṣya strīṇām amātṛpitṛvyabhāryābhaginīnām || 6.7 ||
aviproṣya na strīṇām upasaṃgrahaṇaṃ kartavyam | mātā prasiddhā | pitṛvyabhāryā pitur anujasya bhāryā | bhaginī sodarā jyeṣṭhā | etā varjayitvā | āsām anvaham upasaṃgrahaṇaṃ kartavyam | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
upasaṃgrahaṇaṃ kuryān mātaraṃ bhaginīṃ tathā |
pitṛvyabhāryāṃ ca tathā so ‘nvahaṃ tv abhigamya tu || iti | anyāsāṃ gurusthānīyānāṃ viproṣya pratyāgatenābhivādanam upasaṃgrahaṇaṃ vā kartavyam | tatrātyantagurusthānīyānāṃ mātulabhāryādīnām upasaṃgrahaṇam anyāsām abhivādanam iti draṣṭavyam || 6.7 ||
samavāye ‘nvaham ity asyāpavādo ‘yam | mātṛpitṛvyabhāryābhaginīvyatiriktānāṃ strīṇām aviproṣyopasaṃgrahaṇam abhivādanaṃ ca na kāryam | kiṃ tu viproṣya pratyāgamana eva kāryam | mātrādīnāṃ tv aviproṣyāpi pratyaham | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
upasaṃgrahaṇaṃ kuryād bhaginyā mātur eva ca |
tathā pitṛvyabhāryāṇāṃ samavāye ‘nvahaṃ dvijaḥ || iti || 6.7 ||
nopasaṃgrahaṇaṃ bhrātṛbhāryāṇāṃ śvaśrvāś ca || 6.8 ||
H reads “svasṝṇām”
viproṣya pratyāgatenāpy atyantagurusthānīyadvāreṇa prāptaṃ pratiṣidhyate | tataś cābhivādanaṃ siddham | visamāsaḥ sakhyupasaṃgrahaṇārthaḥ | śvaśurasakhibhāryopasaṃgrahaṇārthaś caśabdaḥ || 6.8 ||
viproṣya pratyāgatenāsām upasaṃgrahaṇaṃ na karyam | abhivādanaṃ tu bhavaty eva | tatrātyantagurusthānīyānāṃ mātulānyādīnām upasaṃgrahaṇam anyāsām abhivādanam iti || 6.8 ||
ṛtvikśvaśurapitṛvyamātulānāṃ tu yavīyasāṃ pratyutthānam anabhivādyāḥ || 6.9 ||
H reads: “abhivādyāḥ”
ṛtvigādīnām ātmano yavīyasām upasthitānāṃ pratyutthānamātreṇaiva pūjā kartavyā | yasmād anabhivādyāḥ, ato nopasaṃgrāhyā api | tuśabdo viśeṣavācī | samānavayasaś ca nābhivādyāḥ, viśeṣato yavīyāṃsa iti || 6.9 ||
ṛtvigādīnām ātmano yavīyasāṃ pratyutthānamātreṇa pūjā kāryā na punas te ‘bhivādyāḥ || 6.9 ||
tathānyaḥ pūrvaḥ pauro ‘śītikāvaraḥ śūdro ‘py apatyasamena || 6.10 ||
yathaite nābhivādyāḥ pratyuttheyās tathāyam apy anyaḥ ṛtvigādibhyaḥ pūrvo vayasā, pauraḥ samāna-sthānavāsī, aśītikāvaraḥ dṛḍham aśītivarṣastha ity arthaḥ | tataś ca tatprabhṛty eva draṣṭavyaḥ śūdro ‘pi | apiśabdāt kṣatriyavaiśyāv api | apatyasamena brāhmaṇena kṣatriyeṇa vaiśyena vā anya asamāna-jātīyo ‘pi | anyaśabde saty api apatyasamaśabdaḥ samā-najātīyaviṣaya eveti kasyacid āśaṅkā syād iti || 6.10 ||
ṛtvigādayo yathā pratyuttheyā nābhivādyās tathāyam api | anyas tebhyo ‘nyaḥ | pūrvo vayasādhikaḥ | pauraḥ puravāsī | vayasādhikye ‘pi puravāsād apakarṣa uktaḥ | aśītir evāśītikā tayāvaro ‘śītikāvaraḥ | nyūnāśītika ity arthaḥ | evaṃvidhaḥ śūdro ‘py apatyasamena pratyuttheyo nābhivādyaḥ | apatyasamenety antayavīyastā darśitā | śūdragrahaṇam avaravarṇopalakṣaṇam | tataś ca śūdras tribhir api varṇair vaiśyo dvābhyāṃ kṣatriyas tu brāhmaṇeneti sidhyati || 6.10 ||
avaro ‘py āryaḥ śūdreṇa || 6.11 ||
avaro ‘py atyantanikṛṣṭavayasko ‘pi āryo dvijātiḥ śūdreṇa pratyuttheyaḥ | śūdragrahaṇaṃ vaiśyādīnām apy upalakṣaṇam, tataś ca śūdreṇa brāhmaṇa-kṣatriyavaiśyāḥ pratyuttheyāḥ, vaiśyena brāhmaṇa-kṣatriyau, kṣatriyeṇa brāhmaṇa iti draṣṭavyaḥ | namaskārasamuccayārthaḥ apiśabdaḥ || 6.11 ||
nyūnāśītikena śūdreṇāvaro ‘py āryo yavīyān apy āryas traivarṇikaḥ pratyuttheyo nābhivādyaḥ | atrāpi śūdragrahaṇam avaravarṇopalakṣaṇam | tataś ca śūdreṇa trayo varṇāḥ | vaiśyena dvau | kṣatriyeṇa brāhmaṇa ity avaravayasaḥ pratyuttheyā nābhivādyā iti sidhyati || 6.11 ||
nāma cāsya varjayet || 6.12 ||
H reads: “vāsya”
asyagrahaṇaṃ vīpsārtham asyāsyeti, uttamasyottama-sya nikṛṣṭo nikṛṣṭo na nāmoccārayet | nirupapadam iti śeṣaḥ | caśabdāt parihasanaṃ ca | tataś cottama uttamo nikṛṣṭasya nikṛṣṭasya nāmakāraṃ gṛhṇīyāt || 6.12 ||
asyeti atra vīpsālopaḥ | asyāsyotkṛṣṭotkṛṣṭasyāpakṛṣṭo na nāma gṛhṇīyāt | kiṃ tv aupacārikaṃ nāma gṛhṇīyāt || 6.12 ||
rājñaś cājapaḥ preṣyaḥ || 6.13 ||
rājñaś ca nāma varjayet preṣyaḥ preṣaṇakaraṇaḥ brāhmaṇaḥ | nikṛṣṭatvāt prāptasya pratiṣedhaḥ | sa ced ajapaḥ aśrotriya iti yāvat | cakārād vaiśyasyāpi | śūdrasya tu na doṣaḥ || 6.13 ||
ajapo ‘śrotriyaḥ | preṣyaḥ preṣakaraḥ | sa utkṛṣṭavarṇo brāhmaṇo ‘pi rājño ‘bhiṣiktasya nāma varjayet || 6.13 ||
bho bhavann iti vayasyaḥ samāne ‘hani jātaḥ || 6.14 ||
bho bhavann ity evam abhidhātavyaḥ | bhobhavan-śabdau ca kāryāpekṣayā prayoktavyau | etāvaty eva pūjā | yo vayasyaḥ sakhā tasya lakṣaṇaṃ samāne ‘hani jāta iti | ahaḥśabdo ‘tra saṃvatsaraparyāyaḥ, “abdaḥ saṃvatsaram ahar jalam” iti naighaṇṭuke saṃvatsara-paryāyaśravaṇāt | tataś ca samāne saṃvatsare jātaḥ | loke ‘pi ca tulyasaṃvatsarajāteṣu samānavayastvaṃ prasiddham | tataś cordhvaṃ saṃvatsarād vṛddho bhavati || 6.14 ||
vayasā tulyo vayasyaḥ | samāne ‘hani jātaḥ | atrāhaḥśabdaḥ saṃvatsaravācakaḥ | ekasmin saṃvatsare jātaḥ sa bho bhavann ity anayor anyatareṇa śabdena saṃbhāṣyaḥ || 6.14 ||
daśavarṣavṛddhaḥ pauraḥ || 6.15 ||
daśavarṣavṛddhaḥ pauraḥ pañcabhiḥ kalābharaḥ śrotriyaś cāraṇas tribhiḥ || 6.15 ||
bho bhavann iti vartate | bho bhavann iti vayasyavad abhibhāṣya ity arthaḥ | daśavarṣavṛddho ‘pi pauraḥ samānasthānavāsī || 6.15 ||
pure vasan guṇahīno daśavarśavṛddhaś ca tatrāpi kalābharaś catuḥṣaṣṭikalāsv anyatamayā jīvan pañcabhir varṣair vṛddhaś ca | śrotriyo ‘dhītavedaḥ | cāraṇaḥ sahādhyāyī | ete sarve ‘pi bho bhavann iti saṃbhāṣyāḥ | āpastambas tu sarvatrābhivādanam icchati |
daśavarṣaṃ paurasakhyaṃ pañcavarṣaṃ tu cāraṇam |
trivarṣapūrvaḥ śrotriyo ‘bhivādanam arhati || iti | [ĀpDh 1.14.13] || 6.15 ||
pañcabhiḥ kalābharaḥ || 6.16 ||
samānasthānavāsy api yaḥ kalābhir gāndharvacitra-patracchedādibhiḥ kalāvidyādibhir jīvati sa pañca-varṣair vṛddho ‘pi vayasyavad abhibhāṣyaḥ || 6.16 ||
śrotriyaś cāraṇas tribhiḥ || 6.17 ||
śrotriyaḥ mantrabrāhmaṇādhyāyī | cāraṇa samāna-caraṇaḥ |
kecin nṛttagītādiśīlaṃ varṇayanti |
tad ayuktam, kalābharaśabdenaiva gatatvāt || 6.17 ||
rājanyavaiśyakarmā vidyāhīnaḥ || 6.18 || — || 6.16 ||
rājanyakarmaṇā vā vaiśyakarmaṇā vā ya upajīvati sa utkṛṣṭakarmāpi tatsthaḥ tatkarmoparamaṇāt prāg atyantavṛddho ‘pi | yaś ca vidyāhīnaḥ so ‘pi vidyotkṛṣṭasya || 6.18 ||
karmaśabdaḥ pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyate | rājanya-karmā vaiśyakarmā | brāhmaṇo ‘pi rājanyakarmaṇā vaiśyakarmaṇā vā jīvann atyantavṛddho ‘pi bho bhavānn iti saṃbhāṣyaḥ | vidyāhīnaś ca vṛddho ‘pi vidyādhikena tathā bhāṣyaḥ || 6.16 ||
dīkṣitaś ca prāk krayāt || 6.19 || — || 6.17 ||
yo vayasyatvayukto ‘pi dīkṣitaḥ saḥ somavikrayāt prāg eva vayasyaḥ | tataḥ paraṃ mānya ity evamartha upadeśaḥ | cakārād uttamāśramastho ‘pi trivṛtprāśanāt prāg eveti sāmarthyād draṣṭavyam | tathāha uśanā: “yena trivṛtprāśitaḥ sa sarveṣāṃ gurur bhavati” iti
|| 6.19 ||
vayasyaviṣayam idam | dīkṣitaś ca vayasyaḥ soma-krayāt pūrvaṃ tathā bhāṣyaḥ | tataḥ paraṃ vṛddhavan mānyaḥ | uttamāśramaviṣaya uśanā: “śrotriyavat prāśitaḥ sarveṣāṃ gurur bhavati” iti || 6.17 ||
vittabandhukarmajātividyāvayāṃsi
mānyāni || 6.20 ||
vittabandhukarmajātividyāvayāṃsi mānyāni parabalīyāṃsi || 6.18 ||
vittādīnāṃ sākṣānmānyatvāsaṃbhavāt tadvanto mānyā iti draṣṭavyam | vittavān dhanāḍhyaḥ, bandhumān viśiṣṭasodaryādibahulaḥ, karmavān yathoktakārī, jātimān abhijanayuktaḥ, vidyāvān adhītavedaśāstraḥ, vayovān prakṛṣṭavayāḥ || 6.20 ||
vittādīnāṃ sākṣāt mānyatvāsaṃbhavāt tadvanto mānyā ity upalakṣyante | vittavān āḍhyaḥ | bandhumān viśiṣṭaiḥ sodaryādibhir yuktaḥ | karmavān yathoktakarmakārī | jātimān abhijanayuktaḥ | vidyāvān adhītavedaśāstraḥ | vayasvān vayasādhikaḥ | etādṛśā atādṛśair mānyāḥ | parasparasamavāye tu paraḥ paro balīyān prathamamānyaḥ | mānye ‘bhivādanādisaṃmānaḥ || 6.18 ||
parabalīyāṃsi || 6.21 ||
yad yat paraṃ tat tat pūrvasmāt pūrvasmād gurutaraṃ vijñeyam | tataś ca vittavato bandhumān mānyaḥ | ubhābhyām api karmavān ityādi yojyam | mānam abhivādanādi || 6.21 ||
śrutaṃ tu sarvebhyo garīyaḥ || 6.22 || — || 6.19 ||
śrutaṃ vedārthaparijñānam |
nanu ca pūrvasūtra evaitadanta upadeṣṭavyam | tataś ca parabalīyastvād evaitat sidhyatīty ataḥ pṛthag upadeśo ‘narthaḥ |
ucyate – viśeṣārtho ‘yaṃ pṛthagārambhaḥ | viśiṣṭaṃ śrutam atrābhipretam | tac ca vedārthapari-jñānam | pūrvaṃ vedamātrapāṭha eva | tadviśeṣābhi-dhāyy eva tuśabdo ‘pi | tataś cāyaṃ sarvair api mānyaḥ || 6.22 ||
śrutaṃ mantrabrāhmaṇavibhāgena vedārthaparijñānam | tatsarvebhyo vittādibhyo garīyo gurutaram | pūrvasūtre parabalīyāṃsīti śrutam aparam upanyastaṃ tadvyāvṛttyarthaṃ pṛthaksūtram || 6.19 ||
kutaḥ |

tanmūlatvād dharmasya || 6.23 ||
tanmūlatvād dharmasya śruteś ca || 6.20 ||
yac chrutavān ācarati tad api dharmasya mūlatvena pūrvam eva pratipāditam: “tadvidāṃ ca smṛtiśīle” [GDh 1.2] iti | tathā ca manuḥ |
vedo ‘khilo dharmamūlaṃ smṛtiśīle ca tadvidām |
ācāraś caiva sādhūnām ātmanas tuṣṭir eva ca || iti | [MDh 2.6]
atha vā vedārthaparijñānapūrvakatvāt smṛteḥ | yathāha manuḥ |
śrutiṃ paśyanti munayaḥ smaranti tu tathā smṛtim |
iti || 6.23 ||
śrutamūlam anuṣṭhānam anuṣṭhānamūlo dharma iti śruteś cāpy anucchinnasaṃpradāyo mūlam | tasmāc chrutasya garīyastvam | śrutasya garīyastvaṃ chāndogye pratipāditam: “brāhmaṇaṃ śaiśavaṃ bhavati śiśur vai āṅgiraso mantrakṛtāṃ mantrakṛd āsīt” iti | manur api |
adhyāpayāmāsa pitṝñ śiśur āṅgirasaḥ kaviḥ |
putrakā iti hovāca jñānena parigṛhya tān || [MDh 2.151] iti || 6.20 ||
śruteś ca || 6.24 ||
śruter api sa eva garīyān | śrutir api manunoktaṃ śaiśavabrāhmaṇaṃ draṣṭavyam |
adhyāpayāmāsa pitṝn śiśur āṅgirasaḥ kaviḥ |
putrakā iti hovāca jñānena parigṛhya tān || [MDh 2.151] ityādi | śrutyudāharaṇopādānaṃ śrutasyādhyayanaprakarṣe sati balīyastvajñāpanārtham || 6.24 ||
cakridaśamīsthānugrāhyavadhūsnātakarājabhyaḥ patho dānam || 6.25 || — || 6/21 ||
cakryādīnām atadguṇair anyaiḥ patho dānenopakartavyam | cakraṃ śakaṭaṃ tataḥ cakrī | daśamīstho daśamyāṃ daśāyāṃ vartamānaḥ atyantavṛddha ity arthaḥ | anugrāhyo rogārtaḥ | vadhūr garbhiṇītyevamādiḥ | snātako vidyāvratasnātakaḥ | rājā abhiṣiktaḥ || 6.25 ||
cakri cakravac chakaṭādi | tatsthaś cakristhaḥ | daśamyāṃ daśāyāṃ sthito daśamīstho vṛddhaḥ | anugrāhyo rogārtaḥ | vadhūr garbhiṇī | snātako vidyāvratasnātaḥ | rājābhiṣiktaḥ | pathi saṃgama etebhyo ‘nyaiḥ panthā deyaḥ || 6.21 ||
