tatra viśeṣaḥ |
rājñā tu śrotriyāya śrotriyāya || 6.26 || — || 6.22 ||
tuśabdo viśeṣaṇārthaḥ | yadi rājālpataraḥ syāt śrotriyas tv adhikasaṃpannaḥ prakṛta eva snātakaḥ, tatas tasmai rājñā panthā deyaḥ | evaṃ cānyai rājña eva deyaḥ | pūrveṣām api yathānugrāhyaṃ parasparasaṃnidhāne draṣṭavyam || 6.26 ||
śrotriyasamāgame rājñaiva panthā deyaḥ | abhyāso ‘dhyāyasamāptyarthaḥ || 6.22 ||
atha saptamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
“śrutaṃ tu sarvebhyo garīyaḥ” [GDh 6.22] iti vidyāprādhānyam uktam | tadadhigamaś ca brāhmaṇāt kartavyaḥ, tasyaiva pravacanādhikārāt | yadā tu pravacanasamarthabrāhmaṇābhāvaḥ tadā katham ity āha |
śrutaṃ tu sarvebhyo garīya iti vidyāprādhānyam uktam | sā vidyā brāhmaṇād adhigantavyeti prathamaḥ kalpaḥ | tadabhāve vidyāyā avaśyādhigantavyatvād āpatkalpam āha |
āpatkalpo brāhmaṇasyābrāhmaṇād vidyopayogaḥ || 7.1 ||
āpatkalpa ityādhyāyaparisamāpter adhikāraḥ kriyate | āpat pravacanasamarthabrāhmaṇābhāvaḥ, tatra yaḥ kalpaḥ vidhiḥ saḥ āpatkalpaḥ | āpady api kṣatriya-vaiśyayor brāhmaṇād eva vidyādhigamaprāpaṇārthaṃ brāhmaṇagrahaṇam | abrāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriyavaiśyāḥ, itareṣām adhyayanādhikārābhāvāt | tatrāpi prathamaṃ kṣatriyāt tato vaiśyād iti draṣṭavyam, “tadalābhe vaiśyavṛttiḥ” [GDh 7.7] iti darśanāt | vidyāś caturdaśa | tathā smṛtyantaram |
aṅgāni vedāś catvāro mīmāṃsā nyāyavistaraḥ |
purāṇaṃ dharmaśāstraṃ ca vidyās tv etāś caturdaśa || iti | upayogaḥ svīkaraṇam, yujeḥ saṃbandhavacanatvāt | anayaivānyathānupapattyā kṣatriyavaiśyayor adhyāpanam anumīyate | tad apy adṛṣṭārtham eva,
ṣaṇṇāṃ tu karmaṇām asya trīṇi karmāṇi jīvikā |
yājanādhyāpane caiva viśuddhāc ca pratigrahaḥ || iti | [MDh 10.76] brāhmaṇasyaiva dṛṣṭārthaṃ netarayor iti manunā pratiṣiddhatvāt | abrāhmaṇād adhyayanam iti vaktavye vidyopayoga ity ārambhaḥ āpadi abrāhmaṇād adhyayanam eva kartavyam iti niyamārthaḥ | tataś ca brahmahatyādau sarvasvadānacodanāyāṃ pratigrahasamarthabrāhmaṇābhāve ‘pi kṣatriyāder na dātavyam || 7.1 ||
aṅgāni vedāś catvāro mīmāṃsā nyāyavistaraḥ |
purāṇaṃ dharmaśāstraṃ ca vidyā hy etāś caturdaśa || upayogo niyamapūrvakaṃ grahaṇam | abrāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo vaiśyaś ca, tasmād brāhmaṇena vidyopayogaḥ kāryaḥ sa āpatkalpaḥ | āpadvidhir brāhmaṇasyety upalakṣaṇam | tena kṣatriyeṇāpi vaiśyād vidyopayogaḥ kārya iti sidhyati | āpatkalpa ity adhyāyaparisamāpter adhikriyate || 7.1 ||
idānīm adhyayanadvāreṇa samastaśuśrūṣāprāptāv āha
anugamanaṃ śuśrūṣā || 7.2 ||
gacchataḥ pṛṣṭhato ‘nugamanamātram eva śuśrūṣā | nānyad abhivādanādi kartavyam | adhyayanakālād anyad etad ucyate, tatrārthaprāptatvāt || 7.2 ||
tatra yāvad adhyayanakālam anugamanam eva śuśrūṣā nānyat pādasaṃvāhanādi || 7.2 ||
samāpte brāhmaṇo guruḥ || 7.3 ||
samāpte adhyayane ‘nugamanamātram api na kartavyam | brāhmaṇasyaiva tenādhyāpakena kartavyam, “anugamanaṃ śuśrūṣāsamāpteḥ” iti vaktavye “brāhmaṇo gurur” iti vacanāt || 7.3 ||
samāpte tv adhyayane brāhmaṇa eva guruḥ || 7.3 ||
idānīm anyo ‘py āpatkalpa ucyate |
yājanādhyāpanapratigrahāḥ sarveṣām || 7.4 ||
sarvagrahaṇenāpraśastās traya eva varṇā gṛhyante praśastānām anāpadi vidhānāt, śūdrasya ‘vṛttiś cen nāntareṇa śūdrāt” [GDh 17.5] ityārambhāt | pañcamyantaḥ sarvaśabdaḥ prāpnotīti cet – sakāśād ity adhyāhārān na doṣaḥ | sarveṣām iti bahuvacanam anulomopasaṃgrahārtham | brāhmaṇasyaitad ucyate, adhikārāt, “tadalābhe kṣatravṛttiḥ” iti ca liṅgāt | tataś cāsyām avasthāyāṃ garhitayājanādibhir na doṣaḥ, śāstracodanāsāmarthyāt | tathāha manuḥ |
nādhyāpanād yājanād vā garhitād vā pratigrahāt |
doṣo bhavati viprāṇāṃ jvalanāmbusamā hi te || [MDh 10.103]
sarvataḥ pratigṛhṇīyāt brāhmaṇas tv anayaṃ gataḥ || [MDh 10.102] ityādi | tathā ca śrutiḥ: “tasmād yayā kayā ca vidhayā bahvannaṃ prāpnuyāt” [TaitUp 3.10.1] iti || 7.4 ||
yājanādayo brāhmaṇasya vṛttayas tā āpadi sarveṣām anujñāyante na tu brāḥmaṇasyaiveti | apara āha – āpadi sarve yājayitavyāḥ sarve ‘dhyāpyāḥ sarvataś ca pratigrāhyaṃ na tu garhādoṣo ‘stīti | tathā ca manuḥ |
nādhyāpanād yājanād vā garhitād vā pratigrahāt |
doṣo bhavati viprāṇāṃ jvalanāmbusamo hi saḥ || [MDh 10.103] iti || 7.4 ||
pūrvaḥ pūrvo guruḥ || 7.5 ||
eṣāṃ yājanādīnāṃ yo yaḥ pūrvaṃ nirdiṣṭaḥ sa sa uttarasmād gurur jñeyaḥ | tataś ca uttareṇottareṇa jīvanāsaṃbhave pūrveṇa pūrveṇa jīved iti kramapratipādanārthaṃ pūrvavacanam | kramalaṅghane prāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam, pūrvaḥ pūrvaḥ sann iti vaktavye gurugrahaṇāt || 7.5 ||
eteṣāṃ yājanādīnāṃ yo yaḥ pūrvanirdiṣṭaḥ sa sa uttara-smād gurur jñeyaḥ | āpadi pratigraheṇa jīvet tadasaṃ-bhave ‘dhyāpanena tadasaṃbhave yājaneneti || 7.5 ||
tadalābhe kṣatravṛttiḥ || 7.6 ||
syād iti śeṣaḥ | pūrvaprakārāyāḥ brāhmaṇavṛtter alābhe kṣatravṛttiḥ savādinā upajīvanaṃ rakṣaṇādinā vā | tadgrahaṇam atyantanirguṇāpi brāhmaṇavṛttir eva varam iti jñāpanārtham | tathā ca manuḥ |
śreyān svadharmo viguṇaḥ paradharmāt svanuṣṭhitāt |
paradharmeṇa jīvan hi sadyaḥ patati jātitaḥ || iti | [MDh 10.97] | “tathā vā kṣatravṛttiḥ” ity ukte abhāve vaikalpikena vāśabdenaiva brāhmaṇavṛttyabhāve kṣatravṛttir iti labhyamāne alābhagrahaṇaṃ kṣatravṛttyā jīvato ‘pi yadā brāhmaṇavṛttir eva jivanāyālaṃ bhavati tadā kṣatravṛttim utsṛjya brāhmaṇavṛttir evāśrayitavyeti | tathā ca nāradaḥ |
āpadaṃ brāhmaṇas tīrtvā kṣatravṛttyā bhṛte jane |
utsṛjet kṣatravṛttiṃ tāṃ kṛtvā pāvanam ātmanaḥ || NSm 1.55] || 7.6 ||
idaṃ brāhmaṇaviṣayam | garhitayājanāder apy alābhe kṣatravṛttiḥ syāt | brāhmaṇaḥ sevādinā jīvet | āpadi nivṛttāyāṃ nāradaḥ |
āpadaṃ brāhmaṇas tīrtvā kṣatravṛttyā bhṛte jane |
utsṛjet kṣātravṛttiṃ tāṃ kṛtvā pāvanam ātmanaḥ || [NSm 1.55] iti || 7.6 ||
tadalābhe vaiśyavṛttiḥ || 7.7 ||
alābhagrahaṇaṃ vṛttisaṃkaro mā bhūd iti | evaṃ ca saṃkare ‘pi prāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam | tadgrahaṇam uktavyākhyānam | atra tu vidhānābhāvād eva uttama-vṛttyāśrayaṇaprasaṅga eva nāsti | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “na tu kadācij jyāyasīm” [VaDh 2.23] iti || 7.7 ||
kṣatravṛtter apy alābhe vaiśyavṛttyāpi jīved brāhmaṇaḥ | alābhagrahaṇaṃ vṛttisaṃkaro mā bhūd iti | kṣatriyasya vaiśyavṛttyupajīvanaṃ daṇḍāpūpanyāyena siddham || 7.7 ||
tasyāpaṇyam || 7.8 ||
tadgrahaṇaṃ brāhmaṇasyaiva na kṣatriyasya vaiśyavṛttisthitasyeti | evaṃ ca vaiśyasyārthasiddhaṃ vikreyatvaṃ pratiṣiddhaṃ tyaktvā datvā pratiṣiddhasyāpratiṣiddhasya cādānam | tadabhāve apaṇyam api vikreyam || 7.8 ||
tasya vaiśyavṛtter brāhmaṇasyāpaṇyena vikreyaṃ vakṣyate | tasyeti vacanāt kṣatriyasya vaiśyavṛttyupajīvino vakṣyamāṇam apaṇyaṃ na bhavati || 7.8 ||
kiṃ tad apaṇyam ity ata āha |
gandharasakṛtānnatilaśāṇakṣaumājināni || 7.9 ||
gandham anulepanam, rasas tailādi, kṛtānnaṃ modakādi, tilāḥ prasiddhāḥ, śāṇaṃ śaṇavikāraḥ, kṣaumaṃ kṣumā atasī tadvikāraḥ | vikārapratiṣedhāt prakṛter apratiṣedhaḥ | ajinaṃ carma || 7.9 ||
gandhaś candanādiḥ | rasas tailaghṛtalavaṇaguḍādiḥ | kṛtānnaṃ modakāpūpādi | tilāḥ prasiddhāḥ | śāṇaṃ śaṇavikāro goṇyādiḥ | kṣaumaṃ kṣumodbhūtaṃ paṭṭavastraviśeṣaḥ | ajinaṃ carma kaṭādi | etāny avikreyāṇi | śāṇakṣaumayor vikāraniṣedhāt prakṛter apartiṣedhaḥ || 7.9 ||
raktanirṇikte vāsasī || 7.10 ||
raktaṃ lākṣādivikṛtam, nirṇiktaṃ prakṣālitam || 7.10 ||
raktaṃ lākṣādinā vikṛtam | nirṇiktaṃ rajakādinā dhautam | evaṃbhūte api vāsasī apaṇye || 7.10 ||
kṣīraṃ savikāram || 7.11 ||
kṣīraṃ payaḥ vikāraiḥ saha dadhyādibhiḥ | iha vikāragrahaṇāt tilavikārāṇām apratiṣedho vijñāyate | atha vā yatra kevalasya kṣīrasyaiva nirdeśaḥ tatra savikārasyāpi grahaṇam ity evamarthaṃ vikāragrahaṇam | yaḥ pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ tasmin śāṇakṣaumayor vikāranirdeśaḥ prakṛtipratiṣedhārtha iti vaktavyam || 7.11 ||
dadhyādibhir vikāraiḥ saha kṣīram apaṇyam || 7.11 ||
mūlaphalapuṣpauṣadhamadhumāṃsatṛṇodakāpathyāni || 7.12 ||
mūlaṃ haridrādi, phalaṃ kramukaphalādi, puṣpaṃ damanakādi, auṣadhaṃ masūrādi, madhu mākṣikādi | rasagrahaṇenaiva madhunaḥ pratiṣedhe siddhe “sarvathā tu vṛttir aśaktau” [GDh 7.22] ity atrāpi pakṣe pratiṣedhārthaṃ punar vacanam | māṃsodake prasiddhe | tṛṇaṃ prasiddham | apathyaṃ viṣam || 7.12 ||
mūlam ārdrakaharidrādi | phalaṃ pūgādi | puṣpaṃ cambakādi | auṣadhaṃ pippalyādi | madhu mākṣikam | māṃsatṛṇodakāni prasiddhāni | apathyaṃ viṣādi | etāny apaṇyāni | rasaśabdena pūrvam eva niṣiddhe ‘pi punar madhugrahaṇaṃ sarvathā vṛttir aśaktāv ityādipakṣe niṣedhartham || 7.12 ||
paśavaś ca hiṃsāsaṃyoge || 7.13 ||
paśavo gavādayaḥ hiṃsāsaṃyoge na vikreyāḥ | yoga iti siddhe upasargārambhād yajamānahaste ‘pi na vikreyāḥ | tatra hiṃsāyāṃ doṣābhāvād hiṃsāsaṃyogo nāstīti cet, satyam, sa tu yajamānasyaiva netarasyeti | caśabdān mayūrādīni ca || 7.13 ||
paśavo ‘jādayaḥ | hiṃsāsaṃyoge saunikādibhyo hiṃsārthe na vikreyāḥ || 7.13 ||
puruṣavaśākumārīvehataś ca nityam || 7.14 ||
puruṣo manuṣyaḥ, tadgrahaṇaṃ striyā api, jātivacanatvāt | tathā ca śrutiḥ: “puruṣaśīrṣam upadadhāti na striyāḥ” iti | vaśā vandhyā gauḥ | kumārī gaur eva | manuṣyakumāryāḥ strītvāt puruṣagrahaṇenaiva siddhatvāt | vehat garbhaghātinī, sāpi gaur eva, pūrvoktenaiva nyāyena | caśabdaḥ kākavandhyādyupasaṃgrahaṇārthaḥ | nityam, hiṃsāsaṃyogād anyatrāpīti kecit | tad ayuktam, hiṃsāsaṃyoge paśutvād eva siddhe punar ārambhasāmarthyād evānyatra labdhuṃ śakyata iti | nityaśabdena dravyaṃ viśeṣayanti nityam evaṃrūpā iti | tad apy ayuktam, puruṣādayaḥ kadācid apy anevaṃrūpā na bhaveyur iti | tataś ca nityaśabdo ‘nyeṣāṃ tilādīnām anityatvasūcanārthaḥ | tataḥ svayaṃ kṛṣyotpāditānāṃ tilādīnāṃ vikreyatvam, anyathotpāditānām avikreyatvam iti draṣṭavyam | tathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “kāmaṃ vā svayaṃ kṛṣyotpādy tilān vikrīṇīran” [VaDh 2.31] iti | tilān api vikrīṇīran kiṃ punar anyān iti tatrārthaḥ || 7.14 ||
puruṣāḥ dāsādayaḥ | vaśā vandhyā gauḥ | kumārī vatsatarī | vehad garbhopaghātinī | ete nityam apaṇyāḥ | nityam ity uktatvād dhiṃsāsaṃyogād anyatrāpi niṣedhaḥ | apara āha – iha nityagrahaṇāt pūrveṣu tilādiṣv anityaḥ pratiṣedha iti | tatra vasiṣṭhaḥ: “kāmaṃ vā svayam utpādya tilān vikrīṇīran” [VaDh 2.31] iti
|| 7.14 ||
bhūmivrīhiyavājāvyaśvarṣabhadhenvanaḍuhaś caike || 7.15 ||
bhūmiḥ gṛhaṃ “kṣayo veśma gṛhaṃ bhūmir niketanam” ityādi naighaṇṭuke gṛhaparyāyaśravaṇāt | vrīhiḥ prasiddhaḥ, yavaś ca, tadgrahaṇaṃ dhānyopalakṣaṇam | ajaḥ chāgaḥ | aviḥ prasiddhaḥ | aśvo hayaḥ | ṛṣabhaḥ puṅgavaḥ | dhenur dogdhrī | anaḍvān śakaṭavahanasamartho balīvardaḥ | ete na vikeyāḥ hiṃsāsaṃyogād anyatrāpīty eke ācāryāḥ, na tu gautamaḥ | uṣṭropasaṃgrahaṇārthaś cakāraḥ | yathāha uśanā: “uṣṭrarṣabhahayā na vikreyāḥ” iti | nityatvānukarṣaṇārthaś ca ity eke
|| 7.15 ||
bhūmir gṛham | vrīhiyavājāvyaśvāḥ prasiddhāḥ | ṛṣabhaḥ secanasamartho gauḥ | dhenuḥ sakṛtprasūtā | anaḍvān anovāhanayogyo balīvardaḥ | ete cāpaṇyā ity eke manyante | ekaśabdād vayaṃ tv anujānīmaḥ | atrāpy ajāvigrahaṇaṃ hiṃsāsaṃyogaviṣayaparam
|| 7.15 ||
nimayas tu || 7.16 ||
kartavya iti śeṣaḥ | nimayaḥ dravyābhyāṃ pratiṣiddhā-bhyāṃ parivṛttiḥ | tuśabdo viśeṣavācī, samato hīnato vāpi nimayaḥ kartavyaḥ | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “samato hīnato vāpi nimātavyaḥ” iti || 7.16 ||
niyamo vinimayaḥ parivartanaṃ tuśabdena niyamo ‘nujñāyata iti || 7.16 ||
rasānāṃ rasaiḥ || 7.17 ||
nimaye kartavye rasānāṃ tailādīnāṃ rasair guḍādibhir eva nimayaḥ kartavya iti niyamārthaḥ sutrārambhaḥ
|| 7.17 ||
tailaghṛtaguḍādīnāṃ rasair eva vinimayaḥ karyaḥ | tad yathā: tailaṃ datvā ghṛtaṃ grāhyam iti | “rasaiḥ samato hīnato vā” iti vasiṣṭhaḥ [VaDh 2.37] || 7.17 ||
paśūnāṃ ca || 7.18 ||
tṛtīyāntapaśuśabdaprāpaṇārthaś cakāraḥ, paśūnāṃ paśubhir iti | pratiṣiddhavikrayapaśuviṣayaṃ draṣṭavyam | apratiṣiddhānāṃ vikreyatvād eva siddhatvāt || 7.18 ||
paśūnāṃ catuṣpadāṃ paśubhir vinimayaḥ karyaḥ
|| 7.18 ||
na lavaṇakṛtānnayoḥ || 7.19 ||
lavaṇaṃ rasāntareṇa na nimātavyam | rasānāṃ rasair iti niyamāt prāptaṃ pratiṣidhyate | kṛtānnaṃ yena kenacid api dravyāntareṇa, prāptyabhāvāt || 7.19 ||
lavaṇasya kṛtānnasya ca vinimayo ‘pi pratiṣiddhaḥ
|| 7.19 ||
tilānāṃ ca || 7.20 ||
nimayapratiṣedhānukarṣārthaś caśabdaḥ – tilānāṃ yena kenacid api nimayo nāstīty arthaḥ | āpadi dhānyena samena nimayābhyanujñānārthaṃ pṛthaggrahaṇam | tathāha manuḥ: “tilā dhānyena tatsamāḥ” [MDh 10.94] iti || 7.20 ||
tilānāṃ ca vinimayo na kāryaḥ | lavaṇakṛtānnatilānāṃ dravyāntarasvīkāreṇa pradānaṃ niṣiddham, samānadravyaviṣaye pravṛttasaṃbhavāt || 7.20 ||
idānīṃ kṛtānnasya pratiprasavārtham āha |
samenāmena tu pakvasya saṃpratyarthe || 7.21 ||
samena tulyenāmena saha pakvasya nimayo bhavati | saṃpratyarthe yadi tasminn eva kāle kṛtānnam upabhujyate | tuśabdaḥ samaviśeṣavācī, tataś ca taṇḍuleneti draṣṭavyam |
kecit – tenaiva prakāreṇa nimayaḥ kartavyaḥ nānyeneti niyamārthaṃ tuśabdam āhuḥ |
tad ayuktam, sūtrārambhasāmarthyād eva niyamasiddheḥ | yadi yena kenacin nimayo bhavet tadānīm “nimayas tu” iti sāmānyavihitenaiva siddhatvād anārambhaḥ prāpnoti | tataś ca rasādīni yāni dravyāṇi tāni dravyaviśeṣair eva nimātavyāni | śeṣāṇāṃ trayāṇāṃ yena kenacid iti draṣṭavyam | taṇḍulaiḥ prasthādiparimitaiḥ saha tanmātrair eva pakvānnasya niyamaḥ kartavya ity arthaḥ | evaṃ ca “gandharasakṛtānna” ity atra kṛtānnaśabdas taṇḍuleṣv api vartata iti jñāpayati | anyathā vikrayapratiṣedhā-bhāvān nimayārambo na prāpnoti, yasmāt pratiṣiddhānām eva nimayārambha iti || 7.21 ||
samena samaparimāṇenāmena taṇḍulena saṃpratyarthe tādātmikopayogārthe pakvānnasya niyamaḥ kāryaḥ | manus tu “tilā dhānyena tatsamā” [MDh 10.94] iti samena dhānyena tilānāṃ niyamam anujānāti | apaṇyam iti vikrayaniṣedhāt sarvatra yāvad upayogakraye niṣedho na syāt | rasādīnām api niyamaśabdena pradānam eva vivakṣitam | anyathā tv avidyamānena rasāntarāder dravyāṇy āvṛttyasaṃbhavāt || 7.21 ||
sarvathā tu vṛttir aśaktāv aśaudreṇa || 7.22 ||
pratiṣiddhavikrayanimayajātyantaravṛttisamuccayair api jīvet | aśaktau atyantāpadi, aśaudreṇa tatrāpi śūdravṛttim ucchiṣṭāśanaśilpavṛttyādi varjayet | āpad-vihitasya kiṃcit pāpasūcanārthas tuśabdaḥ | udāhṛtaṃ ca smṛtyantaram: “kṛtvā pāvanam ātmanaḥ” iti || 7.22 ||
uktena prakāreṇa kuṭumbadhāraṇasyāsaṃbhavo ‘śaktiḥ | tasyāṃ satyāṃ sarvathā vṛttiḥ | prakāravacane thāl, uktena sarvaprakāreṇa niṣiddhenāpi jīvet | tatrāpi na śaudreṇa karmaṇā jīved iti || 7.22 ||
tad apy eke prāṇasaṃśaye || 7.23 ||
tad api śūdrakarma maraṇasaṃdehe prāpte eke icchanti, prāṇānām avaśyarakṣaṇīyatvāt | tathā ca vyāsaḥ |
dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāṃ prāṇāḥ saṃsthitihetavaḥ |
tān nighnatā kiṃ na hataṃ rakṣatā kiṃ na rakṣitam || iti | prāṇagrahaṇena ca tadadhīnavṛttīnāṃ putra-dārādīnām api grahaṇam “prāṇā hy ete bahiścarāḥ” iti darśanāt | ekegrahaṇān na tu gautamaḥ, asyām apy avasthāyāṃ garhitatvād iti | iha prāṇasaṃśayagrahaṇāt vijñāyate anyāpy āpad astīti | tataś ca yasyām avasthāyāṃ pūrvakalpopadeśaḥ sā nityānāṃ karmaṇām anuṣṭhānasāmarthyalakṣaṇā vijñeyā | yathāha manuḥ |
devatātithibhṛtyānāṃ pitṝṇām ātmanaś ca yaḥ |
na nirvapati pañcānām ucchavasan na sa jīvati || [MDh 3.72] iti || 7.23 ||
eke tv ācāryāḥ prāṇasaṃśaye sati tad api śaudraṃ karmāpy anumanyante | yathāha vyāsaḥ:
dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāṃ prāṇāḥ saṃsthitihetavaḥ |
tān nighnatā kiṃ na hataṃ rakṣatā kiṃ na rakṣitam || iti || 7.23 ||
tadvarṇasaṃkarābhakṣyaniyamas tu || 7.24 ||
tadgrahaṇena śūdra ucyate | saṃkaraḥ sahāsanādinā | abhakṣyaṃ taducchiṣṭādi, tadyogyaṃ vā laśunādi, tayor niyamo varjanam | tuśabdo viśeṣavācī – asyām avasthāyāṃ viśeṣeṇa brāhmaṇasyeti || 7.24 ||
niyamo varjanam | śūdravṛttisthitenāpi brāhmaṇena tena śūdravarṇena sahāsanāṅgasaṃmelanādiḥ saṃkaraḥ | abhakṣyaṃ ca laśunādi | tadubhayavarjanaṃ kartavyaṃ na tu śūdravṛttir asmīti yathākāmyam iti
|| 7.24 ||
prāṇasaṃśaye brāhmaṇo ‘pi śastram ādadīta || 7.25 ||
prāṇagrahaṇena putradārahiraṇyāder api grahaṇam, “prāṇā hy ete bahiścarāḥ” iti darśanāt | apiśabdād vaiśyaśūdrāv api | kṣatriyasyārthasiddhatvāt rakṣaṇādau pravṛtteḥ | yad vā kṣatriyasyāpi || 7.25 ||
prāṇasaṃśaye sati brāhmaṇo ‘pi rakṣārthaṃ śastram ādadīta | tadalābhe kṣatravṛttir iti śastragrahaṇe siddhe punar upādanaṃ brāhmaṇavṛtteḥ sato ‘py aniṣedhā-rtham | apiśabdāt kiṃ punar vaiśyaśūdrau || 7.25 ||
rājanyo vaiśyakarma vaiśyakarma || 7.26 ||
kṣatriyo ‘pi svakarmaṇā jīvitum aśaktaḥ san vaiśyavṛttim ātiṣṭhet tasyāpy apaṇyapratiṣedho nāstīty uktam | yathā brāhmaṇasya kṣatriyavṛttir eva kṣatriyasyāpi vaiśyavṛttiḥ tathā vaiśyasyāpi śūdravṛttiḥ, yathā brāhmaṇasya vaiśyavṛttir evaṃ kṣatriyasyāpi śūdravṛttir ity evaṃ kalpanā na bhaved iti jñāpanārthaḥ sūtrārambhaḥ | kutaḥ? yadi brāhmaṇavad itareṣām abhīṣṭaṃ syāt tadā sarve anantaraikāntarāṃ vṛttim ātiṣṭheyur ity avakṣyat | tad anuktvā “rājanyo vaiśyakarma” ityārambhāt kṣatriyasyaivāpadi vihitā vṛttiḥ na vaiśyaśūdrayoḥ, kṣatriyasyāpi na śūdravṛttir iti jñāpayati | tataś ca kṣatriyo ‘pi vaiśyavṛttim ātiṣṭhed āpadi | tathā ca manuḥ |
jīved etena rājanyaḥ sarveṇāpy anayaṃ gataḥ || [MDh 10.95] iti || 7.26 ||
prāṇasaṃśaye rājanyo vaiśyakarmādadīta | tenātmānaṃ rakṣet [abhyāso ‘dhyāyasamāptyarthaḥ] || 7.26 ||
athāṣṭamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
atha yady āpadvihite karmaṇi kaścid doṣāparijñānāt lobhād vā prasajyeta tato ‘sau kena nivāryaḥ | rāja-brāhmaṇābhyām upadeśadaṇḍābhyām | yatas tāv eva |
āpadvṛttim āśrito yadi tatraiva rameta kenāsau nivāryata ity āha |
dvau loke dhṛtavratau rājā brāhmaṇaś ca bahuśrutaḥ || 8.1 ||
dvāv iti prakarṣavācī na saṃkhyānirdeśaḥ dvivacana-nirdeśād eva dvitvasiddheḥ | asti cātiśayārthaḥ saṃkhyānirdeśaḥ, yathā: “asmin grāme dvāv eva brāhmaṇau” iti | tatra gamyate – anye ‘pi brāhmaṇāḥ santi, dvāv evānuṣṭhānaparāv iti | atrāpi rājabrāhma-ṇayor atiśayena dhṛtavratatvārtham, nānyeṣāṃ pratiṣedhārtham | lokagrahaṇaṃ vipsārtham, lokasaṃghāte loke rāṣṭra ity arthaḥ | anyathā sarvakarmaṇāṃ lokādhikāratvād anarthakam evaitat syād iti | evaṃ ca yathā pratirāṣṭraṃ rājñā bhavitavyaṃ tathā bahuśrutenāpi bhavitavyam iti gamyate | dhṛtavratau vratānāṃ karmaṇāṃ dhārayitārau | rājā kṣatriyaḥ, sa cābhiṣekādiguṇayuktaḥ “sa mūrdhābhi-ṣikto rājā bhavati” iti śaṅkhavacanāt | brāhmaṇaś ca bahuśrutaḥ | saty api brāhmaṇasya prādhānye rājñaḥ pūrvanirdeśo bahuśrutatvasya brāhmaṇaviśeṣaṇārtham | rājñe ‘pi bahuśrutatvānukarṣaṇārthaś cakāra | tataś ca rājño ‘pi bahuśrutatvena bhavitavyam, brāhmaṇasya viśeṣeṇeti draṣṭavyam | bahuśrutaḥ bahv anena śrutam iti | rājabrāhmaṇau bahuśrutāv iti vaktavye visamāsanirdeśo rājño ‘py atiśayena śauryādiguṇayogārthaḥ | tataś ca tau dhṛtavratau syātām iti śeṣaḥ || 8.1 ||
loko rāṣṭram | vīpsālopaś cātra draṣṭavyaḥ | loke loke dhṛtavratau vratānāṃ karmaṇāṃ dhārayitārau dvau rājā bahuśrutaś ca brāhmaṇaḥ | tau sarvasya sarvāpado daṇḍopadeśābhyāṃ nivārayitārau || 8.1 ||
yataḥ |
tayoś caturvidhasya manuṣyajātasyāntaḥsaṃjñānāṃ ca calanapatanasarpaṇānām
āyattaṃ jīvanam || 8.2 ||
tayoḥ samuditayoḥ samānanirdeśāt | caturvidhasya manuṣyajātasya cāturvarṇyasyety arthaḥ | antaḥsaṃjñāḥ anulomapratilomāḥ cakārāt devānāṃ ca, “itaḥ pratidānād dhi devā upajīvanti” iti śruteḥ | calanāḥ sthāvarāḥ vṛkṣādayaḥ, patanāḥ pakṣiṇaḥ, sarpaṇā kīṭādayaḥ | eteṣām āyattam ādhīnaṃ jīvanaṃ prāṇadhāraṇam | kathaṃ? brāhmaṇenānugṛhyamāṇā varṇāśramiṇaḥ dṛṣṭādṛṣṭārthakriyānuṣṭhānāt vṛṣṭyādinimittadvāreṇa lokopakāre vartanta iti tāvad brāhmaṇāyattam | tathā ca manuḥ |
agnau prāstāhutiḥ samyag ādityam upatiṣṭhate | [MDh 3.76] ityādi | rājā ca paripanthinigrahadvāreṇa hiṃsānigrahadvāreṇa ca sākṣāj jīvanahetur iti || 8.2 ||
caturvidhasya manuṣyajātasya cāturvarṇyasyāntaraprabhā(bha)vās tv anulomādayas tanmūlatvāt pṛthaṅ noktāḥ | antaḥsaṃjñā vṛkṣādayaḥ sthāvarā vṛddhikṣayavantaḥ | yeṣām antaḥsaṃjñā na bahis te tathoktāḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
tamasā bahurūpeṇa ceṣṭitāḥ karmahetunā |
antaḥsaṃjñā bhavanty ete samaduḥkhasamanvitāḥ || iti | [MDh 1.49] calanāḥ paśvādayaḥ | patanāḥ pakṣiṇaḥ | sarpaṇāḥ sarīsṛpā bhujagādayaḥ | eṣāṃ manuṣyādīnāṃ jīvanaṃ tayo rājabrāhmaṇayor āyattaṃ tadadhīnam | rājā tu paripanthinigrahādinā teṣāṃ jīvanahetuḥ | itaras tu kathaṃ bahuśruta ity ata āha |
agnau prāstāhutiḥ samyag ādityam upatiṣṭhate |
ādityāj jāyate vṛṣṭir vṛṣṭer annaṃ tataḥ prajāḥ || [MDh 3.76] ityādinyāyena jīvane hetuḥ || 8.2 ||
na ca jīvanamātram eva tayor āyattam, kiṃ tu |
na ca jīvanamātram eva tadadhīnaṃ kiṃ tarhi |
prasūtī rakṣaṇam asaṃkaro dharmaḥ || 8.3 ||
H reads: “prasūtirakṣaṇam”
prasūtir abhivṛddhiḥ, sā ca tadāyattā, rājabrāhmaṇa-daṇḍadhāraṇopadeśābhyāṃ yathoktakāritvāt vṛṣṭyādidvāreṇa rogādyupadravābhāvāc ca vṛddhir bhavatīti | corādinigrahād rakṣaṇaṃ ca daṇḍadhāraṇa-prāyaścittopadeśāt, saṃkarābhāvaś ca yamaniya-mopadeśāt | vihitākaraṇe pratiṣiddhasevane ca daṇḍadhāraṇād dharmo ‘pi tadāyatta eva || 8.3 ||
prasūtir abhivṛddhiḥ | daṇḍopadeśābhyāṃ yathokta-kāritayā vṛṣṭyādidvāreṇa rogādyupadravaśāntyā cābhivṛddhir bhavati | coranigrahād rakṣaṇam api | daṇḍaprāyaścittopadeśābhyāṃ bhavati varṇānām asaṃkaro ‘saṃmelanam api | vihitopadeśāt pratiṣiddhasevāyāṃ daṇḍadhāraṇāc ca dharmo ‘pi bhavati | etat sarvaṃ tayor āyattam || 8.3 ||
idānīṃ kiyatā punaḥ śrutena bahuśruto bhavatīty ata āha |
bahuśruta ity uktaṃ pratipādayati |
sa eṣa bahuśruto bhavati || 8.4 ||
sa eṣa iti parokṣapratyakṣopadeśaḥ | “śrutaṃ tu sarvebhyo garīyaḥ” [GDh 6.22] ity atrāpy evaṃrūpasyaiva grahaṇam iti | bahuśruto bhavati saṃpadyata ity arthaḥ | bhavatigrahaṇaṃ ca yady apy eṣāṃ lokādīnām īṣadvijñānaṃ bhavati kiṃcid anyad api śrutam, tathāpi bhavaty evety evamartham, anyathā sa eva bahuśruta ity eva siddhatvāt || 8.4 ||
sa eṣa iti vakṣyamāṇanirdeśaḥ || 8.4 ||
lokavedavedāṅgavit || 8.5 ||
lokaśabdena lokavyavahārā grāmamaryādādaya ucyante | vedaśabdena upavedā āyurvedadhanur-vedādayaḥ ucyante | dvitīyena vedaśabdena catvāro vedā ucyante | aṅgaśabdena vyākaraṇaśikṣāchando-vicitikalpasūtrajyautiṣaniruktāny ucyante | vicchabdaḥ pratyakam abhisaṃbadhyate | vedavvid ity anenaiva vedapāṭho ‘pi draṣṭavyaḥ, smṛtyantare anadhītavedasyānyavidyāpratiṣedhāt | yathāha manuḥ |
yo ‘nadhītya dvijo vedān anyatra kurute śramam |
sa jīvann eva śūdratvam āśu gacchati sānvayaḥ || [MDh 2.168] iti || 8.5 ||
lokaśabdena lokavyavahārasiddhā janapadādidharmā ucyante | teṣāṃ vedāś catvāra ṛgyajuḥsāmātharvaṇāḥ | aṅgāni ṣaṭ | vyākaraṇaṃ śikṣā chando jyotiṣaṃ kalpasūtrāṇi niruktam api | teṣāṃ vettā pāṭhato ‘rthathaś ca || 8.5 ||
vākovākyetihāsapurāṇakuśalaḥ || 8.6 ||
vākovākyam uktipratyuktirūpaṃ tarkaśāstram ity arthaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
yas tarkeṇānusaṃdhatte sa dharmaṃ veda netaraḥ || iti | [MDh 12.106] itihāso bhāratarāmāyaṇam | purāṇam – yatra sṛṣṭisthitisaṃhārā ucyante tat purāṇaṃ brahmāṇḍādi | eteṣv api kuśalaḥ nipuṇaḥ artheṣv eva na pūrveṣv iva granthārthayoḥ, kriyāpadāntarārambhāt | evaṃ ca na kevalaṃ veda eva paṭhitavyaḥ, aṅgaṃ ca | tathā ca smṛtyantaram: “sāṅgo vedo ‘dhyetavyaḥ” iti
|| 8.6 ||
vedaśāstropayogīni tarkoktipratyuktirūpāṇi vākyāni | yathā mahābhārate – kaḥ svid ekākī carati sūrya ekākī caratītyādīni vākovākyam | bhāratarāmāyaṇādīnītihāsaḥ | purāṇaṃ viṣṇupurāṇaśivapurāṇādyaṣṭādaśavidham | teṣu kuśalaḥ samarthaḥ || 8.6 ||
tadapekṣas tadvṛttiḥ || 8.7 ||
tadapekṣaḥ vedaprasiddhārthānuvacanaśīlaḥ | tadvṛttiḥ tadabhihitakarmānuṣṭhānaparaḥ || 8.7 ||
yāny etāni lokādīny anūktāni tāny apekṣata iti | tadvṛttis tadabhihitānāṃ karmaṇām anuṣṭhātā || 8.7 ||
catvāriṃśatā saṃskāraiḥ saṃskṛtaḥ || 8.8 ||
catvāriṃśatsaṃskāraiḥ saṃskṛtaḥ || 8.8 ||
te ca vakṣyamāṇāḥ garbhādhānādayaḥ, taiś copetaḥ | vakṣyamāṇatvād eva siddhe saṃkhyānirdeśaḥ samuccayārtho mā bhūd iti, bahuvacanasya prayogāt triprabhṛtiṣu kṛtārthateti | “yasya tu khalu saṃskārāṇām ekadeśo ‘pi” [GDh 8.25] ity atrāpiśabdāt samuccayo ‘pi gamyata iti cet, na, ekadeśaśabdasya saṃskārāvayavavācitvenāpi kṛtārthatvaprasaṅgāt | ata iha samuccayābhidhānāt yatra ekadeśagrahaṇena katipayasaṃskāragrahaṇasiddhiḥ || 8.8 ||
catvāriṃśatsaṃskārā garbhādhānādayo vakṣyante | taiḥ saṃskṛtaḥ || 8.8 ||
triṣu karmasv abhirataḥ || 8.9 ||
tāni sāṅgādhyayanadānejyālakṣaṇāni “dvijātīnām adhyayanam” [GDh 10.1] ity atroktāni | teṣv abhirataḥ satatānuṣṭhānaśīlaḥ | “tadvṛttiḥ” [GDh 8.7] ity anenaivānuṣṭhāne siddhe viśeṣārthaḥ punar ārambhaḥ | viśeṣaś cātiśayena tatsevanam, adṛṣṭārthatvajñāpanaṃ vā || 8.9 ||
ijyādhyayanadānāni trīṇi karmāṇi | teṣv abhirataḥ | teṣāṃ sātatyenānuṣṭhātā | tadvṛttir ity anenaiva siddhe punarvacanam atidārḍhyārtham || 8.9 ||
ṣaṭsu vā || 8.10 ||
yājanādhyāpanapratigrahaiḥ saha ṣaḍ bhavanti | eṣām apy adṛṭārthatvajñāpanārthaḥ punarārambhaḥ || 8.10 ||
yājanādhyāpanapratigrahaiḥ saha ṣaṭ karmāṇi, teṣv abhirataḥ | vāśabdena pūrvokteṣu niyamaḥ || 8.10 ||
sāmayācārikeṣv abhivinītaḥ || 8.11 ||
sāmayikāḥ smārtāḥ upanibaddhāḥ | ācārikāḥ śiṣṭācaritāḥ padārthāḥ anupanibaddhāḥ | teṣūbhayavidheṣu karmasv abhivinītaḥ jñātvānuṣṭhānarataḥ || 8.11 ||
pauruṣeyī vyavasthā samayaḥ | tanmūlā ācārāḥ samayācārās teṣu bhavāḥ sāmayācārikāḥ smārtā dharmās teṣv abhivinītaḥ pitrādibhiḥ samyak śikṣitaḥ || 8.11 ||
evaṃrūpo brāhmaṇaḥ |
sa evaṃrūpo brāhmaṇaḥ |
ṣaḍbhiḥ parihāryo rājñā avadhyaś cābandhyaś cādaṇḍyaś cābahiṣkāryaś cāparivādyaś cāparihāryaś ceti || 8.12 ||
ṣaḍbhiḥ parihāryo rājñā || 8.12 ||
abuddhipūrve saty apy aparādhe vadhādiyogye, buddhipūrvakasya prasaṅgābhāvāt tadvṛttitvāt, ṣaḍbhiḥ parivarjanīyaḥ | ṣaḍgrahaṇād aparādhasamuccaye ‘pi | [vadhaḥ] punaḥ punas tāḍanam iti, tasyāprasaṅgāt | bandho nigalādiyogaḥ | daṇḍo dhanāpaharaṇam | bahiṣkaraṇaṃ grāmādibhyo nirvāsanam | parivādo doṣasaṃkīrtanam | parihāras tyāgaḥ | anumantā prayojayitā ca na syād ity evamarthaḥ cakāraḥ | itikaraṇaṃ prakāravāci | tataś ca saṃbhāṣaṇādi na kartavyam || 8.12 ||
ṣaḍbhir vakṣyamāṇair vadhādibhiḥ parihāryo rājñā bhavati | parihāro varjanam || 8.12 ||
tān vadhādīn āha |
avadhyaś cābandhyaś cādaṇḍyaś cābahiṣkāryaś cāparivādyaś cāparihāryaś
ceti || 8.13 ||
vadhas tāḍanam | bandho nigaḍanam | daṇḍo ‘rthāpahāraḥ | bahiṣkāro grāmādibhyo nirasanam | parivādo doṣasaṃkīrtanam | parihāras tyāgaḥ | ṣaḍ ete vadhādaya evaṃbhūte bahuśrute brāhmaṇe saty abuddhipūrvāparādhe rājñā varjyāḥ | buddhipūrvasya tu prasaṅgābhāvāt | itiśabdhaḥ prakāravacane | yac cānyad evaṃrūpasaṃbhāṣādi tad api varjyam iti || 8.13 ||
“catvāriṃśatā saṃskāraiḥ” [GDh 8.8] ity uktam | ke punas te iti tān āha |
catvāriṃśatsaṃskārair ity uktam, tān āha \
garbhādhānapuṃsavanasīmantonnayanajātakarmanāmakaraṇānnaprāśana-caulopanayanam || 8.13 || — || 8.14 ||
garbhādhānādayaḥ pratigṛhyam āmnātāḥ | ihābhidhānaṃ tu saṃskāratvajñāpanārtham | manunāpi caiṣāṃ saṃskāratvam uktam: “niṣekādiḥ śmaśānāntaḥ” [MDh 2.16] ityādi | garbhādhānam ṛvāv eva “saṃveśane hutvā” ityādi pratigṛhyaṃ draṣṭavyam | puṃsavanaṃ tṛtīye māsi codyate prathame garbhe nānyatra, tatra tu vyakte garbhe | pratigarbhaṃ kecid icchanti | kecid ekam eva | tathā sīmantonnayanam api | tat caturthe ṣaṣṭhe aṣṭame vā codyate | jātakarmāpi stanaprāśanānnābhivardhanād vā prāg draṣṭavyam | nāmakaraṇaṃ daśame dvādaśe vāhni puṇye nakṣatre vā | annaprāśanaṃ ṣaṣṭhe māse saṃvatsare vā | yathāha śaṅkhaḥ: “saṃvatsare ‘nnaprāśanaṃ ṣaṣṭhe vā” iti | caulaṃ saṃvatsare tṛtīye vā | upanayanam uktam | upaniṣkramaṇaṃ ca annaprāśana evāntarbhūtatvān noktam | tad uktaṃ manunā |
māse caturthe kartavyaṃ śiśor niṣkramaṇaṃ gṛhāt | [MDh 2.34] iti || 8.13 ||
samāhāradvandvaḥ | garbhādhānam “ṛtāv upeyāt” [GDh 5.1] ityādikālaniyamena sūtrakārokta-vidhānaniṣekaḥ | garbhādhānādayaḥ saṃskārās tattadgṛhyeṣūktāḥ | iha tu saṃskāragaṇanārthaṃ svarūpanirdeśamātraṃ kṛtam || 8.14 ||
kiṃ ca |
catvāri vedavratāni || 8.14 || — || 8.15 ||
brahmagrahaṇārthatvāt pṛthag upadeśaḥ | prativedaṃ catvāri catvāri gṛhītavyānīty evamarthaṃ vedagrahaṇam || 8.14 ||
etāni prativedaṃ pratiśākhaṃ ca gṛhyeṣūktāni || 8.15 ||
kiṃ ca |
snānaṃ sahadharmacāriṇīsaṃyogaḥ pañcānāṃ yajñānām anuṣṭhānaṃ devapitṛmanuṣyabhūta-brahmaṇām eteṣāṃ ca || 8.15 ||
snānaṃ sahadharmacāriṇīsaṃyogaḥ || 8.16 ||
snānaṃ samāvṛttiḥ | sahadharmacāriṇīsaṃyogo vivāhaḥ | visamāso nāvaśyam anantaram eva vivāha iti jñāpanārthaḥ | vivāha iti vaktavye sahadharmacāriṇī-grahaṇaṃ dāraparigrahasya karmārthatvajñāpanārtham | evaṃ cākṛtadārasya vidhurasya ca pañcayajñādy-abhāve na doṣaḥ | pañcānāṃ yajñānāṃ devayajñā-dīnām anuṣṭhānaṃ karaṇam | anuṣṭhānābhisaṃbandhā-rthaḥ ṣaṣṭhyā nirdeśaḥ | anuṣṭhānagrahaṇaṃ ca nāgniṣṭomādivat kadācid eva | kiṃ tu ahar ahar ity evamartham | yady api devayajñādaya eva pañca mahāyajñāḥ, tathāpi devādīnām upādānaṃ paṇca saṃskārā it jñāpanārtham, anyathā pañcayajñānuṣṭhā-nam eka eva saṃskāra ity āśaṅkā syād iti | devādayo vyākhyātāḥ | “eteṣāṃ ca” ity ātmaguṇasamuccayārthaś cakāraḥ | tataś ca pañca yajñā apy ātmaguṇasahitā eva puruṣārthaprāpakāḥ, na pṛthagbhūtā iti siddhaṃ bhavati | eteṣām iti vakṣyamāṇānām upalakṣaṇam, noktāḥ pañca yajñā eva | kutaḥ? yadi pañcayajñānām evātmaguṇasahitatvam iṣyate, tadānīṃ saguṇānāṃ pañcānāṃ yajñānām anuṣṭhānam ity avakṣyat | tataś ca vakṣyamāṇā apy ātmaguṇasahitā eva brahmasāyujyaprāpakā iti draṣṭavyam || 8.15 ||
snānaṃ samāvartanam | sahadharmacāriṇīsaṃyogo vivāhaḥ || 8.16 ||
pañcānāṃ yajñānām anuṣṭhānaṃ devapitṛmanuṣyabhūtabrahmaṇām || 8.17 ||
pañcānāṃ devānāṃ yajñās teṣām anuṣṭhānam | etat pañcamahāyajñānuṣṭhānam ahar ahaḥ kartavyam | na tu sakṛtkṛtena saṃskārasiddhiḥ | pañcagrahaṇāt pañcaite pṛthaksaṃskārā na punaḥ samuditā ekaḥ saṃskāraḥ
|| 8.17 ||
eteṣāṃ ca || 8.18 ||
vakṣyamāṇānām aṣṭakādīnāṃ ca pākayajñānām anuṣṭhānaṃ saṃskāra iti || 8.18 ||
tān āha |
aṣṭakā pārvaṇaḥ śrāddhaṃ śrāvaṇy āgrahāyaṇī caitry āśvayujīti
sapta pākayajñasaṃsthāḥ || 8.16 || — || 8.19 ||
“ūrdhvam āgrahāyaṇyās trayo ‘parapakṣāḥ teṣām ekaikasminn ekaikā bhavati” iti yā ucyante tā aṣṭakāḥ | pārvaṇaḥ (sthālīpākaḥ) parvaṇi bhavatvāt pārvaṇaḥ | śrāddhaṃ māsiśrāddham ucyate śrāvaṇīśabdena śrāvaṇapaurṇamāsīyuktatvād upākaraṇam ucyate | āgrahāyaṇīśabdena āgrahāyaṇam ucyate | caitrīśabdena śūlagavaḥ | āśvayujīti indradhvajahomaḥ | saptagrahaṇam ekaikasya gaṇanārtham | pākagrahaṇaṃ sthālīpākasaṃbandhārtham | yajñagrahaṇam agnisaṃbandhārtham | saṃsthāḥ samyaksthitāḥ gṛhya evoktā ity arthaḥ || 8.16 ||
ūrdhvam āgrahāyaṇyās trayo ‘parapakṣās teṣv ekaikasminn ekāṣṭakā bhavatīti chandogāḥ | hemanta-śiśirayoś caturṇām aparapakṣāṇām aṣṭamīṣv aṣṭakaikasyāṃ cety āśvalāyanaḥ | yā mādhyāḥ paurṇamāsyā upariṣṭān madhyāṣṭakā tasyām aṣṭamī jyeṣṭhayā saṃpadyate | tām ekāṣṭakety ācakṣata ity āpastambaḥ | evaṃbhūtāṣṭakā | parvaṇi bhavaḥ sthālīpākaḥ pārvaṇaḥ | śrāddhaṃ māsi śrāddham | śrāvaṇī sarvabaliḥ | śrāvaṇyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ gṛhyam agnim atipraṇīyetyādi chandogābhihitaḥ | śrāvaṇyāṃ paurṇamāsyām astamite sthālīpāka ity āpastambaḥ | āgrahāyaṇī mārgaśīrṣyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ kriyamāṇaḥ sarpabalir utsargahomaḥ | hemante pratyavaroha-ṇākhyaṃ ca karmāgrahāyaṇīśabdenocyate | caitrī śūlagavaḥ | īśānabalir ity āpastambīyānāṃ prasiddhaḥ sa caitryāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ bhavati | atha śūlagavaḥ śaradi vasante cety āśvalāyanaḥ | āśvayujīṃ rudyāya sa iti chandogāḥ | āśvayujyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ tat karmaniveśanam alaṃkrtya snātāḥ śucivāsasaḥ paśupataye sthālīpākaṃ nirupya juhuyur ity āśvalāyanaḥ | anāhitāgner āgrayaṇam api tatraiva bhavati | tad idaṃ dvayam āśvayujīśabdena vivakṣitam | pākayajña iti gāryāṇāṃ karmaṇām ākhyā | yathāha āpastambaḥ: “laukikānāṃ pākayajñaśabdaḥ” iti | saṃsthāvidhāḥ pākayajñavidhāḥ saptety arthaḥ || 8.19 ||
agnyādheyam agnihotraṃ darśapaurṇamāsāv āgrayaṇaṃ cāturmāsyāni nirūḍhapaśubandhaḥ sautrāmaṇīti sapta haviryajñasaṃsthāḥ || 8.17 || — || 8.20 ||
H reads: “darśapūrṇamāsāv”
śrutiprasiddhakarmānuvādaḥ saṃskāratvārthaḥ | atrāgrayaṇaśabdenāgrahāyaṇeṣṭir ucyate, itarasya pūrvam uktatvāt | agnyādheyam ity ādhānam ucyate | agnihotraṃ nāma haviryajñaḥ śrutau prasiddhaḥ | darśapūrṇamāsau tu saṃskāramadhye eka eva | itare prasiddhāḥ | saptagrahaṇasyoktaṃ prayojanam | śeṣasyāpy uktam eva || 8.17 ||
agnyādheyādayaḥ śrutisiddhāḥ saṃskāreṣu gaṇyante | saptagrahaṇād darśapūrṇamāsau samudāyatayaikaḥ saṃskāraḥ | somasaṃbandhābhāvād dharviryajñā iti
|| 8.20 ||
agniṣṭomo ‘tyagniṣṭoma ukthyaḥ ṣoḍaśī vājapeyo ‘tirātro ‘ptoryāma iti sapta somasaṃsthāḥ || 8.18 || — || 8.21 ||
uktenaiva nyāyena sūtraṃ vyākhyeyam | pākayajñā-dīnāṃ visamāso bhinnaphalatvajñāpanārthaḥ || 8.18 ||
agniṣṭomo rājanyasya | ṣoḍaśigraho gṛhyate yatra so ‘tyagniṣṭomaḥ | brāhmaṇasya katham ayaṃ saṃskāra iti cintyam | anye prasiddhāḥ || 8.21 ||
ity ete catvāriṃśat saṃskārāḥ || 8.19 || — 8.22 ||
ity ete ityuktopasaṃhāraḥ | catvāriṃśad iti niyamārthaḥ, etāvanta eva saṃskārā iti | tataś ca samastasmārtaśrautakarmasaṃpadupeto bhavati || 8.20 ||
ity uktopasaṃhāraḥ | catvāriṃśadgrahaṇād eva tāvanta eva saṃskārāḥ | anyāni smārtakarmāṇi kāmyādīni ceti
|| 8.22 ||
athāṣṭāv ātmaguṇāḥ || 8.20 || — || 8.23 ||
vakṣyanta iti śeṣaḥ | athaśabdo ‘nantaravācī, saṃskārānantaram ātmaguṇā vakṣyanta ity arthaḥ | sarva evaitair garbhādhānādibhiḥ samuccīyante, na tu yathālābham ity evamartham aṣṭaugrahaṇam iti kecit | tad ayuktam, “yasya tu khalu saṃskārāṇām ekadeśo ‘py aṣṭāv ātmaguṇāḥ” ity atrāṣṭaugrahaṇasyāna-rthakyaprasaṅgāt | tataś ca saṃjñārthaṃ vacanam, yatra yatrāṣṭāv ātmaguṇā ucyante tatra tatra dayādīnām eva grahaṇaṃ yathā syād iti | samuccitānām eva praveśo yathā syād ity evamarthaṃ gurusaṃjñākaraṇam
|| 8.20 ||
vakṣyanta iti śeṣaḥ | athaśabdaḥ saṃbhāvanāyām
|| 8.23 ||
dayā sarvabhūteṣu kṣāntir anasūyā śaucam anāyāso maṅgalam akārpaṇyam
aspṛheti || 8.21 || — || 8.24 ||
dayā hitacintanam | tathā purāṇa uktam |
ātmavat sarvabhūteṣu yaḥ śivāya hitāya ca |
vartate satataṃ hṛṣṭaḥ kṛtsnā hy eṣā dayā smṛtā || iti |
sarvagrahaṇāt sthāvareṣv api | kṣāntiḥ bāhyābhyantaradvandvasahiṣṇutā | yathāha vyāsaḥ |
ākruṣṭo ‘bhihato vāpi nākrośen nāpi tāḍayet |
aduṣṭo vāṅmanaḥkāyaiḥ sā titikṣā kṣamā smṛtā || iti |
anasūyā guṇavatām upari pradveṣābhāvaḥ | tathāha |
yo dharmam arthaṃ kāmaṃ vā labhate mokṣam eva vā |
na dviṣyāt taṃ sadā prājñaḥ anasūyeti sā smṛtā || iti |
śaucaṃ dravyādiśaucam | yathāha vyāsaḥ |
dravyaśaucaṃ manaḥśaucaṃ vākśaucaṃ kāyikaṃ tathā |
śaucaṃ caturvidhaṃ proktam ṛṣibhis tattvadarśibhiḥ || iti | anāyāsaḥ ātmapīḍānārambhaḥ | yathāha vyāsaḥ |
yadārambhe bhavet pīḍā nityam atyantam ātmanaḥ |
tad varjayed dharmyam api anāyāsaḥ sa ucyate || iti |
maṅgalaṃ praśastam eva | yathāha vyāsaḥ |
praśastācaraṇaṃ nityam apraśastavivarjanam |
etad dhi maṅgalaṃ proktaṃ munibhis tattvadarśibhiḥ || iti | akārpaṇyam āpady apy adīnatā saṃvibhāgarucitvaṃ ca | yathāha vyāsaḥ |
āpady api ca kaṣṭāyāṃ bhaved dīno na kasyacit |
saṃvibhāgaruciś ca syād akārpaṇyaṃ tad ucyate ||
aspṛhāviṣayasaṃtoṣaḥ paradravyānabhilāṣaś ca | yathāha vyāsaḥ |
vivarjayed saṃtoṣaṃ viṣayeṣu sadā naraḥ |
paradravyābhilāṣaṃ ca sāspṛhā kathyate budhaiḥ || iti | itikaraṇaṃ samāptyartham, etāvān puruṣasya saṃskāraḥ kartavya iti | visamāsaḥ phalapṛthaktvajñāpanārthaḥ | yathāha vyāsaḥ |
kṣamāvān jayate bhūmiṃ dayāvān sukham aśnute |
anasūyur labhet svargaṃ śaucenādhyātmam eva ca ||
maṅgalād api saṃpūjyaḥ iha loke paratra ca |
surasāmyam anāyāsāt akārpaṇyāt prakṛtau layam ||
aspṛho labhate nityam anantaṃ sukham eva ca |
sarvais tu brahmaṇaḥ sthānaṃ saṃskārais tu tathaiva ca || iti | evaṃ sarvālābhe yathālābhaparigraho ‘pi kartavyaḥ, pṛthagbhūtasyāpi phalaśravaṇāt || 8.21 ||
ātmavat sarvabhūteṣu yad dhitāya śivāya ca |
vartate satataṃ hṛṣṭaḥ kṛtsnā hy eṣā dayā smṛtā || 1 ||
ākruṣṭo ‘bhihato vāpi na krośen na ca tāḍayet |
aduṣṭo vāṅmanaḥkāyaiḥ sā titikṣā kṣamā smṛtā || 2 ||
yo dharmam arthaṃ kāmaṃ ca labhate mokṣam eva ca |
na dviṣyāt taṃ sadā prājñaḥ sānasūyā smṛtā budhaiḥ || 3 ||
dravyaśaucaṃ manaḥśaucaṃ vācikaṃ kāyikaṃ tathā |
śaucaṃ caturvidhaṃ proktam ṛṣibhis tattvadarśibhiḥ || 4 ||
yadārambhe bhavet pīḍā nityam atyantam ātmanaḥ |
tad varjayed dharmyam api so ‘nāyāsaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 5 ||
praśastācaraṇaṃ nityam apraśastavivarjanam |
etad dhi maṅgalaṃ proktaṃ munibhis tattvadarśibhiḥ || 6 ||
āpady api ca kaṣṭāyāṃ bhaved dīno na kasyacit |
saṃvibhāgaruciś ca syāt tad akārpaṇyam ucyate || 7 ||
vivarjayed asaṃtoṣaṃ viṣayeṣu sadā naraḥ |
paradravyābhilāṣaṃ ca sāspṛhā kathyate budhaiḥ || 8 || ity uktaprakāreṇāṣṭāv ātmaguṇāḥ || 8.24 ||
eṣām utkarṣam āha |
yasyaite catvāriṃśat saṃskārā na cāṣṭāv ātmaguṇā na sa brahmaṇaḥ sāyujyaṃ
sālokyaṃ ca gacchati || 8.22 || — || 8.25 ||
yasya tu puruṣasya | ete iti saṃskāraparāmarśo guṇānāṃ gauravārthaḥ | catvāriṃśad iti saṃhitānām apy asāmarthayajñāpanārtham | na cāṣṭāv ātmaguṇāḥ, cakārāt samastā vyastāś ca | etad api guṇastutiḥ | na sa brahmaṇaḥ sāyujyaṃ sālokyaṃ ca gacchati | brahmā nāma devatāviśeṣaḥ | sāyujyam aikātmyam, sālokyaṃ samānasthānavāsitvam | nindā ca guṇastutyarthaiva | prādhānyāt sāyujyasyaiva pūrvam upadeśaḥ || 8.22 ||
sālokyaṃ samānalokavāsitvam | ekadeśasaṃyogāt sālokyaṃ samastayogāt sāyujyam iti || 8.25 ||
yasya tu khalu saṃskārāṇām ekadeśo ‘py aṣṭāv ātmaguṇāḥ atha sa brahmaṇaḥ sāyujyaṃ sālokyaṃ ca gacchati gacchati || 8.23 || — || 8.26 ||
tuśabdo viśeṣavācī, ekadeśagrahaṇenopanayanasyaiva grahaṇam iti | khalv ity avadhāraṇe, sa eva gacchatīti | apiśabdāt samastā api | athaśabdād ānantaryeṇaiva gacchatīti | ekadeśayogāt sālokyam, samastayogāt sāyujyam iti draṣṭavyam | iyam api guṇastutiḥ || 8.23 ||
tuśabdo viśeṣavācī | khaluśabdaḥ prasiddhau | yasya catvāriṃśatsaṃskāreṣu dvijatvamūlakatipayasaṃskārasaṃbandhe ‘py aṣṭāv ātmaguṇāḥ santi | athaśabdo nirdhāraṇe brahmaṇaḥ sāyujyaṃ sālokyaṃ ca gacchaty eva | [abhyāso ‘dhyāyasamāptyarthaḥ] || 8.26 ||
atha navamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
rājabrāhmaṇayor atiśayena vratadhāraṇam uktam | sāṃprataṃ vratasvarūpakathanārthaṃ tadavadhikālāvadhāraṇārthaṃ cāha |
sa vidhipūrvaṃ snātvā bhāryām adhigamya yathoktān gṛhasthadarmān prayuñjāna imāni vratāny anukarṣet snātakaḥ || 9.1 ||
gṛhasthadharmān prayuñjāna imāni vratāny anukarṣet || 9.1 ||
sa iti rājabrāhmaṇayoḥ pratyekaṃ parāmarśārtham | vidhipūrvaṃ śāstrīyena vidhinā, vidyayā vratenobhayena vā pūrvagrahaṇāt, anyathā vidhivad ity eva vaktavyam | snātveti bhāryādhigamavacanād evākṣiptam apy ucyate gṛhasthāśramābhyupagama eva snānaṃ nānyāśramapratipattāv iti jñāpanārtham | bhāryām adhigamya dāraparigrahaṃ kṛtvā |
tatra snānānantaraṃ bhāryādhigamaḥ kartavyaḥ, ktvāpratyayanirdeśāt, anāśramiṇaś cāvasthānasyāyuktatvāt, it kecid vyācakṣate |
tad ayuktam, ānantaryāsaṃbhavāt parādhīnatvāt kanyālabhasya, smṛtyantare ca vivāhakālaniyamāt | yathāha uśanā: “triṃśadvarṣo vahet kanyām” iti | tatrāpy ānantarye sati snānakālavidhānād eva siddhatvān na vaktavyam, samācārāc ca | na hi kaścit snānānantaraṃ vivāham akṛtvā prāyaścittaṃ kurvann upalabhyate, śiṣṭair vā nindyata iti, udagayanāpūryamāṇapakṣapuṇyāha-pratīkṣaṇopadeśāc ca, mānave “sāntānikaṃ yakṣyamāṇam” [MDh 11.1] ity akṛtavivāhasyāva-sthānopadeśāt, tatrāpi “gurvarthaniveśa” [GDh 5.22] ity atroktatvāt | ktvāpratyayas tu pūrvakālatāmātra eva vaiyākaraṇaiḥ smaryate | ānantaryeṇa saṃkīrtanam apy akṛtasnānasya gārhasthyapratiṣedhārthaṃ snātvaiva vivāhaḥ kartavya iti ||
yat tūktam anāśramiṇo ‘vasthānam ayuktam iti, tatrocyate – naivāyam anāśramī | katham? samāptabrahmacaryo gurvanujñayā snāto yāvatā kālena yatnaṃ kṛtvā bhāryām adhigacchati tāvantaṃ kālaṃ snātakavratādhigato 'viplutabrahmacaryo gṛhasthāntarbhūta eveti | tadartha eva “gṛhasthaḥ sadṛśīm” [GDh 4.1] ity atrākṛtavivāhe ‘pi gṛhasthaśabdaḥ prayuktaḥ |
na cāniyatakālam evāvasthānam, smṛtyantare āyurvibhāge nāśramapratipattividhānāt | yathāha manuḥ: “caturtham āyuṣo bhāgam” [MDh 4.1] ityādi | yathodāhṛtauśanasavacanāc ca, “pūrṇacatvāriṃśadvarṣo gṛhī bhavet” iti vyāghravacanāc ca, “snātako ‘bdatrayaṃ na tiṣṭhet” iti kaṇvavacanāc ca | tatra durjñeyatvād āyurvibhāgasya śatavarṣacaturbhāgo gṛhyate | so ‘py ekavedādhyāyinaḥ | yad uktam “catvāriṃśadvarṣaḥ” iti tad vedatrayādhyāyinaḥ | yady asau prāg eva parisamāptavidyaḥ | aparisamāptavidyaś ced yāvatā kālena parisamāpya snāto bhavet, tasmād ūrdhvaṃ varṣatrayam akṛtadāro na tiṣṭhed ity evaṃ niyatakālam evāvasthānam | tasmād ayaṃ gṛhasthāntarbhūta eva | tataś ca,
anāśramī na tiṣṭhet tu dinam ekam api dvijaḥ |
iti dakṣavacanasya vidhuraviṣayatvaṃ draṣṭavyam | tatra dinam ekam apīti sati saṃbhava iti śeṣaḥ, parādhīnatvāt kanyālābhasya | atha vā,
anāśramī na tiṣṭhet tu dinam ekam api,
yatnam akṛtveti śeṣaḥ | tatra pūrvasmin pakṣe saṃbhave saty akartur doṣaḥ, asaṃbhave na doṣaḥ | uttarasmin pakṣe yatnam akartur doṣaḥ |
yathoktān gṛhasthadharmān prayuñjānaḥ imāni vratāny anukarṣet | tatraivoktatvād eva siddhe ihopadeśo “gṛhasthaḥ sadṛśīm” [GDh 4.1] ity uktānām api snātakavratāntarbhāvārtham | tatas tadatikrame ‘pi smṛtyantaroktasnātakavratalopaprāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha manuḥ |
snātakavrataloke tu prāyaścittam abhojanam | iti | [MDh 11.203] | anuktā api gṛhasthadharmāḥ santīti kasyacid āśaṅkhā syād iti tannivṛttyarthaṃ yathoktagrahaṇam | tatrokagrahaṇenaiva siddhe ye brahmacāridharmāḥ “uttareṣāṃ caitad avirodhi” [GDh 3.10] ity anenādhikriyante teṣām api grahaṇārthaṃ yathāgrahaṇaṃ tadatikrame ‘pi snātakavratalopaprāyaścittaṃ yathā syād iti |
nanu ca bhāryām adhigamya yathoktān dharmān prayuñjāna iti vaktavyam, bhāryādhigamanottarakālaṃ gṛhasthadharmair evādhikṛtatvād iti |
ucyate – gṛhasthasnātakadharmayoḥ pṛthaktvajñāpanārthaṃ gṛhasthagrahaṇam | uktā eva gṛhasthadharmāḥ, vakṣyamāṇās taiḥ sahitāḥ snātakadharmā iti pṛthaktvajñāpanārthaṃ rājabrāhmaṇayor eva niyamena snātakavratānuṣṭhānaṃ tadatikrame prāyaścittaṃ ca, itareṣāṃ tadanuṣṭhāne 'bhyudayaḥ, akaraṇe pratyavāyaprāyaścittābhāvaś cety evamarthaṃ ca | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
snātakavratalope tu prāyaścittaṃ vidhīyate |
rājabrāhmaṇayor eva netareṣāṃ kadācana || iti |
atrāpi “dvau loke dhṛtavratau” [GDh 8.1] iti viśeṣeṇa vratadhāraṇokter etad eva tātparyam |
imāni vakṣyamāṇāni vratāny anukarṣed yāvajjīvaṃ kuryāt | avaśyakartavyāni na phalārthānīty evamarthaṃ vratagrahaṇam | evaṃ ca snātakaḥ śaśvad brahmalokān na cyavata iti phalopadeśo “jyotiṣṭomena svargakāmo jayeta” ity atra phalopadeśavad draṣṭavyaḥ | vakṣyamāṇāny eva vratāni na vratāntarāṇīty evam āśaṅkānivṛttyartham imānīty asyopādānam | vratatvenānuṣṭhāne siddhe ‘nukarśed iti yāvajjīvārtham | snātaka ity asyopādānaṃ saṃjñārtham – yo vakṣyamāṇavratāny anutiṣṭhed asau gṛhasthaḥ snātaka ity ucyata iti | anyathā “sa vidhipūrvaṃ snātvā” ity anenaiva siddhatvān na vaktavyam || 9.1 ||
tacchabdena pūrvādhyāyoktarājā brāhmaṇaś ca parāmṛśyate | vaiśyasya tu snātakaviṣaye viśeṣaṃ vakṣyati | vidhipūrvaṃ vidhiṃ puraskṛtya snātvā vedavratāni pāraṃ nītvā samāvartanaṃ krtvā bhāryām adhigamya vivāhaṃ kṛtvā tadanantaraṃ yathoktān atithipūjādigṛhasthadharmān anutiṣṭhann imāny api vakṣyamāṇāni vratāny anukarṣet | ātmānaṃ prāpayed anutiṣṭhed iti || 9.1 ||
snātakaḥ || 9.2 ||
calopo draṣṭavyaḥ | snātakaś caitāni gṛhasthavratāny anutiṣṭhet | brahmacaryān nivṛttatvād gārhasthyavyatiriktāśramāntarabhāvāc ca bhāryādhigamād ūrdhvam iti pūrvasūtram ārabdham | snātakasya tu bhāryādhigamāsaṃbhave yāvajjīvaṃ gṛhasthadharmā evānuṣṭheyā iti sūtrāntaram ārabdham | etac ca rājabrāhmaṇayor eva snātakavratānuṣṭhānaṃ tadatikrame prāyaścittaṃ ca vidhīyate | tathā ca smṛtyantaram: “rājabrāhmaṇayor eva netareṣāṃ kathaṃcana” iti || 9.2 ||
kāni punas tānīty ata āha |
kāni punas tāni vratāni |
nityaṃ śuciḥ sugandhasnānaśīlaḥ || 9.2 ||
nityaṃ śuciḥ sugandhiḥ snānaśīlaḥ || 9.3 ||
syād iti śeṣaḥ | nityaṃ śucir ācamanādinā | nityagrahaṇād āturāvasthāyām api | sugandhair āmalakādibhiḥ snātuśīlaṃ yasyāsau sugandhasnānaśīlaḥ | “snānadantadhāvana” [GDh 2.19] iti brahmacāriprakaraṇe pratiṣiddhasya pratiprasavaḥ | śīlagrahaṇād asaṃbhave kevalasnānenāpi na doṣaḥ | visamāsaḥ snātakasya yāni nityaṃ dhāryāṇi teṣām apy upasaṃgrahārthaḥ | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
snātakānāṃ tu nityāni antarvāsas tathottaram |
yajñopavīte dve yaṣṭiḥ sodakaś ca kamaṇḍaluḥ || iti | [VaDh 12.14] sugandhasnānaśīla iti suśabdopādānāt snānottarakālam apy ālepanaṃ kartavyam || 9.2 ||
ācamanādinā nityaṃ śuciḥ śaktiviṣaye na muhūrtam apy aprayataḥ syāt | sugandhiś candanādyanuliptena surabhitāṅgaḥ | yad vā gandhaḥ śīlaṃ suśīlaḥ syād iti | snānaśīlo nityasnāyī syāt | atra snātakaviṣaye vasiṣṭhaḥ |
snātakānāṃ tu nityaṃ syād antarvāsas tathottaram |
yajñopavīte dve yaṣṭiḥ sodakaś ca kamaṇḍaluḥ || iti | [VaDh 12.14] manuḥ |
vaiṇavīṃ dhārayed yaṣṭiṃ sodakaṃ ca kamaṇḍalum |
yajñopavītaṃ vedaṃ ca śubhe raukme ca kuṇḍale || iti | [MDh 4.36] vedo dharbhamuṣṭiḥ || 9.3 ||
sati vibhave na jīrṇamalavadvāsāḥ syāt || 9.3 || — || 9.4 ||
saty anyasaṃbhave jīrṇaṃ malavac ca vāso na dhārayet || 9.3 ||
vibhave ‘nyasya saṃbhave sati jīrṇaṃ malavac ca vāso na dhārayet || 9.4 ||
na raktam ulbaṇam anyadhṛtaṃ vāso bibhṛyāt || 9.4 || — || 9.5 ||
raktaṃ kusumbhādinā, ulbaṇam uddhatārgham araktam, anyadhṛtaṃ guruvarjaṃ taducchiṣṭābhyanujñānāt | pṛthagārambhasāmarthyād āpady api na dhārayet | pratyekaṃ varjanārtho visamāsaḥ || 9.4 ||
kusumbhādirāgayuktam ulbaṇaṃ bahumūlyam anyadhṛtaṃ guruvarjam anyaiḥ pūrvadhṛtam evāmvidhāni vāsāṃsi na dhārayet | sati vibhava ity anuvartate || 9.5 ||
na sragupānahau || 9.5 || — || 9.6 ||
pūrvoktaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭā srag mālā, upānahau lākṣādirakte, na tu kaṣāyeṇa | sūtradvayārambho yathāsaṃkhyanivṛttyarthaḥ || 9.5 ||
sragupānahāv apy anyadhṛte na dhārayet || 9.6 ||
nirṇiktam aśaktau || 9.6 || — || 9.7 ||
anyāsaṃbhave tu vāsaḥprabhṛti kṛtaśaucaṃ dhārayet | aśaktir anyāsaṃbhava iti || 9.6 ||
anyasyālābho ‘śaktiḥ | aśaktāv anyadhṛtaṃ vāsaḥ sragupānahau ca nirṇijya dhārayet | tatra vāsonirṇejanam ūṣarodake pakvaṃ kāryam || 9.7 ||
na rūḍhaśmaśrur akasmāt || 9.7 || — || 9.8 ||
śmaśrugrahaṇaṃ nakhādīnām apy upalakṣaṇam | rūḍhagrahaṇān na vapanam, kiṃ tu samatvena kalpanāmātram| tathā ca manuḥ: “kḷptakeśanakha-śmaśruḥ” [MDh 6.52] iti | tatra keśakalpanā tu malāpakarṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā ślakṣṇatāpādanam | yathāha uśanā: “keśādīni nirmalāni ślakṣṇāny atyantasugandhīni snātako dhārayet” iti || 9.7 ||
śmaśrugrahaṇaṃ nakhādīnām apy upalakṣaṇārtham | akāraṇān na rūḍhaśmaśruḥ syāt | kāraṇe sati rūḍhaśmaśruḥ syāt | kāraṇaṃ tu smṛtyantare paṭhitam |
ṣaṣṭhābde ṣoḍaśābde ca vivāhābde tathaiva ca |
antarvatnyāṃ ca jāyāyāṃ kṣaurakarma vivarjayet ||
iti | vapanasyāpi “gaṅgāyāṃ bhāskarakṣetre” ityādinā coditakālatvāt kathaṃ tarhi syāt | tatra manuḥ “kḷptakeśanakhaśmaśruḥ” [MDh 4.35] iti | kalpanā kartanena samīkaraṇam | yājñavalkyaś ca |
śuklāmbaradharo nīcakeśaśmarunakhaḥ śuciḥ | [YDh 1.131] iti || 9.8 ||
nāgnim apaś ca yugapad dhārayet || 9.8 || — || 9.9 ||
yugapad ekakālam | visamāso ‘pām uttarasūtrābhisaṃbandhārthaḥ | annasamuccayārthaś cakāraḥ | yathāha vyāghraḥ |
na dhārayed apaś cāgnim apaś cānnaṃ tathaiva ca |
yugapat snātako nityaṃ tadbhāryā ca tathaiva ca || iti || 9.8 ||
ekena hastenāgnim apareṇāpaś ca yugapan na dhārayet | atra vyāghro viśeṣam āha |
na dhāreyed apaś cāgnim apaś cānnaṃ tathaiva ca |
yugapat snātako nityaṃ tadbhāryāpi tathaiva ca || iti || 9.9 ||
nāñjalinā pibet || 9.9 || — || 9.10 ||
apaḥ prakṛtatvāt | dvihastasaṃyogo ‘ñjaliḥ | tataś caikahastena na doṣaḥ | kṣirādes tv apratiṣedhaḥ | aviśeṣān na kiṃcit pibed iti kecit | teṣāṃ pūrvasūtravisamāsaprayojanaṃ mṛgyam || 9.9 ||
yat kiṃcit kṣīrodakādi peyam añjalinā na pibet | saṃyuktau hastāv añjaliḥ || 9.10 ||
na tiṣṭhan || 9.10 ||
na tiṣṭhann uddhṛtodakenācāmet || 9.11 ||
tiṣṭhaṃś ca na pibet | tiṣṭhaṃś ceti pratiṣedhe siddhe punaḥ pratiṣedhaḥ kṣīrādīnām apy upasaṃgrahārthaḥ || 9.10 ||
uddhṛtodakena tiṣṭhan nācāmet | āsīna evācāmet | uddhṛtodakeneti vacanāt taṭākādiṣu tīrapradeśasyāsucitve jānudaghne jale tiṣṭhato ‘py ācamanam apratiṣiddham | ācāro ‘py evam eva śiṣṭānām || 9.11 ||
uddhṛtodakenācāmet || 9.11 ||
uddhṛtam udakaṃ yeneti uddhṛtodakaśabdena kamaṇḍalur ucyate, tenācāmet || 9.11 ||
na śūdrāśucyekapāṇyāvarjitena || 9.12 ||
na śūdrajātyā, aśucināpy aśūdrajātyāpi, ekahastena śucinā aśūdrajātyāpi nācāmet | anenāprāptapratiṣedhena pūrvasutrasya niyamābhāvaṃ jñāpayati | tataś ca vikalpo draṣṭavyaḥ – kamaṇḍalunā, aśūdreṇa śucinobhayahastāvarjitena vācāmed iti | evaṃ ca kamaṇḍalunaivācamanasya niyamābhāvāt taddhāraṇasyāpy anityatvaṃ jñāpayati, ācamanārthatvāt taddhāraṇasyeti || 9.12 ||
śūdreṇa śucināpy aspṛśyasparśādidūṣitena dvijenāpy ekena pāṇinā ca yad āvarjitaṃ tenodakena nācāmet | svayaṃ tu vāmahastāvarjitenodakenācamanaviṣaya ekapāṇyāvarjitatvaṃ samānam iti cen, na, hastadvayasyāpy ācamanakarmasaṃbandhāt tathā ca śiṣṭācāradarśanāt || 9.12 ||
na vāyvagniviprādityāpo devatā gāś ca prati paśyan vā mūtrapurīṣāmedhyāni
vyudasyet || 9.13 ||
na vāyvādyabhimukhaḥ anabhimukho ‘pi caitān avalokayan mūtrādi visarjayet | devatāśabdena tatpratikṛtayo lekhyādirūpā ucyante | devatā-grahaṇenaiva vāyvādīnām api siddhe teṣām upādānaṃ nādhikopasaṃgrahārtham | tataś candragrahana-kṣatrādidarśane na doṣaḥ | caśabdāt smarann api caitān na kuryāt | mūtrapurīṣayor amedhyatvāt amedhyasya punar grahaṇam amedhyaviśeṣārtham | tataḥ śukla-nirasanādy api na kartavyam iti draṣṭavyam || 9.13 ||
apśabdānte dvandva ārṣatvāt samāsānto na kṛtaḥ | anityāḥ samāsāntā iti keṣāṃcit pakṣaḥ | devatāḥ pratimāḥ | vāyvādīn prati mūtrādīni na vyudasyet paśyan vā na kuryād iti pratipaśyan vety arthaḥ | prati na kuryād ity ābhimukhyavarjanam | paśyan na kuryād iti niyamād ābhimukhye saty apy anavalokanam | mūtrapurīṣayoḥ pṛthag upādānād amedhyaśabdena niṣṭhīvanocchiṣṭādi vivakṣitam | tarhy amedhya-śabdenaivālam | mūtrapurīṣagrahaṇaṃ tu tayor atiśayena varjanārtham || 9.13 ||
naitā devatāḥ prati pādau prasārayet || 9.14 ||
punar devatāgrahaṇaṃ gobrāhmaṇayor devatātvārtham | pādāv iti dvivacanaṃ na vivakṣitam, ekam api na prasārayet kiṃ punaḥ dvau || 9.14 ||
etā vāyvādyā devatāḥ prati pādau na prasārayet | pādāv ity upalakṣaṇaṃ pādaṃ ca na prasārayet | goṣu vipreṣu ca devatāpadaprayogas tadvad upacārārthaḥ || 9.14 ||
na parṇaloṣṭāśmabhir mūtrapurīṣāpakarṣaṇaṃ kuryāt || 9.15 ||
loṣṭaśabdena mṛd gṛhyate | tataś cānyair apakarṣaṇaṃ kuryāt || 9.15 ||
na parṇādibhir mūtrapurīṣayor karṣaṇam apamārjanaṃ kuryāt | anyais tu kuryād iti || 9.15 ||
na bhasmakeśatuṣakapālāmedhyāny adhitiṣṭhet || 9.16 ||
na bhasmakeśanakhatuṣakapālamedhāny adhitiṣṭhet || 9.16 ||
bhasmādīni nākrāmet | keśā manuṣyāṇām | kapālāni bhinnabhāṇḍāvayavāḥ | amedhyaṃ mūtrādi || 9.16 ||
bhasmādīni nākrāmet | tuṣā vrīhyādīnāṃ tvacaḥ | anye prasiddhāḥ | teṣām upari na tiṣṭhet | adhitiṣṭhed ity anena yādṛcchikasparśamātre na doṣaḥ || 9.16 ||
na mlecchāśucyadhārmikaiḥ saha saṃbhāṣeta || 9.17 ||
mlecchāḥ pārasikādayaḥ | aśucayaḥ āryā api | adhārmikāḥ patitādayaḥ | taiḥ saha na saṃmantrayet | tṛtīyānirdeśād eva sahabhāve siddhe ‘tiśayārthaṃ sahaśabdasyopādānam | tataś cānena sahaikakāryatāṃ gatvā na saṃbhāṣeta | mārgapraśnādau tu na doṣaḥ
|| 9.17 ||
varṇāśramadharmarahite deśe siṃhaladvīpādau ye vasanti te mlecchāḥ | aśucaya āryā api vihitāni saṃdhyāvandanādīni ye na kurvanti te tathoktāḥ | adhārmikāḥ patitādayaḥ | taiḥ saha na saṃbhāṣeta | saṃśabdaprayogād eva siddhe sahagrahaṇaṃ taiḥ sahaikakāryo bhūtvā na saṃbhāṣetety evamartham | tena mārgapraśnādau na doṣaḥ || 9.17 ||
saṃbhāṣya puṇyakṛto manasā dhyāyet || 9.18 ||
yadi tv ajñānāt kāryavaśād vā saṃbhāṣeta tataḥ puṇyakṛtaḥ sukṛtakāriṇaḥ, bahuvacanaprayogāt bahūn, manasā cintayet | manograhaṇam avayavanirūpaṇena smaraṇārthan || 9.18 ||
yadi kāraṇavaśāt taiḥ saha saṃbhāṣeta tataḥ puṇyakṛto vasiṣṭhādīn manasā dhyāyet | manaseti dhyānasvabhāvānuvādaḥ || 9.18 ||
brāhmaṇena vā saha saṃbhāṣeta || 9.19 ||
puṇyakṛd eva brāhmaṇaḥ | sahagrahaṇād brāhmaṇo yadi bhāṣeta | anantarānuṣṭhānārtham aprakaraṇe prāyaścittavidhānam, lāghavārthaṃ vā || 9.19 ||
prakaraṇād brāhmaṇo ‘pi puṇyakṛd eva || 9.19 ||
adhenuṃ dhenubhavyeti brūyāt || 9.20 ||
adhenur vandhyā gauḥ, tāṃ dhenubhavyety evam abhidadhyāt | itikaraṇaḥ prakārāvarodhārthaḥ, mā bhūd anyeṣām apy evaṃrūpāṇāṃ saṃśabdanam iti | etad eva prayojanam itikaraṇānāṃ vakṣyamāṇānām api || 9.20 ||
dhenuḥ payasvinī gauḥ | adhenus tadviparītā | tām api dhenubhavyeti brūyān na punar adhenur iti || 9.20 ||
abhadraṃ bhadram iti || 9.21 ||
abhadram abhajanīyam aśrīkatvād amaṅgalatvād vā | tad bhadram ity evam abhidadhyāt | yathā durbhagaṃ subhagam iti || 9.21 ||
abhadram api vastu bhadram ity eva brūyat || 9.21 ||
kapālaṃ bhagālam iti || 9.22 ||
kapālaṃ bhinnabhāṇḍāvayavaṃ bhagālam iti brūyāt
|| 9.22 ||
kapālaṃ bruvan bhagālam iti brūyāt || 9.22 ||
maṇidhanur itīndradhanuḥ || 9.23 ||
indradhanur manidhanur iti brūyāt | vacanaprāmāṇyād eṣām evābhidhānam || 9.23 ||
indradhanur iti bruvan maṇidhanur iti brūyāt || 9.23 ||
gāṃ dhayantīṃ parasmai nācakṣīta || 9.24 ||
gāṃ pāyayantīṃ parasmai nācakṣīta | paragrahaṇād ātmīyām ācakṣīta || 9.24 ||
“dheṭ pane” [Dhātu Pāṭha 902] | vyatyayenāyaṃ karmaṇi kartṛpratyayaḥ | vatsena dhīyamānāṃ gāṃ parasmai svāmine na brūyāt | yasya haviṣe vatsā apākṛtā dhayeyur ityādike nimitte tv ākhyātavyam eva “saṃsṛṣṭāṃ ca vatsena” [ĀpDh 1.31.10] ity āpastambīye viśeṣāt || 9.24 ||
na caināṃ vārayet || 9.25 ||
enām iti parakīyām eva | cakārāt parītādāv api || 9.25 ||
na ca svayam apy enāṃ vārayed iti || 9.25 ||
na mithunībhūtvā śaucaṃ prati vilambeta || 9.26 ||
mithunībhūtvā striyam āsevya śaucaṃ prati na vilambeta, sadya eva śaucaṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ | vītyupasargād yāvatā kālena ratir na nivartate tāvantaṃ kālaṃ vilambetāpi | na maithunaviṣaya eva śaucavilambanam | kiṃ tu mūtrapurīṣādiviṣaye ‘pi na vilambanaṃ kāryam ity evamarthaṃ pratigrahaṇam |
nanu “nityaṃ śuciḥ” [GDh 9.2] ity anenaivaitat siddham |
atrocyate – atra śaucaśabdena malāpakarṣaṇamātram ucyate, ācamanārhodakāsaṃbhave tallepāpakarṣaṇaṃ yena kenacit kartavyam ity evamarthaḥ sūtrārambha iti | evaṃ ca “nityaṃ śuciḥ” [GDh 9.2] ity asya sati saṃbhave nityaṃ śuciḥ syād ity eṣo ‘rtho labhyate | tataś cāsaṃbhavād aśuceḥ sato na doṣaḥ || 9.20 ||
mithunībhūya śtriyam upagamya śaucaṃ prati na vilambeta | tatkṣaṇa eva kuryat | śaucaṃ tv āpastambenābhihitam: “udakopasparśanam api vā lepān prakṣālyācamya prokṣaṇam aṅgānām” iti [ĀpDh 2.123; 2.2.1] || 9.26 ||
na ca tasmin śayane svādhyāyam adhīyīta || 9.27 ||
yasmin śayane maithunam ācaritaṃ tasmin śayane nādhīyīta svādhyāyagrahaṇād vedam | caśabdāt pūjyāṃś ca tatra nopaveśayet || 9.27 ||
yasmin mithunam ācaritam || 9.27 ||
na cāpararātram adhītya punaḥ pratisaṃviśet || 9.28 ||
paścime rātribhāge ‘dhītya punar na svapyāt | punar-grahaṇāt pūrvam asuptasyāpratiṣedhaḥ | apararātra-grahaṇāt pūrvarātre na doṣaḥ | yathā ca pūrvaṃ saṃviṣṭaḥ tathā punar na saṃviśet na tu viśramārtham apīti pratigrahaṇam || 9.28 ||
yaḥ pūrvarātre suptvāpararātra utthāyādhīte, na sa punaḥ pratisaṃviśet | kāladairghye sati punar na svapyāc cheṣāṃ rātriṃ jāgṛyād eveti | punargrahaṇāt pūrvarātre ‘suptasya svāpe na doṣaḥ || 9.28 ||
nākalyāṃ nārīm abhiramet || 9.29 ||
asvasthaśarīrām analaṃkṛtaśarīrāṃ vā akalyāṃ nārīṃ striyam nābhiramayet | abhīty upagamārtham | tataḥ vinodārthāyāḥ krīḍāyāḥ apratiṣedhaḥ || 9.29 ||
akalyāṃ rogādināsvasthāṃ nārīṃ nābhiramet | nānayā mithunī bhavet || 9.29 ||
na rajasvalām || 9.30 ||
udakyāṃ ca nopeyāt |
nanu ca “udakyāgamane trirātram” [GDh 23.35] iti prāyaścittavidhānād eva pratiṣedhaḥ siddhyati |
ucyate – “trirātraṃ rajasvalāśucir bhavati” iti smṛtyantaradarśanāt trirātrāntargamane prāyaścittam uktam | iha tūrdhvam anivṛtte rajasi pratiṣedhaḥ |
anye tv akālapravṛtte rajasi rajasvalām āhuḥ |
tad ayuktam, kālapravṛtte eva rakte rajaḥśabdapravṛtteḥ, tasyaivāśucikaratvāc ca | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
akāle yad bhavet strīṇāṃ raktam āhur manīṣiṇaḥ |
kāle tu tad rajaḥ proktaṃ tasmāt tatraiva sāśuciḥ || iti | tathā ca bhārgavīyam api |
akāle yad bhavet strīṇāṃ śuci tat tu vinirdiśet |
āśuddhaṃ tad vijānīyān māsi māsi bhavet tu yat ||
iti | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ |
striyaḥ pavitram atulaṃ naitā duṣyanti karhicit |
māsi māsi rajo hy āsāṃ duritāny apakarṣati || iti | [VaDh 28.4] tathā ca śrutiḥ: “saiṣā bhrūṇahatyā māsi māsy āvir bhavati” iti | tasmāt trirātrād ūrdhvaṃ pratiṣedhārthaḥ | evaṃ ca trirātrād ūrdhvam apy anuparate rajasy eṣāśucir eva | tathā ca manuḥ |
rajasy uparate sādhvī snānena strī rajasvalā || iti | [MDh 5.65] tatra trirātrād ūrdhvam anuparate ‘pi sparśayogyaiva, rajasvalā caturthe ‘hani snāyād iti sparśanārthatvāt snānacodanāyāḥ | rajasvalāṃ ceti siddhe uttarārthaḥ pṛthag ārambhaḥ || 9.30 ||
rajasvalām api nārīṃ nābhiramayet | “udakyāgamane trirātram” [GDh 23.33] iti prāyaścittaṃ vakṣyati | tenaiva siddhe vacanam idaṃ trirātrād ūrdhvam apy anivṛtte rajasi gamanapratiṣedhārtham || 9.30 ||
na caināṃ śliṣyet || 9.31 ||
na caināṃ śliṣyen na kanyām || 9.31 ||
enām iti trirātrābhyantararajasvalopasaṃgrahārtham | “na rajasvalām” [GDh 9.30] iti pṛthagārambhasyāpy etad eva prayojanam | evaṃ trirātrād ūrdhvam anuparate ‘py āliṅgane na doṣaḥ |
nanu ca “patitacaṇḍālasūtikodakyāśava” [GDh 14.29] ityādinā sparśapratiṣedhād āliṅganasyāpy arthataḥ pratiṣedhaḥ sidhyatīti |
tatrocyate – āliṅgane kṛte snātakavratalopaprāyaścittapraveśārtha ārambha iti | cakāra enāṃ cākalyāṃ ceti samuccayārthaḥ || 9.31 ||
enāṃ rajasvalāṃ kanyām anūḍhām api na śliṣyen nāliṅget || 9.31 ||
na kanyām || 9.32 ||
aduṣṭabāvenāliṅgane na doṣa iti jñāpanārtham | evaṃ ca – duṣṭabhāvenāliṅganamātreṇa kanyāyā dūṣaṇaṃ bhavati | tathā ca paiśāce vivāhe saṃgamanaśabdenāliṅganam evocyata iti yuktam |
atra kim ayaṃ kanyādūṣaṇaprāyaścittenādhikriyate, uta snātakavratalopaprāyaścitteneti |
atrocyate – snātakavratalopaprāyaścittenaivādhi-kāraḥ, na kanyāgamanaprāyaścittena, smṛtyantare “retaḥsekaḥ” ity ārambhāt | yathāha manuḥ |
retaḥsekaḥ svayonīṣu kumāriṣv antyajāsu ca |
[MDh 11.58] iti || 9.32 ||
agnimukhopadhamanavigṛhyavādabahirgandhamālyadhāraṇapāpīyasāvalekhana-bhāryāsahabhojanāñjantyavekṣaṇakudvārapraveśapādapādadadhavanāsandīstha-bhojananadībāhutaraṇavṛkṣaviṣamārohaṇāvarohaṇaprāṇavyāyacchanāni
varjayet || 9.33 || — || 9.32 ||
H reads: “bhāryayāsaha-” and “praveśana-”
agnimukhopadhamanaṃ agner mukhenopadhmānam | vigṛhya vādo laukiko vākkalahaḥ, jalparūpasya brahmacāriprakaraṇa eva siddhatvāt | bahirgandhamālyadhāraṇam, nirgandhamālya-dhāraṇam, tathā ca smṛtyantaram: “nāgandhāṃ srajaṃ dhārayed anyatra hiraṇyasrajaḥ” iti | pāpīyasāva-lekhanam aśucidravyāvayavena kāṣṭhādinā śiraḥprabhṛtikaṇḍūyanam | bhāryāsahabhojanaṃ tayā sahaikādhārasthānnabhyavahāraḥ | “ardho vā eṣa ātmano yat patnī” ity ardhavādadarśanāt prāptau satyāṃ pratiṣedhaḥ, na punar anyayā sahābhyanu-jñānam, smṛtyantare strīśūdrocchiṣṭasāmānyaprati-ṣedhāt | añjantyavekṣaṇaṃ tailādyabhyaṅgasādhana-dravyasevāyāṃ bhāryāvalokanam | kudvārapraveśaṇaṃ gṛhāder aprasiddhadvārapraveśaḥ | pādapādadhāvanaṃ pādasya pādena prakṣālanam | āsandīsthabhojanaṃ pīṭhikādyavasthitādhārānnopayogaḥ | nadībāhutaraṇaṃ bāhubhyāṃ tatpāragamanam | nadīgrahaṇaṃ taṭākādīnām apy upalakṣaṇam | vṛkṣasyārohaṇam, viṣamasya kūpāder avarohaṇam avataraṇam | prāṇavyāyacchanaṃ prāṇoparodhaḥ ullaṅghanādibhiḥ | etāni varjayet || 9.33 ||
upadhamanam upadhmānaṃ nāgniṃ mukhenopadhamayen na jvalayet | vigṛhyavādo vākkalahaḥ | gandhamālyayor bahirdhāraṇaṃ prakāśadhāraṇam iti | “anāviḥsraganulepanaḥ” syād ity āpastambaḥ [ĀpDh 1.32.5] | pāpīyasāvalekhanam aśucinā kāṣṭhādinā śiraḥprabhṛteḥ kaṇḍūyanam | tṛtīyāyā alukchāndasaḥ | bhāryayā saha bhojanaṃ bhāryayā sahaikasmin bhājane bhojanam | keṣucid deśeṣv ācārāt prāptau satyāṃ niṣedhaḥ | anye tv ekasmin kāle bhojanaṃ sahabhojanam icchanti | añjantyavekṣaṇam | añjantī tailābhyaṅgaṃ kurvaty añjanādibhir alaṃkriyamāṇā vā, tasyā avekṣaṇam | tac ca bhāryāviṣayam ity eke | strīmātraviṣayam ity anye | kudvārapraveśanaṃ dvāravyatiriktapradeśena devālayagṛhādeḥ praveśanam aprasiddhamārgeṇa nagaragrāmādeḥ praveśanam iti | yathā ca āpastambaḥ: “na kusṛtyā grāmaṃ praviśed yadi praviśen namo rudrāya vāstoṣpataya ity etām ṛcaṃ japed anyāṃ vā raudrīm” iti [ĀpDh 1.31.21] | pādapādadhāvanaṃ pādena pādaprakṣālanam | āsandīsthabhojanam, āsandī pīṭhikā tatrasthasyānnasya bhojanam āsandīsthabhojanam | yad vā yatrāsīno bhuṅkte tatrāsane bhojanapātraṃ nidhāya yad bhojanaṃ tad vā | nadībāhutaraṇaṃ bāhubhyāṃ nadyāstaraṇaṃ pāragamanam | bāhutaranāt plavādau na doṣaḥ | nadīgrahaṇaṃ taḍāgādīnām apy upalakṣaṇam | vṛkṣaviṣamārohaṇāvarohaṇe vṛkṣasyārohaṇaṃ viṣamasya kūpāder avarohaṇaṃ ca | vṛkṣaviṣamagrahaṇenātyunnatanimnasthalaṃ lakṣyate | prāṇavyāyacchanaṃ prānoparodhyullaṅghanajala-yantrādyadhirohaṇam | etāny agnimukhopadhama-nādīni varjayet || 9.32 ||
na saṃdigdhāṃ nāvam adhirohet || 9.34 || — || 9.33 ||

sarvāpāyebhyaḥ śarīraṃ rakṣet | yathā – “naikaḥ prapadyetādhvānam” [MDh 4.60], “nārīsahāyaḥ” ityādi | sarvagrahaṇāt pratiṣiddhasevanenāpi | evaśabdād grahaśāntyādibhir api | ātmaśabdena śarīram ucyate, tadrakṣaṇam api yathā na vinaśyati tathā kartavyam ity arthaḥ | yady api kāla eva vināśakaḥ, tathāpi durjñeyatvād āyuṣaḥ pauruṣahīno na bhaved ity abhiprāyaḥ | tathā jyotiḥśāstraṃ |
āyurjñānaṃ vayojñānaṃ garbhiṇīnāṃ ca lakṣaṇam |
ṛṣayaś cāpi muhyanti kiṃ punar māṃsacakṣuṣaḥ || iti |
kecid anyathā vyācakṣate – sarvopāyaiḥ paramātmānaṃ gopāyed vijājīyāt, dhātūnām anekārthatvāt | tathā ca śrutiḥ: “ātmā mantavyo nididhyāsitavyaḥ” [BṛĀrUp 2.4.5] iti |
anye tu – ātmaśabdena paramātmakṣetrajñajīvātmabhūtātmānaś catvāra ucyanta ity āhuḥ | yathāha manuḥ |
yo ‘syātmanaḥ kārayitā taṃ kṣetrajñaṃ pracakṣate |
yaḥ karoti tu karmāṇi sa bhūtātmocyate budhaiḥ ||

yena vedayate sarvaṃ sukhaṃ duḥkhaṃ ca janmasu || [MDh 12.12–13]
praśāsitāraṃ sarveṣm aṇīyāṃsam aṇor api |
rukmābhaṃ svapnadhīgamyaṃ vidyāttaṃ puruṣaṃ param || [MDh 12.122] iti || 9.35 ||

prāvṛtya vastrādinā śiraḥ pidhāya divā samantāc caṅkramaṇaṃ na kuryāt | alpāvakāśaṃ gacched api, pariśabdaprayogāt | tatra śirovedanāparihārārthaṃ na doṣaḥ, “sarvata evātmānaṃ gopāyet” [GDh 9.35] ity uktatvāt || 9.36 ||
