chedanabhedanavilikhanavimardanāvasphoṭanāni nākasmāt kuryāt || 9.51 ||
chedanaṃ tṛṇādīnām, bhedanaṃ ghaṭādeḥ, vilikhanaṃ nakhādibhiḥ kuḍyabhūmyādīnām, vimardanaṃ loṣṭādīnāṃ saṃgharṣaṇam, avasphoṭanam aṅgulyādīnāṃ sandhisaṃśabdanam – etāni viśiṣṭena nimittena vinā na kuryāt | brahmacāriprakaraṇe bāhusaṃyatatvena siddhe chedanādīnāṃ kāraṇe saty abhyanujñānārtham || 9.51 ||
chedanaṃ tṛṇādīnām | bhedanaṃ ghaṭādeḥ | vilekhanaṃ kuḍyabhūmyādau nakhādibhir vilekhanam | vimardanaṃ loṣṭādīnāṃ cūrṇīkaraṇam | avasphoṭanam aṅgulīnāṃ saśabdaṃ prasāraṇam | etad akasmān na kuryāt | kāraṇe tv avasphoṭanādiṣu na doṣaḥ | chedanādiṣv api yathāsaṃbhavaṃ mṛgyam || 9.51 ||
nopari vatsatantīṃ gacchet || 9.52 ||
yayā rajjvā vatso badhyate sā vatsatantī | tām upary ullaṅghya na gacchet | vatsaśabdo gojātyupalakṣaṇam
|| 9.52 ||
vatsabandhanī rajjur vatsatantī | tām upari na gacchet | vatsaśabdo gojāter upalakṣaṇam || 9.52 ||
na kulaṃkulaḥ syāt || 9.53 ||
gṛhād gṛhaṃ na gacched ity arthaḥ | atha vā vinā kāraṇena gṛhāt gṛhaṃ na gacchet | kulam eva vā kulaṃ yasya saḥ kulaṃkulaḥ | saty api dharmādhikāre pitṛkula eva na tiṣṭhed ity arthaḥ || 9.53 ||
kulam eva kulaṃ yasya sa kulaṃkulaḥ | chāndaso mumāgamaḥ | evaṃvidho na syāt | anyatra gamane ‘dhyayanādilābhe sati svakula eva na tiṣṭhed iti | apara āha – kulāt kulāntaragāmī kulaṃkulo dattādirūpeṇa tathāvidho na syāt | svasūtraparityāgena parasūtraṃ na bhajed iti | tatra smṛtyantaram |
yaḥ svasūtraṃ parityajya parasūtraṃ niṣevate |
śākhāraṇḍaḥ sa vijñeyaḥ sarvakarmabahiṣkṛtaḥ || iti || 9.53 ||
na yajñam avṛto gacchet || 9.54 ||
anupāmantrito yajñaṃ neyāt || 9.54 ||
avṛto ‘nupāmantrito yajñaṃ na gacchet || 9.54 ||
darśanāya tu kāmam || 9.55 ||
kāmam iti nāvaśyam, evam api gacchato lāghavam avaśyaṃbhāvīti | tuśabdo niyamārthaḥ, darśanārtham eva na tu bhojanārtham apīti || 9.55 ||
avṛto ‘pi kāmaṃ darśanāya yajñaṃ gacchet | na tv ārtvijyādilipsayā || 9.55 ||
na bhakṣān utsaṅge bhakṣayet || 9.56 ||
bhakṣāḥ pṛthukādayaḥ | yeṣām utsaṅge bhakṣaṇaṃ prasaktaṃ tān utsaṅgopari kṛtvā na bhakṣayet
|| 9.56 ||
bhakṣāḥ pṛthukādayas tān utsaṅge kṛtvā na bakṣayet
|| 9.57 ||
na rātrau preṣyāhṛtam || 9.57 ||
rātrau preṣyeṇa śūdrādinā āhṛtaṃ yat kiṃcin na bhakṣayet | kuta etat? bhakṣān iti prakṛte ekavacananirdeśāt | divānītasyāpratiṣedhaḥ || 9.57 ||
rātrau preṣyeṇa kiṃkareṇa yad ānītaṃ tad yat kiṃcd api na bhakṣayet | na bhakṣān eva, ekavacananirdeśāt
|| 9.57 ||
uddhṛtasnehavilayanapiṇyākamathitaprabhṛtīni cāttavīryāṇi nāśnīyāt || 9.58 ||
H reads: “vilapana”
uddhṛtasneham āttāgramaṇḍaṃ yad dadhyādi | vilayanaṃ ghṛtādiniṣpannam | piṇyākam āttarasam, itarasya doṣābhāvāt | tathā cārthavādaśrutiḥ: “vaiśākhyāṃ paurṇamāsyām anāttasāraṃ piṇyākaṃ dadyuḥ” iti | mathitaṃ takram, tad apy uddhṛtanavanītam, itarasya doṣābhāvāt | tathā ca gṛhyasmṛtiḥ: “dadhi nirmathyānāttanavanītam aśnīyāt” iti | prabhṛtigrahaṇāt kīlāṭakucakādīni | rātryanukarṣaṇārthaś cakāraḥ | etāni rātrau nāśnīyād iti |
atha vā – uddhṛtasneha iti pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyate | uddhṛtasnehaṃ yad vilayana, yac ca piṇyākam, yāni ca mathitaprabhṛtīni | prabhṛtigrahaṇena dadhikṣīre ucyete | tayor uddhṛtasnehatvam āttāgramaṇḍatvam | uddhṛtasnehāni nāśnīyād iti siddhe vilayanādīnām upādānam anyeṣām āmraphalaguḍādīnām uddhṛtasnehānām abhyanujñānārtham | etāni rātrau nāśnīyāt, divā na doṣaḥ |
āttavīryāṇīti vīryaśabdena sārabhūtam ucyate | tad āttaṃ yasmin tad āttavīryam ucyate kadalīphalatvagādi | pṛthag ārambhād divāpi |
nanu cābhakṣyabhakṣaṇaprakaraṇa evedaṃ vaktavyam iti |
atrocyate – evaṃ vyatikrame snātakavratalopaprāyaścittaṃ syāt, nābhakṣyabhakṣaṇaprāyaścittam ity atropādānam iti | apreṣyāhṛtāny api nāśnīyād rātrāv iti vilayanādīnāṃ pṛthag ārambhaḥ || 9.58 ||
āttavīryāṇy upāttasārāṃśāni nāśnīyāt | kāni punas tāni teṣām udāharaṇaprapañcaḥ | uddhṛtasnehe upāttāgramaṇḍe dadhipayasī | vilapanaṃ navanītamalam | yantre pīḍitānāṃ tilānāṃ kalkaḥ piṇyākam | yasya mathanamātraṃ nāmbusaṃsargas tad api mathitam | yad āhur naighaṇṭikāḥ:
takraṃ hy udaśvin mathitaṃ pādāmbv ardhāmbu nirjalam || iti | tac ca dvividham, āttanavanītam itarac ca | tatrāpy asyeha grahaṇaṃ tad dhy āttavīryasyodāharaṇam | prabhṛtigrahaṇena yac cānyad evaṃvidhaṃ kalkarūpaṃ tasya grahaṇam | uddhṛta-navanītaṃ takram āśyam anāśyam iti cintyam | ācāras tva aśanam eva | abhakṣyaprakaraṇe vaktavya iha vacanāt snātakavratalope yat prāyaścittaṃ tad evaiṣām aśane bhavati nābhakṣyabhakṣaṇanimittam || 9.58 ||
sāyaṃ prātas tv annam abhipūjitam anindan bhuñjīta || 9.59 ||
antarā bhojanapratiṣedhārtho ‘yam ārambhaḥ, arthaprāptatvāt | tathā ca manuḥ |
sāyaṃ prātar dvijātīnām aśanaṃ śruticoditam |
nāntarā bhojanaṃ kuryād agnihotrasamo vidhiḥ ||
iti | tuśabdo viśeṣavācī, annaviśeṣasya vipratiṣedho na modakādīnām apīti | abhipūjitaṃ namaskāraḥ | abhiśabdaprayogāt samantrakaṃ darśayati | tathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “rocata iti sāyaṃ prātar aśanāny abhipūjayet” [VaDh 3.69] iti | anindan kadannatvādidoṣeṇākutsayan bhuñjīta || 9.59 ||
tuśabdas tv avadhāraṇe | sāyaṃ rātriḥ prātas ahas tayor dvayor ekakālayor aśanaṃ bhuñjīta | nāntareti prāptasya bhojanasya parisaṃkhyeyam | tatra guṇavidhir abhipūjitam anindann iti | abhipūjitaṃ rocata iti | anindan kadannatvādidoṣeṇākutsayan | “sāyaṃ prātar aśanāny abhipūjayet” [VaDh 3.69] iti vasiṣṭhaḥ || 9.59 ||
na kadācid rātrau nagnaḥ svapet || 9.60 ||
kadācit nimitteṣv api | rātrāv eveti niyamārthaṃ rātrigrahaṇam, yadi divāpīṣyate tadānīṃ na kadācin nagnaḥ svaped ity ukte sarvaṃ siddhyatīti | tataś ca divā na doṣaḥ | kiṃ ca – anenaitad api jñāpayati – divā-svapnapratiṣedho ‘sya nāstīti | anyathā tadānīm api na kadācin nagna svaped ity ukte divāsvapnapratiṣedhād evārthato divālabhyamānatvāt pāriśeṣyād rātrāv eva bhavatīti rātrigrahaṇam anarthakaṃ syād iti || 9.60 ||
supyād rātrau na tu nagnaḥ svapet | tad evaṃ rātrau nagnasya svāpapratiṣedho divā tu sarvatheti | kadācid grahaṇād idaṃ labhyate | anyathā rātrau na nagnaḥ svaped ity eva vācyaṃ syāt || 9.60 ||
snāyād vā || 9.61 ||
pṛthagārambhād divāpi | vāśabdāj jalāvataraṇam api | tathā ca smṛtyantaram: “nagno jalaṃ nāvataret” iti
|| 9.61 ||
na nagna ity eva | “nagno jalaṃ nāvataret” iti smṛtyantaram || 9.61 ||
idānīm ānantyād ācārasyāśeṣato vaktum aśakyatvāt saṃkṣipyāha |
ānantyād ācārāṇāṃ pratipadapāṭho na śakya iti saṃkṣipyāha |
yac cātmavanto vṛddhāḥ samyagvinītā dambhalobhamohaviyuktā vedavida ācakṣate tat samācaret || 9.62 ||
caśabdas tuśabdasyārthe, so ‘pi viśeṣavācī, adṛṣṭam uddiśyeti | ātmavanto jñānakarmasamuccayaniṣṭhā ity arthaḥ | vṛddhāḥ vedāvasthitatattvajñānāḥ | samyagvinītāḥ gurubhiḥ suśikṣitācārāḥ | dambho lokarañjanārthaṃ dharmasevanam | lobho ‘nyāyena paradravyāditsā | moho lokaviruddhajñānam, etair viyuktā avyāptāḥ | vedavidaḥ adhītasakalavedāḥ | yad adṛṣṭārtham uddiśyācakṣate: “tvaṃ mama gobhyo yavasaṃ dehi” ityādi | dṛṣṭārthatvam “mama gobhyo yavasaṃ datvā āḍhakaṃ gṛhāṇa” ityādi | tat sakalam, amāyayā, yattador nityasaṃbandha iti yacchabdopā-dānād eva siddhe punas tacchabdena parāmarśāt | tad ācaret kuryāt prasanno bhūtvā, samupasargaprayogāt | sarvaguṇayogābhāve dviguṇatriguṇādiyoge kartavyam ity evamartho visamāsaḥ | ekasya niyogakaraṇād eva kṛtārtho na syāt, kiṃ tu bahūnāṃ niyogakaraṇād eveti bahuvacanaprayogaḥ | evaṃvidho yadi svayam eva niyuṅkte tad eva kartavyaṃ na puruṣāntaraniyogaḥ, vadantīti vaktavye ācakṣata iti gurusūtrakaraṇāt | ā maraṇāt punaḥ punaḥ kartavyam, kartavyam iti vaktavye ācared ity ārambhāt saṃbhūya vā pṛthag vā ekaviṣaye nānāviṣaye vā yo niyogaḥ kartavyaḥ kṛtsno ‘sau kartavya eva, ātmavatāṃ vṛddhānāṃ samyagvinītānāṃ dambhalobhamohaviyuktānāṃ vedavidāṃ vacanaṃ kartavyam iti vaktavye evam abhidhānāt || 9.62 ||
ātmavanto jitendriyāḥ | vṛddhāḥ pariṇatavayaso yauvane viṣayavaśyatāsaṃbhavāt | samyag vinītā gurubhiḥ śikṣitāḥ | dambho dharmacchalena lokavañcanam | lobho ‘nyāyena paradravyāditsā | moho ‘jñānaṃ lokaviruddhajñānaṃ vā tena tyaktāḥ | vedavidaḥ pāṭhataś cārthataś ca vedānāṃ coditāraḥ | atra vṛddhā iti viśeṣyam | evaṃbhūtā vṛddhā yad ācakṣate tat kartavyam iti | bahuvacananirdeśād bahūnām aikamatye tad bhavati || 9.62 ||
yogakṣemārtham īśvaram adhigacchet || 9.63 ||
alabdhalābho yogaḥ, labdhasya rakṣaṇaṃ kṣemaḥ, arthaśabdaḥ prayojanavācī, yogakṣemaprayojanam iti | īśvaraśabdena rājocyate | taṃ gacchen na kārpaṇyena, adhiśabdaprayogāt || 9.63 ||
alabdhasya lābho yogaḥ | labdhasya paripālanaṃ kṣemaḥ | tadartham īśvaraṃ rājānam adhigacchet | adhiśabdaprayogād adhir aiśvarya ity asmād akārpaṇyena svatantro gacched iti || 9.63 ||
nānyam anyatra devagurudhārmikebhyaḥ || 9.64 ||
anyaḥ ubhayakaraṇāsamarthaḥ | aprāptipūrvakaprati-ṣedhārambhād rājñaḥ anyo ‘pi ya ubhayakaraṇa-samarthaḥ so ‘dhigantavya iti jñāpayati | anyatra varjayitvā, devatā pratimā, guruḥ pitrādiḥ, dhārmikaḥ jñānakarmasamuccayānuṣṭhānaśīlaḥ, teṣām abhigamanasyādṛṣṭārthatvāt || 9.64 ||
anyaṃ rājavyatiriktaṃ yogakṣemasamartham api nādhigacched ity anuvādaḥ | devā indrādayaḥ | guravaḥ pitrādayaḥ | dhārmikā dharmācaraṇaśīlāḥ | etebhyo ‘nyatra | etān adhigacched eveti || 9.64 ||
prabhūtaidhodakayavasakuśamālyopaniṣkramaṇam āryajanabhūyiṣṭham analasasamṛddhaṃ dhārmikādhiṣṭhitaṃ niketanam āvasituṃ yateta || 9.65 ||
bahukāṣṭhodakatṛṇadarbhapuṣparamyanirgamanabhūmim, brāhmaṇādivarṇatrayabahulam, anuṣṭhānaśīlajana-saṃkīrṇam, nyāyavṛttarājaparigṛhītaṃ ca sthānam āśrayituṃ prakarṣeṇa yatnaṃ kuryāt | samuccayābhāve yathālābhaguṇayoge ‘pi nivased ity asamāsaḥ | evaṃvidham eva dharmāya rataye ca paryāptaṃ bhaviṣyatīti || 9.65 ||
edhaḥ kāṣṭham udakaṃ snānapānayogyaṃ yavasaṃ tṛṇāni gavārtham | kuśāḥ prasiddhāḥ | mālyāni puṣpāṇi devādyarcanārtham | upaniṣkramyate yatra tad upaniṣkramaṇaṃ bahiravakāśaḥ saṃcārādy-artham | evamādīni prabhūtāni yatra | āryās traivarṇi-kās ta eva janās tair bhūyiṣṭhaṃ vyāptam | alasāḥ kṛtyeṣu nirudyamās tadviparītā analasās tair sam-ṛddham | dhārmikā dharmaśīlās tair adhiṣṭhātṛbhir adhiṣṭhitam | evaṃbhūtaṃ niketanam āvasituṃ yate-ta | evaṃbhūte sthāne yatnenāpi vased iti || 9.65 ||
praśastamaṅgalyadevāyatanacatuṣpathādīn pradakṣiṇam āvarteta || 9.66 ||
H reads: “catuṣpadaṃ”
praśastaṃ gobrāhmaṇaprajñātavanaspatyādi | maṅga-lyaṃ dadhighṛtapūrṇakumbhādi | devāyatanaṃ devatāsthānam | catuṣpathaḥ prasiddhaḥ | eteṣāṃ pradakṣiṇaṃ yāyāt, nāpasavyam, yathaitāni gacchataḥ pradakṣiṇenātikrāmanti, na punar ekaikasya bhrama-ṇam, kuryād iti vaktavye āvartetety ārambhāt || 9.66 ||
nirgamanapraveśādiṣu yathā te dakṣiṇapārśve bhavanti tathā kuryād iti || 9.66 ||
manasā vā tatsamagram ācāram anupālayed āpatkalpaḥ || 9.67 ||
yadi tvaritatvāt pradakṣiṇaṃ na karoti, tato manodhyāna-mātreṇaiva saṃpādayed ity eke varṇayanti |
tad ayuktam, manasā vānupālayed ity etāvataiva labhyamānatvāt | tasmān manasā vā – tacchabdopādānād yacchabdo ‘dhyāhartavyaḥ – yat “nityaṃ śuciḥ” ityādyācārajātum uktaṃ, tatsamagram, – samagraśabdasyopādānam anantarasyaiva mā bhūd iti, ācāragrahaṇaṃ gṛhasthadharmasyāpi grahaṇaṃ mā bhūd iti – anupālayec cintayed ity arthaḥ | āpatkalpaḥ āpadvidhiḥ | āpat tvaritatvāt tatkāryāsaṃbhavaḥ |
abhāvavaikalpikārthenaiva vāśabdenāyam artho labhyata ity āpatkalpagrahaṇam anarthakam iti cet, na, āpadvihitakarmānuṣṭhānād akaraṇadoṣābhāvamātraṃ bhavati nābhyudayo ‘pīty evamartham āpatkalpagrahaṇam iti | adhyāyānte vaktavye atropādānam “satyadharmāryavṛttaḥ” [GDh 9.68] ityādivakṣyamāṇavarjanārtham || 9.67 ||
saṃbhave tu sākṣād anuṣṭhānam eveti || 9.67 ||
satyadharmāryavṛttaḥ || 9.68 ||
satyadharmā || 9.68 ||
satyadharmaś cāsāv āryavṛttaś ceti satyadharmārya-vṛttaḥ | satyadharmā satyasaṃdhaḥ | yad vadati saṃkalpayati vā tad eva karotīty arthaḥ | yena vṛttenāryāṇāṃ sādhūnāṃ priyo bhavati priyabhāṣaṇādinā tad asya vṛttam ity āryavṛttaḥ || 9.68 ||
satyavacanasvabhāvaḥ | syād iti vakṣyamāṇam apekṣate || 9.68 ||
āryavṛttaḥ || 9.69 ||
pūrvabhāṣī priyaṃvada ityādyāryāṇāṃ vṛttam iva vṛttaṃ yasya sa tathā | uṣṭramukhavan madhyamapadalopaḥ || 9.69 ||
śiṣṭādhyāpakaḥ śaucaśiṣṭaḥ śrutinirataḥ
syāt|| 9.69 ||
śiṣṭādhyāpakaḥ || 9.70 ||
śiṣṭādhyāpakaḥ satām adhyāpayitā na tv ayogyānām | jalādibhiḥ śaucaśiṣṭaḥ tatparaḥ | “nityaśuciḥ, na mithunībhūtvā śaucaṃ prati vilambeta” [GDh 9.2, 26] iti ca siddhe “manasā vā” [GDh 9.67] ity āpatkalpānupraveśo mā bhūd iti śaucasya punarupadeśaḥ | śrutinirato vedābhyāsaparaḥ | śrutigrahaṇaṃ manvādinivṛttyartham || 9.69 ||
satām adhyāpayitā na tv ayogyānām || 9.70 ||
śaucaśiṣṭaḥ || 9.71 ||
śiṣṭaṃ śāstravihitaṃ śaucaṃ yasyāsti sa tathā | niṣṭhān tasya paranipātaḥ | śāstravihitena śaucena tadvān | śaucasya punaḥ punar vacanaṃ tātparyārtham || 9.71 ||
śrutinirataḥ syāt || 9.72 ||
vedābhyāsarataḥ || 9.72 ||
nityam ahiṃsro mṛdur dṛḍhakārī damadānaśīlaḥ || 9.70 || — || 9.73 ||
nityagrahaṇāt nimitte ‘pi sati ahiṃsraḥ prāṇipīḍāvarja-naśīlaḥ | mṛduḥ aparādheṣv api pratyāpattiśīlaḥ | dṛḍhakārī ārabdhasya samāpayitā, ālocitakāryāram-bhako vā | damaśīlaḥ indriyāṇāṃ svaviṣayātiprasakter nivārayitā | dānaśīlaḥ alpadhanāpi saṃvibhāgaruciḥ | sarvāsaṃbhave yathāsaṃbhavānuṣṭhāne ‘pi kṛtārthatājñāpanārtho visamāsaḥ || 9.70 ||
nityaṃ nimitte saty apy ahiṃsro ‘hiṃsāśīlaḥ | mṛduḥ kṛtāparādhe ‘pi sahakaḥ | dṛḍhakārī pārabdhasya samāpayitā na prākramikaḥ | dama indriyanigrahaḥ | dānaṃ saṃvibhāgaḥ | tacchīlaḥ syād iti sarvatrāpekṣyate || 9.73 ||
evamācāro mātāpitarau pūrvāparān saṃbandhān duritebhyo mokṣayiṣyan snātakaḥ śaśvad brahmalokān na cyavate na cyavate || 9.71 || — 9.74 ||
H reads: “pūrvāparāṃś ca”
evamācāra evaṃkarmā snātaka iti vyavahitena saṃbandhaḥ | prakṛtasyāpi punarvacanaṃ snātakavratānām etat phalam iti jñāpanārtham | mātāpitarau “pitā mātrā” ity ekaśeṣaḥ prāpnotīti cen, na, dvāv asya pitarau, tayor gṛhyamāṇatvāt – ācārya utpādakaś ceti | pūrvasaṃbandhāḥ pitāmahādayaḥ | aparasaṃbandhā mātāmahādayaḥ | etān pāpebhyo mocayitum icchan mokṣayati caitān, svayaṃ ca brahmalokān nāpaiti | ācāragrahaṇam aśeṣasnātaka-dharmaphalārtham, mā bhūn nityaṃ śucir ityāder eveti | śaśvadgrahaṇaṃ āpekṣikanityatvanivṛttyartham | dviruktir adhyāyaparisamāptijñāpanārthā || 9.71 ||
evam uktaprakāra ācāro yasya sa evamācāraḥ | evaṃbhūtaḥ snātako mātāpitarau pūrvasaṃbandhāḥ pitāmahādayaḥ | aparasaṃbandhāḥ putrādayaḥ | tāṃś ca pūrvāparasaṃbandhān duritebhyaḥ pāpebhyo mokṣayiṣyan ye pūrvaṃ bhūtās tāṃs tadaiva narakādibhyo mocayati ye tu bhaviṣyantaḥ putrādayas tāṃś ca mokṣayiṣyan | sapratyayasyārtho mṛgyaḥ (?) | mocayiṣyan bhavati | sa evaṃbhūtaḥ snātakaḥ śaśvad bahukālaṃ brahmalokān na cyavate | dviruktir adhyāyasamāptyarthā | punaḥsnātakagrahaṇaṃ snātakadharmāṇām evaitat phalaṃ na gṛhasthadharmasahitānām ity evamartham || 9.74 ||
atha daśamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
uktāḥ prāyaśa āśramadharmāḥ | atha varṇadharmān āha |
dvijātīnām adhyayanam ijyā dānam || 10.1 ||
adhyayanaṃ vedasya grahaṇadhāraṇābhyāsajapādi-rūpam | ijyā yāgaḥ devapitṛpūjā | dānaṃ yathāśakty arthijanasya dravyasaṃvibhāgaḥ | asamāsaḥ samucca-yārthaḥ, dvijātīnāṃ yathāsaṃkhyaṃ mā bhūd iti | adhikagrahaṇād evaital labhyata iti cet, ekaikasya ekaikadharmaprādhānyajñāpanārtham ity adoṣaḥ | tad yathā – brāhmaṇasyādhyayanaṃ pradhānam, yathāha manuḥ |
japyenaiva tu saṃsidhyet brāhmaṇo nātra saṃśayaḥ | [MDh 2.87] ityādi | kṣatriyasyejyā | tathāha “yajeta rājā kratubhiḥ” [MDh 7.79] iti | vaiśyasya dānam, tathā cāha |
dadyāc ca sarvabhūtebhyo ‘py annam eva prayatnataḥ | iti | [MDh 9.333] | pūrvasya pūrvasya vā balīyastvajñāpanārtho ‘pi visamāsaḥ | yathāha uśanā |
dānāc chataguṇo yāgo yāgāc chataguṇo japaḥ || iti |
tathā manunāpi yāgāj japasya prādhānyaṃ darśitam |
ye pākayajñāś catvāro vidhiyajñasamanvitāḥ |
sarve te japayajñasya kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm || iti | [MDh 2.86] atra dvijātīnām adhyayanam ijyā dānam ity upanayanottarakālam eva dānasya vidhānāt, anupanītena yat kṛtaṃ dānaṃ tasyāsiddhatvaṃ draṣṭavyam || 10.1 ||
yathāsaṃkhyam atra na bhavati | uttaratrādhikagrahaṇāt tatraiva vaktavyaṃ bhaviṣyati | adhyayanaṃ vedagrahaṇābhyāsarūpam | ijyā yāgo devapitṛpūjā | dānaṃ pātre dravyatyāgaḥ | dvijātīnām iti vacanād yadā dvijātayaḥ saṃpannāḥ kṛtopanayanās tata ārabhyaite dharmāḥ | tenānupanītānāṃ dāne ‘py adhikāro nāstīti kecit | neti ca vayam | dvijātīnām ity upalakṣaṇaṃ yeṣāṃ dvijātir janma teṣām iti | tenānupanītasyāpy arthavato hitaiṣibhiḥ pravartitasya dānaṃ bhavaty eva || 10.1 ||
brāhmaṇasyādhikāḥ pravacanayājanapratigrahāḥ || 10.2 ||
brāhmaṇagrahaṇaṃ dvijāter adhikārād ādhikyaṃ pūrveṣu satsu bhavatīti |
nanu “vaiśyasyādhikam” [GDh 10.48], “rājño ‘dhikam” [GDh 10.7] iti ca vakṣyati | tataś ca brāhmaṇasyaivaitad bhavatīti brāhmaṇagrahaṇaṃ na kartavyam iti |
ucyate – pūrvavan naite āśramadharmāḥ, kiṃ tu varṇadharmā iti jñāpanārtham | tataś cānupanītasyāpi pratigrahādhikāro ‘sti | yājanādhyāpane tv asaṃbhavān na staḥ | brāhmaṇasya pravacanayājanapratigrahāś ceti vaktavye adhikagrahaṇam ete vṛttyarthā api bhavantīti jñāpanārtham | pūrvasūtravad asamāso na kṛtaḥ samuccayenāpy anuṣṭhānārtham ekārthatvād vikalpo mā bhūd iti | pravacanam anuvacanam | śeṣaṃ nigadavyākhyānam || 10.2 ||
pravacanam adhyāpanam | yājanam ārtvijyam | pratigrahaḥ prasiddhaḥ | ete brāhmaṇasyādhikāḥ pūrvebhyo 'dhyayanādibhiḥ | te cāmī ca samuccitā ity uktaṃ bhavati | atrāpy anupanītasyāpi pratigraho bhavati | yājanādhyāpane tv asaṃbhavān na bhavataḥ | brāhmaṇasya pravacanayājanapratigrahā ity eva siddhe ‘dhikagrahaṇaṃ pūrvatra yathāsaṃkhyaṃ mā bhūd iti pūrve tāvad avasthitāḥ || 10.2 ||
pūrveṣu niyamas tu || 10.3 ||
prathameṣv adhyayanādiṣv avaśyakartavyatā tataś cākriyāyāṃ doṣaḥ kriyāyāṃ cābhyudayaḥ | pravacanā-diṣv aniyamaḥ, tataś ca kriyāyām abhyudaya akriyāyāṃ pratyavāyābhāvaś ca | tuśabdo viśeṣavācī kṣatriyavaiśyayor apy eteṣu niyamaḥ, brāhmaṇasya viśeṣata iti | ata evaivaṃ sūtraṃ na kṛtam: “dvijātīnām adhyayanam ijyā dānaṃ niyamataḥ” iti, uttareṣv api niyamādhikāraḥ prasajyeteti || 10.3 ||
niyamo ‘vaśyakartavyatā | pūrvāṇy adhyayanādīny avaśyakartavyāni | akurvan pratyavaiti kurvaṃś cābhyudeti | pravacanādīni tu vṛttyarthāni | ato ‘karaṇe na pratyavāyaḥ karaṇe nābhyudayaḥ || 10.3 ||
ācāryajñātipriyagurudhanavidyāniyameṣu brahmaṇaḥ saṃpradānam anyatra
yathoktāt || 10.4 ||
ācārya uktalakṣaṇaḥ | jñātir mātṛpitṛsaṃbandhaḥ | priyaḥ sakhā | guruḥ pitā jyeṣṭhaḥ ṛtvik ityādiḥ | dhananiyamo dhanaparivartanam | vidyāniyamo vidyāntareṇa parivartanam | brahmagrahaṇaṃ vedārtham | tataś cāṅgādāv aniyamaḥ | saṃpradānam adhyāpanam, kartavyam iti śeṣaḥ | anyatra yathoktāt “anujñāta upaviśet” ityādikāyāḥ śuśrūṣāyāḥ | anyatroktād iti vaktavye yathāgrahaṇaṃ yena vidhinā adhyayanam uktaṃ tasyaivāyam apavādo na punaḥ “śiṣṭādhyāpakaḥ” [GDh 6.69] ity asyāpi | tataś ca dhanavidyāniyamenāpy ayogyāya na deyam || 10.4 ||
brahmaḥ vedaḥ | tasya saṃpradānaṃ samyak pradānam | “anujñāta upaviśet” [GDh 1.55] ity ārabhya śuśrūṣavo ‘dhyāpyā ity uktaṃ sa yathoko niyamaḥ | tasmād anyatra vināpīti tenācāryādibhyo brahma pradeyam ity ucyate | ācārya uktaḥ | jñātayo bhrātṛpitṛvyādayaḥ | priyaḥ sakhā | guravo mātulādayaḥ | eteṣu brahma samyak pradeyam | tathā dhanavidyāniyameṣu | dhanena vidyāyāḥ parivartanaṃ dhananiyamaḥ | vidyāntareṇa parivartanaṃ vidyāniyamaḥ | teṣv api brahma saṃpradeyam | dhanavidyāniyamo ‘pi yogyaviṣaya eva, “śiṣṭādhyāpakaḥ” [GDh 9.70] ity uktatvāt | na ca tasyāpy ayam apavādaḥ | yathāvad grahaṇavidhinādhyayanam uktaṃ tasyaivāyam apavāda iti || 10.4 ||
kṛṣivāṇijye cāsvayaṃkṛte || 10.5 ||
H reads: “vāsvayaṃkṛte”
kṛṣivāṇijye prasiddhe, te anyena kārite brāhmaṇasyādhike | caśabdāt paśupālanaṃ cāsvayaṃkṛtam eva || 10.5 ||
kṛṣiḥ karṣaṇena sasyotpādanaṃ vāṇijyā krayavikrayavyavahāraḥ | te ca brāhmaṇasyādhike yady asvayaṃkṛte | anyena kārayituṃ śakyete || 10.5 ||
kusīdaṃ ca || 10.6 ||
kusīdam upacayārtho dhanaprayogaḥ, tad asvakṛtam eva, caśabdaprayogāt | pṛthaggrahaṇaṃ cāsya svayaṃ-kṛtasyāpy anujñānārtham | tathā ca “kusīdavṛddhir dharmyā” [GDh 12.26] iti vakṣyati || 10.6 ||
kusīdam upacayārtho dhanaprayogaḥ | tad apy asvayaṃkṛtaṃ ced brāhmaṇasyādhikam || 10.6 ||
rājño ‘dhikaṃ rakṣaṇaṃ sarvabhūtānām || 10.7 ||
rājñaḥ kṣatriyasyābhiṣekavato rakṣaṇaṃ pālanaṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ sarvaprāṇinām | sarvaśabdaprayogāt sthāvarāṇāṃ ca taddvāreṇa vṛttir bhavatīty adhikagrahaṇam || 10.7 ||
rājño ‘bhiṣiktasya sarvabhūtānāṃ rakṣaṇam adhikam | sarvagrahaṇāt sthāvarādīnām apy aśvatthādīnāṃ chedananirodhena || 10.7 ||
tat punaḥ kathaṃ kartavyam ity ata āha |
nyāyyadaṇḍatvam || 10.8 ||
lokāviruddhadaṇḍadhāritvam, na punar aiśvaryābhimā-nāt svecchayā daṇḍapātanam | vakṣyati ca: “daṇḍo da-manād ity āhus tenādāntān damayet” [GDh 11.30] iti | adāntanigraheṇa itareṣāṃ rakṣaṇaṃ siddhyatīti || 10.8 ||
nyāyād anapeto nyāyyaḥ śāstrāviruddho daṇḍo yasya tadbhāvo nyāyyadaṇḍatvam | nyāyadaṇḍatvam ity api pāṭha eṣa evārthaḥ | sa ca rājño dharmaḥ | rāgadveṣādinā na nyūnādhikadaṇḍaḥ syād iti || 10.8 ||
kiṃ cānyat |
bibhṛyād brāhmaṇān śrotriyān || 10.9 ||
brāhmaṇān brahmaguṇasaṃpannān śrotriyān śrutimataḥ, sāmarthyāt svaviṣayavāsinaḥ | bharaṇam annādidānena || 10.9 ||
śrotriyā adhītavedās tān brāhmaṇān annādidānena bibhṛyāt || 10.9 ||
nirutsāhāṃś cābrāhmaṇān || 10.10 ||
nirutsāhāṃś ca brāhmaṇān || 10.10 ||
nirutsāhatvam upajīvanārthakarmānuṣṭhānāsamar-thatvam | abrāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriyādayaḥ | iha cābrāhmaṇagrahaṇāt pūrvasūtre labdham api brāhmaṇagrahaṇaṃ kriyate, kathaṃ nāmāśrotriyāṇām api brāhmaṇānāṃ jīvanāsamarthānāṃ bharaṇaṃ syād iti | caśabdād dīnānāthāṃś ca || 10.10 ||
jīvanārtham utsāhaṃ kartum asamarthā nirutsāhās tān brāhmaṇān [abrāhmaṇān?] api bibhṛyāt | kiṃ punar brāhmaṇān | pūrvasūtre sarjane samarthān api śrotriyān samam āhūya bibhṛyād iti || 10.10 ||
akarāṃś copakurvāṇāṃś ca || 10.11 ||
akarāṃś ca || 10.11 ||
akarāḥ pravrajitāḥ abrāhmaṇā eva, teṣām evādhikārāt | brāhmaṇapravratijānāṃ tu pratigrahānadhikārād eva noktam | tāṃś ca sādhanādidānena bibhṛyāt | caśabdād vyādhibhayādyupahatāṃś ca | upakurvāṇā lokopakā-riṇo vaidyādayaḥ | ātmīyaparicārakā iti kecit | tad ayuktam, arthasiddhatvāt teṣām | cakārāt kālajñāṃś ca || 10.11 ||
ye pūrvair dattā akarā brāhmaṇādibhyas tāṃś ca yathāpūrvaṃ bibhṛyād bādhakādinirāsena | svayaṃ ca nāpūrvaṃ karam utpādayed iti || 10.11 ||
upakurvāṇāṃś ca || 10.12 ||
ādhīyānā [adhī-?] brahmacāriṇa upakurvāṇās tāṃś ca bibhṛyād annādidānena | yady arthinaḥ svayaṃ jīvitavanto vanasūkarādivyāvartanena | apara āha – upakurvāṇā lokopakurvāṇā vaidyādaya iti || 10.12 ||
yogaś ca vijaye || 10.12 || — || 10.13 ||
vijaye kartavye yogaḥ kartavyaḥ | alabdhalābho yogaḥ | vāllabhyādinā yair āṭavikādibhiḥ karo na dattaḥ tān yatnato gṛhṇīyād ity arthaḥ | cakārāl labdhapari-rakṣaṇaṃ ca | corādayo yathā na haranti tathā kartavyam ity arthaḥ | evaṃ ca pūrvaṃ yogaṃ kṣemaṃ ca kṛtvā paścād arijaye yatnaḥ kartavya iti draṣṭavyam || 10.12 ||
yoga upāyo vijayaviśayaś ca yogaḥ kāryaḥ | ayam api rājño ‘dhiko dharma iti || 10.13 ||
bhaye viśeṣeṇa || 10.13 || — || 10.14 ||
bhayam anyābhibhavaṃ tasmin viśeṣeṇātyantaṃ yatnato yogakṣemau kartavyau || 10.13 ||
anyābhibhavādinimitte viśeṣeṇa yogaḥ kāryaḥ || 10.14 ||
caryā ca rathadhanubhyām || 10.14 || — || 10.15 ||
caryā rāṣṭrasya sarvato ‘ṭanam | rathagrahaṇaṃ hasty-aśvādivāhanopalakṣaṇam, dhanurgrahaṇaṃ khaḍgādī-nām | cakārād ekasminn avasthānaṃ ca | caryāvasthā-nayor vyavasthā kāryavaśād draṣṭavyā || 10.14 ||
caraṇaṃ caryā | bahiḥpradeśe caran ratham ārūḍho dhanurhastaś ca caret | rathagrahaṇaṃ hastyaśvāder upalakṣaṇaṃ dhanurgrahaṇaṃ ca khaḍgādeḥ || 10.15 ||
saṃgrāme saṃsthānam anivṛttiś ca || 10.15 || — || 10.16 ||
saṃgrāme yuddhe saṃsthānaṃ nirbhayasyāvasthānam, anivṛttiś ca tatraiva prāṇaparityāgaḥ | cakārād yuddhaṃ ca | visamāsād guptāśṛayatvaṃ ca || 10.15 ||
saṃgrāmo yuddhaṃ tatra saṃsthānaṃ prāṇātyayaḥ | nivṛttiḥ palāyanaṃ tadabhāvo ‘nivṛttiḥ | etau ca rājño ‘dhikau dharmau || 10.16 ||
idānīṃ nityam ahiṃsra ity asyāpavādam āha |
na doṣo hiṃsāyām āhave || 10.16 || — || 10.17 ||
āhavo yuddham | prakṛtasyāpi punarvacanaṃ viśeṣā-rtham – yasminn anyo ‘nyam āhūya puruṣāḥ pravar-tante tatraiva na doṣo nānyāvasthāyām iti || 10.16 ||
yatra parasparam āhvayante sa āhavaḥ | tādṛśe yuddhe śatrūṇāṃ hiṃsāyām api na doṣaḥ | nityam ahiṃsra ity asyāyam apavādaḥ || 10.17 ||
anyatra vyaśvasārathyāyudhakṛtāñjaliprakīrṇakeśaparāṅmukhopaviṣṭasthalavṛkṣārūḍha-dūtagobrāhmaṇavādibhyaḥ || 10.17 || — || 10.18 ||
H reads: “vṛkṣādhirūḍha”
evaṃvidhān hiṃsato doṣaḥ | viśabdaḥ tribhiḥ saṃbadhyate – vyaśvaḥ, visārathiḥ, vyāyudha iti | kṛtāñjaliḥ baddhāñjaliḥ | prakīrṇakeśo vimuktakeśaḥ | parāṅmukhaḥ anyamukhaḥ | upaviṣṭaḥ āsīnaḥ | sthalārūḍhaḥ parvatārūḍhaḥ | vṛkṣārūḍhaḥ prasiddhaḥ | dūto ‘haṃ brāhmaṇo ‘ham iti ca yo vadati, yaś ca govacchabdaṃ karoti, ebhyo ‘nyatra na doṣaḥ || 10.17 ||
viśabdaś ca tribhiḥ saṃbadhyate | vyaśvo visārathir vyāyudha iti | yasyāśvo hataḥ sa vyaśvaḥ | yasya sārathir hataḥ sa visārathiḥ | yasyāyudhaṃ kṛttaṃ patitaṃ vā sa vyāyudhaḥ | kṛtāñjalir bhayena | prakīrṇakeśaḥ keśān api niyantum akṣamaḥ | parāṅmukho bhayena pṛṣṭhīkṛtya palāyamānaḥ | upaviṣṭaḥ palāyitum apy asamartha āsīnaḥ | sthalavṛkṣādhirūḍhaḥ | sthalam unnatapradeśas tam vṛkṣaṃ vārūḍhaḥ | dūto vārtāharaḥ | gaur asmi brāhmaṇo ‘smīti ye vadanti te gobrāhmaṇavādinaḥ | etebhyo ‘nyatrāhave hiṃsāyāṃ na doṣaḥ | eteṣu doṣa iti || 10.18 ||
kṣatriyasyābhiṣekaguṇayuktasya dharma uktaḥ | idānīm itarasyāha |
kṣatriyaś ced anyas tam upajīvet tadvṛttyā || 10.18 || — || 10.19 ||
anyo jātimātrasaṃbandhaḥ taṃ rājānam upajīvet tadvṛttyā “caryā ca rathadhanubhyām” [GDh 10.14] ityādinā || 10.18 ||
anyaś cet kṣatriyas taṃ rājānaṃ deśopaplavādino-pajīvet tadā tadvṛttyā tasya rājño yā vṛttiś caryā rathadhanurbhyām ityādikā tayā yuktaḥ saṃjīvet | tena rājñaivam asau saṃmata iti || 10.19 ||
jetā labheta sāṃgrāmikaṃ vittam || 10.19 || — || 10.20 ||
yadi rājñā niyuktaḥ paraṃ nirjitya dhanam āsādayati sa tat sāṃgrāmikaṃ dhanaṃ labheta || 10.19 ||
rājñā niyukto rājabhṛtyādiḥ saṃgrāme śatrūn nirjitya yad vittaṃ labhate tat sa eva jetā labheta na rājā
|| 10.20 ||
asyāpavādam āha |
vāhanaṃ tu rājñaḥ || 10.20 || — || 10.21 ||
vāhanaṃ hastyādi | tuśabdo viśeṣavācī | mahārghaṃ yad ratnādi tad api rājña eva, viśeṣato vāhanam iti | tataś ca vāhanaviṣaye svalpam api draṣṭavyam
|| 10.20 ||
vāhanaṃ hastyaśvādikaṃ nirjitya labdhaṃ rājño bhavati na jetuḥ || 10.21 ||
uddhāraś cāpṛthagjaye || 10.21 || — || 10.22 ||
apṛthagjayaḥ sarājako jayaḥ tatroddhāro rājñaḥ sāra-dravyam | vāhanasamuccayārthaś cakāraḥ || 10.21 ||
yadi sarve sainikāḥ saṃbhūya jayeyur jitvā ca kim api labheraṃs tasminn apṛthagjaye rājñaḥ uddhāro viśeṣadravyaṃ svayaṃ vṛto deyaḥ || 10.22 ||
anyat tu yathārhaṃ bhājayed rājā || 10.22 || — || 10.23 ||
śauryādyapekṣayā yodhebhyo dadyāt | rājagrahaṇam evaṃ kurvann anurāgād rājā saṃpadyata iti | svayam eva vibhajanārthaṃ vā, na parair iti || 10.22 ||
yat svayaṃ vṛtaṃ māṇikyādi tato ‘nyad yathārhaṃ yasya yāvān vyāpāro yāvad vā śauryaṃ tadanurūpeṇa bhājayet | yathaite tadanurūpaṃ bhajeraṃs tathā kārayed iti || 10.23 ||
adhunā rakṣaṇanimittām asya vṛttim āha |
rājñe balidānaṃ karṣakair daśamam aṣṭamaṃ ṣaṣṭhaṃ vā || 10.23 || — || 10.24 ||
niyuktāya deyam iti rājagrahaṇam | pratisaṃvatsaraṃ deyam iti baligrahaṇam | dānaṃ kartavyam iti śeṣaḥ | karṣakair yāvantaḥ kṛṣijīvinaḥ, na tu vaiśyenaiva | daśamaṃ vāṣṭamaṃ vā ṣaṣṭhaṃ vā adhamamadhyamottamabhūbhāgakrameṇa vyavasthitavikalpo draṣṭavyaḥ || 10.23 ||
karṣakaiḥ kṣetre yal labdhaṃ tasya daśamabhāgo ‘ṣṭamaḥ ṣaṣṭho vāṃśo rājño balidānaṃ kararūpeṇa deyaḥ | asya rājñaḥ karṣakaiḥ kṣetre yal labdhaṃ tad rakṣaṇanimittā vṛttir eṣā | kṛṣṭāyā bhūmer atibhoga-madhyamabhogālpabhogaviṣayo ‘yaṃ vyavasthito vikalpaḥ | atibhoge daśamāṃśo madhyamabhoge ‘ṣṭamāṃśo ‘lpabhoge ṣaṣṭhāṃśa iti || 10.24 ||
paśuhiraṇyayor apy eke pañcāśadbhāgaḥ || 10.24 || — || 10.25 ||
paśupālanenopajīvataḥ sakāśāt paśūnāṃ pañcāśadbhāgaṃ gṛhṇīyāt | hiraṇyaṃ vārdhuṣikasakāśāt |
samārghaṃ dhanam uddhṛtya mahārghaṃ ya prayacchati |
sa vai vārdhuṣiko nāma brahmavādiṣu garhitaḥ || [VaDh 2.41] iti vārdhuṣikasya pratiṣedhād evāsyābhāvaḥ prāpnotīti cet, naiṣa doṣaḥ, “kāmaṃ pariluptakṛtyāya pāpīyase dadyātām” [VaDh 2.43] iti vasiṣṭhena prakārāntareṇābhyanujñānāt | ekegrahaṇān na tu gautamaḥ, tatra yeṣām apratiprasavaḥ teṣu na gṛhṇīyāt | yeṣāṃ pratiṣedhābhāvād eva vārdhuṣikatvaṃ syāt, teṣu gṛhṇīyād ity evaṃ draṣṭavyam || 10.24 ||
ye paśubhir jīvanti ye vā hiraṇyaprayoktāro vārdhuṣikās taiḥ pañcāśattamo bhāgo rājñe deya ity eke | tad yathā – yasya pañcāśat paśavaḥ santi sa pratisaṃvatsaram ekaṃ paśuṃ rājñe dadyāt | yasya vā pañcāśan niṣkair vṛddhiprayogaḥ sa pratisaṃvatsaram ekaikaṃ niṣkaṃ rājñe balirūpeṇa dadyād iti || 10.25 ||
viṃśatibhāgaḥ śulkaḥ paṇye || 10.25 || — || 10.26 ||
paṇyaṃ paṇanīyaṃ yad vaṇigbhir vikrīyate hiṃgvādi teṣu viṃśatibhāgaṃ gṛhṇīyāt | śulkagrahaṇaṃ saṃjñārtham, tataś ca “prātibhāvyavaṇikśulka” [GDh 12.38] ityādau vyavahārasiddhiḥ || 10.25 ||
yad vaṇigbhir vikrīyate tat paṇyam | tatra viṃśatitamo bhāgo rājñe deyas tasyaiva dīyamānasya śulka iti saṃjñā | śulkapradeśāḥ pratibhāvyaṃ vaṇikśulkam ityādayaḥ || 10.26 ||
mūlaphalapuṣpauṣadhamadhumāṃsatṛṇendhanānāṃ ṣāṣṭhyaḥ || 10.26 || — || 10.27 ||
mūlaṃ haridrādi, phalaṃ marīcādi, puṣpaṃ kusumbhādi, auṣadham abhayādi, tṛṇaṃ yat kiṃcit kaṭādi | śeṣāḥ prasiddhāḥ || 10.26 ||
mūlaṃ haridrādi | phalam āmrādi | puṣpam utpalādi | auṣadhaṃ bilvādi | śiṣṭāni prasiddhāni | eteṣu paṇyeṣu ṣaṣṭi(ṣṭha)tamo bhāgo rājñe deyo vikretrā || 10.27 ||
kasmāt punar etad rājño deyam ity āha |
kasmāt punar evaṃ rājñe deya ity ata āha |
tadrakṣaṇadharmitvāt || 10.27 || — || 10.28 ||
teṣāṃ karadāyināṃ rakṣaṇaṃ tadrakṣaṇam, sa eva dharmaḥ yasyāsau tadrakṣaṇadharmī, tasya bhāvas tadrakṣaṇadharmitvam, tasmāt tadrakṣaṇadharmitvāt tacchīlatvād ity arthaḥ | vacanagamye ‘rthe hetuvacanaṃ deśakālāpekṣayā uktaparimāṇād apy alpatarabhāgagrahaṇārtham, itarathā sarvam eva rājā rakṣatīti sādhāraṇo ‘yaṃ hetuḥ syād iti | tathā cāha uśanā: “deśakālalābhānurūpataḥ karān prakalpayet” iti || 10.27 ||
teṣāṃ karadāyināṃ rakṣaṇarūpeṇa dharmeṇa tadvattvāt teṣām ayaṃ rakṣaka iti kṛtveti || 10.28 ||
teṣu tu nityayuktaḥ syāt || 10.28 || — || 10.29 ||
teṣu tu balyādāneṣu sarvadā saty api kāryavyagratve tatparo bhavet | tuśabdo viśeṣavācī anyeṣv api dravyārjanopāyeṣu dharmād anapeteṣu tatparo bhavet, atra viśeṣata iti || 10.28 ||
teṣu karṣakādiṣu nityayuktaḥ syād rakṣaṇe nityam avahitaḥ syāt | apara āha – teṣu balyādiṣu nityayuktaḥ syāt | tātparyeṇādadīta śulkam | hyasyaitad (?) dhanam iti || 10.29 ||
adhike na vṛttiḥ || 10.29 ||
adhikena vṛttiḥ || 10.30 ||
syād iti śeṣaḥ | kuṭumbapoṣaṇād adhikaṃ yat kośarūpeṇānupraviṣṭaṃ tasmin kośe vṛttir na syāt, kuṭumbapoṣaṇārtham, anyatrāpadaḥ, tasmin na gṛhṇīyād ity arthaḥ | tathā ca vyāghraḥ |
kuṭumbapoṣaṇaṃ kuryān nityaṃ kośaṃ ca vardhayet |
anyatrāpattitaḥ kośaṃ na gṛhṇīyāt kadācana || iti |
kecid vyācakṣate – adhikena rakṣaṇadvārāgatena jīva-naṃ syād iti | tatra “rājño ‘dhikam” ity anena punar-uktaprasaṅgo ‘sti uta nāstīti nirūpaṇīyam || 10.29 ||
rājño ‘dhikaṃ rakṣaṇam iti yad uktaṃ taddvāreṇa yad āgataṃ dhanaṃ tad adhikaṃ tenātmanaḥ poṣyavargasya ca hastyaśvādīnāṃ ca vṛttiḥ syān na tu pūrvair yat saṃcitya khātaṃ kośarūpena tena jīvet | āpadi tu tenāpi jīvet | tathā ca vyāghraḥ |
kuṭumbapoṣaṇaṃ kuryān nityaṃ kośaṃ ca dhārayet |
āpado ‘nyatra kośāt tu na gṛhṇīyāt kadācana ||
iti || 10.30 ||
śilpino māsi māsy ekaikaṃ karma kuryuḥ || 10.30 || — || 10.31 ||
śilpino lohakārādayaḥ māsi māsy ekaikam ahar ātmānurūpaṃ rājñaḥ karma kuryuḥ, tad eva teṣāṃ śulkaṃ nānyat kiṃcid iti || 10.30 ||
ekenāhnā sādhyam ekaṃ karma | śilpino lohakārādayaḥ | te ‘pi pratimāsaṃ rājñe svīyam ekam ahaḥ karma kuryuḥ | eṣa eṣāṃ śulkaḥ || 10.31 ||
etenātmopajīvino vyākhyātāḥ || 10.31 || — || 10.32 ||
ātmopajīvino naṭanartakādayaḥ, te ‘py ekam aho rājñaḥ karma kuryur iti śilpyātmopajīvina iti vaktavye pṛthaggrahaṇam ātmopajīvinām alpopakāritvād anityatvārtham | ātmopajīvinaś cety evam api na kṛtam, smṛtyantare ‘pi śilpino yad yad uktaṃ tasya sarvasyāpy anupraveśārtham, yathāha uśanā: “śilpino māsi māsi karmaikaṃ proktam, tadabhāve kārṣāpaṇaṃ vā dadyāt” iti || 10.31 ||
ātmopajīvino ye śarīrāyāsena jīvanti kāṣṭhavāhādayas te ‘py ete ca śilpiṣūktaprakāreṇa vyākhyātā māsi māsy ekaikaṃ karma kuryur iti | nartakādiṣv apy eṣaiva gatiḥ || 10.32 ||
naucakrīvantaś ca || 10.32 || — || 10.33 ||
cakraṃ śakaṭam, naucakrābhyāṃ ya upajīvanti | bahuvacanāt vardhakināpitādayaḥ | cakārāt vanyamṛgaghātakādayaḥ | pūrvavad anityā mā bhūd iti pṛthaggrahaṇam || 10.32 ||
nauś ca cakraṃ ca naucakre | cakraśabdena tadvacchakaṭaṃ lakṣyate | tadvanto naucakrīvantaḥ | āsandīvad aṣṭhīvad ityādinā kathaṃcid rūpasiddhiḥ | nauvanto naujīvinaḥ cakra(krī)vantaḥ śakaṭajīvinaḥ | te ‘pi rājña ekam ahas tatkarma kuryuḥ || 10.33 ||
bhaktaṃ tebhyo dadyāt || 10.33 || — || 10.34 ||
tebhyaḥ śilpiprabhṛtibhyaḥ bhaktaṃ bhojanaṃ śulkaṃ dadyāt | tadgrahaṇam anantarāṇām eva mā bhūd iti
|| 10.33 ||
śilpino māsi māsīty ārabhya ye ‘nukrāntās tebhyaḥ karma kurvadbhyo bhaktam annaṃ divā bhojanaṃ dadyād rājā || 10.34 ||
paṇyaṃ vaṇigbhir arghāpacayena deyam || 10.34 || — || 10.35 ||
māsi māsy ekaikam ity anuvartate | śulkād adhikam idam | arghāpacayaḥ arghāvaramūlyam | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
śulkaṃ dadyāt tato māsam ekaikaṃ paṇyam eva ca |
arghārdhāvaramūlyena vaṇijas te pṛthak pṛthak || iti || 10.34 ||
māsi māsy ekaikam ity anuvartate | viṃśatibhagaḥ śulkaḥ paṇya ity uktam | tataḥ śulkād adhikam idaṃ māsi māmsy ekaṃ paṇyam arthāpacayena prāptasya mūlyasya kiṃcin nyūnatāṃ kalpayitvā vaṇijo rājñe dadyuḥ | tatra bṛhaspatiḥ |
śulkaṃ dadus tato māsam ekaikaṃ paṇyam eva ca |
ardhāvaraṃ ca mūlyena vaṇijas te pṛthak pṛthak ||
iti || 10.35 ||
pranaṣṭam asvāmikam adhigamya rājñe prabrūyuḥ || 10.35 || — || 10.36 ||
pranaṣṭaṃ svāmisakāśād apagatam asvāmikam avijñāyamānasvāmikam – jñāyamānasvāmikaṃ tu svāmina eva kathayet | adhigamya labdhvā rājñe nāmātyādibhya prabrūyuḥ, praśabdād amāyayā kathayeyuḥ, idam asmin deśe evaṃ cāsāditam, anviṣyatāṃ kasyaitad iti | bahuvacanaprayogāt sāmantādibhir api vaktavyam eveti || 10.35 ||
pranaṣṭaṃ svāmisakāśāt prabhraṣṭam | asvāmikam ajñāyamānasvāmikam | adhigamya bhūmau patitam upalabhya janapadapālane niyuktā ete rājñe prabrūyuḥ | anye vā kecid dṛṣṭavantas te ‘pi brūyuḥ || 10.36 ||
evaṃ kathitam |
tataḥ kiṃ kartavyaṃ rājñā |
vikhyāpya saṃvatsaraṃ rājñā rakṣyam || 10.36 || — || 10.37 ||
vikhyāpya paṭahakenāvaghuṣya amukajātīyaṃ vastv āsāditam, yasyaitat sa svayam upatiṣṭhatām iti | saṃvatsaraṃ nirupadravaṃ rakṣyaṃ pālanīyam | prāk saṃvatsarād yadi svāmy āgacchati tasmai deyam, rakṣaṇasya tadāyattatvāt adhikārāc ca labdhasyāpi rājaśabdasyopanyāsaḥ tadrakṣaṇe atyāptā niyoktavyā iti | tathā cāha uśanā: “vidyābhijanayuktān pūrvadṛṣṭapramāṇān vṛddhān nidhipālane niyuñjyāt” iti || 10.36 ||
vikhyāpya – “idam evaṃjātīyakaṃ vastv āsāditaṃ rakṣyate | yasyaitat sa āgacchatu” iti nagare paṭahena ghoṣayitvā saṃvatsaraṃ rakṣyam | prāk cet saṃvatsarāt svāmy āgacchati tato lakṣaṇāni pṛṣṭvā sāmyaṃ cet tat tasmai deyam | vaiṣamye sa daṇḍyaḥ | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
pranaṣṭādhigataṃ deyaṃ nṛpeṇa dhanine dhanam |
vibhāvayen na cel liṅgais tatsamaṃ daṇḍam arhati || iti | evam adhigamyāprabruvato daṇḍyāḥ || 10.37 ||
atha saṃvatsarād ūrdhvaṃ kiṃ kāryam ity āha |
ūrdhvam adhigantuś caturthaṃ rājñaḥ śeṣaḥ || 10.37 || — || 10.38 ||
saṃvatsarāt parato yenākhyātaṃ tasmai caturtham, rājñaḥ śeṣam | ākhyātuś caturthaṃ datvā śeṣaṃ svayaṃ gṛhṇīyāt uta niyuktāya deyam iti saṃdehaḥ syād iti tannirākaraṇārthaṃ śeṣaṃ rājña ity uktam || 10.37 ||
yenādhigamyākhyātaṃ tasmai caturtham aṃśaṃ datvā śeṣo rājñā grāhyaḥ || 10.38 ||
atha yo ‘rvāk saṃvatsarān mamedam iti prabrūyāt sa svāmīti katham avagamyate ity ata āha |
svāmī rikthakrayasaṃvibhāgaparigrahādhigameṣu || 10.38 || — || 10.39 ||
eteṣu kāraṇeṣu pratipāditeṣu jñāyamāneṣu sa svāmī bhaved iti | rikthaṃ pituḥ sakāśāt prāptam | krayo mūlyena vikrītam | saṃvibhāga utsannaṃ yad dāyādi svarūpeṇa prāptam | parigrahaḥ strīdhanam | ananyapūrvasya svīkaraṇam, yathā nadyādiṣu vṛkṣādir ity eke tatrādhigamena punar uktam asti uta nāstīti vicāraṇīyam | adhigamaḥ ākarādibhyo labdhaṃ ratnādi || 10.38 ||
rikthaṃ pitrādīnām abhāve prāptam | krayo mūlyena svīkāraḥ | saṃvibhāgo bhrātrādīnāṃ sādhāraṇasya parasparavibhāgaḥ | parigraho vanyeṣv asvāmikeṣu vṛkṣādiṣu pūrvasvīkāraḥ | adhigamaḥ pranaṣṭasyājñātasvāmikasya nidhyādeḥ svīkāraḥ | eteṣu kāraṇeṣu dravyasvīkartā svāmī bhavati | tena pranaṣṭo ‘dhigate rājño ‘dhigantuś ca svāmyam upapannam iti prakaraṇasaṃgatiḥ | kṣetreṣūtpannāni sasyādīni kṣetravad eva kṣetravataḥ svāni | etenākareṣūtpannaṃ lavaṇādi vyākhyātam | etāni sarvavarṇasādhāraṇāni svāmyakāraṇāni ||
|| 10.39 ||
etāni tāvat sarvavarṇānāṃ sādhāraṇāni |
brāhmaṇasyādhikaṃ labdham || 10.39 || — || 10.40 ||
pratigrahopāttam, taddharmatvāt | etad uktaṃ bhavati – yājanādhyāpanopāttam api draṣṭavyam iti || 10.39 ||
yal labdhaṃ dānarūpeṇa tad brāhmaṇasyādhikaṃ svāmyamūlam || 10.40 ||
kṣatriyasya vijitam || 10.40 || — || 10.41 ||
nigṛhya śastreṇa nirjitaṃ yat tat kṣatriyasyādhikam
|| 10.40 ||
vijayena labdhaṃ kṣatriyasyādhikaṃ svam || 10.41 ||
nirviṣṭaṃ vaiśyaśūdrayoḥ || 10.41 || — || 10.42||
nirviṣṭaṃ karmaṇopāttam, vaiśyasya kṛṣyādinā, śūdrasya tu śuśrūṣādinā | adhikam iti vartate || 10.41 ||
nirviṣṭaṃ karmaṇopāttam | kṛṣyādinā vaiśyasya śuśrūṣādinā śūdrasya | tad adhikam anayoḥ || 10.42 ||
pranaṣṭādhigatād adhigantuś caturtham uktam | tad viśinaṣṭi |
atha pranaṣṭādhigatādhigantuś caturtham ity asyāpavādam āha |
nidhyadhigamo rājadhanam || 10.42 || — || 10.43 ||
nidheḥ pūrvanihitasyādhigamo lābhaḥ sa rājña eva, na tato ‘dhigantuś caturthaḥ | evaṃ ca nidher anyad dravyaṃ yat tasmād eva caturthāṃśa iti draṣṭavyam |
nanu ca nidher yad anyad dravyaṃ rājñaḥ kathaṃ tatprāptiḥ, adhigamasya sādhāraṇatvenoktatvāt yena pūrvam adhigamyate tenaiva tasya gṛhyamāṇatvād iti |
atrocyate – yasminn anubhūtacihnāni dṛśyante tad rājñe kathayet itarat svayaṃ gṛhṇīyād iti evaṃ cānubhūtacihnāni muṣitvā gṛhṇato doṣaḥ | yathāha lokākṣiḥ: “anubhūtacihnāni muṣitvā gṛhṇataḥ pūrvasāhasaṃ daṇḍaḥ taddravyadviguṇaṃ ca rājā haret” iti asati dhanagrahaṇe nidhyadhigamo rājña ity ukte arājña iti pratiṣedho ‘py āśaṅkyeta || 10.42 ||
nidhiś ced adhigatas tad rājadhanam eva bhavati | adhi-gantre ‘nugrahānurūpaṃ kiṃcid deyam iti || 10.43 ||
na brāhmaṇasyābhirūpasya || 10.43 || — 10.44 ||

abhirūpaḥ ṣaṭkarmanirataḥ | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “brāhmaṇaś ced adhigacchet ṣaṭsu karmasu vartamāno na rājā haret” [VaDh 3.14] iti, tasya karmasādhanatvāt || 10.43 ||
abhirūpaḥ ṣaṭkarmanirataḥ | tasya brāhmaṇasya cen nidhyadhigamo na tad rājadhanam | kiṃ tarhi | adhigantur brāhmaṇasyaiveti || 10.44 ||
abrāhmaṇo ‘py ākhyātā ṣaṣṭhaṃ labhetety eke || 10.44 || — || 10.45 ||
abrāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyādiḥ | ākhyātā pūrvaṃ jñāpayitā | ṣaṣṭham aṃśaṃ labhetety eke manyante, cāritrakleśaphalaṃ hi tad iti | eka iti vacanān na tu gautamaḥ, aparimitasāratvān nidhīnām | ato yat kiṃcid asya deyam iti na tu ṣaṣṭha evāṃśa iti | apiśabdād brāhmaṇo ‘py anabhirūpa iti || 10.44 ||
abrāhmaṇo ‘pi nidhim adhigamya yady ācaṣṭa idam ittham āsāditam iti sa tasya nidheḥ ṣaṣṭaṃ labhetey eke smartāro manyante | brāhmane ‘nabhirūpe kalpyaḥ
|| 10.45 ||
The order of sutras in M and H is different from here until 10.51
rakṣyaṃ bāladhanam ā vyavahāraprāpaṇāt samāvṛtter vā || 10.45 ||
rakṣyaṃ bāladhanam ā vyavahāraprāpaṇāt
|| 10.48 ||
bāla aprāptaṣoḍaśavarṣaḥ | tadgrahaṇam anyeṣām api rakṣaṇāsamarthānām upalakṣaṇam | taddhanaṃ bandhubhyo rakṣet | anyebhyas tu rakṣaṇasya “rakṣa-ṇaṃ sarvabhūtānām” [GDh 10.7] ity anenaiva siddhastvāt | ā vyavahāraprāpaṇāt rakṣaṇasāmarthyopajananāt, ā samāvṛtter vā saty api sāmarthyalakṣaṇe | vikalpas tu adhyayanādyabhiyogāpekṣayā varṇanīyaḥ || 10.45 ||
bālo ‘prātpaṣoḍaśavarṣaḥ | tasya yadi hitaiṣiṇo rakṣakāś ca pitrādayo na santi santo vā mūrkhāś cādhārmikāś ca tadā taddhanaṃ rājñā rakṣyam | ā kutaḥ | vyavahārapāpaṇāt | yāvad asau vyavahāraprāptaḥ ṣoḍaśavarṣo bhavati || 10.48 ||
samāvṛtter vā || 10.49 ||
āṅ anuvartate | adhītavedasya gurukulān nivṛttiḥ samāvṛttiḥ | ā vā tasyā iti || 10.49 ||
corahṛtam apajitya yathāsthānaṃ gamayet || 10.46 || — || 10.46 ||
H reads: “caura-“
corādyapahṛtaṃ tata ācchidya svāmina eva pratyarpayet | na tu “jetā labheta sāṃgrāmikam” [GDh 10.19] ity anena kaṇṭakamardanavyājena vā kiṃcid upajīvet | ācchidyeti vaktavye apajityeti vacanaṃ yo ‘py anya ācchinatti asāv apy amuṣmai dāpanīya iti | svāmine dadyād iti vaktavye “yathāsthānaṃ gamayet” ity ārambhaḥ svāmyabhāve ‘pi tadbhṛtyebhyo yathārhato ‘rpayed iti | coragrahaṇaṃ balātkaraṇāder apy upalakṣaṇam || 10.46 ||
caurair hṛtaṃ dravyaṃ tān apajitya yathāsthānaṃ gamayet | svāmina eva dadyāt | jetus tu jayaphalaṃ kiṃcit || 10.46 ||
kośād vā dadyāt || 10.47 || — || 10.47 ||
anapajaye, durgadeśādiṣu gatatvāt, yāvanmātram apahṛtaṃ tat svadhanād dadyāt | kośād veti noktam, yathāsthānaṃ gamayet kośād vā gamayed iti mā bhūd āśaṅketi || 10.47 ||
yady anviṣyāpi corā na dṛṣṭās ta eva vā jitvā gatās tadā svakośād ādāya tāvad dhanaṃ svāmine dadyād yāvad apahṛtaṃ caurair iti || 10.47 ||
evaṃ rājño ‘dhikaṃ svatvamūlam uktam | sāṃprathaṃ vaiśyasyāha |
vaiśyasyādhikaṃ kṛṣivaṇikpāśupālyakusīdam || 10.48 || — || 10.50 ||
kṛṣyādi prasiddham | kṛṣiś ca vaṇik ceti dvandvaikavadbhāvaḥ | vaṇig iti vāṇijyaṃ karmocyate
|| 10.48 ||
kṛṣiḥ prasiddhā | vaṇig iti vāṇijyam | paśupālasya karma pāśupālyam | kusīdaṃ vṛddhyartho dhanaprayogaḥ | kṛṣyādibhir yal labdhaṃ tad adhikaṃ svaṃ vaiśyasya || 10.50 ||
śūdraś caturtho varṇaḥ || 10.49 ||
śūdraś caturtho varṇa ekajātiḥ || 10.51 ||
śūdra iti saṃjñā śrutito dravatīti | varṇa iti trayāṇāṃ brāhmaṇādīnāṃ varṇasaṃjñāpratipattyartham | ataś caturṇām eva varṇasaṃjñā, nāntarajānām | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “catvāro varṇā brāhmaṇakṣatriya-vaiśyaśūdrāḥ” [VaDh 2.1] iti || 10.49 ||
caturtho varṇa iti | varṇasāmānyatve saty api caturthagrahaṇaṃ pūrveṣāṃ trayāṇāṃ brāhmaṇādivarṇānāṃ pṛthagvarṇatvopapādanārtham | traivarṇikā api siddhatvād ekajatir upanayanaṃ pūrveṣāṃ dvitīyajanma tad asya nāstīti | upanayanapratiṣedhāt tatpūrsakam adhyayanam api na bhavati | tadviṣaye gṛhyakāra āha: “śūdrasyāpi niṣekapuṃsavanasīmantonnayanajātakarmanāma-karaṇānnaprāśanacaulāny amantrakāṇi yathākālam upadiṣṭāni” iti | vivāho ‘py amantrako yathācāraṃ bhavati || 10.51 ||
ekajātiḥ || 10.50 ||
nāsya dvitīyā jātir vidyate “upanayanam iti dvitiyaṃ janma” iti tasyāpi jātisaṃjñātvāt | evaṃ cāsyopanayanapratiṣedhaḥ | tataś ca tasyaiva pratiṣedhāt tasmāt prāktanānām amantrakāṇāṃ yathākālam anuṣṭhānaṃ draṣṭavyam | tathā ca gṛhyakāravacanam: “śūdrasyāpi niṣekapuṃsavana-sīmantonnayanajātakarmanāmakaraṇopaniṣkra-maṇānnaprāśanacaulāny amantrakāṇi yathākālam upadiṣṭāni” iti | uparitānāṃ tūpanayanamūlatvād eva pratiṣedhasiddhiḥ || 10.50 ||
tasyāpi satyam akrodhaḥ śaucam || 10.51 || — || 10.52 ||
tasya śūdrasya | apiśabdāt utkṛṣṭā avāntarajāś ca gṛhyante | dvijātīnāṃ brahmacāriprakaraṇe snātakadharme coktānām api varṇadharmaprāpaṇārthaḥ ihānuvādaḥ anupanītānām api praveśo yathā syād iti | satyaṃ yathābhūtārthavāditvam | akrodhaḥ parānabhidrohabuddhiḥ | śaucaṃ mṛdādibhiḥ | asteyendriyasaṃyamādīnām upasaṃgrahārtho visamāsaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
ahiṃsā satyam akrodhaḥ śaucam indriyasaṃyamaḥ |
etān sāmāsikaṃ dharmam || iti | [MDh 10.63]
tathā ca vasiṣṭho ‘pi: “sarveṣāṃ satyam akrodho dānam ahiṃsā prajananaṃ ca” [VaDh 4.4] iti || 10.51 ||
upanayanādhyayanarahitatve ‘pi yathāvṛttikatvaṃ mā bhūd iti tasyāpi śūdrasya satyādayo dharmā bhavanti | satyaṃ yathādṛṣṭārthavāditvam | akrodhaḥ parānabhidrohabuddhiḥ | śaucaṃ pūrvoktadravya-śaucaṃ manaḥ śaucam ityādi | vasiṣṭhas tu: “sarveṣāṃ satyam akrodho dānam ahiṃsā prajananaṃ ca” [VaDh 4.4] iti || 10.52 ||
ācamanārthe pāṇipādaprakṣālanam evaike || 10.52 || — || 10.53 ||
ācamanārthe snānārthe pāṇipādaprakṣālanamātram eka icchanti, amantrakaṃ snānaṃ kartavyam eveti gautamaḥ | vikalpaś ca sacchūdrāpekṣayā varṇanīyaḥ | tathāha uśanā: “sacchūdraḥ snāyāt, asacchūdraḥ pāṇipādau prakṣālayet” iti | nityasnānasyāyam | naimittikasya tu pakṣāntarābhāvāt snānam eva kartavyam |
atha vā ācamanam evocyate | tadarthaṃ pāṇipādaprakṣālanam evaika icchanti, na tu gautamaḥ | pāṇipādaprakṣālanaṃ kṛtvā sakṛt pānaṃ ca kartavyam iti tasyābhiprāyaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ: “strīśūdrau tu sakṛt sakṛt” [MDh 5.137] iti | vikalpas tu pūrvavad varṇanīyaḥ || 10.52 ||
pūrveṣāṃ varṇānāṃ yatrācamanam uktaṃ tasmin viṣaye śūdrasya pāṇipādaprakṣālanam eva bhavati nānya ācamanakalpa ity eke manyante | manus tu sakṛd ambupānam icchati: “strīśūdrau tu sakṛt sakṛt” [MDh 5.139] iti | nityasnānaviṣaye tu uśanā: “sacchūdraḥ snāyād asacchūdraḥ pāṇipādaṃ prakṣālayet” iti
|| 10.53 ||
śrāddhakarma || 10.53 || — || 10.54 ||
“atha śrāddham” ityādi [GDh 15.1] vakṣyati | karma-grahaṇaṃ samastaśrāddhopasaṃgrahārtham | yathāha kaṇvaḥ |
nityaṃ naimittikaṃ kāmyaṃ vṛddhiśrāddham athāparam |
pārvaṇaṃ ceti vijñeyaṃ śrāddhaṃ pañcavidhaṃ smṛtam || iti |sśrāddhagrahaṇenaiva labhyamānatvāt karmagrahaṇaṃ samastavidhyupasaṃgrahārtham ity eke | kecin mā bhūn mantrapratiṣedhāt tatsādhyavidhipratiṣedho ‘pīti || 10.53 ||
“amāvāsyāyām” [GDh 16.35] ity ārabhya yac chrāddhakarma vakṣyate tad api śūdrasya kartavyaṃ mantravarjam || 10.54 ||
bhṛtyabharaṇam || 10.54 || — || 10.55 ||

|| 10.54 ||

svadāravṛttiḥ || 10.55 || — || 10.56 ||
upanayanamūlatvād uparitanānāṃ sarveṣāṃ pratiṣedhe prāpte dāraparigrahamātrasya pratiprasavaḥ kriyate | samānajātīyaivāsya bhāryā na tu pratilometi svaśabdasyopādānam | tataś ca brāhmaṇādīnāṃ vijātīyaparigrahaḥ siddho bhavati | tathāha manuḥ |
śūdraiva bhāryā śūdrasya sā ca svā ca viśaḥ smṛte |
te ca svā caiva rājñaḥ syus tāś ca svā cāgryajanmanaḥ || iti | [MDh 3.13] svadāra iti siddhe vṛttiśabdasyopādānaṃ devarādiniyogapratiṣedhārtham, svadāra eva vṛttiḥ na paradāra iti | asamāso gurvarthādidānopasaṃgrahārtham | gārhasthyam evāśramo ‘sya netarāṇīti pṛthagyogakaraṇam || 10.55 ||
sveṣv eva dāreṣv asya vṛttiḥ | sajātīyeṣv api paradāreṣu veśyāsu ca prasañjan daṇḍya iti | apara āha – svadāra-vṛttir evāsya bhavati nāśramāntaraprāptir iti || 10.56 ||
paricaryā cottareṣām || 10.56 || — || 10.57 ||
paricaryā paricaraṇaṃ śuśrūṣā uttareṣāṃ trayāṇāṃ varṇānām | caśabdāt kṛṣyādi ca | sacchūdrasya śuśrūṣā, itarasya kṛṣyādir it kecit | gautamasya tāvad ayam abhiprāyaḥ – yaḥ sacchūdras tenobhayam apy avirodhena kartavyam, itareṇa kṛṣyādy eveti | itareṣām iti siddhe uttaragrahaṇaṃ yo ya uttaras tatra tatra phalabhūyastvajñāpanārtham | tathā ca āpastambaḥ: “pūrvasmin pūrvasmin varṇe niḥśreyasaṃ bhūyaḥ” [ĀpDh 1.1.8] iti || 10.56 ||
uttareṣāṃ trayāṇāṃ varṇānāṃ paricaryā śuśrūṣā ca
|| 10.57 ||
idānīṃ yasya śuśrūṣā sa kathaṃ vartata ity ata āha |
saiṣā vṛttyarthety āha |
tebhyo vṛttiṃ lipseta || 10.57 || — || 10.58 ||
yān paricaret tebhya eva jīvanaṃ labdhum icchet | evaṃ ca vṛttyarthina eva paricaryā nāvaśyaṃ sarvasyeti siddham || 10.57 ||
tebhyaḥ paricaritebhyo jīvanaṃ lipseta || 10.58 ||
tatra pūrvaṃ pūrvaṃ paricaret || 10.59 ||
tathā ca āpastambaḥ: “pūrvasmin pūrvasmin varṇe niḥśreyasaṃ bhūyaḥ” iti [ĀpDh 1.1.8] | tad evaṃ yathā yājanādhyāpanapratigraheṣu brāhmaṇasya pratigraho mukhyā vṛttis tathā śūdrasya paricaryā | tatrāpi pūrvasmin pūrvasmin varṇa iti || 10.59 ||
kiṃ punar ebhyaḥ prārthayet, tair evāsmai kiṃ deyam, ity āha |
jīrṇāny upānacchatravāsaḥkūrcādīni || 10.58 || — || 10.60 ||
jīrṇāny upabhuktāni | ādiśabdāc cānyāni cālpasārāṇi | yathāha manuḥ |
ucchiṣṭam annaṃ dātavyaṃ jīrṇāni vasanāni ca |
pulākāś caiva dhānyānāṃ jīrñṇāś caiva paricchadāḥ || iti | [MDh 10.125] chatragrahaṇaṃ śikhipatrakṛtānām apy upalakṣaṇam | anyat prasiddham || 10.58 ||
kūrcaṃ tṛṇādi | śeṣaṃ prasiddham | jīrṇāny upabhuktāny upānadādīni paricarate śūdrāya deyāni | ayaṃ tu śuśrūṣāvṛtteḥ śūdrasya niyamo na gṛhasthavṛtteḥ | tasya tu vṛttyanapakṣaṃ sāmānyākāreṇa viśeṣatvam || 10.60 ||
punaḥ prakṛtam anusarati |
ucchiṣṭāśanaṃ śilpavṛttiś ca || 10.59 ||
ucchiṣṭāśanam || 10.61 ||
pānīyasyāniyamaḥ, aśanaśabdasyopādānāt | ucchiṣṭaṃ bhuktaśiṣṭam | tad apy akṛtadārasyaiva, itarasya tu pākayajñādhikārād apravṛtteḥ | kuta etat | asamāsanirdeśāt, smṛtyantarāc ca | yathāha vyāghraḥ |
ucchiṣṭam annaṃ dātavyaṃ śūdrāyāgṛhamedhine |
gṛhasthāya tu dātavyam anucchiṣṭaṃ dine dine || iti |
śilpavṛttiḥ takṣāyaskārādikarmopajīvanam | caśabdāt kārukarmāṇi | visamāsaḥ āpatkalpapradarśanārthaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
aśaknuvaṃs tu śuśrūṣāṃ śūdraḥ kartuṃ dvijanmanām |
putradārātyayaṃ prāpto jīvet kārukakarmabhiḥ || iti | [MDh 8.250] śuśrūṣāṃ kartum aśaknuvan śilpavṛttyopajīvet | tayāpi jīvanāsaṃbhave kāruvṛttir iti draṣṭavyam | śilpakārukarmaṇoś bhedo lokād avagantavyaḥ | kārukarmāṇi nāpitarajakacitrakārādīni, pratilomakarmatvād anulomakarmabhyo ninditāni | śilpāni tu takṣāyaskārādīni anulomakarmatvāt kārukarmabhyo viśiṣṭānīti || 10.59 ||
bhojanapātre yad bhuktāvaśiṣṭaṃ tad asyāśanam | “nābrāhmaṇāyocchiṣṭaṃ prayacchet” [ĀpDh 1.31.22] ity etat tu dāsaviṣayam | gṛhasthaśūdraviṣayam anye | tathā ca vyāghraḥ |
ucchiṣṭam annaṃ dātavyaṃ śūdrāyāgṛhamedhine |
gṛhasthāya tu dātavyam anucchiṣṭaṃ dine dine || iti || 10.61 ||
śilpavṛttiś ca || 10.62 ||
śilpāni citrakarmādīni | tair apy ayaṃ varteta | atra mānavo viśeṣaḥ |
aśaknuvaṃs tu śuśrūṣāṃ śūdraḥ kartuṃ dvijanmanām |
putradārātyayaṃ prāpto jīvet kārukakarmabhiḥ || [MDh 10.99] iti || 10.62 ||
yaṃ cāryam āśrayīta bhartavyas tena kṣīṇo ‘pi || 10.60 || — || 10.63 ||
H reads: “āśrayed”
ya brāhmaṇādim āśrayet paricaret tenāsau poṣaṇīyaḥ | caśabdād anāryaṃ svajātīyam api karmāntareṇāryāṇām eva paricaraṇopadeśāt | yaṃ cāśrayeteti vaktavye āryagrahaṇaṃ samānajātīye ‘pi sādhūnāṃ viśeṣajñāpanārtham | teneti tasyāvaśyakartavyat-āsūcanārtham | kṣīṇo ‘pi vyādhijarādyupahato ‘pīty arthaḥ || 10.60 ||
paricaryayā vartamānaḥ śūdro yadi kṣīṇaḥ karma kartum asamartho bhavati tathāpi (tadāpi) yam asau pūrvam āśritaḥ karmāṇy akarot tenāsau bhartavyaḥ pūrvakṛtāpekṣayā || 10.63 ||
tena cottaraḥ || 10.61 || — ||10.64 ||
tena ca śūdreṇottaro yam āśrayeta | evaṃ ca vṛttikṣīṇasya śūdrāśrayaṇam apy astīti jñāpayati | āśrayaṇam apy ātmasaṃdarśanamātram | yathāha jātūkarṇiḥ |
yo nīcam āśrayed āryaḥ ātmānaṃ darśayet sadā |
ātmānaṃ dāsavan matvā caren nīco ‘pi taṃ prati ||
caśabdād anāśrito ‘py avasannaḥ | tathā ca jātūkarṇiḥ |
daridro brāhmaṇo dānto vedānāṃ caiva pāragaḥ |
śūdreṇāpi sadā hy eṣa bhartavyo ‘nāśrito ‘pi san || iti | tena cetara iti vaktavye uttargrahaṇaṃ brāhmaṇa eva niyamato bhartavyaḥ, itaro ‘niyamata iti jñāpanārtham | tathā ca jātūkarṇiḥ |
bibhṛyād brāhmaṇṃ nityaṃ sarvayatnena buddhimān |
anyaṃ cāpy ānṛśaṃsyārthaṃ śūdro ‘pi dravyavān sadā || iti || 10.61 ||
tena ca śūdreṇottaro vṛttikṣīṇo bhartavyaḥ śilpādibhiḥ pūrvakṛtāpekṣayaiva | atra jātūkarṇyaḥ |
yo nīcam āśrayed ārya ātmānaṃ darśayet sadā |
ātmānaṃ dāsavat kṛtvā caren nīco ‘pi taṃ prati ||
daridro brāhmaṇo dānto vedānāṃ caiva pāragaḥ |
śūdreṇāpi sadāpy eṣa bhartavyo ‘nāśrito ‘pi san ||
bibhṛyād brāhmaṇaṃ nityaṃ sarvayatnena buddhimān |
anyaṃ cāpy ānṛśaṃsyārthaṃ śūdro ‘pi dravyavān bhavet || iti || 10.64 ||
tadartho ‘sya nicayaḥ syāt || 10.62 || — || 10.65 ||
tadarthaḥ uttarapoṣaṇārthaḥ asya śūdrasya nicayaḥ saṃcayaḥ syād bhavati | yasmād ity adhyāhartavyam | evaṃ cāsya anyārtho dravyaparigrahaḥ pratiṣiddho bhavati | hetuvacanam api pūrvavad asāradravyeṇa bharaṇaṃ na bhavati, kiṃ tu svakuṭumbāvirodhena sarvasvadāneneti || 10.62 ||
asya śūdrasya nicayo ‘rthasaṃcayas tadarthaḥ syāt tasyottarasya poṣaṇārthaḥ syāt | pūrvasūtrasya hetur ayam || 10.65 ||
anujñāto ‘sya namaskāro mantraḥ || 10.63 || — || 16.66 ||
anujñātaḥ śrutau śiṣṭair vāsya sacchūdrasya na tv asataḥ | namaskāro nāma mantraḥ | so ‘yam |
devatābhyaḥ pitṛbhyaś ca mahāyogibhya eva ca |
namaḥ svadhāyai svāhāyai nityam eva bhavaty uta || iti | asya pitṛkārye viniyogaḥ | yathāha gṛhyakāraḥ: “brāhmaṇān upaveśya devatādikaṃ mantraṃ japet” iti | mantragrahaṇaṃ namaskāra evāsyānujñāta iti mā bhūd āśaṅketi || 10.63 ||
asya śūdrasya vaiśvadevadiṣu tattaddevatāpadaṃ caturthyantaṃ manasā dhyātvā namo nama ity evaṃrūpo mantro ‘nujñāto dharmajñaiḥ | apara āha |
devatābhyaḥ pitṛbhyaś ca mahāyogibhya eva ca |
namaḥ svadhāyai svāhāyai nityam eva namo namaḥ || ity ayaṃ mantro namaskāraśabdena vivakṣitaḥ | sa pitryeṣu karmasu bhavati | tac cāha gṛhyakāraḥ: “brāhmaṇān upaveśya devatādikaṃ mantraṃ japet”
|| 10.66 ||
pākayajñaiḥ svayaṃ yajetety eke || 10.64 || — || 10.67 ||
pākayajñāḥ pākasādhyāḥ aṣṭakādayaḥ taiḥ svayaṃ yajeta na paraṃ niyuñjyāt | evaṃ cānyasya paraniyoga-siddhiḥ | ekegrahaṇāt na tu gautamaḥ | so ‘yaṃ vikal-paḥ sacchūdrāsacchūdraviṣayo vyākhyeyaḥ || 10.64 ||
pakvaguṇakeṣv apakvaguṇakeṣu ca gārhyeṣu karmasu pākayajñaśabdaḥ prasiddhaḥ | yathāha āpastambaḥ: “laukikānāṃ pākayajñaśabdaḥ” iti || 10.67 ||
na kevalaṃ śūdra evaivaṃ kuryāt, kiṃ tarhi |
sarve cottarottaraṃ paricareyuḥ || 10.65 || — || 10.68 ||
sarve nikṛṣṭāḥ adhikaṃ varṇaṃ śuśrūṣeyuḥ |
nanu ca śūdrasyoktatvāt brāhmaṇasya cottarābhāvāt kṣatriyavaiśyārthaḥ ārambhaḥ | ato bahuvacanānupapattiḥ “paricareyuḥ sarve” iti |
ucyate – samānajātīyam apy adhikaguṇaṃ hīnaḥ paricared iti bahuvacanam | sarvaśabdād anulomāś cāpi, anyathā varṇādhikārān na bhavati iti | śūdrasyāpi svajātau guṇādhikaparicaraṇārthaś cakāraḥ || 10.65 ||
sarva eva vaiśyādayo ‘py uttaram uttaraṃ varṇaṃ pari-careyur na kevalaṃ śūdra eva | brāhmaṇasya tūttaro nāsti | madhye kṣatriyavaiśyau | tathāpi sarvaśabde bahuvacanam avāntaraprabhavāṇāṃ grahaṇārtham | apara āha – samāne ‘pi varṇe yo yo ‘pi guṇata uttaras taṃ tam avaro ‘varaḥ paricared ity evamartham
|| 10.68 ||
āryānāryayor vyatikṣepe karmaṇaḥ sāmyaṃ (sāmyam) || 10.66 || — || 10.69 ||
āryasya utkṛṣṭajāter anāryasya nikṛṣṭajāteḥ vyatikṣepe vyatikare parasparavyatyāse karmaṇaḥ ācārasya sāmyaṃ samatvaṃ tayor avagantavyam | āryo ‘pi brāhmaṇādiḥ anāryakarmā na śūdreṇa paricaraṇīyaḥ | anāryo ‘pi śūdro ya uktakārī sa āśrayaṇīyaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ śūdrān adhikṛtyāha |
dharmepsavas tu dharmajñāḥ satāṃ vṛttim anuṣṭhitāḥ |
mantravarjaṃ na duṣyanti praśaṃsāṃ prāpnuvanti ca || iti | [MDh 12.127] evaṃ cānena na jātisāmyam uktam, kiṃ tu jātibalam āśritya kutsitaṃ na kartavyaṃ | itaro ‘pi hīno ‘ham iti kṛtvānādaro na kartavya iti | vyatyaye sati vihitākaraṇe pratiṣiddhasevane ca yad vakṣyati prāyaścittaṃ tad draṣṭavyam || 10.66 ||
āryas traivarṇikaḥ | anāryaḥ śūdraḥ | tayoḥ karmaṇa ācārasya vyatikṣepe vyatyāse sati tayoḥ sāmyam eva bhavati na paricāryaparicārakabhāvaḥ | brāhmaṇādir apy anāryakarmā cen na śūdreṇa paricaraṇīyaḥ | śūdro ‘py āryakarmā ced anāryakarbhir itarair jātyapakarṣeṇa nāvamantavya iti | etena brāhmaṇakṣatriyau kṣatriyavaiśyau ca vyākhyātau | [abhyāso ‘dhyāyasamāptyarthaḥ] || 10.69 ||

rājā sarvasyeṣṭe brāhmaṇavarjam || 11.1 ||
rājā pūrvam uktaḥ sarvasya viṣayavāsino janasya, nigrahānugrahācārabhoganiyogeṣu prabhuḥ, nigrahe aparādhinām, anugrahe ‘nugrāhyāṇām, ācāre maryādā-vasthāne bhoge karagrahaṇe, niyoge puruṣāṇāṃ śiṣṭa-karaṇe | asyāpavādaḥ – brāhmaṇavarjaṃ brāhmaṇān muktvety arthaḥ | apacāre ‘pi sataḥ sāntvapūrvam etān svadharme sthāpayet, na tv itaravarṇavan nigraheṇeti draṣṭavyam | ārambhaprayojanaṃ sarvakriyāsu svatantratākhyāpanārtham | tathā ca nāradaḥ |
asvatantrāḥ prajāḥ sarvāḥ svatantraḥ pṛthivīpatiḥ | [NSm 1.29] iti || 11.1 ||

H reads: “vābhivinītaḥ”
taryī ṛgyajuḥsāmalakṣaṇā | ānvīkṣikī nyāyavidyā ātmavidyā ca | anayor abhivinītaḥ kuśalaḥ | visamāsād daṇḍanītyā ca | caśabdād vārtāyāṃ ca | tathā ca manuḥ |
traividyebhyas trayīṃ vidyāṃ daṇḍanītiṃ ca tadvidaḥ |
ānvīkṣikīṃ cātmavidyāṃ vārtārambhaṃ ca lokataḥ || [MDh 7.43] iti || 11.3 ||
ṛgyajuḥsāmātmakās trayo vedās trayī | atharvaṇaś ca vedas teṣv antarbhavati | tatrāpi hi mantrā ṛco yajūṃṣi vā bhedavyavahāras tu pravacananimittaḥ | śāntikapauṣṭikādiprameyabhedanibandho vā | ānvīkṣikī nyāvavidyā | tayor abhivinīto gurubhiḥ samyak śikṣitaḥ | manus tu |
traividebhyas trayīṃ vidyāṃ daṇḍanītiṃ ca śāśvatīm |

śucir alubdhaḥ | jitendriyaḥ mṛgayādivyasanarahitaḥ | guṇavatsahāyaḥ guṇavadbhir vidyācāritrādibhir guṇair yuktaiḥ sahāyair mantrisāmantadūtādibhir upetaḥ | upāyair deśakālāvasthādyapekṣayā prayuktaiḥ sāmādibhiś ca yuktaḥ | guṇavatsahāyopāyavān iti vaktavye saṃpannagrahaṇam anisrāvamantratvopa-saṃgrahārtham || 11.4 ||
śuciḥ antaḥ paradravyādiṣv aspṛhaḥ bahiḥ snānādiparaḥ | jitendriyaḥ striyo ‘kṣā mṛgayā pānam ityādivyasana-rahitaḥ | guṇāḥ śāntyādayaḥ | tadvadbhis trisāmādibhiḥ (?) sahāyaiḥ saṃpannaḥ samavetaḥ | sāmādibhiś copā-yaiḥ saṃpanno deśakālāvasthānurūpaṃ teṣāṃ prayoktā | sarvatra syād iti vakṣyamāṇam apekṣyate || 11.4 ||

tam evaṃguṇasaṃpannaṃ rājānam upari sthitaṃ nīcaiḥ samīpataḥ severan kṣatriyādayaḥ | abrāhmaṇā iti vaktavye “anye brāhmaṇebhyaḥ” iti guṇavad-brāhmaṇopasaṃgrahaṇārtham, na tu jātimātrasyeti | tad adhastād āsīrann iti siddhe upari-grahaṇam utsavādiṣv apy uparibhāvārtham || 11.7 ||
tam evaṃguṇaṃ rājānam upari siṃhāsanādāv uccair āsīnam adhastād bhūmāv evāsīran | kim aviśeṣeṇa | na | anye brāhmaṇebhyo brāhmaṇavyatiriktāḥ | adha upāsīrann ity eva siddha upary āsīnam iti svabhāvānuvādaḥ | sarvadāyam upary āsīno bhavati na tu rahasy api bhūmāv iti || 11.7. ||
te ‘py enaṃ manyeran || 11.8 ||
te ‘pi guṇavanto brāhmaṇā api enam adhastāt sthitaṃ manyeran pūjayeyur āśīrvādādibhiḥ lokapālāṃśa iti matvā | yathā ca manuḥ |
yasmād eṣa surendrāṇāṃ mātrābhyo nirmito nṛpaḥ | [MDh 7.5] ityādi | apiśabdān nīcaiḥ sthitā aguṇavanto brāhmaṇāś ca || 11.8 ||

varṇān āśramāṃś ca nyāyato ‘bhirakṣet || 11.9 ||
varṇā anupanītā brāhmaṇādayaḥ | uttarakālam āśramāḥ | nyāyataḥ lokaśāstrāviruddhena mārgeṇa yathaiṣāṃ śāstravihitakarmānuṣṭhānopadravo lokavyavasthā-bhaṅgaś ca na bhavati tathā rakṣed ity arthaḥ | abhigrahaṇam ābhimukhyārtham, tataś ca svayam eva vicārya rakṣet | yeṣāṃ varnatvam āśramatvaṃ ca nāsti pratilomānām, teṣām api rakṣaṇārtho visamāsaḥ | yeṣāṃ varṇatvaṃ nāsti āśramatvam evānulomānāṃ teṣām apy upasaṃgrahārtham āśramagrahaṇam | teṣāṃ tu “śūdras caturtho varṇaḥ” [GDh 10.49] ity atra varṇatvanirākaraṇāt | “pratilomāt tu dharmahīnaḥ” [GDh 4.25] ity atropanayanavidhānād āśramatve saty api varṇatvānapagamāc ca varṇagrahaṇenaiva labhyamānatvād iti | cakārād devatāpratimāś ca | tathā ca vyāghraḥ |
brāhmaṇān kṣatriyān vaiśyān śūdrān antarajāṃs tathā |
devatāpratimāś cāpi rakṣed bhūpaḥ prayatnataḥ || iti | “rakṣaṇaṃ sarvabhūtānām” [GDh 10.7] iti corādibhyo rakṣaṇasyoktatvāt | anyonyāsaṃkarārtham ihopadeśaḥ || 11.9 ||
varṇā brāhmaṇādayaḥ | āśramā brahmacaryādayaḥ | tān nyāyato yathāśāstraṃ ṣaṣṭhāṃśādibhāgasvīkāreṇābhirakṣed abhito rakṣet | yathā varṇāśramadharmānuṣṭhānena nirapāyās te bhaveyuḥ | atha vā nyāyata iti yathā deśādidharmāṇāṃ bhaṅgo na bhavati tathā rakṣed iti | anulomādayo ‘vāntaraprabhavā varṇā eṣv evāntarbhūtāḥ | rakṣaṇaṃ sarvabhūtānām iti corādibhyo rakṣaṇaṃ pūrvoktam | idaṃ tu vacanaṃ varṇāśramadharmeṣu saṃkaro mā bhūd iti || 11.9 ||
calataś caitān svadharme sthāpayet || 11.10 ||
etān pūrvoktān yady ālasyādinā ye calanti na kurvanti tān nigṛhya svadharmam eva kārayed ity arthaḥ | cakārāt pratiṣiddhasevane ca || 11.10 ||
te yady ālasyādinā svadharmāc caleyus tataś calata etān svadharma eva nigṛhya sthāpayed iti || 11.10 ||

dharmasya hy aṃśabhāg bhavatīti vijñāyate || 11.11 ||
H omits “vijñāyate”, but commentary seems to presuppose it
hiśabdo hetvarthaḥ | yasmād rakṣaṇataḥ dharmasyāṃśo bhavatīti arakṣaṇato ‘py adharmasyety arthasiddham | aṃśaḥ ṣaṣṭho bhāgaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
sarvato dharmaṣaḍbhāgo rājño bhavati rakṣaṇāt |
adharmād api ṣaḍbhāgo bhavaty asya hy arakṣataḥ || iti | [MDh 8.304] amūrtasya dharmasya vibhāgāsaṃbhavāt, yāvān varṇāśramāṇāṃ dharma utpadyate tataḥ ṣaṣṭhāṃśaparimāṇo rājño ‘pi vihitakarmānuṣṭhānād utpadyata iti draṣṭavyam | itikaraṇaś copasaṃhārapradarśanārthaḥ, yataḥ etad evam ato rakṣet sthāpayec ceti | vijñāyata iti śrutisūcanārtham | tad api sarvadharmāṇāṃ śrutimūlatvād rakṣaṇe gauravotpādanārtham || 11.11 ||
vijñāyate hi yasmād rakṣato dharmasyāṃśo bhavati | upalakṣaṇam etat | arakṣato ‘py adharmasyāṃśo bhavatīti jñeyam | atra manuḥ |
sarvato dharmaṣaḍbhāgo rājño bhavati rakṣaṇāt |
adharmasyāpi ṣaḍbhāgo bhavaty asya hy arakṣataḥ || [MDh 8.304] iti || 11.11 ||

brāhmaṇaṃ puraḥ agre sarvakāryeṣu kuryāt | caśabdāt senāpatiṃ ca kuryāt || 11.12 ||
“sa eṣa bahuśruto bhavati” [GDh 8.4] ity ārabhyoktā vidyā | viśiṣṭakule janmābhijanaḥ | vāk saṃskṛtā bhāratī | rūpaṃ manoharam | vayo madhyamaṃ nātibālo nātisthavira iti | śīlam antaḥkaraṇaśuddhir bāhyaṃ vānuṣṭhānam | etair vidyādibhiḥ saṃpannaṃ samṛddham | nyāyavṛttaṃ lokāviruddhācāram | tapasvinam abhogaparam | evaṃbhūtaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ purodadhīta purohitaṃ kurvīta || 11.12 ||
vidyābhijanavāgrūpavayaḥśīlasaṃpannam
|| 11.13 ||
vidyāśabdenātharvaveda ucyate, sa eva śāntyādikarma-samartha iti | abhijanaḥ cāritram, tadvān kārye pravar-tata iti | vāksaṃpannaḥ saṃskṛtavaktṛtvena sarvaṃ rañjatīti | rūpasaṃpannaḥ priyadarśanatvāc chraddhe-yavākyo bhavatīti | vayaḥsaṃpannaḥ nātiyuvā nātistha-viraḥ, asāv eva mahatkarmayogyo bhavatīti | śīlasaṃ-pannaḥ kṣamāyuktaḥ tasyāsādhyaṃ nāstīti || 11.13 ||
kiṃ cānyat |
nyāyavṛttaṃ tapasvinam || 11.14 ||
nyāyavṛttaḥ aviruddhalokācāropetaḥ, saḥ kṛcchre ‘pi rājānaṃ na parityajatīti | tapasvī ṛtāv eva gamanaśīla ity arthaḥ, so ‘bhyantarapraveśādiṣu yogyo bhavatīti | anye ‘pi sahāyā evaṃrūpā eva kartavyā iti yogavibhāgaḥ | visamāso dharmaśāstrakuśalopa-saṃgrahārthaḥ || 11.14 ||
sarveṣu karmasu puro dhīyata iti purohitaḥ, tad darśayati |
tatprasūtaḥ karmāṇi kurvīta || 11.15 || — || 11.13 ||
tacchabdaḥ sarvasahāyopasaṃgrahārthaḥ | prasūtaḥ abhyanujñātaḥ | tair vicāryety arthaḥ | karmāṇi dṛṣṭādṛṣṭārthāni kurvīta || 11.15 ||
tena purohitena prasūto ‘nujñāta idam itthaṃ kartavyam iti kṛtopadeśaḥ karmāṇi śrautasmārtādīni paurāṇikāni nityanaimittikāni śāntikapauṣṭikāny ābhicārikāṇi kurvīta | tatprasūta ity asya mūlatvena brāhmaṇam ākarṣati || 11.13 ||

brahmaprasūtaṃ hi kṣatram ṛdhyate na vyathata iti ca vijñāyate || 11.16 || — || 11.14 ||
hiśabdo yasmādarthe | yasmād brāhmaṇānujñayā kṣatrakarmapravartamānam ṛdhyate avaśyaṃ tatphalena yujyata ity arthaḥ | vidhinaiva kṛtatvāt pātradoṣādyabhāvān na vyathate anyathā na bhavatīty arthaḥ | iticaśabdau śrutidvayasūcanārthau | vijñāyta iti śrutisūcanārtham | vicāryaiva kartavyānīty evamarthaḥ śrutyupanyāsaḥ || 11.16 ||
brahma brāhmaṇas tena prasūtam anujñātaṃ hi kṣatraṃ kṣatriyam ṛdhyate [kṣatriyajātir ṛdhyate] samṛddhaṃ bhavatīti na vyathate na kutaścid bibheti | nirapāyaṃ syād ity arthaḥ | ity evaṃ prakāreṇa vijñāyate paramparayā dṛśyate || 11.14 ||
kiṃ ca |
yāni ca daivotpātacintakāḥ prabrūyus tāny ādriyeta || 11.17 || — || 11.15 ||
daivacintakāḥ jyotiḥśāstravidaḥ | utpātacintakāḥ śakunanimittajñāḥ | prabrūyuḥ niścayena brūyuḥ | caśabdād guravaś ca | tāny ādriyeta śraddadhīta, tadanurūpaṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ || 11.17 ||
daivacintakā jyotirvidaḥ | utpātacintakāḥ śakunajñāḥ | utpātānāṃ cāgre phalāni jānate | te yat prabrūyur idam anyagrahavaikṛtam idam adya duḥśākunam ayam adyotpāto ‘yam eṣāṃ parihāra iti ca tāny api sarvāṇy ādriyeta nopekṣeta || 11.15 ||

tadadhīnam api hy eke yogakṣemaṃ pratijānate || 11.18 || — || 11.16 ||
daivādhīnā puruṣakārādhīnā vā karmaphalaprāptiḥ | daivaśabdena pūrvajanmopāttaṃ karmocyate, puruṣakāraśabdenaitajjanmakṛtaṃ karma | tathā ca kaṇvaḥ |
pūrvajanmakṛtaṃ karma daivam ity abhidhīyate |
etajjanmakṛtaṃ yat tu puruṣakāram ihocyate || iti |
tatra puruṣakārahīnānām api paṅgvādīnāṃ nidhānādilābhadarśanāt, prakṛṣṭakarmakṛtām api phalādarśanād daivādhīnam eva phalaṃ na puruṣakārādhīnam iti manyamānāḥ tadadhīnam iti pratijānate | tacchabdena daivotpātakacintakavacanam, tenaivādṛṣṭaphalavijñānāt | tadāyattaṃ yogakṣemam | alabdhalābho yogaḥ, labdhasya rakṣaṇaṃ kṣemaḥ | yogas tāvat tadadhīnaḥ nimittena tallābhālābhavijñāne sati tadanurūpayatnasya karaṇākaraṇāt | kṣemaś cāpi nimittena upadravajñāne sati śāntyādinā tadapanetuṃ śakyatvāt | pratijānate pratijñāṃ kurvanti | anyābhiprāyasūcanārtho ‘piśabdaḥ |
anye py evaṃ bruvate – puruṣakāram antareṇānnādisaṃbandhasyānupapadyamānatvāt, puruṣakāra eva pravṛttānāṃ kadācid eva niṣphalatvadarśanāt, sarvadā vyabhicārābhāvāc ca puruṣakārādhīnam eveti manyamānāḥ daivotpātakavacanasya kadācid avybhicāradarśanān na tadadhīnam iti |
ekegrahaṇād ubhayasmād iti gautamaḥ | naikāntena daivasya prādhānyam, na ca puruṣakārasya | kiṃ tarhi | kadācid daivaṃ puruṣakāram apekṣate, tathā puruṣakāro ‘pi daivam iti | yathā puruṣakāro daivam apekṣate kṛṣyādau, na daivaṃ varṣādikam antareṇa kṛṣyādīnāṃ puruṣakāreṇaiva phalasiddhir iti | tathā annādilābhe daivaṃ puruṣakāram apekṣate, tam antareṇa tatsaṃbandhānupapatteḥ | gautamasyāyam abhiprāyaḥ – daive daivotpātacintakavacanam ādriyeta, puruṣakāre tv āptavacanam iti || 11.18 ||
na kevalaṃ rakṣaṇādivihitānuṣṭhānaṃ kiṃ tarhi tadadhīnam api daivotpātacintakair grahavaikṛtādau yat kartavyatayā proktaṃ tadadhīnam api yogakṣemaṃ bhavati | alabdhasya lābho yogaḥ | labdhasya rakṣaṇaṃ kṣemaḥ | tayoḥ samāhāradvandvaḥ | “āyogaprajā vindet,” “yogakṣemo naḥ kalpantām” [TaitS 7.5.18.1] ityādāv ekaviṃśatyādivat paraval liṅgatā | tad yathā – ekaś ca viṃśatiś vaikaviṃśatiḥ | taṃ yogakṣemaṃ pratijānata eka ācāryā iti || 11.16 ||
śāntipuṇyāhasvastyayanāyuṣyamaṅgalasaṃyuktāny ābhyudayikāni vidveṣaṇa-saṃvananābhicāradviṣadvyṛddhiyuktāni ca śālāgnau kuryāt || 11.19 || — || 11.17 ||
saṃyuktaśabdaḥ pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyate | śāntisaṃyuktaṃ daivotpātasūcitāpāyanivṛttyarthaṃ yat kriyate grahaśāntyādi | puṇyāhasaṃyuktaṃ dinadoṣanāśanārthaṃ vivāhādau yat kriyate, taddoṣanāśanāt tat karma śubham eva bhavatīti | svastyayanasaṃyuktaṃ yātrādau yat kriyate | tathāha vyāsaḥ |
yātrāyāṃ vāsanaṃ daivaṃ nārāsiṃhaṃ tathā yudhi |
bhaye dhyāyed vāsudevaṃ trayaṃ svastyayanaṃ smṛtam || iti | āyuṣyasaṃyuktaṃ janmanakṣatre āyuṣyavṛddhyarthaṃ yat kriyate | maṅgalasaṃyuktam arthādyarthaṃ yat kriyate śrīhomādi | etāny evābhyudayikāny abhyudayahetutvāt | svayam evābhyudayahetutvād evaṃ na vaktavyam iti cet, tatkartur doṣābhāvasūcanatvād adoṣaḥ | evaṃ coparitanānāṃ kartuḥ kiṃcit prāyaścittam astīti jñāpayati | tathā ca paiṭhīnasiḥ |
rājño ‘bhyudayikaṃ karma yas tu kuryād dvijottamaḥ |
parapīḍākaram uktvā na sa pāpena lipyate ||
parapīḍākaraṃ kṛtvā viproktād vātha niṣkrayāt |
rājanyānumatād vāpi śuddhir uktā manīṣibhiḥ || iti |
parapīḍākaratvād uparitanānāṃ pṛthag upanyāsaḥ | vidveṣaṇayuktaṃ yenāsya paraḥ prakṛtīnāṃ vidviṣṭo bhavati | saṃvananayuktaṃ yenāsya śatruḥ praṇipatati | ābhicārayuktaṃ yenāsya śatrur mriyate | vigatā ṛddhiḥ vyṛddhiḥ, vidviṣṭo vyṛddhiḥ dviṣadvyṛddhiḥ tayā yuktaṃ yenāsya paro vigataiśvaryo bhavati uccāṭanādi | upari ṛtviggrahaṇād etāni purohitaḥ kuryāt śālāgnau | caśabdāt laukike ca | tatra pūrvoktāni laukike, itarāṇi śālāgnāv iti draṣṭavyam || 11.19 ||
tatra āpastambo “rājñas tu viśeṣād vakṣyāmaḥ” [ĀpDh 2.25.1] iti prakṛtya, veśmāvasathaḥ sabheti tristhānāny abhisaṃdhāyāha “sarveṣv evājasrā agnayaḥ syur agnipūjā ca nityā yathā gṛhamedhe” iti [ĀpDh 2.25.6–7] | teṣām anyatamo ‘tra śālāgnir naupāsano nāpi tretāgnir gārhyaṣu śrauteṣu karmasu tayor niyatatvāt | śāntisaṃyuktaṃ daivotpātacintakasūcitāpacaya-nivṛttyarthaṃ yat kriyate grahaśāntimahāśantyādi | puṇyāhasaṃyuktaṃ dinadoṣanāśāya vivāhādau yat kriyate | svastyayanasaṃyuktaṃ yātrādau yat kriyate | āyuṣmatsaṃyuktaṃ janmanakṣatrādāv āyurvṛddhyarthaṃ yat kriyate | dūrvāhomādi maṅgalasaṃyuktaṃ gṛhapraveśādau yat kriyate vāstuhomādi | etāny ābhyudayikāny abhyudaya-nimittāni | vidveṣaṇasaṃyuktaṃ yenāsya śatruḥ prakṛtīnāṃ vidveṣyo bhavati | saṃvananasaṃyuktaṃ yenāsya śatravo vaśyāḥ praṇipatanti | abhicāra-saṃyuktaṃ yenāsya śatravo miryante | ṛddher abhāvo vyṛddhiḥ | dviṣatāṃ vyṛddhir dviṣadvyṛddhiḥ | yenāsya śatravo vigataiśvaryā bhavanti | uccāṭanādīny etāni ca śālāgnau kuryāt | kaḥ | rājā | tasya ca kartṛtvam idam eva | yat tat saṃvidhātṛtvam arthasaṃpradānādinā | tad yathā yo ‘py ekānte tūṣṇīm āsīno bhaktabījabalīvardaiḥ pratisaṃvidhatte  so ‘py ucyante pañcabhir halaiḥ kṛṣyatīti | apara āha – ābhyudayikāni purohitaḥ svayaṃ kuryād itarāṇi kārayed iti | yasmiṃś cāgnāv ābhyudyikāni na tatretarāṇi kurvanti kiṃ tv agnyantare pūrvoktānām anyasmin || 11.17 ||
yathoktam ṛtvijo ‘nyāni || 11.20 || — || 11.18 ||
putrakāmeṣṭyādīny anyāni yathoktam ṛtvijaḥ kuryuḥ | rājā dakṣiṇāṃ prati lubdho mā bhūd iti yathoktagrahaṇam || 11.20 ||
anyāni gārhyāṇi śrautāni ca tāni karmāṇi yathoktaṃ yasmin karmaṇi yāvanta ṛtvija uktās tāvantaḥ kuryuḥ | tad yathā – aupāsane cāgihotre cādhvaryur ekaḥ | darśapūrṇamāsayoś catvāraḥ | cāturmāsye pañca | paśubandhe ṣaṭ | jyotiṣṭomādau ṣoḍaśa | atra manuḥ |
purohitaṃ ca vṛṇuyād vṛṇuyād eva cartvijaḥ || iti | [MDh 7.78] tatra yeṣv eva ṛtvik tatra purohito ‘dhvaryur brahmety anye || 11.18 ||
tasya ca vyavahāro vedo dharmaśāstrāṇy aṅgāny upavedāḥ purāṇam || 11.21 || — || 11.19 ||
vyavahriyate aneneti vyavahāraḥ | tasya prajāpālanā-dhikṛtasya rājño vyavasthāsādhanāny etāni | vedādīni prasiddhāni | vede “gartā rug iva sanaye dhanānām” [RV 1.124.7] ityādivyavahāradarśanāt, dharmaśāstre varṇāśramādipratipādanāt, aṅge śabdādivyavasthāprati-pādanāt, upavede astrādivyavahārapratipādanāt, purāṇe jagatsthitivijñānāt | visamāsaḥ pūrvasya pūrvasya balīyastvajñāpanārthaḥ | tataś ca pūrvottaravirodhe pūroktam evācaret | vyavahāratattvadarśane vedādīny aṅgāni bhavantīti vyavahāraśabdenocyante | etāny ālocya vyavahārān sthāpayed ity arthaḥ || 11.21 ||
vyavaharanty aneneti vyavahāraḥ | tasya rājñaḥ prajāpālane ‘dhikṛtasya vedādīni vyavahārasādhanāni | yathā vedādiṣv abhihitaṃ tathā vyavahared iti | vyavahāro lokamaryādāsthāpanam || 11.19 ||
deśajātikuladharmāś cāmnāyair aviruddhāḥ pramāṇam || 11.22 || — || 11.20 ||
deśadharmaḥ kautukamaṅgalādiḥ | jātidharmaḥ ābhīrā-dīnāṃ dantarāgābhāvādiḥ | kuladharmo “dakṣiṇataś cūḍā vasiṣṭhānām” ityādi | āmnāyair vedadharmaśāstrā-dibhiḥ pūrvoktaiḥ bahuvacanaprayogāt, aviruddhā abādhitāḥ pramāṇam | vakṣyamāṇānāṃ karṣakādīnām apy āmnāyāviruddhatvajñāpanārthaś cakāraḥ | deśa-dharmādīn api samyak jñātvā āmnāyāvirodhena gṛhṇīyāt | virodhe tyaktvā ity evamartham upadeśaḥ
|| 11.22 ||
deśadharmeṣu jātidharmeṣu ca pratiniyatam anuṣṭhīyamāneṣu yady api vedādi mūlabhūtaṃ nopalabhyate tathāpi yadi vedādibhir virodho na bhavati tathaiva te paripālanīyā na tu mūlāniyogena vihantavyā iti | tatra deśadharmāḥ – meṣasthe savitari cauleṣu kumāryo nānāvarṇai rajobhir bhūmāv ādityaṃ saparivāram ālikhya sāyaṃ prātaḥ pūjayanti | mārgaśīrṣyāṃ cālaṃkṛtā grāme paryaṭya yal labdhaṃ tad devāya nivedayante | karkaṭasthe savitari pūrvayoḥ phalgunyor bhagavatīm umām ārādhya yathāvibhavam arudbhyo ‘ṅkuritaṃ mudgalavaṇaṃ ca prayacchanti | mīnasthe savitary uttarayoḥ phalgunyor gṛhamedhinaḥ śriyaṃ devīṃ pūjayanti | jātidharmāḥ śūdrā vivāhe madhe sthūṇāṃ nikhāya sahasravartīr ekasyāṃ sthālyāṃ nidhāya prativarti dīpān āropya vadhūṃ haste gṛhītvā pradakṣiṇayanti | anyad apy evaṃjātīyakaṃ draṣṭavyam | kuladharmāḥ – kecin madhyaśikhāḥ | kecit pṛṣṭaśikhāḥ | pravacanādayas tu kālabhedenobhayataḥśikhāḥ | saṃbandhaś caitais taiḥ svavargair iti | ye tv āmnāyaviruddhā mātulasutāpariṇayanam, anadhītya vedān anyatra śrama ityādayo deśadharmā neha pramāṇam || 11.20 ||
karṣakavaṇikpaśupālakusīdikāravaḥ sve sve varge || 11.23 || — || 11.21 ||
pramāṇam iti vartate | rājani praṣṭum aśakye svavarga eva praṣṭavya ity evamartha upadeśaḥ | karṣakāḥ kṛṣijīvinaḥ | vaṇijaḥ prasiddhāḥ | paśupālā gopālāḥ | kusīdino vārdhuṣikāḥ | kāravo ‘yaskārādayaḥ | svavarga iti vaktavye vīpsāvacanaṃ na kevalaṃ karṣakādaya eva svavarge pramāṇam, kiṃ tu brāhmaṇādayo ‘pīti | tatrāpi smṛtyantarasāmarthyāt tryavarā eva praṣṭavyāḥ, “svavarge tryavarā dharmān prabrūyuḥ” iti prajāpativacanāt | “āmnāyair aviruddhā pramāṇam” [GDh 11.22] ity uktatvāt yadi te tadviruddhaṃ brūyus tadā sarvair brāhmaṇasakāśaṃ gantavyam, teṣām eva tadavirodhena vaktuṃ sāmartham iti | “tasya vyavahāraḥ” [GDh 11.21] ity uktatvāt | tatrāpy aparitoṣe rājasakāśaṃ gantavyam iti draṣṭavyam | tatra tenāpy āmnyāyāvirodhena vaktavyatvāt ekasyaiva tadavirodhajñāpanasāmarthyābhāvāt, tajjñaiḥ brāhmaṇaiḥ saha brūyād ity arthasiddham | tathā ca manuḥ |
vyavahāraṃ didṛkṣus tu brāhmanaiḥ saha pārthivaḥ | [MDh 8.1] ityādi || 11.23 ||
karṣakāḥ kṛṣijīvinaḥ | vaṇijaḥ krayavikrayavyavahāraparāḥ | paśupālā gopālāḥ | kusīdino vārdhuṣikāḥ | kāravas takṣarajakādayaḥ | ete sve sve varge svavargasaṃvede pramāṇam || 11.21 ||
idānīṃ yadi karṣakādayo rājānam eva gaccheyus tadā kathaṃ rājā paricchinattīty āha |
tataś ca karṣakādiṣu dharmavipratipattau satyām |
tebhyo yathādhikāram arthān pratyavahṛtya dharmavyavasthā || 11.24 || — || 11.22 ||
tebhyaḥ karṣakādibhyaḥ, yathādhikāraṃ yo yatrādhikṛtaḥ prativargaṃ sthitaḥ ity arthaḥ | arthān ācārān pratyavahṛtya svayam eva samyak pṛthak pṛthag vicāryety arthaḥ | kuta etat? avahṛtyeti vaktavye pratyavahṛtyety ārambhāt | tato dharmavyavasthā kartavyeti śeṣaḥ | tattvaparicchedaḥ kartavyaḥ ity abhiprāyaḥ | brūyād iti vaktavye vyavasthāgrahaṇaṃ rājñā paricchinnasya punar nivṛttir mā bhūd iti | evaṃ cāparitoṣe ‘nyatra siddhasyāpi punaḥ punaḥ karaṇam astīti jñāpayati | dharmabuddhyaiva vyavasthāpayet nārthāśayeti dharmagrahaṇam || 11.24 ||
tebhyas tattadvargebhyo yathādhikāraṃ ye yatra varge vyavasthāpakatvenādhikṛtās tebhyo ‘rthān ācāraprakārān pratyavahṛtya śrutvāvadhārya dharmavyavasthā kāryā | ittham asmākaṃ nikāma ācāra iti tair ukte tathaiva vyavasthāpyam iti || 11.22 ||
atha te pakṣapātena mithyā brūyus tadā kathaṃ tattvaṃ jñātavyam |
nyāyādhigame tarko ‘bhyupāyaḥ || 11.25 || — || 11.23 ||
nyāyayuktasyādhigame avadhāraṇe tarkaḥ anumānam abhyupāyaḥ prathamopāya ity arthaḥ | tathāha nāradaḥ |
yathā nayaty asṛkpātair mṛgasya mṛgayuḥ padam |
tathānumānena nayed dharmasya nṛpatiḥ padam || [= MDh 8.44] iti || 11.25 ||
nyāyayuktasyārthasyādhigame ‘vadhāraṇe tarko ‘numānam abhyupāyaḥ | abhir dhātvarthānuvādī | tatra manuḥ |
ākārair iṅgitair gatyā ceṣṭayā harṣitena ca |
netravaktravikāraiś ca gṛhyate ‘ntargataṃ manaḥ || [MDh 8.26] iti || 11.23 ||
tataś ca |
tenābhyūhya yathāsthānaṃ gamayet || 11.26 || — || 11.24 ||
tenānumānenābhyūhya | yathāha manuḥ |
ākārair iṅgitair gatyā ceṣṭayā bhāṣitena ca |
netravaktravikāraiś ca gṛhyate ‘ntargataṃ manaḥ || iti | [MDh 8.26] yathāsthānaṃ yathātattvaṃ gamayet prāpayet || 11.26 ||
tena tarkeṇābhyūhyaivam ayam artho bhavitum arhatīti niścitya yathāsthānaṃ yatra pakṣe ‘rthas tatra gamayet || 11.24 ||
athātmana ekākinas tarkeṇāpi duradhigamatve sati |
vipratipattau traividyavṛddhebhyaḥ pratyavahṛtya niṣṭhāṃ
gamayet || 11.27 || — || 11.25 ||
vipratipattiḥ ekasyaiva tarkeṇāvadhāraṇāsāmarthyam, atisaukṣmyāt kāryasya ubhayathā liṅgadarśanāc ca, tasyām, traividyavṛddhāḥ sakalavedatrayapāragāḥ, tebhyaḥ pratyavahṛtya avagamya asyedaṃ liṅgaṃ asya paricchedakam, pūrvam asyedaṃ liṅgam idānīṃ liṅgāntaram idaṃ kiṃ paricchinattīty evamādi vicārya tattvaṃ jñātvety arthaḥ | āmnāyādyavirodhaparijñāpanārthaṃ ye sahāyā uktās ta evānūdyante traividyalakṣaṇaprāpaṇārtham | yathāha manuḥ |
mantrajñair mantribhiś caiva vinītaḥ praviśet sabhām || iti | [MDh 8.1] tatraiva vaktavyam iti cen, naiṣa doṣaḥ, āmnāyāviruddhavicāraṇāyām eva na kevalaṃ tair vicāraḥ kriyate kiṃ tu liṅgasaṃdehe ‘pi cety evamartha ihopadeśa iti | asati liṅgasaṃdehe virodhe cāsati rājña eva paricchedakatvaṃ draṣṭavyaṃ sabhāyās tadubhayārthatvād iti | liṅgasaṃdehe traividyavṛddhair eva prāpayet na svayam, yathāsthānaṃ gamayed ity adhikārād eva siddhe niṣṭhāṃ gamayed iti punar ārambhāt || 11.27 ||
vipratipattau satyāṃ duradhigamatve sati traividyavṛddhān samānāyya taiḥ saha vicāryārtha-tattvaṃ tebhyaḥ pratyavahṛtya niṣṭhāṃ gamayet | yatra pakṣe ‘rtho niṣṭhitas taṃ gamayet || 11.25 ||
kim evaṃ kurvato bhavatīty āha |
kim evaṃ kurvato bhavati |
tathā hāsya niḥśreyasaṃ bhavatīti || 11.28 ||
tathā hy asya niḥśreyasaṃ bhavati || 11.26 ||
tasya nirṇayaṃ kurvataḥ niḥśreyasam ubhayalokavija-yitvam amutra dharmaprāptyā, ihāpi janānurāgād iti | itiśabdo hetvarthaḥ, yasmād evam iti || 11.28 ||
evam asya nirṇayaṃ kurvato niśreyasam ubhayor lokayor bhavati | iha janānurāgenāmutra dharmaprāptyā ceti hetoḥ || 11.26 ||
evaṃ rājño bhavati, traividyavṛddhānāṃ tu sahāyarūpeṇa sthitānāṃ kiṃ bhavatīti tadanurūpāṃ śrutir udāharati |
na kevalaṃ rājña eva siddhiḥ | kiṃ tarhi | saha nirṇetṝṇāṃ brāhmaṇānām apīti darśayituṃ śrutim udāharati |
brahma kṣatreṇa pṛktaṃ devapitṛmanuṣyān dhārayatīti vijñāyate || 11.29 || — || 11.27 ||
H reads “saṃpṛktaṃ”
brahma traividyalakṣaṇaṃ kṣatreṇa rājñā pṛktaṃ sahitam, rājñā saha dharmavivecanaṃ kurvad ity arthaḥ | nirupadravakarmānuṣṭhānadvāreṇa “itaḥ pradānaṃ devā upajīvanti” ity evaṃ devapitṝn dhāra-yati āhutyādidvāreṇa, annaniṣpattyā manuṣyān | ato rā-jasu praśnavivecanaṃ dharmāyeti vaktavyam || 11.29 ||
brahma traividyalakṣaṇaṃ kṣatreṇa saṃyuktaṃ rājñā saha dharmaṃ viviñcad devapitṛmanuṣyān dhārayantīti śrutisiddham | evaṃ nirṇaye kṛte yathokaṃ karmānutiṣṭhanti manuṣyāḥ | tac ca dharmyaṃ karma devā upajīvanti pitaraś ca na kṣīyanta iti nyāyena sarveṣāṃ dhāraṇaṃ bhavatīti || 11.27 ||
atha tv evam api vyavasthāṃ kurvataḥ te dauḥśīlyān nāvatiṣṭhante, tataḥ |
atha dauḥśīlyād vyavasthāṃ nānumanyate tataḥ |
daṇḍo damanād ity āhus tenādāntān damayet || 11.30 || — || 11.28 ||
damayaty adāntān damyante vāsminn iti daṇḍaḥ | yataś ca damanayogād daṇḍa ity evaṃ manvādaya āhuḥ | yathāha manuḥ |
devadānavagandharvā rakṣāṃsi patagoragāḥ |
te ‘pi bhogāya kalpante daṇḍenaiva nipīḍitāḥ || iti | [MDh 7.23] ato yo yathā damyate vācā dhanādinā vā, yathāha manuḥ |
dhigdaṇḍo prathamaṃ kuryād vāgdaṇḍaṃ tadanantaram |
tṛtīyaṃ dhanadaṇḍaṃ tu vadhadaṇḍo tv ataḥ param || iti | [MDh 8.129] evaṃ kuryād dauḥśīlyādinā vikarmasthān || 11.30 ||
damanayogād daṇḍaśabdasya daṇḍatvam ity āhur dharmajñāḥ | tenādāntān avaśyān damayed vaśaṃ nayet | daṇḍenādāntān damayed ity evaṃ siddhe daṇḍaḥ –
dhigdaṇḍaṃ prathamaṃ kuryād vāgdaṇḍaṃ tadanantaram |
tṛtīyaṃ dhanadaṇḍaṃ tu vadhadaṇḍaṃ tataḥ param || [MDh 8.129]
devadānavagandharvā rakṣāṃsi patagoragāḥ |
te ‘pi bhogāya kalpyante daṇḍenaiva nipīḍitāḥ || [MDh 7.23] iti || 11.28 ||
evaṃ kṛtāḥ prajāḥ kathaṃ pravartanta ity āha |
athaivaṃ śāstravaśyatayā rājñā ca svadharme sthāpyamānānāṃ varṇānām āśramāṇāṃ ca kathaṃ siddhir ity ata āha |
varṇā āśramāś ca svakarmaniṣṭhāḥ pretya karmaphalam anubhūya tataḥ śeṣeṇa viśiṣṭadeśajātikularūpāyuḥśrutavṛttavittasukhamedhaso janma
pratipadyante || 11.31 || — || 11.29 ||
H reads “varṇāśramāḥ sva-”
varṇā brāhmaṇādayaḥ anupanītāḥ, āśramāḥ brahmacāryādayaḥ, svakarmaniṣṭhāḥ svakarmānuṣṭhānaparāḥ, pretya paraloke, karmaphalam anubhūya upabhujya tatphalopabhogasyāvaśeṣeṇeha viśiṣṭadeśādisaṃbandhaṃ janma pratipadyante | viśiṣṭaśabdaḥ pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyate – viśiṣṭadeśa āryāvartādiḥ, viśiṣṭajātir brāhmaṇādiḥ, viśiṣṭaṃ kulam adhyayanādisaṃpannam, viśiṣṭaṃ rūpaṃ priyadarśanatvam, viśiṣṭam āyuḥ samagraśatavarṣādi, viśiṣṭaṃ śrutaṃ vedārthavijñānasahitam, viśiṣṭaṃ vṛttaṃ viśiṣṭaṃ cāritram, viśiṣṭaṃ vittaṃ gavāśvādilakṣaṇam, viśiṣṭaṃ sukham abhilaṣitapadārthasyāvighnenānubhavasiddhiḥ, medhaḥśabdenārogyam ucyate, viśiṣṭārogyam abhilaṣitabhakṣaṇe ‘py arogotpattiḥ | evaṃ ca bruvatā nityakarmaṇāṃ ca kartā tatphalasya copabhoktā śarīravyatirikta ātmā pratipādito bhavati | tathā ca śrutiḥ: “sa eṣa mahān aja ātmā yo ‘yaṃ vijñānamayaḥ kartā bhoktā sa u kṣetrajño ‘mṛtamayaḥ prāṇe pratiṣṭhitaḥ” iti | [cf. BṛĀrUp 4.4.22] | tathā ca manuḥ |
yo hy ātmanaḥ kārayitā taṃ kṣetrajñaṃ pracakṣate |
yaḥ karoti tu karmāṇi sa bhūtātmocyate budhaiḥ || [MDh 12.12] ityādi | tasya ca nityasya sataḥ karmakṣayāt tadārabdhasya śarīrasya patane tatkṛtaṃ karmaphalaṃ svargādi tatkarmaṇaivārabdhena tatphalopabhogayogyena śarīreṇāhaṃkārendriyaśakti-manaḥprāṇasahitenānubhūyauṣadhādidvāreṇa annamayo bhūtvā retorūpeṇa pariṇamya tatsthena vāyunā pūrvaṃ mātur udaraṃ praviṣṭenākṛṣṭaḥ san tacchoṇitasahitaḥ pūrvānubhuktaśiṣṭakarmānugṛhītasya śarīrasyopādānaṃ janmety ācakṣate |
evaṃ vyavasthite sūtrārthe codyate āśramagrahaṇam anarthakaṃ varṇānām evāśramavidhānāt āśramatve ‘pi varṇatvānapagamāc ca | svagrahaṇaṃ cānarthakam, anuṣṭhānād eva svatvasiddheḥ | karmagrahaṇam apy anarthakam, karmaphalam anubhūyety atra karmagrahaṇenaivārtha-siddhatvāt | niṣṭhāgrahaṇam apy anarthakam, buddhipūrvatvena vinā tatkaraṇāsaṃbhavāt | pretyety api na kartavyam, janma pratipadyanta ity anenaivārthasiddhatvāt | phalam anubhūyeti vaktavye karmagrahaṇam atiricyate, phalasya karmasādhyatvāt | tata ity etad apy anarthakam, adhikārād eva karmaṇaḥ parāmarśasiddheḥ | śeśānupapattiś ca, kṛtsnasya karmaṇaḥ phalopabhogenopakṣīṇatvāt | na hi phaladānābhimukhībhūtasya karmaṇa ekadeśenāvasthānam upapadyate | na hi kṣīrādyekadeśena dadhyādikāryārambhaṇam iti | na ca ghṛtaghaṭādivad vāsanāśeṣaḥ śeṣaśabdavācyaḥ, amūrtatvāt karmaṇo vāsanāśeṣānupapatteḥ | na cāmutrānuṣṭhitaṃ karmeha śeṣaśabdenocyate, tatrādhikārābhāvāt, upayuktetarārthatvāsaṃbhavāc ca | na hi phalopabhogavicchedasyaiva śeṣatvam adhyavasituṃ yuktam, śrutahānyaśrutakalpanā-prasaṅgāt, karmaṇo hi phalaṃ śrutaṃ na phalavicchedasya | na ca akṣīṇe karmaṇy upabhogavicchedaḥ saṃbhavati, tasya karmāśritatvāt | yathā pradīpas tailāśritaḥ tailakṣayād eva kṣīyate | api ca tasyopasaṃhāraḥ śalabhādayo vidyante na tu karmaphalasya | tasmān na phalavicchedasya śeṣaśabdavācyatā | na ca karmaphalasyaiva vibhāgaḥ kartuṃ śaknoti – anenāmutrasukham anubhokṣye anena viśiṣṭadeśādisaṃbandhaṃ lapsye iti, niṣpannatvāt | aniṣpannasya hi vibhāgaḥ śrūyate – idaṃ vrīhīṇāṃ kṣetram idaṃ yavānām
