iti, na tu niṣpannā vrīhayo yavā kriyanta iti | yadi ca svakarmaniṣṭhā apy amutra phalopabhogaśeṣeṇeha janma pratipadyante evaṃ mokṣābhāvaprasaṅge śruter virodo bhavati – “brahmavid āpnoti param” [TaitUp 2.1], “tam eva viditvā atimṛtyum eti” [ŚveUp 3.8] ityādikāyāḥ | yadi karmaśeṣeṇa viśiṣṭadeśādiprāptilakṣaṇaṃ janma bhavati, tato jagadvaicitryānupapattiprasaṅgaḥ, dṛśyate hi tad iti | prāptasya ca karmaṇaḥ phalābhyupagame puruṣakāro ‘pi niṣpala eveti | tasmāc cheṣaśabdavācyābhāvāt sūtreṇa samastenāpi prakrāntasyārthasyānupapadyamānatvāt asamañjasam idam sūtram iti ||
tatra samādhiḥ – ubhayagrahaṇaṃ tāvat kartavyam, varṇānām āśramāṇāṃ ca dharmabhedāt | prāg upanayanād varṇāḥ ūrdhvam āśramā iti | asti cānupanītānām api kaiścid varṇadharmair adhikāra iti | asamāso dvandvaparigrahārtham, mā bhūt samānādhikaraṇāśaṅketi, dandvapakṣe tūbhayapadapradhānatvād ubhayor upādānaṃ siddhaṃ bhavati iti | sṛṣṭyantrajopasaṃgrahārthaś caśabdaḥ, teṣām api svakarmānuṣṭhānād utkarṣo labhyata iti | tathāha manuḥ |
brāhmaṇārthe gavārthe vā dehatyāgo ‘nupaskṛtaḥ |
strībālābhyupapattau ca bāhyānāṃ siddhikāraṇam || iti | [MDh 10.62] svagrahaṇam api prāthamikakalpopasaṃgrahārtham | āpadvihitasya prāyaścittavidhānād evaitat siddham iti cen, na, varṇāntarakarmānuṣṭhāna eva prāyaścittavidhānam, na svakarmaṇa eva viguṇasyeti | tathā coktaṃ nāradavākyam |
āpadaṃ brāhmaṇas tīrtvā kṣatravṛttyā mṛte jane |
utsṛjet kṣatravṛttiṃ tāṃ kṛtvā pāvanam ātmanaḥ || [NSm 1.55] ityādi | tasmād viguṇasya karmaṇaḥ phalapratiṣedhārthaṃ svagrahaṇam | evaṃ ca viguṇaṃ yat karma na tat phalasādhanam, prāyaścittavidhānā-bhāvāt nāpi doṣasādhanaṃ ceti draṣṭavyam | niṣṭhāgrahaṇaṃ sātatyārtham, yo hy ā śarīranipātanāt karma karoti sa evānena phalena saṃbadhyata iti | pretyagrahaṇam avaśyabhāvi phalam iti jñāpanārtham, yathā dṛṣṭaphalair iha siddhis tathā avaśyaṃ tatrāpy adṛṣṭaphalair iti | na tu kṛtaṃ karma niṣphalaṃ bhavatīti | karmagrahaṇaṃ tu kevalaṃ karma kṛta evānena phalena saṃbandhaḥ, na jñānakarmasamuccayakāriṇa iti | asau mukta eva bhavatīti | anenaiva mokṣābhāvaprasaṅgacodyam api parihṛtaṃ draṣṭavyam | tata ity etad api karmanānātvapratipādanārtham | tasya tasya karmaṇaḥ śeṣenātirekeṇa | ataś ca karmaṇāṃ tāratamyāt phalavaicitrye sati jagadvaicitryam upapadyata iti | tāratamyaṃ ca karmaṇāṃ deśakālaśaucadravya-śraddhāpātrābhyāsaviśeṣād iti śeṣaśabdaś ca karmasamudāyāpekṣayā draṣṭavyaḥ | kathm? yat karma svargaphalaṃ tatraiva phalaṃ dadāti, yat tv aihikaphalaṃ tena viśiṣṭadeśādisaṃbandhaṃ janma bhavati, sa ca tasya samudāyasya śeṣa iti | pradhānakarmaṇo ‘gnihotrāder aṅgabhūtaṃ yat karma tac cheṣaśabdenocyate | yathā – “godohanena paśukāmasya praṇayet” [ĀpŚr 1.16.2], “khādiraṃ vīryakāmasya yūpaṃ kuryāt” [ṢaḍviṃBr 4.4] ityādi | tatra pradhānakarmaphalam amutropabhujyate, aṅgaphalaṃ iheti | vṛkṣavad vaitad draṣṭavyam | vṛkṣo hi bahubhiḥ mūlair jāyate | sa yadā chinnapradhāna-bhūyiṣṭhamūlo bhavati tadālpaniḥsāramūlatvād bhāram asahamānaḥ patati, evaṃ karmaṇy api pradhānarūpe upabhogād upakṣīṇe śiṣṭarūpaṃ kartāraṃ tatra sthāpayitum aśaknuvad viśiṣṭadeśādisaṃbaddhaṃ phalaṃ dadātīti | ghṛtaghaṭavad vā draṣṭavyam | yathā – ghṛtaghaṭe upayukte ‘pi snehavāsanāśeṣād agninā saṃyukto jvalati, evaṃ karmakṛd api vāsanāśeṣād eva viśiṣṭadeśādy anubhaviṣyatīti | sāvayavatvāc ca karmaṇo vāsanopapadyata eveti, sāvayavatvam api kāryatvād iti |
yat tūktaṃ puruṣakāro ‘narthaka iti, tatrocyate – puruṣakārayuktasyaiva phalasaṃbandhaḥ, tadvihīnasyānnādisaṃbandho ‘pi nāstīti | tasmāt phaladānakāraṇaṃ pūrvaśiṣṭaṃ saṃbandhakāraṇaṃ puruṣakāra iti nānarthakaḥ puruṣakāra iti sarvam idaṃ sūtram upapadyata eveti || 11.31 ||
varṇā brāhmaṇādayaḥ | āśramā brahmacaryādayaḥ | te svadharmaniṣṭhā varṇaprayuktān āśramaprayuktān ubhayaprayuktāṃś ca dharmān anuṣthitavantaḥ pretya maraṇena lokāntaraṃ gatvā tasya tasya karmaṇaḥ phalaṃ svargādikam anubhūya tatas tadanantaraṃ śeṣeṇa bhuktāvaśiṣṭena karmaṇā viśiṣṭadeśādikān bhuktvā janma pratipadyante | tatra viśiṣṭaśabdo deśadibhiḥ sarvaiḥ saṃbadhyate | viśiṣṭo deśa āryāvartādiḥ | viśiṣṭajātir brāhmaṇajātiḥ | viśiṣṭakulam adhyayanādisaṃpannam | viśiṣṭarūpaṃ kāntimat | viśiṣṭāyuḥ sahaṣoḍaśaṃ varṣaśatam, “sahaṣoḍaśaṃ varṣaśatam ajīvat” iti darśanāt | rogarahitatvam apy āyuṣo viśeṣaḥ | viśiṣtaśrutaṃ “brāhmaṇaś ca bahuśrutaḥ” [GDh 8.1] ity atra vyākhyātam | viśiṣṭavṛttam anupādhi cāritram | viṣiṣṭavittaṃ dharmārjitaṃ dharme prayujyamānaṃ ca | sukhaṃ nirapāyasthānādhiṣṭhānenāniṣiddhamukhasevanam | viśiṣṭamedhā granthārthayor grahaṇaśaktir iti | medhāśabde sakārāntatvam ārṣaṃ sumedhaso durmedhasa ityādiṣv eva darśanāt | karmāṇi bhujyamānāni puṇyāny apuṇyāni ca saśeṣāṇy evaṃ bhujyante | aihikasya śarīragrahaṇāder api puṇyāpuṇyanibandhanatvāt || 11.29 ||
viśvañco viparītā naśyanti || 11.32 || — || 11.30 ||
ye punaḥ na svayaṃ karma kurvanti rājñā ca na sthāpyante te viśvañcaḥ tiryaggatayo naśyanti, viśiṣṭadeśādisaṃbandhābhāvāt | viparītāḥ svakarmaniṣṭhetarāḥ | evaṃ ca bruvatā ye svakarmaniṣṭhās teṣām uttamagatitvam, ye rājñā svadharme sthāpyante teṣāṃ madhyamagatitvam, ye svakarmaniṣṭhā na bhavanti na rājñā ca sthāpyante teṣām adhamagatitvaṃ ca darśitaṃ bhavati | tathā cokaṃ purāṇe |
śubhair bhavati devatvaṃ vyāmiśrair janma mānuṣam |
aśubhaiś cāpy adhojanma karmabhir labhate naraḥ || 11.32 ||
ye varṇāśramāḥ svāni karmāṇi yathāvan nānutiṣṭhanti te viparītā viṣvañco nānāyonīr gacchanto naśyanti | anarthaparamparām anubhavantīti || 11.30 ||
tān ācāryopadeśo daṇḍaś ca pālayate || 11.33 || — || 11.31 ||
tān ājñān avihitānuṣṭhānena pratiṣiddhasevanayā ca naśyato varṇāśramiṇaḥ ācāryopadeśo rakṣati, itarāṃs tu daṇḍa iti || 11.33 ||
tān viparītān yathoktam akurvato varṇān āśramāṃś cācāryopadeśas tāvat pālayate | tatrāpy atiṣṭhato rājadaṇḍaḥ || 11.31 ||
yata evam |
tasmād rājācāryāv anindyāv anindau || 11.34 || — || 11.32 ||
tataś ca rakṣaṇahetutvāt kāraṇe sati pāruṣyādy api vaktavyam | ataḥ paruṣam apy upadiśann ācāryaḥ, ugram api dhārayan rājā ca, atyantopakāritvād akutsanīyau || 11.34 ||
tasmād dheto rājācāryau mānyāv anindyāv iti | yady api niyamanakāle hitaiṣitayā pramukhapuruṣau bhavatas tathāpi tayor nindā na kāryā | [abhyāso ‘dhyāyasamāptyarthaḥ] || 11.32 ||
atha dvādaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
“daṇḍo damanād ity āhus tenādāntān damayet” [GDh 11.30] iti sāmānyenābhihitam | tatra kvāparādhe kiyān daṇḍa iti tad vaktavyam ity āha |
daṇḍenādāntān damayed ity uktam | tatra kiyaty aparādhe kiyān daṇḍa ity ata āha |
śūdro dvijātīn atisaṃdhāyābhihatya ca vāgdaṇḍa-pāruṣyābhyām aṅgaṃ mocyo yenopahanyāt || 12.1 ||
H reads: “abhisaṃdhāyā-”
śūdra uktaḥ | sa dvijātīn upanītān brāhmaṇādīn atisaṃ-dhāya buddhipūrvam atikramya na tu parihāsādinā | abhihatya ca abhiśabdo buddhipūrvajñāpanārthaḥ | abhihatya tāḍayitvā | krameṇa vāgdaṇḍapāruṣyābhyām | tatra vākpāruṣyeṇātisaṃdhāya daṇḍapāruṣyeṇābhi-hatyeti kramo draṣṭavyaḥ | vākpāruṣyeṇātikramaṇam atikramya paruṣādivacanam | paruṣaśabda ugra-paryāyaḥ | ugrayā vācā atikramya ugreṇa ca daṇḍenābhihatya cety arthaḥ | tataś ca upalādinā na doṣaḥ | aṅgaṃ śarīrāvayavaḥ mocyaḥ chedyaḥ yena hastādinā upahanyāt pīḍayet | evaṃ ca vināpi daṇḍena daṇḍapāruṣyaṃ bhavatīti jñāpayati | vākpāruṣye vākchedanam | hastādinā daṇḍapāruṣye tadaṅga-cchedanam iti draṣṭavyam | caśabdād ubhayāparādhe ubhayaṃ mocyaḥ || 12.1 ||
śūdraś caturtho varṇaḥ | sa dvijātīn brāhmaṇādīṃs trīn varṇān | vākpāryuṣyeṇābhisaṃdhāyābhibhūya daṇḍapāruṣyenābhihatya ca | abhir abhisaṃdhipūrve buddhipūṛvaṃ tāḍayitvā | daṇḍagrahaṇaṃ hastāder apy upalakṣaṇam | evaṃ kurvann aṅgaṃ mocyo ‘vayavena viyojanīyo yenāṅgenopahanyād aparādhaṃ kuryāt tad aṅgaṃ mocyaḥ | hastena tāḍane hastacchedaḥ pādena tāḍane pādacchedo vācā jihvācchedaḥ | atra manuḥ |
yenāṅgenāvaro varṇo brāhmaṇasyāparādhnuyāt |
tad aṅgaṃ tasya chettavyaṃ tan manor anuśāsanam || iti | [MDh 8.279] pāruṣyagrahaṇāt parihāsenāpriyavacane parihāsādinā tāḍane ca nedaṃ bhavati || 12.1 ||
āryastryabhigamane liṅgoddhāraḥ svaharaṇaṃ ca || 12.2 ||
āryāṇāṃ brāhmaṇādīnāṃ āryavṛttā cet strī | kuta etat stryabhigamana iti vaktavye āryastryabhigamana ity ārambhāt evaṃ ca veśyārūpeṇa sthitāyām adaṇḍyaḥ | striyām evābhigamanaprasiddher āryāgamana ity eva siddhe strīgrahaṇam āryaparigṛhītāyāṃ śūdrāyām apīty evamartham | abhigamane kṛte abhiśabdo buddhipūrvā-rthaḥ | tataś ca svapnādāv abuddhasya tathaiva kṛtasya laghutaro daṇḍo draṣṭavyaḥ | liṅgasyoddhāraḥ utpāṭa-naṃ sarvasvaharaṇaṃ ca kartavyam | talliṅgoddhāro dhanasya ceti vaktavye svaharaṇaṃ cety abhidhānāt | cakāraḥ samuccayārthaḥ, vikalpo mā bhūd ity asamāsaḥ, kṣatriyavaiśyastrīgamane yathāsaṃkhyenai-kaikam, brāhmaṇasya tūbhayam iti || 12.2 ||
śūdra iti prakṛtaṃ ṣaṣṭhyantam apekṣate | āryās traivarṇikāḥ | teṣāṃ cet striyaṃ śūdro ‘bhigacchet tasya liṅgoddhāro liṅgotpāṭanaṃ kāryaṃ yac ca yāvac ca svaṃ tasya ca haraṇaṃ daṇḍaḥ | āryābhigamanam ity eva siddhe strīgrahaṇam āryagṛhītānāṃ śūdrāyām apīti sūcanārtham | tatra vaiśyastriyāṃ svaharaṇaṃ kṣatriyāyāṃ liṅgoddhāraḥ | brāhmaṇyām ubhayam iti || 12.2 ||
goptā ced vadho ‘dhikaḥ || 12.3 ||
yadi tāsāṃ rakṣitā bhavati tatas tadabhigamane pūrvoktam ubhayaṃ kṛtvā adhikagrahaṇāt, pramāpaṇaṃ kartavyam || 12.3 ||
sa yadi śūdras tāsāṃ rakṣitā bhavati tadā vadhaḥ kāryaḥ | adhikagrahaṇāt pūrvoktadaṇḍadvayam api bhavati || 12.3 ||
atha hāsya vedam upaśṛṇvatas trapujatubhyāṃ śrotrapratipūraṇam udāharaṇe jihvācchedo dhāraṇe śarīrabhedaḥ || 12.4 ||
pañcavarṣād ūrdhvam ayam, athaśabdopādānāt | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
vedaṃ śrutvā tu pañcābdaḥ śūdraś ced daṇḍabhāg bhavet |
aprāptapañcavarṣo na daṇḍam arhati kutracit || iti |
haśabdo buddhipūrvasūcanārthaḥ | na goptur evety evamartham asya śabdasyopādānam | vedaṃ sāṅgam | kutaḥ? “sāṅgo vedaḥ strīśūdrasakāśe nādhyetavyaḥ” iti gṛhyasmṛtidarśanāt | upaśabdena samīpavācinā akṣaraśravaṇa eva doṣa iti darśayati | tataś ca | dhvanimātraśravaṇe na doṣaḥ | haśabdena buddhipūrva eva daṇḍavidhānāt pramādāt kadācid akṣaraśravaṇe ‘pi doṣābhāvo draṣṭavyaḥ | trapujatubhyāṃ trapunā jatunā ceti draṣṭavyam | taptābhyām iti ca draṣṭavyam, pūraṇopadeśasāmarthyāt | śrotradvayaparigrahārthaḥ pratiśabdaḥ udāharaṇe dvijātibhiḥ saha jihvācchedaḥ kartavyaḥ | dhāraṇe svayam evodāharaṇa ity arthaḥ | paraśunā śarīrabhedaḥ kartavyaḥ || 12.4 ||
atha heti vākyālaṃkāre | upaśrutya buddhipūrvam akṣaragrahaṇam upaśravaṇam | asya śūdrasya vedam upaśṛṇvatas trapujatubhyāṃ trapuṇā jatunā ca dravīkṛtena śrotre pratipūrayitavye | upaśravaṇaśabdena yadṛcchayā dhvanimātraśravaṇe na doṣaḥ | sa ced dvijātibhiḥ saha vedākṣaraṇy udāhared uccaret, tasya jihvā chedyā | dhāraṇe sati yadānyatra gato ‘pi svayam uccārayituṃ śaknoti tataḥ paraśvādinā śarīram asya bhedyam || 12.4 ||
āsanaśayanavākpathiṣu samaprepsur daṇḍyaḥ || 12.5 ||
āsanādiṣv āryais tulyatvaṃ svecchayā kāmayamānaḥ | āsanādiṣūcchritādiguṇeṣu samatvaṃ vāksāmyaṃ samakāloccāraṇam, pathi sāmyaṃ pṛṣṭhato muktvā saha gamanam | daṇḍaḥ sthānādyapekṣayā śatād arvāg draṣṭavyaḥ || 12.5 ||
śūdraś ced āsanādiṣu dvijātibhiḥ saha sāmyaṃ prepsati tattulyabhāvaṃ tato ‘sau daṇḍyaḥ | daṇḍaś ca āpastambena darśitaḥ: “vāci pathi śayyāyām āsana iti samī bhavato daṇḍatāḍanam” [ĀpDh 2.27.15] iti | danḍenāsau tāḍya iti | atra mānavo viśeṣaḥ |
sahāsanam abhiprepsur utkṛṣṭasyāpakṛṣṭakaḥ |
kaṭyāṃ kṛtāṅko nirvāsyaḥ sphijau vāpy asya kartayet || [MDh 8.281] iti || 12.5 ||
śataṃ kṣatriyo brāhmaṇākrośe || 12.6 ||
kṛta iti śeṣaḥ | ākrośe vākpāruṣye | daṇḍapāruṣye tu dviguṇaṃ kalpyam, smṛtyantarasāmarthyāt | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
vākpāruṣye kṛte yasya yathā daṇḍo vidhīyate |
tasyaiva dviguṇaṃ daṇḍaṃ kārayen maraṇād ṛte || iti | tathā cāha uśanā: “avagūraṇe vākpāruṣyatulyaṃ tāḍane taddviguṇaṃ śoṇitotpādane triguṇam” ityādi | śataṃ kārṣāpaṇānāṃ kṣatriyo daṇḍyaḥ brāhmaṇākrośe || 12.6 ||
kṣatriyaś ced brāhmaṇam ākrośed vācā paruṣayā nindet tataḥ śataṃ daṇḍyaḥ | daṇḍaprakaraṇe sarvatra tāmrikasya kārṣāpaṇasya grahaṇam iti smārto vyavahāraḥ | śataṃ kārṣāpaṇāni daṇḍyaḥ | daṇḍapāruṣye dviguṇam | athāha bṛhaspatiḥ |
vākpāruṣye kṛte yasya yathā daṇḍo vidhīyate |
tasyaiva dviguṇaṃ daṇḍaṃ kārayen maraṇād ṛte || iti || 12.6 ||
adhyardhaṃ vaiśyaḥ || 12.7 ||
brāhmaṇākrośa iti vartate | ardhenādhikaṃ yac chataṃ tadadhyardhaṃ taddaṇḍyo vaiśyaḥ || 12.7 ||
vaiśyas tu brāhmaṇākrośe ‘dhyardhaṃ śataṃ daṇḍyo ‘rdhādhikaṃ pañcāśadadhikaṃ śataṃ daṇḍyaḥ || 12.7 ||
brāhmaṇas tu kṣatriye pañcāśat || 12.8 ||
ākruṣṭe iti vartate | brāhmaṇena kṣatriye ākruṣṭe brāhmaṇa aśrotriyaś cet | kutaḥ | tuśabdopādānāt, pañcāśad daṇḍyaḥ | samavarṇeṣu dvādaśa vā kalpya avacanīyeṣu dviguṇaṃ kalpyam | tathā ca manuḥ |
samavarṇe dvijātīnāṃ dvādaśaiva vyatikrame |
vādeṣv avacanīyeṣu tad eva dviguṇaṃ bhavet || [MDh 8.269] || 12.8 ||
kṣatriyākrośe brāhmaṇas tu pañcāśatpaṇān daṇḍyaḥ
|| 12.8 ||
tadardhaṃ vaiśye || 12.9 ||
vaiśye ākruṣṭe brāhmaṇaḥ pañcaviṃśatir daṇḍyaḥ
|| 12.9 ||
vaiśyākrośe tadardhaṃ pañcaviṃśatipaṇān daṇḍyaḥ || 12.9 ||
śūdre na kiṃcit || 12.10 ||
ākruṣṭe na kiṃcid brāhmaṇo daṇḍyaḥ | atha vā, avacanād eva siddham iti cen, na, kṣatriyavaiśyayor daṇḍaprāpaṇārthatvāt | evaṃ ca tadviṣaye daṇḍaḥ kalpyaḥ, kṣatriye caturviṃśatipaṇam, ṣaṭtriṃśataṃ vaiśya iti | tathāha uśanā: “śūdram ākruśya kṣatriyaś caturviṃśatidaṇḍabhāg vaiśyaḥ ṣaṭtriṃśat” ityādi
|| 12.10 ||
tv ākruṣṭe na kiṃcid api dravyaṃ brāhmaṇo daṇḍyaḥ | tad idaṃ na vaktavyam: avacanād eva daṇḍābhāvaḥ sidhyet | kiṃ tu kṣatriyavaiśyayoḥ śūdrākrośe daṇḍaprāpaṇārtham uktam | tad uktam uśanasā: “śūdram ākruśya kṣatriyaś caturviṃśatipaṇān daṇḍabhāg vaiśyaḥ ṣaṭtriṃśat” iti || 12.10 ||
brāhmaṇarājanyavat kṣatriyavaiśyau || 12.11 ||
yat brāhmaṇakṣatriyayoḥ parasparākrośa uktaṃ tat kṣatriyavaiśyayor api draṣṭavyam | kṣatriye śataṃ vaiśye pañcāśad iti | evam antarajānām api draṣṭavyam | tathāha jamadagniḥ: “mātṛtulyam anulomānāṃ pitṛtulyaṃ pratilomānām” iti || 12.11 ||
brāhmaṇarājanyayoḥ parasparākrośe yādṛśo daṇḍas tādṛśaḥ kṣatriyavaiśyayoḥ parasparākrośe | tataś caivaṃ sūtram ūhitavyam | śataṃ vaiśyaḥ kṣatriyākrośe | kṣatriyas tu vaiśyaṃ pañcāśat | evam antaraprabhaveṣv api draṣṭavyam | atra jamadagniḥ: “mātṛtulyam anulomānāṃ pitṛtulyaṃ pratilomānām” iti || 12.11 ||
sāhasadaṇḍam uktvā steya idānīm āha |
uktaḥ sāhasadaṇḍaḥ | steyadaṇḍam āha |
aṣṭāpādyaṃ steyakilbiṣaṃ śūdrasya || 12.12 ||
steyena yad upāttam adharmakāraṇāt tad dravyaṃ kilbiṣaśabdenocyate steyakilbiṣaṃ steyadhanam ity arthaḥ | tadaṣṭaguṇaṃ daṇḍaḥ, “samaprepsur daṇḍyaḥ” [GDh 12.5] ity atra daṇḍyaśabdasya nadīsroto-nyāyenādhikṛtasyātra ṣaṣṭyanurūpārthaṃ daṇḍa ity evaṃ bhavatīti | kilbiṣaśabdena vā daṇḍa ucyate | steyakilbiṣaṃ steyadaṇḍa ity arthaḥ | sa gṛhītaḥ syād aṣṭaguṇa iti | tatra brāhmaṇasuvarṇavarjaṃ draṣṭavyam, tasya mahāpātakamadhye upadeśād daṇḍagauravaṃ bhavatīti || 12.12 ||
steyaṃ cauryam | steyopāttaṃ dravyaṃ kilbiṣanimittatvāt kilbiṣam ucyate | steyenopāttaṃ dravyam aṣṭaguṇam āpādanīyaṃ śūdrasya | kartari ṣaṣṭhy eṣā | steyakilbiṣaṃ śūdro ‘ṣṭaguṇam āpādayed rājñe daṇḍarūpeṇa pratipādayed iti | tatraiko guṇaḥ svāmine deyaḥ | śeṣo rājñe | uktaṃ ca “corahṛtam avajitya” [GDh 10.46] ityādinā || 12.12 ||
dviguṇottarāṇītareṣāṃ prativarṇam || 12.13 ||
itareṣāṃ vaiśyakṣatriyabrāhmaṇānām, prātilomyena prativarṇam ity ārambhāt, dviguṇottarāṇi daṇḍaḥ uttaram uttaraṃ dviguṇam iti | vaiśyasya ṣoḍaśaguṇam, kṣatriyasya dvātriṃśadguṇam, brāhmaṇasya catuḥṣaṣṭiguṇam iti | sarvatra dhanine dhanārpaṇaṃ draṣṭavyam, “corahṛtam apajitya yathāsthānaṃ gamayet” [GDh 10.46] ity uktatvāt || 12.13 ||
itareṣāṃ vaiśyādīnāṃ steyakilbiṣāṇi prativarṇaṃ dviguṇottarāṇy āpādanīyāni | vaiśyasya ṣoḍaśaguṇaṃ kṣatriyasya dvātriṃśadguṇaṃ brāhmaṇasya catuḥṣaṣṭiguṇam iti || 12.13 ||
kasmād idam evam ity āha |
viduṣo ‘tikrame daṇḍabhūyastvam || 12.14 ||
jātyutkarṣavad vijñānotkarṣād api daṇḍabhūyastvaṃ draṣṭavyam, “tat svajātivihitād adhyardhaṃ viduṣo jñeyam” iti smṛtyantaradarśanāt | upapannaṃ caitat, yato ‘asau jñātvātikrāmatīti | viduṣo daṇḍabhūyastvam iti siddhe atikramagrahaṇaṃ niyamārtham, cauryaviṣaya evedam, na sāhasaviṣaye | prāyaścitta-viṣaye ‘pīti | evaṃ ca bruvatā daṇḍavidhānāt prāyaścittasyāpi gurulaghubhāvakalpanāstīti pradarśitaṃ bhavati | sāhasaprakaraṇe ca yad uktam “śrotriyasyārdhadaṇḍaḥ" iti tac copapannam || 12.14 ||
yathā yathā varṇotkarṣeṇa vidyotkarṣas tathā tathā vihitātikrame daṇḍabhūyastvaṃ bhavati | niṣedhadoṣaṃ jñātvāpi pravartamānasya doṣādhikyaṃ bhavati | ajānatas tv andhakūpapatanavad anugraho ‘sti | aṣṭāpādyam ityāder apavādaḥ || 12.14 ||
asyāpavādam āha |
phalaharitadhānyaśākādāne pañcakṛṣṇalam alpe || 12.15 ||
H reads “alpam”
phalānām āmrādīnām, haritadhānyasya vrīhyādeḥ apakvasyety arthaḥ | śākasya ca mūlakāder apaharaṇe māṣamātradaṇḍaḥ | kutaḥ “pañcakṛṣṇalako māṣaḥ” [MDh 8.134] iti smṛtyantaradarśanāt | alpe udara-pūraṇamātre | adhike anyadravye vā “aṣṭāpādyam” [GDh 12.12] ity etad eva draṣṭavyam || 12.15 ||
phalam āmrādi | haritadhānyaṃ stambe ‘vasthitaṃ vrīhyādi | śākaṃ vāstūkādi | eteṣāṃ steyenādāne pañcakṛṣṇalaṃ daṇḍaḥ | kṛṣṇalaṃ guñjābījapramāṇam |
māṣo viṃśatibhāgas tu jñeyaḥ kārṣāpaṇasya hi |
kṛṣṇalas tu caturthāṃśo māṣasyaiva prakīrtitaḥ || iti |
pañcānāṃ kṛṣṇalānāṃ samāhāraḥ pañcakṛṣṇalam | alpaṃ tac cet phalādi alpam udarapūraṇamātram | adhike tv aṣṭāpādyam eva || 12.15 ||
paśupīḍite svāmidoṣaḥ || 12.16 ||
paśubhir upahate sasyādau yasya paśavas tasya daṇḍo vakṣyamāṇaḥ || 12.16 ||
paśubhir upahate sasyādau paśumato doṣaḥ | daṇḍaparimāṇaṃ vakṣyati || 12.16 ||
pālasaṃyukte tu tasmin || 12.17 ||
pālānvite pāla eva daṇḍabhāg ity arthaḥ | tuśabdaḥ pālena kārite daṇḍabhūyastvajñāpanārthaḥ | bhūyastvam api dviguṇam, “pālakārite dviguṇo daṇḍaḥ” iti smṛtyantaradarśanāt || 12.17 ||
sa cet paśuḥ pālāya svāminā samarpitas tadā tasmin pāle doṣaḥ | pālayatīti pālo gopālaḥ | idaṃ pramādakṛte, buddhipūrve tu dviguṇo daṇḍaḥ, tathā smṛtyantare darśanāt || 12.17 ||
pathi kṣetre ‘nāvṛte pālakṣetrikayoḥ || 12.18 ||
pālasyāyatnakaraṇād itarasyāpi vṛter akaraṇāt tāv ardham ardhaṃ dadyātām ity arthaḥ || 12.18 ||
kṣetrikaḥ kṣetrāny asya kṣetraṃ pathy anāvṛtaṃ bhavati tatra paśupīḍite pālakṣetrikayor ubhayor daṇḍo ‘rdham ardham | pālasyānavadhānāt kṣetrikasya vṛtyakaraṇāc ca,
vṛtiṃ ca tatra kurvīta yām uṣṭro nāvalokayet | [MDh 8.239] iti mānave darśanāt || 12.18 ||
idānīṃ paśuviśeṣāpekṣayā daṇḍaviśeṣam āha |
daṇḍaparimāṇam āha |
pañca māṣā gavi || 12.19 ||
māṣaḥ kārṣāpaṇasya viṃśatibhāgaḥ | tathāha uśanā |
māṣo viṃśatibhāgas tu jñeyaḥ kārṣāpaṇasya tu |
kākaṇī tu caturbhāgo māṣasyaiva prakīrtitaḥ || iti |
te pañca gavy aparādhe daṇḍyaḥ || 12.19 ||
uśanasā māṣo darśitaḥ |
māṣo viṃśatibhāgas tu jñeyaḥ kārṣāpaṇasya hi |
kākiṇī tu caturthāṃśo māṣasyaiva prakīrtitaḥ || iti ||
māṣāḥ pañca gopīḍite sasyādau daṇḍaḥ || 12.19 ||
ṣaḍ uṣṭrakhare || 12.20 ||
uṣṭrāparādhe kharāparādhe ca pratyekaṃ ṣaṇmāṣā daṇḍaḥ || 12.20 ||
dvandvaikavadbhāvaḥ | uṣṭtrakhare tūpahantari pratyekaṃ ṣaṇmāṣā daṇḍaḥ || 12.20 ||
aśvamahiṣyor daśa || 12.21 ||
atrāpi pratyekaṃ daśa māṣā daṇḍaḥ || 12.21 ||
liṅgam avivakṣitam | aśve mahiṣe ca pratyekaṃ daśa māṣā daṇḍaḥ || 12.21 ||
ajāviṣu dvau dvau || 12.22 ||
bahuvacanaṃ vatsanivṛttyartham | tat pūrvatrāpi draṣṭavyam | dvau dvau māṣau daṇḍaḥ | ante vīpsābhidhānaṇaṃ pūrve ‘bhyanuṣaṅgārtham, itarathā saṃdehaḥ syād iti || 12.22 ||
ajeṣv aviṣu copasaṃhantṛṣu dvau dvau māṣau | saṃbhūya carantīti bahuvacanam | pratyajaṃ pratyavikaṃ dvau dvau daṇḍaḥ || 12.22 ||
sarvavināśe śadaḥ || 12.23 ||
sarvavināśe prarohaṇasāmarthye yāvad vināśitaṃ tāvan niṣpattim avekṣya kṣetrasvāmine śadaḥ phalaṃ deyam | rājñaś cānurūpo daṇḍaḥ || 12.23 ||
yathā punaḥ praroho na bhaveti tathā sarvavināśe śado daṇḍaḥ | śada iti bhāgābhidhānam | yāvāṃs tatra bhāga utpatsyate tāvat svāmine deyam | rājñe cānurūpo daṇḍaḥ || 12.23 ||
śiṣṭākaraṇe pratiṣiddhasevāyāṃ ca nityaṃ celapiṇḍād ūrdhvaṃ svaharaṇam || 12.24 ||
H reads “cailapiṇḍād”
nityaṃ śiṣṭākaraṇe nityaṃ vihitasyānanuṣṭhāne, na sakṛt, pratiṣidhasyābhakṣyabhakṣaṇāder āsevane, na pramādāt, celapiṇḍād bhaktācchādanamātrād yad anyat tasyāpahāraḥ kartavyaḥ | bhaktācchādanaṃ tu yāvad anyasya dravyasyāgamanakālas tāvad asya moktavyam | tato ‘pi yady atikrāmati punar asya svam apahartavyam eveti | evaṃ kṛte daṇḍabhayāt svakarmaṇy eva pravartata iti | cakārāt prāyaścittaṃ ca kārayitavyam || 12.24 ||
śiṣṭaṃ vihitam | nityaṃ śiṣṭasyākaraṇe nityaṃ ca pratiṣiddhasevāyāṃ cailapiṇḍād ūrdhvaṃ cailam ācchādanaṃ piṇḍo grāsas tābhyām ūrdhvaṃ yāvatā tayor nivṛttis tato ‘dhikaṃ yat svaṃ tasya haraṇaṃ kāryam | ācchādanāsanārthaṃ yat kiṃcit parihāpyāvaśiṣṭam asya svaṃ hartavyam ity evam ato nivṛtteḥ || 12.24 ||
brahmacāriprakaraṇe uktasyādattādāna-pratiṣedhasyedānīm apavādam āha |
adattādānaniṣedhaviṣaye ‘pavādam āha |
go ‘gnyarthe tṛṇam edhān vīrudvanaspatīnāṃ ca puṣpāṇi svavad ādadīta phalāni cāparivṛtānām || 12.25 ||
gavārthaṃ tṛṇāni | agnigrahaṇena śrautasya smārtasyāpi grahaṇaṃ na laukikasya, tadartham edhān kāṣṭhāni | vīrudhāṃ karavīrādīnāṃ puṣpāṇi – vanaspatiśabdo ‘tra vṛkṣaparyāyaḥ, sākṣād vanaspatīnāṃ puṣpāsaṃbhavāt | yathāha manuḥ |
apuṣpāḥ phalavanto ye te vanaspatayaḥ smṛtāḥ || iti | [MDh 1.47] puṣpāṇi devārcanārthāni nānubhavārthāni, go’gnisāhacaryāt | cakārāt patrāṇi brāhmaṇabhojanārthāni | vīrudvanaspatipuṣpāṇi ceti vaktavye oṣadhārthaṃ mūlāder api brāhmaṇārtham asamāsaḥ | svavat yathā teṣāṃ pīḍā na bhavati tathā gṛhītavyam iti | yathāha vyāsaḥ |
pakvaṃ pakvaṃ pracinvīta mūlacchedaṃ tu varjayet |
mālākāra ivārāme na yathāṅgārakārakaḥ || iti |
phalāni cāmraphalādīni | āpatsūcanārthaṃ pṛthag abhidhānam | cakārāc chākaṃ ca | aparivṛtānām anārāmīkṛtānām | ubhayaviśeṣaṇaṃ cedam | evaṃ cārāme tṛṇāder api pratiṣedhaḥ siddhaḥ || 12.25 ||
agniḥ śrautasmārtādir na laukikaḥ | gavārthe tṛṇāni | agnyartha edhān vīrudvanaspatīnām | latānāṃ vṛkṣāṇāṃ puṣpāṇi devatārcanārthāni nopabhogārthāni | gavāgnisāhacaryād devatārthānīti gamyate | etāni tṛṇādīni svāmibhir adattāny api svavad ādadīta | yathā svāmī niḥśaṅkam ādatte tadvad ādadīta | te vīrudvanas-patayo ‘parivṛtāś cet teṣāṃ phalāny api svavad ādadīta na svāmyapekṣā | phalaviṣayam etad aparivṛtatvaṃ na tṛṇādiviṣayam, pṛthagvākyatvāt || 12.25 ||
idānīṃ nyāyenāpi paradravyādānaṃ steyam eveti tatparihārārtham idam ārabhyate |
kusīdavṛddhir dharmyā viṃśatiḥ pañcamāṣikī māsam || 12.26 ||
kusīdaṃ dravyaprayogaḥ | tatra vṛddhir upacayaḥ dharmyā dharmād anapetā yadā viṃśatiḥ kārṣāpaṇānāṃ māsaṃ prayuktā bhavanti tadā tasyāḥ pratimāsaṃ pañcamāṣaparimāṇā vṛddhir bhavati, pratimāsaṃ niṣkasyārdhaguñjāmātrā vṛddhir ity arthaḥ | māṣaśabdena vrīhiparimitaṃ vastv abhidhīyata iti | evaṃ ca manunoktasyāśītibhāgasyaiva grahaṇaṃ saṃpadyate | tathāha manuḥ |
vasiṣṭhavihitāṃ vṛddhiṃ sṛjed vittavivardhinīm |
aśītibhāgaṃ gṛhṇīyān māsaṃ vārdhuṣikaḥ śateḥ || iti | [MDh 8.140] vasiṣṭhavihitāṃ “kāmaṃ vā pariluptakṛtyāya pāpīyase dadyātām” [VaDh 2,43] iti | suvarṇakārṣāpaṇapakṣe catuḥṣaṣṭibhāgo bhavati: “te suvarṇaṃ tu ṣoḍaśa” [YDh 1.358] iti || 12.26 ||
vṛddhyarthaṃ prayuktasya dravyasya kusīdasaṃjñā | “māṣaḥ kārṣāpaṇasya viṃśatitamo bhāgaḥ” ity uśanasoktam | pañca māṣā vṛddhirūpeṇa dīyante yatra viṃśatau sā pañcamāṣikī | tad asmin vṛddhyāyalābhaśulkopadā dīyata ity atrārthe prāgvahaseṣṭhak | adhyardhapūrvadvigor lug asañjāyām iti lukprāpto na kṛtaḥ svācchandyād ṛṣiṇā | kārṣāpaṇānāṃ viṃśatiḥ pratimāsaṃ pañcamāṣikī yathā bhavati tathā bhavantī kusīdavṛddhidharmād anapetā | atra manuḥ |
vasiṣṭhavihitāṃ vṛddhiṃ sṛjed vittavivardhinīm |
aśītibhāgaṃ gṛhṇīyān māsād vārdhuṣikaḥ śataḥ || iti | [MDh 8.140] atrāpīyam eva vṛddhir uktā | katham | paṇasya viṃśatitamo bhāgo māṣaḥ | paṇānāṃ viṃśatiś catuḥśatī māṣāṇāṃ saṃpadyate | catuḥśatyāḥ pañca māṣā vṛddhir aśīter ekaḥ | pañcaśatīti yaś caturaśatīti (?) | yājñavalkyas tu |
aśītibhāgo vṛddhiḥ syān masi māsi sabandhake |
varṇakramāc chataṃ dvitricatuḥpañcakam anyathā || iti | [YDh 2.37] viśvāsārthaṃ yad ādhīyate suvarṇādi tad bandhakam | tad ukte dhanaprayoge varṇānupūrvyād brāhmaṇādiṣv adhamarṇeṣu dhanaṃ dvitryādiyuktaṃ kramād bhavati || 12.26 ||
nātisāṃvatsarīm eke || 12.27 ||
māsam atikramya saṃvatsaravṛddhir na grāhyety eke manyante | etad uktam bhavati – māsi māsi yad grahīta-vyaṃ tat sarvaṃ saṃbhūya saṃvatsare paripūrṇa eva gṛhṇāmīty evaṃ matvā pratisaṃvatsaraṃ na grahīta-vyam iti | pratisaṃvatsarapratiṣedhāt pratimāsaṃ pratiṣāṇmāsaṃ cābhyanujñāyate | tataś ca māsi māsi ṣaṇmāse ṣaṇmāse vā grahītavyaṃ na pratisaṃvatsaram ity ekeṣām abhiprāyaḥ | pratimāsavṛddhigrahaṇe caitad draṣṭavyam | yāvad dhanasaṃ-vyavahāras tāvad grāhy-aiveti gautamaḥ | tadvicchede tu “nātisāṃvatsarīm” iti vyavasthitavibhāgo draṣṭavyaḥ || 12.27 ||
yeyam aśītibhāgalakṣaṇā dharmyā vṛddhis tām atisāṃvatsarīṃ saṃvatsare ‘tikrānte bhavāṃ na gṛhṇīyāt, ekasminn eva saṃvatsare pratimāsam aśītibhāgo grāhyas tata ūrdhvaṃ na kiṃcid api grāhyam eṣā dharmyā bhavatīty eke manyante | atisāṃvatsarīm iti rūpasiddhiś cintyā || 12.27 ||
svamatam āha |
cirasthāne dvaiguṇyaṃ prayogasya || 12.28 ||
cirasthāne agṛhītāyāṃ vṛddhau tayā vṛddhyā yāvatā kālena dviguṇaṃ bhavati tataḥ paraṃ na vardhate | prayogagrahaṇaṃ prayogāntare dvaiguṇyavyatikra-mārtham | yathāha manuḥ |
kusīdavṛddhiḥ dvaiguṇyaṃ nātyeti sakṛd āhitā || iti | [MDh 8.151] hiraṇyasyaiva dviguṇānatikramaṇam iti jñāpanārthaṃ sthānagrahaṇam | dhānyādayo dviguṇam apy atikramya vardhanta eva | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “dviguṇaṃ hiraṇyaṃ triguṇaṃ dhānyaṃ dhānyenaiva rasā vyākhyātā, puṣpamūlaphalāni ca tulādhṛtam aṣṭaguṇam” [VaDh 2.44–47] iti | evaṃ ca bruvatā kusīdavṛddhir dharmyeti yoktā vṛddhis tasyāḥ sārvatrikatvaṃ pradarśitaṃ bhavati, anyathā prasaṅgābhāvād dhiraṇyasyaiva siddhatvāt tadarthaṃ sthānagrahaṇam anarthakam iti | tataś ca sarvatrāśītibhāgo draṣṭavyaḥ | ciragrahaṇaṃ saṃvatsarasahasrair api na vardhata iti || 12.28 ||
yāvatā kālena prayuktaṃ dhanaṃ dviguṇaṃ bhavati tāvantam eva kālaṃ dharmyayā vṛddhyā vivardhate nātaḥ param iti | suvarṇādidravyaviṣayam etat | atra vasiṣṭhaḥ: “dviguṇaṃ hiraṇyaṃ triguṇaṃ dhānyam | dhānyenaiva rasā vyākhyātāḥ | vṛkṣamūlaphalāni ca tulādhṛtam aṣṭaguṇam” [VaDh 2.44–47] iti | ciragrahaṇāt sahasreṇāpi saṃvatsarair na dvaiguṇyāt paraṃ vardhate iti || 12.28 ||
bhuktādhir na vardhate || 12.29 ||
bhoga eva tatra vṛddhir bhavatīti || 12.29 ||
viśvāsārthaṃ yad ādhīyate kāṃsyābharaṇādi sa ādhiḥ | sa ced upabhuktaḥ prayukto ‘rtho na vardhate | bhoga eva tatra vṛddhir iti || 12.29 ||
kiṃ ca |
ditsato ‘varuddhasya ca || 12.30 ||
dātum icchato dhanine sa yadā dhanī vṛddhilobhād vyājena na gṛhṇāti tato ‘pi na vardhate | yo datum icchati tena sākṣimal lāñchanā saṃsthā kartavyā, anabhijñānād buddhivyāpārasya | evam avaruddhasya ca niḥsvo bhūtvā deśāntaragatasyety arthaḥ | cakāraḥ pūrvaviśeṣaṇāśaṅkā mā bhūd iti | taddivasād ārabhyeti draṣṭavyam | kecid rājādinā nigalabandhanam āhuḥ tatra bhūmidevāḥ pramāṇam || 12.30 ||
dhanine dhanaṃ dātum icchato ‘dhamarṇasya dhanaṃ na vardhate | dhanī vṛddhilobhād vyājena na gṛhṇāti cet tasminn eva divase parahaste sthāpyaṃ tad ārabhya vṛddhir na vardhate tathā yo ditsann adhamarṇo rājādināvaruddhas tasyāpi dātum asamarthasya dravyaṃ tata ārabhya na vardhate || 12.30 ||
idānīm āpadi svayaṃ kṛtā vṛddhiprakārā abhyanujñāyante |
athāpadi vṛddhyantarāṇy āha |
cakrakālavṛddhiḥ || 12.31 ||
vṛddhiśabdaḥ pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyate | cakravṛddhiḥ – vṛddhir api punar vardhate yasmin dravyaprayoge sā cakravṛddiḥ | yathāha nāradaḥ |
vṛddher api punar vṛddhiś cakravṛddhir udāhṛtā | iti | [NSm 1.89] kālavṛddhiḥ – asmāt kālāt prāk kevalaṃ vā prakṛtavṛddhyā vā mama dātavyam | yadi na dadāsi tatas tasmād ūrdhvaṃ triguṇaṃ ca caturguṇaṃ vā deyam iti samayaṃ kṛtvā yasmin bahvī vṛddhir gṛhyate sā kālavṛddhiḥ | prakṛtalabdham api vṛddhigrahaṇaṃ kriyate svayaṃ kṛtāyā eveyaṃ kalpanā na pañcamāṣaparimitāyā iti | sahanirdeśa ekayā jīvanāsaṃbhave dvābhyām api jīvane na doṣa iti |
nanu ca “kusīdavṛddhir dharmyā viṃśatiḥ pañcamāṣikī māsam” [GDh 12.26] ity atraiva vaktavyam | atraiva pṛthagupadeśād āpadarthatvaṃ ca jñāyate | “nātisāṃvatsarīm” [GDh 12.27] ity etad api vyākhyānato viśeṣapratipattir ity anena nyāyena vyākhyānata eva pañcamāṣaparimitavṛddhiviṣayam iti jñātuṃ śakyata iti |
atrocyate – kusīdasya svayaṃkaraṇapakṣe āpady anāpadi ca asvayaṃkṛtapakṣe cānāpadi pañcamāṣaparimitām eva gṛhṇīyāt nāsvayaṃkṛtapakṣa āpadi cakrādivṛddhim api, anenābhiprāyenopadeśa iti | “nātisāṃvatasarīm” [GDh 12.27] ity asyāpi vispaṣṭīkaraṇārthas tatopadeśa iti || 12.31 ||
vṛddhiśabdaḥ pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyate | yāvatā kālena yāvatī vṛddhis tām api mūlīkṛtya tāvato mūlasya punar vṛddhiś cakravṛddhiḥ || yathāha nāradaḥ |
vṛddher api punar vṛddhiś cakravṛddhir udāhṛtā || iti | [NSm 1.89] iyataḥ kālasyeyatī vṛddhir iti yatra samayena gṛhyate sā kālavṛddhiḥ || 12.31 ||
kāritākāyikāśikhādhibhogāś ca || 12.32 ||
kāritā śāstrānapekṣayā yasminn ātmanaiva kriyate pratisaṃvatsaraṃ niṣkasya tatpādam ardhaṃ vā deyam ity evamādiḥ sā kāritā vṛddhiḥ | kāyikā abhyupagatāyā vṛddher adhikaṃ divase divase māsi māsi saṃvatsare saṃvatsare vā yasmin gṛhyate sā kāyikā | tathā ca nāradaḥ |
kāyāvirodhinī śaśvat paṇapādādikādhikā | iti | [NSm 1.88] śikhā dhānyasya, yathā caturṇāṃ prasthānāṃ pañcama ityādi | adhibhogaḥ vṛddhyartham ahar ahar yasmin ghṛtādi gṛhyate sādhibhogavṛddhiḥ | vṛddhiśabdānukarṣaṇārthaś cakāraḥ | gauravapradarśanārthaḥ pṛthagnirdeśaḥ || 12.32 ||
vṛddhaya it śeṣaḥ | prayoktā gṛ(gra)hītrā ca deśakālakāryāvasthāpekṣayā prabhūtā nyūnā vā svayam eva kalpitā vṛddhiḥ kāritā | kāyikā kāyakarmasaṃśodhyā | tathā bṛhaspatiḥ: “kāyikā karmasaṃyukā” iti | vyāsas tu |
dohyavāhyakarmayuktā kāyikā samudāhṛtā | iti |
śikhāvṛddhiṃ kātyāyana āha |
pratyahaṃ gṛhyate yā hi śikhāvṛddhis tu sā smṛtā |
śikhena vardhate nityaṃ śiraśchedān nivartate ||
mūle datte tathaiveṣā śikhāvṛddhis tataḥ smṛtā || iti |
udāharaṇam – taṇḍulaprasthasya pratyahaṃ taṇḍulamuṣṭir gṛhyata iti | ādhibhoga āhitasya kṣetrasya bhogo ‘nubhavaḥ | tatrānubhava eva vṛddhiḥ | sā ca śatenāpi saṃvatsarair na nivartate | kṣetraṃ cottamarṇasya na bhavati | yadā kadācid api mūlapradāne saty adhamarṇasya bhavati | adhibhoga ity anye | bhogam adhikṛtya vartata ity adhibhogavṛddhiḥ | tatrāpy eṣa evārthaḥ | etāsu cakravṛddhyādiṣu vṛddher dvaiguṇyāt param api bhavaty eva || 12.32 ||
kusīdaṃ paśūpajalomakṣetraśadavāhyeṣu nātipañcaguṇam || 12.33 ||
adhikṛtam api kusīdagrahaṇaṃ kriyate anyarūpo ‘yaṃ prayoga iti jñāpanārtham | paśūpajaṃ ghṛtādi tat krītvā amuṣmin kāle mūlyaṃ dātavyam, tasminn adīyamānaṃ vardhata iti yat paribhāṣitam ity abhiprāyaḥ | vatsasya grahaṇaṃ mā bhūd ity upaśabdaprayogaḥ | loma ūrṇādi | prakṛtigrahaṇena vikārāṇām api grahaṇaṃ draṣṭavyam | tasyāpi krayamūlyasyādīyamānasyaiva | kṣetraśadaḥ kṣetraṃ yadā parimitena dhānyena prayujyate tasmin kāle asya śulkam adīyamānaṃ vardhata iti | vāhyaḥ prāpaṇanimittaṃ yathā balīvardādibhiḥ kasyacid dhānyaṃ prāpayati amuṣmin kāle ‘sya śulkam adīyamānaṃ vardhata iti | eteṣu yat kusīdam upari savṛddhikaṃ dātavyam iti prayuktam ity arthaḥ | tat pañcaguṇatvaṃ nātivartate | paśūpajagrahaṇenaiva siddhasya lomnaḥ punar grahaṇaṃ mayūrapatra-kṛtānām api grahaṇārtham, “pakṣāṇāṃ patraromāṇām agne brūhi mayā śrutam” iti śrutidarśanāt | “cirasthāne dvaiguṇyaṃ prayogasya” [GDh 12.28] ity atraiva vaktavyam iti cet, naiṣa doṣaḥ – eṣāṃ cakravṛddhi-prayogābhāvajñāpanārtha ihopadeśa iti | pañcaguṇam ity anena siddhe nātigrahaṇaṃ prayogāntare atikramaṇasūcanārtham || 12.33 ||
paśor upajātaṃ paśūpajaṃ ghṛtakṣīrādi | ūrṇākambalacāmaravālavyajanādi lomakṣetraśadaḥ kṣetrabhogaḥ | vāhyaṃ balīvardādi | bāhyam iti prāyeṇa paṭhanti, tatrāpy eṣa evārthaḥ | eteṣu paśūpajādiṣu prayukteṣu tat kusīdaṃ yāvat pañcaguṇaṃ vardhate pañcaguṇatāṃ nātyeti | apara āha – paśūpajātiṣu mūlatvena kalpitasya dravyasya tadānīm apradāne yāvat pañcaguṇaṃ vardhate, dharmyayāṃ ca vṛddhyā pañcaguṇatāṃ natyeti || 12.33 ||
idānīm anyo vyavahāra ucyate |
ajaḍāpaugaṇḍadhanaṃ daśavarṣabhuktaṃ paraiḥ saṃnidhau bhoktuḥ || 12.34 ||
jaḍaḥ unmugdhacittaḥ saṃvyavahārāyogyaḥ | pogaṇḍaḥ aprāptavyavahāraḥ | tau muktvā yad anyasya dhanaṃ paraiḥ asaṃbaddhaiḥ tatsamakṣaṃ – svāminy ekagrāmasthita evety arthaḥ – bhujyate tat daśavarṣa-bhoktur eva bhavati, na his sati svāmitve iyantaṃ kālam upekṣaṇam upapadyata ity abhiprāyaḥ | jaḍabāladhanaṃ tu nāhriyate | tadgrahaṇam ādhyādī-nām apy upalakṣaṇārtham | yathāha nāradaḥ |
ādhiḥ sīmā bāladhanaṃ nikṣepopanidhiḥ striyaḥ |
rājasvaṃ śrotriyasvaṃ ca nopabhogena jīryati || iti | [NSm 1.73]
atra ajaḍadhanaṃ na parigaṇitam iti cet, viṃśativarṣe-ṇāpi jīrṇatvaṃ mā bhūd iti | yathāha nāradaḥ |
pratyakṣaparibhogāc ca svāmino dvidaśāḥ samāḥ |
ādhyādīny api jīryeyuḥ strīnarendradhanād ṛte || iti | [NSm 1.74] evaṃ ca viṃśativarṣāt bāladhanaṃ jīryaty eva | saṃnidhāv ity anenaiva pareṣām arthasiddhatvāt paragrahaṇaṃ saṃbandhibhuktasyānaticārajñāpanā-rtham | saṃnidhigrahaṇād anyagrāmasthasya na doṣaḥ || 12.34 ||
jaḍa unmattaḥ paugaṇḍo vyākṛtavyavahāraḥ | yo jaḍo na bhavati paugaṇḍo vā na bhavati tasya dhanaṃ parais tatsaṃnidhāv eva ced daśa varṣāṇi bhuktaṃ bhavati tadā tad dhanaṃ bhoktur eva svam iti niścīyate | sa eva bhogaḥ svāminaḥ sakāśād dānādirūpeṇa tasya dhanasya nirgataṃ sūcayati | katham aparathaitāvantaṃ kālam evam artham aparaloke tūṣṇīm āsīteti | atra kṣetraviṣaye yājñavalkyaḥ |
paśyato ‘bruvato bhūmer hānir viṃśativārṣikī |
pareṇa bhujyamānāyā dhanasya daśavārṣikī || [YDh 2.24] iti |
paśyann anyasya dadataḥ kṣitiṃ yo na nivārayet |
svāmī satāpi lekhena na sa tal labdhum arhati ||
iti bṛhaspatiḥ | atra manuḥ |
yat kiṃcid daśa varṣāṇi saṃnidhau prekṣate dhanī |
bhujyamānaṃ parais tūṣṇīṃ na sa tal labdhum arhati || iti | [MDh 8.147] “anāgamaṃ tu yo bhuṅkte” ityādi tv asaṃnidhiviṣayāṇi jaḍādiviṣayāṇi vā || 12.34 ||
asyāpavādaṃ bhoktṛviśeṣeṇāha ||
asyāpavādaḥ |
na śrotriyapravrajitarājanyapuruṣaiḥ || 12.35 ||
śrotriyādibhir bhujyamānam api nāpahriyate, upekṣākāraṇopapatteḥ śrotriyapravrajitau dharmatṛṣṇayopekṣyete, rājapuruṣo bhayeneti | pravrajitasya katham upapadyata iti ced, atrocyate – kasyacid gṛhasya śūnyatvena sthitasya pravrajitasya śūnyagṛhavāsanopadeśād upapadyata eveti || 12.35 ||
śrotriyādibhir bhujyamānaṃ na bhogamātrāt teṣāṃ bhavati, upekṣākāraṇatvopapatteḥ | śrotriyapravrajitayor dharmatṛṣṇayopekṣeti | rājapuruṣasya tu bhayena | rājapuruṣagrahaṇaṃ sarveṣāṃ balavatām upalakṣaṇam | etena sāhasikā vyākhyātāḥ | aparigrahasyāpi pravrajitasya svasvāmike śūnyagrahādāv upabhogaḥ saṃbhavati || 12.35 ||
adhunā bhujyamānadravyaviśeṣeṇāpavādam āha |
paśubhūmistrīṇām anatibhogaḥ || 12.36 ||
paśuśabdena sarve catuṣpādā yajñiyā gṛhyante, “catuṣpādā vai paśavo yajñiyāḥ” iti śrutidarśanāt | bhūmiḥ kṣetrārāmādiḥ, na gṛham, “daśavarṣād veśmano bhoktur na bhūmiḥ” ity auśanasavacanāt | striyaḥ paricārikāḥ | eṣām anatibhogaḥ, daśavarśānubhavena na svāmyaṃ bhavatīty arthaḥ | tathā ca nāradaḥ |
anāgamaṃ tu yad bhuktaṃ vatsarāṇāṃ śatair api | iti | [NSm 1.75] idam api vacanaṃ paśubhūmistrīviṣayatvena vyākhyātam iti || 12.36 ||
paśavaś catuṣpādaḥ | bhūmiḥ kṣetrārāmādikā | striyaḥ paricārikā dāsyaḥ | paśvādīnāṃ satve  (svatve) nātibhogo ‘pekṣitaḥ | alpenāpi bhogena bhoktuḥ svaṃ bhavati | katham anantaragṛhe dṛśyamānāṃ gāṃ svayaṃ takrādi krītvopayuñjāna upekṣeta, kathaṃ vā bahuphalam ārāmam, kathaṃ vā dāsīṃ yauvanasthām anvahaṃ paricārikām || 12.36 ||
rikthabhāja ṛṇaṃ pratikuryuḥ || 12.37 ||
yasya dhanaṃ ye bhajanti gṛhṇanti ta eva tadṛṇaṃ dadyur ity arthaḥ, na dāyādamātreṇa | putrastrīhāriṇām api grahaṇārthaṃ bahuvacanam | tathāha nāradaḥ |
dhanastrīhāriputrāṇām ṛṇabhāg yo dhanaṃ haret |
putro ‘satoḥ strīdhaninoḥ strīhārī dhanaputrayoḥ || iti | [NSm 1.20] evaṃ ca rikthabhāktve saty asati ca putreṇāvaśyaṃ dātavyaṃ strīhāriṇā ca anyais tu rikthabhāgbhir eva | putrāṇāṃ ca madhye yeṣāṃ rikthabhāktvaṃ ca vidyate ta eva ṛṇaṃ dadyuḥ na putramātreṇeti draṣṭavyam || 12.37 ||
ye yasya rakthabhājas te tadṛṇaṃ pratidadyuḥ | putrapautrais tu rikthābhāve ‘pi deyam | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
ṛṇam ātmīyavat pitryaṃ putrair deyaṃ vibhāvitam |
paitāmahaṃ samaṃ deyaṃ na deyaṃ tatsutasya tat || iti |
nāradaḥ |
kramād abhyāgataṃ prāptaṃ putrair yan narṇam uddhṛtam |
dadyuḥ paitāmahaṃ pautrās tac caturthān nivartate || [NSm 1.4] | yājñavalkyaḥ |
pitari proṣite prete vyasanābhiplute ‘pi ca |
putrapautrair ṛṇaṃ deyaṃ nihnave sākṣibhāvitam || [YDh 2.50] iti || 12.37 ||
idānīṃ putraṃ praty apavādam āha |
prātibhāvyavaṇikśulkamadyadyūtadaṇḍā na putrān adhyābhaveyuḥ || 12.38 ||
prātibhāvyavaṇikśulkamadyadyūtadāṇḍāḥ putrān nābhyābhaveyuḥ || 12.38 ||
pratibhuvas trayaḥ | yathāha vyāghraḥ |
pādadarśī ca viśvāsyo hy arthadaś ceti kīrtitāḥ | iti |
atra dānapratibhuvaṃ muktvā itarayor grahaṇam | kutaḥ?
dānapribhuvi prete dāyādān api dāpayet |
[MDh 8.161] iti manuvacanāt | pratibhūnimittaṃ yad ṛṇaṃ tat prātibhāvyam | vaṇig vāṇijyanimittaṃ cety arthaḥ | yathā mūlyaṃ dadāmīti paribhāṣya kasyacit sakāśe dravyaṃ gṛhītvā vāṇijyakaraṇārthaṃ deśāntaraṃ gatvā mriyeta, putreṇa tad aprāptaṃ ca bhavati tatas tad ṛṇaṃ putrasyopari na bhavatīty abhiprāyaḥ | śulkam āsurādivivāhe śulkaṃ pratiśrutya vivāhaṃ kṛtvā yady asau mriyate tac chulkaṃ putrasopari na bhavatīti | madyaṃ madyanimittaṃ brāhmaṇasya pratiṣiddhatvāt kṣatriyādiviṣayam etad draṣṭavyam | dravyaṃ dadāmīti madyapāne kṛte dravyam adatvā ‘sau mriyeta tad api putrasya na bhavatīti | tathā dyūtanimittaṃ ca ṛṇam | tathā daṇḍanimittaṃ ca vyavahārāpajayanimittam | naite putrān adhyābhaveyuḥ putrāṇām upari na bhavatīty arthaḥ | rikthabhāktve sati draṣṭavyam || 12.38 ||
atra nāradaḥ |
upasthānāya dānāya pratyayāya tathaiva hi |
trividhaḥ pratibhūr dṛṣṭas triṣv evārtheṣu sūribhiḥ || iti || [NSm 1.104]
tasya pratibhuvi prete dāyādān api dāpayet || iti || [MDh 8.160] | viṣṇu-yājñavalkyau |
darśane pratyaye dāne prātibhāvyaṃ vidhīyate |
ādye tu vitathe dāpyāv itarasya sutā api || iti | [YDh 2.53; ViDh 6.41] | tasmād idam api dānapratibhūvyatiriktaviṣayaṃ draṣṭavyam |
aham enaṃ darśayiṣyāmīti prātibhāvyaṃ tam darśayitvā pitari prete na tatputreṇāsau darśayitavya iti | vaṇig vāṇijyārtham upāttaṃ dravyaṃ tad api na putrān abhyābhavati | yadā salābhamūlaṃ dāsyāmīti paribhāṣya kasyacit sakāśād dravyaṃ gṛhītvā vāṇijyāya deśāntaraṃ gato mriyeta tadā tatputreṇa na tat pratikartavyam iti | tathā śulkaṃ pratiśrutya vivāhaṃ kṛtvā mṛte tatputraṃ na tacchuklam abhyābhavati | tathā mūlaṃ dāsyāmīti madyaṃ bahu pītvā mṛte na tatputreṇa tad dātavyam | tathā dyūtaṃ kṛtvā parājitas tatpaṇadravyam adatvaiva yadi mriyate tatputro na dātum arhati | yathā (tathā) vyavahāre parājito rājñe daṇḍam adatvaiva yadi mriyate tadā na so ‘pi daṇḍaḥ putrān abhyābhavati || 12.38 ||
nidhyanvādhiyācitāvakrītādhayo naṣṭāḥ sarvān aninditān apuruṣāparādhena || 12.39 ||
nidhiḥ nikṣepaḥ, anvādhir upanidhiḥ, yācitam utsavādiṣu yācñayā ānīta ābharaṇādi, avakrītam adattamūlyam ardhadattamūlyaṃ vā, ādhir gopyādhiḥ kāṃsyādir ity arthaḥ, ete coraharaṇāgnidāhādinā naṣṭā santo na kevalaṃ putrān eva adhyābhaveyuḥ | kiṃ tarhi? yeṣāṃ sakāśe nikṣepādayaḥ kṛtāḥ tān api, sarvagrahaṇāt | aninditān apramādakāriṇa ity arthaḥ | apuruṣāparādhena yadi naśyanti | puruṣāparādhaḥ puruṣapramādaḥ ālasyādiḥ | evaṃ ca ninditaiḥ pramādakāribhiḥ te dātavyā eva || 12.39 ||
nidhir nikṣepaḥ |
svaṃ dravyaṃ yatra visrambhān nikṣipaty aviśaṅkitaḥ | sa nikṣepaḥ | anvādhir upanidhiḥ | aupanidhikam iti smṛtyantare prasiddham | tatra yājñavalkyaḥ |
bhājanastham anākhyāya haste nyasya yad arpayet |
dravyaṃ tad aupanidhikaṃ pratideyaṃ tathaiva tat || iti | [YDh 2.65] | yācitam utsavādiṣv ābharaṇādi | avakrītam adattamaulyam ardhadattamaulyaṃ vā | ādhir gopyādhiḥ | ete nidhyādayo yadi puruṣāparā-dhena vinā naṣṭā bhavanti corādibhir apahṛtāḥ [vā] sarvāṃs tān aninditān āhur adoṣān āhuḥ | na kevalaṃ putrān eva nābhyābhaveyuḥ kiṃ tarhi yeṣāṃ sakāśe nidhyādayaḥ kṛtās tān api nābhyābhavanti | aninditeti te yadi pūrvaṃ dṛṣṭadoṣā bhavanti tadā pūrvam idam | puruṣāparādhas tu yadi dhārayitāraḥ svadravyavan na rakṣayeyuḥ, yady agnibhayādau svadravyaṃ gṛhītvā nidhyādy upekṣeran svadravyaṃ vā guptaṃ nidhāya bahir nidhyādi sthāpayeyuḥ | etasmin puruṣāparādhe sati dadyur eva || 12.39 ||
yadi caiṣāṃ putrādīnāṃ pitryam ṛṇaṃ na pratikuryāt kaścit, tatah steno bhavatīti, asya “aṣṭāpādyaṃ steyakilbiṣam” [GDh 12.12] iti daṇḍaś ca vihita ity anenaiva prasaṅgena hiraṇyastenasyāpi daṇḍa ucyate |
stenaḥ prakīrṇakeśo musalī rājānam īyāt karmācakṣāṇaḥ || 12.40 ||
H reads “iyāt”
stenaḥ suvarṇastenaḥ, anyadravyāpahāriṇas tu “aṣṭāpāyam” iti uktatvāt, smṛtyantradarśanāc ca |
suvarṇastenakṛd vipro rājānam abhigamya tu |
svakarma khyāpayan brūyāt māṃ bhavān anuśāstv iti || [MDh 11.99]
daṇḍaprakaraṇe gamanopadeśaḥ prāyaścittārthaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
rājabhir dhṛtadaṇḍas tu kṛtvā pāpāni mānavāḥ |
nirmalāḥ svargam āyānti santaḥ sukṛtino yathā || iti | [MDh 8.318] | steye kṛte steno bhavati | steyaḥ parasvāpahāraḥ | buddhipūrvaviṣaya evedam | abuddhi-pūrvakasteyaṃ nopapadyata iti | mahaty apahṛte tv etad draṣṭavyam, viśeṣānārambhāt | prakīrṇakeśo muktakeśo mṛtasamam ātmānaṃ manyamānaḥ | musalī svavadhasādhanamusalahasto rājānaṃ ca gacchet, karma kathayan steno ‘ham asmīti || 12.40 ||
stenaḥ suvarṇasteyakṛt |
suvarṇasteyakṛd vipro rājānam abhigamya tu |
svakarma khyāpayan brūyān māṃ bhavān anuśāstv iti || [MDh 11.99] iti mānavam | prakīrṇakeśo muktakeśaḥ | āyasaḥ khādiro vā musala iti smṛtyantaram | tadvān | “aṃse musalam ādhāya” [ĀpDh 1.25.4] ity āpastambaḥ | rājānam iyāt karmācakṣāṇaḥ | “evaṃkarmāsmi praśādhi mām” iti bruvāṇaḥ || 12.40 ||
tataḥ evam upasthitaḥ |
pūto vadhamokṣābhyām || 12.41 ||
pūtaḥ śuddho bhavatīti | vadhān māraṇād vā mokṣād vā | vadhas tāḍanam, tad api sakṛd eva, smṛtyantara-darśanāt |
gṛhītvā musalaṃ rājā sakṛd dhanyāt tu taṃ svayam | iti | [MDh 11.100] tatra rājñā yadi niḥśaṅkena tāḍito na mriyeta tato ‘pi pūta eva bhavatīti draṣṭavyam | abrāhmaṇaviṣayaṃ cedaṃ tāḍanam – “na śārīro brāhmaṇadaṇḍaḥ” [GDh 12.43] iti vakṣyamāṇatvāt | tathā ca manuḥ |
vadhena śudhyati steno brāhmaṇas tapasaiva vā | iti | [MDh 11.100] atraivakārāt brāhmaṇas tapasaiveti bhāṣyakṛtā vyākhyātam | tataś ca abrāhmaṇasya tāḍanam, brāhmaṇasya mokṣa iti draṣṭavyam | tathāpi brāhmaṇasya svayaṃ gamanapakṣe mokṣaḥ | yas tu balād ānīyate tasya mokṣaḥ tapaś ca draṣṭavyam | evaṃ sarvatra svayaṃ gamanapakṣe daṇḍaś ca prāyaścittaṃ ceti draṣṭavyam | tapas tv aśrotriyabrāhmaṇasya sāntapanaṃ draṣṭvayam, aśrotriyabrāhmaṇasya suvarṇaharaṇaṃ cet | tathāha manuḥ |
caret sāntapanaṃ kṛcchraṃ niryāty ātmaviśuddhaye | iti | [MDh 11.124] | niryāty eti sarvasteyaśeṣatvenānvetīti draṣṭavyam | śrotriyasya āpastamboktaṃ draṣṭavyam: “steyaṃ kṛtvā surāṃ pītvā gurudāraṃ ca gatvā brahmahatyām akṛtvā caturthakālāmitabhojinaḥ syuḥ” [ĀpDh 1.25.9–10] ityādi | kathaṃ śrotriyasya gauravam iti cet, “viduṣo ‘tikrame daṇḍabhūyastvam” [GDh 12.14] iti uktatvāt | śrotriyabrāhmaṇasuvarṇaharaṇe tv aśrotriyasya caitad eva śrotriyasya ṣaḍvarṣaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha uśanā: “suvarṇasteyakṛt ṣaḍvarṣaṃ brāhmaṇo vrataṃ caret” iti | kṣatriyasyāpi yadā prāyaścittaṃ samuccīyate tadā aśrotriyasuvarṇaharaṇe śrotriyasya bhārgavīyaṃ draṣṭavyam |
brāhmaṇasya hared yas tu carec cāndrāyaṇādikam | iti | aśrotriyasya pūrvam uktam auśanasaṃ draṣṭavyam | śrotriyasvaharaṇe tv etad eva dviguṇam “śrotriyasvaharaṇe dviguṇam” iti kaṇvavacanāt | vaiśyasyāpi kṣatriyavad draṣṭavyam – “vaiśyasya kṣatriyavac coradaṇḍaḥ” ity auśanasavacanāt | śrotriyasvaharaṇe –
tapasāpanunutsus tu suvarṇasteyajaṃ malam |
cīravāsā dvijo ‘raṇye caret brahmahaṇo vratam || [MDh 11.101] iti manunokta vā draṣṭavyam | śūdrasya prāyaścittaṃ sarvatra “niṣkālako vā ghṛtākto gomayāgninā pādaprabhṛty ātmānam avadāhayet” [VaDh 20.42] iti vasiṣṭhokaṃ draṣṭavyam | apahṛtadravyavaśād api prāyaścittasya gurulaghubhāvaḥ kalpyaḥ | kutaḥ?
tathā parimeyānāṃ śatād abhyadhikaṃ vadhaḥ |
iti liṅgāt | kārṣāpaṇamūlyād adhika eva rājñā tāḍanam | itaratra sarvatra tu mokṣa eva draṣṭavyaḥ | kārṣāpaṇamūlyād adhika eva samastaprāyaścitta-praveśaḥ | “ardhe ardhaṃ pāde pādam” iti ca draṣṭavyam | śūdrasya ca vaiśyavat kalpyam, “anyūnabhāve vaiśyavac chūdrasya kalpyam” iti śaṅkhavacanād iti || 12.41 ||
vadhas tāḍanaṃ maraṇāntikam | “tenaiva hanyāt” [ĀpDh 1.25.4] ity āpastambaḥ | sakṛd eva tāḍanam,
gṛhītvā musalaṃ rājā sakṛd dhanyāt tu taṃ svayam | [MDh 11.100] iti smaraṇāt | mokṣo mocanam | “punar evaṃvidhaṃ mā kārṣīr gaccha” iti | tābhyāṃ ca vadhamokṣābhyāṃ stenaḥ pūto bhavati | hato ‘pi śudhyati mukto ‘pi śudhyatīti || 12.41 ||
aghnannn enasvī rājā || 12.42 ||
dayādiyogād aghnan enasvī rājā bhavati | corasya yāvat pāpaṃ tāvad asya bhavatīty arthaḥ | tathā ca smṛtyantaram: “stenasyāpnoti kilbiṣam” [NSm 19.56] iti | | yathā vadhārhasyāvadhe doṣa evaṃ mokṣārhasyāmokṣe doṣo draṣṭavyaḥ | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “eno rājānam ṛcchaty utsṛjantaṃ sakilbiṣam” [VaDh 19.46] iti || 12.42 ||
yadi dayādinā taṃ na hanyād rājā svayam enasvī bhavati | corasya yad enas tad asya bhavatīti || 12.42 ||
ayaṃ tu daṇḍo brāhmaṇavarjam iti darśayati |
na śārīro brāhmaṇadaṇḍaḥ || 12.43 ||
svayam upasthitasyāpi yaḥ śārīro daṇḍaḥ vadhaḥ sa brāhmaṇasya nāsti | tasmān na kartavyaḥ |
nanu ca karmaviyogādidaṇḍāntaravidhānād eva śarīrasya nivṛttir bhavaty eveti |
ucyate – satyam | evamārambhas tu anyatrāparādhe brāhmaṇasya śarīradaṇḍapratiṣedhārtha iti || 12.43 ||
svayam upasthitasyāpi brāhmaṇasya śārīro daṇḍo na kartavyo mokṣa eva | tathā ca manuḥ |
vadhena śudhyati steno brāhmaṇas tapasaiva ca || iti | [MDh 11.100]
atraivakārabalāt tadānīṃ tasyāpi brāhmaṇasya tapasā mokṣaḥ | na kvāpi nimitte hastacchedādikam api kartavyam ity evamarthaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
triṣu varṇeṣu tāni syur akṣato brāhmaṇo vrajet || iti | [MDh 8.124] tapasvibrāhmaṇaviṣayam idam || 12.43 ||
tarhi katham asau śāsya ity ata āha |
anyasya tu yathāparādhaṃ daṇḍam āha |
karmaviyogavikhyāpanavivāsanāṅkakaraṇāni || 12.44 ||
yathā punas tat karma na karoti tathā kartavyam ity arthaḥ | vikhyāpanaṃ cauryacihnena sarvataḥ paribhramaṇakaraṇam | vivāsanaṃ nirviṣayīkaraṇam | aṅkanaṃ cihnakaraṇam | yathāha manuḥ |
gurutalpe bhaga kāryaḥ surāpāne surādhvajaḥ |
steye tu śvapadaṃ kāryo brahmaghnas tv aśirāḥ pumān || iti | [MDh 9.237] eṣām abhyāsāpekṣayā yathārho daṇḍo draṣṭavyaḥ || 12.44 ||
yathā punas tat karma na karoti tathā karaṇaṃ karmaviyogaḥ | sarvasvaharaṇaṃ pratibhūgrahaṇam ityādi | vikhyāpanaṃ cauryacihnena grāmanagarādiṣv āghoṣaṇam | vivāsanaṃ nirvāsanam | yathāparādhaṃ grāmanagarād rāṣṭrād vā | aṅkakaraṇaṃ cihnakaraṇam |
tatra manuḥ |
gurutalpe bhagaḥ kāryaḥ surāpāne surādhvajaḥ |
steye tu śvapadaṃ kāryaṃ brahmahaṇy aśirāḥ pumān || iti | [MDh 9.237] eṣāṃ karmaviyogādīnām enaḥsu guruṣu gurūṇi laghuṣu laghūnīti nyāyenāparādhānurūpā vyavasthā | etan mahāpātaka-viṣayam | aṅkakaraṇaṃ tu tapasvibrāhmaṇasyāpi bhavaty eva || 12.44 ||
avṛttau prāyaścittī saḥ || 12.45 ||
H reads “apravṛttau”
acore evaṃ kṛte sa rājā prāyaścittī bhavati | prāyaścittaṃ tu smṛtyantarād draṣṭavyam | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “daṇḍyotsarge rājaikarātram upavaset trirātraṃ purohitaḥ kṛcchram adaṇḍadaṇḍane purohitas trirātraṃ rājā ca” [VaDh 19.40–43] iti || 12.45 ||
yas tu rājā coraviṣayeṣv evaṃdaṇḍako na vartate tasyām apravṛttau svayaṃ prāyaścittī bhavati | tatra vasiṣṭhaḥ: “daṇḍyotsarge rājāikarātram upavaset trirātraṃ purohitaḥ | kṛcchram adaṇḍyadaṇḍane purohita ekarātraṃ trirātraṃ rājā” iti [VaDh 40–43] || 12.45 ||
corasamaḥ sacivo matipūrve || 12.46 ||
corasya sacivaḥ sahāyaḥ buddhipūrve pratiśayānnādidāne coravan nigrāhyaḥ | evaṃ cābuddhipūrve na doṣaḥ || 12.46 ||
sācivyaṃ pratiśrayāśanadānādi sāhāyyam | tac cen matipūrvaṃ coro ‘yam iti jñātvāpi yadi sācivyaṃ karoti sa corasamaś coravad daṇḍyaḥ | ajñāte punar ajñānam eva śaraṇam || 12.46 ||
pratigrahītāpy adharmasaṃyukte || 12.47 ||
matipūrva ity vartate | pratigrahītāpy adharmasaṃyukte, stenasya prakṛtatvād eva siddhe adharmasaṃyuktagrahaṇaṃ yo yat pāpaṃ karoti tasya sakāśāt matipūrvaṃ pratigrahītur api avakīrṇiprāyaścittam ityādi | abuddhipūrve tu laghutaraṃ kalpyam || 12.47 ||
apiśabdān matipūrva ity anuvartate | yo ‘nyasya dravyam anena coritam iti jānann eva tataḥ pratigṛhṇāti so ‘pi tasminn adharmasaṃyukte pratigrahe corasamaḥ | prakaraṇād eva siddhe ‘dharmasaṃyuktagrahaṇam anyatrāpi pāpaviṣaye pratigrahītus tat tat pāpaṃ bhavatīti jñāpanārtham || 12.47 ||
puruṣaśaktyaparādhānubandhavijñānād daṇḍaniyogaḥ || 12.48 ||
puruṣavijñānāj jātyādi jñātvety arthaḥ | śaktir durbalatvādiḥ | aparādhaḥ sāhasabhāṣaṇādi | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “steno ‘nupraveśān na duṣyate | śastradhārī sahoḍho vraṇasaṃpannaś ca vyapadiṣṭas tv ekeṣām” [VaDh 19.38–39] iti | anubandhaḥ adhyavasāyaḥ | etān avadhārya daṇḍadhāraṇaṃ kartavyam || 12.48 ||
puruṣo brāhmaṇādijātiḥ | śaktir arthadaṇḍe bahvartho ‘lpārtha iti, śarīradaṇḍe durbalaḥ prabalo veti cintā | aparādhaḥ sākṣāt kartṛtvaṃ sācivyakartṛtvaṃ veti | anubandho ‘abhyāsaḥ | etān puruṣādīn vijñāya tadanurūpo daṇḍo niyoktavya iti || 12.48 ||
anujñānaṃ vā vedavitsamavāyavacanād vedavitsamavāyavacanāt || 12.49 ||
vedavidāṃ saṅghavacanān mokṣo vā kartavyaḥ | saṅghaḥ samavāyaḥ caturṇāṃ trayāṇāṃ vā | yathāha manuḥ |
catvāro vā trayo vāpi ya brūyur vedapāragāḥ |
sa dharma iti vijñeyo netareṣāṃ sahasraśaḥ || iti |
vakṣyamāṇadaśāvarapariṣadanujñayā vā | evaṃ ca svayaṃkaraṇe doṣo draṣṭavyaḥ || 12.49 ||
vedavidāṃ trayāṇāṃ caturṇāṃ vā samavāyaḥ saṃghaḥ | atra manuḥ |
catvāro vā trayo vāpi yaṃ brūyur vedaparagāḥ |
sa dharma iti vijñeyo netareṣāṃ sahasraśaḥ || iti |
tasya saṃghasya vacanād anujñānaṃ vā kartavyam | anuparodho dharmo vacanīya iti yadi te brūyus tadā vaktavyam anujānāmi tvāṃ gaccha yatheṣṭam iti | [abhyāso ‘dhyāyasamāptyarthaḥ] || 12.49 ||
atha trayodaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
vākpāruṣye sapratipattau daṇḍa uktaḥ | yadā tu vipratipadyante tadā katham ity ata āha |
vipratipattau sākṣinimittā satyavyavasthā || 13.1 ||
vipratipattir nānāvidhā pratipattiḥ arthipratyarthinoḥ | tasyāṃ satyāṃ sākṣihetukā satyavyavasthā satyasthāpanā kartavyā | sākṣiṇaḥ pañcaprakārāḥ, yathāha nāradaḥ |
likhitaḥ smāritaś caiva yadṛcchābhijña eva ca |
gūḍhaś cottamasākṣī ca sākṣī pañcavidhaḥ smṛtaḥ || iti | [NSm 1.130] tān vicārya tattvāvabodha ity arthaḥ | sākṣinimittaṃ satyam iti vaktavye vyavasthāgrahaṇaṃ sākṣiṇāṃ vipratipattau bahūnāṃ vacanaṃ pramāṇam | tatrāpi saṃdehe tair eva śapathapūrvaṃ pratipāditam ity evamartham || 13.1 ||
vipratipattau sākṣiṇaḥ praṣṭavyāḥ | tair yathoktaṃ tathā satyaṃ vyavasthāpyam | atra nāradaḥ |
ekādaśavidhaḥ sākṣī śāstre dṛṣṭo manīṣibhiḥ |
kṛtaḥ pañcavidhas teṣāṃ ṣaḍvidho ‘kṛta ucyate ||
likhitaḥ smāritaś caiva yadṛcchābhijña eva ca |
gūḍhaś cottarasākṣī ca sākṣī pañcavidhaḥ kṛtaḥ ||
anye punar anuddiṣṭāḥ sākṣiṇaḥ samudāhṛtāḥ |
grāmaś ca prāḍvivākaś ca rājā ca vyavahāriṇām ||
kāryeṣv abhyantaro yaś ca arthinā prahitaś ca yaḥ |
kulyākulyavivādeṣu bhaveyus te ‘pi sākṣiṇaḥ || [NSm 1.129–132] iti || 13.1 ||
te punaḥ kiyantaḥ kiṃlakṣaṇā ity ata āha |
te punaḥ kīdṛśāḥ kiyanto vety āha |
bahavaḥ syur aninditāḥ svakarmasu prātyayikā rājñām || 13.2 ||
bahavaḥ syur aninditāḥ svakarmasu prātyayikā rājñāṃ niṣprītyanabhitāpāś cānyatarasmin
|| 13.2 ||
bahavaḥ tryavarāḥ syur ity arthaḥ | yathāha manuḥ |
tryavaraiḥ sākṣibhir bhāvyaṃ nṛpabrāhmaṇa-saṃnidhau || iti | [MDh 8.61]
syurgrahaṇaṃ saṃdehavyudāsārtham | aninditāḥ svakarmasu vihitānuṣṭhānā ity arthaḥ | svagrahaṇaṃ dhanaputrādāv api | yathāha manuḥ: “gṛhiṇaḥ putriṇo maulyāḥ” [MDh 8.62] ityādi | karmasv iti bahuvacanam āpadvihitasyāpy upasaṃgrahārtham | prātyayikā rājñām, pratyayena ye caranti te prātyayikāḥ, rājñāṃ bahuṣu karmasu parīkṣitāḥ sarvatrāpramattā eveti niścitā ity arthaḥ | bahuvacanād rājaniyuktānāṃ sabhāsadādīnām api || 13.2 ||
varṇaprayuktāny āśramaprayuktāny ubhayaprayuktāni svāni karmāṇi śrautāni smārtāni ca | teṣv aninditā akaraṇād anyathākaraṇād vā | atha yājñāvalkyaḥ |
tryavarāḥ sākṣino jñeyāḥ śrautasmārtakriyāparāḥ || iti | [YDh 2.69]
pratyayo viśvāsas tena ye caranti te prātyayikāḥ | ya evaṃbhūtā[s te] rājñām adṛṣṭadoṣatayā viśvasanīyāḥ | arthipratyarthinor anyatarasmin niṣprītayo niḥsnehā anabhitāpā akṛtadveṣāḥ | evaṃbhūtā bahavaḥ sākṣiṇaḥ syuḥ | atra yājñavalkyaḥ |
ubhayānumataḥ sākṣī bhavaty eko ‘pi dharmavit || iti | [YDh 2.72]
abhyantaras tu nikṣepe sākṣym eko ‘pi vācyate |
arthinā prahitaḥ sākṣī bhavaty eko ‘pi yācitaḥ ||
iti kātyāyanaḥ |
pramāṇam eko ‘pi bhavet sāhaseṣu viśeṣataḥ |
iti vyāsaḥ || 13.2 ||
niṣprītyabhitāpāś cānyatarasmin || 13.3 ||
niṣprītayo nirabhitāpāś ca, na kṛtasnehā na śatravaś cety arthaḥ | cakārān nārthasaṃbandhinaś ca | tathā ca manuḥ |
nārthasaṃbandhino nāptā na sahāyā na vairiṇaḥ | [MDh 8.64] ityādi | anyatarasminn arthini pratyarthini vety arthaḥ || 13.3 ||
api śūdrāḥ || 13.4 || — || 13.3 ||
evaṃvidhāḥ śūdrā api | kiṃ punar dvijātaya iti | evaṃ ca guṇavaddvijādyabhāve evaṃvidhāḥ śūdrā api bhavantīti draṣṭavyam || 13.4 ||
śūdrā apy evaṃvidhāś cet sākṣiṇo bhaveyuḥ kiṃ punar dvijātaya iti | evaṃ ca guṇavaddvijātyabhāve śūdrā apy evaṃvidhā bhavantīti draṣṭavyam || 13.3 ||
brāhmaṇas tv abrāhmaṇavacanād anavarodhyo ‘nibaddhaś cet || 13.5 || — || 13.4 ||
abrāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyādiḥ | abrāhmaṇagrahaṇād eva siddhe brāhmaṇagrahaṇaṃ śrotriyaparigrahārtham | itaro grāhya eveti | tuśabdāt anye ‘pi liṅgino na grāhyā viśeṣeṇa brāhmaṇā iti | abrāhmaṇavacanād anavarodhyo na grāhyaḥ, asya vṛttāntasyāsau brāhmaṇaḥ sākṣīty abrāhmaṇenoke rājñāsau sākṣitvena na grāhya ity arthaḥ | anibaddhaś cet brāhmaṇaḥ patrake ‘nāropitaś cet | āropito bhavaty evety abhiprāyaḥ || 13.5 ||
brāhmaṇo nātra śrotriyaḥ | asya vṛttāntasyāsau brāhmaṇaḥ sākṣīty abrāhmaṇenokte rājñā sākṣitvena nāvarodhyo na nirbandhena grāhyaḥ, anibaddhaś cet | sa cel lekhanibaddho na bhavati | lekhyārūḍhas tu bhavaty eva sākṣī | nātra kaścid dhetur asti vacanam eva pramāṇam | atra nāradaḥ |
asākṣy api hi śāstreṣu dṛṣṭaḥ pañcavidho budhaiḥ |
vacanād doṣato bhedāt svayam ukter matāntarāt ||
śrotriyād yā vacanataḥ stenād yā doṣadarśanāt |
bhedād vipratipattiḥ syād vivāde yatra sākṣiṇām ||
svayamuktir anirdiṣṭaḥ svayam evaitya yo vadet |
mṛtāntaro ‘rthini prete mumūrṣuḥ śrāvitād ṛte || iti | [NSm 1.137–139] tad iha śrotriyaḥ kvacid api sākṣī na bhavatīti nāradasya pakṣaḥ | ihābrāhmaṇavacanād ity uktatvād brāhmaṇenoktaḥ śrotriyo ‘pi bhavaty eva sākṣī || 13.4 ||
nāsamavetā apṛṣṭāḥ prabrūyuḥ || 13.6 || — || 13.5 ||
samuditā yāvantas te samavetā api rājñā prāḍvivākena vā apṛṣṭāḥ na prabrūyuḥ | ā praśnāt tadvyāpāraṃ prati maunina eva bhaveyur iti praśabdopādānam || 13.6 ||
asamavetā asamuditā rājñā prāḍvivākena vāpṛṣṭāḥ santo na brūyuḥ | kiṃ tu samavetāḥ pṛṣṭāś ca prabrūyuḥ || 13.5 ||
kiṃ ca |
avacane ca doṣiṇaḥ syuḥ || 13.7 || — 13.6 ||
praśnakāle ‘py avacane cakārād anyathā vacane ca doṣiṇo daṇḍabhājaḥ syuḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
sabhā vā na praveṣṭavyā vaktavyaṃ vā samañjasam |
abruvan vibruvan vāpi naro bhavati kilbiṣī || iti | [MDh 8.13] tasmāt tathyam eva vaktavyam ity abhiprāyaḥ || 13.7 ||
te caivaṃbhūtā yadi jānanta eva na [brūyur anyathā vā] brūyus tadā doṣiṇo duṣṭāḥ syuḥ | iha rājñā daṇḍyāḥ paratra ca nārākiṇaḥ || 13.6 ||
na kevalaṃ tathyavacane daṇḍābhāvamātraṃ viparyaye daṇḍabhāktvam eva | kiṃ tarhi |
svargaḥ satyavacane viparyaye narakaḥ || 13.8 || — || 13.7 ||
satyavacananimittād dharmāt svargaś ca bhavati, vipar-yaye anṛtavacananimittād adharmān narakaś ca bhavati | vacanagrahaṇaṃ sākṣiṇām etat phalam, na tu vyavasthāhetor nṛpasyeti || 13.8 ||
bruvantas tu yadi satyaṃ bruvanti tadā svargo bhavati | viparyaye ‘satyavacane narako bhavatīti || 13.7 ||
idānīṃ yatra pañcaprakārā api sākṣiṇo na bhavanti tatra katham ity ata āha |
anibaddhair api vaktavyam || 13.9 || — || 13.8 ||
nibaddhāḥ niścayena baddhāḥ smṛtāḥ, pañcavidhatvena smṛtāḥ ity arthaḥ | ye pañcavidhatvena na smaryante te anibaddhāḥ, yena kenacit prakāreṇa tadvyāpārajñā tadgrāmasthā ity arthaḥ | tair api vaktavyam | apiśabdo nibaddhābhāvasūcanārthaḥ | anibaddhā apīty ukte ‘pi sākṣiprakaraṇatvāt sākṣiṇaḥ syur iti sāmarthya-labdhatvād eva samastārthe labhyamāne vaktavya-grahaṇam – tatrāpi yeṣāṃ vacanaṃ pramāṇatvena nirdiṣṭhaṃ teṣām eva vacanaṃ grāhyam, nārthasaṃ-bandhināṃ vacanam, tataś cārthasaṃbandhādi-doṣarahitair eva vaktavyam iti || 13.9 ||
nibaddhā nirdiṣṭā yūyam atra sākṣiṇa iti | tadviparītā anibaddhās tair api sākṣyaṃ vaktavyam | te ca nāradena “anye punar anirdiṣṭāḥ” ity ārabhya kathitā draṣṭavyaḥ || 13.8 ||
na pīḍākṛte nibandhaḥ || 13.10 || — || 13.9 ||
atra bandhaśabdena vyavasthā ucyate | niścayaṃ bandho nibandhaḥ | pīḍākṛte hiṃsādau | na nibandhaḥ na pratiṣedho ‘stīty arthaḥ | pañcaprakārair anyaiś ca arthasaṃbandhādidūṣaṇayuktair api vaktavyam evety abhiprāyaḥ | tathā ca uśanā: “sāhase sarve bhavanti” iti || 13.10 ||
pīḍākṛtaṃ pīḍākaraṇam | nibandho nibandhanam arthasaṃbandhādi | pīḍākaraṇe hiṃsāviṣaye sākṣiṇāṃ nibandho na nirūpyaḥ | arthasaṃbandhādi na kiṃcid api dūṣaṇaṃ bhavati | āha vyāghraḥ |
steye ca sāhase caiva saṃsarge ca striyās tathā |
garādīnāṃ prayoge ca na doṣaḥ sākṣiṣu smṛtaḥ || iti || 13.9 ||
tathā |
pramattokte ca || 13.11 || — || 13.10 ||
pramattaḥ ajñātaḥ, vañcanayā vā, praśabdopādānāt | yadā nigūḍhāḥ sākṣiṇaḥ kṛtvā upāyair enaṃ rahasy āpādayanti tataḥ tatsthāḥ arthasaṃbandhādi-dūṣaṇayuktā api sākṣiṇo bhavantīti | pramattenokte paribhāṣita ity arthaḥ | cakārāt strīgamanavyāpāre ‘pi | tathā ca vyāghraḥ |
steye ca sāhase caiva saṃsarge ca striyas tathā |
garādīnāṃ prayoge ca na doṣaḥ sākṣiṣu smṛtaḥ || iti |
viparyaye naraka uktaḥ | saḥ na kevalaṃ teṣām eva
|| 13.11 ||
pramādo ‘navadhānam | antye pare vākye sākṣiṇā yadṛcchayā yad uktaṃ tatrāpi nibandho na bhavati | arthasaṃbandhādidūṣaṇaṃ na bhavati || 13.10 ||
kiṃ tarhi |
viparyaye naraka uktaḥ | na sa kevalaṃ sākṣiṇa eva, kiṃ tarhi |
sākṣisabhyarājakartṛṣu doṣo dharmatantrapīḍāyām || 13.12 || — || 13.11 ||
yatra dharmatantrapīḍā kriyate samyagvyavahāradarśanāt tatra yasyaiva [yathaiva] doṣeṇa kartā lipyate, evaṃ sākṣyādayo ‘pi |
nanu ca sākṣiṇo doṣa ukta eva | kim arthaṃ punar ihābhidhānam iti |
ucyate – yadīha sākṣigrahaṇaṃ na kriyate tataḥ sabhyādīnām asākṣika eva vyavahāre doṣa uktaḥ syāt sasākṣike ‘pi doṣaprāpaṇārtham | yāvatā doṣeṇa kartā saṃbadhyate tāvān doṣaḥ sabhyādīnām api svāparādhāt pṛthak pṛthag upapadyate na tu khaṇḍaśa iti draṣṭavyam | tatraśabdena lokavyavahāra ucyate | tataś ca tatpīḍā ca na kartavyeti tadgrahaṇam || 13.12 ||
tantraṃ lokavyavahāraḥ | dharmatantrayoḥ pīḍāyāṃ satyāṃ sākṣiṣu sabhyeṣu rājani kartari ca sarveṣu doṣo bhavati | kartṛgrahaṇaṃ dṛṣṭāntārtham | yāvān kartur doṣas tāvān sākṣyādīnām apīti | yady api sākṣiṇaḥ pūrvaṃ doṣa uktas tathāpīha grahaṇaṃ sabhyādīnāṃ sasākṣike ‘pi doṣagrahaṇārtham | anyathāsākṣikavyavahāre sabhyādīnāṃ doṣaḥ | sasākṣike tu sākṣiṇām evety uktaṃ syāt || 13.11 ||
śapathenaike satyakarma || 13.13 || — || 13.12 ||
yadā tu sākṣyādipramāṇāntarapratyastamayaḥ tadā śapathena kośādinā eke satyakarma satyavyavasthā-panam icchanti | śapathaś ca prayujyamānasya, itareṇa vyavasitatvāt | satyam iti vaktavye karmagrahaṇaṃ vyavahārād anyatrāpi laukikavyavahārasaṃdehe śapathasyānupraveśārtham | tathā ca śrutiḥ: “anyām udīya yadi yātudhāno asmi” ityādi | gautamas tu nyāyenaiva | tatra pramāṇaleśe sati sapatham, asati nyāya eveti draṣṭavyam || 13.13 ||
yatra sākṣiṣu tathā viśvāso na bhavati tatra śapathena satyakarma śapathaṃ kārayitvā satyaṃ vācanīyam ity eke manyante || 13.12 ||
tad devarājabrāhmaṇasaṃsadi syād abrāhmaṇānām || 13.14 || — || 13.13 ||
tac chapathaṃ devasaṃsadi devasaṇnidhau | devaśabdena devatāpratimāḥ ādityādayo vā ucyante | rājā vyavahāradarśī | brāhmaṇāḥ sabhyādayaḥ | tadgrahaṇaṃ laukikavyavahāranivṛttyartham | evaṃ ca vyavahāre devatādisaṃnidhau, laukike yatra kutracid iti draṣṭavyam | arthāntaranimitto vyavahāraḥ, aparādhanimitto laukika iti | abrāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriyādayaḥ | syādgrahaṇaṃ kramanivṛttyartham | tatra devatālābhe viprasaṃsadi, tadalābhe rājasaṃsadi draṣṭavyam | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
abrāhmaṇān nṛpaḥ samyak kārayed devasaṃnidhau |
śapathaṃ tadalābhe ca brāhmaṇasya samīpataḥ ||
tadalābhe nṛpasyātha bhagavān kāśyapo ‘bravīt || iti | alābho ‘pi kāryāntaravyagrāpekṣayā draṣṭavyaḥ | aviprāṇām iti vaktavye gurusūtrakaraṇam aśrotriyabrāhmaṇasyāpi devatāsaṃnidhipraveśārtham | evaṃ ca śrotriyabrāhmaṇasya satyaśabdoccāraṇamātrād eva śapathaṃ bhavatīti draṣṭavyam |
smṛtyantaropasaṃgrahārthaṃ syādgrahaṇam ity eke | yathāha manuḥ |
satyena śāpayed vipraṃ kṣatriyaṃ vāhanāyudhaiḥ |
gobījakāñcanair vaiśyaṃ śūdraṃ sarvais tu pātakaiḥ || iti | [MDh 8.113]
devarājabrāhmaṇasamakṣam iti vaktavye saṃsadigrahaṇaṃ devatāyāḥ saṃnihitasthānopasaṃ-grahārtham | sabhāyāś ca chalādidoṣarahitāyāḥ | yathāha nāradaḥ |
na sā sabhā yatra na santi vṛddhā
na te vṛddhā ye na vadanti dharmam |
nāsau dharmo yatra na satyam asti
na tat satyaṃ yac chalenānuviddham || [NSm Mātṛkā 3.17] iti || 13.14 ||
tac chapathena satyakarma devasaṃsadi, ugrāṇāṃ devatānāṃ saṃnidhau brāhmaṇānāṃ saṃsadi pariṣadi vā bhavati | kṣatriyādīnām arthagurutvalaghutvāpekṣo vikalpaḥ | mahaty arthe devatāsaṃnidhāv alpīyasy anyatreti | abrāhmaṇānām iti vacanād brāhmaṇānāṃ śapathakarma na bhavati | atra viṣṇuḥ: “pṛcched brūhīti brāhmaṇam | satyaṃ brūhīti rājanyam | gobījakāñcanair vaiśyam | sarvapātakaiḥ śūdram | evaṃ hi sākṣiṇaḥ pṛcched varṇānukramato nṛpaḥ” [ViDh 8.20–23, 38] iti | manus tu |
satyena śāpayed vipraṃ kṣatriyaṃ vāhanāyudhaiḥ |
gobījakāñcanair vaiśyaṃ śūdraṃ sarvais tu pātakaiḥ || [MDh 8.113] iti || 13.13 ||
sākṣiṇāṃ sāmānyena doṣa uktaḥ | viśeṣārtham idānīm āha |
viparyaye naraka iti sāmānyena sākṣiṇo doṣa uktaḥ | idānīṃ vyavahāraviśeṣe doṣaviśeṣam āha |
kṣudrapaśvanṛte sākṣī daśa hanti || 13.15 || — || 13.14 ||
kṣudrapaśavaḥ ajāvikādayaḥ | tadviṣaye ‘nṛtavacane daśa puruṣān hanti | aviśeṣāt svabandhūn hantīti jñāyate | daśasvabandhuhanane yāvat pāpaṃ tāvad asya bhavatīty arthaḥ | evaṃ sarvatra | tataś ca tadanurūpeṇa daṇḍaprāyaścittakalpanam | sākṣigrahaṇaṃ sabhyādīnām apy upalakṣanārtham || 13.15 ||
kṣudrapaśavo ‘jāvikādayaḥ | tadviṣaye ‘nṛtavadane sākṣī daśa hanti | teṣāṃ daśānāṃ vadhe yāvān doṣas tāvān asya bhavatīti | daṇḍaprāyaścitte api tadanuguṇe draṣṭavye || 13.14 ||
go’śvapuruṣabhūmiṣu daśaguṇottarān || 13.16 || — || 13.15 ||
uttaram uttaraṃ daśaguṇaṃ bhavati | goviṣaye śataguṇam | aśvaviṣaye sahasraguṇam ityādi |
pañca paśvanṛte hanti daśa hanti gavānṛte |
śatam aśvānṛte hanti sahasraṃ puruṣānṛte || [MDh 8.98] ityādi yad uktaṃ smṛtyantare tatparigrahaviśeṣāpekṣayā prayojanāpekṣayā vā draṣṭavyam || 13.16 ||
uktānām uttaraṃ daśa guṇān daśaguṇottarān | gavādiviṣaye ‘nṛte sākṣī pūrvoktād daśaguṇottaraṃ tattadvadhayuktadoṣo bhavati | etad uktaṃ bhavati | gavānṛte sākṣiṇo gośatahananadoṣaḥ | aśvānṛte ‘śvasahasrahananadoṣaḥ | puruṣānṛte ‘yutapuruṣahananadoṣaḥ | bhūmyanṛte yasya sā bhūmis tajjātīyānāṃ lakṣahananadoṣa iti |
pañca paśvanṛte hanti daśa hanti gavānṛte |
śatam aśvānṛte hanti sahasraṃ puruṣānṛte || [MDh 8.98] ity etat tv atyantakṣudrapaśvādiviṣayam || 13.15 ||
sarvaṃ vā bhūmau || 13.17 || — || 13.16 ||
bhūmiviṣaye śatasahasraṃ sarvaṃ vā manuṣyajātam | kṛtākṛtakṣetrabhedena vikalpo draṣṭavyaḥ || 13.17 ||
yadi vā bhūmiviṣaye ‘nṛte sarvam eva manuṣyajātaṃ hanti | grāmadeśādimahābhūmiviṣayo vikalpaḥ || 13.16 ||
haraṇe narakaḥ || 13.18 || — || 13.17 ||
bhūmer iti vipariṇāmaḥ | aviśeṣāt ā kalpāt bhavati | doṣavacanaprasaṅgenaitad uktam || 13.18 ||
prāsaṅgikam idam | bhūmer iti vipariṇāmena saṃbandhaḥ | bhūmer haraṇe narako bhavati | kālāntarāvadhiḥ śāstrāntarāvaseyaḥ || 13.17 ||
idānīṃ prakṛtam āha |
prakṛtam āha |
apsu maithunasaṃyoge ca || 13.19 ||
bhūmivad apsu || 13.18 ||
apsu kūpādyādhārāvasthitāsu maithunasaṃyoge ca parastrīgamanādāv ity arthaḥ | saṃyogagrahaṇāt kanyālakṣaṇādāv api | cakāraḥ abgrahaṇaṃ maithuna-viśeṣaṇaṃ mā bhūd ity evamarthaḥ | evaṃ ca haraṇe ‘pi narako draṣṭavyaḥ || 13.19 ||
abviṣaye ‘nṛte bhūmival lakṣahananadoṣo haraṇe naraka iti ca samānam | apśabdena kūpataḍāgādi-rūpalakṣitaḥ || 13.18 ||
maithunasaṃyoge ca || 13.19 ||
