paśuvan madhusarpiṣoḥ || 13.20 ||
madhughṛtaviṣaye kṣudrapaśvanṛtatulyo doṣaḥ
|| 13.20 ||
madhusarpirviṣaye ‘nṛte kṣudrapaśuvad doṣaḥ || 13.20 ||
govad vastrahiraṇyadhānyabrahmasu || 13.21 ||
vastrādiviṣaye ‘nṛte gavānṛtavad doṣaḥ brahmānṛtam adhītavedam anadhītavedam ity ukte || 13.21 ||
brahma vedaḥ | vastrādiviṣaye ‘nṛte govad doṣaḥ | adhī-tya nāsmān mayādhītam ityādi brahmānṛtam || 13.21 ||
yāneṣv aśvavat || 13.22 ||
hastyādiyānanimitte ‘nṛte aśvānṛtatulyo doṣaḥ | yāneṣv iti bahuvacanam acetanānāṃ śibikādīnām apy upasaṃgrahārtham |
kecid anyathā vyācakṣate – paśuvan madhusarpiṣor ity ārabhya paśvanṛtavad doṣaḥ madhusarpiṣor haraṇe | vastrādiharaṇe ca govad doṣaḥ | tatra brahmaśabdena vedāṅgāni, likhitāni pustakāny āhuḥ | yānaharaṇe cāśvānṛtatulyo doṣa iti || 13.22 ||
hastiśakaṭaśibikādīni yānāni | tadviṣaye ‘nṛte ‘śvavad doṣaḥ | anye tu kṣudrapaśvanṛta ity ārabhya sākṣiśrāvaṇe yojayanti | kṣudrapaśvanṛte sākṣiṇo daśapaśuhananadoṣaḥ | tasmāt tvayā satyam eva vaktavyam iti sākṣī śrāvayitavya iti | evaṃ sarvatropariṣād api || 13.22 ||
yadi tv evam api doṣe ukte sākṣyanṛtam eva vakti tataḥ |
evam adṛṣṭaviṣaye doṣam uktvā dṛṣṭaviṣaye sākṣiṇo daṇḍam āha |
mithyāvacane yāpyo daṇḍyaś ca sākṣī || 13.23 ||
anṛta ukte yāpyaḥ garhyaḥ sarvalokasamakṣaṃ saṃvyavahāravicchedanārtham | puruṣaśaktyādi-vijñānāt tanmūlyapramāṇena daṇḍyaś ca bhavati | arthaprayojanāpekṣayā parigrahaviśeṣāpekṣayā ca samastavyastaparikalpanārthaś cakāraḥ | adhikārād eva siddhe sākṣigrahaṇaṃ sa eva yāpyo daṇḍyaś ca | sabhyādayo daṇḍyā eveti || 13.23 ||
mithyāvacane dṛṣṭe sākṣī yāpyo garhyaḥ sarvair ayam asaṃvyavahārya iti, daṇḍyaś ca rājñā | atra manuḥ |
lobhāt sahasraṃ daṇḍyas tu mohāt pūrvaṃ tu sāhasam |
bhayād dvau madhyamau daṇḍyau maitryāt pūrvaṃ caturguṇam ||
kāmād daśaguṇaṃ pūrvaṃ krodhāt taddviguṇaṃ param |
ajñānād dve śate pūrṇe bāliśyāc chatam eva tu || [MDh 8.120–121]
kūṭasākṣyaṃ tu kurvāṇāṃs trīn varṇān dhārmiko nṛpaḥ |
pravāsayed daṇḍayitvā brāhmaṇaṃ tu vivāsayet || iti | [MDh 8.123] viṣṇuḥ: “kūṭasākṣiṇāṃ sarvasvāpahāra utkocopajīvināṃ [?] ca” [ViDh 5.180–181] iti || 13.23 ||
asyāpavādam āha |
nānṛtavacane doṣo jīvanaṃ cet tadadhīnam || 13.24 ||
yadā satyavacanāt parasyāvaśyabhāvī jīvanacchedaḥ tadā sākṣiṇānṛtam api vaktavyam | tasminn ukte na pūrvokto doṣaḥ || 13.24 ||
yadā satyavacanāt parasparavadho ‘nṛtavadane tu tadadhīnam anṛtavacananibandhanam anyasya jīvanaṃ bhavati na vadhas tatrānṛtavacane na pūrvokto doṣa iti | atra yājñavalkyaḥ |
varṇināṃ hi vadho yatra tatra sākṣy anṛtaṃ vadet |
tatpāvanāya nirvāpyaś caruḥ sārasvato dvijaiḥ || [YDh 2.83] iti || 13.24 ||
na tu pāpīyaso jīvanam || 13.25 ||
tuśabdo viśeṣārthaḥ etaj jñāpayati – avaśyaṃbhāvy-anṛtavacane doṣaḥ, tathāpi tu vadhanimittāt satyād anṛtam eva viśiṣṭam alpataradoṣatvād iti | tathā ca manuḥ |
śūdraviṭkṣatraviprāṇāṃ yatroktārthaṃ bhaved vadhaḥ |
tatra vaktavyam anṛtaṃ tad dhi tatra viśiṣyate || iti | [MDh 8.104] tad evaṃ manyeta – jīvann apy ayaṃ pāpakarmaratir eva bhavati steyādy eva karotīti tadā satyam eva vaktavyam ity abhiprāyaḥ | pāpīyasa ity ātiśāyanikapratyayanirdeśaḥ | parapīḍā kartavyeva satyaṃ vaktavyam, na vihitākaraṇamātre pratiṣiddhakaraṇamātre veti || 13.25 ||
yadi tv anṛtavacanena pāpīyasaḥ pāpavattarasya parapīḍāratasya jīvanaṃ bhavati tadā na tu na doṣaḥ | api tu doṣa eveti || 13.25 ||
uktāḥ sākṣiṇaḥ, kena praṣṭavyā ity ata āha |
atha sākṣiṇaḥ kena praṣṭavyāḥ, tam āha |
rājā prāḍvivāko brāhmaṇo vā śāstravit || 13.26 ||
pṛcchatīti prāṭ | pṛṣṭaṃ vivektīti vivākaḥ | pṛcchati vā asau vivādinaḥ sākṣiṇo vā yad uktaṃ taṃ vicārayatīti prāḍvivākaḥ | sa rājaiva bhavet, śrānte tasminn anyakarmapravṛtte brāhmaṇo vā śāstravid bahuśruta ity arthaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
yadā svayaṃ na kuryāt tu nṛpatiḥ kāryadarśanam |
tadā niyuñjyād vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ kāryadarśinam || [MDh 8.9] iti || 13.26 ||
pṛcchatīt prāṭ | vivicya vaktīti vivākaḥ | nyaṅkādiṣu darśanād vṛddhikutve | rājā prāḍvivākaḥ syāt | anyapare tu tasmiṃs tena niyukto brāhmaṇo vā śāstravit | atra manuḥ |
yad vā svayaṃ na kuryāt tu nṛpatiḥ kāryanirṇayam |
tadā niyuñjyād vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ kāryanirṇaye || [MDh 8.9] iti || 13.26 ||
evam uktalakṣaṇam |
prāḍvivākam adhyābhavet || 13.27 ||
adhi ābhavet upary enaṃ gacched ity arthaḥ | idam api brāhmaṇapakṣe, rājñas tu “tam upary āsīnam adhastāḍ upāsīran” [GDh 11.7] ity uktatvāt | tam adhyābhaved iti siddhe prāḍvivākagrahaṇaṃ niyamārtham, tam evoparisthaṃ gacchen na sahāyam api | āṅgrahaṇam arthinam eva paricchedayen na punar utpādayed iti | tathā ca manuḥ: “notpādayet svayaṃ kāryam” [MDh 8.43] ityādi || 13.27 ||
uparibhāva aiśvarye vā | āṅ āgamanārthe | enam uktalakṣaṇaṃ prāḍvivākam upary āsīnam adhaḥsthitaś ciraṃ vā guṇabhūtaḥ sann āgacchet kāryārthī | na tu prāḍvivākaḥ svayaṃ kāryam utpādyāhvayed iti | tathā ca manuḥ |
notpādayet svayaṃ kāryaṃ rājā nāpy asya pūruṣaḥ | [MDh 8.43] iti || 13.27 ||
saṃvatsaraṃ pratīkṣetāpratibhāyām || 13.28 ||
yadābhiyuktasyārthinaḥ sākṣiṇāṃ vā pratibhānaṃ na bhavati apratipattir bhavati tadā saṃvatsaraṃ teṣāṃ pratīkṣeta | saṃvatsarād ūrdhvaṃ yathāpratibhaṃ paricchedayed ity arthaḥ || 13.28 ||
yadābhiyuktasyārthinaḥ sākṣiṇo vāpratibhā bhavati vaktavyaṃ na pratibhāti svayaṃ jāḍyādyupetatvād arthasya vā ciranirvṛttatvādinā durnirūpyatvāt tadā saṃvatsaraṃ pratīkṣeta | etāvatā kālena nirūpya brūhīti kālaṃ dadyāt | atra kātyāyanaḥ |
asvatantrajaḍonmattabāladīkṣitarogiṇām |
kālaḥ saṃvatsarād arvāk svayam eva yathepsitam ||
nāradaḥ |
gahanatvād vivādānām asāmarthyāt smṛter api |
ṛṇādiṣu haret kālaṃ kāmaṃ tattvabubhutsayā || iti | [NSm Mā. 1.38] prajāpatiḥ |
dinam ekam atha dve vā trīṇi vā pañca sapta vā |
kālas tv ṛṇādau gahana ā tripakṣād api smṛtaḥ ||
|| 13.28 ||
asyāpavādaḥ |
dhenvanaḍutstrīprajananasaṃyukte ca śighram || 13.29 ||
dhenvādisaṃbandhe vyavahāre na saṃvatsaraṃ pratīkṣeta, kālakṣepe dhenvādyupabhogakālo ‘tīto bhavatīti | dhenuḥ sakṣīrā gauḥ | anaḍvān balīvardaḥ, asāv api saṃvatsarabhogena jīryatīti | strīprajanana-saṃyukte ‘pi ca, saṃyuktagrahaṇasya pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyamānatvāt | strīprajananaṃ kanyāvivāhaḥ, saṃvatsarapratīkṣaṇe tasyāpi kālātikramo bhavatīti | vivāha iti vaktavyam iti cen, na prajananayogyagrahaṇārthatvāt | evaṃ cāsamarthāyāḥ saṃvatsarapratīkṣaṇe ‘pi na doṣaḥ | strīgrahaṇaṃ savarṇajātyupasaṃgrahārtham | tataś cāsavarṇāyāṃ pratīkṣaṇaṃ kartavyam iti sūcayati | cakāraḥ pratyekam iti sūcanārthaḥ | dhenusaṃyukte anaḍutsaṃyukte ityādi, mā bhūt samuditānām eva viṣaya iti | śīghra-grahaṇaṃ savarṇajātyupasaṃgrahārtham || 13.29 ||
saṃyuktaśabdaḥ pratyekaṃ saṃbadhyate | dhenvādisaṃyukte vivāde śīghraṃ vivādayet | prajananaṃ vivāhas taddhetutvāt | strī dāsyādiḥ | tathāha kātyāyanaḥ |
dhenāv anuḍuhi kṣetre strīṣu prajanane tathā |
nyāse cāritrake datte tathaiva krayavikraye ||
kanyāyā dūṣaṇe steye kalahe sāhase nidhau |
upadhau kūṭasākṣye ca sadya eva vivādayet ||
iti || 13.29 ||
ātyayike ca | 13.30 ||
śīghraṃ nirṇayaṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ | atyayakarma yathā sārdham uktasya sārdho gacchati, mriyamāṇāvastho vānyataro bhavati, grāmopaplavo vā bhavatītyādi | atyayavaśāt dhenvādiyuktād api pūrvaṃ vicāraṇīyam iti pṛthak karaṇam | caśabdaḥ steyasāhasadaṇḍapāruṣyādyupasaṃgrahaṇārthaḥ | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
sāhasasteyapāruṣyago’bhiśāpātyayastriyām |
vivādayed sadya eva kālo ‘nyatrecchayā smṛtaḥ || [YDh 2.12] iti || 13.30 ||
vyapaiti gauravaṃ yatra vināśas tyāga eva ca |
kālaṃ tatra na kurvīta kāryam ātyayikaṃ hi tat ||
iti kātyāyanaḥ | evamādāv ātyayike śīghraṃ vivādayen na kālaṃ dadyād iti | yājñavakyaḥ |
sāhasasteyapāruṣyago’bhiśāpātyaye striyām |
vivādayet sadya eva kālo ‘nyatrecchayā smṛtaḥ || [YDh 2.12] iti || 13.30 ||
sarvadharmebhyo garīyaḥ prāḍvivāke satyavacanaṃ satyavacanam || 13.31 ||
sarvadharmebhyaḥ śrutismṛtyupadiṣṭebhyo dharmasā-dhanebhyo gurutaraṃ prāḍvivāke vyavahāradraṣṭari yathābhūtārthadarśitvam ity arthaḥ | prāḍvivāke vā saṃnihite sākṣyādīnāṃ yat satyavacanam iti || 13.31 ||
śrutismṛticoditebhyaḥ sarvadharmebhyo gurutaram idaṃ yat prāḍvivāke pṛcchati sati satyaṃ brūyāt | dviruktir adhyāyasamāptyarthā || 13.31 ||
atha caturdaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
vihitākaraṇe daṇḍaprāyaścitte ukte | kasmin kāle vihitam akurvato neṣyete ity ata āha |
athāśaucanirṇayaḥ |
śāvam āśaucaṃ daśarātram anṛtvigdīkṣitabrahmacāriṇāṃ sapiṇḍānām || 14.1 ||
śavanimitto ‘śucibhāvo daśāhorātrāṇi bhavati | keṣāṃ? sapiṇḍānām | tatsvarūpaṃ vakṣyati | ṛtvigādivarjitānām | ṛtvig yājakaḥ pravṛttakarmātra gṛhyate dīkṣita-sāhacaryāt | yadi bhūtapūrvagatir ihāśrīyate “ṛtvik śvaśuraḥ” ityādivat, dīkṣitabrahmacāriṇor apy evaṃ prasajyeta | tataś ca sarvasya bhūtapūrvagatyā brahmacāritvād ṛtvigdīkṣitagrahaṇam anarthakaṃ syāt | tasmāt sarva eva pravṛttakriyā gṛhyante | dīkṣito yajamānaḥ | brahmacārī yāvad brahmacaryam | upariṣṭāt kṣatriyādīnām upadeśād brāhmaṇaviṣayam idam | sūtakasya vakṣyamāṇatvād āśaucaṃ daśarātram ity ukta eva śavanimittasya siddhe śāvagrahaṇaṃ śavaprabhṛti divasagaṇaṇā kartavyety evamartham |
nanu ca smṛtyantare saṃskāraprabhṛti śrūyate:
araṇye kāṣṭhavat tyaktvā kṣipet [kṣapet?] tryaham eva vā | [MDh 5.69] iti pūrvakālakriyāyām | tathā “āhitāgniś cet pravasan mriyeta punaḥ saṃskāraṃ kṛtvā śavavac chaucam” [VaDh 4.37] iti | tenedaṃ viruddham iti |
atrocyate – viṣayavibhāgenāvirodha iti | tathā vyāghradharme viṣayavibhāgapratipādanaparaṃ vākyaṃ śrūyate |
maraṇād eva kartavyaṃ saṃyogo yasya nāgnibhiḥ |
dahanād eva kartavyaṃ yasya vaitāniko vidhiḥ || iti |
“patitacaṇḍālasūtikodakyāśavaspṛṣṭi” [GDh 14.29] ityanena śavanimittāśaucasparśane snānavidhānād evāśucibhāve siddhe | āśaucagrahaṇm adhyayanāder apy anadhikārārtham | tathā ca manuḥ |
ubhayatra daśāhāni kulasyānnaṃ na bhujyate |
dānaṃ pratigraho yajñaḥ svādhyāyaś ca nivartate || iti | śāvāśaucam iti vaktavye śāvam āśaucam iti visamāsakaraṇaṃ śavāṅgabhūtānām asthnāṃ yāvat saṃcayanaṃ tāvad eva parasparasparśaṇaṃ praty aśucibhāva iti jñāpanārtham | tathā ca saṃvartaḥ |
asthisaṃcayanād ūrdhvam aṅgasparśo vidhīyate | iti | asthisaṃcayanasvarūpqṃ smṛtyantarād avagantavyam | tathā ca vyāghraḥ |
prathame ca dvitīye ca tṛtīye ca caturthake |
asthisaṃcayanaṃ kuryād bandhubhir hitam īpsubhiḥ || iti | tatra sadyaḥśaucādhikāriṇāṃ prathame ‘hni, ekāhasaṃbandhināṃ dvitīye ‘hni, tryahasaṃbandhināṃ tṛtīye ‘hni, caturahasaṃbandhi-nāṃ daśāhasaṃbandhināṃ caturthe ‘hnīti draṣṭavyam | tathā tatraivāparaṃ vākyaṃ śrūyate |
caturthe pañcame caiva saptame daśame tathā |
asthisaṃcayanaṃ kuryād varṇānām ānupūrvyaśaḥ || iti | tatra brāhmaṇasya caturthe kṣatriyasya pañcame vaiśyasya saptame śūdrasya daśama iti draṣṭavyam | brāhmaṇasyoktam, evaṃ kṣatriyavaiśyayor api vṛttasvādhyāyāpekṣayā svalpāśaucānāṃ svalpam ity anena nyāyena kalpyam iti | brahmacārigrahaṇaṃ parivrājakādīnām apy upalakṣaṇam | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
naiṣṭhikānāṃ vanasthānāṃ yatīnāṃ brahmacāriṇām |
nāśaucaṃ sūtake proktaṃ śāve cāpi tathaiva ca || iti | dīkṣitagrahaṇaṃ cāndrāyaṇādau pravṛttānām api grahaṇārtham | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ |
na rājñām aghadoṣo ‘sti vratināṃ satriṇāṃ tathā |
aindrasthānam upāsīnāḥ brahmapūtā hi te sadā || iti | [VaDh 19.48] “śrutvā cordhvaṃ daśamyāḥ” [GDh 14.24], “ekādaśarātraṃ kṣatriyasya” [GDh 14.2] iti ca liṅgād eva daśarātraṃ grahītuṃ śakyate | daśarātragrahaṇaṃ brāhmaṇasyāśauce kṣatriyāder api daśarātram eveti niyamārtham | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
kṣatraviṭchūdradāyādā ye syur viprasya bāndhavāḥ |
teṣām aśauce viprasya daśāhaṃ sūtakaṃ bhavet || iti | viprasyāśauce prāpte teṣāṃ daśāham eveti tatrārthaḥ | manur api |
sarve tūttamavarṇānām āśaucaṃ kuryur ādṛtāḥ |
tadvarṇavidhidṛṣṭena svaṃ tu śaucaṃ svayoniṣu || iti | brāhmaṇāśaucae teṣāṃ daśāhopadeśāt teṣām āśauce viprasya smṛtyantaroktaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha lokākṣiḥ |
daśāhaṃ śāvam āśaucaṃ janmahānyoḥ svayoniṣu |
ṣaṭ trir ekaṃ tathā proktaṃ kṣatraviṭśūdrayoniṣu ||
iti || 14.1 ||
śavanimittaṃ śāvam | aśucibhāva āśaucam | daśarātraṃ daśāhorātraṃ bhavati sapiṇḍānām ṛtvigādivarjitānām | ṛtvig yājakaḥ karmaṇi vartamānaḥ | dīkṣitaḥ kṛtadīkṣaṇīyaḥ karmaṇi vartamānaḥ | brahmacārī prasiddhaḥ | āvabhṛthaṃ pūrvayoḥ | ābrahmacaryaparyantaṃ parasya | kiṃ punar idam āśaucalakṣaṇam | karmaṇy anadhikāro ‘bhojyānnatāspṛśyatā dānādiṣv anadhikāritā | atra manuḥ |
ubhayatra daśāhāni kulasyānnaṃ na bhujyate |
dānaṃ pratigraho yajñaḥ svādhyāyaś ca nivartate ||
na spṛśeyur anāsannāḥ pretasyāsannabāndhavān || iti ca | aṅgirāḥ |
sūtake tu yadā vipro brahmacārī viśeṣataḥ |
pibet pānīyam ajñānāt samaśnīyāt spṛśeta vā ||
pānīyamāne kurvīta pañcagavyasya bhakṣaṇam |
trirātraṃ bhojane proktaṃ spṛṣṭvā snānaṃ vidhīyate || iti | yājñavalkyaḥ |
udakyāśaucibhiḥ snāyāt saṃspṛṣṭas tair upaspṛśet || iti | [YDh 3.30] saṃvartaḥ |
asthisaṃcayanād ūrdhvam aṅgasparśo vidhīyate || iti | vyāghraḥ |
maraṇād eva kartavyaṃ saṃyogo yasya nāgnibhiḥ |
dahanād eva kartavyaṃ yasya vaitāniko vidhiḥ || iti |
śaṅkhaḥ |
caturthe daśarātraṃ syāt ṣaṇniśāḥ puṃsi pañcame |
ṣaṣṭhe caturahāc chuddhiḥ saptame tu dinatrayam || iti | etat sarvaṃ nirguṇaviṣayam | guṇavadviṣaye parāśaraḥ |
ekāhāc chudhyate vipro yo ‘gnivedasamanvitaḥ |
tryahāt kevalavedas tu nirguṇo daśabhir dinaiḥ ||
iti | bṛhaspatiḥ |
trirātreṇa viśudhyeta vipro vedāgnisaṃyutaḥ |
pañcāhenāgnihinas tu daśāhād brāhmaṇabruvaḥ || iti | atra brahmacārigrahaṇaṃ gṛhasthavyatiriktānām āśramāṇām upalakṣaṇārtham | atra bṛhaspatiḥ |
naiṣṭhikānāṃ vratasthānāṃ yatīnāṃ brahmacāriṇām |
nāśaucaṃ sūtake proktaṃ śāve vāpi tathaiva ca || iti | dīkṣitagrahaṇaṃ cāndrāyaṇādivratapravṛttānām upalakṣaṇārtham | atra vasiṣṭhaḥ |
na rājñām atha doṣo ‘sti vratināṃ satriṇāṃ tathā |
aindrasthānam upāsīnā na cāpūtā hi te sadā || [VaDh 19.48] iti || 14.1 ||
ekādaśarātraṃ kṣatriyasya || 14.2 ||
ṛtvigdīkṣitabrahmacārivarjitānāṃ sapiṇḍānāṃ śāvam āśaucam iti vartate || 14.2 ||
dīkṣitabrahmacārivyatiriktasya jñātimaraṇe kṣatriyasyaikādaśarātraṃ bhavati | “dvādaśarātreṇa” [YDh 3.22] iti yājñavalkyaḥ | “pañcadaśarātreṇa” [VaDh 4.28] iti vasiṣṭhaḥ | “daśarātreṇa” iti parāśaraḥ | “ṣoḍaśāham” iti paiṭhīnasiḥ | eteṣāṃ vṛtāgnisvādhyāyasamāsavyāsāpekṣo vikalpaḥ
|| 14.2 ||
dvādaśarātraṃ vaiśyasya || 14.3 ||
dvādaśarātraṃ vaiśyasyārdhamāsam eke
|| 14.3 ||
pūrvavat anuvartate || 14.3 ||
“ekādaśarātram” parāśaraḥ | “viṃśatirātram” [VaDh 4.29] vasiṣṭha-paiṭhīnasī | pūrvavad vikalpaḥ || 14.3 ||
ardhamāsam eke || 14.4 ||
pañcadaśāham ity eke | vṛttasvādhyāyāpekṣayā vikalpo draṣṭavyaḥ | evaṃ ca brāhmaṇādīnām api vṛttasvādhyāyāpekṣāstīti gamyate | tathā ca parāśaraḥ |
ekāhāc chudyate vipro yo ‘gnivedasamanvitaḥ |
tryahāt kevalavedas tu nirguṇo daśabhir dinaiḥ || iti | [PārSm 3.5] tathā ca manur api |
daśāhaṃ śāvam āśaucaṃ sapiṇḍeṣu vidhīyate |
arvāk saṃcayanād asthnāṃ tryaham ekāham eva vā || iti | [MDh 5.59] atra arvāk saṃcayanād iti caturahopalakṣaṇam | ekaguṇayoge caturaheṇaiva śuddhiḥ, dviguṇayoge tryaheṇa, sarvaguṇayoge tv ekāhena | sarvaguṇayoge sati vṛttisaṃkoco ‘pi bhavati tadā sadyaḥśaucam iti bhāṣyakṛtā vyākhyātam | tathā kṣatriyasyāpi vākyacatuṣṭayam ārabhyate – “ekādaśarātreṇa” [GDh 14.2] iti gautamaḥ | “pañcadaśarātreṇa” [VaDh 4.28] iti vasiṣṭhaḥ | “dvādaśarātreṇa” [YDh 3.22] iti yājñavalkyaḥ | “daśarātreṇa” iti parāśaraḥ | brāhmaṇasya daśarātravat kṣatriyasyāpi pañcadaśāha eva nityaḥ | tatraikaguṇa-yoge dvādaśarātram, dviguṇayoge ekādaśarātram, sarvaguṇayoge daśāham, nirguṇasya pañcadaśāhaḥ | tathā vaiśyasyāpi – ekaguṇayoge “ardhamāsam eke” ity ardhamāsaḥ, dviguṇayoge “dvādaśarātraṃ vaiśyasya” iti dvādaśāhaḥ, sarvaguṇayoge parāśaroktam ekādaśarātram, nirguṇasya viṃśatirātraṃ vasiṣṭhenoktam | tad eva nityam ity evaṃ kalpanā draṣṭavyā || 14.4 ||
māsaṃ śūdrasya || 14.5 || — || 14.4 ||
asya dīkṣādyabhāvāt sapiṇḍānām ity eva | vṛttādyabhā-vāc caitad eva | asacchūdraviṣayaṃ cedam, “sacchū-drāṇām ardhamāsam” ity auśanasavacanāt || 14.5 ||
“sacchūdrāṇām ardhamāsam” ity uśanā | ye traivarṇikān paricarantas tebhyo vṛttiṃ lipsante te sacchūdrāḥ | sā hi teṣām uttamā vṛttir ity avocāma | dāsaviṣaye bṛhaspatiḥ |
dāsāntevāsibhṛtakāḥ śiṣyāś caikatravāsinaḥ |
svāmitulyena śaucena śudhyanti mṛtasūtake || iti |
atra kramavivāhe baudhāyanaḥ |
kṣatraviṭśūdrajātīyā ye syur viprasya bāndhavāḥ |
teṣām aśauce viprasya daśāhāc chuddhir iṣyate ||
rājanyavaiśyāv apy evaṃ hīnajātiṣu bandhuṣu |
svam evāśaucaṃ kuryātāṃ viśucchyartham asaṃśayaḥ || iti | bṛhaspatis tu |
śudhyed vipro daśāhena janmahānyoḥ svayoniṣu |
saptapañcatrirātrais tu kṣatraviṭśūdrayoniṣu || iti |
atra viṣṇur viśeṣam āha: “brāhmaṇasya kṣatriyaviṭśūdreṣu ṣaḍrātratrirātraikarātraiḥ | kṣatriyasya viṭśūdrayoḥ ṣaḍrātratrirātrābhyām | vaiśyasya śūdre ṣaḍrātrāc chuddhiḥ” [ViDh 22.22–24] iti prakṛtam | eṣāṃ vṛttādyapekṣayā vyavasthā | adhikavarṇaviṣaye manuḥ |
sarve tūttamavarṇānām āśaucaṃ kuryur ādṛtāḥ |
tadvarṇavidhidṛṣṭena svaṃ tv āśaucaṃ svayoniṣu || iti | uttamavarṇānāṃ maraṇaprayuktam āśaucam uktaṃ tadvarṇavidhidṛṣṭena prakāreṇa kuryuḥ, svayoniṣu tu svāśaucaṃ svajātinimittaṃ kuryur iti | ata eva jñāyate mātṛjātiyuktā anulomānāṃ dharmā iti || 14.4 ||
tac ced antaḥ punar āpatec cheṣeṇa śudhyeran || 14.6 || — || 14.5 ||
tad eva samānajātīyamaraṇe maraṇam, janane jananam iti draṣṭavyam, tadgrahaṇāt | punaḥ bhūyo ‘pi anekaśo ‘pīty arthaḥ | āpated āgacchet | tataḥ pūrvāśaucāvaśiṣṭenaiva kālena śuddhiḥ syāt nāgatasya kālapratīkṣaṇaṃ kartavyam ity arthaḥ | antargrahaṇaṃ vispaṣṭārtham “śeṣeṇa śudhyeran,” [GDh 6.7] “rātriśeṣe dvābhyām, prabhāte tisṛbhiḥ” [GDh 18.8] ity etair eva madhyamasya jñātuṃ śakyatvāt |
anye tu “janane ‘py evam” [GDh 14.14] ity atideśād eva siddhe tadgrahaṇaṃ samānakālāpekṣam āhuḥ | yathā daśarātrapramāṇāśauce daśarātrapramāṇāśaucam iti | yadi svalpapramāṇake mahatpramāṇam āgacchati tat pūrveṇa na gacched iti | tathā ca paiṅgigṛhyasmṛtiḥ: “aghavṛddhau paścimena samāpayet” iti | aghavṛddhāv ity uktaḥ alpāghaṃ gacched eveti draṣṭavyam || 14.6 ||
antaritasya pratiyogyapekṣāyāṃ śāvam āśaucaṃ daśarātram iti prakṛtaṃ daśarātrādy abhisaṃbadhyate | śāvasya daśarātrāder āśaucasya madhye tasmin vartamāne yady anyac chāvāśaucaṃ samānakālaṃ nyūnakālaṃ vā punar āpated āgacchet tataḥ śeṣeṇa pūrvaṃ vartamānasya daśarātrāder yāni śiṣṭāny ahāni tair eva śudhyeran | na punar āpatitasya kālapratīkṣeti | atra janane ‘py evam ity atideśāt pūrṇasya jananāśaucasya madhye samānakālaṃ vā nyūnakālaṃ vā jananāśaucam āgacchec cheṣeṇa śudhyeran | yatra nyūnakālasyāśaucasya madhye pūrṇakālam āpatet tenaiva gacchati | atra manuḥ |
antardaśāhe syātāṃ cet punar maraṇajanmanī |
tāvat syād aśucir vipro yāvat tat syād anirdaśam || iti | [MDh 5.79] devalaḥ |
ādyānāṃ yaugapadye tu jñeyā śuddhir garīyasī ||
iti | aṅgirāḥ |
mātary agre pramītāyām aśuddhau mriyate pitā |
pituḥ śeṣeṇa śuddhiḥ syān mātuḥ kuryāt tu pakṣiṇīm || iti |
sūtakād dviguṇaṃ śāvaṃ śāvād dviguṇam ārtavam |
ārtavād dviguṇā sūtis tato ‘dhi śavadāhake || iti |
vṛddhātriḥ: “anena dāhakasya sūtikāyāś ca pūrvāśaucaviśeṣeṇottarasya śuddhiḥ” iti | atra ṣaṭtriṃśanmatam |
śāvāśauce samutpanne sūtakaṃ tu yadā bhavet |
śāvena śudhyate sūtir na sūtiḥ śāvaśodhanī ||
iti || 14.5 ||
rātriśeṣe dvābhyām || 14.7 || — 14.6 ||
yady ekarātryavaśiṣṭe pūrvāśauce tadanyad āpatet tato na pūrveṇaiva śuddhiḥ kiṃ tu dvābhyāṃ rātribhyām | evaṃ ca trirātraṃ parigṛhītaṃ bhavati || 14.7 ||
pūrvasminn āśauce rātriśeṣe sati yady anyad āpatet tato dvābhyām ahobhyāṃ śudhyeran || 14.6 ||
prabhāte tisṛbhiḥ || 14.8 || — 14.7 ||
prabhāte snānāt pūrvam, saṅgavakālāt pūrvam ity arthaḥ, “aghabhājaḥ saṅgave snātvā grāmaṃ praviśeyuḥ” ity arthavādadarśanāt | yady āpatet tato na rātriśeṣeṇa śuddhiḥ | kiṃ tu tisṛbhiḥ rātribhiḥ || 14.8 ||
atha daśāhādau vyatīte ‘paredyuḥ prabhāte saṃgave yady anyad āpater tatas tisṛbhī rātribhiḥ śudhyeran | atra manuḥ |
vigataṃ tu videśasthaṃ śṛṇuyād yo hy anirdaśam |
yac cheṣaṃ daśarātrasya tāvad evāśucir bhavet | [MDh 5.75] | iti || 14.7 ||
gobrāhmaṇahatānām anvakṣam || 14.9 || — || 14.8 ||
gavārthe brāhmaṇārthe vā hatānāṃ yāvad darśanam āśaucam | sadyaḥ śaucam ity arthaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
gobrāhmaṇasya caivārthe yasya cecchati pārthivaḥ || iti | [MDh 5.95] | sadyaḥśaucādhikāre idam uktam | gobrāhmaṇahatānām anyatarapramāpitānāṃ vā | tathā ca uśanā: “gobhir hatānāṃ brāhmaṇena hatānāṃ ca sadyaḥśaucam” iti || 14.9 ||
gavārthe brāhmaṇārthe vā hatānāṃ ye sapiṇḍās teṣām āśaucam anvakṣam | anvakṣyate pratyakṣyate śavas tāvat saṃskārānte snātvā śudhyerann iti | atra sadyaḥśaucādhikāre manuḥ |
gobrāhmaṇasya caivārthe yasya cecchati pārthivaḥ || iti | [MDh 5.95] gavā brāhmaṇena vā ye hatās tajjñātīnām āśaucam anvakṣam iti cārthaḥ | tathāh uśanā: “gobhir hatānāṃ brāhmaṇair hatānāṃ ca sadyaḥ śaucam” iti || 14.8 ||
rājakrodhāc cāyuddhe || 14.10 ||
rājakrodhāc ca || 14.9 ||
anvakṣam iti vartate | caśabdo ‘piśabdasyārthe, bhinnakramaś ca | rājakrodhād ayuddhe ‘pīti | apiśabdāt yuddhe ‘pi | tathā ca manuḥ |
udyatair āhave śastraiḥ kṣatradharmahatasya tu |
sadyaḥ saṃtiṣṭhate yajñas tadāśaucam iti sthitiḥ || iti | [MDh 5.98] caśabdaḥ samuccayārtho vā | tataś ca ya rājā krodhāt ghātayed ayuddhe tasya sadyaḥśaucam | yuddhe akrodhenāpi hatasyeti |
kecid evaṃ vyācakṣate – rājakrodhād dhatānāṃ yuddhe ‘pi janapadakṣobhādibhiḥ hatānām iti | tathā ca manuḥ |
ḍimbāhavahatānāṃ ca vidyutā pārthivena ca | iti | [MDh 5.95]
anye tu vivāhakālam āhuḥ | ayuddhe rājakrodhād dhatānāṃ sadyaḥśaucam, vivāhakāle ca sadyaḥśaucam iti | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
kṛcchre vivāhe yajñe ca saṃgrāme deśaviplave |
āpady api ca kaṣṭāyāṃ sadyaḥśaucaṃ vidhīyate ||
iti || 14.10 ||
hatānām ity upasamastam apekṣate | rājakrodhād ye hatās tajjñātīnām apy anvakṣam āśaucam || 14.9 ||
yuddhe || 14.10 ||
cakāro ‘nuvartate | yuddhe ca hatānām anvakṣam āśaucam | atra manuḥ |
ḍimbāhavahatānāṃ ca vidyutā pārthivena ca || iti | [MDh 5.95] ḍimbo janasaṃmardaḥ | sadyaḥśaucaṃ prakṛtam | pāṭhāntaraṃ tv asmabhyaṃ na rocate | yadi vā sa eva pāṭhaḥ | āyuddheti padacchedaḥ | āyuddham āyodhanam | saṃgrāma iti yāvat | sarvathā nañsamāso na rocate || 14.10 ||
prāyānāśakaśastrāgniviṣodakodbandhanaprapatanaiś cecchatām || 14.11 ||
anvakṣam iti vartate | buddhipūrvaṃ prāyādibhir ātmānaṃ vyāpadayatāṃ sadyaḥśaucam | prāyaḥ mahāprasthānam | tad anicchato ‘pi rājabhayādinā saṃbhavatīti icchatām ity uktam | anāśaka abhojanam, etad api durbhikṣādāv anicchato ‘pi saṃbhavatīti | śastraṃ khaḍgādi, tatrāpi pramādāt saṃbhavaty eva | udbandhanaṃ rajjvādinā, atrāpi parihāsādinā anicchato ‘pi saṃbhavati | prapatanaṃ parvatād vṛkṣād vā, tatrāpi daivād abuddhipūrvam api saṃbhavaty eva | evaṃ cābuddhipūrvam etair mṛtānāṃ yathāprāptam eveti draṣṭavyam | cakārād anyair apy evaṃbhūtair ātmāhananahetubhir iti draṣṭavyam || 14.11 ||
prāyo mahāprasthānam | tad anicchato ‘pi rājabhayādinā saṃbhavatītīcchatām ity uktam | evam uttareṣv api yathāsaṃbhavam icchānicche draṣṭavye | aśa bhojane ‘śanam āśaḥ | sa evāśakas tadviparyayo ‘nāśakaḥ | saty eva bhojyadravye krodhādinā bhojana-nivṛttiḥ | śastrāgniviṣodakodbandhanāni prasiddhāni | prapatanaṃ vṛkṣāt parvatād vā pātaḥ | etaiḥ prāyādibhir ātmavyāpādane cakārād anyair apy evaṃvidhair anvakṣam āśaucam iti | atra brahmapurāṇe |
krodhāt prāyaṃ viṣaṃ vahniḥ śastram udbandhanaṃ jalam |
girivṛkṣaprapātaṃ ca ye kurvanti narādhamāḥ ||
brahmadaṇḍahatā ye ca ye caiva brāhmaṇair hatāḥ |
mahāpātakino ye ca patitās te prakīrtitāḥ ||
patitānāṃ na dāhaḥ syān na ca syād asthisaṃcayaḥ |
na cāśrupātaḥ piṇḍo vā kāryā śrāddhakriyā na ca || iti | atra aṅgirāḥ |
yadi kaścit pramādena mriyetāgnyudakādibhiḥ |
tasyāśaucaṃ vidhātavyaṃ kartavyā codakakriyā || iti || 14.11 ||
idānīṃ sapiṇḍasvarūpam āha |
sapiṇḍānām ity uktam | ke te sapiṇḍās tān āha |
piṇḍanivṛttiḥ saptame pañcame vā || 14.12 ||
ṣaṭpuruṣān atītya saptame prāpte | pitrādibhyas tribhyaḥ piṇḍadānam | teṣu jīvatsu yebhyaḥ pitā dadāti tebhya eva putro ‘pīty aparebhyas tribhya iti ṣaṭpuruṣā piṇḍasaṃbandhinaḥ | yadā jīvapitṛkasya “na jīvantam ati dadāti” it jīvapitā pitāmahāya prapitāmahāyeti dadyād ity āśrīyate tadā pañcame nivṛttiḥ | strīṇām apy etat tulyaṃ pitṛpakṣe vā saptame nivṛttiḥ | mātṛpakṣe pañcama iti || 14.12 ||
kūṭastham ārabhya ṣaṣṭhaparyantaṃ sāpiṇḍyam | saptame tu nivṛttiḥ | kevalaṃ saptame sodakatvam, “saptame tu nivartate” [MDh 5.60] ity uktatvāt | tataḥ paraṃ sagotratvam eva | pañcame veti yad uktaṃ tad aurasavyatiriktaviṣayam | tatrāpi yathāsaṃbhavaṃ draṣṭavyam | evaṃ cārthaḥ – pitṛpitāmahaprapitā-mahebhyas tatparam api dvābhyām ity ātmanā saha ṣaṣṭhaparyantaṃ piṇḍaṃ dadyāt | saptame tu nivṛttiḥ | pañcame veti putrikāputraviṣayam etat | atra baudhāyanaḥ – “kathaṃ kalu putrikāputrasya piṇḍadānam | etat te ‘muṣyai pitaḥ, mama pitāmaha ye ca tvām anu, etat te ‘muṣyai pitāmaha mama prapitāmaha ye ca tvām anu, etat te ‘muṣyai prapitāmaha mama prapitāmaha ye ca tvām anu” iti | asyaivaṃ piṇḍaṃ dadataḥ pañcame prāpte piṇḍanivṛttiḥ | mātsyapurāṇe |
lepabhājaś caturthādyāḥ pitrādyāḥ piṇḍabhāginaḥ |
saptamaḥ piṇḍadas teṣāṃ sāpiṇḍyaṃ sāptapauruṣam || iti || 14.12 ||
janane ‘py evam || 14.13 ||
daśāha eva atidiśyate | anyathā mātur api sadyaḥśaucaṃ prāpnoti | tad ācāreṇa virudhyate, gṛhyasmṛtyā ca “daśarātraṃ dampatī sūtakinau bhavataḥ” iti | tataḥ yad eva śāva upadiṣṭaṃ daśarātraṃ tat sapiṇḍānāṃ sarveṣāṃ bhavatīti pratipattavyam | tathā ca manuḥ |
dantajāte ‘nujāte ca kṛtacaule ca saṃsthite |
aśuddhā bāndhavāḥ sarve sūtake ca tathocyate || iti | [MDh 5.58] | tathā vyāghro ‘pi |
sūtake tu sapiṇḍānāṃ pitror vā mātur eva vā |
āśaucaṃ daśarātraṃ syāt tryahaṃ toyapradāyinām || iti | tatrāpi dīkṣit ādivarjanaṃ draṣṭavyam | apiśabdāt “tac ced antaḥ punar āpatet” [GDh 14.6]
ityādi || 14.13 ||
śāvam āśaucaṃ daśarātram ityādeḥ prabhāte tisṛbhir ityantasyātideśaḥ | yathā śāvam āśaucaṃ tathā janane ‘pīti draṣṭavyam || 14.13 ||
mātāpitros tat || 14.14 ||
mātāpitros tan mātur vā || 14.14 ||
mātāpitror vā sūtakam iti | uparitanavāśabdo ‘trāpy adhyāhartavyaḥ | tathā ca vasiṣṭho ‘py atraiva vāśabdam uktavān: “mātāpitror vā tannimittatvāt” [VaDh 4.21–22] iti || 14.14 ||
tajjananāśaucaṃ mātāpitror vā mātur eva vā | mukhyatvāj jananyāḥ pituḥ prāg eva | jñātīnāṃ tatra vyāghraḥ |
sūtakaṃ tu sapiṇḍānāṃ pitror vā mātur eva vā || iti |
mātāpitror vā tannimittatvād iti | manus tu |
janane ‘py evam eva syān nipuṇāṃ śuddhim icchatām |
sarveṣāṃ śāvam āśaucaṃ mātāpitros tu sūtakam || iti | [MDh 5.61–62] yājñavalkyaḥ |
trirātraṃ daśarātraṃ vā śāvam āśaucam iṣyate |
ūnadvivarṣa ubhayoḥ sūtakaṃ mātur eva hi || iti | [YDh 3.18] baudhāyanaḥ: “janane tāvan mātāpitror daśāham āśauce | mātur ity eke, tatpariharaṇāt | pitur ity eke, śukraprādhānyāt | ayonijā hy api putrāḥ śrūyante | mātāpitror eva tu saṃsargasāmānyāt” [BDg 1.11.19–23] | aṅgirāḥ |
nāśaucaṃ sūtake proktaṃ sapiṇḍānāṃ kathaṃcana |
mātāpitror aśaucaṃ syāt sūtakaṃ mātur eva ca ||
sarveṣāṃ śāvam āśaucaṃ mātāpitros tu sūtakam |
mātur vā sūtakaṃ tasmād upaspṛśya pitā śuciḥ || iti | śaṅkhalikhitau: “janane ‘py evam | tatra mātāpitarāv aśucī iti | mātety eke” iti | paiṭhīnasiḥ |
janane sapiṇḍāḥ śucayo mātāpitros tu sūtakam |
sūtakaṃ mātur eva syād upaspṛśya pitā śuciḥ || iti |
atra vṛttādyapekṣo daśāho naiṣāṃ vikalpaḥ | anye bhaṇanti anadhikāralakṣaṇam āśaucaṃ sarveṣāṃ bhavati, “ubhayatra daśāhāni kulasyānnaṃ na bhujyate” iti mānave darśanāt | asparśitālakṣaṇaṃ tu mātāpitror eveti | gṛhāntare vasatas tatsaṃsargam agacchataḥ pituś ca neti | “sūtake sūtakāvarjaṃ saṃsparśo na niṣidhyate” iti ca paṭhanti || 14.14 ||
mātur vā || 14.15 ||
tasyaiva vā jātasya mātur na sapatnyā ity arthaḥ | vyavasthitavikalpo ‘tra draṣṭavyaḥ – yadi sarve vṛttahīnās tadānīṃ sarveṣāṃ sūtakaṃ syāt | yadi sarve vṛttavantaḥ pitā vṛttahīnaḥ tadā pitroḥ | yadā pitā ca vṛttavān saṅkocavṛttiś ca tadā mātur eveti | yadi tu pitā agnihotrī saṅkocavṛttir na bhavati tadā upaspṛśyāgni-hotraṃ hutvā punar āśaucaṃ gṛhṇīyād iti || 14.15 ||
garbhamāsasamā rātrīḥ sraṃsane garbhasya || 14.16 || — || 14.15 ||
garbhasraṃsane tu yāvān māso garbhas tāvanty ahorātrāṇi bhavanti | ā pañcamān māsāt garbhasraṃsanam, ata ūrdhvaṃ tu garbhapatanam iti veditavyam | kutaḥ? “tasmāt pañcame māsi garbhā vikriyante” iti brāhmaṇaliṅgāt | tataś ca yāvanmāso garbhaḥ sraṃsate tattulyāny ahorātrāṇi bhavantīty arthaḥ || 14.16 ||
ā caturthād bhavet srāvaḥ pātaḥ pañcamaṣaṣṭhayoḥ |
ata ūrdhvaṃ tu nārīṇāṃ sravaḥ prasava ucyate || iti |
tisro garbhavipadas tāḥ sarvāḥ sraṃsanaśabdenocyante | yāvatithe māse garbhasya sraṃsanaṃ tanmāsasamā rātrīr āśaucaṃ bhavati | atyantasaṃyoge dvitīyā | dvitīyamāsādiyathāmāsasaṃkhyāny ahānīti || 14.15 ||
tryahaṃ vā || 14.17 || — || 14.16 ||
dvaimāsikatraimāsikagarbhasraṃsane tryaham, ūrdhvaṃ tu māsatulyam ity vyavasthā draṣṭavyā | patane ‘pi māsatulyam evāśaucaṃ draṣṭavyam, “garbhe tanmāsasamam ahar āśaucam” iti paiṅgigṛhyavacanāt | āśaucaṃ mātur eva, tadadhikārāt | tathā ca devagṛhyavacanam: “garbhasthe prete mātur eva syād āśaucam” iti | māsatulyanyāyenaiva jananottarakālaṃ mṛtasyāpi daśarātraṃ siddham | garbhasthe prete mātur evety evakārāt jananottarakālaṃ pitror bhavatīti gamyate | tathā ca vyāghraḥ |
bāle mṛte sapiṇḍānāṃ sadyaḥśaucaṃ vidhīyate |
daśāhenaiva dampatyoḥ sodarāṇāṃ tathaiva ca || iti |
sodarāṇāṃ daśāhagrahaṇaṃ nāmakaraṇād ūrdhvam eva draṣṭavyam | tathā ca paiṅgigṛhyasmṛtiḥ: “garbhasthe prete mātur eva syād āśaucaṃ jāta ubhayoḥ | kṛtanāmni sodarāṇāṃ ca | akṛtanāmny apīty eke | dantajāte kṛtacūḍe sarveṣām” ityādi | dantajāte kṛtacaule sarveṣāṃ dvādaśaputrāṇāṃ daśāha ity eva draṣṭavyam | “māsasamā rātrīḥ sraṃsane garbhasya” [GDh 14.16] ity eva siddhe garbhagrahaṇaṃ garbhasaṃnikṛṣṭāyā mātur evedam āśaucam iti niyamārtham || 14.17 ||
dvitīye ‘pi māse tryahaṃ vāpi bhavati | caturṣu tūtkarṣaḥ || 14.16 ||
śrutvā cordhvaṃ daśamyāḥ pakṣiṇīm || 14.18 || — || 14.17 ||
daśamīgrahaṇaṃ sarvāśaucapradarśanārtham | pakṣiṇy ahardvayamadhyagatā rātriḥ, rātridvayamadhyagatam ahar vā | sapiṇḍajananaṃ maraṇaṃ vā śrutvā pakṣiṇy āśaucaṃ bhavati | caśabdāt “sametya cākṛtacaulānāṃ tisṛbhiḥ” ity uktatvāt, kṛtacaulānām idaṃ draṣṭavyam | tatrāpi caturahasaṃbandhinām idaṃ draṣṭavyam | tryahasaṃbandhināṃ tu “deśāntarasthe prete ūrdhvaṃ daśāhāc chrutvā ekarātram āśaucam” [VaDh 4.36] iti vasiṣṭhoktaṃ draṣṭavyam | ekāhasaṃbandhināṃ tu “bāle deśāntarasthe” ity anena sadyaḥśaucam | daśāhasaṃbandhināṃ tu,
atikrānte daśāhe tu trirātram āśucir bhavet | [MDh 5.76] iti manunoktaṃ tryahaṃ draṣṭavyam | sarveṣām ūrdhvaṃ saṃvatsarāt snānam eva,
saṃvatsare vyatīte tu spṛṣṭvaivāpo viśuddhyati | [MDh 5.76] iti manuvacanāt || 14.18 ||

asapiṇḍe yonisaṃbandhe sahādhyāyini ca || 14.19 || — || 14.18 ||
asapiṇḍaḥ samānodakaḥ | tasmin mṛte samānodakānāṃ pakṣiṇī | idam api caturahasaṃbandhinaḥ | ekāhasaṃbandhinas tu sadhyaḥśaucam | “bāle deśāntarasthe” ity anena tryaham asaṃbandhina ekāhaḥ kalpyaḥ | daśāhasaṃbandhinaḥ “tryahāt tūdakadāyinaḥ” [MDh 64] iti tryaham ity eva draṣṭavyam | yonisaṃbandhinaḥ mātulamātṛ-bhāgineyapaitṛṣvasreyāḥ | teṣu mṛteṣu pakṣiṇī | tathā sahādhyāyini ca | sahādhyāyī ekopādhyāyakaḥ | caśabdāt suhṛdi ca || 14.19 ||
sapiṇḍatā yasya nivṛttā so ‘sapiṇḍaḥ samānodakaḥ | yonisaṃbandho mātāmahamātṛṣvasṛtatputrādayaḥ strīṇāṃ prattānāṃ pitrādayaḥ svasrādayaś ca | sahādhyāyī ekasmād upādhyāyād adhītakṛtsnavedaḥ | cakārāt smṛtyantarapaṭhitāḥ pitṛṣvasṛtadapatyādayo ‘nye ca | eteṣu mṛteṣu parasparaṃ pakṣiṇīm āśaucaṃ bhavati | pakṣiṇīkāle tv atīte snānam eva | manus tu samānodake trirātram āha |

pretopasparśane daśarātram āśaucam abhisaṃdhāya cet || 14.22 || — || 14.21 ||
pretasya mṛtasya upasparśane nirharaṇa ity arthaḥ | upaśabdaprayogān na sparśanamātre | daśarātram āśaucam, abhisaṃdhāya ced yadi dṛṣṭam aṅgīkṛtya spṛśati, na dharmārthakāmato vā | saṃsargamātram atrāśaucam, na tu vakṣyamāṇam “adhaḥśayyāsaninaḥ” [GDh 2.27] ityādi, āśaucādhikāre punar āśaucagrahaṇāt | atraiva viṣayavibhāgaḥ pratipattavyaḥ – sapiṇḍānāṃ madhye yaḥ kaścid vahati saṃskartā vā bhavati tasya viśeṣo nāsti tatpratipādakavacanābhāvāt | samānodakānāṃ tu daśarātram,

avaro jaghanyaḥ kṣatriyādi | varṇagrahaṇaṃ pratilomasaṃsarge nāśaucamātram | kiṃ tarhi? prāyaścittam api bhavatīti | yady apy anulomā api na varṇaśabdavācyāḥ, tathāpi “pratilomās tu dharmahīnāḥ” [GDh 4.25] iti pratilomānām eva dharmahīnatvād anulomānāṃ mātṛvat kalpyam iti | pūrvaṃ varṇaṃ brāhmaṇādiṃ tam upaspṛśet vahet, upaśabdaprayogāt | pūrvo vā avaraṃ kṣatriyādim | tatra parasparavahane yac chāva uktam āśaucaṃ tad eva bhavati | tathā kṣatriyasya brāhmaṇasparśane daśarātram | tathā brāhmaṇasya kṣatriyasparśane pañcadaśāha iti | evaṃ sarvatra | āśaucādhikāre punar āśaucagrahaṇaṃ tadvad āśaucārtham, na punaḥ svādhyāyādyabhāvamātram iti | śāvāśaucam iti vaktavye uktagrahaṇaṃ bhojanādivyavahāre ‘pi tadāśaucapraveśārtham | evaṃ ca “anirdaśāhe pāraśave” [VaDh 4.32] ityādi yat smṛtyantare prāyaścittaṃ śrūyate tadā śaucottarakālaṃ draṣṭavyam | atroktam āśaucam iti vaktavye śavagrahaṇaṃ śavanimittasya sarvasyānupraveśārtham | tataḥ dṛṣṭārtham aṅgīkṛtya parasparavahane smṛtyantaroktaṃ draṣṭavyam | tathāha vyāghraḥ |

patito brahmahādiḥ | caṇḍālaḥ uktalakṣaṇaḥ | sūtikā prasūtā asamāptāśaucakālā | udakyā rajasvalā | śavagrahaṇena tannimittaṃ sarvam ucyate | yac chavaṃ ye ca tadāśaucabhājaḥ ye ca kaṭakaraṇaṃ kurvanti ye ca vahanti ye ac saṃskurvantītyādi | upasparśanaśabdaḥ pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyate | patitacaṇḍālasūtikodakyāśavopasparśane spṛṣṭyupasparśane tatspṛṣṭyupasparśaṇa iti | spṛṣṭī patitādīnāṃ sākṣāt spraṣṭā, tatspṛṣṭī patitādispṛṣṭispraṣṭā | upaśabdo buddhipūrvārthaḥ | śavasya buddhipūrvasparśanam alaṃkārādyarthaṃ kevalecchāyā vā | samānajātīyaśavam asamānajātīyaśavaṃ vā viśeṣābhāvāt | abuddhipūrve tu mānavoktaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha |
divākīrtyam udakyāṃ ca patitaṃ sūtikāṃ tathā |

anugamyecchayāpy etaṃ jñātim ajñātim eva vā |
snātvā sacailaṃ spṛṣṭvāgniṃ ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati || iti | [MDh 5.103]
ghṛtaprāśanād ūrdhvam api snānaṃ kecid icchanti | tatra mūlaṃ mṛgyam | yājñavalkyo ‘pi: “spṛṣṭvāgniṃ ghṛtabhuk śuciḥ” [YDh 3.26] ity etāvad evāha | idaṃ sajātīyaviṣayam | brāhmaṇasya kṣatriyānugamane vasiṣṭhoktam: “manuṣyāsthi spṛṣṭvā trirātram āśaucam asthighne tv ahorātraṃ śavānugamane caivam” [VaDh 23.24–26] iti | evam iti trirātrāhorātrayor atideśaḥ | atra kṣatriyānugamana ekarātraṃ vaiśyānugamane trirātram iti vyavasthā | śūdrānugamane tv aṅgirāḥ |
pretībhūtaṃ tu yaḥ śūdraṃ brāhmaṇo jñānadurbalaḥ |
anugacchen nīyamānaṃ trirātraṃ so ‘śucir bhavet ||
trirātre tu tataḥ pūrṇe nadīṃ gatvā samudragām |
prāṇāyāmaśataṃ kṛtvā ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati || iti | kṣatriyavaiśyayor vaiśyaśūdrānugamane brāhmaṇavat kalpyam | kṣtriyasya śūdrānugamana ekarātraṃ prāṇāyāmaśataṃ ca | manuḥ |
nāraṃ spṛṣṭvāsthi sasnehaṃ savāsā jalam āviśet |
ācamyaiva tu niḥsnehaṃ gāṃ spṛṣṭvā vīkṣya vā ravim || iti | [MDh 5.87]
idam abuddhipūrvaviṣayam | vṛddhamanuḥ |
dahanaṃ vahanaṃ cāpi pretasyānyasya garbhavān |
na kuryād ubhayaṃ tatra kuryād eva pituḥ sadā ||
jyeṣṭhasya vānapatyasya mātulasya sutasya vā || iti |
pitur iti mātur apy upalakṣaṇam | āturarodane pāraskaraḥ |
asthisaṃcayanād arvāg ruditvā snānam ācaret |
anatardaśāhe viprasya ūrdhvam ācamanaṃ smṛtam || iti | viprasya mṛtasyāntardaśāhe rudatāṃ sarveṣāṃ varṇānāṃ samānam idam | atra viṣṇuḥ: “sarvasyaiva pretasya bāndhavaiḥ sahāśrupātaṃ kṛtvā snānena | akṛtāsthisaṃcaye sacailasnānena” [ViDh 22.61–62] | śuddhir iti prakaraṇād gamyate | idaṃ kṣatriyadimaraṇe samānāpakṛṣṭānāṃ rodane śūdravarjam | trivarṇaviṣayāturarodane brahmapurāṇe paṭhanti |
anasthisaṃcayo vipro rauti cet kṣatravaiśyayoḥ |
tadā snātaḥ sacailas tu dvitīye ‘hani śudhyati ||
kṛte tu saṃcaye vipraḥ snānenaiva śucir bhavet || iti |
kṣatriyasya vaiśyāturavyañjane ‘py evam evohitavyam | śūdrāturavyañjane pāraskaraḥ |
asthisaṃcayanād arvāg yadi vipro ‘śru pātayet |
mṛte śūdre gṛhaṃ gatvā trirātreṇa viśudhyati ||
asthisaṃcayanād ūrdhvaṃ māso yāvad dvijātayaḥ |
ahorātreṇa śudhyanti vāsasāṃ kṣālanena ca ||
ity alaṃ prasaktānuprasaṅgena || 14.29 ||
śunaś ca || 14.30 ||
upasparśane sacela udakopasparśanāt “śudhyet” iti vartate | spṛṣṭitatspṛṣṭinivṛttyarthaṃ pṛthag grahaṇam | śvagrahaṇaṃ sṛgālādīnām apy upalakṣaṇārtham | cakārād asthi ca, tad api sasneham | yathāha manuḥ |
nāraṃ spṛṣṭvāsthi sasnehaṃ snātvā vipro viśudhyati |
ācamyaiva tu niḥsnehaṃ gāṃ spṛṣṭvā vīkṣya vā ravim || iti | [MDh 5.87]
etat tu abuddhipūrvasparśane | buddhipūrve tu vasiṣṭha āha: “mānuṣāsthi snigdhaṃ spṛṣṭvā trirātram āśaucam asnigdhe tv ahorātram” [VaDh 23.24] iti | cakārād raktādi ca |
kecic chavānugamanam āhuḥ | tatra sacelasnānaṃ ghṛtaprāśanam api draṣṭavyam | tathāha manuḥ |
anugamyecchayā pretaṃ jñātim ajñātim eva vā |
snātvā sacelaḥ spṛṣṭvāgniṃ ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati || iti | [MDh 5.103]
sarveṣāṃ svajātiviṣaya evedam | kṣatriyavaiśyānugamane tu vasiṣṭhoktaṃ draṣṭavyam | tathā cāha: “asnigdhe tv ahorātraṃ śavānugamane caivam” [VaDh 23.25] iti | tatra śavānugamane trirātraikarātayor atideśaḥ | tataś ca kṣatriyānugamane ekarātram, vaiśyānugamane pakṣiṇīṃ śūdrānugamane trirātraṃ prāyaścittaṃ ca draṣṭavyam | tathāha aṅgirāḥ |
pretībhūtaṃ tu yaḥ śūdraṃ brāhmaṇo jñānadurbalaḥ |
nīyamānam anugacchet trirātram aśucir bhavet ||
trirātre tu tataḥ pūrṇe nadīṃ gatvā samudragām |
prāṇāyāmaśataṃ kṛtvā ghṛtam prāśya viśudhyati || iti | kṣatriyasya vaiśyānugamane vaiśyasya śūdrānugamane ca brāhmaṇavat kalpyam | kṣatriyasya śūdrāgamane ekarātrasahitaṃ prāṇāyāmaśataṃ draṣṭavyam || 14.30 ||
upasamastam apy apekṣate | śunaś copasparśane sacailodakopasparśanāc chudhyet | pṛthakkaraṇaṃ tatspṛṣṭinyāyanivṛttyartham || 14.30 ||
yad upahanyād ity eke || 14.31 ||
eke tu yad evāṅgam upaspṛśati śvā tasyaiva prakṣāla-ṇam icchanti | so ‘sya vikalpaḥ – uparikāyasparśane snānam, adhaḥkāyasparśane tu prakṣālanam iti draṣṭavyam | tathā pakṣisparśe ‘pi | tathā jātūkarṇiḥ |
ūrdhvaṃ nābheḥ karau muktvā yad aṅgaṃ spṛśate khagaḥ |
snānaṃ tatra vidhātavyaṃ śeṣe prakṣālya śudhyati || iti | evaṃ ca śvaviṣaye ‘pi karavarjaṃ draṣṭavyam || 14.31 ||
eke tu yad aṅgaṃ śvopahanyāt tasyaiva prakṣālanam icchanti | atra āpastambīyo viśeṣaḥ: “śunopahataḥ sacailo ‘vagāheta | prakṣālya vā taṃ deśam agninā saṃspṛśya punaḥ prakṣālya pādau cācamya prayato bhavati” [ĀpDh 1.15.16–17] iti | ūrdhvāṅgasparśe snānam adhaḥ prakṣālanam iti vyavasthāṃ jātūkarṇya āha |
ūrdhvaṃ nābheḥ karau muktvā spṛśyaty aṅgaṃ kharo yadi |
snānaṃ tatra vidhātavyaṃ śeṣe prakṣālya śudhyati || iti || 14.31 ||
udakadānaṃ sapiṇḍaiḥ kṛtajaṭasya || 14.32 ||
udakadānaṃ sapiṇḍaiḥ kṛtacūḍasya || 14.32 ||
adhikārāt pretasya kṛtajaṭasya kṛtacaulasya sapiṇḍair udakaṃ deyam | na sarvair aśucibhiḥ | udakakriyācoda-nenāśaucam api draṣṭavyam, “udakakriyāśaucaṃ ca” [VaDh 4.9] iti vasiṣṭhena sahanirdeśāt | yasyodakakriyā tasyāśaucam api bhavatīti tatra sahanirdeśasya prayojanam | tadvat smṛtyantarāt trirātraṃ draṣṭavyam | tathāha manuḥ |
nivṛttacailakānāṃ tu trirātrāc chuddhir iṣyate | iti | [MDh 5.66] kṣatriyādīnām apy etat tulyam eva, viśeṣābhāvāt | evaṃ ca vayo’vasthāpekṣayā ye tryahādayaḥ āśaucavikalpāḥ śrūyante te ‘pi sarveṣāṃ tulyarūpeṇa draṣṭavyāḥ, viśeṣābhāvād eva | evaṃ ca ye vṛttādyapekṣayā pañcadaśāhādaya parikalpitāḥ te kṛtopanayane pramīte veditavyāḥ | tathā deśāntarā-śaucaṃ ca | tathā ca vyāghraḥ |
tulyaṃ vayasi sarveṣām atikrānte tathaiva ca |
upanīte tu viṣamaṃ tasminn evātikālajam || iti |
uktād asamāptāśaucakālāt prāg yat śrūyate “yac cheṣaṃ daśarātrasya” [MDh 5.75] ityādi, tad upanītāt prāg api draṣṭavyam | kṛtajaṭasya saṃkāro ‘pi draṣṭavyaḥ smṛtyantarāt | tathāha lokākṣiḥ |
tūṣṇīm evodakaṃ dadyāt tūṣṇīm evāgnim eva ca |
sarveṣāṃ kṛtacūḍānām anyatrāpīcchayā dvayam || iti |
evaṃ ca kṛtajaṭasya niyamato ‘gnisaṃskāraḥ udakadānaṃ ca, akṛtacaulasyāniyama iti draṣṭavyam | tathā ca manuḥ |
nātrivarṣasya kartavyā bāndhavair udakakriyā |
jātadantasya vā kuryur nāmni vāpi kṛt sati || [MDh 5.70] iti || 14.32 ||
kṛtacūḍān tasya pretasya sapiṇḍair udakadānaṃ kartavyaṃ yāvad āśaucam | na tato ‘rvāg iti | agnisaṃskāro ‘py asyaiva | yathāha laugākṣiḥ |
tūṣṇīm evodakaṃ dadyāt tūṣṇīm evāgnim eva ca |
sarveṣāṃ kṛtacūḍānām anyatrāpīcchayā dvayam || iti | evaṃ ca kṛtacūḍasya niyato ‘gnisaṃskāra udakadānaṃ ca | akṛtacūḍasya tv aniyataṃ tad akaraṇe na pratyavāyaḥ | cūḍākaraṇena kālo lakṣyate tṛtīyaṃ varṣam, bahuṣu smṛtiṣu tathā darśanāt | manur api |
nātrivarṣasya kartavyā brāhmaṇair udakakriyā |
jātadantasya vā kuryān nāmni cāpi kṛte sati || iti | [MDh 5.70] agnyudakagrahaṇam aurdhvadehikasyopa-lakṣaṇam | tatra devalo viśeṣam āha |
dvādaśād vatsarād arvāk paugaṇḍamaraṇe sati |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ na syād ekodiṣṭāni kārayet ||
iti || 14.32 ||
tat strīṇāṃ ca || 14.33 ||
tad udakadānam agnisaṃskārasahitam, tadgrahaṇāt | strīṇāṃ ca kartavyam | kṛtajaṭānukarṣaṇārthaś cakāraḥ | āśaucalāghavārthaṃ pṛthag grahaṇam | tat smṛtyantarād avagantavyam | tatra,
aprauḍhāyāṃ tu kanyāyāṃ sadyaḥśaucaṃ vidhīyate |
iti prāk caulakaraṇāt sadyaḥśaucam | prauḍhāśabdena cūḍākaraṇam ucyate |
ahas tv adattakanyāsu bāleṣu ca viśodhanam | [YDh 3.24] iti cūḍākaraṇād ūrdhvaṃ pradānakālāt prāk ekāhaḥ |
strīṇām asaṃskṛtānāṃ tu tryahāc chuddhyanti bāndhavāḥ | [MDh 5.72ab] ity anena pradānakālād ūrdhvam aprattāyāḥ trirātram iti |
yathoktenaiva kalpena śudhyanti tu sanābhayaḥ | [MDh 5.72cd] ity atra daṇḍāpūpikayā strīviṣaye pitror daśāha eva draṣṭavyaḥ | sapiṇḍaviṣaye ‘pi tripūruṣaṃ draṣṭavyam, “aprattānāṃ tu strīṇāṃ tripūruṣam” [VaDh 4.18] iti vasiṣṭhavacanāt || 14.33 ||
tad udakadānaṃ strīṇāṃ ca kṛtacūḍānāṃ kāryam
|| 14.33 ||
eke prattānām || 14.34 ||
udakavāgbhyāṃ prattānām api varapakṣair eva kartavyam ity eke manyante | gautamas tu,
adbhir vācā ca dattāyāḥ mriyetādau varo yadi |
anyasmai vidhivad deyā yathā kanyā tathaiva sā ||
iti kanyāvad ityatideśāt pitrādibhir eva deyam iti | tatra pitṛpakṣābhāve varapakṣaiḥ kartavyam iti draṣṭavyam || 14.34 ||
eke manyante prattānām eva strīṇām udakadānam aprattānāṃ tu naiveti | prattānāṃ ca bhartṛpakṣair deyam || 14.34 ||
idānīm āśauce kathaṃ varteyur ity ata āha |
athāśaucakāle jñātayaḥ kathaṃ varteran |
adhaḥśayyāsanino brahmacāriṇaḥ sarve || 14.35 ||
adhaḥ bhūmāv eva śayīran, āsīraṃś ca, maithunaṃ ca varjayeyuḥ, yāvad āśaucam | sarvagrahaṇaṃ samānodakānām apy evam iti jñāpanārtham || 14.35 ||
bhūmāv eva śarīrann āsīraṃś ca na kaṭāsanādiṣu | maithunaṃ ca varjayeyuḥ | sarvagrahaṇaṃ samānodakārtham || 14.35 ||
na mārjayīran || 14.36 ||
ujjvalaveṣadhāriṇaś ca na syur ity arthaḥ | gātramalā-pakarṣaṇaṃ vā varjayeyuḥ || 14.36 ||
mārjanaṃ gātramalāpakarṣaṇam | tac ca na kuryuḥ || 14.36 ||
māṃsaṃ na bhakṣayeyur ā pradānāt || 14.37 ||
ā śrāddhkaraṇād āmiṣaṃ prāṇātyayādāv api na bhakṣayeyuḥ || 14.37 ||
pradānaṃ śrāddham | ātadantaṃ māṃsaṃ na bhakṣayeyuḥ || 14.37 ||
prathamatṛtīyapañcamasaptamanavameṣūdaka-kriyā || 14.38 ||
prathamatṛtīyasaptamanavameṣūdaka-
kriyā || 14.38 ||
prathamādiṣv ahaḥsu sapiṇḍaiḥ kartavyam, putrais tu pratidinam iti draṣṭavyam | kriyāgrahaṇaṃ gṛhyokto-dakadānavidhyupasaṃgrahaṇārtham | “putrāḥ pretāya sakṛd udakaṃ prohanti” ityādi || 14.38 ||
prathamādiṣv ahaḥsu sapiṇḍaiḥ pretāya tilamiśram udakaṃ deyam evaṃgotrāyaivaṃśarmaṇe pretāyaitat tilodakaṃ dadāmīti | prathame trīn | tṛtīye nava | saptame triṃśat | navame trayaṃstriśat | iti pañcasaptatir jalāñjalayo deyāḥ | ācāras tu prathame ‘hni trayaḥ | dvitīyādiṣv ekottaraṃ dīyate || 14.38 ||
vāsasāṃ ca tyāgaḥ || 14.39 ||
udakadānakāladhāritānāṃ ca vastrāṇāṃ tyāgaḥ | anyāni dhāraṇīyānīty arthaḥ | caśabdāt keśādīni ca | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
dvitīye ‘hani kartavyaṃ kṣurakarma prayatnataḥ |
tṛtīye pañcame vāpi saptame vāpradānataḥ || 14.39 ||
udakadānakāle parihitāni vāsāṃsi prathamatṛtīya-spatamanavameṣu tyājyāni | anyāni krameṇa paridheyāni || 14.39 ||
ante tv antyānām || 14.40 ||
ante navame antyānāṃ śūdrāṇāṃ vāsasāṃ tyāga ity anuvartate | tuśabdo viśeṣārthaḥ | udakadānaṃ prathamādiṣu kartavyam | vāsasāṃ tyāgaḥ anta eveti antyānām iti bahuvacanam anulomānāṃ savyava-hāryāṇām apy evem eveti || 14.40 ||
varṇeṣv antyāyāḥ śūdrās teṣam antye navame ‘hani vāsasāṃ tyāgaḥ || 14.40 ||
dantajanmādi mātāpitṛbhyām || 14.41 ||
dantajanmaprabhṛti niyamato mātāpitṛbhyām udakadānaṃ kartavyam | evaṃ ca tayor api prāg aniyama iti draṣṭavyam || 14.41 ||
dantajanmaprabhṛti putrasya mātāpitarau jalaṃ dadyātām | tūṣṇīṃ mātā || 14.41 ||
bāladeśāntaritapravrajitāsapiṇḍānāṃ sadyaḥśaucam || 14.42 ||
bālaḥ nāmakaraṇāt prāk | tataś ca nāmakaranād ūrdhvaṃ prāg dantajananāt smṛtyantaroktam ekāha draṣṭavyam |
nṝṇām akṛtacaulānām aśuddhir naiśikī smṛtā | iti | [MDh 5.67] dantajananād ūrdhvaṃ prāk cūḍākaraṇāt tryaham |
araṇye kāṣṭhavat tyaktvā kṣapeyuḥ tryaham eva tu | iti | [MDh 5.69] deśāntare yo mṛtaḥ tasminn eva kāle na śrūyate sa deśāntaritaḥ | pravrajitaḥ naiṣṭhiko vānaprasthaḥ parivrājakaś ca | tasmin mṛte asapiṇḍānāṃ sadyaḥśaucam iti | asapiṇḍaḥ samānodakaḥ, tasya cokto viṣāyaḥ || 14.42 ||
bālo ‘kṛtacūḍaḥ | deśāntarito deśena vyavahito deśāntarasthaḥ | pravrajitā naiṣṭhikavānaprasthaparivrājakāḥ | asapiṇḍāḥ samānodakāḥ | teṣāṃ maraṇe jñātīnāṃ sadyaḥśaucaṃ snānena śuddhiḥ | bālaviṣaye yājñavalkyaḥ |
ūnadvivārṣikaṃ pretaṃ nikhanen nodakaṃ tataḥ || [YDh 3.1]
ā dantajanmanaḥ sadya ā cūḍān naiśikī smṛtā |
trirātram ā vratādeśād daśarātram ataḥ param || iti | [YDh 3.23] āṅgirāḥ |
yady apy akṛtacūḍo vai jātadantas tu saṃsthitaḥ |
dāhayitvā tathāpy enam āśaucaṃ tryaham ācaret || iti | manus tu |
ūnadvivārṣikaṃ pretaṃ nidadhyur bāndavā bahiḥ |
alaṃkṛtya śucau bhūmāv asthisaṃcanayād ṛte ||
nāsya kāryo ‘gnisaṃskāro nāsya kāryodakakriyā |
araṇye kāṣṭhavat tyaktvā kṣapet tryaham eva tu || iti | [MDh 5.68–69] āśvalāyanaḥ: “adantajāte parijāta ekāham” iti | āpastambas tu: “mātuś ca yonisaṃ-bandhebhyaḥ pituś cā saptamāt puruṣād yāvatā vā saṃbandho jñāyate teṣāṃ pretṣūdakopasparśanaṃ garbhān parihāpyāparisaṃvatsarān mātāpitarāv eva teṣu hartāraś ca” [ĀpDh 2.15.2–4] iti | eteṣāṃ deśakuladharmāpekṣayā vyavasthā | atra kanyāviṣaya āpastambaḥ:
aprauḍhāyāṃ tu kanyāyāṃ sadyaḥśaucaṃ vidhīyate | iti | aprauḍhā akṛtacūḍā | [yājñavalkyaḥ]:
ahas tv adattakanyāsu bāleṣu ca viśodhanam | iti | [YDh 3.24] | idaṃ caulād ūrdhvam | vyāghra āha |
bāle mṛte sapiṇḍānāṃ sadyaḥśaucaṃ vidhīyate |
daśāhenaiva dampatyoḥ sodarāṇāṃ tathaiva ca || iti |
idaṃ tu sūtakaṃ daśāhāntarmaraṇaviṣayam | tathā ca |
antardaśāhe jātasya śiśor niṣkramaṇaṃ yadi |
sūtakenaiva śuddhiḥ syāt pitroḥ śātātapo ‘bravīt || || 14.42 ||
rājñāṃ ca kāryavirodhāt || 14.43 ||
sadyaḥśaucam iti vartate | rājñām iti bahuvacanaṃ rājasadṛśānām amātyānām api grahaṇārtham | cakārād yasya cecchati | tathā ca manuḥ: “yasya cecchati pārthivaḥ” [MDh 5.95] iti | kāryām rakṣaṇādi tasya virodhaḥ asaṃbhavaḥ || 14.43 ||
rājñaś ca sadyaḥśaucaṃ kāryavirodhāt | kāryaṃ prajārakṣaṇādi | bahuvacananirdeśād ye cānye ‘mātyādayas tatkāryavantas teṣām api | “yasya cecchati pārthivaḥ” [MDh 5.95] iti manuḥ || 14.43 ||
brāhmaṇasya ca svādhyāyānivṛttyarthaṃ svādhyāyānivṛttyartham || 14.44 ||
hetuvacanaṃ vṛttasvādhyāyāpekṣayā āśaucaṃ bhavatīti jñāpanārtham | cakārāt kṣatriyāder api svādhyāya-nimittapraveśārthaṃ pratipāditāḥ viṣayāḥ | dviruktir adhyāyaparisamāptyarthā || 14.44 ||
brāhmaṇasya ca sadyaḥśaucaṃ svādhyāyanivṛttir mā bhūd iti | bahuśiṣyasyādhyāpayata idam uktam | [abhyāso ‘dhyāyasamāptyarthaḥ] | ity āśaucam
|| 14.44 ||

atha śrāddham amāvāśyāyāṃ pitṛbhyo dadyāt || 15.1 ||
atha śrāddham || 15.1 ||
śrāddhaṃ pañcavidham, yathāha vyāghraḥ |
nityaṃ naimittikaṃ kāmyaṃ vṛddhiśrāddhaṃ tathāparam |
pārvaṇaṃ ceti vijñeyaṃ śrāddhaṃ pañcavidhaṃ smṛtam || iti | tatrāhar ahar yat kriyate tan nityam, yathāha manuḥ |
dadyād ahar ahaḥ śrāddham annādyenodakena vā |
payomūlaphalair vāpi pitṛbhyaḥ prītim āvahan || iti | [MDh 3.82] | tathā tatraivoktam |
ekam apy āśayed vipraṃ pitrarthaṃ pāñcayajñike |
na caivātrāśayet kiṃcid vaiśvadevaṃ prati divjam || iti | [MDh 3.83] | tathā ca smṛtyantaram api |
brahmacaryam anadhyāyaṃ muktvā śrāddhaṃ tu naityakam | iti | yad uktaṃ naimittiam edoddiṣṭaṃ ekam evoddiśya kriyamāṇatvād ekoddiṣātam, yathāha kātyāyanaḥ: “athaikoddiṣṭam eko dvijaḥ ekaṃ pātram ekaṃ pavitram eko ‘py arghyaḥ ekaḥ piṇḍo nāvāhanaṃ nāgnaukaraṇam” iti, tad āśaucānantaram ārabhya sapiṇḍīkaraṇāt prāk kartavyam, yathāha laukākṣiḥ:
ekoddiṣṭaṃ tu kartavyaṃ pratimāsaṃ tu vatsaram |
pratisaṃvatsaraṃ caivam ādyam ekādaśe ‘hani || iti |
tathā vyāghro ‘pi |
ekādaśe ‘hni kartavyaṃ tripakṣe ca tathaiva ca |
ṣaṇmāse ca tathā kuryād ekoddiṣṭaṃ prayatnataḥ || iti | tataḥ saṃvatsare paripūrṇe sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ kartavyam | tathā ca jātūkarṇiḥ |
tataḥ saṃvatsare pūrṇe tripakṣe vā tathaiva ca |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ kuryād arvāg vṛddhikaraṃ bhavet || iti | tripakṣa iti āhitāgniviṣayam | kutaḥ? bṛhaspativacanāt | tathāha |
anantaram amāvāsyāṃ daśarātrasya ced bhavet |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ tatra kuryād evāgnimān sutaḥ ||
tadalābhe tripakṣe tu sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ smṛtam || iti |
“tripakṣe sapiṇḍīkaraṇam” iti jābālau śrūyate śrutir iti | sapiṇḍīkaraṇād ūrdhvaṃ saṃkrāntyādau piṇḍatrayanirvāpeṇa śrāddhaṃ kartavyam | pratisaṃvatsare vipanne ahany ekoddiṣṭaṃ kartavyam | yathāha uśanā |
saṃkrāntyām uparāge ca parvāyanamahālaye |
trīn piṇḍān nirvapet tatra vipanne ‘hny ekam eva tu || iti | pratisaṃvatsaram āgrahāyaṇyā ūrdhvam aṣṭakā kartavyā | tathāha gṛhyakāraḥ: “ūrdhvam āgrahāyaṇyās trayoparakṣās teṣām ekaikasminn ekaikāṣṭakā bhavati śākāṣṭakā māṃsāṣṭakāpūpāṣṭakā” ityādi | tatra kāmyaṃ kāmanayā yat kriyate, yathā “ekādaśyāṃ putrakāmaḥ” ityādi | vṛddhiśrāddhaṃ nāndīmukham | pārvaṇam nāma māsiśrāddham | atha vā piṇḍatrayanirvāpeṇa yad yat kriyate aṣṭakādi śeṣaṃ sarvaṃ tannaimittikam iti |
asmin pañcavidhe sthite – “atha śrāddham amāvāsyāyāṃ pitṛbhyo dadyāt” | athānantaraṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇāt | kutaḥ? “amāvāsyāyāṃ pitṛbhyaḥ śrāddhaṃ dadyād” ity ārambhāt, idaṃ māsiśrāddham iti jñātaṃ śakyatvāt, smṛtyantare sapiṇḍīkaraṇād arvāk māsiśrāddhapratiṣedhāt | yathāha bṛhaspatiḥ |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇād arvāk pārvaṇaṃ yaḥ karoti tu |
krimir bhūtvā śvaviṣṭhāyāṃ pitṛbhiḥ saha majjati || iti | evaṃ tarhi sapiṇḍīkaraṇād arvāg yāni śrāddhāni tāny eva vaktavyānīti cet, keṣāṃcit tadabhāvasūcanārtham anantaraṃ pārvaṇam eva kartavyam iti | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
yatīnāṃ tu mṛtānāṃ ca kriyā kācin na dṛśyate |
ahany ekādaśe prāpte pārvaṇaṃ tu vidhīyate ||
naiṣṭhikānāṃ vanasthānāṃ muktānāṃ ca tathaiva ca |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ noktaṃ ekoddiṣṭaṃ tathaiva ca || iti |
śrāddham iti karmanāmadheyam, atiśayavacchraddhā-sādhyatvāt | śraddadhāna eva hi pratipadyate, yathā “putrair dattenānnena yatratatrasthāḥ pitaras tadupabhogena sukham āsate” ity arthavādadarśanāt | amāvāsyāyām, sūryācandramasoḥ parasmin saṃnikarṣe pitṛbhyaḥ pitṝn uddiśya, bahuvacanāt pitāmahaprapitā-mahayor api deyam || 15.1 ||
athaśabdo ‘dhikārārthaḥ | śrāddhaṃ nāma karmādhikriyate | śraddhā yatra vidyate tac chrāddham | tac ca pañcavidham |
nityaṃ naimittikaṃ kāmyaṃ vṛddhiśrāddham athāparam |
pārvaṇaṃ ceti vijñeyaṃ śrāddhaṃ pañcavidhaṃ budhaiḥ ||
tatra nityaṃ manur āha |
dadyād ahar ahaḥ śrāddham annādyenodakena vā |
payomulaphalair vāpi pitṛbhyaḥ prītim āvahan ||
ekam apy āśayed vipraṃ pitrarthe pāñcayājñike |
na caivātrāśayet kiṃcid vaiśvadevaṃ pratidvijam || iti | [MDh 3.82–83] | grahaṇādiṣu yat kriyate tan naimittikam | tasya prayogaḥ pārvaṇavat | ekoddiṣṭaṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇam aṣṭakety etāny api naimittikāny eva | tatraikoddiṣṭaṃ ṣoḍaśavidham |
mṛte ‘hani tu kartavyaṃ pratimāsaṃ tu vatsaram |
pratisaṃvatsaraṃ caivamādyam ekādaśe ‘hani || [YDh 1.256] iti yājñavalkyaḥ | ekoddiṣṭaṃ tu kartavyam ity ādyapāde laugākṣiḥ | vyāghraḥ |
ekādaśe ‘hni kartavyaṃ tripakṣe ca tathaiva ca |
ṣaṇmāse ca tataḥ kuryād ekoddiṣṭaṃ prayatnataḥ || iti |
tataḥ saṃvatsare pūrṇe tripakṣe vā tathaiva ca |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ kuryād arvāg vā vṛddhisaṃbhave || iti | jātūkarṇyaḥ |
caturthe pañcame caiva navamaikādaśe tathā |
yad annaṃ dīyate jantos tan navaśrāddham ucyate ||
iti ca paṭhanti | varṇānupūrvyavyavasthitāni catvāry etāni | tad eva śrāddham ekam ekādaśe ‘hanīti pradhānam | ekādaśagrahaṇam āśaucāntopalakṣaṇam | tatra ca māse tadaiva māsikam | sapiṇḍīkaraṇe pretasyaikoddiṣṭaṃ pitradibhyaḥ pārvaṇam | tatrāpi māse tad eva māsikam | madhye daśa māsikāni tripakṣaṣāṇmāsikayoḥ pratisaṃvatsaraṃ cāparaṃ mṛtāhe sāṃvatsarikam iti ṣoḍaśaikoddiṣṭāni | paṭhanti ca |
navatripakṣaṣaṇmāsyasāṃvatsarikamāsikam |
śrāddhaiḥ ṣoḍaśabhiḥ pretaḥ piśācatvaṃ vimuñcati ||
yasyaitāni na kurvanti ekoddiṣṭāni ṣoḍaśa |
piśācatvaṃ sthiraṃ tasya kṛtaiḥ śrāddhaśatair api || iti | hemantaśiśirayoś caturṇām aparapakṣāṇām aṣṭamīṣv aṣṭakā ekasyāṃ vā | prathame ‘hani kriyamāṇe stryapatyaṃ jāyata ityādi kāmasaṃyogena kriyamāṇaṃ kāmyam | tad api pārvaṇavad eva | vivāhādivṛddhikarmasu pūrvedyuḥ kartavyaṃ vṛddhiśrāddham | tasmāt pitṛbhyaḥ pūrvedyuḥ kriyata iti brāhmaṇamūlam | tatra yugmā brāhmaṇā yavās tilārthāḥ | anyat pārvaṇavat | pārvaṇaṃ tu vakṣyate
|| 15.1 ||
amāvāsyāyāṃ pitṛbhyo dadyāt || 15.2 ||
sūryācandramasau yasyāṃ saha vasataḥ sāmāvāsyā | pitṛbhya iti bahuvacanāt pitṛpitāmahaprapitāmahabhyo dadyāt | dānaprakāro gṛhyeṣūkāḥ “homo brāhmaṇabhojanaṃ piṇḍanirvapaṇam” iti | iha tu brāhmaṇabhojane viśeṣaḥ || 15.2 ||
pañcamīprabhṛti vāparakṣasya yathāśrāddham || 15.2 ||
pañcamīprabhṛtiṣu vāparapakṣasya || 15.3 ||
kṛṣṇapakṣasya vā pañcamyā ārabhya dadyāt | yathāśrāddhaṃ yathāśraddhāsti tathā kartavyam ity arthaḥ | aṣṭakāśrāddhaṃ pañcamīprabhṛti dadyād ity abhiprāyaḥ | aparapakṣagrahaṇam amāvāsyādhikārāt pūrvapakṣe mā bhūd iti || 15.2 ||
aparapakṣaḥ kṛṣṇapakṣaḥ | tasya pañcamīprabhṛti vā dadyāt || 15.3 ||
sarvasmin vā || 15.3 ||
yathāśraddhaṃ sarvasmin vā || 15.4 ||
sarvasmin vāparapakṣe pratipatprabhṛti vā kuryād ity arthaḥ | prabhūtadravyasyāyaṃ draṣṭavyaḥ || 15.3 ||
sarvasmin vāparapakṣe dadyād yathāśraddhaṃ yathā śraddhā bhavatīti || 15.4 ||
dravyadeśabrāhmaṇasaṃnidhāne vā || 15.4 ||
dravyadeśabrāhmaṇasaṃnidhāne vā kālaniyamaḥ || 15.5 ||
dravyasya rajatādeḥ | tathā ca vyāghraḥ |
rajataṃ kālaśākaṃ ca khaḍgamatsyāmiṣaṃ madhu |
ānantyāyaiva kalpyante munyannāni ca sarvaśaḥ || iti | deśasya puṣkarādeḥ | yathāha vyāghraḥ |
puṣkareṣv akṣayaṃ śrāddhaṃ kurukṣetre tathaiva ca |
nadyāṃ madhodadhau caiva hrade goṣṭhe girau tathā || iti | brāhmaṇānāṃ yatīnāṃ paṅktipāvanānām | yathā ca vyāsah |
svadhā ca rājataṃ pātraṃ dauhitraḥ kutapas tilāḥ |
vastraṃ ca pāvanīyāni tridaṇḍī yatir eva ca || iti |
saṃnidhāne upapattau | pūrvapakṣe vā kuryād ity abhiprāyaḥ || 15.4 ||
“tilamāṣa” [GDh 15.15] ity ārabhya vakṣyamāṇaṃ tat tat praśastaṃ dravyam | deśo gayāpuṣkarādiḥ |
puṣkareṣv akṣayaṃ śrāddhaṃ kurukṣetre tathaiva ca |
dadyān mahodadhau caiva hradagoṣthe girau tathā ||
iti vyāsaḥ |
yad dadāti gayāsthaś ca sarvam ānantyam aśnute | [YDh 1.261] iti yājñavalkyaḥ | brāhmaṇāḥ paṅktipāvanā vakṣyamāṇāḥ ṣaḍaṅgavidādayaḥ | eteṣāṃ dravyādīnāṃ saṃnidhāne samavāye kālaniyamaḥ saṃnidhir eva kāla iti | vāśabdo vikalpārthaḥ || 15.5 ||
kālaniyamaḥ śaktitaḥ || 15.5 ||
śaktitaḥ prakarṣed guṇasaṃskārvidhir annasya || 15.6 ||
yadā asya dravyāder asaṃbhavo ‘tyantaṃ tadā ūrdhvam āgrahāyaṇyā ity uktaḥ kālaniyamo nāsti | yasmin māse atyantasaṃbhavas tasminn eva kartavyam ity abhiprāyaḥ | dravyādyasaṃbhave yathā pakṣaviśeṣo nāsti tathā māsaviśeṣo ‘pi nāstīty abhiprāyaḥ | evaṃ cānenaiva nyāyena tithiniyamasyāpy abhāvaḥ pratipāditaḥ || 15.5 ||
annasya bhakṣyabhojyor guṇavidhayaḥ pāyasatvaviśadasiddhatvādayaḥ | saṃskāravidhayo bharjanamarīcījīrakalavaṇādibhiḥ surabhīkaraṇādayaḥ | etān yathāśakti prakarṣet prakṛṣṭān kuryāt || 15.6 ||
sarvadā ca śrāddhe |
prakarṣed guṇasaṃskāravidhīn annasya
|| 15.6 ||
prakarṣed atiśayena kuryāt, annasya bhakṣya-bhojyādeḥ, guṇān jātiviśeṣān śākāpūpādīn saṃskārān susiddhatvasurabhitvādīn vidhīn darbhatilāpa-savyatvādīn || 15.6 ||
navāvarān bhojayet || 15.7 ||
navāvarān bhojayed ayujaḥ || 15.7 ||
sati sāmarthye evam || 15.7 ||
navasaṃkhyāvarā yeṣāṃ te navāvarās tāvanto brāhma-ṇān bhojayet | ayujo ‘yugmasaṃkhyān | navapakṣe pitus trīn pitāmahasya trīn prapitāmahasya trīn | avara-grahaṇād adhikā api bhavanti pañcadaśaikaviṃśatir ity-ādayaḥ | ayuja iti vacanād dvādaśādivyāvṛttiḥ || 15.7 ||
ayujo vā yathotsāhaṃ vā || 15.8 ||
yathotsāhaṃ vā || 15.8 ||
ayugmān yathotsāhaṃ yathāsāmarthyam, arvāg navabhyaḥ, pūrvatrāvaragrahaṇāt, ūrdhvaṃ navabhyaḥ prāptyabhāvāt |
nanu ca ayujo vety eva vaktavyam | tataś cordhvaṃ navabhyo ‘prāpitatvāt ūnaprāpaṇārtha evārambhaḥ syāt | tataś cāsāmarthyakṛta eva vikalpo bhaviṣyatīti yathotsāham ity atiriktam |
ucyate – utsāhe ‘pi sati satkriyādyavirodhena bhojayitavyam iti | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
dvau daive pitṛkārye trīn ekaikam ubhayatra vā |
bhojayet susamṛddho ‘pi na prasajyeta vistare ||
satkriyāṃ deśakālau ca śaucaṃ brāhmaṇasaṃpadam |
pañcaitān vistaro hanti tasmāt taṃ parivarjayet || iti | [VaDh 11.27–28] | devāḥ viśvedevāḥ tadaṅgabhūtāḥ | yathāha vyāsaḥ |
dakṣasya duhitā sādhvī viśvā nāma prakīrtitā |
tasyāḥ putrā mahātmāno viśve devā mahābalāḥ ||
kratur dakṣo vasuḥ satyaḥ kālaḥ kāmyaḥ śucī ruciḥ |
purūravādravau ceti vijñeyās te daśāmarāḥ ||
iṣṭiśrāddhe kratudakṣāv aṣṭakyāṃ kālakāmyakau |
nāndīmukhe vasusatyau kāmye cāpi śucī ruciḥ ||
purūravādravau caiva pārvaṇe tu prakīrtitau ||
āhvayen nāmatas teṣāṃ tatra tatra niyogataḥ || iti |
tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
devādyantaṃ bhavec chrāddhaṃ pitrādyantaṃ na tad bhavet |
pitrādyantaṃ yadi bhavet tad vai rakṣāṃsi bhuñjate || iti | tathā bhārgavīyam api |
eka eva yadā vipro dvitīyo nopapadyate |
pitṝṇāṃ brāhmaṇe yojyaṃ daivaṃ haste niyojayet || iti | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
dvau daive ‘tharvaṇau viprau prāṅmukhau viniveśayet |
trīṃs tathodaṅmukhān pitrye ṛgyajuḥsāmagān dvijān || iti || 15.8 ||
yathāsāmarthyaṃ navabhyo ‘rvāg api bhojayet | ayuja ity anuvṛttes trīn eva | tathā ca āpastambaḥ: “ayugmāṃs tryavarān” [?] iti | śāstrāntareṣu viśvebhyo devebhyo brāhmaṇabhojanam āmnātaṃ mātāmahānāṃ ca | yathāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
dvau daive prāk trayaḥ pitrya udag ekaikam eva vā |
mātāmahānām apy evaṃ tantraṃ vā vaiśvadevikam || iti | [YDh 1.228] | daive dvau brāhmaṇau prāṅmukhāv upaveśyau | pitrarthe tatrodaṅmukhāḥ | ekaikasyaikam eva veti | devānām ekaḥ pitrādīnāṃ trayāṇām eka iti | mātāmahānām apy evaṃ pitṛśrāddavat | dvau daive mātāmahādyarthe trayaḥ | vaiśvadevikaṃ tantraṃ vā bhavati pituḥ śrāddhasya mātāmahaśrāddasya ca
|| 15.8 ||
kīdṛśān brāhmaṇān bhojayed ity āha |
kīdṛśān bhojayet | tatrāha |
śrotriyān vāgrūpavayaḥśīlasaṃpannān || 15.9 ||
śrotriyān chando’dhyāyinaḥ, vāksaṃpannān saṃskṛta-bhāṣiṇaḥ, rūpasaṃpannān manojñavapuṣaḥ, vayaḥ-saṃpannān pariṇatavayasaḥ, śīlasaṃpannān praśānta-manasaḥ, evaṃguṇān brāhmaṇān bhojayet || 15.9 ||
śrotriyān adhītavedān | vāksaṃpattiḥ suśikṣitaṃ vākyaṃ saṃskṛtabhāṣaṇādi | rūpasaṃpannān saumyaveṣān anyūnān anadhikāṅgāñ śvitrādyadūṣitān vayaḥsaṃpannān anatibālān | śīlam antaḥkaraṇaśuddhis tatsaṃpannān | evaṃguṇān bhojayet || 15.9 ||
vayaḥsaṃpannatvasyāpavādam āha |
yuvabhyo dānaṃ prathamam || 15.10 ||
yat prathamam ekoddiṣṭaṃ tat taruṇebhyo deyam
|| 15.10 ||
evaṃguṇebhyo yuvabhyaḥ śrāddhadānaṃ mukhyaḥ kalpaḥ || 15.10 ||
eke pitṛvat || 15.11 ||
yadvayasaḥ pitrādayaḥ pramītāḥ tadvayobhya eva dadyād ity eke manyante | sapiṇḍīkaraṇe yad ekoddi-ṣṭaṃ tadviṣayam idaṃ veditavyam | sapiṇḍīkaraṇe ekoddiṣṭam uktam eva smṛtyantare | yathāha yamaḥ |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇe prāpte kuryāc chrāddadvayaṃ tathā |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇe viprān bhojayet pārvaṇe yathā ||
pṛthak ca kalpayed annam ekoddiṣṭavidhānataḥ || iti | atha vā pitṛvad iti pitaram uddiśya taruṇaṃ pitāmaham uddiśya vṛddhaṃ prapitāmaham uddiśya vṛddhataraṃ bhojayed iti || 15.11 ||
eke manyante pitṛvat pitrādyanurūpaṃ dānam iti | yathā – pitre taruṇāḥ pitāmahāya vṛddhāḥ prapitāmahāya vṛddhatarā iti || 15.11 ||
na ca tena mitrakarma kuryāt || 15.12 ||
tena śrāddhena na mitrakāryaṃ kuryād anyonyam | cakārāt amitrasaṃvaraṇam api || 15.12 ||
na ca tena śrāddhena mitrakarma kuryāt | yena maitrī kāryā tasminn arthāpekṣitaṃ na bhojayet | mitralobha-kārārthaṃ na bhojayed ity arthaḥ | āpastambas tu “anarthāpekṣo bhojayet” iti viśeṣeṇāha || 15.12 ||
putrābhāve sapiṇḍā mātṛsapiṇḍāḥ śiṣyāś ca dadyuḥ || 15.13 ||
ayaṃ tāvat prathamaḥ kalpaḥ, yat putrāḥ śrāddhaṃ kurvantīti | tadabhāve tu sapiṇḍā uktāḥ | tadabhāve mātṛsapiṇḍāḥ mātulādayaḥ | tadabhāve śiṣyāḥ saṃbandhiśabdatvāt ācāryāya, caśabdāḍ yājyaś ca ṛtvija iti | ekoddiṣṭaviṣayaṃ caitat, mātṛsapiṇḍaśiṣya-yājyānāṃ dātṛtvenopadeśāt | na hy ekasya mātṛ-sapiṇḍaḥ tatpitur api mātṛsapiṇḍo bhavati | evaṃ śiṣyayājyāv apīti | evaṃ ca sapiṇḍīkaraṇādāv anyaḥ kramo draṣṭavyaḥ | yathāha uśanā: “putrābhāve sapiṇḍāḥ samānodakāḥ sagotrāḥ samānārṣeyāś ca” ityādi | ekenaivaikoddiṣṭaṃ kāryaṃ nāśaucavat sarvair iti visamāsaḥ || 15.13 ||
putrā dadyur iti prathamaḥ kalpaḥ | tadabhāve sapiṇḍā bhrātṛtatputrādayaḥ | tadabhāve mātṛsapiṇḍā mātṛ-bhrātṛtatputrādayaḥ | tadabhāve śiṣyaḥ || 15.13 ||
tadabhāve ṛtvigācāryau || 15.14 ||
ṛtvig yājyāya ācāryaḥ śiṣyāya tadabhāve pūrvasyābhāve | pṛthakkaraṇe śrāddhaṃ kurvatā ṛtvigācāryāṇāṃ prāyaścittābhāvajñāpanārtham | evaṃ ca pūrveṣām avaśyaṃ prāyaścittaṃ kartavyam iti draṣṭavyam | ṛtvigācāryāṇāṃ śrāddhaṃ kurvatām abhyudayaś ca bhavati codanāsāmarthyāt | tadabhāvagrahaṇaṃ pūrvatra "mātṛsapiṇḍāḥ śiṣyāś ca" [GDh 15.13] ity atrāpy adhikārajñāpanārtham | yogyānām eva śrāddhaṃ draṣṭavyam | kutaḥ? smṛtyantarasāmarthyāt | yathāha prajāpatiḥ |
gobrāhmaṇahatānāṃ ca vidyutā pārthivena ca |
ātmanas tyāgināṃ caiva patitānāṃ tathaiva ca ||
sarpair hatānāṃ viprāṇāṃ carantīnāṃ ca kāmataḥ |
pāṣaṇḍam āśritānāṃ ca pretakarma na vidyate ||
snehāt kāryabhayādibhyo yadi kuryān narādhamaḥ |
caṇḍālena hatasyāpi tasya vakṣyāmi niṣkṛtim ||
eṣām anyatamaṃ pretaṃ yo vaheta daheta vā |
caret sāntapanaṃ kṛcchraṃ matyā cet taptakṛcchrakam ||
tacchavaṃ kevalaṃ spṛṣṭvā pātayitvāśru vā tathā |
ekarātraṃ tu nāśnīyāt trirātraṃ buddhipūrvake ||
śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā caret kṛcchraṃ matyā sāntapanaṃ caret |
tathā śrāddhabhujāṃ caiva dviguṇaṃ brahmacāriṇām || iti || 15.14 ||
śiṣyābhāva ṛtvik, tadabhāva ācāryaś ca dadyur iti
|| 15.14 ||
na bhojayet stenaklībapatitanāstikatadvṛtti-vīrahāgredidhiṣūdidhiṣūpatistrīgrāma-yājakājapālotsṛṣṭāgnimadyapakucarakūṭasākṣi-prātihārikān || 15.15 ||
na bhojayet stenaklībapatitanāstikatadvṛtti-vīrahāgredidhiṣupatistrīgrāma-yājakājāpālotsṛṣṭāgnimadyapakucara-kūṭasākṣiprātihārikān || 15.15 ||
śrotriyādiguṇayogenaiṣāṃ prāptau satyāṃ pratiṣi-dhyate | steno hiraṇyastenādayaḥ, tasya patitatvād eva pratiṣedhāt | klībaḥ bhagnotsāhaḥ | tathā ca bhagavān vāsudevaḥ: “mā klaibhyaṃ gaccha kaunteya” [BhG 2.3] ityādi, na tṛtīyaprakṛtiḥ, tasya śrotriyatvābhāvena prāptyabhāvāt | patito brahmahādiḥ | nāstikaḥ paralokā-bhāvādyavasthitaprajñaḥ, so ‘pi manoduṣṭaḥ, kriyāduṣ-ṭasya tu ninditakarmābhyāsāt patitatvopadeśāt | tadvṛttiḥ tenaiva ca nāstikyena ya upajīvati lokāyata-śāstradvāreṇa | vīrahā puruṣamātraghātakaḥ, “avīrāyāś ca yoṣitaḥ” [MDh 4.213] ityādau puruṣe vīraśabdapra-yogāt | vṛthā somābhiṣavakṛd vā “vīrahā vā eṣa devānāṃ yaḥ somam abhiṣuṇoti” iti śrutidarśanāt | agredidhiṣūdidhiṣūpatiḥ, patiśabdaḥ pratyekam abhi-saṃbadhyate | jyeṣṭhāyāḥ pūrvaṃ prattā agredidhiṣūḥ, punaḥ sā jyeṣṭhā didhiṣūḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
jyeṣṭhāyāṃ yady anūḍhāyāṃ kanyā yā tūhyate ‘nujā |
sā cāgredidhiṣūr jñeyā pūrvā tu didhiṣūḥ smṛtā || iti |
tayor bhartā | atha vā patiśabdayuktaḥ sa eva, yathāha prajāpatiḥ |
bhrātur mṛtasya bhāryāyāṃ yo ‘nurajyeta kāmataḥ |
dharmeṇāpi niyuktāyāṃ sa jñeyo didhiṣūpatiḥ ||
sa caiva jīvato bhrātuḥ sa cāgredidhiṣūḥ smṛtaḥ || iti |
vyāghoktau vā |
parapūrvāpatiṃ dhīrā vadanti didhiṣūpatim |
iṣṭo ‘gredidhiṣūr vipraḥ saiva yasya kuṭumbinī ||
strīgrāmayājakaḥ, yājakaśabdaḥ pratyekam abhisaṃ-badhyate | strīṇāṃ kāmadevādi prati yo yājayati sa strīyājakaḥ | grāmayājako bahuyājakaḥ | ajapālaḥ ajārakṣaṇopajīvakaḥ | utsṛṣṭāgniḥ śāstreṇa vinā śrautasya smārtasya vā agneḥ tyāgaṃ ya karoti sa utsṛṣṭāgniḥ | madyapaḥ nālikerāder madyasya rakaṣaṇa-dvāreṇopajīvakaḥ, surāvyatiriktamadyabhoktā vā | kucaraḥ kutsitācāraḥ kutsitācāratvam api putrācārya-tvadevatādyarcanayācanaśīlatvādi | kūṭasākṣī prasiddhaḥ | prātihārikaḥ indrajālopajīvakaḥ | etān na bhojayet | patitānāṃ sparśapratiṣedhād eva bhojanapratiṣedhe siddhe kṛtaprāyaścittānāṃ bhojanapratiṣedhārtha ārambhaḥ || 15.15 ||
steno hiraṇyastenaḥ | klībo moghavīryo na tṛtīyāprakṛtiḥ | aśrotriyatvāt patito brahmahādiḥ | nāstikaḥ pretyabhāvāpavādī | tadvṛttis nāstikavṛttiḥ | pretyabhāvam aṅgīkṛtyāpi yas tadanukūlaṃ na ceṣṭate saṃsargavaśāt | vīrahā yo buddhipūrvam agnīn udvāsayīta satyām apy upapattau | śrūyate hi — “vīrahā eṣa devānāṃ yo ‘gnim udvāsayate” iti | agredidhiṣū iti dīrghāntaṃ kecit paṭhanti | patiśabdaḥ pratyekaṃ saṃbadhyate | agredidhiṣupatir didhiṣupatir iti |
jyeṣṭhāyāṃ yady anūḍhāyāṃ kanyāyām ūhyate ‘nujā |
sā tv agredidhiṣūr jñeyā pūrvā tu didhiṣūḥ smṛtā || iti |
tayoḥ patī | naighaṭukās tu –
punarbhūr didhiṣūrūḍhā dvis tasyā didhiṣuḥ patiḥ |
sa tu dvijo ‘gredidhiṣuḥ saiva yasya kuṭumbinī || iti |
strīgrāmayājakaḥ | strīṇāṃ vratānām upadeṣṭānuṣṭhā-payitā sa strīyājakaḥ | grāmayājako bahuyājakaḥ | ajāpālo ‘ajārakṣaṇajīvakaḥ | utsṛṣṭāgnir āśaucādy-anupapattyā pramādād vā vicchinnāgniḥ | madyapaḥ surāvyatiriktamadakaradravyasya pātā | surāpas tu patitatvenoktaḥ | kucaraḥ kutsitācāraḥ | sākṣye ‘nṛtavaktā kūṭasākṣī | pratihāriko dvārapālavṛttiḥ | etān na bhojayet | yeṣāṃ patitādīnāṃ darśanasparśanādikaṃ pratiṣiddhaṃ teṣāṃ pratiṣedhaḥ kṛtaprāyaścittānām api varjanārthaḥ || 15.15 ||

H reads: “śyāvadantaśvitri”
kuṇḍagrahaṇaṃ golakasyāpy upalakṣaṇam, tasyānnaṃ yo ‘śnāti sa kuṇḍāśī | kuṇḍāśipratiṣedhāc cārthataḥ kuṇḍagolakayor api pratiṣedhaḥ siddhaḥ | tayoḥ svarūpaḥ smṛtyantare jñātavyam | yathāha manuḥ |
paradāreṣu jāyete dvau sutau kuṇḍagolakau |

kāmato retasaḥ sekaṃ vratasthasya dvijanmanaḥ |
atikramaṃ vratasyāhur dharmajñā brahmavādinaḥ || [MDh 11.120] | gaṇapreṣyaḥ gaṇānāṃ preṣaṇakṛt | agamyāgāmī kanyādūṣakaḥ | samānapravarastrīgāmīty eke | “sakhisayonisagotrāśiṣyabhāryāsnuṣāyāṃ gavi ca talpasamaḥ” [GDh 23.12] iti sāmyopadeśāt patitagrahaṇena gṛhyata iti | hiṃsraḥ prāṇināṃ vadharuciḥ | parivittiḥ, jyeṣṭhe akṛtavivāhe kanīyān yo vivāhaḥ karoti sa parivettā, tataḥ sa jyeṣṭhaḥ parivittiḥ | paryāhitaḥ, yasminn akṛtādhāne kanīyān ādhānaṃ karoti sa paryāhitaḥ, kaniṣṭhaḥ paryādhātā |
nanu ca parivittiparivettṛśabdau krameṇa paryāhitaparyādhātror api vartete, smṛtyantare tathā dṛṣṭatvāt | yathāha manuḥ |

ityādi | tyaktātmā sāhasikaḥ | śastrapāṇir ity eke | durvālaḥ śipiviṣṭaḥ | hīnaprajanana ity eke | kunakhī akāraṇena vinaṣṭanakhaḥ | śyāvadan kṛṣṇadaśanaḥ rogeṇa vinā | paunarbhavaḥ svairiṇīputraḥ | kitavo dyūtakṛt | mṛnmayahastyaśvādivyavahārasyāpy upalakṣaṇam | ajapa upanītamātro ‘śrotriyaḥ | rājapreṣyo rājñaḥ preṣaṇakṛt dūtādiḥ | prātirūpikaḥ kūṭatulāmānādivyāvahārikaḥ yena kenacit prakāreṇa vañcako vā | śūdrāpatiḥ saiva bhāryā yasya | nirākṛtiḥ na kvacid āśrame vyavasthitaḥ | kilāsī kuṣṭhī bhūmihartā vā | kusīdī vārdhuṣikaḥ pratiṣiddena mārgeṇa | vaṇik sati saṃbhave vaiśyavṛttyupajīvī | śilpopajīvī takṣacitrakarmādyupajīvī teṣāṃ sakāśe tadvidyāśikṣakaś ca, upaśabdaprayogāt | jyāśīlo dhanurvedopajīvī | vāditraśīlo vaṃśavīṇādyupajīvī | tālaśīlo bheryāditāḍanopajīvī | nṛttaśīlo nṛttopajīvī | gītaśīlo gītopajīvī | etān na bhojayet | etasmād eva pratiṣedhāt eteṣām akāryakāritvam api siddham | ataḥ steyādi na kartavyam || 15.17 ||
