tasmāt pariśrite dadyāt || 15.25 || — || 15.26 ||
yasmād evaṃ tasmāt pracchannapradeśe dadyāt | hetu-vacanaṃ śrāddhād anyatrāpi pariśrita eva bhoktavyam iti || 15.25 ||
pariśrayaṇaṃ tiraskariṇyādinā vyavadhānam || 15.26 ||
tadaśaktau |
tilair vā vikiret || 15.26 || — || 15.27 ||
aparivṛtadoṣaśamanaṃ bhavati | smṛtyantarasāmārthyāt pitṝṇāṃ ca tṛptir bhavati | yathāha bhṛguḥ |
pānīyam api yad dattaṃ tilair miśraṃ dvijasya tu |
pitṛbhyaḥ kāmadhuk tat syāt pitṛgrāhyam idaṃ tataḥ || || 15.26 ||
atra bhṛguḥ |
pānīyam api yad dattaṃ tilair miśraṃ dvijasya tu |
pitṛbhyaḥ kāmadhuk tat syāt pitṛguhyam idaṃ tataḥ || iti || 15.27 ||
paṅktipāvano vā śamayet || 15.27 || — 15.28 ||
paṅktipāvano vakṣyamāṇaḥ, aparivṛtadoṣaṃ śamayet
|| 15.27 ||
paṅktir yena pāvyate sa paṅktipāvanaḥ | śvādyavekṣaṇe yo doṣas taṃ śamayet || 15.28 ||
sa kaḥ punar asau tam āha |
paṅktipāvanāḥ ṣaḍaṅgavit jyeṣṭhasāmagas triṇāciketas trimadhus trisuparṇaḥ pañcāgniḥ snātako mantrabrāhmaṇavid dharmajño brahmadeyānusantāna iti || 15.28 || — || 15.29 ||
H reads “jyeṣṭhasāmikas”
ete ca sati śrotriyatve adhikaguṇayogāt paṅktiṃ śodhayanti nirdoṣāṃ kurvantīti paṅktipāvanāḥ | ṣaḍaṅgavit ṣaṇṇām aṅgānām anyatamasyārthābhijñaḥ | tathā purāṇe |
caturdaśānāṃ vidyānām ekasyāḥ pārago ‘pi yaḥ |
ātmakaivalyavic caiva sarve te paṅktipāvanāḥ || iti |
jyeṣṭhasāmagaḥ, jyeṣṭhasāma chandogānāṃ sāmaviśeṣaḥ, tadadhyāyī jyeṣṭhasāmagaḥ | triṇāciketo nāma adhvaryūṇām ṛgviśeṣaḥ | trimadhur bahvṛcānāṃ sūktaviśeṣaḥ | trisuparṇo bahvṛcānāṃ taittirīyāṇāṃ ca | pañcāgniḥ sabhyāvasathyābhyāṃ saha | snātako vidyāvratasnātakaḥ | mantrabrāhmaṇavit vināpy aṅgavijñānena | dharmajño dharmaśāstrāthajñaḥ | brahmadeyānusantāno brāhmavivāhoḍhāsantatiḥ | itikāraḥ prakāravācaḥ | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “triṇāciketaḥ pañcāgnis trisuparṇaś caturmedhā vājasaneyī ṣaḍaṅgavic chandogo jyeṣṭhasāmiko mantrabrāhmaṇavidyaḥ svadharmān adhīte yasya ca daśapuruṣaṃ mātṛpitṛvaśaḥ śrotriyo vijñāyate vidvāṃsaḥ snātakāś caite paṅktipāvanā bhavanti” [VaDh 3.19] ity evamādīnāṃ smṛtyantaroktānām apy upalakṣaṇārtham || 15.28 ||
śikṣā kalpo vyākaraṇaṃ jyotiṣaṃ niruktaṃ chandovicitir iti ṣaḍaṅgāni | teṣāṃ pāṭhato ‘rthataś ca jñātā ṣaḍaṅgavit | jyeṣṭhasāmikaḥ – talavakārāṇām “ud u tyāṃ citram” ity etayor gāyako jyeṣṭhasāmagaḥ, chandogānāṃ tu tad idāsītī tīyaṃ tad yogayeti(?) jyeṣṭhaṃ sāma tadveditā jyeṣṭhasāmikaḥ | triṇāciketo – nāciketo bahuṣu śākhāsu vidhīyate taittirīye kaṭhavallīṣu śatapathe ca | taṃ yo veda brāhmaṇena saha sa triṇāciketaḥ | “madhu vātā ṛtāyate” ity etat ṛcaṃ trimadhu | tatra pratyṛcaṃ trayo madhuśabdāḥ | āśvalāyano ‘py āha: “tṛptāñ jñātvā madhumatīḥ śrāvayet” iti | iha tu tadadhyāyī puruṣas trimadhuḥ | trisuparṇa ṛgvede “ekaḥ suparṇaḥ sa samudram āviveśa” ityādikas tṛcaḥ | taittirīyake “brahmam etu mām” ityādayas trayo ‘nuvākāḥ | tatra hi “ya imaṃ trisuparṇam ayācitaṃ brāhmaṇāya dadyāt” iti śrūyate | pūrvavat puruṣe vṛttiḥ | pañcāgniḥ sabhyāvasathābhyāṃ saha pañcānām anuvākānām adhyetā | snātako vidyāvratābhyām mantrabrāhmaṇavin mantrabrāhmaṇayor arthajñaḥ | dharmajño dharmaśāstrāṇām arthajñaḥ | brahmadeyānusantāno brāhmavivāhoḍhāsantānaḥ | itikaraṇādyaś cānya evaṃyuktaḥ | ye mātṛtaḥ pitṛtaś ceti daśavarṣaṃ samanuṣṭhitā vidyātapobhyāṃ puṇyaiś ca karmabhir yeṣām ubhayato nābrāhmaṇaṃ ninayeyuḥ | pitṛta ity eka ity evamādilakṣaṇaḥ | sa eṣa sarvaḥ paṅktipāvanaḥ || 15.29 ||
haviḥṣu caivam || 15.29 || — || 15.30 ||
haviḥṣu abhyudayakarmasu | bahuvacanaṃ na kevalaṃ pitryāṅgabhūte daiva evam | kiṃ tarhi? sarvābhyuda-yeṣv apīti | caśabdād eva siddhe evaśabdaḥ pariśrita-paṅktipāvanārthaḥ || 15.29 ||
haviḥśabdena daivāni mānuṣāṇi ca karmāṇy ucyante | jyeṣṭhā apy evam uktalakṣaṇā eva brāhmaṇā bhojayitavyā na tu pratiṣiddhāḥ stenādaya iti || 15.30 ||
durvālādīn śrāddha evaike śrāddha
evaike || 15.30 ||
durvālādīñ śrāddha evaike || 15.31 |
durvālād ārabhya ye anukrāntās tān śrāddha eva na bhojayet, na tu daive | ekegrahaṇāt na tu gautamaḥ, saṃbhavāsaṃbhavakṛto vikalpa iti || 15.30 ||
eke tu durvālān ārabhya ye ‘nukrāntās tāñ śrāddha eva na bhojayen na tu daivamānuṣayor iti manyante | svamate tu te tatrāpy abhojyā eveti || 15.31 ||
akṛtānnaśrāddhe caivaṃ caivam || 15.32 ||
dviruktiḥ pūrvavat || 15.32 ||
atha ṣoḍaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
sāmānyo ‘yaṃ dvijātīnāṃ dharma ity ata āha |
śravaṇādi vārṣikaṃ proṣṭhapadīṃ vopākṛtyādhīyīta chandāṃsi || 16.1 ||
H reads “śrāvaṇādi”
śravaṇena yuktā paurṇamāsī śravaṇā | taddhitavikalpo draṣṭavyaḥ | tadādita ārabhya varṣāsu bhavaṃ vārṣikam, etad api saṃjñārtham, tataś ca “abhito vārṣikam” [GDh 16.40] iti vyavahārasiddhiḥ | proṣṭhapadī vā paurṇamāsīm āditaḥ kṛtvā upākṛtya upākaraṇaṃ kṛtvādhīyīta | adhyayanaṃ ca apūrvādhigamo gṛhītābhyāsaś ca | chandāṃsi mantrabrāhmaṇam | etad uktaṃ bhavati – śrāvaṇyāṃ pauṣṭhapadyāṃ vopākṛtya tadādi vedānāṃ varṣākālasaṃbandhyadhyayanaṃ kurvīteti || 16.1 ||
śraveṇena yuktā paurṇamāsī śravaṇā | nakṣatreṇa yuktaḥ kāla ity uktasyāṇo lubaviśeṣa iti lup | yuktavad bhāvas tu na bhavati | vibhāṣā phālgunīśravaṇeti nirdeśāt | śrāvaṇīty api bhavati | paurṇamāsyāṃ hi lubaviśeṣa iti na bhavati | phālgunī kārtikī caitrīti nirdeśāt | śravaṇaśabde tūbhayaṃ bhavatīti |
meṣādisthe savitari yo yo darśaḥ pravartate |
cāndramāsās tattadantāś caitrādyā dvādaśa smṛtāḥ ||
teṣu yā yā paurṇamāsī sā sā caitryādikā smṛtā |
kādācitkena yogena nakṣatrasyeti nirṇayaḥ ||
tad evaṃ siṃhasthe savitari yāmāvāsyā tadante cāndramase māse paurṇamāsī sā śravaṇā srāvaṇīti cocyate | śravaṇayogas tu bhavatu mā vā bhūt | etena proṣṭhapadī vyākhyātā | proṣṭhapadīm ity adhikaraṇe dvitīyā | atyantasaṃyoge vā kathaṃcit | śravaṇāyāṃ prauṣṭhapadyāṃ vā paurṇamāsyām upākṛtyopākarmākhyaṃ karma yathāgṛhyaṃ kṛtvā tadā chandāṃsi mantrabrāhmaṇalakṣaṇāny adhīyīta | ācāryo ‘dhyāpayec chiṣyā adhīyīran | tad idam adhyayanaṃ vārṣikam ity ācakṣate | varṣartau pratisaṃvatsaraṃ vā bhavatīti | adhyāpanam apy ātmāpekṣayādhyayanaṃ pārāyaṇādivat | śiṣyāpekṣayā tv adhyāpanam || 16.1 ||
kiyantaṃ kālam adhīyītety āha |
kiyantaṃ kālam adhīyīta |
ardhapañcamān māsān pañca dakṣiṇāyanaṃ vā || 16.2 ||
upākaraṇottarakālaṃ vikalpaḥ kṛtārthākṛtārthāpekṣayā draṣṭavyaḥ || 16.2 ||
ardhaṃ pañcamaṃ yeṣāṃ tān ardhapañcamān ardhādhikāṃś caturo māsān pūrṇān vā pañca māsān yāvad vā dakṣiṇāyanam | evam adhīyānaḥ || 16.2 ||
adhīyānaḥ |
brahmacāry utsṛṣṭalomā na māṃsaṃ bhuñjīta || 16.3 ||
brahmacārī maithunavarjakaḥ, utsṛṣṭalomā akṛtaśmaśruḥ | lomagrahaṇena nakhādīnām api grahaṇam | na māṃsaṃ bhakṣayet devārcanaśiṣṭam api | gṛhasthasyāyaṃ niyamaḥ, brahmacāriṇaḥ prāptyabhāvāt | brahmacaryarakṣaṇam ṛtugamanam | kutaḥ? “gṛhasthasya ṛtāv eva gamanaṃ brahmacaryam upadiśanti” iti smṛteḥ | yady utsṛṣṭalomaśabdena kḷptalomatvaṃ parigṛhyate tadā “na rūḍhaśmaśrur akasmāt” [GDh 9.7] ity anenaiva siddhatvād anārambhaḥ prāpnoti | tasmād ārambhasāmarthyāt akḷptatvam eva gṛhyate || 16.3 ||
brahmacārī bhavet strīsaṅgaṃ varjayet | utsṛṣṭalomā na rūḍhaśmaruḥ | akasmād ity atroktaṃ lomakarma tad utsṛṣṭaṃ yena sa utsṛṣṭalomā | evaṃbhūto bhaven na māṃsaṃ bhakṣayet | ayam adhyāpayitur upadeśaḥ, brahmacāriṇaḥ prāptyabhāvāt | āpastambo ‘py āha: “pravacanyukto varṣāśaradaṃ maithunaṃ varjayet” [ĀpDh 1.32.1] iti | yaś ca kevalāni vratāni pāraṃ nītvā jāyām upayamya paścād adhīte so ‘py evaṃ tasyātra grahaṇārtham apy evam | āśvalāyano ‘py āha: “samāvṛtto brahmacārikalpena” iti | tatra tv ṛtugamanaṃ pākṣikam abhyupagatam | tathāha jāyopeyety eke prājāpatyam tad iti || 16.3 ||
dvaimāsyo vā niyamaḥ || 16.4 ||
ayaṃ brahmacaryaniyamo māsadvayaṃ vā | śaktyapekṣayā vikalpaḥ | niyamagrahaṇam avaśyakartavyatārtham | evaṃ ca – niyamābhāve adhyayanaprasaṅganivṛttir draṣṭavyā || 16.4 ||
dvau māsau bhūtabhāvinau vā dvimāsyaḥ | māsād vayasi yat khañau | dvigor yabavayasy api prayuñjate | sa eva dvaimāsyaḥ | ayaṃ brahmacaryādiniyamo māsadvayaṃ vā bhavati | śaktyapekṣo vikalpaḥ || 16.4 ||
evam adhīyānaḥ |
athānadhyāyā ucyante |
nādhīyīta vāyau divā pāṃsuhare || 16.5 ||
yāvad divā pāṃsuvarṣuko vāyur vahati tāvan nādhīyīta | divā pāṃsuhara ity eva siddhe vāyugrahaṇaṃ vātyāyām api nādhīyītety evamartham | tathāha uśanā: “vātyāsau piśācikā tasmāt tasyāṃ nādhīyīta” iti | divāgrahaṇād rātrau na doṣaḥ, yadi rātrāv apīṣyate divety anārambheṇaiva sidhyatīti || 16.5 ||
pāṃsūn haratīti pāṃsuharaḥ | vāyau divā pāṃsuhare vāti sati nādhīyīta | apāṃsuhare na doṣaḥ | pāṃsuhare ‘pi rātrau na doṣaḥ || 16.5 ||
karṇaśrāviṇi naktam || 16.6 ||
karṇaśrāviṇi naktaṃ rātrau tu yadā vāyuḥ karṇaśrāvī bhavati śrotraṃ śabdena pūrayati tadā nādhīyīta | naktagrahaṇād divā na doṣaḥ || 16.6 ||
vyatyayenāyaṃ karmaṇi kartṛpratyayaḥ | karṇābhyāṃ śrūyata iti karṇaśrāvī | evaṃbhūte mahāghoṣe vāyau vāti sati naktaṃ nādhīyīta || 16.6 ||
vāṇabherīmṛdaṅgagartārtaśabdeṣu || 16.7 ||
vāṇo vīṇā, “vāṇaḥ śatatantur bhavati” iti mahāvrate darśanāt | bherīmṛdaṅgau prasiddhau | gartaśabdo rathaśabdaḥ, avyakto vā dhvanih | ārtaśabdaḥ kranditadhvaniḥ, ākrośādir vā | śabdaśabdaḥ pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyate yāvad eteṣāṃ dhvanis tāvad anadhyāyaḥ 16.7 ||
vāṇo vīṇāviśeṣaḥ, vāṇaḥ śatatantur iti mahāvrate darśa-nāt | bherīmṛdaṅgau prasiddhau | garto rathaḥ, “ārohataṃ varuṇa mitra gartam,” “stuhi śrutaṃ gartasadam,” ityādau darśanāt | ārto bandhumaraṇādinā duḥkhitaḥ | teṣāṃ śabde śrūyamāṇe tāvantaṃ kālaṃ nādhīyīta || 16.7 ||
śvasṛgālagardabhasaṃhrāde || 16.8 ||
hrādaśabdaḥ pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyate, śvādīnāṃ samuditānāṃ śabde śvasaṃhrāde sṛgālasaṃhrāde gardabhasaṃhrāde ca, samity ārambhāt, “sam ity ekībhāve” saty bhāṣyakāreṇoktatvāt | daṇḍāpūpikayā trayāṇāṃ samuditānām api draṣṭavyam | evaṃ caikaikasya na doṣaḥ || 16.8 ||
saṃhrādaḥ sahaśabdanam | śunāṃ śṛgālānāṃ gardabhā-nāṃ saṃhrāde nādhīyīta | trayāṇāṃ tu sahaśabdena daṇḍāpūpikayā siddhaḥ pratiṣedhaḥ || 16.8 ||
lohitendradhanurnīhāreṣu || 16.9 ||
H reads “rohit-”
lohite indradhanuṣi nīhāre ca nādhīyīta lauhityam ākāśasya lohitavarṇatvaṃ tasmin dṛṣṭe nādhīyīta | tathendudhanuṣi, tat prasiddham | nīhāro himaḥ | yāvad eteṣāṃ darśanaṃ tāvan nādhīyīta || 16.9 ||
ākāśe lohite, indradhaniṣi dṛśyamāne, nīhāro himānī tasyāṃ ca | tāvantaṃ kālaṃ nādhīyīta || 16.9 ||
abbhradarśane cāpartau || 16.10 ||
anṛtau sodakameghadarśane, na yasya kasyacit | cakārād vidyuddarśane ca | yāvad darśanamātraṃ tāvad anadhyāyaḥ | tathā ca kaṇvaḥ: “vidyuddarśane nādhīyīta” iti || 16.10 ||
apartur avarṣartuḥ | tatra sodakasya meghasya darśane nādhīyīta || 16.10 ||
mūtrita uccāritaḥ || 16.11 ||
yāvan mūtroccārāv utpadyete anutsarge ‘py anadhyāyaḥ, kṛte tūtsarge “mānasam apy aśuciḥ” [GDh 16.47] iti vakṣyamāṇatvāt || 16.11 ||
saṃjātamūtre ‘lpe mūtritaḥ | uccārito ‘pi tathā | tatra śreyān api nādhīyīta | utsarge tu mānasam apy aśucir iti vakṣyati || 16.11 ||
niśāsaṃdhyodakeṣu || 16.12 ||
niśāyāṃ saṃdhyodakeṣu || 16.12 ||
niśāśabdenārdharātram ucyate, na rātrimātram, “vidyuti naktaṃ ca” [GDh 16.25] ityādiviśeṣānārambhāt | tenārdharātre saṃdhyāyāṃ codake cāvasthito nādhīyīta || 16.13 ||
niśā rātrer madhyamo bhāgas tasmin saṃdhyāyām udake cāvasthito nādhīyīta || 16.12 ||
varṣati ca || 16.13 ||
yāvad varṣakarma tāvan nādhīyīta | caśabdāt anṛtau varṣaty ahorātraṃ ca || 16.13 ||
varṣati ca deve tāvan nādhīyīta | dhātvarthamātraṃ vivakṣitaṃ na parimāṇaviśeṣaḥ || 16.13 ||
eke valīkasantānam || 16.14 ||
eke acchinnadhāraṃ varṣati nādhīyītetīcchanti | adhīyīteti gautamaḥ | kṛtārthākṛtārthāpekṣayā vikalpaḥ || 16.14 ||
eke manyante valīkasaṃtānaṃ valīkaṃ nīdhraṃ gṛhapaṭalāntas tatra varṣadhārā saṃtanyate yathā tathā varṣati deve nādhyeyam || 16.14 ||
ācāryapariveṣaṇe || 16.15 ||
ācāryau surāsurayoḥ śukrabṛhaspatī, tayoḥ pariveṣaṇe nādhīyīta | ācāryapariveṣaṇam upādhyāyasyānnāpa-haraṇam ity eke || 16.15 ||
eke manyante valīkasaṃtānaṃ valīkaṃ nīdhraṃ gṛhapaṭalāntas tatra varṣadhārā saṃtanyate yathā tathā varṣati deve nādhyeyam || 16.14 ||
jyotiṣoś ca || 16.16 ||
ādityacandrasamo dvivacananirdeśāt, pariveṣaṇe nādhīyīta cakārād ācārye vyasanagate || 16.16 ||
prasiddhajyotiṣī sūryācandramasau | tayoś ca pariveṣaṇe nādhīyīta | pūrvasūtre dvitīyapakṣe ‘nnānuvṛttasya pariveṣaṇaśabdasyārthabhedo ‘ṅgīkaraṇīyaḥ || 16.16 ||
bhīto yānasthaḥ śayānaḥ prauḍhapādaḥ || 16.17 ||
bhīto vartamānabhayaḥ, yānastha aśvādyārūḍhaḥ, śayānaḥ śayyām āsevamānaḥ, prauḍhapādaḥ pāde ekapādārūḍhaḥ, pīṭhikādyāropitacaraṇo vā | vṛkṣārūḍhasyāpi grahaṇartho visamāsaḥ || 16.17 ||
bhīto vartamānabhayaḥ | yānastho ‘śvādyārūḍhaḥ | śayānaḥ śayyām āsevamānaḥ | prauḍhapādaḥ pāde pādāntarādhāyī pīṭhāsanādyāropitapādo vā | evaṃbhūtena nādhyeyam || 16.17 ||
śmaśānagrāmāntamahāpathāśauceṣu || 16.18 ||
śmaśānaṃ śavadahanasthānam, tatsamīpe | grāmānto grāmasīmā tatsamīpe | mahāpatho rājamārgaḥ, aśaucam aśucisthānam | eteṣv avasthito nādhīyīta | āśaucaṃ jananamaraṇanimittam ity eke | tad ayuktam, pūrvam uktatvāt | sadyaḥśaucapakṣe ‘py adhyayanābhāvajñāpa-nārtham ity eke, sadyaḥśauce ‘pi dānapratigrahabhoja-nāny eva kartavyāni na tv adhyayanam | daśarātrād arvāg ity apare | mātāpitros tanmātur vety asmin pakṣe ‘pi sapiṇḍānām adhyayanābhāvajñāpanārtham iti keṣāṃcid darāsnam | āśaucavataḥ samīpe nādhye-tavyam ity evamartha upadeśaḥ gaṅgāyāṃ ghoṣa ityādivad iti | śmaśānagrahaṇaṃ ca bhūtapūrvagatyā pratiṣedhārtham, idānīṃtanasya “śmaśānābhyadhya-yane caiva” [GDh 1.66] ity anenaivoktatvāt | tataś ca śravaṇaparaṃparayā yat sthānaṃ śmaśānam iti śrūyate tatra nādhyetavyam iti || 16.18 ||
śmaśānaṃ śavadāhasthānam | grāmānto grāmasīmā | mahāpathaḥ prasiddhaḥ | aśaucaṃ śaucarāhityam | eteṣu sthāneṣu nādhyeyam | atha vāśaucaṃ jananamaraṇanimittam asparśalakṣaṇaṃ tasminn api nādhyeyam || 16.18 ||
pūtigandhāntaḥśavadivākīrtyaśūdrasaṃnidhāne || 16.19 ||
pūtigandho durgandhaḥ | antaḥśave grāme anirhṛtaprete | divākīrtyo nāpitaḥ śūdro ‘pi, na tu caṇḍālaḥ, tasya śūdrapratiṣedhād evārthataḥ siddheḥ, tasya samīpe na paṭhet | atha vā divākīrtyagrahaṇena tatkarma gṛhyate | tataḥ kṛtaśmaśrur akṛtaśauco nādhīyīteti | kecid divākīryaśabdena caṇḍālam evoktvā, antaḥśabdam adhikṛtya vyācakṣate – antaścaṇḍāle grāma iti | tathāha āpastambaḥ: “antaḥśavam antaścaṇḍālam abhinirhṛtānāṃ tu sīmny anadhyāyaḥ” [1.9.14–16] iti | śūdrasya saṃnidhāne badhirasyāpi kutaḥ? “atha hāsya vedam upaśṛṇvataḥ” ity anenaiva siddhe iha punar ārambhāt || 16.19 ||
pūtigandhe ghrāṇagandhe | divākīrtyaś caṇḍālaḥ | antaḥśabdaḥ ubhābhyāṃ saṃbadhyate | antaḥśave ‘ntardivākīrtye ca grāma iti | śūdrasaṃnidhāne [ca] nā-dhyeyam | dvandvaikavadbhāvaḥ | āpastambo ‘pi: “an-taḥśave ‘ntaścaṇḍāle” [cf. ĀpDh 1.16.20] iti || 16.19 ||
śuktake codgāre || 16.20 ||
bhuktake codgāre || 16.20 ||
amle udgāre nādhīyīta | cakārād anyasmin jvarādau nādhīyīta || 16.20 ||
bhukta[ka]m amlam amle codgāre vartamāne nādhīyīta || 16.20 ||
ṛgyajuṣāṃ ca sāmaśabdo yāvat || 16.21 ||
ṛgyajuṣāṃ cānadhyāyaḥ yāvat sāmadhvaniḥ śrūyate | śākhāntare sāmno ‘py anadhyāyārthaś cakāraḥ | tathā ca smṛtyantaram: “śākhāntare ca sāmnām anadhyāyaḥ” iti | dvivacanena vaktavye ṛgyajuṣām iti bahuvacanaṃ tadaṅgabhūtānāṃ sūtrādīnām apy upasaṃgrahārtham | dhvanimātraśravaṇe ‘pi nādhyetavyam iti yāvadgrahaṇam, anyathā ṛgyajuṣāṃ ca sāmaśabda ity avakṣyat || 16.21 ||
ṛk ca yajuś ca ṛgyajuṣam | acaturetyādinā nipātaḥ | yāvat sāmaśabdaḥ śrūyate tāvad ṛgvedaṃ yajurvedaṃ ca nādhīyīta | ṣaṣṭhyantapāṭhas tu nāsmabhyaṃ rocate
|| 16.21 ||
ākālikā nirghātabhūmikamparāhudarśanolkāḥ || 16.22 ||
yasmin kāle ete bhavanti tasmāt kālād ā dvitīyād anadhyāyakāriṇa ity ākālikāḥ | nirghātaḥ aśanipātaḥ, bhūmikalpo bhūmicalanam, rāhudarśanaṃ grahaṇam, ulkā ulkāpātaḥ |
tryahaṃ na kīrtayet brahma rājño rāhoś ca sūtake | [MDh 4.110] | ity evamādibhir adhikāhaḥpratipādakaiḥ smṛtyantaraiḥ kṛtārthākṛtārthāpekṣayā vikalpo draṣṭavyaḥ || 16.22 ||
nirghāto ‘śanipātaḥ | bhūmikampo bhūcalanam | rāhudarśanaṃ grahaṇam | ulkolkāpātaḥ [ulka ulkāpātaḥ?] | eta ākālikā anadhyāyahetava iti prakaraṇād gamyate | yasmin kāla ete bhavanti paredyus tatparyantaṃ kāla ākālaḥ | tatsaṃbaddha ākālikaḥ || 16.22 ||
stanayitnuvarṣavidyutaś ca prāduṣkṛtāgniṣu || 16.23 ||
prakāśiteṣv agniṣu agnihotrahomakāle saṃdhyāyām ity arthaḥ | ete stanayitnuprabhṛtayo bhavanta ākālikā bhavanti | sarvasamuccayārthaḥ cakāraḥ, ekaikasya dvayor vā mā bhūd iti || 16.23 ||
stanayitnur meghaśabdaḥ | prasiddham anyat | prāduṣkṛteṣv agniṣv agnihotrahomakāle saṃdhyāyāṃ stanayitnuprabhṛtayo bhavantaḥ pratyekam ākālikān adhyāyahetavaḥ | apartāv idam || 16.23 ||
ṛtāv āha |
ahar ṛtau || 16.24 ||
varṣāsu saṃdhyāyām ete bhavanto nākālikāḥ | aharmātrānadhyāyakāriṇaḥ | ahaḥśabdena rātrir apy abhidhīyate “ahaś ca kṛṣṇam ahar arjunaṃ ca” ityādiśrutidarśanāt | tataś ca prātaḥsaṃdhyāyām upalabhyamāneṣu ahar anadhyāyaḥ, sāyaṃ-saṃdhyāyāṃ rātrir iti draṣṭavyam || 16.24 ||
varṣartāv ete yadi bhaveyuḥ saṃdhyāyāṃ tadā prātaś ced aharmātram anadhyāyaḥ | sāyaṃ tu rātrāv anadhyāya ity arthasiddhatvād anuktam || 16.24 ||
vidyuti naktaṃ cāpararātrāt || 16.25 ||
vidyuti saṃdhyāyām ṛtau dṛśyamānāyāṃ tribhāgād arvāg rātrir anadhyāyaḥ | cakārāt prātar upalabhyamānāyām ahaś ca sarvam anadhyāyaḥ na rātrivat tribhāgād arvāk, “vidyuti prātar ahar anadhyāyaḥ” iti jābāligṛhyadarśanāt || 16.25 ||
yadi naktaṃ vidyud dṛśyate na saṃdhyāyāṃ tadāpara-rātrād rātres tṛtīyo bhāgo ‘pararātra ā tasmād anadhyā-yaḥ | tato ‘dhyeyam | prātas tu saṃdhyāyāṃ vidyuti jā-bāla āha: “vidyuti prātar ahar anadhyāyaḥ” iti || 16.25 ||
tribhāgādipravṛttau sarvam || 16.26 ||
yā saṃdhyāyām upalabhyate tasyā ahnaḥ pūrvabhāgāv atikramya tata ārabhya pravṛttir asti cet tataḥ sarvaṃ naktam anadhyāyaḥ || 16.26 ||
yady ahnas tṛtīyād bhāgād ārabhya vidyut pravartate na kevalāyāṃ saṃdhyāyāṃ nāpi naktaṃ tadā sarvarātram anadhyāyaḥ || 16.26 ||
ulkā vidyutsamety ekeṣām || 16.27 ||
ulkā ca vidyuttulyā | yathā vidyuty anadhyāyaḥ tatholkāpāte ‘pi draṣṭavyam ity ekeṣāṃ mataṃ na tu gautamasya | kṛtārthākṛtārthāpekṣayā vikalpaḥ || 16.27||
ulkā ca vidyuttulyā | yathā vidyuty anadhyāyo vidyuti naktaṃ cāpararātrād ity evam ulkāpāte ‘pīty ekeṣāṃ matam || 16.27 ||
stanayitnur aparāhṇe || 16.28 ||
stanayitnur aparāhṇe bhavan vidyutsamo bhavati | naktam āpararātrād anadhyāyaṃ karotīty arthaḥ
|| 16.28 ||
stanayitnur aparāhṇe yadi bhavati na saṃdhyāyāṃ tadā vidyutsamo bhavati | ā apararātrād anadhyāyaṃ karoti
|| 16.28 ||
api pradoṣe || 16.29 ||
prathame ‘pi rātribhāge pravṛttaḥ pūrvavad āpararātrād anadhyāyaṃ karotīty arthaḥ || 16.29 ||
pradoṣe ‘pi bhavaḥ stanayitnur vidyutsamaḥ | ā apararātrād anadhyāyahetuḥ || 16.29 ||
sarvaṃ naktam ārdharātrāt || 16.30 ||
prathamāt rātribhāgād ārabhyārdharātrāt pravṛttaḥ sarvaṃ naktam anadhyāyaṃ karoti || 16.30 ||
prathamād rātribhāgād ārabhya ārdharātrāt pravṛttaḥ stanayitnuḥ sarvaṃ naktam anadhyāyahetuḥ || 16.30 ||
ahaś cet sajyotiḥ || 16.31 ||
prāg aparāhṇād yad ahaḥ stanayitnur bhavati tataḥ sajyotir anadhyāyaḥ, divasam evety arthaḥ || 16.31 ||
ahaś cet stanayitnur bhavati prāg aparāhṇāt tadā sajyo-tir anadhyāyaḥ | sakalaṃ divasam ity arthaḥ || 16.31 ||
viṣayasthe ca rājñi prete || 16.32 ||
viṣayasthe svarāṣṭrasthe ity arthaḥ, rājani pramīte | ākālikānukarṣaṇārthaś cakāraḥ | tataś ca ākāliko ‘nadhyāyaḥ || 16.32 ||
yasmin viṣaye svayaṃ vasati tatrasthe tasyādhipatau rājñi prete sajyotir anadhyāyaḥ | ākālikam ity anye
|| 16.32 ||
viproṣya cānyonyena saha || 16.33 ||
yadā śiṣyācāryau sahādhyāyinau vā parasparam aviyuktau pravaseyuḥ | tadā cākāliko ‘nadhyāyaḥ | cakārāt sametya ca || 16.33 ||
yadā sahādhyāyinaḥ parasparaṃ vipravaseyuḥ kecic cācāryeṇa saṃgatās tadā sajyotir anādhyāyaḥ | ā pareṣāṃ melanād ity eke | ākālikam ity anye || 16.33 ||
saṃkulopāhitavedasamāpticchardiśrāddhamanuṣya-yajñabhojaneṣv ahorātram || 16.34 ||
saṃkulaḥ kenacit kāraṇena grāmādyupadravaḥ | upāhito ‘gnisaṃbhramaḥ agnidāhaḥ | vedasamāptiḥ vedasthaprakaraṇasamāptiḥ, grāmo dagdha ityādivat | śākhāsamāptir vā | chardiḥ prasiddhā | śrāddham ekoddiṣṭādi, tatra bhoktuḥ | manuṣyayajñe mitramelake | bhojane anekabrāhmaṇabhojane | eteṣv ahorātram anādhyāyaḥ || 16.34 ||
saṃkulaś corādibhir grāmādyupadravaḥ | upāhito ‘gnidāhaḥ | vedasamāptiḥ śākhāsamāptiḥ | chardanaṃ bhuktodgāraḥ | śrāddham ekoddiṣṭādi | manuṣyayajño vasantotsvādiḥ | bhojanaśabda ubhābhyāṃ saṃbadhyate | śrāddhabhojane manuṣyayajñabhojana iti | eteṣu nimitteṣv ahorātram anadhyāyaḥ | manuṣyaprakṛtīnāṃ devānāṃ yajño manuṣyayajña ity anye | yathāha āpastambaḥ: “manuṣyaprakṛtīnāṃ devānāṃ yajñe bhuktvety eke” [ĀpDh 1.11.3] iti | ye manuṣyā bhūtvā prakṛṣṭena tapasā devāḥ saṃpannās tadyajñas tatprītyarthaṃ brāhmaṇabhojanam || 16.34 ||
amāvāsyāyāṃ ca || 16.35 ||
ahorātram anadhyāyaḥ | caśabdāt paurṇamāsyāṃ ca
|| 16.35 ||
amāvāsyāyām ahorātram anadhyāyaḥ || 16.35 ||
dvyahaṃ vā || 16.36 ||
amāvāsyāyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ ca dvyahaṃ vā pūrvo-ktam ahorātraṃ vā anadhyāyaḥ | ubhayatra catur-daśyām iti draṣṭavyam | tatra vedasya dvyaham aṅgāder ahorātram eveti vyavasthitavikalpo draṣṭavyam | tathā cāha uśanā: “parvaṇītihāsavarjitānāṃ sarvāsāṃ vidyānām anadhyāyaḥ” iti || 16.36 ||
tad ahaḥ pūrvedyuś ca dvyaham anadhyāyaḥ | śuklacaturdaśyāṃ tv anadhyāyasya mūlāntaraṃ mṛgyam | evaṃ pratipatsu ca || 16.36 ||
kārtikī phālguny āṣāḍhī paurṇamāsī || 16.37 ||
caśabdāt pūrvam anadhyāyaprāpaṇād iha paurṇamāsīśabdena pratipado gṛhyante | etāsv anadhyāyaḥ | evaṃ cānyāsu pratipatsu na doṣaḥ | tatra “pratipatsu na cintayet” ity auśanasavacanena kṛtārthākṛtārthāpekṣayā vikalpo draṣṭavyaḥ || 16.37 ||
kārtikyādyās tisraḥ paurṇamāsyo ‘nadhyāyahetavo ‘horātram | paurṇamāsyantareṣv anadhyāye mūlaṃ mṛgyam || 16.37 ||
tisro ‘ṣṭakās trirātram || 16.38 ||
tisra ūrdhvam āgrahāyaṇyā aparapakṣehvaṣṭakās trirātram anadhyāyakāriṇyaḥ | atas tāsu trirātram anadhyāyo bhavati, saptamyām aṣṭamyāṃ navamyām iti || 16.38 ||
ūrdhvam āgrahāyaṇyās triṣv aparapakṣeṣu tisro ‘ṣṭakāḥ | tās trirātram anadhyāyahetavaḥ tadahaḥ pūrvedyur aparedyuś ca || 16.38 ||
antyām eke || 16.39 ||
antyām evāṣṭakām anadhyāyakāritvenecchanti | pūrvavad vikalpaḥ || 16.39 ||
eke ‘ntyām ekāṣṭakām anadhyāyahetuṃ manyante
|| 16.39 ||
abhito vārṣikam || 16.40 ||
ādāv ante cety arthaḥ | varṣākāle bhavaṃ vārṣikam iti tatkālasaṃbandhy adhyayanam ucyate | yat kṛtvā yat prārabhyate ādāv ante ca yat kriyate abhitaḥśabdena tad dvayaṃ gṛhyate | trirātram anadhyāya ity anuvartate | upākarmaṇi cotsarge trirātram anadhyāya ity arthaḥ | tathā cāha uśanā: “upākarmaṇi cotsarge tryaham anadhyāyaḥ” iti || 16.40 ||
śravaṇādi vārṣikam iti yad uktaṃ vārṣikam anadhyayanaṃ tad abhitas tasyobhayoḥ pārśvayor ye karmaṇī upākaraṇotsarjane tayor api kṛtayor tryaham anadhyāyam eke icchanti | tathā ca manuḥ |
upākarmaṇi cotsarge trirātraṃ kṣapaṇaṃ smṛtam || iti | [MDh 4.119] | uśanā: “upākarmaṇi cotsarge tryaham anadhyāyaḥ” iti || 16.40 ||
sarve varṣavidyutstanayitnusaṃnipāte || 16.41 ||
H reads “varṣā”
sarve ācāryā varṣādīnāṃ saṃnipāte trirātram anadhyāyam icchanti | gautamas tu “stanayitnu-varṣavidyutaś ca prāduṣkṛtāgniṣu” [GDh 16.23] ity ākālikam | tatra pūrvavad vikalpaḥ || 16.41 ||
varṣādīnāṃ trayāṇāṃ yugapatsaṃnipāte trirātram anadhyāya iti sarva evācāryā
manyante || 16.41 ||
prasyandini || 16.42 ||
vidyutstanayitnusaṃnipāte trirātram ity anuvartate | yasminn ahani viralaśo varṣati devas tasminn evāhani vidyudagnisaṃnipātaś ca tatrāpi trirātram icchanti
|| 16.42 ||
prakṛṣṭaṃ syandanaṃ varṣaṃ prasyandas tadvati ca kāle yāvat prasyandanam anadhyāyo dvyahaṃ tryahaṃ caturahaṃ vā || 16.42 ||
ūrdhvaṃ bhojanād utsave || 16.43 ||
utsave sarvābhyudaye bhojanād ūrdhvaṃ tad ahorātram anadhyāyaḥ | upanayanādāv utsave bhuktvā taduttarakālam anadhyāya ity arthaḥ || 16.43 ||
upanayanādāv utsave bhojanād ūrdhvaṃ tadahar anadhyāyaḥ || 16.43 ||
prādhītasya ca || 16.44 ||
prādhītasya ca niśāyāṃ caturmuhūrtam
|| 16.44 ||
prādhītasya ca adhyetuṃ prathamaṃ pravṛtta-syānutsave ‘pi bhojanottarakālam anadhyāyaḥ | cakārād vedāntādhyetuś ca bhojanottarakālam anadhyāyaḥ
|| 16.44 ||
upākṛtyādhyetuṃ pravṛttaḥ prādhītaḥ | ādikarmaṇi ktaḥ kartari ca | tasya niśāyāṃ caturmuhūrtaṃ caturo muhūrān aṣṭau nāḍikā anadhyāyaḥ | “śrāvaṇyāṃ paurṇamāsyām adhyāyam upākṛtya māsaṃ pradoṣe nādhīyīta” [ĀpDh 1.9.1] ity āpastambīyena samānārtham idam | cakārāt trayodaśīpradoṣe ‘pi niśāyāṃ caturmuhūrtaṃ niṣedho darśitaḥ || 16.44 ||
niśāyāṃ caturmuhūrtam || 16.45 ||
rātryāṃ prathamāṃś caturo muhūrtān nādhīyīta, smṛtyantarasāmarthyāt trayodaśyām | yathāha uśanā: “trayodaśyāṃ prathamāṃś caturo muhūrtān nādhīyīta” iti || 16.45 ||
nityam eke nagare || 16.46 || — || 16.45 ||
eke nagare grāme sadaivānadhyāyam icchanti | na tu gautamaḥ, grāmanivāsināṃ sadaivānadhyāyaprasaṅgāt | tatra śūdrādibhūyiṣṭhe anadhyāyaḥ, dvijātibahule adhyetavyam iti draṣṭavyam || 16.46 ||
eka ācāryā nagare nityam evādhyayanaṃ necchanti | nityagrahaṇaṃ niśādhikāranivṛttyartham || 16.45 ||
mānasam apy aśuciḥ || 16.47 || — || 16.46 ||
aprayato manasāpy adhyayanaṃ na kuryāt | evaṃ cānyeṣv apy anadhyāyeṣu manasādhyayanaṃ na pratiṣidhyate | nityārthaṃ vā mānasagrahaṇam | aśuciḥ san naityakam api nādhīyīteti | evaṃ ca naityake anye ‘nadhyāyā na santīti draṣṭavyam | tathāha manuḥ |
naityake nāsty anadhyāyo homamantreṣu caiva hi |
naityake nāsty anadhyāyo brahmasatraṃ hi tat smṛtam || [MDh 2.105–06] | iti || 16.47 ||
aprayataḥ san mānasam apy adhyayanaṃna kuryāt | evaṃ cānyeṣv anadhyāyahetuṣu mānasam aniṣiddham || 16.46 ||
śrāddhinām ākālikam || 16.48 || — || 16.47 ||
dātṝṇām, bhoktṝṇāṃ tu “saṃkulopāhita-” [GDh 16.34] ity uktatvāt | bahuvacanaṃ sarvaśrāddhasūcanārtham, ekoddiṣṭe dātṝṇām eva mā bhūd iti || 16.48 ||
śrāddham asyāstīti śrāddhī śrāddhasya kartā | ata iniṭhanau | na tu śrāddham anena bhuktam iti, bhoktari pūrvam eva niṣiddhatvāt | teṣāṃ śrāddhadātṝṇām ākālikam anadhyāyaḥ | apara āha – ye śrāddhe kevalaṃ bhuktavanto na pitrādyarthaṃ pātratayā teṣāṃ pūrvako ‘horātraniṣedhaḥ | ayaṃ tv ākālikaniṣedhaḥ pitrādyarthaṃ pātratayā bhuktavatām iti || 16.47 ||
akṛtānnaśrāddhikasaṃyoge ca || 16.49 || — || 16.48 ||
H reads “-saṃyoge ‘pi”
yadākṛtānnaṃ pitṝn uddiśya dīyate bhojanābhāve tad akṛtānnaśrāddhikam | tatsaṃyogaṃ dātṛpratigrahītror ubhayoḥ saṃyogavacanāt, ākāliko ‘nadhyāyaḥ | cakārād anyatrāpi pitrarthe | yathāha manuḥ |
prāṇi vā yadi vāprāṇi yat kiṃcic chrāddhikaṃ bhavet |
pratigṛhyāpy anadhyāyaḥ pāṇyāsyā brāhmaṇāḥ smṛtāḥ || iti | [MDh 4.117] | āmaśrāddhasyaitad eva liṅgam || 16.49 ||
bhojanāsaṃbhave yadya(yada)kṛtānnaṃ pitṛbhyo dīyate tad akṛtānnaśrāddhikam | tatsaṃyoge ‘py ākālikam anadhyāyaḥ | na kevalaṃ bhuktavataḥ | tatra manuḥ |
prāṇi vā yadi vāprāṇi yat kiṃcic chrāddhikaṃ bhavet |
tad ālabhyāpy anadhyāyaḥ pāṃyāsyā brāhmaṇāḥ smṛtāḥ || iti | [MDh 4.117] | āmaśrāddhasyaitad eva liṅgam || 16.48 ||
pratividyaṃ ca yān smaranti yān smaranti || 16.50 || — || 16.49 ||
prativedaṃ pratiśākhaṃ ca yān anadhyāyān gṛhyakārāḥ smaranti cakārād dharmāntarev api, nādhīyīteti saṃbandhaḥ || 16.50 ||
pratividyaṃ pratidharmaśāstraṃ yān anadhyāyān smaranti smartāras teṣv api hetuṣu nādhīyīta | tatra vasiṣṭhaḥ: “digdāhaparvataprapāteṣūpalarudhirapāṃsu-varṣeṣv ākālikam” [VaDh 13.35] iti |
śleṣmātakasya śālmalyā madhūkasya tathāpy adhaḥ |
kadācid api nādhyeyaṃ kovidārakapitthayoḥ || iti |
evam anyatrāpi draṣṭavyam || 16.49 ||
atha saptadaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
mānasam apy aśucir ity abhihitam | tadaśucitvam āhārajanitam api bhavatīti bhojyābhojyavivakṣayāha |
mānasam apy aśucir ity uktam | tac cāśucitvam āhārajanitam api bhavatīti bhakṣyābhakṣyaprakaraṇam ārabhyate |
praśastānāṃ svakarmasu dvijātīnāṃ brāhmaṇo bhuñjīta || 17.1 ||
prakarṣeṇa stutānāṃ svakarmasu na kṣatriyādikarmasu arthakāmaparityāgena mukhyakarmānuṣṭānaratānām ity arthaḥ, teṣāṃ dvijātīnāṃ upanītānām | bhojanasyārthaprāptatvāt parisaṃkhyārtham idaṃ vacanam | brāhmaṇagrahaṇaṃ dvijātipradarśanārtham | kṣatriyādīnām apraśastābhyanujñānārtham ity eke | bhuñjīta bhojanaṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ | praśastānām ity ukte āhopuruṣikāmātreṇāpi praśastā bhavantīti svakarmagrahaṇam | praśastānāṃ sva ity eva siddhe karmagrahaṇam kāmyanivṛttyartham | svakarmasv iti bahuvacanaṃ jñānakarmasamuccayakāriṇāṃ prādhānyakhyāpanārtham || 17.1 ||
svakarmasu varṇaprayukteṣv āśramaprayukteṣūbhayaprayukteṣu ca ye praśastāḥ “aho ayaṃ svakarmānutiṣṭhati” iti teṣāṃ dvijātīnāṃ gṛhe brāhmaṇo bhuñjīta | kṣudupaghātārthā bhojane pravṛttiḥ | śakyā ca yasya kasyacid gṛhe bhuñjānena kṣud upahantum | tatra parisaṃcaṣṭa eṣām eva gṛhe brāḥmaṇo bhuñjīta nānyeṣām iti || 17.1 ||
pratigṛhṇīyāc ca || 17.2 ||
eṣāṃ dhanam | etān adhyāpayed yājayec cety evamarthaś cakāraḥ || 17.2 ||
pratigraho ‘py eṣām eva sakāśān nānyeṣām iti || 17.2 ||

asyāpavādaḥ |
edhodakayavasamūlaphalamadhvabhayābhyudyataśayyāsanāvasathayānapayodadhi-dhānāśapharipriyaṅgusraṅmārgaśākāny apraṇodyāni sarveṣām || 17.3 ||
H reads “śaparī”
edhaḥ kāṣṭham, udakaṃ kumbhādyavasthitam api, yavasaṃ tṛṇāni, mūlaphale prasiddhe, madhu mākṣikam, abhayaṃ paritrāṇam, abhyudyataśayyāsa-nāni prasiddhāni, āvasathaṃ pratiśrayādiḥ, yānādīni prasiddhāni, śapharī matsyaviśeṣaḥ, priyaṅguḥ dhānyaviśeṣaḥ, srak grathitāni kusumāni, mārgaṃ mṛgamāṃsam, śākaṃ prasiddham, apraṇodyāni apratyākhyeyāni, ayācitāni, apraṇodyagrahaṇāt | asmin sūtre abhyudyataśabdena pratigrahītṛsakāśaṃ yad ānīyate tad ucyate | tatra jātiviśeṣo nāsti | tathāha manuḥ |
udyatām āhṛtāṃ bhikṣāṃ purastād apraveditām |
bhojyāṃ prajāpatir mene api duṣkṛtakarmaṇaḥ || iti | [MDh 4.248] | asyāpavādaḥ |
śraddadhānasya bhoktavyaṃ corasyāpi viśeṣataḥ |
na tv eva bahuyājyasya yaś copanayate bahūn || iti | [VaDh 14.17] | tathā cāparam |
cikitsakasya mṛgayoḥ śalyakartuś ca pāśinaḥ |
ṣaṇḍasya kulaṭāyāś ca udyātāpi na gṛhyate || iti | [VaDh 14.19] | tatrāpi bhojyadravyam eva draṣṭavyam, “bhojyāṃ prajāpatir mene” [MDh 4.248] iti liṅgāt | evaṃ cābhyudyatam api bhojyam, na pratigrāhyam | apraṇodyagrahaṇāt praṇodane doṣaḥ | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ |
nāśnanti pitaras tasya daśa varṣāṇi pañca ca |
na ca havyaṃ vahaty agnir yas tām abhyavamanyate || iti | [VaDh 14.18] | tatrāpi bhojyadravyam eva pratigṛhṇīyāt apraśastebhyo dvijātibhyaḥ pṛthaggrahaṇāt | sarvagrahaṇāt śūdrād api | sarvebhya iti vaktavye sarveṣām ity upādānam ātmasaṃbandhijñāpanārtham | evaṃ ca parakīyaṃ muṣitvā yo dadāti tasyāgrahaṇe na doṣaḥ || 17.3 ||
edhaḥ kāṣṭham | udakaṃ ghaṭādistham api | yavasaṃ tṛṇādi | mūlam ārdrakādi | phalam āmrādi | madhu mākṣikam | abhayaṃ paritrāṇam | abhyudyatam ayācitenāpi dātrā svayam ānītam idaṃ gṛhāṇeti | śayyā kaṭādi | āsanaṃ pīṭhādi | āvasathaḥ pratiśrayaḥ | yānaṃ śakaṭādi | dadhipayasī prasiddhe | dhānā bhṛṣṭā yavāḥ | śapharī matsyaviśeṣaḥ | [priyaṅgū rājikā] | sraṅ mālā | mārgaṃ mṛgamāṃsam, panthā vā mārgaḥ | śākaṃ vāstukādi | etāny edhādīny apraṇodyāni sarvataḥ pratigrāhyāṇi yācitvāpi | abhyudyataṃ pakvānnādy apraṇodyam apratyākhyeyaṃ pratyākhyāne doṣaḥ | tathā ca āpastambaḥ |
udyatām āhṛtāṃ bhikṣāṃ purastād apraveditām |

na tasya pitaro ‘śnanti daśa varṣāṇi pañca ca |

pitṛbharaṇam avicchedena śrāddhakaraṇam | devabharaṇam agnihotrādi | guravaḥ pitrādayaḥ | bhṛtyāḥ putradāsādayaḥ | teṣāṃ bharaṇaṃ bhaktādidānam | eteṣu nimitteṣv anyad apy uktād anyad apy apraṇodyam | manuś ca |

paśupālaḥ paśūnāṃ saṃrakṣitā sa paśusvāmino bhojyānnaḥ | tathā kṣetrakarṣakaś ca | tathāha uśanā: “svagopālo bhojyānnaḥ svakṣetrakarṣakaś ca” ityādi | kulasaṃgataḥ pāraṃparyeṇa mitrarūpeṇāgataḥ | kārayitā nāpitaḥ | paricārako dāsaḥ | etasmāl liṅgān nāpitasya śūdratvam adhyavasīyate | tathā cāha manuḥ |

yo yasya paśūn pālayati kṣetraṃ ca karṣati, yaś ca yasya kule saṃgataḥ pāramparyeṇa mitrarūpeṇāgataḥ, yaś ca yasya paricārako dāsas te teṣāṃ bhojyānnāḥ | pakvam apy annaṃ teṣāṃ bhuñjīran | kāruḥ kārayitā | “ūrdhvaṃ nāpitaḥ śmaśrūṇi kārayati” iti hi dṛśyate | sa ca viprād vaiśyāyām anūḍhāyāṃ jātaḥ so ‘pi bhojyānnaḥ | tatra manuḥ |

yat kiṃcit snehasaṃyuktaṃ bhakṣyaṃ bhojyam agarhitam |
tat paryuṣitam apy ādyaṃ haviḥśeṣaṃ ca yad bhavet || iti | [MDh 5.24] | māṃsaṃ cāsnehasaṃyuktam api | snehagrahaṇenaiva madhunaḥ siddhe pṛthaggrahaṇam paryuṣitasyāpi madhunaḥ striyāḥ pratiṣedhārtham | tathā ca kalpakārāṇām apy upaniṣat – “tasmād uta striyo madhu nāśnanti” iti || 17.14 ||
udayāstamayāntaritaṃ paryuṣitam | divā pakvaṃ rātrau rātripakvaṃ divā tad aśuktam apy abhojyam | śākādi tu paryuṣitam api bhojyam | śākam uktam | bhakṣāḥ pṛthukāpūpādayaḥ | sneho ghṛtatailādiḥ | māṃsaṃ prasiddham | madhu ca | etāni paryuṣitāny ap bhojyāni | snehamadhvādīnām apakvatvād evāparyuṣitatvam | tasmāt snehamadhugrahaṇaṃ tatsaṃsṛṣṭasyāpi paryuṣitasya paryudāsārtham | tena tatsaṃsṛṣṭaṃ paryuṣitam api bhojyam agarhitam |
tat paryuṣitam apy ādyaṃ haviḥśeṣaṃ ca yad bhavet |
iti || 17.16 ||
saṃsargaduṣṭaṃ kāladuṣṭaṃ coktvā sāṃpratam āśrayaduṣṭam āha |
utsṛṣṭapuṃścalyabhiśatānapadeśyadāṇḍikatakṣakadaryabandhanikacikitsaka-mṛgayvaniṣucāryucchiṣṭa-bhojigaṇavidviṣāṇām || 17.15 || — || 17.17 ||
utsṛṣṭam asvāmikam | puṃścalī vyabhicāriṇī | anenaiva gaṇikā cārthasiddhā | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “gaṇānnaṃ gaṇikānnaṃ ca” [VaDh 14.10] iti | abhiśastaḥ doṣenābhikhyātaḥ | anapadeśyaḥ strīliṅgena puṃliṅgena vā anirdeśyaḥ tṛtīyā prakṛtir ity arthaḥ | dāṇḍiko rājñā nigrahe niyuktaḥ | takṣo rathakāraḥ | kadaryaḥ asabhyabhāṣaṇarataḥ, “nāślīlaṃ kīrtayed yady aślīlaṃ kīrtayet taṃ kadaryam āhuḥ” iti brāhmaṇadarśanāt | bandhaniko guptipālaḥ | cikitsako vaidyo brāhmaṇaḥ, “tasmād brāhmaṇena bheṣajaṃ na kāṛyam” iti taittirīyāṇāṃ brāhmaṇe darśanāt | tathā ca vasiṣṭho ‘pi:
nānṛjur brāhmaṇo bhavati na vaṇiṅ na kuśīlavaḥ |
na śūdrapreṣaṇaṃ kurvan na steno na cikitsakaḥ || iti | [VaDh 3.3] | stenopajīvika ity eke | śalyakartety apare,
cikitsakasya mṛgayor śalyahartuś ca pāśinaḥ | [VaDh 14.19] | iti darśanāt | mṛgayur lubdhakaḥ, sa ced aniṣucārī vāgurikaḥ | ucchiṣṭabhojī ucchiṣtānnabhojakaḥ | gaṇo janasamudāyaḥ | vidviṣāṇaḥ śatruḥ | eṣām annam abhojyam | praśastānām apraśastānām abhojanopadeśāt, āpady apy eṣām annaṃ na bhojyam || 17.15 ||
utsṛṣṭaḥ pitṛbhyāṃ parityaktaḥ |
gaṇḍasyoparijātānāṃ parityāgo vidhīyate |
ityādinā kāraṇena durbhikṣe rakṣaṇāśaktyā, prātikūlyena vā | puṃścalī – aniyatapuṃsakā vyabhicāriṇī gaṇikā ca | abhiśastaḥ satāsatā vā doṣeṇa khyātaḥ | anapadeśyaivaṃbhūto ‘yam iti vyapadeśānarhaḥ | strītvapuṃstvābhyām anirdeśyā tṛtīyā prakṛtir ity anye | daṇḍiko rājñā daṇḍādhikāre niyuktaḥ | śūdrāt prātilomyena vaiśyāyāṃ jātas takṣā | vaiśyāt kṣatriyāyāṃ jāto māhiṣyaḥ | śūdrāyām ūḍhāyāṃ vaiśyāj jātā karaṇī, tasyāṃ māhiṣyāj jāto rathakāraḥ | sa takṣety anye | kadaryo lubdhaḥ, yam adhikṛtya manur āha |
śrotriyasya kadaryasya vadānyasya ca vārdhuṣeḥ | iti | [MDh 4.224] bandhaniko bandhakāgārarakṣī | cikitsiko vaidyaḥ śalyakartā vā | yo mṛgayuḥ sann iṣucārī na bhavati kiṃ tu pāśacārī sa mṛgayvaniṣucārī vāgurikaḥ | ucchiṣṭabhojī nigadasiddhaḥ | gaṇo janasamudāyaḥ | vidviṣāṇaḥ śatruḥ | eteṣām utsṛṣṭādīnām annam abhojyam | ye ‘tra praśastā dvijātayo na bhavanti teṣāṃ grahaṇam uditapratiṣedhārtham | tathā ca āpastambaḥ: “cikitsikasya mṛgayoḥ” [ĀpDh 1.19.14] ityādi | āpady api pratiṣedhārtham ity anye || 17.17 ||
apāṅktyānāṃ prāg durvālāt || 17.16 || — 17.18 ||
ye cāpāṅktyāḥ prāg upadiṣṭāḥ stenādayas tyaktātma-paryantās teṣām akṛtaprāyaścittānām annam abhojyam, brahmahādīnāṃ tu kṛtaprāyaścittānām api, “nāsyāsmin loke pratyāpattir vidyate kalmaṣaṃ tu nirhaṇyate” [ĀpDh 1.24.25] ity āpastambavacanāt || 17.16 ||
ye cāpaṅktyāḥ prāg upadiṣṭās tyakātmaparyantās teṣām apy annam abhojyam [cf. GDh 15.18]
|| 17.18 ||
vṛthānnācamanotthānavyapetāni || 17.17 || — || 17.19 ||
vṛthānnaṃ yad ātithyādyanuddeśenātmārthaṃ pacyate | tathā bahvṛcānām ṛk |
mogham annaṃ vindate apracetāḥ
satyaṃ bravīmi vadha it sa tasya |
nāryamaṇaṃ puṣyati no sakhāyam
kevalāgho bhavati kevalādī || iti || [RV 10.170.6] | ācamanavyapetam, yad ardhaṃ bhuktvā ācamya punas tad eva bhuṅkte utthānavyapetam, yad ardhaṃ bhuktam evotthāya bhuṅkte etat trividham apy abhojyam | ācamanotthānavyapetam iti vaktavye bahuvacanam ekapaṅktāv anyatareṇācamane utthāne ca kṛte anyatarasyābhojyam iti | tathāha uśanā: “agurubhir ācamanotthāne kṛtaṃ ca” iti | evaṃ gurubhiḥ kṛte na doṣaḥ || 17.17 ||
yad ātmārthaṃ pacyate nātithyādyarthaṃ tad vṛthānnam | śrūyate hi “mogham annaṃ vindate apracetāḥ” ityādi | bhojanamadhye yatra kopādinā punar ācamyata utthīyate vā | apetād anyad vyapetaṃ sahitam iti | ete ācamanotthānavyapete anne | etāni vṛthānnādīny abhojyāni | atra uśanā: “agurubhir ācamanotthānaṃ ca” iti | ekasyāṃ paṅktau bahuṣu bhuñjāneṣv ekenāpi guruvyatirikenācamana utthāne vā kṛta itareṣām apy abhojyam iti | gurubhiḥ kṛte na doṣaḥ
|| 17.19 ||
samāsamābhyāṃ viṣamasame pūjātaḥ || 17.18 || — || 17.20 ||
samena vidyākulāditulyena saha viṣamāṃ pūjām āsanaparicaraṇādikāṃ kṛtvā asamena ca samāṃ kṛtvā yatrānnaṃ dīyate tatra pūjātaḥ viṣame ca same ca sati na bhoktavyam || 17.18 ||
kulaśīlādibhis tulyaḥ samaḥ | viparīto ‘samaḥ | viṣama-samaśabdau bhāvaparau | viṣamasama iti samāhāra-dvandvaḥ | pūjātaḥ pūjāyām āsanaparicaraṇādikāyāṃ samena saha pūjāyāṃ viṣame ‘samena ca sāmye kriyamāṇe tadannam abhojyam || 17.20 ||
anarcitaṃ ca || 17.19 || — || 17.21 ||
annamantreṇānamaskṛtam | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “rocata iti sāyaṃ prātar aśanāny abhipūjayet” [VaDh 3.69] iti | cakārāt “ko bhokṣyate” ity abhikruṣṭam | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “ko bhokṣyata iti cākruṣṭam” [VaDh 14.9] iti || 17.19 ||
yac cānarcitaṃ dīyate “vaidhaveya bhakṣaya” iti tad apy abhojyam | pratigrahe ‘pi tulyam etat | yathāha manuḥ |
yo ‘rcitaṃ pratigṛhṇāti dadāty arcitam eva yaḥ |
tāv ubhau gacchataḥ svargaṃ narakaṃ tu viparyaye || iti | [MDh 4.235] | “sāyaṃ pātar aśanāny abhipūjayet” [VaDh 3.69] iti vasiṣṭhaḥ | tadakaraṇam anarcitam ity anye || 17.21 ||
goś ca kṣīram anirdaśāhāḥ sūtake || 17.20 || — || 17.22 ||
goḥ prasūtāyā anatikrāntadaśāhāyā kṣīram abhojyam | caśabdād dadhyādi ca || 17.20 ||
sūtakaṃ prasavaḥ | prasūtāyā anatikrāntadaśāhāyāḥ goḥ kṣīram ābhojyam || 17.22 ||
ajāmahiṣyoś ca || 17.21 || — || 17.22 ||
anirdaśāhayor ajāmahiṣyoḥ kṣīram apeyaṃ sadadhyādi | ajānām adhikārārthaś caśabdaḥ | pṛthag grahaṇaṃ gokṣīrasya kadācit saptarātrād ūrdhvam adoṣārtham
|| 17.21 ||
ajāmahiṣyoḥ sūtake ‘nirdaśāhayoḥ kṣīram apeyam
|| 17.23 ||
nityam āvikam apeyam auṣṭram aikaśaphaṃ ca || 17.22 || — || 17.23 ||
nityagrahaṇaṃ na kevalaṃ daśāha eveti | vikārapratyayanirdeśāt mūtrādy apy āsām apeyam iti | evaṃ ca pūrveṣāṃ mūtrādeḥ peyatvam avagamyate | ekaśaphāḥ ekakhurā vaḍavādayaḥ | itare prasiddhāḥ | visamāso mṛgādīnām āraṇyānāṃ sarveṣām upasaṃgrahārthaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
āraṇyānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ mṛgāṇāṃ mahiṣaṃ vinā | iti | [MDh 5.9] | cakārān mānuṣastrīkṣīraṃ ca | prakṛtam apy apeyagrahaṇaṃ nityārthaṃ kriyate | tataś cājāmahiṣyor atyantāpady anirdaśāhayor apy anugrahaḥ siddhaḥ || 17.22 ||
nityagrahaṇān na kevalam anirdaśāham eva | avir evāvikaḥ | uṣṭraḥ prasiddhaḥ | ekaśaphā ekakhurā aśvādayaḥ | avikādīnāṃ saṃbandhi kṣīraṃ nityam apeyam | manus tu |
ārāṇyānāṃ tu sarveṣāṃ mṛgāṇāṃ mahiṣaṃ vinā |
strīkṣīraṃ caiva varjyāni sarvaśuktāni caiva hi || [MDh 5.9] iti || 17.24 ||
syandinīyamasūsandhinīnāṃ ca || 17.23 || — || 17.25 ||
nityaṃ sadadhyādi kṣīram apeyam ity anuṣaṅgaḥ | syandinī nitāntagranthistanī | yamasūḥ yamalāpatyā | sandhinī ekāṃ velām antarīkṛtya duhyate vatsāntareṇa vā | garbhiṇī yā duhyate sā sandhinīty eke | caśabdād vyādhitānāṃ ca || 17.23 ||
yasyāḥ stanebhyaḥ kṣīraṃ syandate sā syandinī | yamasūr yugmavatsasūtikā | yā garbhiṇī dugdhe sā saṃdhinī | ekakāladohanety anye | evaṃbhūtānāṃ gavādīnāṃ kṣīram apeyam || 17.25 ||
vivatsāyāś ca || 17.24 || — || 17.26 ||
yāś ca vyapetavatsāḥ yāś ca nāśamaraṇābhyāṃ vatsair viyuktāḥ tāsām apeyam | pṛthag grahaṇaṃ dadhyādī-nāṃ pratiṣedhābhāvārtham, kṣīramātrasyaiva pratiṣe-dhaḥ syād iti | ajāmahiṣyor apy upasaṃgrahārthaś cakāraḥ || 17.24 ||
vatsena viyuktā vivatsā | tasyāś ca gavādeḥ kṣīram apeyam | atra prakaraṇe pratiṣiddhavikārasyāpi dadhyādeḥ pratiṣedham icchanti | ācāras tv anirdaśāyāṃ tathānyatrāniyataḥ || 17.26 ||
sāṃprataṃ ye svarūpata evābhakṣyās ta ucyante |
idānīṃ svarūpata evābhakṣyān āha |
pañcanakhāś cāśalyakaśaśaśvāviḍgodhākhaḍga-kacchapāḥ || 17.25 || — || 17.27 ||
abhakṣyā ity uttaratra vakṣyati | tasyehānukarṣaḥ | pañca nakhā yeṣāṃ hastacaraṇayoḥ, pañca pañcanakhāḥ sṛgālavānarādayaḥ, te cābhakṣyāḥ śalyakādi varjayitvā | caśabdaḥ payomūtrocchiṣṭānām upasaṃgrahārthaḥ | śalyakaḥ kaṇṭakavarāhaḥ | śaśaḥ prasiddhaḥ | śvāviṭ ulūkaḥ | godhā prasiddhā | khaḍgaḥ śṛṅgamṛgaḥ | kacchapaḥ prasiddhaḥ | ye abhakṣyās teṣāṃ mūtrādy apeyaṃ draṣṭavyam,
abhakṣyāṇāṃ tu yan mūtraṃ taducchiṣṭaṃ tathaiva ca |
abhojyam iti nirdiṣṭaṃ viṣṭā caiva tathaiva ca ||
iti vatsavacanāt | pañcanakhāś ceti prathamānideśaḥ śarīrāvayavānāṃ sarveṣāṃ grahaṇaṃ yathā syād iti | pañcanakhāś ceti prathamānirdeśaḥ śarīrāvayavānāṃ sarveṣāṃ grahaṇaṃ yathā syād iti | yadi māṃsam eva abhīṣṭaṃ tadā ṣaṣṭhyā nirdeśo yukta iti | evaṃ ca rudhirādayo ‘py apeyā eva | evaṃ sarvatra draṣṭavyam || 17.25 ||
abhakṣyā ity uttaratra vakṣyati | yeṣāṃ pāṇipādeṣu pañcoccā nakhās te pañcanakhā vānarādayo ‘bhakṣyāḥ | śalyādīn varjayitvā | śalyako varāhaviśeṣo yasya nārācākārāṇi lomāni | śaśaḥ prasiddhaḥ | śvāvit kalpako yasya carmaṇā tanutrāṇaṃ kriyate | godhā kṛkalāsākṛtir mahākāyaḥ | khaḍgo mṛgāvaśeṣaḥ [mṛgaviśeṣaḥ?] | śṛṅgamṛtyuḥ | kacchapaḥ prasiddhaḥ | atra paṭhanti |
abhakṣyāṇāṃ tu yan mūtraṃ tad ucchiṣṭaṃ tathaiva ca |
abhojyam iti nirdiṣṭaṃ viṣṭhā caiva prayatnataḥ || iti || 17.27 ||
ubhayatodatkeśyalomaikaśaphakalaviṅkaplavacakravākahaṃsāḥ || 17.26 || — 17.28 ||
ubhayatodataḥ manuṣyādayaḥ | teṣāṃ pañcanakhatvād eva pratiṣedhe siddhe prāyaścittagauravārthaṃ punar abhidhānam | evam anyatrāpi viśeṣanirdeśe draṣṭavyam | keśinaḥ keśātiśayayuktāś camaryādayaḥ | alomāḥ sarpādayaḥ | ekaśaphāḥ aśvādayaḥ | kalaviṅko grāmacaṭakaḥ | plavaḥ śakaṭabilaḥ | cakravākahaṃsau prasiddhau || 17.26 ||
ubhayatodantā aśvādayaḥ | dadbhāva ārṣaḥ | keśinaḥ keśātiśayayuktāś camaryādayaḥ | alomānaḥ sarpādayaḥ | ekaśaphā ekakhurāḥ | anubhayatodantārtham idam | kalaviṅko grāmacaṭakaḥ | plavaḥ śakaṭabilākhyaḥ pakṣī | haṃsacakravākau prasiddhau | ete cābhakṣyāḥ
|| 17.28 ||
kākakaṅkagṛdhraśyenā jalajā raktapādatuṇḍā grāmyakukkuṭasūkarau
|| 17.27 || — || 17 29 ||
kākādayaḥ pakṣiṇaḥ prasiddhāḥ | jalajāḥ sarva evāviśe-ṣeṇa | raktapādāḥ pārāvatādayaḥ | raktatuṇḍāś cakorā-dayaḥ | grāmyaśabdaḥ kukkuṭasūkarābhyāṃ saṃbadh-yate | evaṃ cāraṇyānām apratiṣedhaḥ | visamāsa anyeṣāṃ kravyādādīnām api pratiṣedhārtham || 17.27 ||
kākādayaḥ prasiddhāḥ | jalajā api pakṣiṇa eva kākādisaṃnidhānāt | teṣāṃ viśeṣaṇaṃ raktapādatuṇḍā iti | grāme bhavo grāmyaḥ | uttarayoś caitad viśeṣaṇaṃ grāmyakukkuṭo grāmyasūkara iti | āraṇyayor apratiṣedhaḥ || 17.29 ||
dhenvanaḍuhau ca || 17.28 || — || 17.30 ||
dhenuḥ payasvinī gau | anaḍvān balīvardaḥ | tayor grahaṇaṃ vatsādīnām apy upalakṣaṇam | evaṃ tarhi gauś ceti vaktavyam iti ced, atrocyate – bhakṣyatvasamuccayārthaś cakāraḥ | tasmin bhakṣyatvapakṣe tayor eva grahaṇaṃ yathā syāt na vatsādīnām apīty evamartha upadeśaḥ | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “bhakṣyau tu dhenvanaḍuhau medhyau vājasaneyake vijñāyate” [VaDh 14.46] iti | tatrātithirūpeṇāgatasya dhenvanaḍuhau bhakṣyau itareṣām abhakṣyāv iti draṣṭavyam, vasiṣṭhaliṅgāt, yathā “pitṛdevātithipūjāyām apy evaṃ paśūn hiṃsyād iti mānavam” (VaDh 4.5), “athāpi brāhmaṇāya vā rājanyāya vābhyāgatāya mahokṣāṇaṃ mahājaṃ vā paced evam evāsmā ātithyaṃ kurvanti” (VaDh 4.8) iti || 17.28 ||
dhenuḥ payasvinī gauḥ | anaḍvān anovahanayogyo balīvardaḥ | dvandve ‘caturetyādisamāsāntanipātanād dhenvanaḍvāhāv iti [na] prāpno[tī]iti tad anādṛtam | apapāṭho vā | dhenvanaḍuhau cābhakṣyau | āpastambīye tu gotrābhyāṃ (yā) māṃsaṃ bhakṣyam uktvā “dhenvanuḍuhor bhakṣyaṃ medhyam ānaḍuham iti vājasaeyakam” [ĀpDh 1.17.30–31] ity uktam | ānaḍuhaṃ ca kevalaṃ bhakṣyaṃ kiṃ tarhi medhyam apīty arthaḥ | bahvṛcabrāhmaṇeṣu śrūyate – “tad yathaivādo manuṣyarāja āgate ‘nyasmin vārhaty ukṣāṇaṃ vā vehataṃ vākṣadanta” iti | tatrātither bhakṣyam anyeṣām abhakṣyam iti | vadho ‘pi kila tatrānujñātaḥ “dāśagoghnau saṃpradāne” gaur yasmai hanyate sa goghno ‘tithir iti | evaṃ kila pūvam ācāraḥ | idānīṃ gandho ‘pi (?) || 17.30 ||
apannadadavasannavṛthāmāṃsāni || 17.29 ||
apannadannavasannavṛthāmāṃsāni || 17.31 ||
apannadat apatitadantaḥ | avasanno vyādhitaḥ | svayaṃ mṛta ity apare | vṛthāmāṃsam ātmārtham eva pakvam | vṛthāgrahaṇād eva siddhe prāyaścittagauravārthaḥ punarupadeśaḥ | etac ca bhakṣyaviṣayaṃ draṣṭavyam, abhakṣye prāptyabhāvāt || 17.29 ||
apannadann apatitadantaḥ | so ‘pratiṣiddho ‘pi na bhakṣyaḥ | “yadā vai paśor dantāḥ padyante ‘tha sa medhyo bhavati” iti bahvṛcabrāhmaṇam | “yo ‘pannadan malaṃ tat paśūnām iti vijñāyate” ity āpastambaḥ | avasanno vyādhitaḥ | vṛthāmāṃsaṃ vṛthānnena vyākhyātam | punaḥ pratiṣedhas tu māṃsasya prāyaścittagauravārthaḥ || 17.31 ||
kisalayakyākulaśunaniryāsā lohitāḥ || 17.30 ||
kisalayakyākulaśunaniryāsāḥ || 17.32 ||
kisalayaṃ sarveṣām agraprarohaḥ | kyākuḥ ahicchatraṃ chatrākam | laśunaṃ prasiddham | niryāsāḥ lohitāś cet | tathā ca manuḥ: “lohitān vṛkṣaniryāsān” [MDh 5.6] ityādi | evaṃ ca hiṅgvādīnām apratiṣedhaḥ || 17 30 ||
kisalayaḥ pallavo ‘graprarohaḥ | kyākuś chatrākaḥ | laśunaṃ prasiddham | niryāso vṛkṣatvagbhūto ghanībhūto raso hiṅgvādiḥ | kisalayādayo ‘py abhakṣyāḥ || 17.32 ||
vraścanāś ca || 17.31 ||
lohitā vraścanāḥ || 17.33 ||
vraścanaprabhavāḥ vṛkṣāṇām anujā ity arthaḥ | te lohitāś cālohitāś cābhakṣyāḥ | evaṃ cāvraścanaprabhavatvād alohitatvāt karpūrādīnām apratiṣedhaḥ | kecit “lohitā vraścanāś ca” iti sūtrabhedaṃ kurvanti | teṣām api pakṣe karpūrādīnām apratiṣedhaḥ | anye niryāsā vārkṣā iti vyācakṣate | teṣāṃ hiṅgvādīnāṃ pratiṣedhaḥ | caśabdāt palāṇḍugṛñ-janādīni smṛtyantaropadiṣṭāni gṛhyante || 17.31 ||
vṛkṣādiṣu vṛkṇapradeśe bhavā vaścanā niryāsās te lohitāś cen na bhakṣyāḥ | svayaṃsūnā niryāsā lohitā alohitāś cābhakṣyāḥ | vraścanaprabhavās tu lohitā eva | manus tu |
lohitān vṛkṣaniryāsān vraścanaprabhavāṃs tathā | iti | [MDh 5.6] | kecit tu lohitaśabdaṃ kisalayādiṣv api paṭhanti | hiṅgus tu niryāso vraścanaprabhavo na veti cintyam | sarvathā śiṣṭā api bhakṣayanti | karpūras tu na niryāso na vraścanaprabhavo na lohitaḥ, tasmād bhakṣya eva || 17.33 ||

anyataropadeśād evānyatarasya siddheḥ ubhayavargo-padeśo yeṣāṃ vidhiḥ pratiṣedho vā nāsti teṣām āpady anugrahārthaḥ | pratudāḥ pratudya pratudya mukhena ye bhakṣayanti mayūrādayaḥ | viṣkirāḥ vikīrya vikīrya ye bhakṣayanti lāvakādayaḥ | ajālapādāḥ ye ‘jālapādā na bhavanti tumbakādayaḥ || 17.33 ||
tuṇḍena pratudya pratudya ye bhakṣayanti te pratudāḥ | ye pādābhyāṃ vikīrya bhakṣayanti mayūrādayas te viṣkirāḥ | jālākārau pādau yeṣāṃ te jālapādāḥ | ete bhakṣyāḥ | yady apy abhakṣyeṣūkteṣv anye bhakṣyā iti gamyate tathāpi bhakṣyā ity upādānam anuktānām āpady eva bhakṣaṇam [yathā] syād anāpadi mā bhūd iti || 17.35 ||
matsyāś cāvikṛtāḥ || 17.34 || — || 17.36 ||

yajñādau dharmanimitte ye hatā pūrvam abhakṣyā api bhakṣyāḥ | cakārāt prāṇānām atyaye ca | tathā ca manuḥ |
prokṣitaṃ bhakṣayen māṃsaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ ca kāmyayā |

nanu ca yajñādau hatānāṃ śrutividhānād eva siddhatvād ihopadeśo ‘narthaka iti |
atrocyate – ye adṛṣṭam evoddiśya kriyante tacchiṣṭabhakṣaṇa eva prāyaścittābhāvaḥ na tu dṛṣṭaprayojane śiṣṭabhakṣaṇa iti | ata eva yajñārthe ity anuktvā dharmārthe ity uktam | anenaivāvakīrṇipaśor api māṃsabhakṣaṇaṃ nirastam, tasya prāyaścittārthatvāt, na tv adṛṣṭārthatvam iti | evaṃ ca yathā dṛṣṭaprayojanamāṃsabhakṣaṇe smārtaprāyaścittānupraveśaḥ tathā tadviṣayahiṃsāyām api bhavatīti gamyate | anenaivābhiprāyeṇa manunāpy uktam,

vyālahatādṛṣṭadoṣavākpraśastāny abhyukṣyopayuñjītopayuñjīta || 17.36 || — || 17.38 ||
H reads “praśastān”
vyālaiḥ siṃhādibhiḥ hatasya paśor māṃsam abhyukṣaṇamātraṃ kṛtvā bhakṣayet | adṛṣṭadoṣe vyālahataviśeṣaṇam, tathā vākpraśaste ca | vyālahatasya paśor māṃsaṃ bhakṣyaṃ tac ced adṛṣṭadoṣaṃ vākpraśastaṃ ca bhavatīti | adṛṣṭadoṣam abhakṣyavarge cānupadiṣṭam, bhakṣyatvopadeśād eva siddhatvāt | vākpraśastaṃ cānyaiḥ | evaṃ ca yad bhakṣyavarge abhakṣyavarge cānupadiṣṭam āpadi vihitaṃ tasyānāpdy anena prakāreṇābhyanujñānam | bahuvacanam api viśeṣaṇatrayayuktasyaiva śucitvaṃ naikaviśeṣaṇayuktasyeti | evaṃ “baiṣkam apy upayuñjīta” [GDh 3.31] ity atrāpy adṛṣṭadoṣatvādi draṣṭavyam | aśnīyād iti vaktavye upayuñjīteti bhakṣaṇād varjanam eva śreya iti | tathā ca manuḥ |

atha aṣṭādaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ

asvatantrā dharme strī || 18.1 |
asvatantrā parādhīnā dharme pāralaukike bhartrā sahāsyā dharmādhikāraḥ, na tu puruṣavat svātantryeṇa | tathāha manuḥ |
nāsti strīṇāṃ pṛthag yajño na vrataṃ nāpy upoṣitam |
patiṃ śuśrūṣate yena tena svarge mahīyate || iti | [MDh 5.155] | śaṅkhenāpy uktam: “na ca vratopavāsaniyamejyādānadharmo vānugrahakaraḥ strīṇām anyatra patiśuśrūṣāyāḥ kāmaṃ tu bhartur anujñayā vratopavāsaniyamādīnām abhyāsaḥ strīdharmaḥ” iti |
nanu ca śrautasmārtānāṃ puruṣoddeśena vihitatvāt svātantryeṇa striyāḥ prāptyabhāvāt pratiṣedhānupapattiḥ | athocyeta asvātantryavacanaṃ sahadharmakriyāyāṃ striyā api phalam astīty evamartham iti, tad ayuktam, sahadharmacāriṇītvād evāsyārthasya labdhatvāt | tathā asvatantreti na jñāyate kiṃtantrayā anayā bhavitavyam iti | bhartradhīnatayeti cet, na, upariṣṭāt “nāticared bhartāram” [GDh 18.2] ity anena bhartṛparatantratvasya vakṣyamāṇatvāt | tenāvyabhicāro vakṣyata iti cet, na, (ca) aticārasya dharmārthakāmaviṣayasya pratiṣiddhatvād iti |
atrocyate – upavāsadānāder dharmasādhanasya bhartur anujñāyāṃ satyāṃ striyāḥ prāpaṇārtho ‘yam ārambhaḥ, āśramadharmasya bhartṛdvāreṇa prāptatvāt | tathā ca śaṅkhavacanaṃ yad darśitam “kāmaṃ tu bhartur anujñayā” ityādi | tathā dānaviṣaye ‘pi nārada āha |
strīkṛtāny apramāṇāni kāryāṇy āhur anāpati |

bāle pitur vaśe tiṣṭhet pāṇigrāhasya yauvane |
