naṣṭe bhartari ṣaḍvārṣikaṃ kṣapaṇam
|| 18.15 ||
deśāntaratiraskṛte bhartari ṣaḍvarṣāṇy apatyotpattir na kartavyā | tataḥ param icchantī guruniyogaṃ kārayitvā apatyam utpādayed ity abhiprāyaḥ | kutaḥ | “aniyuktāyām utpanna utpādayituḥ putro bhavatīty āhuḥ” [VaDh 17.63] iti vasiṣṭhasmṛtidarśanāt || 18.15 ||
śrūyamāṇe ‘bhigamanam || 18.16 || — || 18.15 ||
ṣaḍvarṣād ūrdhvaṃ yadi bhartā śrūyamāṇo bhavati tasmin śrūyamāṇe tam evābhimukhyena gacchet, nānyasmād apatyam utpādayed ity abhiprāyaḥ || 18.16 ||
yadā tu bhartā śrūyate tasmin deśe sthita iti tadā tam abhigacchet || 18.15 ||
pravrajite tu nivṛttiḥ prasaṅgāt || 18.17 || — || 18.16 ||
yadi pravrajito bhartā bhavati tasmin prasaṅgāt taṃ prati gamanān nivṛttiḥ | taṃ prati na gacched ity arthaḥ | atha vā prasaṅgād apatyotpādanān nivṛttiḥ, apatyaṃ notpādayet | tuśabdo viśeṣārthaḥ | abhilāṣābhāve notpādayet saṃyatayā bhavitavyam ity eke varṇayanti | pravrajite apatyaṃ notpādayet | tuśabdāt svayam api pravrajet, deśāntarasthasyābhiprāyo jñātum aśakya iti
|| 18.17 ||
yadi tu bhartā pravrajito bhavati mokṣāśramaṃ prāpto bhavati tadā sarvasmāt prasaṅgān nivṛttiḥ | svayam api nivṛttimukhī saṃyataiva syād iti || 18.16 ||
dvāda śavarṣāṇi brāhmaṇasya vidyāsaṃbandhe || 18.18 || — || 18.17 ||
vidyādhigamārthaṃ proṣitasya brāhmaṇasya bhāryā dvādaśa varṣāṇi kṣapayet apatyotpattiṃ tadabhimukhagamaṇam ca, pravrajite tu nivṛttiṃ ca kuryād iti draṣṭavyam | atra brāhmaṇagrahaṇāt pūrvaṃ kṣatriyādiviṣayam iti gamyate | atha vātra vidyāgrahaṇād arthārthaṃ gatasya brāhmaṇasyaiva | smṛtyantarasāmarthyāt kāmaviṣaye kṣapaṇaṃ nāstīti | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “proṣitapatnī pañca varṣāṇy upāsītordhvaṃ pañcabhyo varṣebhyo bhartṛsakāśaṃ gacched yadi dharmārthābhyāṃ pravased atha tu kāmād yathā prete evaṃ vartitavyaṃ syāt” [VaDh 17.75–77] iti | evaṃ ca kṣatriyāder vidyārthaṃ gatasyāpi brāhmaṇādibhyo ‘rdham ardhaṃ parikalpyam | arthārthaṃ gatasya vasiṣṭhoktaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “evaṃ brāhmaṇī pañcāprajātā ṣaṭ prajātā, catvāni rājakanyāprajātā pañca prajātā, trīṇi vaiśyā aprajātā catvāri prajātā, dve śūdrā aprajātā trīṇi prajātā” [VaDh 78] iti | anenaiva nyāyena vidyāsaṃbandhe ‘py aprajātāyā ekavarṣanyūnabhāvo draṣṭavyaḥ || 18.18 ||
vidyādhigamārthaṃ proṣitasya brāhmaṇasya bhāryā dvādaśa varṣāṇi kṣapayet | nāpatyotpattir nābhigamanam || 18.17 ||
bhrātari caivaṃ jyāyasi yavīyān kanyāgnyupayameṣu || 18.19 || — || 18.18 ||
bhrātary akṛtadāre anāhitāgnau ca proṣite jyeṣṭhe kanīyān evam eva kuryāt | evaṃśabdāt samastātideśaḥ śrūyamāṇe tadabhigamanam, pravrajite tu tadabhigamananivṛttiś ca tato draṣṭavyā | evaṃ tarhi pravrajite saṃyatatvam api prāpnotīti cet, na, smṛtyantaradarśanāt | yathāha uśanā: “pravrajati jyeṣṭhe kṣapaṇaṃ nāsti sadya eva niviśet” ityādi | evam apy arthārthaṃ gatasya ṣaḍvārṣikaṃ prāpnotīti cet, na, tatrāpi smṛtyantarasāmarthyāt | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
dvādaśaiva tu varṣāṇi jyāyān dharmārthayor gataḥ |
nyāyyaḥ pratīkṣituṃ bhrātrā śrūyamāṇaḥ punaḥ punaḥ || iti |
cakārāt smṛtyantaroktānām apy upasaṃgrahaḥ | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “aṣṭau daśa dvādaśa varṣāṇi jyeṣṭhaṃ bhrātaram aniviṣṭam apratīkṣamāṇaḥ prāyaścittī bhavati” iti | tatra bahuśaḥ śrūyamāṇe dvādaśa, punaḥ punar ity uktatvāt | kiṃcic chrūyamāṇe daśavarṣāṇi, sarvathā aśrūyamāṇe aṣṭāv iti draṣṭavyam | kṣatriyādeḥ pūrvavad ardham ardhaṃ parikalpyam | asyāpavādaḥ |
unmattaḥ kilbiṣī kuṣṭhī patitaḥ klība eva ca |
rājayakṣmāmayāvī ca na nyāyyaḥ syāt pratīkṣitum ||
ityādismṛtyantarokto draṣṭavyaḥ | jyeṣṭhagrahaṇād eva kanīyaso ‘rthataḥ siddheḥ saṃbandhiśabdatvād ekamātṛkasyaiva doṣa iti khyāpanārtham | smārtasyāpy ager upsaṃgrahārthaṃ bahuvacanam || 18.19 ||
jyeṣṭhe bhrātary akṛtadāre ‘nāhitāgnau ca proṣite kanīyān bhrātaivaṃ dvādaśa varṣāṇi pratīkṣeta | tataḥ kanyām upayacched agnīṃś cādadhīta | atra vāsiṣṭho viśeṣaḥ: “aṣṭau daśa dvādaśa varṣāṇi jyeṣṭhaṃ bhrātaram aniviṣṭaṃ na pratīkṣamāṇaḥ prāyaścittīyo bhavati” iti |
dvādaśaiva tu varṣāṇi jyāyān dharmārthayogyataḥ |
nyāyyaḥ pratīkṣituṃ bhrātā śrūyamāṇaḥ punaḥ punaḥ || iti ca || 18.18 ||
ṣaḍ ity eke || 18.20 || — || 18.19 ||
ṣaḍ eva varṣāṇi pratīkṣetety eke manyante | tatra proṣito ‘tyantavṛddhaś cet ṣaḍvarṣāṇi kṣapayet, no ced dvādaśavarṣāṇīti draṣṭavyam || 18.20 ||
eke manyante ṣaḍ eva varṣāṇi pratīkṣeteti | proṣite cātyantavṛddhe sthite cātyantadharmapara idam
|| 18.19 ||
prāsaṅgikam uktvā strīdharmam evāha |
gataṃ prāsāṅgikam, punar api strīdharmān āha |
trīn kumāry ṛtūn atītya svayaṃ yujyetāninditenotsṛjya pitryān alaṃkārān
|| 18.21 || — || 18.20 ||
trīn ṛtūn kumārī kanyā atītya yady etāṃ pitrādir na dadyāt svayam eva bhartāraṃ pratipadyeta aninditaṃ kulābhijanādisamanvitam ity arthaḥ | utsṛjya pitrādidattālaṃkārān | pitṛgrahaṇaṃ pradarśanārtham | tathā cāha manuḥ |
alaṃkāraṃ nādadīta pitryaṃ kanyā svayaṃvarā |
mātṛkaṃ bhrātṛdattaṃ vā steyaṃ syād yadi taṃ haret || [MDh 9.92] iti || 18.21 ||
yadi kanyāṃ pitrādir na dadyat tatas trīn ṛtūn atītya svayam evāninditena kulavidyāśīlādiyuktena bhartrā yujyeta pitryān pitṛkulāyātān alaṃkārān utsṛjya | atra manuḥ |
alaṃkāraṃ nādadīta pitryaṃ kanyā svayaṃvarā |
mātṛkaṃ bhrātṛdattaṃ vā steyaṃ syād yadi kiṃcana || [MDh 9.92] iti || 18.20 ||
kasmin kāle pitrādibhir dātavyety ata āha |
ata eva |
pradānaṃ prāg ṛtoḥ || 18.22 || — || 18.21 ||
ṛtudarśanāt prāg enāṃ dadyāt || 18.22 ||
ṛtudarśanāt prāg eva deyā kanyā || 18.21 ||
yataḥ |
aprayacchan doṣī || 18.23 || — || 18.22 ||
na kevalam aprayacchato dānanimittaguṇābhāvaḥ | kiṃ tarhi? dampatyoḥ brahmahatyāsamaṃ pāpaṃ bhavati | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
yāvantaḥ kanyām ṛtavaḥ spṛśanti
tulyaiḥ sakāmām abhiyācyamānām |
bhrūṇani tāvanti hatāni tābhyāṃ
mātāpitṛbhyām iti dharmavādaḥ || [VaDh 17.71] | iti || 18.23 ||
tasmin kāle ‘prayacchan pitrādir doṣavān bhavati | atra yājñavalkyaḥ |
pitā pitāmaho bhrātā sakulyo jananī tathā |
kanyāpradaḥ pūrvanāśe prakṛtisthaḥ paraḥ paraḥ ||
aprayacchan samāpnoti bhrūṇahatyām ṛtāv ṛtau || [YDh 1.63–64] iti || 18.22 ||
prāg vāsasaḥ pratipatter ity eke || 18.24 || — || 18.23 ||
durvijñeyatvād ṛtoḥ vāsasaḥ pratipatteḥ prāg dadyāt, yadaiva lajjitā tadaiva dadyād ity arthaḥ | evem eke manyante | varasaṃbhavato vikalpaḥ || 18.24 ||
eke manyante yadā kanyā vāsaḥ pratipadyate ‘tha vā lajjate tāvad eva pradeyeti || 18.23 ||
yadā pitrādir dadyāt tadā ācchādyālaṃkṛtāṃ dadyād ity uktam | tatra dravyābhāve katham ity ata āha |
dravyādānaṃ vivāhasiddhyartham || 18.25 ||
dravyādānaṃ vivāhasiddhyarthaṃ dharmatantrasaṃyoge ca śūdrāt || 18.24 ||
dravyasya parakīyasyādānam ananujñātasya svīkaraṇaṃ vivāhasiddhaye yāvatā vivāho nirvartate ātmīyābhāve tāvad apahṛtya dadyād ity arthaḥ || 18.25 ||
dravyam ananujñātam api śūdrāc cailādikam ādeyaṃ vivāhasiddhyarthaṃ yāvatā vivāhaḥ sidhyati tāvat | adhike doṣaḥ | tathā dharmasya paśubandhādeḥ pravṛttasya yat tantram aṅgam aśvādi tasya saṃyoge ‘vicchedasiddhyarthe yāvatā tan nivartate [nirvartate] tāvad ananujñātam apy ādeyaṃ śūdrāt | adhike doṣaḥ
|| 18.24 ||
kiṃ ca |
dharmatantrasaṅge ca || 18.26 ||
dharmatantrasyāgnihotrādeḥ pravṛttasya vicchedaḥ saṅgaḥ, tasmiṃś ca sati parasvāpaharaṇaṃ kuryāt | tantragrahaṇaṃ yat prakrāntam avaśyakartavyaṃ tasyaiva saṅge, na tv apravṛttasya pravṛttyartham | siddhyartham ity asyānukarṣaṇārthaś cakāraḥ | yāvatā nirvartate tāvad eva gṛhṇīyāt na tato ‘dhikam iti | evaṃ cādhikagrahaṇe steyam eva bhavati || 18.26 ||
kutas tad ādeyam ity ata āha |
śūdrāt || 18.27 ||
yajñārthaṃ hi dhanam | na ca śūdrasya vaidikeṣu karmasv adhikāraḥ | ataḥ prathamaṃ tāvac chūdrād ādadīta || 18.27 ||
tadalābhe |
anyatrāpi śūdrāt || 18.28 ||
anyatrāpi śūdrād bahupaśor hīnakarmaṇaḥ
|| 18.25 ||
anyatrāpīti vaktavye śūdragrahaṇaṃ vaiśyakṣatriyaprāpaṇārtham | tadalābhe vaiśyāt | tadalābhe kṣatriyād iti || 18.28 ||
“itarābhyo ‘pi dṛśyante” iti pañcamyās tral | śūdrād anyato ‘pi dravyam ādeyaṃ sa ced bahupaśus tathā hīnakarmā bhavati | tadanurūpaṃ karma na karoti niṣiddhaṃ vā karma sevate | śūdragrahaṇaṃ vidhir ayaṃ yathā syād iti | tena śūdrālābhe vaiśyat | tadalābhe kṣatriyāt || 18.25 ||
tatrāpi |
bahupaśor hīnakarmaṇaḥ || 18.29 ||
hīnakarmā yaḥ ananurūpaṃ karma karoti, kiṃcid api na karotīti vā sa hīnakarmā | tatsakāśād ādadīta
|| 18.29 ||
uktam evārtham udāharaṇena darśayati |
śatagor anāhitāgneḥ || 18.30 || — || 18.26 ||
anyakarmakṛto ‘pi śatagoḥ agnyādhānam akurvataḥ | śatagrahaṇaṃ dravyaparimāṇopalakṣaṇārtham, śataniṣkasyety arthaḥ | evaṃ sarvatra || 18.30 ||
gograhaṇam upalakṣaṇam | yas tāvad dravyo bhavaty agnīṃś ca nādhatte | niṣiddhakarmasevī tu daṇḍāpūpikayā vyākhyātaḥ || 18.26 ||
sahasragoś cāsomapāt || 18.31 || — || 18.27 ||
yaś cāhitāgnir api sahasraguḥ somaṃ na pibati tasmād apy ādadīta | caśabdād anyto ‘pi | yaḥ prabhūtadhanatve sati tadanurūpaṃ karma na karoti tasmād apy ādadīta
|| 18.31 ||
pūrveṇa gatam | yaḥ sahasraguś ca bhavati somaṃ ca na pibati tasmād iti || 18.27 ||
na kevalaṃ nimittadvayam eva paradravyādāne, kiṃ ca |
saptamīṃ cābhuktvānicayāya || 18.32 || — || 18.28 ||
saptamīṃ velām abhuktvā dhanakṣayāt paradravyādānaṃ kuryāt | tathāha manuḥ |
tathaiva saptame bhakte bhaktāni ṣaḍ anaśnatā |
aśvastanavidhānena hartavyaṃ hīnakarmaṇaḥ || iti | [MDh 11.16] | hīnakarmādhikārārthaḥ cakāraḥ | tathodāhṛtaṃ ca manuvacanam | anicayāya udarapūraṇamātram || 18.32 ||
saptamyarthe dvitīyā | ṣaṭsu velāsu bhojyālābhenābhuktvā saptamyāṃ velāyāṃ yāvatā vṛttis tāvad ananumatam apy ādeyam | anicayaḥ punas tena nicayo na kartavyaḥ | śvo bhojyam api nādeyam | atra manuḥ |
tathaiva saptame bhakte bhaktāni ṣad anaśṇatā |
aśvastanavidhānena hartavyaṃ hīnakarmaṇā || [MDh 11.16] iti || 18.28 ||
apy ahīnakarmabhyaḥ || 18.33 || — || 18.29 ||
asyām avasthāyāṃ yathāvihitakarmakāribhyo ‘pi | apiśabdād āhitāgneḥ somapāc ca || 18.33 ||
asyām avasthāyām ahīnakarmabhyo ‘py ādeyam | apiśabdaḥ kathaṃcid asyānujñātam iti darśayati | tena prāṇasaṃśaya evedaṃ bhavati || 18.29 ||
yady ādadāno rājapuruṣeṇa nṛpasamīpaṃ nīyate cauryanimittaṃ ca pṛcchyate tadā |
ācakṣīta rājñā pṛṣṭaḥ || 18.34 || — || 18.30 ||
ācakṣīta kathayet na tu bhayād apahnuvīta pṛṣṭaḥ san | na tu svayaṃ gatvā kathayet || 18.34 ||
yady asāv evaṃ kurvan svāmibhir gṛhīto rājasakāśaṃ nītas tena pṛṣṭaḥ kim ittham akārṣīr iti tadā svām avasthām ācakṣīta | na tu mithyā vaded iti || 18.30 ||
yasmāt |
tena hi bhartavyaḥ śrutaśīlasaṃpannaś cet || 18.35 || — || 18.31 ||
hiśabdo yasmādarthe | yasmāt tena hi bhartavyaḥ, “bibhṛyāt brāhmaṇān śrotriyān” [GDh 10.9] iti vacanāt | śrutaśīlasaṃpanno yadi bhavati | śrutena vedārthavijñānena, śīlena taccoditakarmānuṣṭhānenāpi saṃpannaḥ samyak stutaḥ | “bibhṛyāt brāhmaṇān śrotriyān” [GDh 10.9] iti śrotriyāṇāṃ bharaṇopadeśāt bhartavyavacanenaiva śrutaśīlasaṃpannatā siddheti cet, ucyate – aśrotriyāṇām api bharaṇopadeśāt “nirutsāhāṃś ca brāhmaṇān [cābrāhmaṇān]” [GDh 10.10] iti | evaṃ ca parasvāpaharaṇe abrāhmaṇo na bhartavya iti pradarśitam | teneti vacanaṃ rājño dharmārtham evāsya bharaṇaṃ na vṛttyartham iti
|| 18.35 ||
hiścārthe | tena ca rājñā sa na kevalam adaṇḍyaḥ kiṃ tarhi tata ārabhya bhartavyas taveyam avasthā mayā na jñāteti sāntvayitvā | sa cec chrutavṛttaśīlasaṃpanno bhavati | śrutaṃ śāstraparijñānam | śīlaṃ tadanukūla ācāraḥ | itaro ‘pi na daṇḍyaḥ | bharaṇaṃ tu tasya tādṛśaṃ na kāryam | daṇḍābhāvaḥ pūrvayor api nimittayoḥ samānaḥ || 18.31 ||
dharmatantrapīḍāyāṃ tasya karaṇe ‘doṣo ‘doṣaḥ || 18.36 ||
dharmatantrapīḍāyāṃ tasyākaraṇe doṣo ‘karaṇe doṣaḥ || 18.32 ||
yasmād bhartavyas tasmād asya dharmatantrapīḍāyām – dharmaśabdenāgnihotrādaya ucyante, tantraśabdena vivāhaḥ, pīḍāśabdenātmapīḍā saptamīṃ cābhuktvety anenoktā | dharmavicchede vivāhasiddhaye ātmapīḍāyāṃ cety arthaḥ | tasya cauryasya karaṇe adoṣaḥ | daṇḍo nāstīty arthaḥ | rājña upekṣayā dharmatantrapīḍāyāṃ satyāṃ cauryaṃ kṛtvā svadoṣād āgate caurye brāhmaṇasya daṇḍapātanaṃ na yuktam ity abhiprāyaḥ |
kecid vyācakṣate — śrutaśīlasaṃpannaś cet bhartavyatvād ācakṣīta | aśrotriyo ‘py ācakṣīta | yasmāt tasmād aśrotriyasyāpi dharmatantrapīḍāyāṃ daṇḍo nāstīti |
apare vyācakṣate — bibhṛyāc chrotriyān iti yo ‘sau bhartavyaḥ, so ‘sminn aparādhe ‘pi bhartavya eva | hiśabda evaśabdārthe | tadanurūpo ‘rthaḥ asyāpi dātavya ity arthaḥ | aśrotriyam api na daṇḍayet, yatas tasyāpi tasminn aparādhe daṇḍo nāstīti | kutaḥ? dharmatantrapīḍāyām ity asya sūtrasyāśrotriyārthatvād ārambhasyeti || 18.36 ||
yadi paśubandhādau dharme pravṛttasya tadaṅgaṃ pāśvādi kenacit pīḍitaṃ bhavati hatam apahṛtaṃ vā tasmin nivedite tadaiva tasya pratividhānaṃ kāryaṃ rājñā | akaraṇe doṣo bhavati | abhyāso ‘dhyāyasamāptyarthaḥ || 18.32 ||
atha ekonaviṃśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
pañcavidho dharmo vyākhyeyatayā prakrāntaḥ | tatra caturvidha uktaḥ | idānīṃ pañcamo dharmo naimittiko vaktavyaḥ | tadabhidhānārtham uktopasaṃhāraṃ karoti |
pañcavidho dharmaḥ – varṇadharma āśramadharma ubhayadharmo guṇadharmo naimittika[dharma]ś ceti | tatra varṇaprayukto dharmo varṇadharmaḥ “upanayanaṃ brāhmaṇasyāṣṭame” [GDh 1.6] iti | āśramaprayukta āśramadharmo brahmacāryādeḥ samidādhānādir iti | ubhayaprayukta ubhayadharmo brāhmaṇasya brāhmacāriṇaḥ pālaśo daṇda ityādi | abhiṣekaguṇayuktaḥ prajāpālanādir guṇadharmaḥ | brahmahatyādau nimitte kartavyo naimittiko dharmaḥ prāyaścittam | tatra naimittikaṃ vakṣyann uktam anubhāṣate |
ukto varṇadharmaś cāśramadharmaś ca || 19.1 ||
kiṃ punar anena sūtreṇa kriyate | anuktaprakaraṇopanyāsārtham uktānuvādaḥ kriyata iti cet, ayuktam, anupasaṃhāre ‘py uttarasyābhidhātuṃ śakyatvāt | tarhi śrotṝṇām adhikākāṅkṣānivṛttiḥ kriyata iti cet, tad apy ayuktam, naimittikārambhād eva tatsiddheḥ | kiṃ ca varṇāśramagrahaṇaṃ na kartavyam, tatsaṃbandhenaiva dharmābhidhānāt, ukto dharma ity etāvatāpi tadupasaṃhāraḥ kṛto bhavatīti, kṛtsnopasaṃhāreṇa hi varṇāśramadharmaguṇadharmayor apy abhidhānaṃ kartavyam iti |
niṣprayojanatvāc cakārāv apy anarthakau | tasmād idam asamañjasaṃ sūtram iti |
atra brūmaḥ —
kecit tāvad varṇayanti – ādhāraṃ prasidhyartham iti | idam ayuktam, “varṇā āśramāś ca” [GDh 11.31] iti sūtrārambhād eva jñāyamānatvāt |
apare punar varṇayanti – śūdrasyābhakṣyabhakṣaṇaprāyaścittābhāvārtham idam iti | tad apy ayuktam – “praśastānāṃ svakarmasu dvijātīnām” [GDh 17.1] ity atra brāhmaṇagrahaṇaṃ dvijātipradarśanārtham uktaṃ na śūdrapradarśanārtham api | kutaḥ? smṛtyantaradarśanāt | yathāha manuḥ |
etad uktaṃ dvijātīnāṃ bhakṣyābhyakṣaym aśeṣataḥ | [MDh 5.26] iti śūdrasya bhakṣyābhakṣyādhikārābhāvāt prāyaścittābhāvo ‘rthasiddha iti |
anye varṇayanti – upanayanāt prāg api kaiścid varṇadharmair adhikārārtham idam iti | tad apy asamyak, “varṇā āśramāś ca” [GDh 11.31] ity atraivoktatvāt |
apare varṇayanti – varṇāśramadharmāṇāṃ striyā api prāptyartham iti | tad apy ayuktam, vivāhāt prāg anupanītā sā bhavatīti “varṇā āśramāś ca” [GDh 11.31] ity atra varṇagrahaṇenaiva siddham, uttarakālam apy upanītety āśramagrahaṇād āśramadharmair adhikriyata iti | tatrāha manuḥ |
vaivāhiko vidhiḥ strīṇām aupanāyanikaḥ smṛtaḥ | [MDh 2.67] ityādi | atas tata eva siddhe kim anena sūtreṇa kriyata iti |
apara āhuḥ – uttarārtho ‘yam anuvādaḥ | yeṣāṃ varṇavibhāgena dharmā uktās teṣām eva vakṣyamāṇo ‘pīti | tataś ca śūdrasyāpi prāyaścittasiddhir iti | tad apy ayuktam, dvayam eva prāyaścittānāṃ nidānaṃ vihitākaraṇaṃ pratiṣiddhasevanaṃ ca | tatra vihitākaraṇe manur āha |
vedoditānāṃ nityānāṃ karmaṇām ativartane |
snātakavratalope ca prāyaścittam abhojanam || iti | [MDh 11.203]
pratiṣiddhasevāyāṃ ca bṛhaspatir āha |
pratiṣiddhaṃ tu yaḥ kuryād brāhmaṇas tu viśeṣataḥ |
ekarātram anādeśa ādiṣṭe tu pṛthak pṛthak || iti |
tataś ca śūdrasyāpy arthasiddhatvāt na tatprāpaṇārtham idam ārabdham iti |
atrocyate — āśaṅkānivṛttyartham idam, upariṣṭād vaidikāni punaḥstomādīny udāhariṣyante tāni śūdrasya na bhavanti | atas tadvad itareṣām api tannivṛttir mā bhūd ity āśaṅkāpanīyata iti | vayo’vasthāviśeṣāpekṣayā vā prāyaścittasyānugrahārthaṃ varṇagrahaṇam | yathāha bhṛguḥ |
aśītir yasya varṣāṇi bālo vāpy ūnaṣoḍaśaḥ |
prāyaścittārdham arhanti striyo rogiṇa eva ca || iti |
balaviśeṣāpekṣayā vā | yathāha bhṛguḥ |
durbalasyāpi yat proktaṃ tad uktaṃ bālavṛddhayoḥ |
saṃkocavṛttinā vāpi tathā kālaviparyaye || iti |
vyāso ‘pi |
deśaṃ kālaṃ vayaḥ śaktiṃ jñānaṃ buddhikṛtaṃ tathā |
abuddhikṛtam abhyāsaṃ jñātvā niṣkramaṇaṃ vadet || iti |
caśabdo ‘py avāntarajopasaṃgrahārthaḥ | tadupasaṃgrahaś ca teṣām api prāyaścittārdhaprāptyarthaḥ | tataś ca mātṛjātivat parikalpyam | āśramagrahaṇaṃ prāyaścittānāṃ saṃbhavaprāpaṇārtham, yathaiva pāpakṣayārthaṃ prāyaścittānāṃ kalpanā kriyate evam āśramāpakṣayāpi | tataś ca japādīnāṃ saṃbhavato viśeṣaḥ, na hi homadānayoḥ bhukṣuke saṃbhavaḥ, anagnitvād aparigrahatvāc ca | tathā brahmacāriṇāṃ dāne | dvitīyaś cakāraḥ strīdharmānukarṣaṇārthaḥ | varṇāśramagrahaṇenaiva tasya siddheḥ punar apy anuvādaḥ prāyaścittakalpanārthaḥ | tatrodāhṛtaṃ ca bhṛguvacanam: “aśītir yasya varṣāṇi” ityādi | tathā ca aṅgirāḥ |
nāry apy akāmataḥ prāptā pāpam ardhaṃ samācaret |
arvāk tu dvādaśād varṣāt puruṣo ‘py ardhabhāg bhavet || iti || 19.1 ||
ubhayadharmaguṇadharmayor apy upalakṣaṇam etat | yady apy anyatroktaṃ nānubhāṣyate ‘nanubhāṣaṇe ‘pi vakṣyamāṇaṃ śakyate vaktum iti tathāpīhānubhāṣyata āśaṅkānivṛttyartham | anyathopariṣṭād daivikāni punaḥstomādīni prāyaścittaṇy udāhariṣyante tāni ca śūdrasya na saṃbhavanty atas tadvad eva punaḥstomādīni prāyaścittāntarāṇy api śūdrasya na syur iti kaścid āśaṅketa | apara āha – ya ukto dharmaḥ sa eva varṇinām āśramiṇāṃ ca dharmaḥ | vakṣyamāṇas tu puruṣamātradharmaḥ | yad āha “atha khalv ayaṃ puruṣaḥ” [GDh 19.2] iti | kiṃ siddhaṃ bhavati | pratilomānām api prāyaścitteṣv adhikāraḥ siddho bhavati | yady api teṣāṃ bhakṣyābhakṣyaviveko nāsti tathāpi gobrāhmaṇādivadhe brāhmaṇasvarṇādiharaṇe ca prāyaścittaṃ bhavaty eva | akurvāṇā eva tu prāyaścittaṃ rājñā vadhyāḥ | ahiṃsāsatyāsteyabrahmacaryāparigrahādimanuṣya-mātradharmā na varṇeṣv āśrameṣu vā niyatāḥ | atas tadatikrame yuktam eva prāyaścittam | yat tu pūrvam uktam “pratilomās tu dharmahīnāḥ” [GDh 4.20] iti, tad aihikāmuṣmikaśreyaḥsādhaneṣu karmasv adhikāranivṛttiparam iti || 19.1 ||
idānīṃ prāyaścittakāraṇam āha |
prāyaścittasya nimittāny āha |
atha khalv ayaṃ puruṣo yāpyena karmaṇā lipyate || 19.2 ||
atha khalv ayaṃ puruṣo yāpyena karmaṇā lipyate yathaitad ayājyayājanam abhakṣyabhakṣaṇam avadyavadanaṃ śiṣṭasyākriyā pratiṣiddhasevanam iti || 19.2 ||
nanu ca śiṣṭasyākriyā pratiṣiddhasevanaṃ ca prāyaścittakāraṇam | tac coparitanasūtre pratipādayiṣyata ity anarthakam evaitat sūtram iti |
atrocyate – ātmānam ajñātvā yaḥ karma karoti tasyaiva karmasaṃbandhaḥ, na tv ātmajñāninaḥ vihitākaraṇe pratiṣiddhasevane vā lepasaṃbandho ‘stīti anātmajñānina eva prāyaścittādhikārārtham idam | tathā cātharvopaniṣat |
bhidyate hṛdayagranthiḥ chidyante sarvasaṃśayāḥ |
kṣīyante cāsya karmāṇi tasmin dṛṣṭe parāvare || iti |
tataś cājñānina idam |
atha karmānuṣṭhānāntaram, khalv ity avadhāraṇe ayam eva puruṣa iti | ayam iti pratyakṣeṇa śarīriṇaṃ kṣetrajñaṃ vyapadiśati, avadhāraṇayā paramātmānaṃ nivartayati | tathā copaniṣat |
dvā suparṇā sayujā sakhāyā samānaṃ vṛkṣaṃ pariṣasvajāte |
tayor anyaḥ pippalaṃ svādvatty anaśnann anyo abhicākaśīti || iti | [MuṇḍUp 3.1.1] | puri śayanāt puruṣaḥ | tathopaniṣat: “yo veda nihitaṃ guhāyāṃ parame vyoman” iti [TaitUp 2.4.1] | yāpyena garhyeṇa karmaṇā lipyate saṃbadhyate | yady apy ayaṃ dharmeṇāpi lipyate, tathāpi dharmasaṃbandhe prāyaścittābhāvād adharmasyaivopādānam | “khalv ayaṃ puruṣo yāpena karmanā lipyate” ity ukte kṛtasya karmaṇo ‘vaśyasaṃbandhāt athaśabdo ‘narthaka iti cen, na, anupalakṣite ‘pi prāyaścittanimitte japāder anuṣṭhānārthaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
saṃvatsarasyaikam api caret kṛcchraṃ dvijottamaḥ |
ajñātabhuktaśuddhyarthaṃ jñātasya tu viśeṣataḥ || iti | [MDh 5.21]
evaṃ cābuddhipūrvake ‘pi prāyaścittam astīti jñāpitam | tathā ca manuḥ |
akāmataḥ kṛte pāpe prāyaścittaṃ vidur budhāḥ |
kāmakārakṛte ‘py āhur eke śrutinidarśanāt || iti | [MDh 11.46] | tataḥ buddhipūrve gurutaram abuddhipūrve laghutaram iti draṣṭavyam | tathā ca vakṣyati “antāvasāyinīgamane kṛcchrābdaḥ amatyā dvādaśarātram” [GDh 23.33–34] ityādi | pūrvajanmakṛtasyāpi saṃbandhajñāpanārthaṃ karmagrahaṇam | tathā ca manuḥ |
iha duścaritaiḥ kecit kecit pūrvakṛtais tathā |
prāpnuvanti durātmāno narā rūpaviparyayam || iti | [MDh 11.48] | tatraivāha “brahmahā kṣayarogitvam” [MDh 11.49] ityādi | evaṃ ca kṣayādiliṅgadarśane pūrvajanmakṛtasyāpi prāyaścittavidhānaṃ kartavyam ity evamartham upadeśaḥ kartavya ity arthaḥ | pāpena karmaṇā lipyata iti vaktavye yāpyagrahaṇaṃ dharmo ‘pi lokaninditaś ced adharmāya bhavatīti jñāpanārtham | tathā ca manuḥ |
parityajed arthakāmau yau syātāṃ dharmavarjitau |
dharmaṃ cāpy asukhodarkaṃ lokasaṃkruṣṭam eva ca || iti | [MDh 4.176] | yāpyeneti visamāsaḥ, yady apy ātmanā na kṛtaṃ tathāpi parair garhitatvād abhiśaṃsanamātreṇāpi prāyaścittī bhavatīti jñāpanārtham | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ | "brāhmaṇam anṛtenābhiśaṃsya patanīyenopapanīyena vā māsam abbhakṣaḥ śuddhavatīr āvartayet "| [VaDh 23.39] pūto bhavatīti vijñāyate | etenābhiśasto vyākhyātaḥ | [VaDh 23.37] iti || 19.2 ||
atha khalv iti vākyālaṃkāre | ayaṃ puruṣa iti saṃghātavartinaṃ pratyagātmānaṃ nirdiśati | yāpyaṃ kutsitam | yāpyena pāpena karmaṇā lipyate | tajjanyenādharmeṇa lipyamāne karmaṇā lipyata iti bhākto vādaḥ | yāpyasya karmaṇa udāharaṇaprapañco yathaitad ityādi | yathety udāharaṇe | ayājyāḥ patitādayas teṣāṃ yājanam | abhakṣyā laśunādayas teṣāṃ bhakṣaṇam | avadyam anṛtāsabhyādi tasya vadanaṃ kathanam | śiṣṭaṃ vihitaṃ saṃdhyopāsanādi tasyākriyākaraṇam | pratiṣiddhasya hiṃsādeḥ sevanaṃ karaṇam | iti samāptau | etāvad eva yāpyaṃ karmeti | pratiṣiddhasevanam ity eva siddher ayājyayājanādigrahaṇaṃ yājanādhyāpanapratigrahāḥ sarveṣām ity āpady anujñā tatrāpi prāyaścittārtham | tatra uśanā: “āpadvihitaiḥ karmabhir āpadaṃ tīrtvā punas teṣāṃ prāyaścittaṃ caturbhāgaṃ kuryāt” iti | abhakṣyabhakṣaṇagrahaṇam apy āpadi vyādhyādau laśunādibhakṣaṇaviṣayaṃ ca | avadyavadanagrahaṇaṃ tu,
prāṇināṃ tu vadho yatra tatra sākṣy anṛtaṃ vadet |
ityādiviṣayam ca | tathā yatra brāhmaṇa iti jñāte tāḍayeyur arthaṃ vā hareyus tatra tadgrahaṇārtham | asabhyānṛtabhāṣaṇenāpi tan nivārya paścāt tām āpadaṃ tīrtvā prāyaścittaṃ caturbhāgaṃ cared iti
|| 19.2 ||
kathaṃ yāpyena karmaṇā lipyata iti cet, tatsādhanasevanāt | kiṃ tatsādhanam iti cet, tad āha |
yathaitad ayājyayājanam abhakṣyabhakṣaṇam avadyavadanaṃ śiṣṭasyākriyā pratiṣiddhasevanam iti || 19.3 ||
yathaitad iti dṛṣṭāntārtham | ayājyayājanam ayājyānāṃ patitānāṃ yājanam | abhakṣyabhakṣaṇaṃ laśunādīnāṃ bhakṣaṇam | avadyavadanam anṛtādīnāṃ vadanam | śiṣṭasya vihitasya saṃdhyopāsanāder akriyā akaraṇam | pratiṣiddhasya hiṃsādeḥ sevanam karaṇam | itikaraṇaṃ parisamāptyartham, etāvad adharmasādhanam iti |
nanu ca pratiṣiddhagrahaṇād evāyājyayājanāder api saṃgrahaṇāt punar abhidhānam anarthakam iti |
ucyate – anujñātānām apy eteṣām akāryatvajñāpanārthaṃ svaśabdenopdānam iti | tataś cāpy avidyasyāpy āpady anuṣṭhāne prāyaścittaṃ siddham, tatra hy abhyanujñātam “yājanādhyāpanapratigrahāḥ sarveṣām” [GDh 7.4] iti | tathā hy abhakṣyāṇāṃ bhakṣaṇam api bhakṣyavargānupadiṣṭānām apy āpady anujñātam | tathā āpady anṛtam api smṛtyantare abhyanujñātam | yathāha vyāghraḥ |
yasyoktau vāṅmanaḥpīḍā nityaṃ bhavati cet tataḥ |
tatra vaktavyam anṛtaṃ vyāghrasya vacanaṃ yathā || ity anujñānasāmarthyāl laghutaraprāyaścittam iti | tad api prathamakalpitāt caturbhāgarūpam | yathāha uśanā: “āpadvihitaiḥ karmabhir āpadaṃ tīrtvā punas teṣāṃ prāyaścittaṃ caturbhāgaṃ kuryāt” iti || 19.3 ||
tatra prāyaścittaṃ kuryān na kuryād iti mīmāṃsante || 19.4 || — || 19.3 ||
tasmin yāpyakarmasaṃbandhe tanniryātanārthaṃ prāyaścittaṃ kartavyam uta na kartavyam iti mīmāṃsante vicārayanti brahmavādinaḥ || 19.4 ||
tatra tasmin yāpyakarmalepe prāyaścittam,
prāyo nāma tapaḥ proktaṃ cittaṃ niścaya ucyate |
taponiścayasaṃyogāt prāyaścittam iti smṛtam ||
ityevaṃlakṣaṇaṃ kartavyaṃ na kartavyam iti vicārayanti brahmavādinaḥ || 19.3 ||
tatra kecit |
na kuryād ity āhuḥ || 19.5 || — || 19.4 ||
eke tāvad upapattibalān na kuryād ity āhuḥ || 19.5 ||
āpatkalpaviṣaye tāvad vidhīyamānatvād adoṣaḥ | anāpady api vihitākaraṇe pratiṣiddhasevane vā prāyaścittam anarthakam | kutaḥ |
tatra hetuḥ |
na hi karma kṣīyata iti || 19.6 || — || 19.5 ||
hiśabdo yasmādarthe | yasmāt kṛtaṃ karma phalapradānam antareṇa na kṣīyate prāyaścittaiḥ, tataś cātmasaṃsthaṃ karma ā vināśāt phalaṃ dadāti | tathāha śaṅkhaḥ |
yathā pṛthivyāṃ bījāni ratnāni ca payonidhau |
evam ātmani karmāṇi tiṣṭhanti prabhavanti ca || iti |
tatphalapradānottarakālaṃ svayam eva vinaśyati | bījavīryam ivāṅkure | tathā śrutiḥ: “puṇyo vai puṇyena karmaṇā bhavati pāpaḥ pāpena” iti | na ceha kevalaḥ sukhopabhogaḥ kasyacid upalabhyate yenānumīye-tāsya prāyaścittenāśubhaṃ hīyata iti | yāni tu śrautāni prāyaścittāni tāni “bhinne juhoti skanne juhoti” ity evamādīni nimitte karmāṅgāni na doṣanirharaṇārthāni | loke ‘pi ca ghaṭasya chidrasya jatvādinā kāryasā-marthyādhānamātraṃ kriyate na pūrvavat pratyāpattiḥ | na cāśubhasyātmānaṃ vyāpyāvasthitasyāpanodaḥ śakyate kartum, udakavyāpina iva lavaṇasya | prāyaścittajanitena dharmeṇādharmo ‘bhibhūyate na tu vināśyate | auṣṇyenevodakaśītatvam, punar api śītabhāvadarśanāt || 19.6 ||
hiśabdo hetau | yasmāt kṛtaṃ karma puṇyaṃ pāpaṃ ca nāntareṇopabhogaṃ kṣīyata iti | tathā ca śaṅkhaḥ |
yathā pṛthivyāṃ bījāni ratnāni nidhayo yathā |
evam ātmani karmāṇi tiṣṭhanti prasavanti ca || iti |
utpanne tu phale naśyati yathā bījam aṅkure | prāyaścittāni tu nimitte karmāntarāṇi | yathā gṛhadāhādau kṣāmavatyadayaḥ || 19.5 ||
kuryād ity aparam || 19.7 || — || 19.6 ||
kuryāt prāyaścittam ity aparaṃ darśanam | na vidyate asmāt param ity aparaṃ siddhānta ity arthaḥ | āpatkalpadharmānuṣṭhāne tāvad vacanena vidhīyamānasya vacanāntareṇa prāyaścittam api darśitam | anāpady api prāyaścittam arthavad eva | katham? japādinimittaduḥkhopabhogenaiva kṣīyate, tatkaraṇatvāj japādinimittasya duḥkhānubhavasya, dīrghakāliko ‘py alpakālikena kṣīyata eva, yathā dīrghakāliko rogo loke ‘lpakālikavyathenauṣadha-pānena kṣīyate |
kathaṃ punar idam avagamyate yathā prabhūtaduḥkhaphalo ‘dharmo ‘lpena duḥkhopa-bhogena kṣīyata iti | āgamād avagamyate | taṃ ca darśayati: “punaḥstomeneṣṭvā punaḥsavanam āyantīti vijñāyate,” “vrātyastomeneṣṭvā,” “tarati sarvaṃ pāpmānaṃ tarati brahmahatyāṃ yo ‘śvamedhena yajate,” “agniṣṭutābhiśasyamānaṃ yājayed iti ca” [GDh 19.8–11] |
ayam asyābhiprāyaḥ – āgamagamya evāyam arthaḥ idaṃ dharmasādhanam idam adharmasādhanam iti | asmin kṛte adharmaḥ kṣīyata iti nopapattigamyaḥ, pratyakṣādipramāṇāntarāviṣayatvāt | tena yāvatya upapattaya uktāḥ prāyaścittākaraṇe, tāḥ sarvā āgamavirodhād eva nirasyante |
yad uktaṃ na hi ghaṭasya chidritasya jatvādipratipūraṇena pratyāpattir astīti,
tatrocyate – tatra hi yogyatāpādanam eva vivakṣitam, na hi ghaṭotpattir ity avirodhaḥ | prāyaścittenāpi cādharmakṣayadvāreṇa puruṣasya yogyataiva kriyata iti | yat tūdakavyāpilavaṇavad ātmavyāpyadharmāpanayanam aśakyam iti, tad vastrarāgasyeva kṣārādibhir apanayanam ity aviruddham | yas tv abhibhava uktaḥ, asāv api “sarvaṃ pāpmānaṃ tarati” ity anenaiva śrutivacanena nirastaḥ | yady abhibhavamātraṃ kriyate tadā taratīti vācoyuktyanupapattiḥ | yat tu karmāṅgāni prāyaścittānīti, tan na sarvatra, yatra hi nimittaṃ vihitākaraṇaṃ pratiṣiddhasevanaṃ vā tatra taddoṣanirghātāya, yathā “tarati brahmahatyāṃ yo ‘śvamedhena yajate” [GDh 19.10] iti | yatra tad ubhayaṃ nāsti tatra nimitte karmāṅgam iti grāhyam |
nanu ca yady ayam arthaḥ āgamena sādhyate tadā ekam evodāharaṇaṃ yuktam, udāharaṇabahutve prayojanaṃ vaktavyam iti |
ucyate viṣayabhedapradarśanārtho ‘nekodāharaṇopanyāsaḥ | punaḥstomodāharaṇena tāvad etat pradarśayati apratigrāhyapratigrahadoṣo naśyatīti, “apratigrāhyāt tu bahu pratigṛhya punaḥstomena yajeta” iti śruteḥ | apratigrāhyapratigrahavacanam abhakṣyabhakṣaṇāvadyavadanayor api pradarśanā-rtham | “abhakṣyabhakṣaṇam avadyavadanaṃ punaḥstomena tarati” iti śrutyantaradarśanāt || 19.7 ||
kuryāt prāyaścittam ity aparaṃ darśanam | nāsmāt param astīty aparasiddhāntaḥ || 19.6 ||
tataś ca tair yadā sarvakarmānadhikṛtā bhavanti tadā |
tatra pramāṇatvena śrutivākyāny udāharati |
punaḥstomeneṣṭvā punaḥ savanam āyantīti vijñāyate || 19.8 || — || 19.7 ||
savanaśabdena karmocyate | punas tadāpattiḥ punar api śrautasmārtakarmayogyo bhavatīty arthaḥ | āyantīti bahuvacanam abhakṣyabhakṣaṇāvadyavadanapradarśa-nārtham | vijñāyata iti śrutisaṃsūcanārtham || 19.8 ||
“apratigrāhyād brahma pratigṛhya punaḥstomena yajeta” iti śrūyate | “abhakṣyabhakṣaṇam avadyavadanaṃ punaḥstomena tarata” iti ca | asatpratigrahādidoṣadūṣitāḥ punaḥstoma-nāmnaikāheneṣṭvā punaḥ savanam āyānti | savana-śabdena karmocyate | punar api śrautāni smārtāni ca karmāṇy āyānty āpnuvanti | tadyogyā bhavanti || 19.7 ||
vrātyastomaiś ceṣṭvā || 19.9 || — || 19.8 ||
cakāraḥ punaḥ savanam āyantīty asyānukarṣaṇārthaḥ | vrātyastomair iti bahuvacanaṃ catvāro vrātyastomāḥ śrutiprasiddhā iti | vrātyā nāma atītopanayanakālāḥ, taiḥ yakṣyamāṇatvād vrātyastomāḥ | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ:
"patitasāvitrīka uddālakavrataṃ caret | dvau māsau yāvakena vartayet māsaṃ payasārdhmāsam āmikṣa-yāṣṭarātraṃ ghṛtena ṣaḍrātram acācitaṃ haviṣyaṃ bhuñjīta trirātram abbhakṣaṇam ahorātram upavaset | aśvamedhāvabhṛtaṃ vā gacchet | vrātyastomena vā yajeta" | iti | [VaDh 11.76–79] | vrātyagrahaṇam apy upapātakoplakṣaṇam, “apāṅktyānāṃ prāg durvālād gohantṛbrahmojjhatanmantrakṛdavakīrṇipatita-sāvitrīkeṣūpapātakam” [GDh 21.11] iti vrātyasyopapātakamadhye pāṭhāt, “vrātyastomenopa-pātakaṃ vinaśyati” iti smṛtyantaradarśanāt | evaṃ cānenopapātakadoṣā vinaśyantīti pradarśitam || 19.9 ||
punaḥ savanam āyāntīty anuṣaṅgaḥ | vrātyā yathākālam anupanītāḥ | teṣāṃ kartavyāḥ prāyaścittayāgā vrātyastomāḥ | bahuvacananirdeśād bahavas te pratyetavyāḥ || 19.8 ||
tarati sarvaṃ pāpmānaṃ tarati brahmahatyāṃ yo ‘śvamedhena
yajata iti ca || 19.10 || — || 19.9 ||
sarvaṃ pāpmānam ity anena vihitākaraṇe pratiṣiddhasevane ca yat pāpaṃ tasya vināśaṃ darśayati | brahmahatyām api tarati mahāpātakasyāpi brahmahatyāyāḥ sarvādharmagarīyastvajñāpanārthaṃ pṛthag abhidhānam, anyathā pratiṣiddhasevanena gṛhyamāṇatvāt na vaktavyam | tathā ca vyāsaḥ |
brahmahatyāśvamedhābhyāṃ na paraṃ puṇyapāpayoḥ || iti || 19.10 ||
iti ceti vakṣyamāṇam apekṣyate | vijñāyata ity anuṣaṅgaḥ [should this go under 19.10?]
|| 19.9 ||
agniṣṭutābhiśasyamānaṃ yājayed iti ca || 19.11 || — || 19.10 ||
agniṣṭududāharaṇena dehāntarādharmanimittadoṣa-kṣayaṃ darśayati, abhiśaṃsanaṃ hi na vinā puruṣāpacāreṇa bhavatīti | cakāraḥ vijñāyata ity asyānukarṣaṇārthaḥ | itikaraṇaḥ pṛthak pṛthak śrutyantarasūcanārtham | evaṃ hi sarvatra viṣaye ‘pi śrutidarśanāt prāyaścittaṃ kartavyam eva |
nanu ca vacanagamye ‘rthe ko ‘yaṃ vicāraḥ karaṇākaraṇayoḥ prāyaścittasya – sa ca nopapadyate | yadi kriyate “upanayanaṃ brāhmaṇasya” ity ataḥ prabhrti kartavyam iti |
ucyate – mahāpātakānām abhyāse samuccaye ca prāyaścittābhāvajñāpanartho ‘yaṃ vicāraḥ | mahāpātakānām abhyāse samuccaye ca prāyaścittaṃ na kartavyam, “na hi karma kṣīyate” iti śrutidarśanāt, pṛthak pṛthak kartavyam iti | evaṃ cānyeṣām upapātakādīnām abhyāse samuccaye ca prāyaścittaṃ bhavati |
eke bruvate – prāyaścittastutyartho ‘yaṃ vicāra iti |
apare bruvate – akāmakṛta eva prāyaścittaṃ na kāmakrta iti |
tad ayuktam – “antāvasāyinīgamane kṛcchrābdo ‘matyā dvādaśarātraḥ” [GDh 23.33], “jñānapūrvaṃ cet trirātram” [GDh 20.9], “doṣavati ca karmaṇy abhisaṃdhipūrve” [GDh 25.10] ityādibhir virodhaprasaṅgāt |
anye bruvate – kāmakṛtasya gurutvakhyāpanārtho ‘yaṃ vicāra iti |
tad ayuktam, “atha khalv ayaṃ puruṣaḥ” [GDh 19.2] iti atraiva jñāpitatvāt || 19.11 ||
agniṣṭun nāmaikāhas tenābhiśasyamānaṃ yājayet | atra punaḥstomādīnāṃ doṣanirghātārthatayā śrutatvād upabhogeneva prāyaścittenāpi pāpakaṃ karma kṣīyate | śaṅkhavacanaṃ cākṛtaprāyaścittaviṣayaṃ puṇyaviṣayaṃ ca | atha kasmād vacanagamye ‘rthe vicāraḥ kriyate | kuryān na kuryād iti | na hy upanayanādāv evaṃ vicāraḥ kṛta iti ucyate | prāyaścittastutyartho ‘yaṃ vicāraḥ || 19.10 ||
idānīṃ yeṣv āhatya na prāyaścittaṃ vihitaṃ teṣu prāyaścittāny upadiśati |
tasya niṣkrayaṇāni japas tapo homa upavāso dānam || 19.12 || — || 19.11 ||
tasya yāpyakarmaṇaḥ niṣkrayaṇāni saṃśodhanāni, yathaiva loke kāṃsyādīnāṃ bhasmādīni saṃśodhanāni evam ete adharmāṇām iti | tasyety asati saṃdehaḥ syāt kiṃ punaḥstomādīny uta japādīnīti | japo vakṣyamāṇasyopaniṣadāder abhyāsaḥ prakāśopāṃśumānasalakṣaṇaḥ | yathāha manuḥ |
vidhiyajñāj japayajño viśiṣṭo daśabhir guṇaiḥ |
upāṃśuḥ syāc chataguṇaḥ sahasro mānasaḥ smṛtaḥ || iti | [MDh 2.85] | tapo vakṣyamāṇaṃ brahmacaryādi | homa ājyāder dravyasya vyāhṛtyādimantrapūrvo ‘gnau prekṣepaḥ | upavāsaḥ indriyāṇāṃ sarveṣāṃ saṃyamaḥ | tathā ca paurāṇikāḥ |
vyāvṛttiś caiva doṣebhyaḥ sahavāso guṇair iha |
upavāsaḥ sa vijñeyo na śarīrasya śoṣaṇam || iti |
yat tu tapomadhye anāśakaṃ paṭhanti tad atyanta-bhaktaparityāgaḥ kleṣakaraiḥ sahopadeśāt anāśaka ity uktatvāc ca | dānaṃ hiraṇyāder dravyasya pātre pratipādanam | visamāsa ekaikasya niṣkrayaṇatva-jñāpanārthaḥ || 19.12 ||
tasya yāpyasya karmaṇo japādīni pañca niṣkrayaṇāni śodhanāni || 19.11 ||
tatra tāvaj japasvarūpam āha |
tatra japa ity uktaṃ japān āha |
upaniṣado vedāntāḥ sarvacchandaḥsu saṃhitā madhūny aghamarṣaṇam atharavaśiro rudrāḥ puruṣasūktaṃ rājanarauhiṇe sāmanī bṛhadrathantare puruṣagatir mahānāmnyo mahāvairājaṃ mahādivākīrtyaṃ jyeṣṭhasāmnām anyatamad bahiṣpavamānaṃ kūśmāṇḍāni pāvamānyaḥ sāvitrī ceti pāvanāni || 19.13 || — || 19.12 ||
upaniṣado rahasyabrāhmaṇāni | vedāntā āraṇyakāni | sarvacchandaḥsu saṃhitā, na padāni kramo vā | sarvacchandaḥsv iti viśeṣaṇaṃ na svaśākhāyām eveti jñāpanārtham | madhūni “madhu vātā ṛtāyate” [RV 1.90.6] ityādīni madhuśabdasaṃyutāni | aghamarṣaṇaṃ “ṛtaṃ ca satyaṃ ca” ityādi | atharvaśiro ‘tharvavede prasiddham | rudrāḥ “namas te rudra manyave” [VS 16.1] ityādirudraśabdayuktāḥ | puruṣasūktam “sahasraśīrṣāḥ puruṣaḥ” [RV 10.90] ityādi | rājarauhiṇe “indraṃ naro nemadhitā havante” [RV 7.27.1] ity asyām ṛci gīte sāmanī | yajuṣor apy evaṃnāmatvāt sāmagrahaṇam | bṛhat “tvām iddhi havāmahe” [RV 6.46.1] ity asyām ṛci gītam | rathantaram “abhi tvā śūra nonumaḥ” [RV 7.32.22] ity asyām ṛci gītam | puruṣagatiḥ “aham asmi prathamajā ṛtasya” [RV 7.27.1] ity asyām ṛci gīyete | “tvām iddhi havāmahe” [RV 6.46.1] ity ābhyāṃ bṛhat | “abhi tvā śūra nonumaḥ” [RV 7.32.22] ity atra rathaṃtaram | “aham asmi prathamajā ṛtasya” [TaitBr 2.8.8.1] ity asyāṃ gītam | mahānāmnyaḥ “vidā maghavan” [AitĀ 4.15] ity asyāṃ sāma | mahāvairājam “pibā somam” [RV 6.17.1] ity asyām ṛci gītam | mahādivākīrtyam “vibhrāḍ bṛhat pibat” [RV 10.170.1] ity asyāṃ sāma | jyeṣṭhasāmnām anyatamat “śaṃ no devīḥ” [RV10.9.4] “citraṃ devānām” [RV 1.115.1] ity anayoḥ tṛceṣv ekaṃ sāma | bahiṣpavamānam “upāsmai gāyatā naraḥ” [RV 9.11.1] ityādyāḥ | kūśmāṇḍāni “yad devā devahelanam” [VS 20.14] ityādīni yajūṃṣi | pāvamānyaḥ pavamānaliṅgāni “svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayā” [RV 9.1.1] ityādīni | sāvitrī prasiddhā | caśabdo ‘nuktopasaṃgrahārthaḥ, tataś ca sāmādīni smṛtyantaroktāni draṣṭavyāni | itiśabdaḥ prakāravācī evaṃprakārā eveti | tathā ca smṛtyantarapaṭhitānāṃ śivasaṃkalpādīnām eva grahaṇaṃ draṣṭavyam, na cāpaṭhitānāṃ śrīsūktādīnām | visamāsaḥ ekaikasyaiva pāvanārthaḥ |
nanu ca japasya niṣkrayatvenoktatvāt tatsādhanatvāc caiṣāṃ pāvanatvaṃ siddham evety anarthakaṃ pāvanagrahaṇam |
ucyate – nimittād anyatrāpy eṣāṃ pavitratvajñāpanārhaṃ pāvanagrahaṇam iti || 19.13 ||
upaniṣado rahasyabrāhmaṇāny ādhyātmikāni | tadvyatiriktā āraṇyakabhāgāḥ vedāntāḥ | sarvachandaḥsu sarveṣu pravacaneṣu saṃhitā saṃhitāpāṭho na padakramādipāṭhaḥ | madhūni madhuśabdayuktāni yajūṃṣi “brahma metu mām” [TaitĀ10.48.1] ityādīni | aghamarṣaṇam “ṛtaṃ ca satyaṃ ca” iti sūktaṃ ṣaḍṛcam aghamarṣaṇena ṛṣiṇā dṛṣṭam | atharvaśiro ‘tharvavede prasiddham, “devā ha vai svargaṃ lokam agaman” ityādi | rudrāḥ “namas te rudra manyave” [VS 16.1] ityādyā anuvākā ekādaśa | ekaśataṃ yajuḥśākhās tāsu sarvāsu paṭhyante | puruṣasūkam “sahasraśīrṣā” [RV 10.90] ityādi | rājatarauhiṇe sāmanī “indraṃ naro nemadhitā havante” [RV 7.27.1] ity asyām ṛci gīyete | “tvām iddhi havāmahe” [RV 6.46.1] ity ābhyāṃ bṛhat | “abhi tvā śūra nonumaḥ” [RV 7.32.22] ity atra rathaṃtaram | “aham asmi prathamajā ṛtasya” [TaitBr 2.8.8.1] ity asyāṃ puruṣagatiḥ | mahānāmnyaḥ “vidā maghavan” [AitĀ 4.15] ityādyā ṛcaḥ | mahāvairājam “pibā somam” [RV 6.17.1] ity asvyāṃ gītaṃ sāma | mahādivākīrtyam “vibhrāḍ bṛhat pibatu” [RV 10.170.1] ity asyām | jyeṣṭhasāmāni talavakāriṇām “ud u tyam” [RV 1.50.1] “citram” [RV 1.115.1] ity etayor gītāni | chandogās tv āhuḥ: “mūrdhānaṃ divaḥ” [RV 6.7.1] ity asyāṃ gītāni trīṇi sāmāny ājyadohādīni, brāhmaṇe tathā śrutatvāt | bahiṣpavamānam “upāsmai gāyatā naraḥ” [RV 9.11.1] ity etāsu gītam | kūṣmāṇḍāni taittirīyake svādhyāyabrāhmaṇe “yad devā devaheḍanam” [VS 20.14], “yad adīvyann ṛṇām” [TaitĀ2.4.1], “āyuṣ ṭe viśvato ‘dadhat” [TaitS1.3.14.4] iti trayo ‘nuvākāḥ | yajuṣṭvābhiprāyo napuṃsakanirdeśaḥ | tatraivācchidrākhye praśne “yad devā devahelanam” [VS 20.14] ity anuvāke yā ṛcas tāḥ kūṣmāṇḍyaḥ | pavamānaḥ somo devatā yāsāṃ tāḥ pāvamānyaḥ “svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayā” [RV 9.1.1] ityādyā ā maṇḍalasamāpteḥ | “tat savitur vareṇyam” ity eṣā sāvitrī prasiddhā | na yā kācana savitṛdevatyā | itiśabdaḥ prakāravacanaḥ | evaṃprakānāṇy anyāny api pāvamānānīti | tatra manuḥ |
kautsaṃ japtvāpa ity etad vāsiṣṭhaṃ ca tṛcaṃ prati |
māhitraṃ śuddhaliṅgaṃ ca surāpo ‘pi viśudhyati ||
sakṛj japtvāsyavāmīyaṃ śivasaṃkalpam eva ca |
suvarṇam apahṛtyāpi kṣaṇād bhavati nirmalaḥ ||
haviṣyāntīyam abhyasya na tamaṃha itīti ca |
japtvā tu pauruṣaṃ sūktaṃ mucyate gurutalpagaḥ ||
somāraudraṃ tu bahvenā māsam abhyasya śudhyati || [MDh 11.249–250, 254] ityādi | prāyaścittaprakaraṇe punaḥ pāvamānānītivacanāt prāyaścittavyatirekeṇāpy ṛddhikāmasyāhar ahar etāni japyāni || 19.12 ||
idānīm japādipravṛttasyāhāraniyamam āha |
jape pravṛttasyāhāraniyamam āha |
payovratatā śākabhakṣatā phalabhakṣatā prasṛtayāvako hiraṇyaprāśanaṃ
ghṛtaprāśanaṃ somapānam iti medhyāni || 19.14 || — || 19.13 ||
payovratatā kṣīrāhāratā | vratagrahaṇād upavāsanyāyo draṣṭavyaḥ | śākaṃ prasiddham | phalaṃ āmraphalādi | prasṛtayāvakaḥ, prakaraṇāt prasṛtayāvakāśanam iti vaktavye evam abhidhānaṃ smṛtyantaroktavidhyupa-saṃgrahaṇārtham | tathāha uśanā: “snātaḥ śucir bhūtvoditeṣu nakṣatreṣu tāmrabhājane prasṛtayāvakaṃ śrapayet yathā yavāgūr bhavati | tasmin śrapaṇakāle rakṣāṃ kuryāt ‘namo rudrāya bhūtānāṃ pataye parvatānāṃ pataye tvam imāṃ rakṣasva’ ity anena mantreaṇa | tataḥ praṇavenāvaropya ‘devasya tvā savituḥ’ ityādinotpūya sāvitryābhidhārya tato ‘bhimantrayet:
yavo ‘si dhānyarājo ‘si vāruṇo madhusaṃyutaḥ |
nirṇodaḥ sarvapāpānāṃ pavitram ṛṣibhiḥ smṛtaḥ ||
vācā kṛtaṃ karmakṛtaṃ manasā durvicintitam |
alakṣmī kālakaṇṭhī ca sarvaṃ punīta me yavāḥ ||
mahāpātakasaṃyuktaṃ dāruṇaṃ rājakilbiṣam |
strībālavṛttadharmaṃ ca sarvaṃ punīta me yavāḥ ||
suvarṇastainyam avratyam ayājyasya ca yājanam |
brāhmaṇānāṃ parīvādaṃ sarvaṃ punīta me yavāḥ ||
śvasūkarāvadhūtaṃ ca kākādyucchiṣṭam eva ca |
mātāpitror aśuśrūṣāṃ sarvaṃ punīta me yavāḥ ||
gaṇānnaṃ gaṇikānnaṃ ca śūdrānnaṃ śavasūtake |
corasyānnaṃ tathābhakṣyaṃ sarvaṃ punīta me yavāḥ ||
ity etaiḥ ṣaḍbhiḥ, tato ‘brahmā devānām’ iti prāśya, ‘prāṇāya tvā’ ityādibhis tato yathākāmaṃ sarvaṃ prāśnīyāt ṣaḍrātraṃ tato niyamātikramajāt pratiṣiddhasevanajāt abhakṣyabhakṣaṇajād upapātakajāc ca sarvasmāt pāpāt pramucyate | saptarātraṃ pītvā bhrūṇahatyāṃ gurutalpasuvarṇastainyasurāpānādi punāti | ekādaśarātraṃ pītvā pūrvakṛtāni pāpāni nudati | ekaviṃśatirātraṃ pītvā gaṇān paśyati gaṇādhipatiṃ paśyati vidyāḥ paśyati vidyādhipatiṃ paśyati | evam ahar ahar anatyāhāro yavāgūṃ prāśnīyāt” iti |
hiraṇyādīni prasiddhāni | itikaraṇena prakāravācinānyeṣāṃ pañcagavyādīnām api prāśanaṃ draṣṭavyam | punaḥ punaḥ kriyāpadābhyāsaḥ ekaikasya medhyatvajñāpanārthaḥ || 19.14 ||
payovratatā kṣīrāhāratā | vratagrahaṇād upavāsanyāyena | śākaṃ vāstukādi | phalaṃ kadalyādeḥ | prasṛtayāvakaḥ prasṛtaparimitair yavaiḥ pakva odanaḥ | tatra auśanaso viśeṣaḥ: “snātaḥ śucir bhūtvoditeṣu nakṣatreṣu tāmrabhājane prasṛtayāvakaṃ śrapayed yathā yavāgūr bhavati | tasya śrapaṇakāle rakṣāṃ kuryāt | namo rudrāya bhūtādhipataye parvatānāṃ pataye tvam imam rakṣasva’ iti | śṛte ‘varopya ‘devasya tvā savitur’ ityādinotpūya tato ‘bhimantrayet |
yavo ‘si dhānyarājo ‘si vāruṇo madhusaṃyutaḥ |
nirṇodaḥ sarvapāpānāṃ pavitram ṛṣibhiḥ smṛtam ||
vācā kṛtaṃ karmakṛtaṃ manasā durvicintanam |
alakṣmīṃ kālakaṇṭhīṃ ca sarvaṃ punata me yavāḥ ||
mahāpātakasaṃyuktaṃ dāruṇaṃ rājakilbiṣam |
bālavṛttam adharmaṃ ca sarvaṃ punata me yavāḥ ||
suvarṇastainyam avratyam ayājyasya ca yājanam |
brāhmaṇānāṃ parīvādaṃ sarvaṃ punata me yavāḥ ||
śvasūkarāvabhūtaṃ ca kākādyucchiṣṭam eva ca |
mātāpitror aśuśrūṣāṃ sarvaṃ punata me yavāḥ ||
gaṇānnaṃ gaṇikānnaṃ ca śūdrānnaṃ śrāddhasūtakam |
corasyānnaṃ tathābhakṣyaṃ sarvaṃ punata me yavāḥ ||
ity etaiḥ ṣaḍbhiḥ |
tato ‘brahmā devānām’ iti prāśya tataḥ prāṇāya svāhetyādibhir yathoktaṃ sarvaṃ prāśnīyāt ṣaḍrātram | tato niyamātikramajāt pratiṣiddhasevanajād abhyakṣyabhakṣaṇajāc ca sarvasmāt pāpāt pramucyate | saptarātraṃ pītvā bhrūṇahatyāṃ gurutalpaṃ suvarṇastainyaṃ surāpānaṃ ca punāti | ekādaśarātraṃ pītvā sarvakṛtapāpaṃ nudati | ekaviṃsatirātraṃ pītvā gaṇān paśyati gaṇādhipatiṃ paśyati vidyāṃ paśyati vidyādhipatiṃ paśyati | evam ahar ahar ananyāhāro yavāgūṃ prāśnīyāt” iti |
sarpirādau hiraṇyaṃ vighṛṣya prāśanaṃ hiraṇyaprāśanam | ghṛtaprāśanaṃ prasiddham | somapānaṃ kratāv uktam | bahir apy anye | itikaranād yac cānyad evam uktaṃ pañcagavyaśaṅkhapuṣpādi tasya prāśanaṃ medhyaṃ vijñeyam || 19.13 ||
idānīṃ japādīnāṃ sthānam āha |
atha japādīnāṃ sthānam āha |
sarve śiloccayāḥ sarvāḥ sravantyaḥ puṇyā hradās tīrthāni ṛṣinivāsagoṣṭhapariṣkandā iti deśāḥ || 19.15 || — || 19.14 ||

sarve śiloccayāḥ parvatāḥ, upariṣṭāt puṇyā ity upādānād apuṇyānām api grahaṇārthaṃ sarvagrahaṇam | puṇyāḥ kailāsādayaḥ, apuṇyāḥ avivakṣitāḥ | puṇyā nadya gaṅgādayaḥ, apuṇyāḥ kṣudranadyaḥ | puṇyā hradāḥ aśoṣyāḥ śrīpuṣkarādayaḥ | tīrthāni kanyātīrthā-dīni | ṛṣinivāsāḥ vasiṣṭhādyāśramāḥ | goṣṭhaṃ prasiddham | pariṣkandaḥ devālayaḥ,

etāny evānādeśe vikalpena kriyeran, enaḥsu guruṣu gurūṇi laghuṣu laghūni || 19.19 ||
etāny evānādeśe vikalpena kriyeran || 19.18 ||
etāny eva japādīni | evety avadhāraṇārthaḥ | yadi hi “etāny anādeśe” ity etāvad ucyeta tato yatra svaśabdena prāyaścittāni nopadiṣṭāni tatraiva japādīnāṃ kalpanā syāt | evakārād upadiṣṭe ‘py abhyāsāt pratipūraṇa-siddhiḥ | anādeśo yatrānyat prāyaścittaṃ nopadiṣṭam ity arthaḥ | anādeśa ity asati upadiṣṭa eva taiḥ saha bhavatīti | vikalpena vividhābhiḥ kalpanābhir ity arthaḥ | kvacij japa eva kvacit tapa evetyādi kriyeran | etasminn asati yathopadiṣṭānāṃ japādīnāṃ dvisamuccyatrisamuccayādikalpanaṃ na syāt | sati tu tasmin kvacij japaḥ kvacit tapaś ca dānaṃ cetyādikalpanāsiddhiḥ | kva punar eṣāṃ kalpanety ata āha: enasi pāpe guruṇi gurūṇi mahānti prāyaścittāni laguni alpe laghūni alpāni prāyaścittāni | katham adharmasya gauravalāghavaṃ vijñāyata iti ced, ucyate – abhisaṃdhyanabhisaṃdhyabhyāsādibhiḥ | yathāha āpastambaḥ: “yaḥ pramatto hanti prāptadoṣaphalam, saha saṃkalpena bhūyaḥ, evam anyeṣv api doṣavatsu karmasu, tathā puṇyaphaleṣu, yathā karmābhyāsaḥ” [ĀpDh 1.29.2–5, 18] iti | yatra tu pratiṣedha eva kevalaḥ, tatrāpi rājadaṇḍāpekṣayā, smṛtyantare vā nindārthavādaphalavipākakathanena pīḍādyatiśayāpe-kṣayā vā gurutvādi veditavyam iti || 19.19 ||
etāny eva japādīni niṣkrayaṇāny anādeśe yatrāhatya prāyaścittam anirdiṣṭaṃ tatra viṣaye vikalpena kartavyāni | evakāraḥ paunarvacanikaḥ | tad yathā – devadatto grāmaṃ gacchatu sa evāraṇyam iti | kiṃ siddhaṃ bhavati | yeṣu niyate(mitte)ṣv āhatya prāyaścittam uktaṃ teṣv apy abhyāsānubandhādau pratipūraṇāpekṣāyāṃ japādīnām anupraveśaḥ siddho bhavati || 19.18 ||
kiṃ tulyavad vikalpo nety āha |
enaḥsu guruṣu gurūṇi laghuṣu laghūni
|| 19.19 ||
abhisaṃdhikṛtam eno guru tadviparītaṃ laghu | evam abhyāsānubandhādāv api draṣṭavyam | yathāha āpastambaḥ: “yaḥ pramatto hanti prāptaṃ doṣaphalaṃ saha saṃkalpena bhūya evam anyeṣv api doṣavatsu karmasu tathā puṇyaphaleṣu yathā karmābhyāse” [ĀpDh 1.29.2–5 iti || 19.19 ||
kṛcchātikṛcchrau cāndrāyaṇam iti sarvaprāyaścittaṃ sarvaprāyaścittam || 19.20 ||
kṛcchrādisvarūpaṃ vakṣyati | sarvaprāyaścittaṃ na kevalam anādeśe ‘pīty arthaḥ | sāmarthyādyapekṣayā guruṇi laghuni vā prakāśe rahasye ceti | cāndrāyaṇasya pṛthagabhidhānaṃ kṛcchrātikṛcchryoḥ sthāne cāndrāyaṇasyaiva kevalasya kalpanārtham | evaṃ ca tasyāpi sthāne tayor api kalpanāsiddhiḥ | vyastānām api kalpanārtham itikaraṇam | tataś ca kṛcchrātikṛcchrau saṃbhūya sarvaprāyaścittam, kevalaṃ cāndrāyaṇam api sarvaprāyaścittam iti | ekavacanaṃ samastānām api kalpanārtham | kecid itikaraṇaṃ prakārasyāpy upasaṃgrahārtham iti vyācakṣate | yathāha manuḥ |
yatātmano ‘pramattasya dvādaśāham abhojanam |
parāko nāma kṛcchro ‘yaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanaḥ || iti | [MDh 11.215] | prājāpatyacāndrāyaṇayoḥ sthāne parāko draṣṭavyaḥ | kutaḥ? tulyadaṇḍadarśanāt | yathāha lokākṣiḥ |
trayastriṃśat paṇānāṃ tu prājāpatyasya dāpayet |
śataṃ sapādaṃ dāpyaṃ syāt tathā sāntapanasya tu ||
atikṛcchrasya tu tathā saptaṣaṣṭiś catuḥśatam |
kṛcchrātikṛcchrayoś caiva paṇapañcaśataṃ tathā ||
dāpayec ca parākasya paṇapañcaśataṃ tathā |
trayastriṃśādhikaṃ daṇḍam ity āhur brahmavādinaḥ || iti | prāyaścittaṃ niṣkrayaṇam ity arthaḥ | evaṃ ca sati sarvaniṣkrayaṇam iti vaktavyam ity cet, atrocyate – yan niṣkrayaṇārthaṃ kriyate tasya prāyaścittam iti saṃjñā bhavatīti jñāpanārtham | anvarthasaṃjñārthaṃ gurusaṃjñākaraṇam | tathāha vyāghraḥ |
prāyo nāma tapaḥ proktaṃ cittaṃ saṃyama ucyate |
tapaḥ saṃyamasaṃyuktaṃ prāyaścittaṃ vidur budhāḥ || iti || 10.20 ||
kṛcchrātikṛcchrau cāndrāyaṇaṃ copariṣṭād vakṣyante | sarvagrahaṇān na kevalam anādeśe | etāni ca gurūny enāṃsi samastāni samuditāni prāyaścittaṃ laghūny ekam ekaṃ laghutare ‘tikṛcchro laghutame kṛcchraḥ | manur apy āha |
