|| 19.20 ||
atha viṃśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
yāpyakarmasaṃbandhe prāyaścittavidhir uktaḥ | tatra yadi pitā yāpyakarmasaṃbandhī bhavati tadā putraḥ kiṃ kuryād ity ata āha |
atha yo yāpyaṃ kṛtvāpi prāyaścittaṃ na pratipadyate sa kiṃ kartavyaḥ | tyājya ity āha |
tyajed pitaraṃ rājaghātakaṃ śūdrayājakaṃ śūdrārthayājakaṃ vedaviplāvakaṃ bhrūṇahanaṃ yaś cāntāvasāyibhiḥ saha saṃvased antāvasāyinyām || 20.1 ||
H reads: “antāvasāyinyāṃ ca”
tyajet vakṣyamāṇena vidhinā | pitaram iti pitṛvacanād daṇḍāpūpikayā putrādīnām arthasiddhas tyāgaḥ | rājaghātakaṃ rājānaṃ nyāyavṛttam abhicāreṇa yo hinasti sa tyaktavyaḥ | evaṃ cānyāyavṛttam abhicāreṇa pramāpayato nāsti tyāgaḥ | tathā purāṇa uktam |
cāturvidaṃ hataṃ tena yo hinasti mahīpatim |
bahiṣkāryaḥ sa sarveṇa pāpakarmā na mantrataḥ || iti |
yat tu vakṣyati “na karhicit mātāpitror avṛttiḥ” [GDh 21.15] iti, tad anyatra draṣṭavyam | śūdrayājakaṃ śūdrasya yo niṣādasthapatīṣṭiṃ kārayati, tasyeṣṭividhānāt | evaṃ tarhi bhrūṇahatyāyāṃ sarvasya codanāvad draṣṭavyam | śūdrārthayajakaṃ ya śūdrād artham adhigamya yāgaṃ karoti | tathāha manuḥ: “ye śūdrād adhigamyārtham” [MDh 11.42] iti | vedaviplāvakam anadhyāpyādhyāpakam | bhrūṇahā brahmahā, tadgrahaṇaṃ patitopalakṣaṇam | yathāha manuḥ |
patitasyodakaṃ kāryaṃ sapiṇḍair bāndhavair bahiḥ |
nindite ‘hani sāyāhne jñātyṛtviggurusaṃnidhau || iti | [MDh 11.182] | “yaś cāntāvasāyibhiḥ saha saṃvaset” | antāvasāyinaḥ cāṇḍālādayaḥ | yathāha aṅgirāḥ |
caṇḍālaḥ śvapacaḥ kṣattā sūto vaidehakas tathā |
māgadhāyogavau caiva saptaite ‘ntāvasāyinaḥ || iti |
taiḥ sahaikatrāvasthāne vasati, cakārāc cākrikādibhiś ca | yathāha kaṇvaḥ |
cākrikaḥ pulkaso jambhaḥ kharajīvī pradīpakaḥ |
ete ca śūdraprabhavāḥ aspṛśyāḥ parikīrtitāḥ || iti |
antāvasāyinyāṃ saṃvased iti saṃbandhaḥ, tayā saha maithunam ācaratīty arthaḥ | atha kim eteṣu rājaghātakatvādiṣu nimitteṣu satsu patitatvaṃ bhavati uta na bhavatīti saṃdehaḥ |
kiṃ cātaḥ? yadi tāvad bhavati tato “brahmahasurāpa” [GDh 21.1] ity atraivaiṣāṃ pāṭhaṃ kṛtvā “tyajet patitaṃ pitaram” ity etāvad eva vaktavyam, evaṃ ceha bhrūṇahanam iti na vaktavyaṃ bhaviṣyati | atha na bhavati, tatas tyāgo na yujyate | smṛtyantare ‘pi patitatvasaṃyoga eva tyāgavidhir uktaḥ | tathā ca darśito mānavaślokaḥ: “patitasyodakaṃ kāryam” [MDh 11.182] ityādi |
atrocyate – patitatvaṃ tāvat bhavaty eṣu nimitteṣu | tathāpi pātakimadhye na paṭhitaṃ prāyaścittalāghavārtham | bhrūṇahagrahaṇaṃ tatsāmyāpādanena tatsamaprāyaścittajñāpanārtham | yatra samīkriyate tatra hīnatāpi gamyate yathā rājasamo mantrīti | eṣāṃ madhye keṣāṃcit prāyaścittaṃ śrūyate “rājanyavadhe ṣaḍvārṣikam” [GDh 22.13] ityādi | tathā “antāvasāyinīgamane kṛcchrābdaḥ” [GDh 23.33] ityādi | yeṣāṃ nopalabyate teṣāṃ sarvaprāyaścittaṃ kalpyam | evaṃ sarvatra samīkaraṇaviṣaye sarvaprāyaścittam eva draṣṭavyam | evaṃ tarhi tulyaprāyaścittatvāt kauṭasākṣyādīnām iha pāṭhaḥ prasajyeteti cet, ucyate – eteṣv eva patiteṣu pitā tyaktavyo na tv anyatreti bhedārambhaḥ | atra rājaghātādiṣv abuddhipūrveṣv api patitatvaṃ draṣṭavyam | kutaḥ? sahopadiṣṭānāṃ madhye ekasyābuddhipūrvaviṣaye ‘pi patitatvadarśanāt | yathāha manuḥ |
caṇḍālāntyastriyo gatvā bhuktvā ca pratigṛhya ca |
pataty ajñānato vipro jñānāt sāmyaṃ tu gacchati || [MDh 11.175] iti || 20.1 ||
rājātrābhiṣikto na jātimātropajīvī | taṃ hatavān rājaghātakaḥ | śūdraṃ yo yājayaty etayā niṣādasthapatiṃ yājayed ityādau viṣaye sa śūdrayājakaḥ | yaḥ śūdrād artham adhigamya yajate sa śūdrārthayājakaḥ | anadhyāyānadhyāpyādhyayanādinā yo vedaṃ viplavāyati vyākulī karoti sa vedaviplāvakaḥ | bhrūṇahā brahmahā | etan mahāpātakinām apy upalakṣaṇam |
caṇḍālaḥ śvapacaḥ kṣattā sūto vaidehikas tathā |
māgadhāyogavau caiva saptaite ‘ntyāvasāyinaḥ ||
ity aṅgirāḥ | taiḥ sahaikasmin sthāne yo vasati sa tathoktaḥ | antyāvasāyinām | yaḥ saha vasatīty apekṣate | tasyāṃ saṃvāso maithunācaraṇam | eteṣu nimitteṣu pitaram api tyajet | pitṛgrahaṇāt kim uta putrādikam iti || 20.1 ||
athedānīṃ tasya tyāgavidhir ucyate |
atha tyāgaprakāraḥ |
tasya vidyāgurūn yonisaṃbandhāṃś ca saṃnipātya sarvāṇy udakādīni
pretakarmāṇi kuryuḥ || 20.2 ||
tasya tyājyasya nātmanaḥ vidyāguravaḥ ācāryagurūpā-dhyāyāḥ bahuvacanaprayogāt trīn api | yonisaṃban-dhāḥ mātulamātāmahādayaḥ | tatrāpi bahuvacanm uktaprayojanam | cakārād ṛtvigyājyādayaś ca | saṃni-pātya ekīkṛtya ayam evaṃkarmā tyakṣyāmy aham enam iti prakāśya putrādayo jñātayo jīvata eva sarvāṇy udakadānaśrāddhādīni pretakarmāṇi kuryuḥ || 20.2 ||
tasya tyājyasya ye vidyāgurava ācāryagurūpādhyāyā yonisaṃbandhā mātulādayas tān sarvān saṃnipātyaikatra samavetān kṛtvodakādīni śrāddhāntāni sarvāṇi pretakarmāṇi kuryuḥ | ke | putrādayo jñātayaḥ, pitaram ity upakramād bahuvacananirdeśāc ca || 20.2 ||
pātraṃ cāsya viparyasyeyuḥ || 20.3 ||
asya tyājyasya pātraṃ ca viparyasyeyuḥ ta eva jñātayaḥ | viparyāso ‘nudakīkaraṇam | udakadānena samuccayārthaś cakāraḥ | śrāddhakarmānantaraṃ mā bhūd iti tataś codakadānaṃ kṛtvā pātraviparyāsaṃ ca kṛtvā śrāddhaṃ kuryād iti draṣṭavyam || 20.3 ||
asya tyājyasya pātraṃ kiṃcit kalpayitvā te eva viparyasyeyuḥ | viparyāso ‘dhomukhīkaraṇam | yathā tad anudakaṃ bhavati || 20.3 ||
kathaṃ viparyasyeyur ity ata āha |
tatra prakāraḥ |
dāsaḥ karmakaro vāvakarād amedhyapātram ānīya dāsīghaṭāt pūrayitvā dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ padā viparyasyed amum anudakaṃ karomīti nāmagrāham || 20.4 ||
dāso dhvjābhṛtādi | yathāha manuḥ |
dhvajābhṛto bhaktadāso gṛhajaḥ krītadattrimau |
paitṛko daṇḍadāsaś ca saptaite dāsayonayaḥ || iti | [MDh 8.415] | karmakaro bhṛtakaḥ | saṃbhavataś cānayor vikalpaḥ | avakarāt varcasthānāt aśuci pātraṃ kiṃcid ādāya dāsī paricāriṇī tasyā ghaṭāt pūrayitvā | anenaivānyatra dāsīghaṭasyāśucitvaṃ draṣṭavyam | dakṣiṇāmukho bhūtvā padā pādena viparyasyet apasavyam utsṛjet “amuṃ devadattam anudakaṃ karomi” iti mantram uktvā | amum ity anenaiva nāmagrahaṇasya siddhatvāt nāmagrāham iti vispaṣṭārtham, na tūpāṃśu japed iti || 20.4 ||
dāsaḥ prasiddhaḥ | karmakaro bhṛtakaḥ | tayor anyataro ‘vakarād avaskarāt | varcaske ‘vaskaraḥ | amedhyāt sathānād aśuci pātraṃ kiṃcid upādāya yena dāsy udakam āharati tasmād ghaṭād gṛhītenodakena pūrayitvā dakṣiṇāmukho bhūtvā yadāpasavyena viparyasyed apasavyam adhomukhaṃ vikṣipet | tatra mantra – amukam anudakaṃ karomīti | nāmagrāham | amukam iti sthāne tyājyasya nāma dvitīyāntaṃ gṛhītvā | nāmnyādiśigrahor iti ṇamul | grāha it pāṭhe rūpasiddhiś cintyā || 20.4 ||
taṃ sarve ‘nvālebheran prācīnāvītino muktaśikhāḥ || 20.5 ||
taṃ dāsaṃ karmakaraṃ vā, sarve sapiṇḍā na kevalaṃ putraḥ, anvālabheran saṃspṛśeyur ity arthaḥ | prācīnāvītinaḥ apasavyayajñopavītinaḥ | muktaśikhāḥ śikhāṃ vikīrya | anenaiva jñāpakenānyasminn ācamanādau śubhakarmaṇi muktaśikhasyāyogyatvaṃ draṣṭavyam || 20.5 ||
taṃ viparyasyantaṃ sarve jñātayaḥ prācīnāvītino muktaśikhāḥ santo ‘nvālabheran spṛśeyuḥ || 20.5 ||
vidyāguravo yonisaṃbandhāś ca vīkṣeran || 20.6 ||
na tu spṛśeyur ity arthaḥ | cakārād ṛtvigādayaś ca
|| 20.6 ||
na tu saṃspṛśeyuḥ || 20.6 ||
apa upaspṛśya grāmaṃ praviśanti || 20.7 ||
evam idaṃ karma kṛtvā tataḥ snātvā gṛhān gacchanti | ekasminn evāhani śrāddhādīni gamyante | grāma-praveśopadeśāc ca bahir idaṃ kartavyam iti || 20.7 ||
evam idaṃ karma kṛtvāpa upspṛśya snātvā grāmaṃ saṃviśanti praviśeyuḥ | ata eva jñāyate grāmād bahir idaṃ karmeti || 20.7 ||
ata ūrdhvaṃ tena saṃbhāṣya tiṣṭhed ekarātraṃ japan sāvitrīm ajñānapūrvam || 20.8 ||
atas tyāgād ūrdhvaṃ tena tyaktena saha saṃbhāṣya saṃbhāṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā | ajñānād idaṃ prāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam | tiṣṭhed ity ekavacanāt jñātyāder anyo ‘pi, adhikārāt jñātaya eva | ekarātram ahorātraṃ na bhuñjītety arthaḥ | sāvitrīṃ japan || 20.8 ||
atas tyāgād ūrdhvaṃ tena tyaktena saha saṃbhāṣaṇam ajñānāt kṛtvaikam ahorātraṃ tiṣṭhen na bhuñjīta na śayīta nāsīteti | ajñānapūrvam iti vacanād ekavacananirdeśāc ca jñātivyatiriktasyāpīdaṃ bhavati || 20.8 ||
jñānapūrvaṃ cet trirātram || 20.9 ||
jñānapūrvaṃ ca trirātram || 20.9 ||
yas tu buddhipūrvam anena saṃbhāṣeta sa trirātram evaṃvidhoktaṃ vidhim ātiṣṭhet | tāvac caitad evaṃ yāvad asāv akṛtaprāyaścittaḥ | kāryāntarasaṃbhāṣaṇam idaṃ na pathi praśnādau | tatra parāśaroktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
kṣute niṣṭhīvite caiva dantaspṛṣṭe tathānṛte |
patitānāṃ bhāṣaṇe ca dakṣiṇaṃ śravaṇaṃ spṛśet || [PārSm 12.19] iti || 20.9 ||
yas tu tena jñānapūrvaṃ saṃbhāṣate sa trirātram uktakrameṇa tiṣṭhet | kāryākāryanirūpaṇādāv idam | paripraśnādau tu parāśaroktam |
kṣute niṣṭhīvane caiva dantaspṛṣṭe tathaiva ca |
patitānāṃ ca saṃbhāṣe dakṣiṇaṃ śravaṇaṃ spṛśet || iti || 20.9 ||
yas tu prāyaścittena śuddhyet tasmin śuddhe śātakumbhamayaṃ pātraṃ puṇyatamād dhradāt pūrayitvā sravantībhyo vā tata enam apa upasparśayeyuḥ || 20.10 ||
tuśabdo ‘vadhāraṇārthaḥ | yas tu prāyaścittena śuddhyet na rājadaṇḍena | evaṃ ca bruvatā anyatra rājadaṇḍasyāpi śuddhikāraṇatvam uktam | tataś cānyatra rājadaṇḍitasyāpi śuddhir veditavyā | tasmin śuddhe lokasamakṣaṃ caritaprāyaścittaḥ ity arthaḥ | śātakumbhamayaṃ pātraṃ sauvarṇapātram atiśayena puṇyād dhradād aśoṣyaprāyāt pūrayitvā sravantībhyo vā sadāpravāhābhyo nadībhyaḥ | bahuvacanāt bahvībhyaḥ | saṃbhavato vikalpaḥ | tatas tasmāt pātrād enaṃ kṛtaprāyaścittaṃ snāpayeyur vakṣyamāṇair mantraiḥ | sāmarthyāt pūrvaṃ parityaktāraḥ || 20.10 ||
prāyaścitteneti vacanād rājadaṇḍena śuddhasya vakṣyamāṇasvīkaraṇavidhir na bhavati | tasya kevalaṃ paratraiva śuddhiḥ |
rājabhir dhṛtadaṇḍās tu kṛtvā pāpāni mānavāḥ |
nirmalāḥ svargam āyānti santaḥ sukṛtino yathā || iti || [MDh 8.318] | tasmiṃl lokasamakṣaṃ śuddhe śāta-kumbhamayaṃ sauvarṇaṃ pātraṃ puṇyatamād dhradān nadibhyo vāhṛtena jalena pūrayitvā tatas tasmād āvarjitā apa enaṃ caritaprāyaścittam upasparśa-yeyus tābhir adbhiḥ snāpayeyur jñātayaḥ || 20.10 ||
athāsmai tat pātraṃ dadyus tat pratigṛhya japec chāntā dyauḥ śāntā pṛthivī śāntaṃ śivam antarikṣaṃ yo rocanas tam iha gṛhṇāmīty etair yajurbhiḥ pāvamānībhis taratsamandībhiḥ
|| 20.11 ||
athāsmai tat pātraṃ dadyus tat saṃpratigṛhya japec chāntā dyauḥ śāntā pṛthivī śāntaṃ śivam antarikṣaṃ yo rocanas tam imaṃ gṛhṇāmīti
|| 20.11 ||
atha anantaram asmai snātāya tat pūrṇapātraṃ dadyuḥ | tat pratigṛhyemaṃ mantraṃ japet | kair mantriḥ? śānteti vakṣyati | etair evoktaiḥ śāntā dyaur ityādibhir yajurbhiḥ | pavamānībhiḥ “pavamānaḥ suvarjanaḥ” ity aṣṭābhiḥ taratsamandībhiś catasṛbhiḥ || 20.11 ||
atha snāpanāntantaram asmai snātāya tat sauvarṇaṃ pātraṃ dadyur jñātayaḥ | sa ca tat pātraṃ pratigṛḥya japec chāntā dyaur ityādi gṛhṇāmītyantam || 20.11 ||
etair yajurbhiḥ pāvamānībhis taratsamandībhiḥ kūṣmāṇḍaiś cājyaṃ juhuyād dhiraṇyaṃ brāhmaṇāya dadyāt || 20.12 ||
homānte dānam || 20.12 ||
kūśmāṇḍaiś cājyaṃ juhuyāt || 20.12 ||
juhuyād ity ekavacananirdeśād eka eva juhuyāt, na sarve jñātyādayaḥ | teṣāṃ madhye yogyasyedaṃ draṣṭavyam | kūśmāṇḍaiḥ “yad devā devaheḍanam” ityādibhir ājyaṃ juhuyāt | caśabdān mahāvyāhṛtibhiś ca || 20.12 ||
hiraṇyaṃ brāhmaṇāya dadyād gāṃ vācāryāya ca || 20.13 ||
homānte suvarṇaṃ brāhmaṇāya dadyād guṇātiśayayuktāya, brāhmaṇasyaiva pratigrahādhikāre brāhmaṇagrahaṇāt | kṛtaprāyaścittasyedam, na homakartuḥ | gāṃ vā ācāryāya ca | cakāro hiraṇyādhikārārthaḥ | ācāryāya homakartre gāṃ vā hiraṇyaṃ vā dadyāt | etasya dvayaṃ mukhyam, gaur hiraṇyam api draṣṭavyam, samavikalpodapeśāt || 20.13 ||
gāṃ vā || 20.13 ||
icchānto vikalpaḥ || 20.13 ||
ācāryāya ca || 20.14 ||
ya ātmana ācāryas tasmā api hiraṇyaṃ dadyād gāṃ vā || 20.14 ||
yasya tu prāṇāntikaṃ prāyaścittaṃ mṛtaḥ śudhyet || 20.14 || — || 20.15 ||
H reads “sa mṛtaḥ”
yasya tu prāṇāntikaṃ maraṇāntikaṃ prāyaścittam upadiṣṭam, yatamāno mriyeta, asau maraṇād eva śudhyati | jñāyamānam apy etad ucyate – pratyuddhāre saty api sapiṇḍatāpratiṣedhārtham | tuśabdo viśeṣārthaḥ | prāṇāntikaṃ prāyaścittam adhyavasya prāyaścittamadhye yadi mriyeta tadāpi śudhyati viśeṣataḥ kṛtaprāyaścitto mṛta iti | tathāha vyāsaḥ |
yatamānaḥ sadā dharme mriyate yadi madhyataḥ |
prāpnoty eva hi tat sarvaṃ atra me nāsti saṃśayaḥ ||
iti || 20.14 ||
uttaravivakṣayedam ucyate | prāyaścittasya śuddhyarthatvād eva siddhā śuddhiḥ || 20.15 ||
sarvāṇy eva tasminn udakādīni pretakarmāṇi kuryuḥ || 20.15 || — || 20.16 ||
sarvāṇy evodakaśrāddhādīni yāvanti pretasaṃskārakarmāṇi tāni sarvāṇi tasmin yo maraṇāntikaprāyaścittakaḥ pūto yaś ca pratyuddhṛto yaś cāntarā mriyate | arthād etadvyatiriktasya na kāryāṇi | sarvaśabdād āśaucam api sapiṇḍāḥ kuryuḥ || 20.15 ||
yady api tasya nāsmiṃl loke pratyāpattis tathāpi maraṇād eva śuddha iti sarvāṇy eva pretakarmāṇi kartavyāni | sarvagrahaṇād āśaucam api | yo ‘pi dvādaśavārṣikādau prāyaścitte pravṛtto madhye mriyate tadvaiṣaye vyāsa āha |
yajamānaḥ sadā dharmye mriyate yadi madhyataḥ |
prāpnoty eva tu tat sarvam atra me nāsti saṃśayaḥ || iti || 20.16 ||
etad eva śāntyudakaṃ sarveṣūpapātakeṣūpapātakeṣu || 20.16 || — || 20.17 ||
H reads: “sarveṣūpapātakeṣu sarveṣūpapātakeṣu”
etad eva yadantaram uktaṃ śāntyudakam iti śāntā dyaur ityādibhir abhimantritaṃ snānāya yaj jalam uktaṃ tad eva sarveṣu, sarvaśabdān mahāpātakasameṣv apy upapātakeṣu vakṣyamāṇeṣu tatprāyaścittādāv etad api kartavyam iti || 20.16 ||
etad evānantaroktaṃ śāntā dyaur ityādibhir abhiman-tritaṃ sarveṣūpapātakeṣu kartavyaṃ prāyaścittasyānte | ādāv ity anye | dviruktiḥ pūrvavat || 20.17 ||
atha ekaviṃśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
patitānāṃ tyāgavidhir uktaḥ | ke punas te ity ata āha |
patitānāṃ tyāgavidhir uktaḥ | ke punas te | tān āha |
brahmahasurāpagurutalpagamātṛpitṛyonisaṃbandhāgastenanāstika-ninditakarmābhyāsipatitātyāgyapatita-tyāginaḥ patitāḥ || 21.1 ||
brahmahā brāhmaṇavadhakṛt | surāpaḥ triprakārāyāṃ surāyāṃ yasya yā pratiṣiddhā tāṃ pītaḥ | yathāha manuḥ |
gauḍī mādhvī ca paiṣṭī ca vijñeyā trividhā surā |
yathaivaikā na pātavyā tathā sarvā dvijottamaiḥ || iti | [MDh 11.94] | gurutalpagaḥ – guror ācāryasya, talpaśabdena śayanavācinā bhāryā lakṣyate, tāṃ maithunāya yo gacchati | mātṛsaṃbandhāgaḥ mātṛpakṣe arvāk pañcamāt yā kanyā tāṃ varayitā | pitṛsaṃbandhāgaḥ pitṛpakṣe ‘pi saptamād arvāk | yonisaṃbandhāgaḥ bhaginībhartā | stenaḥ suvarṇacoraḥ | nāstikaḥ paralokādyabhāvavādī | ninditakarmābhyāsī ninditasya pratiṣiddhasya buddhipūrvaṃ sāṃtatyena sevakaḥ | karmagrahaṇāt kāyikasyaivedam, na vāṅmanasayor apacāreṇa | brahmahādīnāṃ patitānāṃ yas tyāgaṃ na karoty asau patitātyāgī | apatitānāṃ dveṣādinā yaḥ parityāgaṃ karoty asāv apatitatyāgī | ete brahmahādayaḥ patitāḥ
|| 21.1 ||
brāhmaṇasya hantā brahmahā |
gauḍī mādhvī ca paiṣṭī ca vijñeyā trividhā surā |
yathaivaikā na pātavyā tathā sarvā dvijottamaiḥ || [MDh 11.94] iti manuḥ | tatra yā yasya surā pratiṣiddhā sa tasyāḥ pātā surāpaḥ | guruḥ pitācāryaś ca | talpaśabdena bhāryā lakṣyate | gamanaṃ maithunam | mātṛsaṃbandhā mātṛpakṣe prākpañcamād bhavāḥ pitṛsaṃbandhāḥ pitṛpakṣe ‘rvāk saptamād bhavāḥ | yonisaṃbaṃdhā bhaginyādayaḥ | ya etā bhāryātvenādhigacchati sa mātṛpitṛyonisaṃbandhāgaḥ | steno brāhmaṇasuvarṇāpahārī | nāstikaḥ pretyabhāvāpavādī | ninditaṃ pratiṣiddham | tasya karmaṇo buddhipūrvaṃ sātatyenānuṣṭhātā ninditakarmābhyāsī | patitā naiva sataḥ putrādīn snehādīnā (?) yo na tyajati sa patitātyāgī | yas tv apatitān eva dveṣādinā tyajati so ‘patitatyāgī | ete brahmahadaya patitāḥ || 21.1 ||
pātakasaṃyojakāś ca || 21.2 ||
ye pātakeṣu prayojayanti | caśabdād anumantā ca
|| 21.2 ||
pātakaiḥ karmabhir ye parān saṃyojayanti tatra “pravartyanti dravyapradānādinā macchatrum itthaṃ vyāpādaya tvacchatror vyāpādane ‘yam abhyupāyaḥ” iti | tathā kenacij jighāṃsitaṃ palāyamānaṃ yo nivārayati nivāritaś ca hanyate so ‘pi prayojakaḥ | yasyānumatim antareṇārtho na nivartate [nirvartate] sa mantā | sa iha pṛthaṅ nopādīyate prayojakakoṭir evāyam iti | āpastambas tu kiyatāpy avāntarabhedena tam eva pṛthag uditavān: “prayojitā mantā kartā ceti svarganarakaphaleṣu karmasu bhāginaḥ | yo bhūya ārabhate tasmin phalaviśeṣaḥ” iti [ĀpDh 2.21.1–2] |
|| 21.2 ||
taiś cābdaṃ samācaran || 21.3 ||
taiḥ saha saṃvatsaraṃ samācaran yānāsanaśayanaiḥ | tathā ca kaṇvaḥ |
saṃvatsareṇa patati patitena samācaran |
yānāsanaśayair nityam ity āhur brahmavādinaḥ || iti |
cakārād yājanādibhiḥ sadyo ‘pi | tathā ca vṛddhayājñavalkyaḥ |
yājanaṃ yonisaṃbandhaṃ svādhyāyaṃ sahabhojanam |
kṛtvā sadyaḥ pataty eva patitena samācaran || iti |
aviśeṣāt buddhipūrve ‘buddhipūrve ca | tatra abuddhipūrve laghutaraṃ prāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “patitasaṃprayogaṃ ca brāhmeṇa vā yaunena vā yās tebhyaḥ sakāśān mātrā upalabdhās tāsāṃ parityāgas taiś ca na saṃvased udīcīṃ diśaṃ gatvānaśnan saṃhitādhyayanam adhīyānaḥ pūto bhavatīti vjñāyate” iti | [VaDh 20.45–46] | tatra brāhmeṇa vā yaunena veti dvitīyo vāśabdo yānāsanaśayanānām apy upasaṃgrahārthaḥ | adhītavedasyaiva saṃhitādhyayanasaṃbhavāt, anadhītavedasya yāvatā kālena svaśākhyāyāḥ saṃhitādhyayanaṃ samāpyate tāvantaṃ kālaṃ sāvitryā japo draṣṭavyaḥ, “saṃhitādhyayanam adhīyīta tāvantaṃ kālaṃ sāvitrīṃ japet” iti smṛtyantaradarśanāt | tathā ca taittirīyāṇāṃ svādhyāyabrāhmaṇaliṅgam – “anaśnan triḥ svādhyāyaṃ vedam adhīyīta trirātraṃ vā sāvitrīṃ gāyatrīm” ityādi | evaṃ brāhmaṇaliṅgadarśanāt vāsiṣṭhe aviśeṣite ‘pi triḥ saṃhitādhyayanaṃ draṣṭavyam | ekasyādhyayanasyaikaṃ prājāpatyaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha kaṇvaḥ |
ekam adhyayanaṃ kuryāt prājāpatyam athāpi vā |
dadyād vā daśasāhasraṃ gavāṃ muṣṭiṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ || iti | buddhipūrve tu manunoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
yo yena patitenaiṣāṃ saṃsargaṃ yāti mānavaḥ |
sa tasyaiva vrataṃ kuryāt tatsaṃsargaviśuddhaye || iti | [MDh 11.181] | saṃvatsarād arvāk vyāghroktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
patitena tu saṃsargam ajñātvā kurute yadi |
utsargaṃ tasya labdhasya kṛtvā sāntapanaṃ caret || iti | buddhipūrve tu kaṇvoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
patitena tu saṃsargas tv abhisaṃdhikṛto yadi |
kṛtvā labdhasamutsargaṃ carec cāndrāyaṇavratam || iti | sahabhojane tu lokākṣir āha |
patitaiḥ saha bhuktvānnaṃ parākena viśudhyati |
jñātvā tu tadvrataṃ kuryād iti dharmavido viduḥ ||
iti || 21.3 ||
taiḥ patitaiḥ saha yo ‘bdaṃ samācarati yānāsanaśayanādīni so ‘pi patitaḥ | atra kaṇvaḥ |
saṃvatsareṇa patati patitena samācaran |
yānāsanāśanair nityam ity āhur brahmavādinaḥ || iti |
yājanādau tu yājñavalkya āha |
yājanaṃ yonisaṃbandhaṃ svādhyāyaṃ sahabhojanam |
kṛtvā sadyaḥ pataty eva patitena samācaran || iti | [MDh | sahabhojanam ekasmin pātre |
saṃvatsareṇa patati patitena samācaran |
yājanādhyāpanād yaunān na tu yānāsanāśanāt ||
iti mānavam apy evaṃ vyākhyeyam | yānādibhiḥ saṃvatsareṇa patati na tu yājanādibhiḥ kiṃ tu taiḥ sadya eveti || 21.3 ||
kim eṣāṃ patitatvam ity āha |
kiṃ punar eṣāṃ patitatvam |
dvijātikarmabhyo hāniḥ patanam || 21.4 ||
dvijātikarmabhyaḥ śrautebhyo ‘gnihotrādibhyaḥ smārtebhyaś cāṣṭakādibhyaḥ hāniḥ tair anadhikāra ity arthaḥ | karmabhyo hānir iti vaktavye dvijātigrahaṇāc chūdravad avasthānaṃ darśayati | śūdrasyāpi ca patitatvaṃ pratilomavarṇavad avasthānam || 21.4 ||
dvijātīnāṃ yāni kāryāny [karmāṇy] adhyayanādīni śrautāni gārhyāṇi smārtāni ca tebhyo hānis teṣv anadhikāraḥ || 21.4 ||
paratra cāsiddhiḥ || 21.5 ||
H reads: “tathā paratra”
patanīyasevāyāḥ prāg api ca yad etena śreyaḥ samācaritaṃ tatphalenāsaṃbandha iti paratra cāsiddhiḥ | caśabdād iha cākīrtiḥ | tad etat pātakapadārtha-svarūpāvadhāraṇaṃ dvijātikarmabhir anadhikārārthaṃ prāyaścittānuṣṭhānaprarocanārthaṃ ca || 21.5 ||
dvijātīnāṃ yāni kāryāny [karmāṇy] adhyayanādīni śrautāni gārhyāṇi smārtāni ca tebhyo hānis teṣv anadhikāraḥ || 21.4 ||
tam eke narakam || 21.6 ||
taṃ karmabhir anadhikāraṃ paratra ca śubhasyānu-pabhogaṃ narakaṃ manyante suhṛdjñātivarjitasyātīva duḥkhotpatteḥ narako ‘pi duḥkhakāraṇatvāt | yathāha niruktabhāṣyakāraḥ: “narakaṃ nyakkaraṇaṃ nīcair gamanaṃ nāsyāsmin ramaṇaṃ vidyate” iti | gautamas tu narakabhāg iti manyate || 21.6 ||
yeyaṃ karmabhyo hānir yā ca paratrāsiddhis tām evaike narakaṃ manyante | narakasāmānādhikaraṇyāt puṃliṅgam ekavacanaṃ ca | karmabhyo hīnasya bandhubhis tyaktasya duḥkham utpadyate paratrāsiddheḥ sukhalavo na bhavaty ato naraka evāyam iti | svamataṃ tu viśiṣṭe deśe duḥkhaikatānasya vāso naraka iti || 21.6 ||
trīṇi prathamāny anirdeśyāni manuḥ || 21.7 ||
trīṇi prathamāny anirdeśyāny anu || 21.7 ||
eṣāṃ pātakānām ādyāni trīṇi brahmahatyāsurāpānagurutalpagamanāni pātakāny anirdeśyāni, anirdeśyaprāyaścittatvāt | manur āha |
kāmato brāhmaṇavadhe niṣkṛtir na vidhīyate | iti | [MDh 11.89] | tathā surāpāne ‘pi |
matipūrvam anirdeśyaṃ prāṇāntikam iti smṛtam | [MDh 11.145] ity evamādi draṣṭavyam | pratyāpattyabhāvād vā anirdeśyānīty uktam | yathāha āpastambaḥ: “nāsyāsmin loke pratyāpattir vidyate kalmaṣaṃ tu nirhanyate” [ĀpDh 1.24.25] iti | manugrahaṇaṃ smṛtyarthasya samastasya manuḥ smarteti pradarśanārtham | tad āhuḥ paurāṇikāḥ |
ṛco yajūṃṣi sāmāni mantrā ātharvaṇāś ca ye |
saptarṣibhis tu yat proktaṃ smārtaṃ tan manur abravīt || iti || 21.7 ||
anukrāntānāṃ pātakānāṃ madhye prathamāni trīṇi brahmahatyāsurāpānagurutalpagamanāny anirdeśyāny anirdeśyaprāyaścittāni | teṣāṃ prāyaścittam anirdeśyam iti | brahmavadhe manur āha |
kāmato brāhmaṇavadhe niṣkṛtir na vidhīyate || iti | [MDh 11.89]
surāpāne |
matipūrvam anirdeśyaṃ prāṇāntikam iti sthitiḥ | iti | [MDh 11.146] | gurutalpe mṛgyam || 21.7 ||
na strīṣv agurutalpaḥ patatīy eke || 21.8 ||
H edition reads “agurutalpaṃ” but commentary presupposes agurutalpaḥ
strīṣu pravartamānasya agurutalpaṃ gurudārān varjayitvā anyastrīgamanāt na patatīy eke manyante | gautamas tu sarvatra patatīty eveti | so ‘yaṃ buddhipūrvābuddhipūrvabhedena vikalpo draṣṭavyaḥ | nāgurutapa ity eva siddhe strīgrahaṇaṃ cāturvarṇyastrīṇām upasaṃgrahaṇārtham, pratilomāsu patitatvaṃ yathā syād iti | tathāha manuḥ |
caṇḍālāntyastriyo gatvā bhuktvā ca pratigṛhya ca |
pataty ajñānato vipro jñānāt sāmyaṃ tu gacchati || iti | [MDh 11.175] | etat trayaṃ kṛtvā patati na gamanamātreṇeti cet, tad ayuktam – gamanamātra eva prāyaścittopadeśāt | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “athāparaṃ bhrūṇahatyāyāṃ dvādaśarātram abbhakṣo dvādaśarātram upavased aśvamedhāvabhṛtaṃ vā gacched etenaiva caṇḍālīvyavāyo vyākhyātaḥ” [VaDh 23.38, 40–41] iti | idam abuddhipūrve sakṛdgamane draṣṭavyam | tatraivābhyāse gurutalpaprāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha manuḥ |
gurutalpavrataṃ kuryāt retaḥ siktvā svayoniṣu |
sakhyuḥ putrasya ca strīṣu kumārīṣv antyajāsu ca || iti | [MDh 11.170] | tasmād abhyāse sakṛdgamane ca patitaprāyaścittātideśāt gamanamātreṇāpi pataty eveti | strīṣv iti bahuvacanaṃ pūjārtham | strīṣu apatitastrīṣu pataty eva | yathāha kavaṣaḥ |
patitāṃ ca striyaṃ gatvā pataty ajñānato dvijaḥ |
talpavrataṃ tadabhyāse jñānatas tadvrataṃ caret || iti | atrābuddhipūrve ‘bhyāse gurutalpavratātideśāt sakṛdgamane vasiṣṭhoktaṃ draṣṭavyam: “gurvīsakhīṃ gurusakhīm apapātrāṃ patitāṃ ca gatvā kṛcchrābdapādaṃ caret” [VaDh 20.16] iti | buddhipūrve sakṛdgamane ‘pi tadvrataṃ cared iti draṣṭavyam || 21.8 ||
eke manyante strīṣu pravṛtto gurutalpa eva patito nā-nyatreti | svayaṃ tv anyatrāpi patatīti | āha manur api |
cāṇḍālāntyastriyo gatva bhuktvā ca pratigṛhya ca |
pataty ajñānato vipro jñānāt sāmyaṃ tu gacchati || [MDh 11.175] iti || 21.8 ||
atha striyāḥ patanahetum āha |
bhrūṇahani hīnavarṇasevāyāṃ ca strī patati || 21.9 ||
yac caitat brahmahatyādy upadiṣṭaṃ etat puruṣavat striyā api draṣṭavyam | yat tu tasyā adhikaṃ tad ucyate – bhrūṇahanaṃ sevamānā patatīti | bhrūṇahagrahaṇaṃ patitopalakṣaṇam | hīnavarṇaḥ śūdraḥ | kecid upa-sargaprayogāt pratilomam āhuḥ | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
catasras tu parityājyāḥ śiṣyagā gurugā ca yā |
patighnī ca viśeṣeṇa juṅgitopagatā ca yā || [VaDh 21.10] iti tyāgopadeśāt, patitātyāge patitatvaprasaṅgāt | cakāro brahmahatyādibhiḥ samuccayārthaḥ | kecid garbhapatanam āhuḥ | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
trīṇi striyāḥ pātakāni loke dharmavido viduḥ |
bhartur vadho bhrūṇahatyā svasya garbhasya pātanam || iti | [VaDh 28.7]
tatra patitapratilomayoḥ saṃsarge puruṣavat prāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam,
etam eva vidhiṃ vidyād yoṣitsu patitāsv api | [MDh 11.188] iti manuvacanāt | śūdraṃ sevamānā śūdrasya saṃsargaṃ kurvāṇā yady api patati, tathāpi ā prajotpatteḥ prāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
brāhmaṇakṣatriyaviśāṃ striyaḥ śūdreṇa saṃgatāḥ |
aprajātā viśudhyanti prāyaścittena netarāḥ || iti | [VaDh 21.12] | tathā tatraiva prāyaścittam apy uktam |
prativarṇaṃ careyus tāḥ kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇottaram | iti | [VaDh 21.13] | cāndrāyaṇottaraṃ cāndrāyaṇe uttare cāndrāyaṇāny uttarāṇīti | brāhmaṇī kṣatriyā vaiśyānukrameṇa yojyam | “śūdrasaṃgatāḥ aprajātā viśudhyanti” [VaDh 21.12] ity uktatvāt kṣatriyavaiśyasaṃgatāḥ prajātā api viśudhyantīti draṣṭavyam | tatra kṣatriyeṇa saṃgatāyāś cāndrāyaṇam uttaraṃ vaiśyena saṃgatāyāś cāndrāyaṇe uttare iti kalpyam | tathā kṣatriyāyāṃ vaiśyena saṃgatāyāṃ cāndrāyaṇam uttaram iti kalpyam | puruṣāṇāṃ tu pratilomagamane “śūdro brāhmaṇīm” [VaDh 21.1] ityādi vasiṣṭhoktaṃ draṣṭavyam | anulomagamane tu vakṣyati – “dve paradāre” [GDh 22.31] ityādi | atra patitāsv api catasra eva parityājyāḥ | kutaḥ? “catasras tu” [VaDh 21.10] iti tuśabdena niyamavācinā catasṝṇām eva parityāgasya vasiṣṭhena pratipāditatvāt | itareṣāṃ tu manūktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
etam eva vidhiṃ kuryād yoṣitsu patitāsv api |
vastrānnam āsāṃ deyaṃ ca vaseyuś ca gṛhāntike || iti | [MDh 11.188] | ayam api vakṣyati “strī cāticāriṇī” [GDh 22.37] ityādi || 21.9 ||
bhrūṇaheti bhāvaparam | bhrūṇo garbhaḥ | ātmāpekṣāyāṃ garbhahatyāyāṃ strī patati | yo hīnavarṇo brāhmaṇyāḥ kṣatriyādiḥ kṣatriyāyā vaiśyādir vaiśyāyāḥ śūdras tatsevāyāṃ ca strī patati | cakārāḍ brahmahatyādiṣu ca | apara āha – bhrūṇahanaṃ hīnavarṇaṃ ca yā sevate na tasya bhāryā bhavati sā patati | cakārād brahmahatyādiṣu ceti | bhrūṇagrahaṇaṃ patitopalakṣaṇam || 21.9 ||
kauṭasākṣyaṃ rājagāmi paiśunaṃ guror anṛtābhiśaṃsanaṃ pātakasamāni || 21.10 ||
H edition reads “mahāpātakasamāni”, but com. omits “mahā”
kauṭasākṣyaṃ sakṣye anṛtavāditvam “anyatra nānṛtavacane doṣaḥ” [GDh 13.24] ity apavādāt | rājagāmi paiśunaṃ rājakule paradoṣakathanam | rājanīti vaktavye gāmīti vacanād rājakulād anyatrāpi yat pāraṃparyeṇa rājānaṃ gacchati tad api gṛhyate | guror ācāryasya kenacid anṛtadoṣeṇābhimukhyena śaṃsanam ākhyānam | etāni pātakatulyāni mahāpātakatulyānīti draṣṭavyam,
guroś cālīkanirbandhaḥ samāni brahmahatyayā | [MDh 11.55]  iti smṛtyantaradarśanāt | buddhipūrvavyatikrama evaiṣāṃ pātakasamatvam, na pramādamohādibhir iti visamāsakaraṇam | evaṃ ca pramādādibhir na doṣaḥ || 21.10 ||
kūṭasākṣiṇo bhāvaḥ kauṭasākṣyaṃ sākṣiṇo ‘nṛtavacanam | sato ‘sato vā paradoṣasya khyāpanaṃ paiśunam | rājanīti vaktavye rājagāmīti vacanaṃ yatroktaṃ pāraṃparyeṇāpi rājānaṃ gacchati tad api varjyam ity evamartham | guroḥ pitur ācāryasya vānṛtenāsatyena doṣeṇabhiśaṃsanaṃ duṣṭatākhyāpanaṃ guror anṛtābhiśaṃsanam | etāni [mahā]pātakasamāni | sāmyātideśe prāyaścittam ardham iti smārto vyavahāraḥ || 21.10 ||
apāṅktyānāṃ prāg durvālād gohantṛ-brahmojjhatanmantrakṛdavakīrṇi-patitasāvitrīkeṣūpapātakam || 21.11 ||
apaṅktyānāṃ prāg durvālād gohantṛ-brahmaghnatanmantrakṛdavakīrṇi-patitasāvitrīkeṣūpapātakam || 21.11 ||
durvālāt prāg ya upadiṣṭā abhojyānnāḥ patitakūṭasākṣi-varjitāḥ | nāstikās tu pātakāt pṛthagbhūtā evābhojyānnānāṃ madhe paṭhitāḥ | kutaḥ? trividhā nāstikāḥ | tathāhuḥ paurāṇikāḥ|
nāstikās trividhāḥ proktā dharmajñais tattvadarśibhiḥ |
kriyāduṣṭo manoduṣṭo vāgduṣṭaś ca tathaiva ca ||
upapātakas tu vāgduṣṭo manoduṣṭas tu pātakaḥ |
abhyāsāt tu kriyāduṣṭo mahāpātaka ucyate || iti |
atra vāgduṣṭa upapātakamadhye paṭhitaḥ, manoduṣṭaḥ pātakamadhye, kriyāduṣṭo “ninditakarmābhyāsi” [GDh 21.1] ity anenaivoktam iti | atra ya upapātakamadhye paṭhitaḥ tasya vasiṣṭhoktaṃ prāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam: “nāstikaḥ kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caritvā viramen nāstikyāt” [VaDh 21.29] iti | yas tu pātakamadhye paṭhitaḥ tasya āpastamboktaṃ draṣṭavyam: “etad evānyeṣām api patanīyānām api” [ĀpDh 1,28,14] iti | yas tu mahāpātakaḥ tasyābhyāsāt prāk sarvaprāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam | abhyāse tu brahmahatyāprāyaścittaṃ vasiṣṭhoktam “athāparaṃ bhrūṇahatyāyāḥ” [cf. VaDh 23.38] ityādi draṣṭavyam | alam atiprasaṅgena, prastutaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ |
gohantā goghātakaḥ | brahmojjhaḥ yo vedam adhītya pramādād vismarati sa gṛhyate | yas tu buddhipūrvam eva parityajati nāstika eva saḥ | tasya viśiṣṭataro doṣa iti | evaṃ cātra yaḥ pramādād vismarati tasya vasiṣṭhoktaṃ draṣṭavyam: “brahmojjhaḥ kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caritvā punar upayuñjīta vedam ācāryāt” [VaDh 20.12] iti | itarasya manūktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
brahmojjhaṃ vedanindā ca kauṭasakṣyaṃ suhṛdvadhaḥ |
garhitānnādyayor jagdhiḥ surāpānasamāni ṣaṭ || iti | [MDh 11.56] | tanmantrakṛt yo gohantṛbrahmojjayoḥ yājanādi karoti | avakīrṇī viplutabrahmacaryo brahmacārī | asyāpaṅktyatvena gṛhītatvāt punarvacanaṃ yo brahmacārī prajām utpādayaty asau kṛtaprāyaścitto ‘pi yāvad asau punar apy upapātakaprāyaścittam na karoti tāvad asau upapātakitvena varjanīya iti | kecid evaṃ varṇayanti – naiṣṭhikānaiṣṭhikatvena gurulaghuprāyaścittārthaṃ punar vacanam | upakurvāṇasya laghu naiṣṭhikasya gurv iti | tad api dviguṇaṃ naiṣṭhikasya “avakīrṇito dviguṇo daṇḍaḥ” iti kavaṣadharme dviguṇadaṇḍadarśanāt | patitasāvitrīko vrātyaḥ | eteṣu nimitteṣu upapātakaṃ bhavati | saṃjñākaraṇaṃ prāyaścittavidhānārtham || 21.11 ||
stenādayo gītaśīlāntā ekapañcāśan na bhojayitavyā ity uktāḥ śrāddhaprakaraṇe ‘paṅktyāḥ | teṣām apapaṅktyānāṃ madhye durvālāt prāg yāvantas tyaktātmaparyantā [cf. GDh 15.18] ekatriṃśat teṣu patitaḥ kūṭasākṣī cāntarbhūtaḥ | tatra patitasya patitatvaṃ kūṭasākṣiṇas tatsāmyam uktam | vyatirikteṣūpapātakaṃ pāpam | nāstiko ‘pi teṣu paṭhitaḥ | sa ca trividhaḥ | yathāhuḥ paurāṇikāḥ |
nāstikā trividhā jñeyā dharmajñais tattvadarśibhiḥ |
kriyāduṣṭo manoduṣṭo vāgduṣṭaś ceti te trayaḥ || iti |
atra vāgduṣṭa upapātako ‘bhipretaḥ | itarayoḥ pātaka eva | gohantā hananaṃ daṇḍakāṣṭhādinā tāḍanam | brahma vedas tam adhītaṃ yaḥ pramādād abhihatavān vismṛtavān sa brahmaghnaḥ | buddhipūrve mānavam |
brahmaghnatvaṃ vedanindā kauṭasākṣyaṃ suhṛdvadhaḥ |
garhitānnādyayor jagdhiḥ surāpānasamāni ṣaṭ || iti |
tanmantrakṛd gohantṛbrahmaghanor yājanādikṛt | yo brahmacārī striyam upeyāt so ‘vakīrṇī | asyāpaṅktyeṣu paṭhitasya punarvacanaṃ kṛte ‘py avakīrṇiprāyaścitte pṛthag upapātakaprāyaścittam api kartavyam ity evamartham | etac cāpatyotpādanaparyantagamane draṣṭavyam | patitasāvitriko yathākālam anupanīto vrātyaḥ | eteṣūpapātakaṃ pāpam iti || 21.11 ||
ajñānādhyāpanād ṛtvigācāryau patanīyasevāyāṃ ca heyau || 21.12 ||
H reads: “ ajñānād anadhyāpanād”
ajñānād ṛtvik, adhyāpanād ācāryaḥ, etayor nimittayor api parityāgaḥ | anabhijñasyārtvijyāsaṃbhavāt taṃ tyajato yajamānasya nāsti doṣaḥ | evam ācāryo ‘py ālasyādibhiḥ samyaṅ nādhyāpayati tam api tyajato nāsti doṣaḥ śiṣyasya | patanīyasevāyāṃ ca patanīyasya karmaṇaḥ patitena saha yānāsanādeḥ sevāyām ācaraṇe | kutaḥ? patitau heyāv iti vaktavye patanīyasevāyām ity upādānāt | patitasyāpatitatyāgina ity eva siddhatvāt prāg ‘py abdāt parityāgārtham idaṃ vyākhyānam | atrāpy apatitatyāgadoṣaḥ prasajyeteti cet, atrocyate – naiva sahāsanādes tyāgo vidhitsitaḥ | kiṃ tarhi? ṛtvigantaropādānam ācāryāntaragamanaṃ ceti | tatra sahāsanādeḥ tyāgaṃ kurvataḥ sa doṣaḥ, nartvigācāryāntaragamanaṃ kurvata iti | cakārād upapātakināv api || 21.12 ||
ajñānād anadhyāpanād iti | yadi (yaḥ) karmaṇi pravṛtta ṛtviṅmantrān karmapaddhatiṃ vā na jānāti sa ca, ya ālasyādinā nādhyāpayaty ācāryas tāv ubhau heyau tyājyau | idaṃ patitena saha śayanāsanādeḥ sevāyāṃ prāg apy abdāt parityāgārtham | tarhi saṃvatsareṇa patatīti vacanam anarthakam | na tādṛśas tyāgo ‘tra vivakṣitaḥ | kiṃ tarhy ṛtvigācāryāntaram upādeyam | anupādāne doṣa iti || 21.12 ||
anyatra hānāt patati || 21.13 ||
anyatra pūrvoktād ajñānādhyāpananimittād anyatra hānāt tyāgāt patati | tatas tayor anyatra tyāgo na kartavyaḥ || 21.13 ||
anyatrājñānād anadhyāpanād anyatra tayos tyāgo na kartavyaḥ | kurvan patati || 21.13 ||
tasya ca pratigrahītety eke || 21.14 ||
tasya ca ṛtvijam ācāryaṃ vā tyajato yaḥ pratigrahītā ṛtvigācāryau vā tasyārtvjyam adhyāpanaṃ vā yaḥ karotīty arthaḥ | cakārāt tena sahādhyāyī ca patatīty eke manyante | jñānāt patati na tv ajñānād iti gautamaḥ
|| 21.14 ||
tasyartvijam ācāryam īdṛśaṃ tyajataḥ pratigrahītā taṃ yaḥ pratigṛhṇāti yājyatvena śiṣyatvenartvigācāryau vā so ‘pi patatīty eke manyante | eke grahaṇāj jñātvā pratigrahe pātityaṃ nānyatreti || 21.14 ||
na karhicin mātāpitror avṛttiḥ || 21.15 ||
na kasyāṃcid apy avasthāyāṃ patitāvasthāyām apīty arthaḥ | avṛttir aśuśrūṣā sā na kartavyā | tasyām apy avasthāyāṃ śuśrūṣitavyāv ity arthaḥ | śuśrūṣā namaskārādiḥ || 21.15 ||
na kasyāṃcid apy avasthāyāṃ mātāpitror avṛttr aśuśrūṣā kartavyā kiṃ tu patitayor api tayor namaskārādikā śuśrūṣā kartavyā | tathā ca āpastambaḥ: “mātā bhūyāṃsi karmāṇy ārabhate tasyāṃ śuśrūṣā nityā patitāyām api” [ĀpDh 1.28.9] iti || 21.15 ||
dāyaṃ tu na bhajeran || 21.16 ||
dāyaṃ tadīyaṃ dhanaṃ na bhajeran na gṛhṇīyur ity arthaḥ | mātur dāyaṃ strīdhanam | dāyābhāve ātmīyād api dravyād aśanācchādane dātavye iti tuśabdopādānam | niḥsvayoḥ patitāvasthāyām apy aśanācchādanacodanād apatitayor apy arthasiddham | atra patitāyā api mātus tyāgo nāstīti draṣṭavyam | kutaḥ? smṛtyantaradarśanāt | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “mātā tu putraṃ prati na patati” [VaDh 13.47] ityādi | patitāvasthāyām api putreṇa mātā na tyaktavyety abhiprāyeṇa tad uktam iti || 21.16 ||
tadīyaṃ tu dhanaṃ tadabhāve na bhajeran putrādayaḥ | rājagāmi tad bhavati || 21.16 ||
brāhmaṇābhiśaṃsane doṣas tāvat || 21.17 ||
yo doṣeṇa brāhmaṇam abhiyuṅkte “idam enas tvayā kṛtam” ity evamabhiyoge, yāvāṃs tasya adharmo bhavati abhiyoktur api tāvān eveti | tad yathā patita ity abhiyoge patita eva bhavati | sa yathā lokaiḥ pariharaṇīyaḥ tathā abhiyoktāpi pariharaṇīyaḥ | tadīyenāpi prāyaścittenādhikriyata iti || 21.17 ||
yo brāhmaṇam abhiśaṃsati tasya santaṃ doṣaṃ prathamaṃ khyāpayati tasya doṣas tāvān bhavati yāvān kartur iti | yathāha āpastambaḥ: “doṣaṃ dṛṣṭvā na pūrvaḥ parebhyaḥ patitasya samākhyāne syād varjayet tv enaṃ dharmeṣu” [ĀpDh 1.21.20] iti || 21.17 ||
dvir anenasi || 21.18 ||
yas tv anṛtenābhiśaṃsanaṃ karoti tasya dviguṇo ‘bhiyoktur doṣo bhavati anṛtābhivadanād abhiśaṃsanāc ceti | brāhmaṇagrahaṇaṃ cātra dvijātipradarśanārtham | tathā aviśeṣeṇaiva smṛtyantaram paṭhitam |
patitaṃ patitety uktvā coraṃ coreti vā punaḥ |
vacanāt tulyadoṣaḥ syāt mithyā dvirdoṣatāṃ vrajet || iti | [VaDh 20.30] | tatrāpi patitagrahaṇam upapāta-kasyāpy upalakṣaṇam | smṛtyantare: “brāhmaṇam anṛtenābhiśasya patanīyenopapatanīyena vā māsam abbhakṣaḥ śuddhavatīr āvartayan pūto bhavatīti vijñāyate, etenaivābhiśasto vyākhyātaḥ” [VaDh 23.39, 41] iti liṅgāt | evaṃ cātra pātakopapātakābhiśaṃsana eva doṣo na sarvatrety atrāpi draṣṭavyam | pūrvam ākhyātur evāyaṃ doṣaḥ na sarvair vyavahṛtasya | tathāha āpastambaḥ: “doṣaṃ buddhvā na pūrvaḥ parebhyaḥ patitasya samākhyātā syāt” [ĀpDh 1.21.20] iti || 21.18 ||
anenasi viṣaye doṣābhiśaṃsane dvir doṣo ‘sya bhavati | dvir dviguṇaḥ | atrābhiśaṃsanamātre doṣa uktaḥ | mānave tu |
patitaṃ patitety uktvā coraṃ coreti vā punaḥ |
vacanāt tulayadoṣaḥ syān mithyā dvir doṣabhāg bhavet || iti | [=NSm 15–16.22]  | pātityacauryaviṣayam abhiśaṃsanam uktam | vasiṣṭhas tu: “brāhmaṇam anṛtenābhiśaṃsya patanīyenopapatanīyena vā māsam abbhakṣaḥ śuddhavatīr āvartayet pūto bhavati” [VaDh 23.39] iti vijñāyate || 21.18 ||
durbalahiṃsāyāṃ cāvimocane śaktaś cet || 21.19 ||
H edition reads: “ca vimocane” but com. requires the negative
yatra durbalasya hiṃsā vināśo bhavati tatra tadvimocane vimokṣaṇe śaktaś cet tadavimocane yāvān hantur doṣas tāvān asyāpi bhavati | caśabdāt kṣudvyādhyādipīḍitasyāpi bhaktauṣadhācchādanādyadāne śaktau satyāṃ taddhanane yāvān doṣas tāvān evāsyāpi bhavati |
nanu cāhārārthaṃ yaḥ pramāpayati tadvicchede ‘pi doṣaḥ prāpnotīti |
ucyate – atulyatvād āhāraprāṇavicchedayoḥ | āhāravicchede mūlādibhir api kṣunnivṛttiḥ śakyā kartum, prāṇavicchede tu na kaścid asti pratīkāra iti
|| 21.19 ||
durbale prabalena hiṃsyamāne yaḥ śaktaḥ san na mocayati tasyāpi tāvān doṣo yāvān hiṃsituḥ || 21.19 ||
abhikruddhāvagoraṇe brāhmaṇasya varṣaśatam asvargyam || 21.20 ||
H reads “-goraṇaṃ”
abhikruddhena, na parihāsādinā, avagoraṇe praharaṇodyamane brāhmaṇasyānaparādhinaḥ | aparādhinaḥ ātatāyitvaprasaṅgena na doṣa iti | varṣaśatam asvargyaṃ narakapatanam | samānajātīyaviṣayam idam | kṣatriyādibhiḥ kṛte –
dviguṇaṃ triguṇaṃ caiva caturguṇam athāpi vā |
kṣatraviṭchūdrajātīnāṃ brāhmaṇasya vadhe kṛte ||
iti prājāpatyasmṛtiliṅgāt | varṣāṇām api dviguṇatri-guṇatvādi kalpyam | anenaiva nyāyena brāhmaṇena kṛte kṣatraviṭchūdrajātīnāṃ tripādam ardhaṃ pādam iti draṣṭavyam | brāhmaṇakṣatriyavat kṣatriyavaiśyayor api, evaṃ kṣatryavaiśyavad vaiśyaśūdrayor api kalpyam | evaṃ sarvasyottamasyottamasya nīcena nīcena vadhe kṛte draṣṭavyam || 21.20 ||
yo ‘bhikruddhaḥ san brāhmaṇaṃ prahartuṃ hastam āyudhaṃ vāvagurata udyamya kampayati tasya tadavagoraṇaṃ varṣāṇāṃ śatam asvargyaṃ bhavati svargaprāptiṃ niruṇaddhi | tannimittāni sukṛtāni hantīty arthaḥ | asvargyam iti narakapāto vā lakṣyate | sajātīyaviṣayam idam | vijātīyaviṣaye tu –
dviguṇaṃ triguṇaṃ caiva caturguṇam athāpi ca |
kṣattraviṭśūdrajātīnāṃ brāḥmaṇasya vadhe smṛtam || iti | anenaiva nyāyena brāhmaṇenāvagoraṇe kṛte tripādyaṃ dvipādyaṃ pādyaṃ pādaś ceti kṣatriyādiṣu draṣṭavyam | evam anyatrāpi pratilomānulomabhedenādharottarabhāve tāratamyaṃ kalpyam || 21.20 ||
nighāte sahasram || 21.21 ||
āyudhena pāṇinā vā nighāte sahasraṃ varṣāṇām asvargyam, adhikṛtatvāt || 21.21 ||
yaḥ svarṇena hanti tasya varṣasahasram asvargyam | upasamastaṃ varṣapadam apekṣate || 21.21 ||
lohitadarśane yāvatas tat praskandya pāṃsūn saṃgṛhṇīyāt saṃgṛhṇīyāt || 21.22 ||
rudhirotpādane kṛte tad rudhiraṃ brāhmaṇād avasṛtya yāvataḥ pāṃsūn saṃgṛhṇīyāt piṇḍīkuryāt tāvanti varṣasahasrāṇi narakapatanam, aparimitakālam ity arthaḥ | doṣaviśeṣakathanaṃ prāyaścittaviśeṣa-jñāpanārtham | yathāha kaṇvaḥ |
avagūrya caret kṛcchram atikṛcchaṃ nipātane |
kṛcchrātikṛcchrau kurvīta viprasyotpādya śoṇitam || iti | prāyaścittaviśeṣāt daṇḍaviśeṣo draṣṭavyaḥ || 21.22 ||
yadi tena nighātena lohitam utpādayet tatas tal lohitaṃ praskandya niḥsṛtya yāvataḥ pāṃsūn saṃgṛhṇīyāt piṇḍān kuryāt tāvanti varṣāṇi tad asvargyaṃ bhavati | tasmād brāhmaṇāya nāvagureta na nihanyān na lohitaṃ kuryād iti gamyamānatvād anuktam | [abhyāso ‘dhyāyasamāptyarthaḥ] || 21.22 ||
atha dvāviṃśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
prāyaścittanimittāny uktāni | athedānīṃ pratipāpaṃ prāyaścittāni vaktavyāni | tatrādyasya tāvad āha |
evaṃ prāyaścittanimittāny uktāni | atha prāyaścittāny ucyante |
prāyaścittam | agnau saktir brahmaghnas trir avacchātasya || 22.1 ||
prāyaścittam || 22.1 ||
prāyaścittam ity adhikārārtham, prāyaścittam evaitan nādṛṣṭārtham iti | agnau saktiḥ saṅgaḥ patanam ity arthaḥ | trir iti patanaviśeṣaṇam | patane avākchirastvaṃ ca draṣṭavyam | yathāha manuḥ |
prāsyed ātmānam agnau vā samiddhe trir avākchirāḥ | iti | [MDh 11.73] | kasyedaṃ prāyaścittam? brahmaghnaḥ brāhmaṇahantuḥ | avacchātasya avaśīrṇasya bhaktatyāgena karśitadehasyety arthaḥ | tathā ca kāṭhakaśrutiḥ: “anaśanena karśito ‘gnim ārohet” iti || 22.1 ||
adhikāro ‘yam | niścitya tapaso ‘nuṣṭhānaṃ prāyaścittam | tathā ca aṅgirāḥ |
prāyo nāma tapaḥ proktaṃ cittaṃ niścaya ucyate |
taponiścayasaṃyogāt prāyaścittam iti smṛtam || iti || 22.1 ||
agnau saktir brahmaghnas trir avacchātasya
|| 22.2 ||
saktiḥ saṅgaḥ patanam | avacchāto ‘vaśīrṇo bhaktatyāgena kṛśībhūtaḥ | brahmahā bhaktatyāgena kṛśo bhūtvāgnau triḥ pated utthāyotthāya | idam asya prāyaścittam | atra mānavo viśeṣaḥ |
prāsyed ātmānam agnau vā samiddhe trir avākśirāḥ || iti | [MDh 11.73] | kāṭhakaśrutiḥ: “anaśanakarśito ‘gnim ārohet” iti | nedaṃ maraṇāntikaṃ trir iti niyamāt | triḥ patane jīvann api śūdhyatīti || 22.2 ||
lakṣyaṃ vā syāj janye śastrabhṛtām || 22.2 || — || 22.3 ||
lakṣyaṃ śaravedhyam, tad vā bhavej janye senāmadhye | śastrabhṛtām iti dhanuḥprāye yuddhe, nānyaśastraprāye iti || 22.2 ||
janyaṃ yuddham | śastrabhṛta iṣvāsāḥ | lakṣyam iti vacanād yuddha iṣvāsānām iṣūn asyatāṃ lakṣyaṃ bhūtvā tiṣṭhet | tair viddho jīvan mṛto vā śudhyati | yājñavalkyaḥ |
saṃgrāme vā hato lakṣyabhūtaḥ śuddhim avāpnuyāt |
mṛtakalpaḥ prahārārto jīvann api viśudhyati || [YDh 3.248] iti || 21.3 ||
khaṭvāṅgakapālapāṇir vā dvādaśa saṃvatsarān brahmacārī bhaikṣāya
grāmaṃ praviśet karmācakṣāṇaḥ || 22.3 || — || 22.4 ||
khaṭvāṅgaṃ prasiddham | kapālaṃ śavaśiraḥ | yathāha manuḥ: “kṛtvā śavaśiro dhvajam” [MDh 11.72] iti | taddhāraṇaṃ ca cihnārtham | dvādaśasaṃvatsarān evaṃ caret | brahmacārī maithunavarjitaḥ | bhaikṣārtham eva grāmaṃ praviśet | tad eva cāsyāhāraḥ | bhaikṣacaraṇam api mṛnmayena pātreṇa | yathāha śaṅkhaḥ: “mṛnmayaṃ pātram” iti | anyadā araṇya evāvasthānam | brahmacaryabhaikṣopadeśāc ca vratatvam api draṣṭavyam | yathāha kaṇvaḥ |
pavitrapāṇir daṇḍī ca paṅkadanto rajasvalaḥ |
tīrthavāsī kuśācchādī jaṭilo brahmahā bhavet || iti |
karmācakṣāṇaḥ brahmahāsmīti sarvadā kathayan
|| 22.3 ||
khaṭvāṅgaṃ pāśupatānāṃ prasiddham | kapālaṃ svavyāpāditasya brāhmaṇasya śiraḥ kapālam | te pāṇyor yasya sa khaṭvāṅgakapālapāṇiḥ | khaṭvāṅgaṃ dakṣiṇe pāṇau kapālaṃ savye bhikṣārthaṃ pānīyapānārthaṃ bhojanārthaṃ ca | tathā āpastambaḥ: “puruṣaśiraḥ pratipānārtham ādāya khaṭvāṅgaṃ daṇḍārtham” [ĀpDh 1.28.21–29.1] iti | manus tu “kṛtvā śavaśirodhvajam” [MDh 11.72] iti | tasmin pakṣe khaṭvāṅgasyāgre dhvajaḥ | tanmūle śavaśiraḥ | bhikṣācaraṇaṃ tu lohitena khaṇḍaśarāveṇa āpastambīyadarśanāt | evaṃbhūto bhaikṣāya grāmaṃ praviśet | etāvān asya grāme praveśo ‘nyadāraṇye | bhaikṣaṃ ca karmācakṣāṇaś caret |
veśmano dvāri tiṣṭhāmi bhikṣārthī brahmaghātakaḥ |
iti parāśaraḥ | dvādaśa saṃvatsarān evaṃ caran brahmacārī bhavet | strīṣu na prasajet | “bhikṣācaraṇe saptāgārāṇy asaṃkalpitāni” ity āpastambaḥ | saṃvartas tu |
bhikṣāyai praviśed grāmaṃ vanyair yadi na jīvati | iti | “ekakālāhāraḥ” iti vasiṣṭhaḥ || 22.4 ||
patho ‘pakrāmet saṃdarśanād āryasya || 22.4 || — || 22.5 ||
patho mārgād apeyāt dṛṣṭipātād āryasya dvijāteḥ | anenaiva nyāyena pṛṣṭhataḥ pārśvataś ca dūrata eva parihared iti draṣṭavyam | tatra govālavyajanād arvāk saṃnikṛṣṭe sacelasnānaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha vyāghraḥ |
caṇḍālaṃ patitaṃ caiva dūrataḥ parivarjayet |
govālavyajanād arvāk sacelaḥ snānam ācaret || iti |

iti || 22.4 ||
āryas traivarṇikas tasmin dṛṣṭe patho ‘pakrāmed apayāyāt | atra vyāghraḥ |

sthānāsanābhyāṃ viharan savaneṣūdakopasparśī śudhyet || 22.5 || — || 22.6 ||
anyatra virodhibhyaḥ ācamanādikālebhyaḥ kṛtsnam ahas tiṣṭhet | evaṃ rātrim āsanena nayed iti | triṣavaṇasnānaṃ ca kurvan brahmahatyānimittād enaso mucyate | śudhyed ity upasaṃhārārtham || 22.5 ||
“tiṣṭhed ahani rātrāv āsīta” [GDh 26.6] yathāśakti prātar madhaṃdine sāyam iti svaneṣu trisaṃdhyam udakopasparśī syāt snāyāt | evaṃ dvādaśa varṣāṇi carann ante śudhyet | snānavidhānād eva tadantarbhūtamantrādiprāptir iti gamyate | śucinā kartavyam iti ca sarvakarmasādhāraṇam | ataḥ saṃdhyopāsanam apy asya bhavati,
saṃdhyāhīno ‘śucir nityam anarhaḥ sarvakarmasu |
yat kiṃcit kurute karma na tasya phalabhāg bhavet ||
iti dakṣasmaraṇāt | dvijātikarmabhyo hāniḥ patanam ity anena tu prāyaścittavratacaryānaṅgabhūtānāṃ karmaṇāṃ hānir na sarveṣām | atra ca yasya dve brahmahatye, sa caturviṃśativarṣāṇi vrataṃ caret | yasya tisraḥ, sa ṣaṭtriṃśataṃ na punar dehakāla-kartraikyāt prāyaścittasya tantratā | yasya catasro, na tasyeha loke niṣkṛtiḥ | etad eva vratam ottamād ucchvāsāc caret | tathā ca manuḥ |
vidheḥ prāthamikād asmād dvitīye dviguṇaṃ caret |
tṛtīye triguṇaṃ proktaṃ caturthe nāsti niṣkṛtiḥ || iti |
yājñavalkyaḥ |

brāhmaṇasya vā taddhetuke prāṇalābhe sati śudhyet, coravyāghrādibhiḥ pramāpyamānaṃ mocayitvety arthaḥ | ekasya vicchinnāḥ prāṇāḥ anyasmai dattā iti | anenaiva nyāyena sarveṣāṃ hanane tajjātīya-prāṇavicchede vimokṣaṇāt śuddhiḥ draṣṭavyā || 22.6 ||

dravyāpacaye vā tryavaraṃ pratirāddhaḥ || 22.7 || — || 22.8 ||
H omits “vā”
prakaraṇāt brāhmaṇasya yad dravyaṃ hiraṇyādi tad api yajjīvanāya paryāptam – draviṇam api prāṇarūpam “prāṇā hy ete bahiścarāḥ” iti darśanāt – tasya dasyubhir apahriyamāṇasyāpahṛtasya vā apacaye pratimocane śudhyet | mocanāsaṃbhave triḥ pratirāddhaḥ triḥkṛtvā puruṣakāra ity arthaḥ | yathāha manuḥ |
tryavaraṃ pratirāddho vā sarvasvam apajitya vā |
viprasya tannimitte vā prāṇalābhe vimucyate || iti | [MDh 11.80] | asmād eva liṅgāt daridrasyākleśenānu-bhavaparyāptasya dhanasya pradānād api śuddhir jñātavyā || 22.7 ||
brāhmaṇasyeti vartate | brāhmaṇasya dravye corādibhir apacīyamāne ‘pahriyamāṇe tasya pratyānayanāya corādisamīpaṃ gatas taiḥ śastrādibhiḥ kṣato varji(to ‘varji)taḥ sakṛt, punaḥ punaś caivaṃ trivārān nyānaṃ (trivāranyūnaṃ) pratirāddho ‘bhiyuktaḥ sann apratyānīte ‘pi dravye śudhyet | pratyānīte tu sakṛt prayoge ‘pi śudhyet |
tryavaraṃ pratirāddho vā sarvasvam avajitya ca | [MDh 11.80]
iti manuḥ | anenaiva nyāyena svadravyapradānenāpi śuddhir jñeyā | tathā ca manuḥ |
sarvasvaṃ vā vedavide brāhmaṇāyopapādayet |
dhanaṃ vā jīvanāyālaṃ gṛhaṃ vā saparicchadam || [MDh 11.76] iti |
yājñavalkyas tu |
pātre dhanaṃ vā paryāptaṃ datvā śuddhim avāpnuyāt |
ādātuś ca viśuddhyartham iṣṭir vaiśvānarī smṛtā || [YDh 3.250] iti || 22.8 ||
aśvamedhāvabhṛthe vā || 22.8 || — || 22.9 ||
avabhṛtagrahaṇaṃ kriyāntaranivṛttyartham | vacanasāmarthyād enam apa upasparśayeyuḥ | śudhyed iti vartate | vāśabdagrahaṇāt upasaṃhṛtasyābhidhānāc ca etāni svatantrāṇy eva prāyaścittānīti | kecit prakrāntadvādaśavārṣikasyety āhuḥ | tathā ca śaṅkho dvādaśavarṣe śuddhim āpnotīty uktvā āha: “antarāle vā brāhmaṇaṃ mocayitvā gavāṃ dvādaśānāṃ paritrāṇāt” iti | tatrābhyāse prakrāntadvādaśavārṣikasya samāptiviśeṣaṇatvena draṣṭavyam, anabhyāse tu svatantrāṇy eveti || 22.8 ||
snātveti śeṣaḥ | parakīyasyāśvamedhasyāvabhṛte svayaṃ snātvā vā śudhyet | prāṇalābha ityādisūtreṣu vāśabdo vikalpārthaḥ | atra mānavo viśeṣaḥ |
śiṣṭvā vā bhūmidevānāṃ naradevasamāgame |
svam eno ‘vabhṛte snātvā hayamedhe vimucyate || [MDh 11.82] | bhūmidevā brāhmaṇā ṛtvijaḥ | naradevo rājā yajamānaḥ | teṣāṃ samavāye svam enaḥ śiṣṭvā vikhyāpya || 22.9 ||
anyayajñe ‘py agniṣṭudantaś cet || 22.9 || — || 22.10 ||
anyo yajño yasya tad idam anyayajñam avabhṛthaṃ sa ca yajño yad agniṣṭudanto bhavati | agniṣṭunmadhya ity arthaḥ | sa ca sarvamedhaḥ sattraṃ vā tatrāpi snātvā śudhyet || 22.9 ||
aśvamedhād anyayajñe ‘py avabhṛte snātvā śudhyet | kim aviśeṣeṇa | na | tasya ced antar madhye ‘gniṣṭunnāmaikāho bhavati | pañcadaśarātrāder grahaṇam | apara āha – agniṣṭudanto ‘gniṣṭutsamāptiko bhavatīti | atra pakṣe sarvamedhāder grahaṇam | atra ca śudhyed iti dvādaśavārṣikam upasaṃhṛtya vidhānād vāgrahaṇāc ca sarvāṇy etāni svatantrāṇi vaikalpikāni prayojakāni prayojakādiviṣayāṇi vā draṣṭavyāni | anye tu dvādaśavārṣikapravṛttasyety āhuḥ | tathā ca śaṅkhaḥ “dvādaśe varṣe śuddhim āpnoti” ity abhidhāyāha: “antarāle vā brāhmaṇaṃ mocayitvā gavāṃ vā dvādaśānāṃ paritrāṇāt” iti | vāśabdas tu parasparāpekṣayā vikalpārthaḥ || 22.10 ||
na kevalaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ prāṇavicchedenaiva brahmahā bhavati | kiṃ tarhi?
sṛṣṭaś ced brāhmaṇavadhe ‘hatvāpi || 22.10 || — || 22.11 ||
sṛṣṭo niścito ‘dhyavasita ity arthaḥ | brāhmaṇo hantavya ity evaṃ yenādhyavasāyaḥ kṛto bhavati brāhmaṇa-hananaviṣaye asāv ahatvāpi brahmahā bhavati, tasyāpy etad eva prāyaścittaṃ tatsaṃkalpasya dṛṣṭatvād ity abhiprāyaḥ | anenaiva nyāyena sarvaprāṇivadhe tadīyam eva prāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam, atra brāhmaṇagrahaṇasya pradarśanārthatvād iti || 22.10 ||
sarga utsāho niścayaś ca | tadvān sṛṣṭaḥ | yadi brāhmaṇavadhe sṛṣṭo bhavati kenacid daivād vānivāri-taḥ so ‘hatvāpi brahmahā bhavati | atas tasyāpy anan-tarokteṣu prāyaścitteṣu yal laghu tad bhavati || 22.11 ||
ātreyyāṃ caivam || 22.11 || — || 22.12 ||

rājanyavaiśyau cejānau ātreyīm eva ca striyam || iti | [MDh 11.87] | evam iti samastaprāyaścittopa-deśārtham | anye tu atrigotrām ātreyīm āhuḥ | ātreyīgrahaṇād anātreyyāṃ na brahmahā bhavatīti draṣṭavyam || 22.11 ||
“ṛtusnātām ātreyīm āhuḥ | tatra yad apatyaṃ bhavati” [VaDh 20.35–36] iti vasiṣṭhaḥ | tasyām api brāhmaṇyāṃ hatāyām evaṃ brahmahā bhavatīti tadīyam eva prāyaścittam iti | kṣatriyādyātreyīvadhe tattatpuruṣavadhanimittaṃ prāyaścittam | anye tv agtrigotrām ātreyīm āhuḥ || 22.12 ||
garbhe cāvijñāte brāhmaṇasya || 22.12 || — || 22.13 ||
H edition omits “brāhmaṇasya”
brāhmaṇasya garbhe strīpuṃnapuṃsa-katvenāvijñāte hate auṣadhādinā brahmahā bhavati yadi brāhmaṇyām evāhito bhavati | vijñāte tu yathāliṅgam eveti draṣṭavyam | prāyaścittātideśārthaś cakāraḥ | tad etat brahmahatyāprāyaś-cittam anekaprakāraṃ hantuḥ hanya-mānasya cābhisaṃdhyanabhisaṃdhyā-dyapekṣayā yojanīyam |
atra kecit bruvate – hantus tāvad yuktaṃ kuryān na kuryād ity atra kāmākāmakaraṇe gurulāghavasyoktatvāt | “ninditakarmābhyāsi” [GDh 21.1] ity anenābhyāse ‘pi doṣagurutvaśravaṇāt | yat tu hanyamānaṃ prati gurulaghubhāvakalpanaṃ tad ayuktaṃ jñāpakābhāvāt | api ca “brāhmaṇo na hantavyaḥ” ity atra brāhmaṇoddeśena hananapratiṣedho vidhīyate | na coddiśyamānasya viśeṣaṇaṃ vivakṣyate guṇavān nirguṇo veti yathā “daśāpavitreṇa grahaṃ saṃmārṣṭi” [ĀpŚr 12.4.8]ity atraikatvam avivakṣitam | tasmāt brāhmaṇatvajātinibandhana eva pratiṣedhaḥ nāṅgīkṛto guṇa iti |
tad etad ayuktam – hanyamānaguṇāpekṣayā doṣagurutvaprakhyāpakasya vidyamānatvāt | yathāha manuḥ |
guruṃ vā bālavṛddhau vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā bahuśrutam |
ātatāyinam āyāntaṃ hanyād evāvicārayan || iti | [MDh 8.350] | atrātatāyivadhābhyanujñānenaiva bahuśrutādīnām api vadhe siddhe bahuśrutādīnāṃ vadhe viśiṣṭataro doṣa iti jñāpanārthaṃ teṣām upādānam | yathā praśastakarmāpekṣayā ca gurutvam avagamyate yena rājanyavaiśyayor api savanagatayoḥ pramāpaṇe brahmahatyāprāyaścittam upadiṣṭaṃ “rājanyavaiśyau cejānau” [MDh 11.87] ity anena | yathā –
etad eva cared abdaṃ prāyaścittaṃ dvijottamaḥ |
pramāpya vaiśyaṃ vṛttasthaṃ dadyād caikaśataṃ gavām || [MDh 11.129]
iti vṛttasthavaiśyapramāpaṇe pṛthag uktam, itarasya tu “strīśūdraviṭkṣatravadhaḥ” [MDh 11.66] ity anenopapātakaprāyaścittam iti |
yat tūktam uddiśyamānasya viśeṣaṇam avivakṣitam iti, tad apy ayuktam – prāyaścitta-vidhinaiva brāhmaṇavadhapratiṣedho ‘numīyate na hi brāhmaṇavadhapratiṣedhakaṃ vacanam asti yasmin viśeṣaṇam avivakṣitam ity ucyeta | yady api vidyate, tathāpi viśeṣaṇaṃ śakyata eva vivakṣitum uktajñāpakasāmarthyād eva | api ca yathā viśeṣaṇam avivakṣitaṃ tathā saṃkhyāpy avivakṣitaiva prāpnoti, tataś caikasmin hate dvayoś ca tulyam eva prāyaścittaṃ prāpnoti | na caitad iṣṭam | tasmāt “brāhmaṇo na hantav-yaḥ” ity atrāpi guṇavato hanane viśiṣṭa-taro doṣa iti jñātuṃ śakyata evety uk-taṃ sarvam upapannam eveti || 22.12 ||

cāturvidyopapannas tu vidhivad brahmaghātake |

kṣatriyavadhe kṛte ṣaṭsu varṣeṣu bhavaṃ ṣaḍvārṣikaṃ brahmacaryaṃ tad api prākṛtaṃ svābhāvikaṃ khaṭvāṅgādirahitam ity arthaḥ | tataś ca brahmahatyāprāyaścittam eva ṣaḍvarṣāṇi kartavyam ity arthaḥ | buddhipūrvaviṣayam idaṃ vṛttavataś ca hanane | avṛttavato hanane,
“strīśūdraviṭkṣatravadho nāstikyaṃ copapātakam | [MDh 11.66] ity upapātakaprāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam | evam uttaratrāpi | tatra buddhipūrvahanane yad vakṣyati “agnyutsādinirākṛtyupapātakeṣu caivam” [GDh 22.34] iti tat draṣṭavyam | abuddhipūrve tu manunoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
etad eva vrataṃ kuryur upapātakino dvijāḥ |

rājanyavadhe kṛte brāhmaṇavadhe yad uktaṃ brahmacaryaṃ tat ṣaḍvārṣikaṃ kartavyam | tad api prākṛtaṃ svābhāvikaṃ khaṭvāṅgādirahitaṃ brahmahatyāprāyaścittaṃ ṣaḍ varṣāni kuryāt | ekādhikaṃ sahasram ṛṣabha ekasahasrāṃ yāsāṃ tā ṛṣabhaikasahasrā gā dadyāt | atra uśanā: “rājanyavadhe ṣaḍvārṣikaṃ brahmavrataṃ tasyānta ṛṣabhaikasahasragodānaṃ ca” iti | idam abhiṣiktasya śrotriyasya vratavato buddhipūrvavadhe | tasyaivāśrotriyasya vratahīnasya vadhe kevalaṃ godānam | ubhayahīnasyānabhiṣiktasya vadhe kevalaṃ ṣaḍvārṣikam | anabhiṣiktasya tūbhayavato vāsiṣṭham: “brāhmaṇo rājanyaṃ hatvāṣṭau varṣāṇi vrataṃ caret” iti | eteṣām evābuddhipūrve ‘rdhaṃ kalpyam | jātimātravadhe,

pūrvavad buddhipūrvaviṣayam idam | tathā ca manunoktam udāhṛtaṃ ca: “etad eva cared abdam” [MDh 11.129] ityādi | cakārāt pūrveṇa samastam api | tad apy atyantaguṇavato buddhipūrvahanane | abuddhipūrvahanane tu “etad eva vrataṃ kuryuḥ” [MDh 11.117] ity etad eva draṣṭavyam | kṣatriyavaiśyayos tulyaprāyaścittasyānyāyyatvād avṛttavato na syād iti | evaṃ tarhi “agnyutsādi-nirākṛtyupapātakeṣu caivam” [GDh 22.34] ity atrāvṛttavatoḥ kṣatriyavaiśyayos tulatvaṃ prāpnotīti cet, atrocyate – tapasā pūraṇaṃ kartavyam iti | tathā ca manuḥ |
yasmin karmaṇy asya kṛte manasaḥ syād alāghavam |

parākeṇa viśuddhiḥ syāt bhagavān aṅgiro ‘bravīt || iti | śūdraprabhavānām idaṃ buddhipūrve | abuddhipūrve tu cāndrāyaṇaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha lokākṣiḥ |
hanane pratilomeṣu śūdrajānāṃ kathaṃ bhavet |
jñānapūrve parākaḥ syād ajñāte tv aindavaṃ tathā || iti | śūdraprabhavāṇāṃ “asamānāyāṃ tu śūdrāt patitavṛttiḥ” [GDh 4.27] iti samaguṇatvāt prāyaścitta-sāmyam api yuktam eva | itareṣām api tatraivāha |
itareṣāṃ caturbhāgaṃ pitur uktaṃ manīṣibhiḥ |
svajātīnāṃ vadhe yadvac chūdrasya vidhir ucyate || iti | anulomapratilomayoḥ vadhe śūdrasya yat prāyaścittam uktaṃ tad eva svajātihanane ‘pi draṣṭavyam iti tatrārthaḥ | śūdrasya tu tatraivocyate |
sarvam ardhaṃ tripādaṃ ca pādaṃ caiva vrataṃ bhavet |
varṇamātrābhyantarajahiṃsāgamanabhojane ||
iti || 22.18 ||
anātreyyāṃ caivam || 22.19 || — || 22.17 ||
cakārād evātideśe siddhe evaṃkārakaraṇaṃ rājanyavadhaprāyaścittātideśārtham, anantaroktaṃ mā bhūd iti | tathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “anātreyīṃ rājanyahiṃsāyāṃ rājanyāṃ vaiśyahiṃsāyām” [VaDh 20.37–38] ityādi | tarhi tatraiva vaktavyam iti cet, ucyate – ante ‘bhidhānaṃ kṛte prāyaścitte ‘pi na saṃvased ity evamartham | tathā ca manuḥ |
brahmaghnāṃś ca kṛtaghnāṃś ca viśuddhān api dharmataḥ |
śaraṇāgatahantṝṃś ca strīhantṝṃś ca na saṃvaset || [MDh 11.190] iti || 22.19 ||
ātreyīvyatiriktāyā vadhe caivaṃ śūdre saṃvatsaram ṛṣabhaikādaśāś ca gā dadyād iti | idaṃ brāhmaṇyāś cāritravatyāḥ kuṭumbinyā buddhipūrvavadhe | yājñavalkyaś ca |
apraduṣṭāṃ striyaṃ hatvā śūdrahatyāvrataṃ caret || iti | [YDh 3.269]
ṣaṇmāsāñ śūdrahāpy etad dhenur dadyād daśātha vā || iti | [YDh 3.267]
durvṛttā brahmaviṭkṣatraśūdrayoṣāḥ pramāpya tu |
dṛtiṃ dhanur bastam aviṃ kramād dadyād viśuddhaye || iti | [YDh 3.268] | yat tu hārītenoktam: “ṣaḍvarṣāṇi rājanye prākṛtaṃ brahmacaryam | vaiśye trīṇi | sārdham abdaṃ śūdre, kṣatriyavad brāhmaṇeṣu, vaiśyavat kṣatriyāyām, śūdravad vaiśyāyām, śūdrāṃ hatvā nava māsān” iti tad atyantotkṛṣṭācāryādiviṣayam || 22.17 ||
gāṃ ca vaiśyavat || 22.20 || — || 22.18 ||
gāṃ hatvā vaiśyavat prāyaścittaṃ kuryāt | tatra vṛtta-vatyā hananam idam | buddhipūrvatvaṃ ca prakaraṇād adhikriyate | tatra bahukṣīraguṇayuktāyā buddhipūrva-hanane vaiśyoktaṃ traivārṣikaṃ brahmacaryaṃ draṣṭavyam | tatra ca dhanavata ṛṣabhaikasahasra-godānam | kutaḥ? rājavadhe varṣatrayaṃ brahmacarya-rakṣaṇasya gosahasradānasya pratinidhirūpeṇa dṛṣṭatvāt | tathaivodāhṛtaṃ ca manuvākyam “ṛaṣabhaikasahasrā gāḥ” [MDh 11.127] ityādi |
bahukṣīrayuktāyāṃ gurutaro doṣa iti kasmād avagamyata iti cet, ucyate –
kṣudrakāṇāṃ paśūnāṃ tu hiṃsāyāṃ dviśato damaḥ | iti | [MDh 8.297] | tatra kṣudrapaśuśabdena alpaprayojanānāṃ gavāṃ grahaṇam, nājāvikānām,
gardabhājāvikānāṃ tu daṇḍaḥ syāt pañcamāṣakaḥ | [MDh 8.298] iti teṣāṃ pṛthagdaṇḍadarśanāt | tasmāt tadviṣaye daṇḍalaghutvadarśanāt bahuprayojanānāṃ gurutaro doṣa iti gamyate | tasyā evābuddhipūrva-hanane ṛṣabhaikaśatāś ca gā dadyād iti draṣṭavyam | tadasaṃbhave abdaṃ vā brahmacaryam | yad vaiśyasya dānatapasoḥ samuccitayor uktaṃ tad atrāpi draṣṭavyam | tasyāpi viṣaya asyā eva brāhmaṇaparigṛhītāyāḥ buddhipūrvahanane | brāhmaṇaparigṛhītāyāṃ gurutvam api,
goṣu brāhmaṇasaṃsthāsu sthūrikāyāś ca bhedane | [NSm 19.40] iti liṅgāt | atra godānāśaktau niṣkaṃ niṣkārdhaṃ pādam eva veti draṣṭavyam | tathodāhṛtaṃ ca vyāghravacanam,
gavām abhāve niṣkaṃ vā niṣkārdhaṃ pādam eva vā ityādi | tatra rājño gosahasradānaṃ suvarṇasahasra-dānaṃ vā, tatsadṛśasya niṣkārdham ity ayaṃ pakṣaḥ | prākṛtasya tu pādam | kuta? asmin viṣayakaraṇe jātukarṇivacanāt |
gosahasraṃ suvarṇasya sahasraṃ rājña ucyate |
tadardhaṃ madhyamasyoktaṃ pādaṃ syāt prākṛtasya tu || iti |
tathā ca manur api kasmiṃścit prakaraṇe āha |
kārṣāpaṇaṃ bhaved daṇḍyaḥ yatrānyaḥ prākṛto janaḥ |
tatra rājā bhaved daṇḍyaḥ sahasram iti dhāraṇā || iti | [MDh 8.336] | anenāpi prākṛtajanād rājño ‘dhikaṃ gamyate | sarvāsaṃbhave varṣacatuṣṭayaṃ vā prākṛtabrahmacaryarakṣaṇaṃ draṣṭavyam | evaṃ sarvatra | tasyā evābuddhipūrvahanane vasiṣṭhoktaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “gauś ced ghatā syāt tasyāś ca carmaṇārdreṇa pariveṣṭitaḥ ṣaṇmāsān kṛcchraṃ taptakṛcchraṃ vātiṣṭhet” [VaDs 21.18] ityādi | tasya abuddhipūrvaviṣayaparijñānam “brāhmaṇaś ced aprekṣāpūrvam” [VaDh 21.16] ity atra aprekṣāpūrvam ity adhikārāt | tatra “ṛṣabhavehatau dadyātām” [VaDh 21.22] iti dvivacanāt dvau hantārau gamyete | tatra ṛṣabhahanane ṛṣabhaṃ gohanane gavādīti draṣṭavyam | vyāghroktaṃ vā |
gāṃ ced ghanyān naro ‘jñānāt paṇasaptaśataṃ tataḥ |
śrotriyāya daridrāya dadyāc chuddhyartham ātmanaḥ || iti | gāṃ ceti cakārāc chūdroktam api draṣṭavyam | tatrālpaprayojanāyā hanane buddhipūrve vatsaratapaścaraṇasahitam ṛṣabhaikādaśagodānaṃ draṣṭavyam | tasyā evābuddhipūrve jātukarṇyuktaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha:
gāṃ ced akāmato hanyāt brāhmaṇānujñayā tataḥ |
prājāpatyatrayaṃ kṛtvā sparśayet brāhmaṇāya gām || iti | tasyā eva brāhmaṇaparigṛhītāyā buddhipūrve hanane manunoktaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha |
upapātakasaṃyukto goghno māsaṃ yavān pibet |
kṛtavāso vased goṣṭhe carmaṇārdreṇa saṃvṛtaḥ ||
caturthakālam aśnīyād akṣāralavaṇaṃ mitam |
gomūtreṇācaret snānaṃ dvau māsau niyatendriyaḥ ||
divānugacchet tā gās tu tiṣṭhann ūrdhvaṃ rajaḥ pibet |
śuśrūṣitvā namaskṛtvā rātrau vīrāsanaṃ vaset ||
tiṣṭhantīṣv anutiṣṭhet tu vrajantīṣv anu ca vrajet |
āsīnāsu tathāsīta niyato vigataspṛhaḥ ||
āturām abhiṣiktāṃ vā coravyāghrādibhir bhayaiḥ |
patitāṃ paṅkalagnāṃ vā sarvaprāṇair vimocayet ||
uṣṇe varṣati śīte vā mārute vāti vā bhṛśam |
na kurvītātmanas trāṇaṃ gor akṛtvā svaśaktitaḥ ||
ātmano yadi vānyeṣāṃ kṣetre gehe ‘tha vā khale |
bhakṣayantī na kathayet pibantaṃ caiva vatsakam ||
anena vidhinā yas tu goghno gā anugacchati |
sa gohatyākṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tribhir māsair vyapohati ||
ṛṣabhaikādaśā gāś ca dadyāt sucaritavrataḥ |
avidyamāne sarvasvaṃ vedavidbhyo nivedayet || iti | [MDh 11.108–16]
abuddhipūrve tu saṃvatsaraṃ prākṛtaṃ brahmacaryaṃ draṣṭavyam | yadi vatsasyākāmato mṛtyur bhavati tadā vṛṣabhaikādaśagodānaṃ draṣṭavyam | vatsaṃ prati laghutvam api,
vatsānām ardhadaṇḍaḥ syāt samo daṇḍaḥ payasvinīnām |
balīvardānāṃ vṛṣabhāṇāṃ ca tathaivoktaṃ manīṣibhiḥ ||
iti prājāpatyasmṛtidarśanāt | tasya buddhipūrve tu,
vatsaṃ hatvā naro buddhyā tataś cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret |
paṇān pañcaśataṃ vāpi dadyāc chuddhyartham ātmanaḥ ||
iti kavaṣasmṛtyuktaṃ draṣṭavyam | svāmine tanmūlyaṃ dāpyam “dāpyas tasya ca tadhanam” [MDh 8.176] iti liṅgāt | asmin vadhe śūdrāṇām ardhadaṇḍaḥ, lokākṣivacanāt:
dvijātīnāṃ tu sarveṣāṃ sarvo daṇḍo vidhīyate |
śūdrāṇām ardhadaṇḍaḥ syād iti dharmavido viduḥ ||
iti || 22.20 ||
gāṃ ca hatvā vaiśyavadhe yat prāyaścittaṃ “vaiśyavadhe traivāṣikam ṛṣabhaikaśatāś ca gā dadyāt” [GDh 22.15] iti tac caret | idaṃ vṛttasvādhyāyavato durgatasya bahukuṭumbasya yā gaur bahukṣīrā taruṇī tasyā buddhipūrvavadhe | tādṛśyā ekaphalāyā garbhiṇyāḥ karmāṅgabhūtāyā vadhe yāmyam |
gosahasraṃ śataṃ vāpi dadyāt sucaritavrataḥ |
avidyamāne sarvasvaṃ vedavidbhyo nivedayet || iti |
dvaimāsikaṃ vratam atra prakṛtam | atraiva buddhipūrve kātyāyanīyaṃ godānarahitaṃ traivārṣikam |
goghnas taccarmasaṃvīto vased goṣṭhe ‘tha vā punaḥ |
gāś cānugacchet satataṃ mauñjīcīrājinādibhiḥ ||
varṣaśītātapakleśavahnipaṅkabhayārditaḥ |
mokṣayet sarvayatnena pūyate vatsarais tribhiḥ || iti |
vasiṣṭhaḥ: “gāṃ ced dhanyāt tasyāś carmaṇārdreṇa pariveṣṭitaḥ ṣaṇmāsān kṛcchraṃ taptakṛcchraṃ vā tiṣṭhed ṛṣabhavehatau ca dadyātām” [VaDh 21.18, 22] iti | vehad vṛṣbhopahatā gauḥ | dadyātām iti karmaṇi kartṛpratyayaḥ | yājñāvalkyaḥ |
pañcagavyaṃ piban goghno māsam āsīta saṃyataḥ |
goṣṭheśayo go’nugāmī gopradānena śudhyati ||
kṛcchraṃ caivātikṛcchraṃ ca cared vāpi samāhitaḥ |
dadyāt trirātraṃ copoṣya vṛṣabhaikādaśās tu gāḥ || iti | [YDh 3.263–264]
jābālaḥ |
prājāpatyaṃ caren māsaṃ gohantā ced akāmataḥ |
gohito go’nugāmī syād gopradānena śudhyati || iti |
viṣṇuḥ |
goghnasya pañcagavyena māsam ekaṃ palatrayam |
pratyahaṃ syāt parāko vā cāndrāyaṇam athāpi vā || iti |
kāśyapaḥ: “gāṃ hatvā taccarmaṇā parivṛto māsaṃ goṣṭheśayas triṣavaṇasnāyī nityaṃ pañcagavyāhāraḥ” iti | śātātapaḥ: “māsaṃ pañcagavyāhāraḥ” iti | śaṅkha-pracetasau: “goghnaḥ pañcagavyāhāraḥ | pañcaviṃśatirātram upavaset saśikhaṃ vapanaṃ kṛtvā gocarmaṇā parivṛto gāś cānugacched goṣṭheśayo gāṃ ca dadyāt” iti | paiṭhīnasiḥ: “goghno māsaṃ yavāgūṃ prasṛtataṇḍulaśṛtāṃ bhuñjāno gobhyaḥ priyaṃ kurvañ śudhyati” iti | manuḥ |
upapātakasaṃyukto goghno bhuñjīta yāvakam |
kṛtavāpo vased goṣṭhe carmaṇārdreṇa saṃvṛtaḥ ||
caturthakālam aśnīyād akṣāralavaṇaṃ mitam |
gomūtreṇācaret snānaṃ dvau māsau niyatendriyaḥ || [MDh 11.108–109]
ity ārabhya,
anena vidhinā yas tu goghno gā anugacchati |
sa gohatyākṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tribhir māsair vyapohati ||
ṛṣabhaikādaśā gāś ca dadyāt sucaritavratam || iti | [MDh 11.115–116] | sumantuḥ: “goghnasya gopradānaṃ goṣṭhe śayanaṃ dvādaśarātraṃ pañcagavyaprāśanaṃ gavām anugamanaṃ ca” iti | saṃvartaḥ |
saktuyāvakabhaikṣāśī payo dadhi ghṛtaṃ śakṛt |
etāni kramaśo ‘śnīyān māsārdhaṃ susamāhitaḥ ||
brāhmaṇān bhojayitvā tu gāṃ dadyād ātmaśuddhaye || iti | bṛhaspatiḥ: “dvādaśarātraṃ pañcagavyāhāraḥ” iti | eteṣāṃ buddhipūrvābuddhipūrvabhedena brāhmaṇādiparigraheṇa yathārhaṃ viṣayavibhāga ūhitavyaḥ | ṣaṭtriṃaṇmate viśeṣaḥ |
pāda utpannamātre tu dvau pādau dṛḍhatāṃ gate |
pādonaṃ vratam ādiṣṭaṃ hatvā garbham acetanam ||
aṅgapratyaṅgasaṃpūrṇe garbhe cetaḥsamanvite |
dviguṇaṃ govrataṃ kuryād eṣā goghnasya niṣkṛtiḥ ||
bṛhatpracetāḥ |
ekavarṣe hate vatse kṛcchrapādo vidhīyate |
abuddhipūrve puṃsaḥ syād dvipādas tu dvihāyane ||
trihāyane tripādaṃ syāt prājāpatyam ataḥ param || iti |
smṛtyantaram |
ativṛddhām atikṛśām atibhālāṃ ca rogiṇīm |
hatvā pūrvavidhānena cared ardhavrataṃ dvijaḥ ||
brāhmaṇān bhojayec chaktyā dadyād dhematilāṃs tathā || iti |
saṃvarta-āpastambau |
ekā ced bahubhiḥ kaiścid daivād dvyāpāditā kvacit |
pādaṃ pādaṃ tu hatyāyāś careyus te pṛthak pṛthak ||
vyāpannānāṃ bahūnāṃ tu rodhane bandhane ‘pi vā |
bhiṣaṅ mithyopacāre ca dviguṇaṃ govrataṃ caret || iti | bahūnām api vyāpādane dviguṇam eva vacanabalāt | na tu pratinimittaṃ nauprettikāvṛttiḥ (?) | vyāsaḥ |
auṣadhaṃ lavaṇaṃ caiva puṇyārtham api bhojanam |
atiriktaṃ na dātavyaṃ kāle svalpaṃ tu dāpayet ||
atirikte vipattiś cet kṛcchrapādo vidhiyate || iti |
āpastambaḥ |
pāṣāṇair laguḍair vāpi śastrair vānyena vā balāt |
nighātayanti ye gās tu tasmin kuryur vrataṃ hi te ||
pādam ekaṃ cared rodhe dvau pādau bandhane caret |
yojane pādahīnaṃ syāc caret sarvaṃ nipātane || iti |
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
na nālikereṇa na śāṇavālair na cāpi mauñjena na vardhraśṛṅkhalaiḥ |
etais tu gāvo na nibandhanīyā baddhvānutiṣṭhet paraśuṃ pragṛhya || iti || 22.18 ||
maṇḍūkanakulakākabimbadaharamūṣakaśvahiṃsāsu ca || 22.21 || — || 22.19 ||
atra kākagrahaṇaṃ manunā viḍvarāhaślokapaṭhitānāṃ kravyādaślokapaṭhitānāṃ biḍālaślokapaṭhitānāṃ mārjāraślokapaṭhitānāṃ copalakṣaṇam |
viḍvarāhakharoṣṭrāṇāṃ gomāyoḥ kapikākayoḥ |
prāśya mūtrapurīṣāṇi dvijaś cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || [MDh 11.154]
kravyādasūkaroṣṭrāṇāṃ kukkuṭānāṃ ca bhakṣaṇe |
narakākabakānāṃ ca taptakṛcchraṃ viśodhanam || [MDh 11.156]
biḍālakākākhūcchiṣṭaṃ jagdhvā śvanakulasya ca |
keśakīṭāvapannaṃ ca pibet brāhmīṃ suvarcalām || [MDh 11.159]
mārjāranakulau hatvā cāṣaṃ maṇḍūkam eva ca |
śvagodholūkakākāṃś ca śūdrahatyāvrataṃ caret || [MDh 11.131] iti |
eteṣāṃ samudāyavadhe śūdrahatyāvrataṃ cared iti draṣṭavyam | śūdrahatyāprāyaścittānukarṣaṇārthaś cakāraḥ | buddhipūrvābuddhipūrvabhede ‘pi śūdravad eva | pratyekavadhe tv abuddhipūrve viḍvarāhyādīnāṃ madhye keṣāṃcid āhatya vidhīyate smṛtyantare |
ghṛtakumbhaṃ varāhaṃ tu tiladroṇaṃ tu tittiram |
śukaṃ dvihāyanaṃ vatsaṃ krauñcaṃ hatvā trihāyanam ||
hatvā haṃsaṃ balākaṃ ca bakaṃ barhiṇam eva ca |
vānaraṃ śyenabhāsau ca sparśayed brāhmaṇāya gām || iti | [MDh 11.134–35] | idaṃ mahatānāṃ vadhe | pratyekavadhe tu kārṣāpaṇamātraṃ deyam | tathāha vyāghraḥ |
haṃsānāṃ ca mayūrāṇāṃ jalasthānāṃ ca pakṣiṇām |
kapīnāṃ śyenabhāsānāṃ vadhe dadyāt paṇaṃ dvijaḥ ||
vāso dadyād dhayaṃ hatvā pañca nīlān vṛṣān gajam |
ajameṣāv anaḍvāhaṃ kharaṃ hatvaikahāyanam ||
kravyādāṃś ca mṛgān hatvā dhenuṃ dadyāt payasvinīm |
akravyādo vatsatarīm uṣṭraṃ hatvā tu kṛṣṇalam ||
iti | buddhipūrve daṇḍānurūpaṃ parikalpyam | yathāha daṇḍapāruṣye –
manuṣyamāraṇe kṣipraṃ coravat kilbiṣaṃ bhavet |
prāṇabhṛtsu mahatsv ardhaṃ gomāyūṣṭrahayādiṣu ||
kṣudrakāṇāṃ paśūnāṃ tu hiṃsāyāṃ dviśato damaḥ |
pramāpaṇe prāṇabhṛtāṃ tatra daṇḍo vicāritaḥ ||
pañcāśatsu bhaved daṇḍaḥ śubheṣu mṛgapakṣiṣu |
gardabhājāvikānāṃ tu daṇḍaḥ syāt pañcamāṣakaḥ ||
māṣikas tu bhaved daṇḍaḥ śvasūkaranipātane || iti | [MDh 8.296–98]
eṣām āhatya yuktāṇām –
payaḥ pibet trirātraṃ vā yojanaṃ vādhvano vrajet |
upaspṛśet sravantyāṃ vā sūktaṃ vābdaivataṃ japet || [MDh 11.132] iti draṣṭavyam | tatra buddhipūrve payaḥpānam adhvagamanaṃ vā draṣṭavyam | abuddhipūrve tu snānaṃ sūktaṃ japaś ceti | dakṣiṇācodanāvat | vatsānāṃ hanane ‘pi tāratamyaṃ draṣṭavyam | narāṇām uktaṃ varṇādibhedena | varāhāṇām api vakṣyati “varāhe ghṛtaghaṭaḥ” [GDh 22.26] iti | atraitad api draṣṭavyam | yat tu mūtrapurīṣaprāśane cāndrāyaṇam uktam, tatrāpy eteṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ grahaṇaṃ cāndrāyaṇam api buddhipūrve draṣṭavyam | tatra bhakṣaṇe taptakṛccham uktam | yatrāpy ucchiṣṭabhakṣaṇe brahmasuvarcalā-pānam uktam atra sarvatrāpy eṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ grahaṇam iti | atra cāndrāyaṇaṃ manuṣyamūtrapurīṣaprāśane pṛthag uktvā tadvarjitānāṃ draṣṭavyam | tatrāpi mūtragrahaṇe retaso ‘pi grahaṇam, manuṣyamūtrapurī-ṣaprāśane prāyaścittopadeśenāsyāpi samānopadeśāt |
bhuktvāto ‘nyatamasyānnam amatyā tu tryahaṃ kṣipet |
matyā bhuktvā caret kṛcchraṃ reto viṇmūtram eva ca || iti | [MDh 4.222] | ayam api vakṣyati “mūtrapurīṣaretasāṃ ca prāśane” [MDh 23.4] iti | buddhipūrve cāndrāyaṇaṃ draṣṭavyam | abuddhipūrve tu sāntapanam, yathāha lokākṣiḥ |
varāhāṇāṃ kharoṣṭrāṇāṃ kapīnāṃ ca tathaiva ca |
prāśya mūtrapurīṣāṇi caret sāntapanaṃ dvijaḥ || iti |
atrāpy eṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ grahaṇam iti | taptakṛcchravidhānam api buddhipūrvaka eva | tatra punaḥ saṃskāraś ca draṣṭavyaḥ | tathāyaṃ vakṣyati punaḥsaṃskārasahitaṃ taptakṛcchram adhikṛtya “śvāpadoṣṭrakharāṇāṃ cāṅgasya” [GDh 23.5] iti | abuddhipūrve tu vasiṣṭhoktaṃ draṣṭavyam: “śvakukkuṭagrāmasūkarakaṅkagṛdhra-bhāsavāyasapārāvatamānuṣolūkānāṃ māṃsādane saptarātram abhojanaṃ niṣpurīṣībhāvaḥ punaḥsaṃskāraś ca” [VaDh 23.30] iti | evaṃ cātrābhihitānāṃ ca tatrāntarbhāvo draṣṭavyaḥ | yatra tu brahmasuvarcalāpānaṃ vihitaṃ tatrāpi narocchiṣṭaṃ varjayitvā | yathāha uśanā:
brāhmaṇocchiṣṭabhojane prāṇāyāmaśataṃ kuryāt kṣatriyocchiṣṭabhojane prāṇāyāmasahasraṃ vaiśyocchiṣṭabhojane prāṇāyāmadaśasāhasraṃ matipūrvaṃ cet prājāpatyam atikṛcchraṃ kṛcchrātikṛcchaṃ ca | śūdrocchiṣṭabhojane saptarātraṃ yavāgūpānaṃ matipūrvaṃ cet parākam | evaṃ dvijāter dvijātyucchiṣṭabhojane śūdrāṇāṃ ca brāhmaṇocchiṣṭānām apāṃ pāne ṣoḍaśaprāṇāyāmān dhārayet kṣatriyāṇāṃ triṃśat vaiśyānāṃ catvāriṃśat śūdrāṇāṃ kuśavāripānaṃ tryaham, buddhipūrvaṃ cet tryahaṃ caturahaṃ pañcāhaṃ saptāhaṃ ca krameṇa pañcagavyapānaṃ ca krameṇa | antyajānām ucciṣṭabhojane buddhipūrve mahāsāntapanam, abuddhipūrve cāndrāyaṇam | iti |
manunāpi kiṃcid uktam |
śūdrocchiṣṭā apaḥ pītvā kuśavāri pibet tryaham || [MDh 11.148]
abhojyānāṃ ca bhuktvānnaṃ strīśūdrocchiṣṭam eva ca |
jagdhvā māṃsam abhakṣyaṃ ca saptarātraṃ yavān pibet || [MDh 11.152] iti | suvarcalāpānam apy abuddhipūrve aviśeṣād ekarātram eva | buddhipūrve tu sāntapanam | yathāha jātūkarṇiḥ |
jambūkakākamārjāraśvagodhānāṃ gavām api |
matyocchiṣṭaṃ dvijo bhuktvā kṛcchraṃ sāntapanaṃ caret || iti |
atra kavaṣoktam api draṣṭavyam |
viprārdhaṃ kṣatriyasya syād vaiśyānāṃ ca tadardhakam |
tadardham eva śūdrāṇāṃ prāyaścittaṃ vidur budhāḥ || iti | idaṃ sarvatra draṣṭavyam | hiṃsāgrahaṇam adhikārasiddham api kriyate hiṃsāyām eva na tu spṛṣṭasyāpīti || 22.21 ||
bimbaḥ kāmarūpī kṛkalāsaḥ | daharaḥ svalpakāyo mūṣakaḥ | chucchundarīty eke | anye prasiddhāḥ | eteṣāṃ samuditānāṃ vadhe vaiśyavat prāyaścittam | idaṃ buddhipūrvābhyāsaviṣayam | anyatra āpastambīyam: “vāyasabakabalākabarhiṇacakravāka-haṃsabhāsamaṇḍūkanakulasairikāśvahiṃsāyāṃ śūdravat prāyaścittam” [ĀpDh 1.25.13] iti | manur api |
mārjāranakulau hatvā cāṣamaṇḍūkam eva ca |
śvagodholūkakākāṃś ca śūdrahatyāvrataṃ caret || iti | [MDh 11.131] | pratyekaṃ vadhe tu buddhipūrve |
mārjāragodhānakulamaṇḍūkaśvapatatriṇaḥ |
hatvā tryahaṃ pibet kṣīraṃ kṛcchraṃ vā pādikaṃ caret || [YDh 3.270]
iti yājñavalkyoktaṃ draṣṭavyam | buddhipūrve mānavam |
payaḥ pibet trirātraṃ vā yojanaṃ vādhvano vrajet || [MDh 11.132] iti || 22.19 ||
asthanvatāṃ sahasraṃ hatvā || 22.22 || — || 22.20 ||
anyeṣām asthimatāṃ sahasravadhe śūdrahatyā-prāyaścittaṃ bhavati || 22.22 ||
asthimatāṃ kṛkalāsādīnāṃ sahasraṃ hatvā vaiśyavat prāyaścittam || 22.20 ||
anasthimatām anaḍudbhāre ca || 22.23 || — || 22.21 ||
anasthimatāṃ krimyādīnām anaḍudbhāramātre hate etac chūdrahatyāprāyaścittam | atra buddhipūrve śūdrahatyāprāyaścittam, netaratraitad iti draṣṭavyam | evaṃ sarvatrātideśe draṣṭavyam || 22.23 ||
ye ‘sthimanto na bhavanti daṃśamaśakādayas teṣāṃ yāvato ‘naḍvān bhartuṃ śaknoti tāvato hatvā vaiśyavat prāyaścittam | idaṃ dvayam api pūrvābhyāsaviṣayam | anyatra yājñavalkyoktam |
asthanvatāṃ sahasraṃ tu tathānasthimatām anaḥ | [YDh 3.269] | śūdrahatyāvrataṃ ṣāṇmāsikaṃ prakṛtaṃ daśa dhenūr vā dadyāt iti ca || 22.21 ||
api vāsthanvatām ekaikasmin kiṃcit kiṃcid dadyāt || 22.24 || — || 22.22 ||
atha na vā sahasrapūraṇam anadudbhārapūraṇaṃ vā anurudhyate, kiṃ tu anantaram ekaikasmin kiṃcit kiṃcit kākaṇikāmātraṃ dadyāt | vāśabdāt japādi vā kuryāt | apiśabdād anasthimatām api | tathāha manuḥ |
kiṃcid eva tu viprāya dadyād asthimatāṃ vadhe |
anasthnāṃ caiva hiṃsāyāṃ prāṇāyāmena śudhyati || iti | [MDh 11.141] | tatrāsthimatāṃ buddhipūrvahanane kākaṇikāvaramātraṃ deyam, abuddhipūrve tu ṣaṭ prāṇāyāmāḥ | anasthimatāṃ buddhipūrve prāṇāyāmatrayam, abuddhipūrve tv eka eva prāṇāyāmo draṣṭavyaḥ | tathāha kaṇvaḥ |
hanane ‘sthimatāṃ dadyāt matyā kākaṇikāṃ naraḥ |
abuddhyā tu tatas teṣāṃ prāṇāyāmān ṣaḍ ācaret ||
prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kuryāt anasthnāṃ hanane matau |
abuddhyaikaṃ tataḥ kuryād iti dharmavido viduḥ ||
iti || 22.24 ||
api veti vikalpe | asthanvatāṃ yāvanto hatāḥ sahasram ūrdhvam arvāg vā tāvataḥ saṃkhyāya pratyekaṃ kiṃcit kiṃcid dadyāt | idaṃ cāsthimatsu prāyaścittaṃ pūrvkaṃ veti |
aṣṭamuṣṭi bhavet kiṃcit kiṃcid aṣṭau tu puṣkalam |
puṣkalāni tu catvāri āḍhakaḥ parikīrtitaḥ ||
caturāḍhako bhaved droṇa iti mānasya lakṣaṇam ||
iti smṛtiḥ | anasthimatāṃ tu tāvantah prāṇāyāmāḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
kiṃcid eva tu viprāya dadyād asthimātāṃ vadhe |
anasthnāṃ caiva hiṃsāyāṃ prāṇāyāmena śudhyati || [MDh 11.141] iti || 22.22 ||
ṣaṇḍe palālabhāraḥ sīsamāṣaś ca || 22.25 || — || 22.23 ||
ṣaṇḍe tṛtīyāprakṛtau, na tu klībe, tasya tu śrotriyatvena prāptau satyāṃ śrāddhe pratiṣedhāt sasaṃskārasaṃbandhitvāc ca | tasmin hate tasya jātiviśeṣo nāsti – palālabhāro gavāṃ sīsamāṣo brāhmaṇāya | asamāsanirdeśād vyastam, cakārāt samatvaṃ ca | tatrābuddhipūrve vyastaṃ buddhipūrve samastam iti || 22.25 ||
yaṃ prati devala āha |
ṣaṇḍho yo hīnaliṅgaḥ syāt saṃskārārhaś ca naiva saḥ | iti, tasmin hate puruṣabāhyaḥ palālabhāraḥ sīsamāṣaś cety ubhayaṃ militaṃ deyaṃ buddhipūrve | itaratra tv ekaikam | sīsaṃ lohaviśeṣo rajatasadṛśaḥ kṣaṇadrutiḥ | māṣapramāṇaṃ pūrvam eva vyākhyātam | atra ca na kvāpi smṛtau jāviviśeṣaḥ śrūyate, ṣaṇḍhaḥ ṣaṇḍhaka ity etāvad eva śrūyate | tatra yathā jāvisamavāye ‘pi brāhmaṇādiprayuktaḥ saṃskāro na bhavati tathā tadvadhanimittaṃ prāyaścittam api na bhavati yāvad uktam eva tu bhavati | anye mṛgapakṣiviṣayaṃ manyante | mṛgeṣu pakṣiṣu ca ye ṣaṇḍhās teṣu hateṣv iti || 22.23 ||
varāhe ghṛtaghaṭaḥ || 22.26 || — || 22.24 ||
sūkare hate ghṛtapūrṇo ghaṭo brāhmaṇāya deyaḥ | asya tūkto viṣayaḥ || 22.26 ||
varāhe hate ghṛtapūrṇo ghaṭo deyaḥ || 22.24 ||
sarpe lohadaṇḍaḥ || 22.27 || — || 22.25 ||
lohaśabdena kārṣṇāyasam ucyate | yathāha manuḥ |
abhrīṃ kārṣṇāyasīṃ dadyāt sarpaṃ hatvā dvijottamaḥ | iti | [MDh 11.133] | buddhipūrve idam, abuddhipūrve tu māṣamātraṃ deyam, “sarpaṃ hatvā māṣamātraṃ dayāt” ity auśanasavacanāṭ || 22.27 ||
sarpe hate lohadaṇḍo deyaḥ | lohaśabdena kārṣṇāyasam ucyate,
abhrīṃ kārṣṇāyasīṃ dadyāt sarpaṃ hatvā dvijottamaḥ | [MDh 11.133] iti mānave darśanāt | “sarpaṃ hatvā māṣaṃ dadyāt” ity auśanasaṃ buddhipūrvaviṣayam || 22.25 ||
brahmabandhvāṃ calanāyāṃ jīlaḥ || 22.28 ||
brahmabandhvāṃ calanāyāṃ nīlaḥ || 22.26 ||
calanā vyabhicāriṇī | kṣatriyāder manunoktaṃ draṣṭavyam, yathāha |
jīlakārmukabastāvīn pṛthag dadyād viśuddhaye |
caturṇām api varṇānāṃ nārīr hatvānavasthitāḥ || iti | [MDh 11.138] | jīlo dṛtiḥ | calanam api gūḍhatvena bahupuruṣasevitvam | yathāha prajāpatiḥ |
abhigacchati yā nārī bahubhiḥ puruṣair mithaḥ |
vyabhicāriṇīti sā jñeyā pratyakṣaṃ gaṇiketi ca || iti |
evaṃ ca puruṣatrayasaṃsargād arvāk pūrvoktaṃ hananaprāyaścittam eva draṣṭavyam, tata ūrdhvam idam iti | puruṣatrayasaṃsargād arvāg api pūrvoktād ardham ardhaṃ parikalpyam | yathāha kaṇvaḥ |
vṛttasthāyāḥ striyāḥ sarvaṃ prāyaścittaṃ vidhīyate |
pratipūruṣaṃ pravṛttāyā ardhahīnaṃ bhavet tu tat || iti | idaṃ buddhipūrve | abuddhipūrve tu vyāghroktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
vyabhicāriṇyāḥ pāpārdhaṃ veśyānāṃ gamane bhavet |
caturṇām api varṇānāṃ nārī hatvānavasthitām ||
śaṅkhaśuktyajam eṣāṃś ca kramād dayād viśuddhaye || iti || 22.28 ||
jātimātrabrāhmaṇī brahmabandhūḥ | calanā vyabhicāriṇī | tasyāṃ hatāyāṃ nīlo deyaḥ | nīlo vṛṣa iti | manus tu varṇānupūrvyam āha |
nīlakārmukabastāvīḥ pṛthag dadyād viśuddhaye |
caturṇām api varṇānāṃ nārīr hatvānavasthitāḥ || [MDh 11.138] iti || 22.26 ||
vaiśike na kiṃcit || 22.29 ||
vaiśikena kiṃcit || 22.27 ||
vaiśyakarmaṇā jīvantyāṃ hatāyāṃ na kiṃcid api kartavyam | idam abuddhipūrve | buddhipūrve tu vyabhicāriṇyāṃ yad uktaṃ tadardhaṃ draṣṭavyam,
vyabhicāriṇyāḥ pāpārdhaṃ veśyānāṃ gamane bhavet |
caturṇām api varṇānām eṣa dharmaḥ sanātanaḥ ||
iti prajāpatidharmaliṅgāt | yad vā – veśyāsu kriyata iti vaiśikaṃ tat brahmabandhvā saha kṛte prakāśam ācarantyā maithune ācarite ity arthaḥ | na kiṃcit parastrīgamanaprāyaścittaṃ kartavyam ity arthaḥ | evaṃ ca gūḍham ācarantyā saha kṛte kiṃcit prāyaścittam astīti gamyate | tad api hiṃsāvat parikalpyam | atra paradāragamanaprāyaścitta-pratiṣedhād anyad asty evety avagamyate | tathāha śaṅkhaḥ |
paśuveśyāgamane prājāpatyaṃ caret dvijaḥ | iti |
tad api sakṛdgamane, abhyāse tu saṃvartoktaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha |
paśuveśyābhigamane prājāpatyaṃ samācaret |
gogame tu naraḥ kuryāt kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇottaram || iti | tasyām atyantābhyāse manunoktam upapātakaprāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha |
dhānyakupyapaśusteyaṃ madyapastrīniṣevaṇam | iti | [MDh 11.66] | tasyāṃ prasūtasya kaṇvoktaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha |
prasūto yas tu veśyāyāṃ bhaikṣabhuṅ niyatendriyaḥ |
śatasāhasram abhyasya sāvitrīm eti śuddhatām || iti |
brahmabandhvā saha vaiśikācaraṇe yad uktaṃ tad eva kṣatrabandhvādibhir api vaiśikācaraṇe draṣṭavyam | tathāha kavaṣaḥ |
brahmakṣatriyaviṭśūdrabandhubhir vaiśike kṛte |
prājāpatyaṃ cared vidvān abhyāse kṛcchram eva tu || iti | kṣatriyādīnām ardham ardhaṃ parikalpyam |
atra kecid vyācakṣate – brāhmaṇyāḥ śūdrasaṃparke pātityam uktam: “bhrūṇahani hīnavarṇasevāyāṃ ca strī patati” [GDh 21.9] iti | tasmāt yā brāhmaṇī śūdravarṇasaṃbandhā bhavati tayā sahānyeṣāṃ saṃbandhaṃ kurvatāṃ patitāgamanaprāyaścittam eva kartavyam iti |
tad ayuktam,
caṇḍālāntyastriyo gatvā bhuktvā ca pratigṛhya ca |
pataty ajñānato vipro jñānāt sāmyaṃ tu gacchati || [MDh 11.175] iti liṅgāt | tataś ca matyā śūdrādisaṃ-parke ‘pi brāhmaṇyādīnāṃ śūdrādisāmyam amatyā ca taddhīnatvam avagamyata iti | evam api yac chūdrayā avyabhicāriṇyā maithunācaraṇe prāyaścittaṃ tad eva brāhmaṇyāṃ śūdrasaṃbandhāyām api prasajyeteti cet – atrocyate, sāmyavacanān na bhavatīti | tasmād uktam eva prāyaścittam iti |
anye vyācakṣate – abuddhipūrvaṃ śūdrādisaṃparkaṃ yā karoti tayā gamane pātakaprāyaścittam, buddhipūrve tu śūdrābhigamanaprāyaścittam, “jñānāt sāmyaṃ tu gacchati” [MDh 11.175] iti vacanād iti |
tad apy ayuktam | atrāpi buddhipūrvakṛtād abuddhipūrvakṛtasya gurutvaprāptau tad api “saṃkalpena bhūyaḥ” [ĀpDh1.29.3] ityādibhir virudhyeteti | evaṃ ca sati “caṇḍālāntyastriyo gatvā” [MDh 11.175] ity asyāpy ayam evārtho veditavyaḥ | caṇḍālādibhir abuddhipūrvasaṃparke patito bhavati dvijātikarmabhir nādhikriyata ity arthaḥ, na tu brahmahatyādipāpam iti | tad uktam “dvijātir karmabhyo hāniḥ patanam” [GDh 21.4] iti | na jāter apagamaḥ, tasmāt prāyaścittam astīti | buddhipūrve tu jāter apy apagamo bhavati tasmāt prāyaścittaṃ nāstīti | “pataty ajñānataḥ” [MDh 11.175] ity anena dvijātikarmānadhikṛta ity uktaṃ na brahmahatyādipāpam | sāmyavacanāc ca yena saṃparkaṃ karoti buddhipūrvaṃ tena sāmyaṃ gacchati na prāyaścittenādhikriyata iti, sāmyadvāreṇa prāyaścittābhāva iti | brāhmaṇyādīnāṃ śūdrasaṃbandhe buddhipūrve ‘pi prajātāyā eva tat sāmyaṃ draṣṭavyam, aprajātāyāḥ śuddhipratipādanāt |
brāhmaṇakṣatriyaviśāṃ striyaḥ śūdreṇa saṃgatāḥ |
aprajātā viśudhyanti prāyaścittena netarāḥ || [VaDh 21.12] iti śūdreṇa saṃgatāyā aprajātāyāḥ śuddhipratipādanād adhojātisaṃgatāyā aśucitavṃ draṣṭavyam | brāhmaṇakṣatriyaviśām ity uktatvāt śūdrastriyāḥ prajātāyā api śuddhiḥ | anyac ca –
prajātāyāṃ pratipuruṣam ardhahīnaṃ bhavet tu tat |
ity atra brāhmaṇyāṃ kṣatriyasaṃparke ardhaṃ tadardhaṃ vaiśyasaṃbandhe tadardhaṃ śūdrasaṃbandha ity evaṃ parikalpyam | “madyapastrīniṣevaṇam” [MDh 11.66] ity atra codyate – brāhmaṇyā madyapāyā gamane patitāgamanaprāyaścittaṃ bhavitum arhati, nopapātakaprāyaścittaṃ madyapānena hi patitā bhavatīti |
atrocyate – pātityaṃ śūdratulyatvaṃ na brahmahatyāsamatvam, yathāha manuḥ |
yasya kāyagataṃ brahma madyenāplāvyate sakṛt |
tasya vyapaiti tad brahma śūdratvaṃ ca nigacchati || iti | [MDh 11.97] | ataḥ śūdratulyatvād uktam eva prāyaścittaṃ na patitāgamanaprāyaścittam iti | tathātrāpi coktam: “āryānāryayor vyatikṣepe karmaṇaḥ sāmyam” [GDh 10.66] iti | vyatikṣepas tu śūdrasyāpratiṣiddhaṃ madyapānaṃ brāhmaṇādīnāṃ tu pratiṣiddham | tatra śūdre madyapānavarjite brāhmaṇādau ca madyapānaprasakte sati tasmād dhetoḥ sāmyaṃ ca | evaṃ ca madyapo brāhmaṇādis tadvarjitaśūdratulya ity avagantavyaḥ | yady api śūdratulyaḥ tathāpy aspṛśya ity avagantavyaḥ, “patitacaṇḍāla” [GDh 14.29] ity atra patitagrahaṇena gṛhyamāṇatvāt | tadvarjitānāṁ grahaṇam iti cet, na, smṛtyantarasāmarthyāt | tathāha kaṇvaḥ |
brahmahā madyapaḥ steno gurutalpaga eva ca |
aspṛśyāḥ patitā hy ete taiś cāvacaritaḥ samaḥ ||
|| 22.29 ||
abhigacchati yā nārī puruṣair bahubhir mithaḥ |
vyabhicāriṇīti sā jñeyā pratyakṣaṃ gāṇiketi ca ||
iti prajāpatiḥ | vaiśikena veśyākarmaṇā jīvantyāṃ brahmabandhvāṃ hatāyāṃ kiṃcid deyam “aṣṭamuṣṭi bhavet kiṃcit” ity etat || 22.27 ||
talpānnadhanalābhavadheṣu pṛthag varṣāṇi || 22.30 || — || 22.28 ||
lābhavadhaśabdaḥ pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyate, talpalābhavadhe dhanalābhavadha ityādi | talpaśabdena śayanavācinā bhāryocyate | taddhetutvāl lakṣaṇayā kanyāyā atra grahaṇam | annaṃ kṛtam akṛtaṃ vā | dhanaṃ suvarṇagavādi | lābhaḥ prāptiḥ | vadho vighnakaraṇam | etad uktaṃ bhavati – kanyānnadhaneṣu labhyamāneṣu vighnaṃ pratiṣedhaṃ kṛtvā saṃvatsaraṃ brahmacaryaṃ pṛthak pṛthak kartavyam iti | buddhipūrva idam | abuddhipūrve tu uśanasoktaṃ draṣṭavyam: “kanyānnadhanavighne prājāpatyam” iti | brāhmaṇalābhavighnaṃ idam | kṣatriyādiṣv adharmadharmaṃ parikalpyam || 22.30 ||
talpaśabdena śayanavācinā bhāryā lakṣyate | annaṃ kṛtānnam | dhanaṃ suvarṇādi | eteṣāṃ lābhasya vadhe vighna eṣu labhyamāneṣu doṣopanyāsādinā yo hanti sa pṛthak pratinimittaṃ bhedena saṃvatsaraṃ prākṛtaṃ brahmacaryaṃ caret | “kanyānnadhanavighne prājāpatyam” ity auśanasam buddhipūrvaviṣayam | brāhmaṇalābhaviṣayam idam | kṣatriyādiṣv ardham ardham || 22.28 ||
talpānnadhanalābhavadheṣu pṛthag varṣāṇi || 22.30 || — || 22.28 ||
lābhavadhaśabdaḥ pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyate, talpalābhavadhe dhanalābhavadha ityādi | talpaśabdena śayanavācinā bhāryocyate | taddhetutvāl lakṣaṇayā kanyāyā atra grahaṇam | annaṃ kṛtam akṛtaṃ vā | dhanaṃ suvarṇagavādi | lābhaḥ prāptiḥ | vadho vighnakaraṇam | etad uktaṃ bhavati – kanyānnadhaneṣu labhyamāneṣu vighnaṃ pratiṣedhaṃ kṛtvā saṃvatsaraṃ brahmacaryaṃ pṛthak pṛthak kartavyam iti | buddhipūrva idam | abuddhipūrve tu uśanasoktaṃ draṣṭavyam: “kanyānnadhanavighne prājāpatyam” iti | brāhmaṇalābhavighnaṃ idam | kṣatriyādiṣv adharmadharmaṃ parikalpyam || 22.30 ||
talpaśabdena śayanavācinā bhāryā lakṣyate | annaṃ kṛtānnam | dhanaṃ suvarṇādi | eteṣāṃ lābhasya vadhe vighna eṣu labhyamāneṣu doṣopanyāsādinā yo hanti sa pṛthak pratinimittaṃ bhedena saṃvatsaraṃ prākṛtaṃ brahmacaryaṃ caret | “kanyānnadhanavighne prājāpatyam” ity auśanasam buddhipūrvaviṣayam | brāhmaṇalābhaviṣayam idam | kṣatriyādiṣv ardham ardham || 22.28 ||
dve paradāre || 22.31 || — || 22.29 ||
paradāragamane dve varṣe prākṛtabrahmacaryam | brāhmaṇīgamane garbhotpattāv idaṃ draṣṭavyam | garbhotpatteḥ prāk kṛcchracāndrāyaṇe | yathāha vyāghraḥ |
brāhmaṇo brāhmaṇīṃ gacched akāmāṃ yadi kāmataḥ |
kṛcchracāndrāyaṇe kuryād ardham eva pramādataḥ ||
ardham eva sakāmāyāṃ taptakṛcchraṃ sakṛd gatau |
ardham ardhaṃ nṛpādīnāṃ dāreṣu brāhmaṇaś caret ||
etad vrataṃ caret sārdhaṃ śrotriyasya parigrahe |
