aśrotriyaś cet dviguṇaṃ guptāyām ardham eva ca || iti | kṣatriyasya dāre garbhotpattau “dve paradāre” [GDh 22.31] ity asyārdhaṃ draṣṭavyam, “ardham ardhaṃ nṛpādīnām” iti liṅgāt | vaiśyadāre manunoktam upapātakaprāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam, “etad eva vrataṃ kuryuḥ” [MDh 11.117] ityādi | śūdrasya dāre atikṛcchram, kaṇvavacanāt |
śūdradārān gato vipro hy atikṛcchraṃ samācaret |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ viśo rājñaḥ samaṃ tu brahmaṇi vratam || iti | yadi brāhmaṇenaiva cāturvarṇyaprasūtāḥ krameṇa nirviṣṭāḥ tadānīṃ brāhmaṇasya brāhmaṇīgamane yad uktaṃ tad eva pādahīnaṃ kṣatriyādigamane draṣṭavyam, vyāghravacanāt:
vipreṇaiva vinirviṣṭāś cāturvarṇyaprasūtakāḥ |
krameṇa pādato hīnaṃ vrataṃ tāsu gataś caret || iti |
ayam eva nyāyaḥ kṣatriyādiṣv api draṣṭavyaḥ | brāhmaṇabhāryāṃ śūdrāṃ brāhmaṇo gatvā prājāpatyaṃ kuryāt, vasiṣṭhavacanāt: “brāhmaṇaś ced aprekṣāpūrvakaṃ brāhmaṇadārān adhigacchen nivṛttadharmakarmaṇaḥ kṛcchro ‘nivṛttadharma-karmaṇo ‘tikṛcchraḥ” [VaDh 21.16] iti | idam abuddhipūrve sakṛdgamane | buddhipūrve tu dviguṇam, “ardham eva pramādataḥ” iti liṅgāt | abhyāse tu pādahīnanyāyo draṣṭavyaḥ | brāhmaṇasya kṣatriyādibhāryāgamane yad uktaṃ tad eva kṣatriyādīnāṃ svajātibhāryāgamane ‘pi draṣṭavyam | kutaḥ?
vipro nṛpasya bhāryāyāṃ yat karoti samāgame |
tad eva kṣatriyaś cāpi kuryāt tatraiva saṃgataḥ ||
iti prajāpatidharmaliṅgāt || 22.31 ||
paradāragamane dve varṣe prākṛtaṃ brahmacaryam | ṛtukālagamane buddhipūrva idam | akāminaḥ punar etad evārdhakḷptyā yojyam || 22.29 ||
trīṇi śrotriyasya || 22.32 || — || 22.30 ||
garbhotpattāv evāsyāṃ samāgame | garbhotpatteḥ prāg apy uktam:
etad vrataṃ caret sārdhaṃ śrotriyasya parigrahe | iti | [Vyāghra under 22.31]
garbhotpatter ūrdhvam etad eva ā prajāyā draṣṭavyam | bahuśo ‘bhyāse samānaprāyaścittatvam ayuktam iti cet, atrocyate – strīṇāṃ garbhotpatteḥ prāk bahuśo ‘bhyāse ‘pi samānatvaṃ dṛśyate,
brāhmaṇakṣatriyaviśāṃ striyaḥ śūdreṇa saṃgatāḥ | [VaDh 21.12]
ity atra | tadvad atrāpi na doṣa iti | tathā kavaṣavacanam api |
garbhotpattau yad uktaṃ tat tad evāmaraṇāc caret |
brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyaś cāpi vaiśyaś śūdras tathaiva ca || iti |
japādibhir vā pratipūraṇaṃ kartavyam | tad uktaṃ ca mānave |
yasmin karmaṇy asya kṛte manasaḥ syād alāghavam | [MDh 11.233] ityādi | tatraikasyāṃ duṣṭaḥ kṛtaprāyaścittaḥ saṃvatsare ‘tīte punar api tasyām eva yadi duṣṭo bhavati tadā pūrvaprāyaścittāt dviguṇaṃ tena kartavyam | kutaḥ?
saṃvatsarābhiśastasya duṣṭasya dviguṇo damaḥ | [MDh 8.373] iti daṇḍadarśanāt | saṃvatsaraprabhṛti tasya tāvad eveti draṣṭavyam | saṃvatsarād arvāk prathame māsi dvādaśaguṇitasyaiva māsān māsi [māsānumāsi?] bhāgakalpaneti bhāṣyakāreṇa vyākyātam [=Bhāruci] | pratimāsaṃ traimāsikaṃ pūraṇaṃ kalpyam | kṛtaprāyaścittasyaivedam, abhiśastasya duṣṭasyeti padadvayopādānāt | akṛtaprāyaścittasya tu japādibhir eva pūraṇam | tatrātyantaśrotriyabrāhmaṇasya bhāryāṃ prajāvatīṃ pativratāṃ balād avaṣṭabhya kṛtasyācaturthād upagamanāt prāk pātityaṃ draṣṭavyam, āpastamba-vacanāt: “savarṇāyām anyapūrvāyāṃ sakṛt saṃnipāte pādaḥ patatīty upadiśanti | evam abhyāse pādaḥ pādaḥ| caturthe sarvam” [ĀpDh 2.27.11–13] iti || 22.32 ||
pūrvokta eva viṣaye śrotriyasya brāhmaṇasya dārān gacchatas trīṇi varṣāṇi brahmacaryam | atrāpy akāmato ‘rdham | atra śaṅkhaḥ: “vaiśyāyām avakīrṇaḥ saṃvatsaraṃ brahmacaryaṃ triṣavaṇaṃ cānutiṣṭhet | kṣatriyāyāṃ dve varṣe | trīṇi brāhmaṇyām | vaiśyāvac ca śūdrāyāṃ brāhmaṇapariṇītāyām” iti | saṃvartaḥ |
śūdrāṃ tu brāhmaṇo gatvā māsaṃ māsārdham eva vā |
gomūtrayāvakāhāras tiṣṭhet tatpāpamokṣakaḥ || iti |
kāmato māsam akāmato ‘rdhamāsam iti vyavasthito vikalpaḥ | anṛtukāle tu brāhmaṇyādidvijātiṣu mānavāni traimāsikadvaimāsikacāndrāyaṇāni | kṣatriyādīnāṃ ca kṣatriyādistrīṣu brāhmaṇavat prāyaścittam | atra uśanā |
gamane tu vrataṃ yat syād garbhe taddviguṇaṃ caret || iti || 22.30 ||
dravyalābhe cotsargaḥ || 22.33 || — || 22.31 ||
yadi ca parastrīto dravyaṃ labdhaṃ tasyotsargaḥ kāryaḥ | tyāgaś ca vinā pratigrahadharmeṇa parityāgaḥ | caśabdād yad annādy upabhuktaṃ tad api pratipādayet || 22.33 ||
yadi ca parastrīto yat kiṃcid dravyaṃ labdhaṃ tasyotsargas tyāgaḥ kāryaḥ || 22.31 ||
yathāsthānaṃ vā gamayet || 22.34 || — || 22.32 ||
svāmina eva vā bhaktādikaṃ kenacid vyājena pratipādayed ity arthaḥ || 22.34 ||
yatra snāne labdhaṃ tad vā gamayet || 22.32 ||
pratiṣiddhamantrayoge sahasravākaś cet || 22.35 || — || 22.33 ||
pratiṣiddhasyāpraśastadvijāteḥ mantrayogaḥ yājanādhyāpanakriyā, tasmin kṛte saṃvatsaraṃ prākṛtaṃ brahmacaryam | ajñānād idam, itaratra patitatvaṃ syād iti | yadi cāsau mantrayogaḥ sahasravākaḥ syāt | vākaśabdena padam ucyate | sahasraśabdo ‘pi bahutvavacanaḥ | ato yadi mahāntaṃ grantham abhyasyatīty arthaḥ | evaṃ cālpe pūrvoktasahitādhyayanaṃ draṣṭavyam | patitaviṣayam idam | upapātakiviṣaye tu vasiṣṭhoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
patitacaṇḍālaśavaśrāvaṇe trirātraṃ vāgyatā anaśnanta āsīran sahasraparamaṃ vā tad abhyasyantaḥ pūtā bhavantīti vijñāyata ity etenaiva garhitādhyāpakayājakā vyākhyātā dakṣiṇātyāgāc ca pūtā bhavantīti vijñāyate || iti | [VaDh 23.34–36] | atrāpy ajñānāt trirātraṃ vāgyatair anaśnadbhir bhavitavyaṃ sahasravākād arvāk | ūrdhvaṃ tu yāvad adhyāpitaṃ tāvataḥ sahasraparamo japo haviṣyāśino draṣṭavyaḥ | jñānaviṣaye tu sahasravākād ūrdhvam upapātakitā draṣṭavyā | tad uktaṃ ca “tanmantrakṛt” iti | tato ‘rvāk sahasratrayajapo draṣṭavyaḥ, “pratiṣiddhakṛtam anuvākaṃ sahasratrayam abhyaset” ity auśanasavacanāt |
anye tu mantrayogaṃ sahādhyayanaṃ sahayājanaṃ vācakṣate, tataś ca pratiṣiddhena sahaikatrādhyayanaṃ sahayājanaṃ ca kṛtvā prākṛtaṃ brahmacaryam iti |
atrāpi pūrvoktaviṣayabhedena tad eva prāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam, sahādhyayanayājanayos tulyatvād iti || 22.35 ||
mantrayoge ye pratiṣiddhāḥ patitādayas taiḥ saha mantrayoge ‘dhyayanādhyāpanayājyayājanalakṣaṇe saṃvatsaraṃ prākṛtaṃ brahmacaryaṃ sa cen mantrayogaḥ sahasravāko bhavati | vaktīti vākaḥ padam | sahasrapadaś cet | abuddhipūrva idam | buddhipūrve tu patitatvaṃ syād iti | upapātake tu vāsiṣṭham: “patitacaṇḍālaśavasūtakaśravaṇe tu trirātraṃ vāgyatā āsīran sahasrāvaraṃ vā tad abhyasyantaḥ pūtā bhavantīti vijñāyate | etenaiva garhitādhyāpakayājakā vyākhyātāḥ | dakṣiṇātyāgāc ca pūtā bhavantīti vijñāyate” [VaDh 23.34–36] iti | anye tu sahādhyayanaṃ sahayajanaṃ ca mantrayogaṃ vyācakṣate || 22.33 ||
agnyutsādinirākṛtyupapātakeṣu caivam || 22.36 || — || 22.34 ||
yasya deśopaplavādinā śrautāgnīnāṃ bahukālaṃ viyogo bhavati so ‘gnyutsādī | nirākṛtir uktaḥ | nāsau upapātakatvena gṛhyate, durvālād ūrdhvam abhihitatvāt | utsṛṣṭāgnis tv anya eva vyākhyātaḥ śrautānām eva nāstikyena parityakteti | atraivaṃ viṣayavibhāgo draṣṭavyaḥ – yo nāstikyena karmāṇi parityajati, deśopaplavādinā vā bahukālaṃ gacchati, tasya saṃvatsaraṃ prākṛtaṃ brahmacaryam, alpakāle tu prājāpatyam | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “yo ‘gnīn apavidhyet kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caritvā punar ādhānaṃ kārayet” [VaDh 1.23] iti | ya ālasyādinā nāstikyadeśopaplavādyantareṇāpi parityajati tasya cāndrāyaṇaṃ draṣṭavyam, yathāha manuḥ |
agnihotry apavidhyāgnīn brāhmaṇaḥ kāmakārataḥ |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ caren māsaṃ vīrahatyāsamaṃ hi tat || iti | [MDh 11.41]
māsam apaviddhasyedam, tasmād uttaraṃ kṛcchrottaraṃ draṣṭavyam,
agnihotry apavidhyāgnīn māsād ūrdhvaṃ tu kāmataḥ |
kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caiva kuryād evāvicārayan ||
iti vacanāt | māsād arvāg api cāndrāyaṇam eva, “vispaṣṭārthaṃ vā māsagrahaṇam” iti bhāṣyakāreṇoktatvāt | sarvatra punarādhānam atītakālahomaś ca draṣṭavyaḥ | tathā ca jātukarṇiḥ |
atītakālaṃ juhuyād agnau viprāya vā vyayam |
naṣṭe ‘gnau vidhivad dadyāt kṛtvādhānaṃ punar dvijaḥ || iti | idaṃ vākyaṃ śraute smārte ca draṣṭavyam | evaṃ ca bahukālātikramaṇe prāyaścittasya gauravaṃ saṃpadyata iti nātra japādipūraṇaṃ kartavyam | smārtasya tu punar ādhānam atītakālahomaś caiva, na prāyaścittāntaram asti | nāstikye tu prājāpatyaṃ draṣṭavyam | tathāha vyāghraḥ |
yo ‘gnīṃs tyajati nāstikyāt prājāpatyaṃ cared dvijaḥ |
anyatra punarādhānaṃ dānam eva tathaiva ca || iti |
nirākṛter anye ye cāpāṅktatvena abhihitā upapātakavarjitās teṣāṃ manunoktaṃ ca draṣṭavyam |
ṣaṣṭhānnakālatā māsaṃ saṃhitājapa eva ca |
homāś ca śālakā nityam apāṅktānāṃ viśodhanam || iti | [MDh 11.200]
upapātakeṣv iti bahuvacanaṃ smṛtyantaroktānām upalakṣaṇārtham | yathāha manuḥ |
govadho ‘yājyasaṃyājyapāradāryātmavikrayāḥ |
gurumātṛpitṛtyāgaḥ svādhyāyāgnyoḥ sutasya ca ||
parivittatā cānujena parivedanam eva ca |
tayor dānaṃ kanyāyās tayor eva ca yājanam ||
kanyāyā dūṣaṇaṃ caiva vārdhuṣyaṃ vratalopanam |
taṭākārāmadārāṇām apatyasya ca vikrayaḥ ||
vrātyatā bānadhavatyāgo bhṛtādhyāpanam eva ca |
bhṛtāc cādhyayanādānam apaṇyānāṃ ca vikrayaḥ ||
sarvākareṣv adhīkāro mahāyantrapravartanam |
hiṃsauṣadhīnāṃ stryājīvo ‘bhicāro mūlakarma ca ||
indhanārtham aśuṣkāṇāṃ drumāṇām avapātanam |
ātmārthaṃ ca kriyārambho ninditānnādanaṃ tathā ||
anāhitāgnitā stainyam ṛṇānāṃ cānapakriyā |
asacchāstrādhigamanaṃ kauśīlavyasya ca kriyā ||
dhānyakupyapaśusteyaṃ madyapastrīniṣevaṇam |
strīśūdraviṭkṣatravadho nāstikyaṃ copapātakam || iti | [MDh 11.59–65]
atra keṣāṃcid āhatya prāyaścittaṃ smṛtyantare śrūyate | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ:
parivittaḥ kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caritvā niviśeta tāṃ caivopayacchet | atha parivividānaḥ kṛcchrātikṛcchrau caritvā tasmai datvā punar niviśeta tāṃ caivopayacchet | agredidhiṣūpatiḥ kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caritvā niviśeta tāṃ caivopayacchet | didhiṣūpatiḥ kṛcchrātikṛcchrau caritvā tasmai datvā punar niviśet | [VaDh 20.7–10]
ityādi | govadhe atrāpy uktam, ayājyasaṃyājane ‘py uktaṃ pāradārye ‘py uktaṃ svādhyāyāgneḥ parityāge ‘py uktam | kanyādūṣaṇe ca vasiṣṭha āha: “gurvīsakhīṃ gurusakhīm apapātrāṃ patitāṃ ca gatvā kṛcchrābdapādaṃ caret” [VaDh 20.16] iti | etad akāmakṛte draṣṭavyam | buddhipūrve tu,
gurutalpavrataṃ kuryād retaḥ siktvā svayoniṣu |
sakhyuḥ putrasya ca strīṣu kumārīṣv antyajāsu ca || [MDh 11.170] iti manunoktaṃ gurutalpavrataṃ draṣṭavyam | yady api sāmānyenoktam, tathāpi jātiviśeṣāt prāyaścittaviśeṣo draṣṭavyaḥ: “brāhmaṇakanyāyāṃ sarvaṃ kṣatriyakanyāyām ardham” ityādi | vrātyatāyāṃ ca vasiṣṭhoktam udāhṛtam: “patitasāvitrīka uddālakavrataṃ caret” [VaDh 11.76] ityādi | manur apy āha |
yeṣāṃ dvijānāṃ sāvitrī nānūcyeta yathāvidhi |
tāṃś cārayitvā trīn kṛcchrān yathāvidhy upanāyayet || iti | [MDh 11.191] |tatra bahukālātikrama uddālakavratam, madhyame kṛcchratrayam, svalpakālātikrame vrātyastomayāga iti draṣṭavyam | bhṛtakādhyāpanam “vedaviplāvakam” [GDh 20.1] ity uktam | bahuśaḥ kṛte pātityaṃ tatra tyāgaś coktaḥ “tyajet pitaram” [GDh 20.1] ityādi | anyatra manur āha:
śaraṇāgataṃ parityajya vedaṃ viplāvya ca dvijaḥ |
saṃvatsaraṃ yavāhāras tat pāpam apasedhati || iti | [MDh 11.198] | abhicārasyāpi manur āha |
vrātyānāṃ yājanaṃ kṛtvā pareṣām antyakarma ca |
abhicāram ahīnaṃ ca tribhiḥ kṛcchrair vyapohati || iti | [MDh 11.197]
dvijātivadhe idam | śūdrādivadhe tu “śūdram abhicārya prājāpatyaṃ caret” iti paiṭhīnasivacanāt | ninditānnādane tu vakṣyati: “abhojyabhojane niṣpurīṣībhāvaḥ” [GDh 23.23] ityādi | tatra buddhipūrvābhyāse surāpānasamatvam | yathāha manuḥ |
ninditānādyayor jagdhiḥ surāpānasamāni ṣaṭ | iti |
[MDh 11.56] | evaṃ cābuddhipūrvābhyāse upapātakaṃ veditavyam | evaṃ yeṣām upapātakānām āhatya vidhānaṃ nāsti teṣām idaṃ saṃvatsaraṃ prākṛtaṃ brahmacaryaṃ draṣṭavyam | abhyāsaviṣayaṃ cedam | itaratra manunoktam |
etad eva vrataṃ kuryur upapātakino dvijāḥ |
avakīrṇivarjaṃ śuddhyarthaṃ cāndrāyaṇam athāpi vā || iti | [MDh 11.117] | tatrāpy abuddhipūrve cāndrāyanam itaratra govadhaprāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam | cakārād evātideśe siddhe evaṃkārakaraṇaṃ samastātideśārtham | tataś ca “dravyalābhe cotsargaḥ” [GDh 22.33] ityādi draṣṭavyam || 22.36 ||
agnim utsādayituṃ śīlam asyeti buddhipūrvam agnyutsādī | nirākṛtiḥ śaktau satyām anadhyetā | upapātakāni, apaṅktyānāṃ prāg durvālād gohantṛbrahamahatyādīni vyākhyātāni [GDh 21.11] | eṣv agnyutsādyādiṣv evaṃ saṃvatsaraṃ brahmacaryam iti | yo nāstikyād deśopaplavādinā vāgnīn apavidhyati punas tacchāntāv api bahukālaṃ nādhatte tadviṣayam idam | tatraivālpakāle vāsiṣṭham: “yo ‘gnīn apavidhyāt kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caritvā punar ādadhīta” [VaDh 21.27] | ālasyena tyajato mānavam |
agnihotry apavidhyāgnīn brāhmaṇaḥ kāmakārataḥ |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ caren māsaṃ vīrahatyāsamaṃ hi tat || iti | [MDh 11.41] | māsam apavidhyety anvayaḥ |
agnihotry apavidhyāgīn māsād ūrdhvaṃ tu kāmataḥ |
kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caiva kuryād atrāvicārayan || iti | māsād arvāg api cāndrāyaṇam icchanti | smārte tv agnau,
yo ‘gniṃ tyajati nāstikyāt prājāpatyaṃ cared dvijaḥ |
anyatra punarādhānaṃ dānam eva tathaiva ca || iti |
mānavaṃ tu |
ṣaṣṭhānnakālatā māsaṃ saṃhitājapa eva ca |
homaś ca śākalair nityam apaṅktyānāṃ viśodhanam || [MDh 11.200] iti || 22.34 ||
strī cāticāriṇī guptā piṇḍaṃ tu labheta || 22.37 || — || 22.35 ||
H reads “yāticāriṇī”
strī ca etad eva prāyaścittaṃ kuryāt | aticāriṇī vyabhicāriṇī | śūdravyabhicāra uktatvāt dvijātivyabhicāra idam | buddhipūrve sakṛdgamane ced, anyatra “yat puṃsaḥ paradāreṣu” [MDh 11.176] ity uktatvāt, samānajātivyabhicāra idam | brāhmaṇyāḥ kṣatriyavyabhicāre tu vasiṣṭhoktaṃ draṣṭavyam: “vyavāye tu saṃvatsaraṃ ghṛtapaṭaṃ dhārayet | gomayagarte kuśaprastare vā śayītordhvaṃ saṃvatsarād apsu nimagnāyāḥ sāvitryaṣṭasahasreṇa śirobhir juhuyāt pūtā bhavati” [VaDh 21.8] iti | vaiśyagamane tv auśanasaṃ draṣṭavyam: “vyabhicāriṇī kṛcchrābdaṃ caret” iti | uttamābhigamane ca nīcāyām ardham ardhaṃ parikalpyam | tathā saṃbhāṣaṇe ca śrūyate:
manasā bhartur aticāre trirātraṃ yāvakaṃ kṣīrodanaṃ vā bhuñjānādhaḥśayītordhvaṃ trirātrād apsu nimagnāyāḥ sāvitryaṣṭaśatena śirobhir juhuyāt pūtā bhavatīti vijñāyate | vāksaṃbandha etad eva māsaṃ caritvordhvaṃ māsād apsu nimagnāyāḥ sāvitryāś caturbhir aṣṭaśataiḥ śirobhir juhuyāt | [VaDh 21.6–7] iti |
idam api sakṛdbhāṣaṇe | abhyāse tu manunoktam, punaḥśabdaśravaṇāt | yathāha |
sā cet punaḥ saṃpraduṣyet sadṛśenopayantritā |
kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caiva tad asyāḥ pāvanaṃ smṛtam || iti | [MDh 11.177]
hīnasaṃbhāṣaṇe taddviguṇam, uttamasaṃbhāṣaṇe ardhaṃ draṣṭavyam | evaṃ vyabhicāriṇī yā na śuddhā sā guptā rakṣyamāṇā piṇḍaṃ prāṇadhāraṇamātram annaṃ labheta | tuśabdāt kaupīnācchādanārthaṃ vāsaś ca labheta || 22.37 ||
yā strī bhartāram aticarati vyabhicarati puruṣāntareṇa saṃgacchate sā caitad eva prāyaścittaṃ kuryāt saṃvatsaraṃ brahmacaryam | sā ca yāvat samāpyate prāyaścittaṃ tāvad guptā satī piṇḍamātraṃ labhate | buddhipūrve sakṛdgamana idam | anyatra,
yat puṃsaḥ paradāreṣu tac caitāṃ cārayed vratam | [MDh 11.176] ity etat | sajātīyaviṣaye cedam | brāhmaṇyāḥ kṣatriyaviṣaye vāsiṣṭham: “vyavāye saṃvatsaraṃ ghṛtapaṭaṃ dhārayed gomayakardame kuśaprastare vā bhuñjānādhaḥ śayītordhaṃ saṃvatsarād apsu nimagnāyāḥ sāvitryaṣṭasahasreṇa śirobhir juhuyāt” [VaDh 21.8] iti | vaiśyaviṣaye tv auśanasam: “vyabhicāriṇī kṛcchrābdaṃ caret” iti | atra bṛhatprecetāḥ |
viprāḥ śūdreṇa saṃpṛktā na caitasmāt prasūyate |
prāyaścittaṃ smṛtaṃ tasyāḥ kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇatrayam ||
cāndrāyaṇe dve kṛcchraṃ ca viprāyā vaiśyasaṃgame |
kṛcchracāndrāyaṇe syātāṃ tasyāḥ kṣatriyasaṃgame ||
kṣatriyā śūdrasaṃparke kṛcchre cāndrāyaṇadvayam |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ sakṛcchraṃ ca cared vaiśyena saṃgatā ||
śūdraṃ gatvā cared vaiśyā kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇottaram |
ānulomyena kurvīta kṛcchraṃ pādāvaropitam || iti |
prajātāyā brāhmaṇyāś caturviṃśatimate viśeṣaḥ |
vipragarbhe parākaḥ syāt kṣatriyasya tathaindavam |
aindavaṃ ca parākaś ca vaiśyasyākāmakārataḥ ||
śūdragarbhe bhavet tyāgaś caṇḍālo jāyate yataḥ |
garbhasrāve dhātudoṣaiś carec cāndrāyaṇatrayam || iti | kāmakāre punaḥ parākādikaṃ dviguṇaṃ kuryāt | vasiṣṭhas tu |
brāhmaṇakṣatriyaviśāṃ bhāryāḥ śūdreṇa saṃgatāḥ |
aprajātā viśudhyanti prāyaścittena netarāḥ || [VaDh 21.12] āhitapatigarbhāyās tu paścāc chūdrādisaṃgame |
antarvatnī tu yā nārī sametākramya kāminā |
prāyaścittaṃ na sā kuryād yāvad garbho na niḥsṛtaḥ ||
jāte garbhe vrataṃ paścāt kuryān māsaṃ tu yāvakam |
na garbhadoṣas tatrāsti saṃskāryaḥ sa yathāvidhi ||
iti smṛtyantaroktaṃ draṣṭavyam | yā tu dauḥśīlyāt prāyaścittaṃ na karoti tadā,
prātilomye vadhaḥ puṃsāṃ strīṇāṃ nāsādikartanam | [YDh 2.286]
ity etad bhavati |
hīnavarṇopabhuktā yā sāṅkyā vadhyātha vā bhavet |
iti parāśaraḥ | aṅkanaṃ puṃliṅgena || 22.35 ||
amānuṣīṣu govarjaṃ strīkṛte kūśmāṇḍair ghṛtahomo ghṛtahomaḥ || 22.38 || — || 22.36 ||
govarjitāsv amānuṣīṣu vaḍavādyāsu strīkṛte maithune ācarite kūśmāṇḍaiḥ “yad devā devaheḍanam” ityādibhir ghṛtena homaḥ kartavyaḥ abhyāsād dhomāvṛttir draṣṭavyā || 22.38 ||
govarjitāsv amānuṣīṣu mahiṣādistrīṣu strīkṛte maithuna ācarite kūṣmāṇḍair ghṛtahomaḥ kartavyaḥ | govarjam iti vacanaṃ vispaṣṭārtham | vakṣyati “gavi ca gurutalpasamaḥ” [GDh 23.12] iti | tataś ca tad eva gogamane bhaviṣyati | sakṛdgamana idam | abhyāse śaṅkhoktam: “paśuveśyābhigamane prājāpatyam” iti | atra kaṇvaḥ |
prasūto yas tu veṣyāyāṃ bhaikṣabhuk saṃyatendriyaḥ |
śatasāhasram abhyasya sāvitrīm eti śuddhatām || iti |
dviruktir uktā || 22.36 ||
atha trayoviṃśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
adhunā kramaprāptaṃ surāpānaprāyaścittam āha |
kramaprāptaṃ surāpānaprāyaścittam āha |
surāpasya brāhmaṇasyoṣṇām āsiñceyuḥ surām āsye mṛtaḥ śudhyet || 23.1 ||
surāśabdenātra triprakārā surā gṛhyate, brāhmaṇagrahaṇāt, taṃ prati triprakārāyāś ca pratiṣiddhatvāt | yathāha manuḥ |
gauḍī mādhvī ca paiṣṭī ca vijñeyā trividhā surā |
yathaivaikā na pātavyā tathā sarvā dvijottamaiḥ || iti | [MDh 11.94] | “amatyā pāne” [GDh 23.2] iti vakṣyamāṇatvān matipūrva idam iti cāvagamyate | kṣatriyavaiśyayor api paiṣṭīpana idam eva prāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam, tayos tasyā eva pratiṣiddhatvāt | yathāha manuḥ |
surā vai malam annānāṃ pāpmā ca malam ucyate |
tasmād brāhmaṇarājanyau vaiśyaś ca na pibet surām || iti | [MDh 11.93] tathā |
surāṃ pītvā dvijo mohād agnivarṇāṃ surāṃ pibet |
tayā sa kāye nirdagdhe mucyate kilbiṣāṭ tataḥ || iti | [MDh 11.90] | prāyaścittavidhāne dvijagrahaṇāt | yasya yā pratiṣidhyate tasya tatpāne buddhipūrve tasyā evāgnivarṇāyāḥ pānam eva prāyaścittam ity avagamyate | atrāpi brāhmaṇasya gauḍīmādhvyor buddhipūrvābhyāse trayāṇāṃ paiṣṭyā buddhipūrve sakṛtpāne cedaṃ draṣṭavyam | kutaḥ? gauḍīmadhvyoḥ matipūrve sakṛtpāne prāyaścittāntaradarśanāt | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “madyapāne tv asurāyāḥ surāyāś cājñāne kṛcchrātikṛcchau ghṛtaprāśanaṃ punaḥsaṃskāraś ca” [VaDh 20.19] iti | atra “surāyāś cājñāne” ity uktatvān madyapānasya matipūrvatvam avagamyate | atikṛcchraṃ cātra taptakṛcchram, smṛtyantaradarśanāt, “madyaṃ pītvā kṛcchraṃ taptakṛcchraṃ vātiṣṭhet” iti paiṭhīnasivacanāt | uṣṇām agnivarṇām iti draṣṭavyam, “mṛtaḥ śudhyet” ityārambhāt | āsiñceyuḥ ābhimukhyenopadiśeyur ity arthaḥ | hetukartur vyapadeśaḥ na sākṣāt kartuḥ, pānasya kartā svayam eveti | yathāha āpastambaḥ: “surāpo ‘gnivarṇāṃ surāṃ pibet” [ĀpDh 1.25.3] iti | āsiñceyur iti bahuvacanam upadeṣṭṝṇāṃ bahutvasūcanārtham | tathāha manuḥ |
te yāṃ vedavido brūhus trayo ‘py enaḥsu niṣkṛtim |
sā teṣāṃ pāvanāya syāt pavitrā viduṣāṃ hi vāk || iti | [MDh 11.85] |
śaṅkho ‘py āha |
parṣadānumataḥ samyag eno vikhyāpya tattvataḥ |
yad brūyus tadvidhānajñās tacchuddhikaram uttamam || iti | āsye mukhe, mṛtaḥ śuddhyet maraṇād eva pūto bhavati | “surām asye” ity atra surāśabdo gomūtrādīnām apy upalakṣaṇārthaḥ | yathāha manuḥ |
gomūtram agnivarṇaṃ vā pibed udakam eva vā |
payo ghṛtaṃ vāmaraṇāt gośakṛdrasam eva vā || iti | [MDh 11.91] | jātivacanatvāt brāhmaṇaśabdasya brāhmaṇyā api surāpāna etad eva prāyaścittam | yathā śaṅkhaḥ striya evādhikṛtyāha: “surālaśunapalāṇḍu-gṛñjanamāṃsādīny abhakṣyāṇi varjayed āhāramayaṃ śarīraṃ tanmayatvād vā saṃkīrtyate” ityādi | tathā vasiṣṭho ‘pi:
pataty ardhaṃ śarīrasya yasya bhāryā surāṃ pibet |
patitārdhaśarīrasya niṣkṛtir na vidhīyate || iti | [VaDh 21.15] | tathā tatraiva: “yā brāhmaṇī surāpā na tāṃ devāḥ patilokaṃ nayanti iha vai sā bhramati kṣīṇapuṇyāpsu jalūkā bhavati śuktikā vā” [VaDh 21.11] iti |
anye tu deśāntarācāradarśanāṭ,
pūrvaṃ striyaḥ surair bhuktāḥ somagandharmavahnibhiḥ |
gacchanti mānuṣān paścāt tasmān niṣkalmaṣāḥ striyaḥ ||
tāsāṃ somo ‘dadac chaucaṃ gandharvaḥ śikṣitāṃ giram |
agniś ca sarvabhakṣyatvaṃ tasmān niṣkalmaṣāḥ striyaḥ || [VaDh 28.5–6] iti vasiṣṭhasmṛtidarśanāc ca brāhmaṇyāḥ naitat prāyaścittam icchanti |
tatra deśavyavasthayā vyavasthitavikalpo draṣṭavyaḥ || 23.1 ||
trividhā surā | yathāha manuḥ |
gauḍī mādhvī ca paiṣṭī ca vijñeyā trividhā surā |
yathaivaikā na pātavyā tathā sarvā dvijottamaiḥ || iti | [MDh 11.94] | dvijottamā brāhmaṇāḥ | kṣatriyavaiśyayos tu paiṣṭy eva | yathā sa evāha |
surā vai malam annānāṃ pāpmā ca malam ucyate |
tasmād brāhmaṇarājanyau vaiśyaś ca na surāṃ pibet || iti | [MDh 11.93] | annānāṃ malaṃ surā paiṣṭī | atra brāhmaṇagrahaṇaṃ dvijātyupalakṣaṇam | yasya yā pratiṣiddhā surā tasyāḥ pātā surāpaḥ | tasya dvijāter āsye tām eva surām uṣṇām āsiñceyuḥ | upadeṣṭṛṣv ayam āsecanāropaḥ | āsiñcanty eva hi te surām āsye | yena surāpeṇa suroṣṇā pātavyā tasyeyaṃ niṣkṛtir ity upadiśantīti | svayam eva tv āsecanakartā | tathā ca āpastambaḥ: “surāpo ‘gnisparśāṃ surāṃ pibet” [ĀpDh 1.25.3] iti | āsiñceyur iti bahuvacanam upadeṣṭṝṇāṃ bahutvaṃ sūcayati | manur apy āha |
teṣāṃ vedavido brūyus trayo ‘py enaḥsu niṣkṛtim |
sā teṣāṃ pāvanā yasmāt pavitraṃ viduṣāṃ hi vāk || iti | [MDh 11.85] | mṛtaḥ śudhyed ity vacanāt tathā surā tāpayitavyā yathā pātur maraṇaṃ bhavati | “ārdravāsāḥ pibet” iti paiṭhīnasiḥ | “āyasena tāmreṇa vā pātreṇa” iti precatāḥ | atha yājñavalkyaḥ |
surāmbughṛtagomūtrapayasām agisaṃnibham |
surāpo ‘nyatamaṃ pītvā maraṇāc chuddhim ṛcchati ||
vālavāsā jaṭī vāpi brahmahatyāvrataṃ caret |
piṇyākaṃ vā kaṇān vāpi bhakṣayet tu samāṃ niśi || iti | [YDh 4.253–254]
tatra maraṇāntikaprāyaścittaṃ buddhipūrvābhyāsaviṣayam | tatraiva sakṛtpānaviṣayaṃ brahmahatyāvrataṃ dvādaśavārṣikam | atra striyo ‘dhikṛtya śaṅkhaḥ: “surālaśunapalāṇḍugṛñjanamāṃsādīny abhakṣyāṇi varjayed āhāramayaṃ śarīram” iti | vasiṣṭho ‘pi |
pataty ardhaṃ śarīrasya bhāryā yasya surāṃ pibet |
patitārdhaśarīrasya nikṣtir na vidhīyate || iti | [VaDh 21.15] | atra strīṇām api pratiṣiddhā surā | prāyaścittaṃ ca bhavati | tatra strīṇām ardhaprāyaścittam ity uktaṃ purastāt | tatra maraṇāntike ‘rdhakḷpter aśakyatvād buddhipūrvasakṛtpāne dvādaśavārṣikasyārdham | abhyāse tasyaivābhyāsaḥ || 23.1 ||
amatyā pāne payo ghṛtam udakaṃ vāyuṃ pratitryahaṃ taptāni sa kṛcchraḥ || 23.2 ||
amatyā pāne payo ghṛtam udakaṃ vāyuṃ pratitryahaṃ taptāni sa kṛcchras tato ‘sya saṃskāraḥ || 23.2 ||
amatyā ajñānena gauḍīmādhvyoḥ, pānādhikāre punaḥ pānagrahaṇāt | tathā ca manuḥ |
ajñānād vāruṇīṃ pītvā saṃskāreṇaiva śudhyati |
matipūrvam anideśyaṃ prāṇāntikam iti sthitiḥ || iti | [MDh 11.146] | atrāpi “taptakṛcchrasahitena saṃskāreṇa” [Bhāruci] iti bhāṣyakāreṇa vyākhyātam | evaṃ ca paiṣṭyā amatyā sakṛtpāne brāhmaṇasya draṣṭavyam | yathāha manuḥ |
kaṇān vā bhakṣayed abdaṃ piṇyākaṃ vā sakṛn niśi |
surāpānāpanuttyarthaṃ vālavāsā jaṭī dhvajī || [MDh 11.92] | kṣatriyavaiśyayos tu yathodāhṛtaṃ vasiṣṭhoktaṃ draṣṭavyam “surāyāś cājñāne” [VaDh 20.19] ityādi | tasyā buddhipūrvābhyāse ‘pi trayāṇāṃ maraṇāntikam eva prāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
abhyāse tu surāyās tām agnivarṇāṃ pibed dvijaḥ | iti | [VaDh 20.22] | atra tuśabdo viśeṣavācī | dvijātīnāṃ paiṣṭyā abuddhipūrvābhyāse ‘tiviśeṣato ‘gnivarṇāyāḥ tasyāḥ pānaṃ kartavyam, brāhmaṇasya gauḍīmādhvyoś cābuddhipūrvābhyāse agnivarṇāyās tasyāḥ pāne maraṇād eva śuddhiḥ | tathāha vyāghraḥ |
matyā madyam amatyā vā punaḥ pītvā dvijottamaḥ |
tato ‘gnivarṇāṃ tat pītvā mṛtaḥ śudhyet sa kilbiṣāt || iti | | kṣatriyavaiśyayos tu gauḍīmādhvyor apratiṣedhaḥ | tathāha vyāsaḥ |
ubhau madhvāsavakṣībau ubhau candanacarcitau |
ekaparyaṅkaśayanau dṛṣṭau me keśavārjunau || iti |
tataś cāmatipūrve gauḍīmādhvyor brāhmaṇasya sakṛtpāne kṣīrādīnāṃ tryahaṃ tryahaṃ kartavyam | payaādīnāṃ visamāsakaraṇaṃ kramanivṛttyartham | tathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
“tryaham uṣṇāḥ pibed āpaḥ tryaham uṣṇaṃ pibet payaḥ |
tryaham uṣṇaṃ ghṛtaṃ pītvā vāyubhakṣaḥ paraṃ tryaham ||
iti taptakṛcchraḥ” [VaDh 21.21] iti udakaghṛtapayasām evoṣṇatvam, saṃbhavāt na vāyoḥ | “sa kṛcchraḥ” iti kṛcchravidheḥ upasaṃgrahārthaḥ | “tiṣṭhed ahani rātrāv āsīta” [GDh 26.6] ityādi || 23.2 ||
yas tv amatyābuddhipūrvaṃ yavāgvadibuddhyā surāṃ pibati sa payaādīni catvāri dravyāṇi taptāny uṣṇāni | dvitīyāyā nirdeśāt pibed iti gamyate | pratitryahaṃ prathame tryahe payo dvitīye ghṛtaṃ tṛtīya udakaṃ caturthe vāyum | vāyor uṣṇatvaṃ sātape pradeśe | sa kṛcchraḥ sa evaṃbhūtas taptakṛcchro ‘sya prāyaścittam | tataḥ kṛcchrānantaraṃ punaḥ saṃskāraḥ punar upanayanam asya kartavyam | tatra mānavo viśeṣaḥ |
vapanaṃ mekhalā daṇḍo bhaikṣacaryā vratāni ca |
etāni tu nivartante punaḥsaṃskārakarmaṇi || iti | [MDh 11.151] | idam īṣadabhyāsaviṣayam |
ajñānād vāruṇīṃ pītvā saṃskāreṇaiva śudhyati | [MDh 11.146] iti mānavaṃ sakṛtpānaviṣayam | yat tu,
piṇyākaṃ vā kaṇān vāpi bhakṣayet tu samāṃ niśi | [YDh 3.254] iti yājñavakyavacanam | yac ca āpastambīyam: “steyaṃ kṛtvā surāṃ pītvā” [ĀpDh 1.25.10] ityādi tad ubhayam api bahukṛtvo ‘bhyāsa eva |
kaṇān vā bhakṣayed abdaṃ piṇyākaṃ vā sakṛn niśi |
surāpānāpanuttyarthaṃ vālavāsā jaṭī dhvajī || [MDh 11.92] ityādīni mānavādīny abuddhipūrvaviṣaya evābhyāsatāratamyāpekṣayā vyavasthāpyāni || 23.2 ||
tato ‘sya saṃskāraḥ || 23.3 ||
tata iti kṛcchrānantaram eva saṃskāra iti | asyagrahaṇaṃ vispaṣṭārthaṃ yasya pānaṃ tasyeti | tad api paiṣṭyā buddhipūrve sakṛtpāne kṣatriyavaiśyayor api saṃskāraprāpaṇārthaṃ brāhmaṇādhikārāt tayor nivṛttir mā bhūd iti | uttarārthaṃ vā mūtrādiprāśane brāhmaṇasyaiva punaḥsaṃskāra iti | saṃskāra upanayanamātraṃ na tu vedavratadhāraṇādi | punaḥsaṃskārasvarūpaṃ smṛtyantarād avagantavyam | yathāha manuḥ |
vapanaṃ mekhalā daṇḍo bhaikṣacaryā vratāni ca |
etāni tu nivartante punaḥsaṃskārakarmaṇi || [MDh 11.151] iti || 23.3 ||
mūtrapurīṣaretasāṃ ca prāśane || 23.4 || — || 23.3 ||
mūtrādīnāṃ ca prāśane etad eva prāyaścittam | cakāraḥ punaḥsaṃskārānukarṣaṇārthaḥ | idaṃ buddhipūrve | kutaḥ? manuvacanāt:
bhuktvāto ‘nyatamasyānnam amatyā tu tryahaṃ kṣapet |
matyā bhuktvā caret kṛcchraṃ retoviṇmūtram eva ca || iti | [MDh 4.222] | atra kṛcchraśabdena taptakṛcchro ‘bhihitaḥ iti bhāṣyakāreṇoktatvāt [Bhāruci], uttaratrānyeṣām abhakṣyāṇāṃ prāyaścittopadeśān mānuṣāṇāṃ mūtrādiprāśna ity avagantavyam | abuddhipūrve ca,
bhuktvāto ‘nyatamasyānnam amatyā tu tryahaṃ kṣapet | [MDh 4.222] ity anenaiva tryaho veditavyaḥ, buddhipūrve ‘tulyaprāyaścittadarśanāt | yathāha kaṇvaḥ |
retomūtrapurīṣāṇāṃ prāśane ‘matipūrvake |
nāśnīyāt tu tryahaṃ matyā taptakṛcchraṃ cared dvijaḥ || iti | amativiṣaye ‘pi punaḥsaṃskāro draṣṭavyaḥ | yathāha manuḥ |
ajñānāt prāśya viṇmūtraṃ surāsaṃsṛṣṭam eva ca |
punaḥsaṃskāram arhanti trayo varṇā dvijātayaḥ || iti | [MDh 11.150]
kecid etad eva prāyaścittam icchanti, tad api sakṛtprāśane draṣṭavyam | evaṃ ca yatra mūtrapurīṣaprāśane prāyaścittam uktam “kākakharoṣṭṛāṇāṃ ca” ityādau, tatra manuṣyavarjitasya draṣṭavyam || 23.4 ||
mūtrādīnāṃ ca prāśane taptakṛcchrasahitaḥ punaḥsaṃskāraḥ prāyaścittam | idaṃ buddhipūrvaviṣayam |
bhuktvāto ‘nyatamasyānnam amatyā kṣapaṇaṃ tryaham |
matyā bhuktvā caret kṛcchraṃ reto viṇmūtram eva ca || [MDh 4.222] iti |
ajñānāt prāśya viṇmūtraṃ surāsaṃsṛṣṭam eva ca |
punaḥ saṃskāram arhanti trayo varṇā dvijātayaḥ || [MDh 11.150] iti ca |
kaṇvaś ca spaṣṭam āha |
retomūtrapurīṣāṇāṃ prāśane matipūrvake |
nāśnīyāc ca tryahaṃ matyā taptakṛcchraṃ cared dvijaḥ || iti || 23.3 ||
śvāpadoṣṭrakharāṇāṃ cāṅgasya || 23.5 || — || 23.4 ||
prāśana ity anuvartate | śvāpadāḥ śveva padāḥ śvāpa-dāḥ śvasadṛśapadāḥ sṛgālādayaḥ | uṣṭrakharau prasi-ddhau | teṣām aṅgasya māṃsacarmāder bhakṣaṇa etad eva prāyaścittam | śvasadṛśapādānāṃ vidhānād eva śuno ‘py arthasiddham, caśabdān manuṣyāṇāṃ ca
|| 23.5. ||
vyāghrādayo vanacarāḥ śvāpadāḥ | uṣṭrakharau prasiddhau | teṣām aṅgaṃ māṃsacarmādi | tasya prāśane taptakṛcchraḥ punaḥsaṃskāraś ca | buddhipūrvābhyāsa ubhayaṃ militam | sakṛdbuddhi-pūrve cābuddhipūrvābhyāse ca taptakṛcchraḥ | sakṛdmatipūrve saṃskāra eva bhavati || 23.4 ||
grāmyakukkuṭasūkarayoś ca || 23.6 || — || 23.5 ||
aṅgaprāśna etad eva prāyaścittam | pṛthagyogaḥ “pūrvaiś ca daṣṭasya” [GDh 23.8] ity atraitayor varjanārtham | asmin buddhipūrvādau pūrvam evoktam | cakāraḥ punaḥsaṃskārānukarṣaṇārthaḥ || 23.6 ||
grāmyakukkuṭasūkarayoś cāṅgasya prāśana etad eva prāyaścittam | viṣayavyavasthā ca pūrvavat || 23.5 ||
gandhāghrāṇe surāpasya prāṇāyāmā ghṛtaprāśanaṃ ca || 23.7 || — || 23.6 ||
surāpasyāviśeṣitatvāt sarvasya | gandhāghrāṇe surā-gandhāghrāṇe, na tu śarīragandhāghrāṇe | tatrāpi nānyāvasthitasurāgandhāghrāṇe, surāgandhāghrāna iti vaktavye surāpasyeti nirdeśāt | tataś cānyāvasthitasurā-gandhāghrāṇe jātibraṃśakaratvaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha manuḥ |
brāhmaṇasya rujaḥ kṛtvā ghrātir aghreyamadyayoḥ |
jaihmyaṃ puṃsi ca maithunyaṃ jātibhaṃśakaraṃ smṛtam || iti | [MDh 11.67] | tathā prāyaścittam api |
jātabhaṃśakaraṃ karma kṛtvānyatarad icchayā |
caret sāntapanaṃ kṛcchraṃ prājāpatyam anicchayā || iti | [MDh 11.124] | yasya yat pratiṣiddhaṃ tasya tad gandhaghrāṇa iti ca draṣṭavyam, aviśeṣitatvāt | dvijātīnāṃ sāmānyam eva jātibhraṃśakaraprāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam | brāhmaṇasyārdhaṃ kṣatriyasya ity asyānupraveśo ‘tra nāsti, smṛtyantaradarśanāt | yathāha kaṇvaḥ |
brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo vāpi vaiśyaś cāpi tathaiva ca |
surāyā gandham āghrāya caret sāntapanavratam || iti |
prāṇāyāmās trayo draṣṭavyāḥ, tāvatā bahuvacanasya kṛtārthatvāt | ghṛtaprāśanaṃ kāyāplavanamātram, tat kṛtvā bhoktavyam upavāsavidhyabhavāt | visamāsaca-kārau vyastasamastakalpanārthau | ato brāhmaṇasya samastaṃ kṣatriyasya tu prāṇāyāmāḥ vaiśyasya ghṛtaprāśanam iti | tatrāpi – brāhmaṇasyāhitāgner viśeṣo draṣṭavyaḥ | yathāha manuḥ |
brāhmaṇas tu surāpasya gandham āghrāya somapaḥ |
prāṇān apsu trir āyamya ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati || iti | [MDh 11.149] | kṣatriyāder viśeṣābhāvo vacanābhāvāt || 23.7 ||
yas tu surāpas tasya taṃ surāgandham ājighrati na punaḥ śarīragandhaṃ nāpi bhāṇḍasthāyāḥ gandhaṃ tasya prāṇāyāmās trayo ghṛtaprāśanaṃ ca prāyaścittam | brāhmaṇasya militam, kṣatriyasya prāṇāyāmāḥ | vaiśyasya ghṛtaprāśanam iti | somapasya viśeṣo manunā darśitaḥ |
brāhmaṇasya surāpasya gandham āghrāya somapaḥ |
prāṇān apsu trir ācamya ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati || [MDh 11.149] iti |
brāhmaṇasya rujākṛtyaṃ ghrātir aghreyamadyayoḥ |
jaihmyaṃ puṃsi ca maithunyaṃ jātisaṃkaraṃ smṛtam || [MDh 11.67] iti |
jātibhraṃśakaraṃ karma kṛtvānyatamam icchayā |
caret sāṃtapanaṃ kṛcchraṃ prājāpatyam anicchayā || [MDh 11.124] iti [ca] mānavaṃ bhāṇḍasthāyāḥ surāyā gandhāghrāṇe || 23.6 ||
pūrvaiś ca daṣṭasya || 23.8 || — || 23.7 ||
pūrvaḥ śvāpadādibhiḥ samānuṣair daṣṭasya etad eva prāyaścittam | caśabdāt grāmyaiś ca kravyādibhiḥ | tathāha manuḥ |
śvasṛgālakharair daṣṭo grāmyaiḥ kravyādibhir eva ca |
narāśvoṣṭrair varāhaiś ca prāṇāyāmena śudhyati || iti | [MDh 11.199] | atrāpy evaṃviṣayo draṣṭavyaḥ | etair daṣṭasya brāhmaṇasya vasiṣṭhoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
brāhmaṇas tu śunā daṣṭo nadīṃ gatvā samudragām |
prāṇāyāmaśataṃ kṛtvā ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati || iti | [VaDh 23.31] | śvagrahaṇasyaiteṣām apy upalakṣaṇatvāt kṣatriyasyedaṃ prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ draṣṭavyam | vaiśyasya manunokta ekaḥ prāṇāyāmo draṣṭavyaḥ, prāṇāyāmenety ekavacananirdeśāt | śūdrasya tu na doṣaḥ | striyāś ca sacelasnānaṃ draṣṭavyam | tathāha jātukarṇiḥ |
brāhmaṇī kṣatriyā vaiśyā śunā ca śvāpadair api |
daṣṭā sacelam āplutya śudhyanti tu na saṃśayaḥ ||
iti || 23.8 ||
pūrvaiḥ śvāpadādibhir daṣṭasya ca prāṇāyāmā ghṛtaprāśanaṃ ca prāyaścittam | manus tu |
śvasṛgālakharair daṣṭo grāmyaiḥ kravyādibhir eva ca |
narāśvoṣṭrair varāhaiś ca prāṇāyāmena śudhyati || iti | [MDh 11.199] | brāhmaṇaviṣaye vāsiṣṭho viśeṣaḥ |
brāhmaṇas tu śunā daṣṭo nadīṃ gatvā samudragām |
prāṇāyāmaśataṃ kṛtvā ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati || iti | [VaDh 23.31] | jātūkarṇyaḥ |
brāhmaṇī kṣatriyā vaiśyā śunā ca śvāpadair api |
daṣṭā sacailam āplutya śudhyatīti na saṃśayaḥ ||
iti || 23.7 ||
tapte lohaśayane gurutalpagaḥ śayīta || 23.9 || — || 23.8 ||
tapte agnivarṇe āyase gurubhāryāgāmī svapayāt
|| 23.9 ||
gurur atra pitā |
niṣekādīni karmāṇi yaḥ karoti yathāvidhi |
saṃbhāvayati cānyena sa vipro gurur ucyate || [MDh 2.142] iti manuḥ | vipragrahaṇaṃ varṇopalakṣaṇam | talpaśabdena śayanavācinā bharyā lakṣyate | tatrāpi jananī tatsapatnī ca | tadgāmī gurutalpagaḥ | lohaśayane kṛṣṇāyasanirmite tapte yathā maraṇam eva bhavati tathā tapte śayīta || 23.8 ||
sūrmiṃ vāśliṣyej jvalantīm || 23.10 || — || 23.9 ||
H reads “vā śliṣyej”
sūrmiṃ lohamayīṃ strīprakṛtim antaḥsuṣirām | antaḥsuṣirakāṣṭhām ity apare | tāṃ jvalantīm āśliṣyet upagūhet || 23.10 ||
lohamayī strīprakṛtiḥ sūrmī | tāṃ jvalantīm agnivarṇāṃ taptāṃ śliṣyed ā prāṇaviyogāt || 23.9 ||
liṅgaṃ vā savṛṣaṇam utkṛtyāñjalāv ādhāya dakṣiṇāpratīcīṃ vrajed ajihmam ā śarīranipātāt | mṛtaḥ śudhyet || 23.11 ||
liṅgaṃ vā savṛṣaṇam utkṛtyāñjalāv ādhāya dakṣiṇāpratīcīṃ vrajed ajihmam ā śrīranipātāt || 23.10 ||
liṅgaṃ puruṣavyañjanaṃ bījasahitaṃ samūlam utkṛtya hastadvaye sthāpayitvā nairṛṭīṃ diśaṃ vrajed ajihmaṃ kūpādyapariharan | ā śarīranipātāt ā vināśāt | mṛtaḥ śudhyet | ayaṃ ca sarvaśeṣaḥ, maraṇāntikatvāt | evaṃ ca teṣāṃ vikalpe siddhe vāgrahaṇaṃ vyavasthārtham | tataś ca tayor icchātaḥ saṃyoge prathamam, tayā protsāhitasya dvitīyam, puruṣeṇa svena protsāhitāyāṃ tṛtīyam iti | etad api brāhmaṇasya brāhmaṇīgamane buddhipūrve sakṛdgamane | abuddhipūrve sakṛdgamane tu manunoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
khaṭvāṅgī cīravāsā vā śmaśrulo nirjane vane |
prājāpatyaṃ caret kṛcchram abdam ekaṃ samāhitaḥ || iti | [MDh 11.105] | tad apy ubhayor icchātaḥ saṃyoge | tayā protsāhitasya svena protsāhitāyām auśanasam iti draṣṭavyam: “gurutalpagāmī saṃvatsaraṃ brahmahatyāvrataṃ ṣaṇmāsaṃ taptakṛcchraṃ vā” iti | evaṃ brāhmaṇīgamane brāhmaṇasya buddhipūrve sakṛdgamane abuddhipūrve ‘bhyāse maranāntikam eva | kṣatriyāgamane vyāghroktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
kṛcchraṃ caivātikṛcchraṃ ca tathā kṛcchrātikṛcchrakam |
caren māsatrayaṃ vipraḥ kṣatriyāgamane guroḥ || iti |
tatrāpi buddhipūrve sakṛdgamane ubhayor icchātaḥ pravṛttāv atikṛcchraḥ | tayā protsāhitasya kṛcchraḥ, svena protsāhitāyāṃ kṛcchrātikṛcchraḥ tatrāpi buddhipūrve abhyāse maraṇāntikam eva | yathāha kavaṣaḥ |
matyā gatvā guror bhāryāṃ punaḥ kṣatrasutāṃ dvijaḥ |
vṛṣaṇāvarjitaṃ liṅgam utkṛtya ca mṛtaḥ śuciḥ || iti |
abuddhipūrve sakṛdgamane kaṇvoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ taptakṛcchram atikṛcchraṃ tathaiva ca |
sakṛd gatvā guror bhāryām ajñānāt kṣatriyāṃ dvijaḥ || iti | tatrāpy ubhayecchātaḥ pravṛttau taptakṛcchraḥ, tayā protsāhite atikṛcchraḥ, svena protsāhitāyāṃ cāndrāyaṇam iti | abhyāse tu –
guroḥ kṣatrasutāṃ bhāryāṃ punar gatvā tv akāmataḥ |
vṛṣaṇamātram utkṛtya śudhyej jīvan mṛtaś ca saḥ ||
iti jātūkarṇyuktaṃ draṣṭavyam | vaiśyāgamane buddhipūrve sakṛdgamane kavaṣa āha |
taptakṛcchraṃ parākaṃ ca tathā sāntapanaṃ guroḥ |
bhāryāṃ vaiśyāṃ sakṛd gatvā buddhyā māsaṃ cared dvijaḥ || iti | tatrāpy ubhayecchātaḥ pravṛttau taptakṛcchaḥ, tayā protsāhitasya sāntapanam, svena protsāhitāyāṃ parākaḥ | abhyāse liṅgasyāgracchedaḥ kartavyaḥ | yathāha lokākṣiḥ |
guror vaiśyāṃ punar gatvā gatvā cāpi punaḥ punaḥ |
liṅgāgraṃ chedayitvā tu tataḥ śudhyet sa kilbiṣāt || iti | asmād eva jñāpakād abhyāse yad uktaṃ gurutalpaprāyaścittaṃ tad eva bahuśo ‘bhyāse ‘pi draṣṭavyam | abuddhipūrve sakṛdgamane prajāpatir āha |
pañcarātraṃ tu nāśnīyāt saptāṣṭau vā tathaiva ca |
vaiśyāṃ bhāryāṃ guror gatvā sakṛd ajñānato dvijaḥ || iti | tatrāpy ubhayecchātaḥ pravṛttau saptarātram, tayā protsāhitasya pañcarātram, svena prosāhitāyām aṣṭarātram iti | abhyāse tv ā maraṇāt brahmacaryarakṣaṇam | tathāha hārītaḥ |
abhyasya vipro vaiśyāyāṃ guror ajñānamohitaḥ |
saṣaḍaṅgaṃ brahmacaryaṃ sa cared yāvad āyuṣam || iti | śūdrāgamane buddhipūrve jābālir āha |
atikṛcchraṃ taptakṛcchraṃ parākaṃ ca tathaiva ca |
guroḥ śūdrāṃ sakṛd gatvā buddhyā vipraś cared tataḥ || iti | atrāpy ubhayecchātaḥ pravṛttau taptakṛcchraḥ, tayā protsāhitasyātikṛcchraḥ, ātmanā protsāhitāyāṃ parāka iti draṣṭavyam | abhyāse tu dvādaśavarṣaṃ brahmacaryarakṣaṇaṃ kartavyam | yathāha upamanyuḥ |
punaḥ śūdrāṃ guror gatvā buddhyā vipraḥ samāhitaḥ |
brahmacaryam aduṣṭātmā sa cared dvādaśa samāḥ || iti | ajñāne dīrghatapā āha |
prājāpatyaṃ sāntapanaṃ saptarātropavāsakam |
guroḥ śūdrāṃ sakṛd gatvā cared vipraḥ samāhitaḥ || iti | atrāpy ubhayecchātaḥ pravṛttau sāntapanam, tayā protsāhitasya prājāpatyam, svena protsāhitāyāṃ saptarātram upavāsa iti | abhyāse tu manunoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ vā trīn māsān abhyasen niyatendriyaḥ |
haviṣyeṇa yavāgvāpi gurutalpāghanuttaye || iti | [MDh 11.106] | sādhāraṇastrīṇāṃ gurutalpadoṣo nāsti, vyāghravacanāt |
jātyuktaṃ paradāryaṃ ca kanyādūṣaṇam eva ca |
sādhāraṇastriyāṃ nāsti gurutalpatvam eva ca || iti |
ācāryavyatiriktāḥ pitrādayo guravaḥ, “ācāryaputraśiṣyabhāryāsu caivam” [VaDh 20.15] ity atideśād ācāryādīnāṃ bhāryāsu vasiṣṭhena || 23.11 ||
sabījaṃ liṅgam utpāṭya kṣurādinā nikṛtya svasyāñjalau sthāpayitvā nairṛtīṃ diśam ā śarīranipātād vrajed ajihmam | “kūpādy apariharan yatraiva pratihatas tatraiva tiṣṭhed āpralayāt” [VaDh 20.13] iti vasiṣṭhaḥ |
|| 23.10 ||
mṛtaḥ śudhyet || 23.11 ||
sarvaśeṣo ‘yam | pūrvokteṣu prakāreṣv anyatamena mṛta eva gurutalpagaḥ śudhyen nānyatheti | tritayam apy etaj jananīgamane svabhāryādibuddhyābuddhi-pūrvaṃ tatsapatnyāṃ ca, savarṇāyāṃ buddhipūrva-gamane |
pitṛbhāyāṃ tu vijñāya savarṇāṃ yo ‘bhigacchati |
jananīṃ vāpy avijñāya nāmṛtaḥ sa viśudhyati |
iti ṣaṭtriṃśanmate darśanāt | jananyāṃ kāmakṛte vāsiṣṭham: “niṣkālako ghṛtābhyakto gomayāgninā pādaprabhṛty ātmānam avadāhayet” [VaDh 20.42] iti | akāmato ‘bhyāse ‘py evam eva | akāmatas tu mātuḥ sapatnyāḥ savarṇāyā utkṛṣṭhāyāś ca gamanābhyāse śaṅkhoktam |
adhaḥśāyī jaṭādhārī parṇamūlaphalāśanaḥ |
ekakālaṃ samaśnan vai varṣe tu dvādaśe gate ||
rukmasteyī surāpaś ca brahmahā gurutalpagaḥ |
vratenaitena śudhyanti mahāpātakinas tv ime || iti |
sakṛdgamana ubhayor icchātaḥ pravṛttau mānavam |
khaṭvāṅgī cīravāsā vā śvaśrulo vijane vane |
prājāpatyaṃ caret kṛcchram abdam ekaṃ samāhitaḥ || iti | [MDh 11.105] | tayā protsāhitasya svena vā protsāhitāyām auśanasaṃ prāyaścittadvayaṃ krameṇa draṣṭavyam: “gurutalpagāmī saṃvatsaraṃ brahmacārivrataṃ ṣaṇmāsāṃs taptakṛcchraṃ ca” iti | evam uttareṣv api prāyaścitteṣu yad guru tad ātmanā protsāhitāyāṃ yal laghu tat tayā protsitasya madhyamaṃ tūbhayor icchātaḥ pravṛttāv iti draṣṭavyam | tatra vyāghraḥ |
kṛcchraṃ caivātikṛcchraṃ ca tathā kṛcchātikṛcchrakam |
caren māsatrayaṃ vipraḥ kṣatriyāgamane guroḥ || iti |
idaṃ sakṛd gamane ‘buddhipūrve | buddhipūrvābhyāsa ekavarṣam |
matyā gatvā punar bhāryāṃ guroḥ kṣatrasutāṃ dvijaḥ |
vṛṣaṇavarjitaṃ liṅgam utkṛtya sa mṛtaḥ śuciḥ || iti |
kaṇvaḥ |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ taptakṛcchram atikṛcchraṃ tathaiva ca |
sakṛd gatvā guror bhāryām ajñānāt kṣatriyāṃ dvijaḥ || iti | jātūkarṇyaḥ |
guroḥ kṣatrasutāṃ bhāryāṃ punar gatvā tv akāmataḥ |
vṛṣaṇamātram utkṛtya śuddho jīvan mṛto ‘pi vā || iti |
kaṇvaḥ |
taptakṛcchraṃ parākaṃ ca tathā sāntapanaṃ guroḥ |
bhāryāṃ vaiśyāṃ sakṛd gatvā buddhyā māsaṃ cared dvijaḥ || iti | laugākṣiḥ |
guror vaiśyāṃ punar gatvā (sakṛd) gatvā vāpi punaḥ punaḥ |
liṅgāgraṃ chedayitvā tu tataḥ sudhyet sa kilbiṣāt || iti | prajāpatiḥ |
pañcarātraṃ tu nāśnīyāt saptāṣṭau vā tathaiva ca |
vaiśyāṃ bhāryāṃ guror gatvā sakṛd ajñānato dvijaḥ || iti | hārītaḥ |
abhyasya vipro vaiśyāyāṃ guror ajñānamohitaḥ |
saṣaḍaṅgaṃ brahmacaryaṃ sa cared yāvad āyuṣam || iti | jābāliḥ |
atikṛcchraṃ taptakṛcchraṃ parākaṃ ca tathaiva ca |
guroḥ śūdrāṃ sakṛd gatvā buddhyā vipraś caret tataḥ || iti | upamanyuḥ |
punaḥ śūdrāṃ guror gatvā buddhyā vipraḥ samāhitaḥ |
brahmacaryam aduṣṭātmā dvādaśābdaṃ samācaret || iti | dīrghatapāḥ |
prājāpatyaṃ sāntapanaṃ saptarātropavāsanam |
guroḥ śūdrāṃ sakṛd gatvā cared ajñānato janaḥ || iti |
tatraivābhyāse mānavaṃ draṣṭavyam |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ vā trīn māsān abhyasen niyatendriyaḥ |
haviṣyeṇa yavāgvā vā gurutalpāpanuttaye || iti | [MDh 11.106] | atra vyāghraḥ |
jātyuktaṃ pāradāryaṃ ca gurutalpatvam eva ca |
sādhāraṇastriyā nāsti kanyādūṣaṇam eva ca ||
iti || 23.11 ||
sakhīsayonisagotrāśiṣyabhāryāsu snuṣāyāṃ gavi ca talpasamaḥ || 23.12 ||
H reads “gurutalpasamaḥ”
sakhī mitram, sayoniḥ bhaginī, sagotrā ekārṣeyā | etāsu śiṣyabhāryāyāṃ putrabhāryāyāṃ ca maithunaṃ ācarite gurutalpatulyaṃ niṣkrayaṇam | visamāsaḥ sakhyādibhāryopasaṃgrahārthaḥ | tathāha manuḥ |
gurutalpavrataṃ kuryād retaḥ siktvā sayoniṣu |
sakhyuḥ putrasya ca strīṣu kumārīṣv antyajāsu ca || iti | [MDh 11.170]
caśabda āśritādibhāryopasaṃgrahārthaḥ | tathāha vyāghraḥ |
āśritasyāpi viduṣa āhitāgneś ca yoginaḥ |
ācāryasya ca rājñaś ca bhāryāṃ pravrajitāṃ tathā ||
dhātrīṃ putrīṃ ca pautrīṃ ca sakhīṃ mātus tathaiva ca |
pituḥ sakhīṃ tathā gatvā gurutalpavataṃ caret ||
iti || 23.12 ||
sakhī mitrabhūtā | sayonir bhaginī | sagotraikagotrā | snuṣā putrabhāryā | etāsu śiṣyabhāyāyāṃ gavi ca mithunībhāve yāvān gurutalpadoṣas tāvān asyeti | yājñavalkyaḥ |
sakhibhāryākumārīṣu svayoniṣv antyajāsu ca |
sagotrāsu sutastrīṣu gurutalpasamaṃ smṛtam ||
pituḥ svasāraṃ mātuś ca mātulānīṃ snuṣām api |
mātuḥ sapatnīṃ bhaginīm ācāryatanayāṃ tathā ||
ācāryapatnīṃ svasutāṃ gacchaṃs tu gurutalpagaḥ |
liṅgaṃ chittvā vadhas tasya sakāmāyāḥ striyā api || iti | [YDh 3.231–233] | nāradaḥ |
mātā mātṛṣvasā śvaśrūr mātulānī pitṛṣvasā |
pitṛvyasakhiśiṣyastrī bhaginī tatsakhī snuṣā ||
duhitācāryabhāryā ca sagotrā śaraṇāgatā |
rājñī pravrajitā dhātrī sādhvī varṇottamā ca yā ||
āsām anyatamāṃ gacchan gurutalpaga ucyate |
śiśnasyotkartanāt tatra nānyo daṇḍo vidhīyate || iti | [NSm 12.72–74] | kātyāyanaḥ |
jananyāś ca bhaginyāś ca svasutāyās tathaiva ca |
snuṣāyā gamanaṃ caiva vijñeyam atipātakam ||
atipātakinas tv ete praviśeyur hutāśanam ||
bṛhadyamaḥ |
retaḥ siktvā kumārīṣu svayoniṣv antyajāsu ca |
sapiṇḍāsv anyadāreṣu prāṇatyāgo vidhīyate || iti |
sa eva |
cāṇḍālīṃ pulkasīṃ mlecchīṃ snuṣāṃ ca bhaginīṃ sakhīm |
mātāpitroḥ svasāraṃ ca nikṣiptāṃ śaraṇāgatām ||
mātulānīṃ pravrajitāṃ sagotrāṃ nṛpayoṣitam |
śiṣyabhāryāṃ guror bhāryāṃ gatvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || iti ca || 23.12 ||
avakara ity eke || 23.13 ||
eke tv avakīrṇivrataṃ kartavyam iti manyante na tu gurutalpavratam iti | itikaraṇaḥ saṃdehavyāvṛttyarthaḥ | yady evam ucyate avakara eka iti tato ‘vakīrṇino ‘pi gurutalpasamo doṣa ity āśaṅkā syād iti | so ‘yaṃ vikalpo ‘bhisaṃdhyapekṣayā draṣṭavyaḥ | abhisaṃdhau gurutalpasamaḥ, anabhisaṃdhau tv avakīrṇivratam iti | evaṃ cātra pāradāryanimittaṃ kṛcchraṃ kṛtvā punar avakīrṇivratam iti draṣṭavyam | tathāha jātūkarṇiḥ |
ācāryādes tu bhāryāsu gurutalpavrataṃ caret |
avakīrṇivrataṃ caiva tataḥ kuryāt samāhitaḥ || iti |
gurutalpasame ‘pi pāradāryād adhikaṃ gurutalpād dhīnaṃ kalpyam | tad api paradāraprāyaścittam eva cāndrāyaṇād adhikaṃ draṣṭavyam | cāndrāyaṇasya ca varṇakrameṇa pādahīnakalpanā kartavyā | tathā ca prajāpatiḥ |
ācāryādeś tu bhāryāsu carec cāndrāyaṇavratam |
krameṇa pādaśo hīnaṃ varṇānām iti ca sthitiḥ || iti |
avakīrṇiprāyaścittam api rahasyoktam “tad āhuḥ katidhāvakīrṇī” [GDh 25.1] ityādi vakṣyati | tathā ca prajāpatiḥ:
ācāryādes tu bhāryāsu gamane pāradāryaṃ parisamāpyāvakīrṇī varṇakrameṇa māsaṃ pakṣaṃ daśāhaṃ saptāhaṃ pañcāham ity agnāv ājyena juhuyāt, “kāmāvakīrṇo ‘smi kāma kamāya svāhā, kāmābhidrugdho ‘smy abhidrugdho ‘smi kāma kāmāya svāhā” iti dvābhyām | etaṃ homaṃ śrotriyabhāryāgamane dviguṇaṃ kuryād icchantyāṃ gamane ardham ity upadiśanti || iti |
sarvatra samīkaraṇaviṣaye sarvaprāyaścittam eva draṣṭavyam iti yad uktaṃ tad anyaprāyaścittānulabdhau draṣṭavyam || 23.13 ||
eke manyante sakhyādigamane ‘vakaro doṣaḥ | atra prāyaścittam apy avakīrṇivrataṃ na gurutalpavratam iti | yāny etāni sakhyādigamane ‘nukrāntāni prāyaścittāni teṣu maraṇāntikāni sapratyayānubandhātyantābhyāsaviṣayāṇi | yāni punar atyantalaghūni tāni svabhāryābuddhyā pravṛttasya madhye jñātvā retaḥsekād arvāṅ nivṛttaviṣayāṇi | madhye madhyāni kalpyāni |
na jātu brāhmaṇaṃ hanyāt sarvapāpeṣv avasthitam | [MDh 8.380] iti mānavaṃ tu maraṇāntikayogya-mahāpātakādivyatiriktaviṣayam || 23.13 ||
atra nihīnavarṇagamane striyāḥ pātityam uktam | tasyāḥ sāmānyataḥ patitaprāyaścitte
prāpta āha ||
atra prāyaścittam akurvatīnāṃ strīṇāṃ daṇḍam āha |
śvabhiḥ khādayed rājā nihīnavarṇagamane striyam prakāśam || 23.14 ||
H reads “ādayed”
nihīnavarṇo vyākhyātaḥ “bhrūṇahani hīnavarṇa-sevāyāṃ ca” [GDh 21.9] ity atra | tadgamane tāṃ śvabhiḥ khādayed rājā prakāśaṃ janasamakṣam | tathāha manuḥ |
bhartāraṃ laṅghayed yā tu jātistrīguṇadarpitā |
tāṃ śvabhiḥ khādayed rājā saṃsthāne bahusaṃsthite || iti | [MDh 8.371] | abuddhipūrve ayaṃ rājadaṇḍaḥ, buddhipūrve vasiṣṭhoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
śūdro brāhmaṇīm upagacchet vīraṇair veṣṭayitvā śūdram agnau prāsyet, brāhmaṇyā śirasi vapanaṃ kārayitvā sarpiṣābhyajya nagnāṃ kṛṣṇakharam āropya mahāpatham anusaṃvrājayet, pūtā bhavatīti vijñāyate | vaiśyaś cet brāhmaṇīm upagacchel lohitadarbhair veṣṭayitvā vaiśyam agnau prāsyet, brāhmaṇyāḥ śirasi vapanaṃ kārayitvā sarpiṣābhyajya nagnāṃ gaurakharam āropya mahāpatham anusaṃvrājayet pūtā bhavatīti vijñāyate | rājanyaś cet brāhmaṇīm upagacchec charapatrair veṣṭayitvā rājanyam agnau prāsyet brāhmanyāḥ śirasi vapanaṃ kārayitvā sarpiṣābhyajya nagnāṃ śvetakharam āropya mahāpatham anusaṃvrājayet pūtā bhavatīti vijñāyate | evaṃ vaiśyo rājanyāyāṃ śūdraś ca rājanyavaiśyayoḥ || iti | [VaDh 21.1–5]
nihīnavarṇagamana ity uktatvāt kṣatriyavaiśyābhyāṃ buddhipūrvagamane brāhmaṇyāḥ kalpyam | yathāha manuḥ |
jaghanyaṃ sevamānāṃ tu saṃyatāṃ vāsayed gṛhe |
uttamāṃ sevamānas tu jaghanyo vadham arhati || iti | [MDh 8.366] | ayam eva kṣatriyāvaiśyāgamane ‘pi draṣṭavyaḥ, sāmānyenoktatvāt | evaṃ ca nihīnavarṇa ity ayam api daṇḍo dvijātistrīṇāṃ sāmānyaḥ, sāmānyenoktatvād eva | anulomasaṃparke tu vyāghra āha |
varṇānām anulomānāṃ parasparasamāgame |
vyutkrameṇa tato rājā khādayed vānaraiḥ striyam ||
sṛgālair buddhipūrvam cet puruṣo vadham arhati |
ayam evānulomānāṃ svajātivyutkrameṣv api || iti |
pratilomasamāgame buddhipūrve cābuddhipūrve ca manunoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
pratilome vadhaḥ puṃso strīṇāṃ nāsādikartanam | iti |
nanu ca:
etad eva vidhiṃ kuryād yoṣitsu patitāsv api | [MDh 11.188] iti,
yat puṃsaḥ paradāreṣu yac caināṃ cārayed vratam | [MDh 11.176] iti ca siddhe ayaṃ daṇḍavidhir anarthaka iti |
atrocyate | yā svayam eva rājānaṃ gacchati tasyā daṇḍa eva, yayā tu balād ānīyate tasyā daṇḍaś ca prāyaścittaṃ ca, yā svayam api na gacchati na balād nānīyate tasyāḥ prāyaścittam eveti | ayam eva nyāyaḥ sarvatra daṇḍaprāyaścittayor draṣṭavyaḥ | atra pratilomānāṃ svajātivyutkrame “pratilomas tu dharmahīnaḥ” [GDh 4.25] iti prāyaścittābhāvād anyeṣāṃ saṃkaradoṣaparihārārthaṃ daṇḍaḥ kalpyaḥ | evaṃ ca,
pratilome vadhaḥ puṃsāṃ strīṇāṃ nāsādikartanam |
iti teṣām api draṣṭavyam | tathā pātakopapātakaviṣaye ‘pi brāhmaṇyā anulomānantarajasya yo daṇḍa uktaḥ tasyārthaṃ draṣṭavyam, tathaikāntaradvyantarayoś ca | kutaḥ?
caṇḍālasya samīpe tu nādhyetavyaṃ kadācana |
tathā pāraśavasyāpi caṇḍālārdho his sa smṛtaḥ ||
iti vyāghradharmaliṅgāt || 23.14 ||
nihīnavarṇena saha yā maithunam ācarati tāṃ prakāśaṃ sarveṣām eva paśyatāṃ parṣatsthānagato rājā śvabhir ādayet khādayet | atra manuḥ |
bhartāraṃ laṅghayed yā tu jātistrīguṇagarvitā |
tāṃ śvabhiḥ khādayet rājā saṃsthāne bahubhiḥ sthitaḥ || iti | [MDh 8.371] | vasiṣṭhas tu jātiviśeṣeṇa viśeṣam āha –
śūdraś ced brāhmaṇīm abhigacchet tṛṇair veṣṭayitvā śūdram agnau prāsya brāhmaṇyāḥ śirasi vapanaṃ kārayitvā sarpiṣābhyajya nagnāṃ kharam āropya mahāpatham anusaṃvrājayet pūtā bhavatīti vijñāyate | vaiśyaś ced brāhmaṇīm abhigacchel lohitadarbhair veṣṭayitvā vaiśyam agnau prāsyet | brāhmaṇyāḥ śirasi vapanaṃ kārayitvā sarpiṣābhyajya nagnāṃ kharam āropya mahāpatham anusaṃvrājayet pūtā bhavatīti vijñāyate | rājanyaś ced brāhmaṇīm abhigacchec charapatrair veṣṭayitvā rājanyam agnau prāsyet | brāhmaṇyāḥ śirasi vapanaṃ kārayitvā sarpiṣābhyajya nagnāṃ kharam āropya mahāpatham anusaṃvrājayet pūtā bhavatīti vijñāyate | evaṃ vaiśyo rājanyāṃ śūdraś ca rājanyavaiśyayoḥ | iti | [VaDh 21.1–5]
anulomeṣu pratilomaṃ gacchatsu vyāghra āha |
varṇānām anulomānāṃ parasparasamāgame |
vyutkrameṇa tato rājā khādayed vānaraiḥ striyam ||
śṛgālair buddhipūrvaṃ cet puruṣo vadham arhati |
ayam evānulomānāṃ svajātivyutkrameṣv iti ||
iti || 23.14 ||
pumāṃsaṃ ghātayet || 23.15 ||
prakāśam ity anuvartate | ghātanaprakāraś ca vasiṣṭho-kto draṣṭavyaḥ | udāhṛtaś ca viśeṣāt | buddhipūrve ‘buddhipūrve sakṛdgamane abhyāse ca viśeṣavacanān-tarābhāvāc ca sarvatra hananam eva draṣṭavyam | evaṃ varṇānām anulomānāṃ pratilomānāṃ ca svavarga-vyutkrame parasparavyutkrame ca hananam eva draṣṭavyam | tathā ca smṛtyantaravākyāni codāhṛtāni | evaṃ ca strīṇām api sakṛdgamane ‘bhyāse ca pūrvokta eva daṇḍa iti draṣṭavyam || 23.15 ||
anantarokte viṣaye gantā pumān rājñā ghātayitavyaḥ | vadhaprakāraś cānantaram eva vasiṣṭhavacanena darśitaḥ || 23.15 ||
yathoktaṃ vā || 23.16 ||
liṅgoddhāra ityādi yathoktaṃ vā śūdrasya draṣṭavyam | tatra sacchūdrasya yathoktam itarasyedam iti draṣṭavyam || 23.16 ||
liṅgoddhāra ityādi yathoktaṃ vā daṇḍapraṇayanaṃ kartavyam | sapratyayāpratyayābhyāsānabhyāsāpekṣo ‘yaṃ vikalpaḥ || 23.16 ||
gardabhenāvakīrṇī nirṛtiṃ catuṣpathe yajeta || 23.17 ||
avakīrṇī viplutabrahmacaryo brahmacārī | yathāha jātukarṇiḥ |
khaṇḍitaṃ vratinā reto yena syāt brahmacāriṇā |
kṣaraṇāt kāmataḥ prāhur avakīrṇīti taṃ budhāḥ || iti |
yatra tv anyat prāyaścittaṃ nāsti putrikākaraṇādau tatredaṃ prāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam | evaṃ cānyatra kanyāgamanādiprāyaścittena saha prāyaścittadvayasiddhiḥ | gardabhena khareṇa kāṇenāvakīrṇī nirṛtidaivatyena catuṣpathe araṇya eva, na sarvatra laukike ‘gnau | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “brahmacārī cet striyam upeyāt araṇye catuṣpathe laukike ‘gnau rakṣodaivataṃ gardabhaṃ paśum ālabheta,  nairṛtaṃ vā caruṃ nirvapet” [VaDh 23.1–2] iti | tatrāśrotriyasya gardabhaḥ, śrotriyasya carur iti draṣṭavyam | sthālīpākavidhānena yajeta juhuyāt | rātrāv eva na divā | yathāha manuḥ |
avakīrṇī tu kāṇena gardabhena catuṣpathe |
sthālīpākavidhānena yajeta nirṛtiṃ niśi || iti | [MDh 11.118] | atrāyaṃ homavidhir veditavyaḥ – ā pradhānayāgāt sthālīpākavidhānena homaṃ kṛtvā vasiṣṭhoktair mantraiḥ paśumāṃsair hotavyam, tatra “tasya juhuyāt” [VaDh 23.3]  ity uktatvāt | tatrāhutayaḥ – “kāmāya svāhā kāmakāmāya svāhā nirṛtyai svāhā rakṣodevatābhyaḥ svāhā” [VaDh 23.3] ity catasraḥ | vapāhomatvāt sviṣṭakṛn nāsti “sviṣṭakṛd anyatra vapāhomājyahomābhyām” iti jaiminigṛhye pratipāditatvāt | evaṃ haviṣā hutvā punaḥ sarpiṣā vāyvādīnāṃ “saṃ mā siñcantu” ity anena mantreṇa hotavyam | yathāha manuḥ |
hutvāgnau vidhivad dhomān antataś ca sam ity ṛcā |
vāyvindraguruvahnīnāṃ juhuyāt sarpiṣāhutīḥ || iti | [MDh 11.119] | evam hutvā punar api sthālīpākavidhānena homaśeṣaṃ ninayed iti | abhyāsa-viśeṣād dhomāvṛttir draṣṭavyā | yathāha kaṇvaḥ |
prathamadivase rātrāv avakīrṇī gardabhena tu |
yajed evaṃ yathābhyāsaṃ so ‘bdenaikena śudhyati || iti | evaṃ cābhyāse homāvṛttir eva na kālāvṛttir
iti || 23.17 ||
avakīrṇī bhaved gatvā brahmacārī tu yoṣitam | [YDh 3.280]
iti yājñavalkyaḥ | sa catuṣpathe gardabhena paśunā nirṛtiṃ yajet | atra mānavo viśeṣaḥ |
avakīrṇī tu kāṇena gardabhena catuṣpathe |
pākayajñavidhānena yajeta nirṛtiṃ diśi || iti | [MDh 11.118]
vasiṣṭhas tu |
brahmacārī cet striyam upeyād araṇye catuṣpathe laukike ‘gnau rakṣodaivataṃ gardabhaṃ paśum ālabheta, nairṛtaṃ vā caruṃ nirvapet tasya juhuyāt kāmāya svāhā, kāmakāmāya svāhā, nirṛtyai svāhā, rakṣodevatābhyaḥ svāhā | [VaDh 23.1–3]
iti || 23.17 ||
tasyājinam ūrdhvavālaṃ paridhāya lohitapātraḥ sapta gṛhān bhaikṣaṃ caret karmācakṣāṇaḥ || 23.18 ||
tasya gardabhasyājinaṃ carma | adhikāreṇaiva siddhe tasya grahaṇaṃ yena gardabhena yāgaḥ tasyaiva carma nānyasyeti niyamārtham | tad api sāṅgam ūrdhvavālaṃ bahirloma, tad upari vasitvā | lohitapātraḥ tāmramayapātraḥ raktavarṇapātro vā | pratidinaṃ saptaiva gṛhān bhaikṣaṃ caret karmācakṣāṇaḥ “avakīrṇine bhikṣāṃ dehi” iti, evam “avakīrṇy asmi” iti kīrtayan | evaṃ ca bhaikṣācaraṇakāla eva carmaṇaḥ paridhānaṃ na homakāle ‘pi | tathā ca manur api homād ūrdhvam eva vakṣyati |
etasminn enasi prāpte vasitvā gardabhājinam |
saptāgāraṃ caret bhaikṣaṃ svakarma parikīrtayan || iti | [MDh 11.122] | bhojanam apy ekakālam eva | yathāha manuḥ |
tebhyo labdhena bhaikṣeṇa vartayann ekakālikam |
upaspṛśan triṣavaṇam abdenaikena śudhyati ||
[MDh 11.123] iti || 23.18 ||
evaṃ gardabheneṣṭvā tasyaiva gardabhasyājinam ūrdhvavālaṃ paridhāya lohitapātraḥ pākena lohitaṃ mṛnmayaṃ pātraṃ haste gṛhītvā karmācakṣāṇo ‘vakīrṇine bhikṣāṃ dehīti bruvāṇaḥ sapta gṛhān bhaikṣaṃ caret | saptasu gṛheṣu yāval labdhaṃ tāvad evāśanam | alābha upavāsaḥ || 23.18 ||
saṃvatsareṇa śudhyet || 23.19 ||
evam ācaran saṃvatsareṇa pūto bhavati | aviśeṣād dvijātīnām idaṃ draṣṭavyam | tathā ca manunā dvijātigrahaṃ kṛtam |
kāmato retasaḥ sekaṃ vratasthasya dvijanmanaḥ |
atikramaṃ vratasyāhur dharmajñā brahmavādinaḥ || iti | [MDh 11.120] | evaṃ ca brāhmaṇasyārdhaṃ kṣatriyasyety ayaṃ nyāyo ‘tra nāvatarati | yathāha śāṇḍilyo ‘pi |
avakīrṇī dvijo rājā vaiśyaś cāpi khareṇa tu |
iṣṭvā bhaikṣāśano nityaṃ śudhyaty abdāt samāhitaḥ || iti | atra striyam antareṇa prayatnotsarge ‘pi homamātraṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “etad eva retasaḥ prayatnotsarge ‘pi” [VaDh 23.4] iti | tathā puruṣamaithune ‘pi | tathāha kāśyapaḥ |
puṃsi maithunam āsevya yatnotsarge kṛte tathā |
brahmacārī tathābhyāsāt snātvātha hiviṣā yajet || iti |
vānaprasthādīnāṃ cāyam eva kṛcchrādhiko draṣṭavyaḥ | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “vānaprastho dīkṣābhede kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caritvā mahākakṣaṃ vardhayet, bhikṣur vānaprasthavat somavṛddhivarjam” iti | dvādaśarātragrahaṇam atra parākatrayopasaṃ-grahaṇārtham | yathāha śāṇḍilyaḥ |
vānaprastho yatiś caiva khaṇḍane sati kāmataḥ |
parākatrayasaṃyuktam avakīrṇivrataṃ caret || iti |
caritaprāyaścittayor api saṃsargo na kartavyaḥ, yathāha kauśikaḥ |
naiṣṭhikānāṃ vanasthānāṃ yatīnāṃ cāvakīrṇinām |
śuddhānām api loke ‘smin pratyāpattir na vidyate || iti | yatīnāṃ vanasthānāṃ ca prayatnotsarge kaṇvoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
yatnotsargaṃ gṛhī kṛtvā vāruṇībhir upaspṛśet |
vānaprastho yatiś caiva cared cāndrāyaṇavratam ||
gṛhyamantrais trir ācāmet bhūr bhuvaḥ svar iti tribhiḥ |
yatir vanasthaś ca japet punar mām iti ṣoḍaśa || iti |
idam api svapnād anyatra | svapne ‘pi kāśyapa āha |
sūryasya trīn namaskārān svapne kṛtvā gṛhī caret |
yatiś caiva vanasthaś ca triḥ kuryād aghamarṣaṇam || iti | gṛhasthasya puruṣamaithune manunoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
maithunaṃ tu samāsevya puṃsi yoṣiti vā punaḥ |
goyāne ‘psu divā caiva sacelasnānam ācaret || iti |
evaṃ ca jātibhraṃśakaraprāyaścittam asmād anyatra draṣṭavyam | yoṣiti svabhāryāyām eva, ṛtau gamane ca | tathāha aṅgirāḥ |
ṛtau tu garbhaśaṅkitvāt snānaṃ mithuninaḥ smṛtam |
anṛtau tu yadā gacchec chaucaṃ mūtrapurīṣavat || iti |
divā maithune vāruṇībhiś ca apamārjanaṃ kartavyam | yathāha vṛddhavasiṣṭhaḥ |
aṣṭamyāṃ ca caturdaśyāṃ kṛtvā parvaṇi maithunam |
sacelaḥ snātvā divā ca vāruṇībhiś ca mārjayet ||
śāvāśauce tathā śrāddhe carec cāndrāyaṇavratam |
saṃkrāntyām uparāge ca taptakṛcchraṃ samācaret || iti | puruṣamaithune vānaprasthaparivrājakayoḥ kaṇvoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
puṃsi maithunam āsevya vānaprastho yatis tathā |
kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caiva kṛtvā śudhyet sa kilbiṣāt || iti || 23.19 ||
saṃvatsaram etad vrataṃ carec chuddho bhavati | atra manuḥ |
tebhyo labdhena bhaikṣeṇa vartayann ekakālikam |
upaspṛśaṃs triṣavaṇam abdenaikena śudhyati || iti | [MDh 11.123] | idaṃ ca vārṣikaṃ śrotriyasya viprasya vaiśyapatnyāṃ draṣṭavyam | āhatuḥ śaṅkhalikhitau: “guptāyāṃ vaiśyāyām avakīrṇaḥ saṃvatsaraṃ triṣavaṇam anutiṣṭhet kṣatriyāyāṃ dve varṣe brāhmaṇyāṃ trīṇi varṣāṇi” iti | guptāyāṃ cec chrotriyapatnītvādiguṇaśālinyām | aṅgirāḥ |
avakīrṇinimittaṃ tu brahmahatyāvrataṃ caret |
cīravāsās tu ṣaṇmāsāṃs tathā mucyeta kilbiṣāt || iti |
tad akāmato gautamīyaṃ kāmaviṣayam | punaḥ śaṅkhalikhtau:
svairiṇyāṃ vṛṣalyām avakīrṇaḥ sacailaṃ snāta udakumbhaṃ dadyād brāhmaṇāya | vaiśyāyāṃ caturthakālāhāro brāhmaṇān bhojayed yavabhāraṃ ca gobhyo dadyāt | kṣatriyāyāṃ trirātram upoṣito ghṛtapātraṃ dadyāt | brahmaṇyāṃ ṣaḍrātram upoṣito gāṃ dadyāt | goṣv avakīrṇaḥ prājāpatyaṃ caret | ṣaṇḍhāyām avakīrṇaḥ palālabhāraṃ sīsamāṣakaṃ ca dadyāt | iti |
idaṃ cāvakīrṇaprāyaścittaṃ sarveṣām eva traivarṇikabrahmacāriṇāṃ samānam | tathā ca śāṇḍilyaḥ |
avakīrṇī dvijo rājā vaiśyaś cāpi khareṇa tu |
iṣṭvā bhaikṣāśano nityaṃ śudhyaty abdāt samāhitaḥ || iti || 23.19 ||
alam atiprasaṅgena | adhunā prastutam ārabhyate |
retaḥskandane ‘bhaye ‘roge ‘svapne ‘gnīndhanabhaikṣacaraṇāni saptarātram akṛtvā ājyahomaḥ samidhor vā retasyābhyām || 23.20 ||
retaḥskandane bhaye roge svapne ‘gnīndhanabhaikṣacaraṇāni saptarātram akṛtvājyahomaḥ samidho vā retasyābhyām
|| 23.20 ||
abuddhipūrvaviṣaya idam, buddhipūrve pūrvam uktatvāt | retaḥskande retasaḥ kṣaraṇe sati, abhaye aroge asvapne, agnīndhanabhaikṣacaraṇāni anāturaḥ saptarātram akṛtvā | tathā ca manuḥ: “akṛtvā bhaikṣacaraṇam” [MDh 2.187] ityādi | ājyasamidhor vā homo retasyābhyām ṛgbhyām, “punar mām aitu, punar manaḥ” ity ābhyām | samidājyayoḥ saṃbhavato vikalpaḥ | svapne tu kṣaraṇe manunoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
svapne siktvā brahmacārī dvijaḥ śuklam akāmataḥ |
snātvārkam arcayitvā triḥ punar mām ity ṛcaṃ japet || iti | [MDh 2.181]
bhayaroganimittābhyām api kṣaraṇe idam eva draṣṭavyam,
bhaye roge tathā svapne siktvā śuklam akāmataḥ |
ādityam arcayitvā tu punar mām ity ṛcaṃ japet ||
iti prajāpatidharmaliṅgāt | evaṃ ca vānaprastha-parivrājakayoś ca yad uktaṃ svapne retaḥseke tad eva bhayaroge ‘pi draṣṭavyam | naiṣṭhikasya yad uktaṃ brahmacāriṇas tad eva dviguṇaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha hārītaḥ |
upakurvāṇas tu yat kuryāt kāmato ‘kāmato ‘pi vā |
tad eva dviguṇaṃ kuryād brahmacārī ca naiṣṭhikaḥ || iti |
anye tu vyācakṣate – retaḥskandane svapne bhayarogau varjayitveti, teṣāṃ manunoktena tulyavikalpo draṣṭavyaḥ | svapnād anyatrāpi prāyaścittaṃ mṛgyam || 23.20 ||
bhaye roge svapne vā yadi brahmacāriṇo retaḥ skandet tato retasyābhyāṃ mantrābhyām ājyahomaḥ kartavyaḥ | samidho vā | homa ity upasamastam apekṣyate | etat tu bhaye roga ityādi brahmacārivyatiriktasyāpi | tathāgnīndhanaṃ samidādhānaṃ bhaikṣacaraṇaṃ ca saptarātram akṛtvā pūrvavad dhomaḥ | retasye ṛcau “punar mām aitv indriyam” iti | “punar manaḥ punar ātmā ma āgāt” ity eke | āśvalāyanena tu “punar mām aitv indriyam” iti, “ime ye ‘dhiṣṭhayāso ‘gnaye” iti |
bhaye roge tathā svapne siktvā śukram akāmataḥ |
ādityam arcayitvā tu punar mām ity ṛcaṃ japet || iti |
prājāpatyaṃ sakṛtsekaviṣayam | gautamīyam abhyāsaviṣayam | hārītaḥ |
yaḥ kuryād upakurvāṇaḥ kāmato ‘kāmato ‘pi vā |
tad eva dviguṇaṃ kuryād brahmacārī tu naiṣṭhikaḥ || iti | atra vasiṣṭhaḥ: “etad eva retasaḥ prayatnotsarge divā svapne ca vratāntareṣu caivam” [VaDh 23.4] iti | gardabhaṃ paśum ālabheta nairṛtaṃ vā caruṃ nirvaped iti prakṛtam |
vānaprastho yatiś caiva khaṇḍane sati kāmataḥ |
parākatrayasaṃyuktam avakīrṇivrataṃ caret ||
iti śāṇḍilyaḥ |
puṃsi maithunam āsevya yatnotsarge ca retasaḥ |
brahmacārī yathābhyāsaṃ snātvātha haviṣā yajet ||
puṃsi maithunam āsādya vānaprastho yatis tathā |
kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caiva kṛtvā śudhyati kilbiṣāt || iti kaṇvaḥ |
sūryasya trīn namaskārān svapne siktvā gṛhī caret |
yatiś caiva vanasthaś ca triḥ kuryād aghamarṣaṇam || iti kāśyapaḥ |
maithunaṃ tu samāsādya puṃsi yoṣiti vā punaḥ |
goyāne ‘psu divā caiva svāpe ca snānam ācaret || [MDh 11.174] iti mānavam | gṛhasthasya –
ṛtau tu garbhaśaṅkitvāt snānaṃ maithuninaḥ smṛtam |
anṛtau tu yadā gacchec chaucaṃ mūtrapurīṣavat ||
ity aṅgirāḥ | vṛddhavasiṣṭhaḥ |
yas tu pāṇigṛhītāyām asye kurvīta maithunam |
tasya retasi taṃ māsaṃ pitaras tasya śerate ||
iti || || 23.20 ||
sūryābhyudito brahmacārī tiṣṭhed ahar abhuñjāno ‘bhyastamitaś ca rātriṃ
japan sāvitrīm || 23.21 ||
sūryābhyuditaḥ yasya śayānasya sūrya udetīty arthaḥ | brahmacārigrahaṇam adhikāralabdham api kriyate gṛhasthāder anyat prāyaścittam iti, teṣām api “uttareṣāṃ caitad avirodhi” [GDh 3.10] iti prāptir mā bhūd iti | gṛhasthasya tāvad āpastambhoktam ekīyaṃ mataṃ draṣṭavyam: “ā tamitoḥ prāṇam āyacched ity eke” [ĀpDh 2.12.15] iti | yativanasthayoś ca brahma-kūrcasahita upavāsaḥ | yathāha vṛddhavasiṣṭhaḥ |
vanasthaś ca yatiś caiva sūryeṇābhyuditau yadi |
brahmakūrcāśinau bhūtvā japetāṃ praṇavaṃ tv ahaḥ || iti | brahmakūrcavidhānam api smṛtyantarād avagantavyam | yathāha prajāpatiḥ |
pālāśaṃ padmapatraṃ vā tāmraṃ vāpi hiraṇmayam |
gṛhītvāsādayitvā ca tataḥ karma samārabhet ||
gāyatryā gṛhya gomūtraṃ gandhadvāreti gomayam |
āpyāyasveti ca kṣīraṃ dadhikrāvṇeti vai dadhi ||
śukram asi tejo ‘sīty ājyaṃ devasya tveti kuśodakam |
caturdaśyām upoṣyātha paurṇamāsyāṃ samācaret ||
gomayād dviguṇaṃ mūtraṃ sarpir dadyāc caturguṇam |
kṣīram aṣṭaguṇaṃ deyaṃ dadhi pañcaguṇaṃ tathā ||
sthāpayitvātha darbheṣu pālāśaiḥ patrakair atha |
tat samuddhṛtya hotavyaṃ devatābhyo yathākramam ||
agnaye caiva somāya savitre ca tathaiva ca |
praṇavena tathā hutvā sviṣṭakṛc ca tathaiva ca ||
evaṃ hutvā tataḥ śeṣaṃ pāpaṃ dhyātvā samāhitaḥ |
āloḍya praṇavenaiva nirmanthya praṇavena tu ||
uddhṛtya praṇavenaiva pibec ca praṇavena tu |
etad brahmakṛtaṃ kūrcaṃ māsi māsi caran dvijaḥ ||
sarvapāpavimuktātmā svargalokaṃ sa gacchati |
yady apy asthigataṃ pāpaṃ dehe tiṣṭhati dehinām |
brahmakūrco dahet sarvaṃ pradīptāgnir ivendhanam || iti |
tiṣṭhed ahar ahar abhuñjānaḥ nirāhāro ‘hani tiṣṭhet, rātrau tu bhoktavyam | evam abhyastam itaḥ yasya saṃdhyām upāsīnasya sato ravir astam eti asāv api rātrau nāśnīyāt | abhyāsanaṃ draṣṭavyam, “tiṣṭhed ahani rātrāv āsīta” [GDh 26.6] iti liṅgāt | ubhayatra japan sāvitrīm | tathāha manuḥ |
taṃ ced abhyudiyāt sūryaḥ śayānaṃ kāmakārataḥ |
nimroced vāpy avijñānāj japaṃs tūpavased dinam || iti | [MDh 2.220] | buddhipūrve abuddhipūrve ca idam eva, “nimroced vāpy avijñānāt” iti liṅgāt | atra sacelasnānaṃ draṣṭavyam, “sūryābhyuditanimruktaḥ sacelasnānaḥ sāvitrīṃ savanānugatāṃ japet” iti śaṅkhavacanāt | atrāpy “abhaye aroge” iti cānuvartate tatrādoṣakhyāpanārtham | tathāha jābāliḥ: “abhayarogasthaḥ sūryābhyuditaḥ prāyaścittīyo bhavati” iti | atra brāhmaṇasyārdhaṃ kṣatriyasyety etad draṣṭavyam || 23.21 ||
yas tu sūrya udayati svapiti sa sūryābhyudito brahmacārī sarvam ahar abhuñjānas tiṣṭhet | abhyastamitaś ca rātiṃ sarvām āsīta, “tiṣṭhed ahani rātrāv āsīta” [GDh 26.6] iti kṛcchre darśanāt | japan sāvitrīm ity ubhayatra samānam | brahmacārigrahaṇād gṛhasthādīnām anyat prāyaścittam | “ā tamitoḥ prāṇam āyacched ity eke” [ĀpDh 2.12.15] ity āpastambīyaṃ gṛhasthasya | āha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
vanasthaś ca yatiś caiva sūryeṇābhyudito yadi |
brahmakūrcāśinau bhūtvā japetāṃ drupadāṃ tv ahaḥ || iti | abhyastamitayor apīdam eva | āha prajāpatiḥ |
pālāśaṃ padmapatraṃ vā tāmraṃ vātha hiraṇmayam |
gṛhītvā sādayitvā ca tataḥ kūrcaṃ samārabhet ||
gāyatryādāya gomūtraṃ gandhadvāreti gomayam |
āpyāyasveti ca kṣīraṃ dadhikrāvṇeti vai dadhi ||
śukram api jyotir asīty ājyaṃ devasya tvā kuśodakam |
caturdaśīm upoṣyātha paurṇamāsyāṃ samācaret ||
gomayād dviguṇaṃ mūtraṃ sarpir dadyāc caturguṇam |
kṣīram aṣṭaguṇaṃ deyaṃ dadhi pañcaguṇaṃ tathā ||
sthāpayitvātha darbheṣu pālāśaiḥ patrakair atha |
tat samuddhṛtya hotavyaṃ devatābhyo yathākramam ||
agnaye caiva somāya sāvitryā caiva mantrataḥ |
praṇavena tathā hutvā sviṣṭakṛt praṇavena tu ||
etad brahmakṛtaṃ kūrcaṃ pavitraṃ ca tathaiva ca |
evaṃ hutvā tataḥ śeṣaṃ pāpaṃ dhyātvā samāhitaḥ ||
āloḍya praṇavenaiva nirmathya praṇavena tu |
uddhṛtya praṇavenaiva pibec ca praṇavena tu ||
etad brahmakṛtaṃ kūrcaṃ māsi māsi cared dvijaḥ |
sarvapāpaviśūddhātmā svargalokaṃ sa gacchati ||
yat tvagasthigataṃ pāpaṃ dehe tiṣṭhati dehinām |
brahmakūrco dahet sarvaṃ pradīptāgnir ivendhanam || iti |
buddhipūrve ‘buddhipūrve sādhāraṇam idam | tathā ca manuḥ |
taṃ ced abhyudiyāt sūryaḥ śayānaṃ kāmakārataḥ |
nimloced vāpy avijñānāj japann upavased dinam || iti | [MDh 2.220] | dinam ity upalakṣaṇaṃ nimlocane rātrim upavased iti | abhayarogasya iti jābālivacanād bhaye roge ca prāyaścittam na bhavati || 23.21 ||
aśuciṃ dṛṣṭvādityam īkṣeta prāṇāyāmaṃ kṛtvā || 23.22 ||
aśuciḥ caṇḍālādiḥ, taṃ dṛṣṭvā ekaṃ prāṇāyāmaṃ kṛtvā ādityaṃ paśyet | prakaraṇāt brahmacāriṇo niyamakāla idam | naiṣṭhikādīnāṃ manunoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
ācamya prayato nityaṃ japed asucidarśane |
sauryān mantrān yathotsāhaṃ pāvamānīś ca śaktitaḥ || iti | [MDh 5.86] | gṛhasthasya praṇavo draṣṭavyaḥ, “aśucidarśane dvijaḥ praṇavaṃ japet” iti jābāligṛhyadarśanāt | sarveṣāṃ niyamakālād anyatrādityadarśanamātram eva, “aśucidarśana āditayadarśanaṃ brāhmaṇadarśanaṃ vā gavām agner vā” ity auśanasavacanāt | jābāligṛhye dvijagrahaṇāc ca dvijātīnām idaṃ sāmānyam, śūdrasya na vidhiḥ na pratiṣedhaḥ || 23.22 ||
aśuciś caṇḍālādiḥ | taṃ dṛṣṭvā prāṇāyāmam ekaṃ kṛtvā sūryam īkṣeta | japādiniyamakāla idaṃ brahmacāriṇaḥ |
ācamya prayato nityaṃ japed aśucidarśane |
sauryān mantrān yathotsāhaṃ pāvamānīś ca śaktataḥ || [MDh 5.86] iti mānavaṃ naiṣṭhikādīnām | aśucidarśane dvijaḥ praṇavaṃ japed iti jābāligṛhyavacanaṃ gṛhasthaviṣayam | aśucidarśana ādityadarśanaṃ brāhmaṇadarśanaṃ gavām agner vety auśanasaṃ niyamakālād anyatra | jābāligṛhye dvijagrahaṇāc chūdrasya na vidhir na pratiṣedhaḥ
|| 23.22 ||
abhojyabhojane niṣpurīṣībhāvaḥ || 23.23 ||
abhojyabhojane ‘medhyaprāśane vā niṣpurīṣībhāvaḥ || 23.23 ||
abhojyānāṃ laśunādīnāṃ bhojane chardanena virecanena vā niṣpurīṣībhāvaḥ kartavyaḥ | tathāha manuḥ |
abhojyam annaṃ nāttavyam ātmanaḥ śuddhim icchatā |
ajñātabhuktam udgāryaṃ śodhyaṃ vāpy āśu śodhanaiḥ || iti | [MDh 11.160]
jīrṇatvāt kālātikrame chardanavirecanāsaṃbhave smṛtyantaroktaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha prajāpatiḥ |
abhojyabhojanaṃ kṛtvā dadyād viprāya tadvyayam |
ārdravāsā dinaṃ tiṣṭhed yad vā dadyād gavāhnikam || iti | parigrahaduṣṭabhojana idam, viprāya tadvyayaṃ dadyād yathābhyastaṃ tadasaṃbhave gavāhnikaṃ vā dadyāt, tasyāpy asaṃbhave haviṣyaṃ sakṛt bhuktvā jalārdravāsās tiṣṭhed yāvanti dināny abhyastāni tāvanty ahorātrāṇīti tatra kramaḥ | kālāśrayasvabhāvaduṣṭānāṃ vyāghroktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
abhojyabhojanaṃ kṛtvā naktabhojanam ācaret |
akṣāralavaṇaṃ vāpi bhūñjīyāc ca dine dine ||
kapilāyās tu dugdhāyā dhāroṣṇaṃ goḥ payaḥ pibet |
eṣa vyāghrakṛtaḥ kṛcchraḥ śvapākam api śodhayet || iti | atrāpi kāladuṣṭabhojane naktabhojanam, āśrayaduṣṭabhojane akṣāralavaṇānnabhojanam | svabhāvaduṣṭabhojane kapilākṣīrapānam iti | dine dine iti sarvaśeṣaḥ | yāvanti dināni bhuktāni tāvanti dinānīty arthaḥ | tatrāpi sakṛdbhojane pūrvaślokoktānāṃ madhye vyayadānādīnāṃ tāvanmātrasyaiva dānaṃ draṣṭavyam | ārdravāsastāpi dinamātram eveti | naktabhojanaśloke ‘pi sakṛdbhojane saṃpūrṇāhāratā dvirbhojane ardhāhārateti | kṣatriyavaiśyayos tv abhakṣyabhakṣaṇe ardhasyārdhārdhasya ca praveśe kavaṣokto nyāyaḥ | evaṃ ca sati vyayadānaślokoktānām ardham ardhaṃ parikalpyam | naktabhojanaśloke rasadravyarahitaṃ brāhmaṇasya, kiṃcidrasadravyasahitaṃ kṣatriyasya, tadbahulaṃ vaiśyasyeti draṣṭavyam | kṣīrapānaśloke ‘pi kṣatriyasya dviḥ pānam, vaiśyasya triḥ pānam iti | evaṃ caturvidhasya abhakṣyasyājñānabhojane chardanavirecanavyayadānāni kṛtvā vakṣyamānam “trirātrāvaram abhojanam” [GDh 23.24] ityādiprāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam | buddhipūrve vyayadānādi kṛtvā prāyaścittaṃ kartavyam, vyayadānādiśloke viśeṣābhāvāt | chardanavirecanaviṣaye “jñātaṃ dagdham” ity uktatvāc ceti || 23.23 ||
“nityam abhojyaṃ keśakīṭāvapannam” [GDh 17.8–9] ity ārabhyābhojyāny uktāni | tāni ca bahuprakārāṇi | jātiduṣṭāni laśunādīni | kāladuṣṭāni paryuṣitādīni | parigrahaduṣṭāny utsṛṣṭādīnām annāni | saṃsargaduṣṭāni keśakīṭādyupahatāni | kriyāduṣṭāny ācamanotthānavyapetādīni | teṣām abhojyānāṃ bhojane ca | medhyaṃ pavitram | amedhyam apariśuddhaṃ sthānapātrapākasparśapradātrādinā | teṣām amedhyānāṃ prāśane ca niṣpurīṣībhāvaḥ kāryaḥ | yathā niṣpurīṣam udaraṃ bhavati tathā kāryam || 23.23 ||
adhunā prāyaścittam āha |
tat katham |
trirātrāvaram abhojanam || 23.24 ||
“prāk pañcanakhebhyaḥ” [GDh 23.27] ity anenāśrayaparigrahaṇakāladuṣṭānāṃ vakṣyamāṇatvāt svabhāvaduṣṭānām idaṃ veditavyam | tatra laśunādīnāṃ paśūnāṃ ca “saptarātraṃ vā, svayaṃ śīrṇāni” [GDh 23.25–26] iti dvābhyāṃ sūtrābhyāṃ vakṣyamāṇatvāt, tebhyo ‘nyeṣām idam iti cāvagantavyam | tatrāpi buddhipūrve sakṛdbhojana idam, abuddhipūrve sakṛdbhojane manunoktaṃ draṣṭavyam: “śeṣeṣūpavased ahaḥ” [MDh 5.20] iti | atrāpi bakādīnāṃ pakṣiṇāṃ buddhipūrve sakṛdbhojane smṛtyantaroktaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha śaṅkhaḥ: “bakabalākacakravāka-haṃsaplavakhañjarīṭakāraṇḍa-vavaṭahāracaṭakagṛhakapotaśukaśārikakiraka-sārasaṭiṭṭibholūkakapotaraktapādajālapādacāṣa-bhāsamadgubhañjiśiṃśumāranakrama-karatimitimiṅgilavalmīkalabhisamāṃsabhakṣaṇe dvādaśarātram anāhāraḥ pibed vā gomūtrayāvakam” iti | abuddhipūrve uśanā āha: “balākaplavahaṃsa-kāraṇḍavacakravākakhañjarīṭagṛhakapotavaṭahāra-vīṭakaraktapādolūkaśukasārasaṭiṭṭibhabhāsamadgu-mahāṭiṭṭibhacāṣabhāsajālapādanakrakulika-vikṛtamatsyakravyādādīnāṃ māṃsabhakṣaṇe pañcagavyaṃ pibet trirātram” | avagūraṇāt sarvālpatvam ataś ca trirātraṃ pañcarātraṃ veti sāmarthyāpekṣayā draṣṭavyam || 23.24 ||
tisro rātrīr na kiṃcid bhuñjīta | na kiṃcit khādayet | na kiṃcit pibet | evaṃ niṣpurīṣībhāvo ’vāpyate | avaragrahaṇāc catūrātrāder api bhāvaḥ (lābhaḥ) | parameṇa saptarātram | tathā ca āpastambaḥ: “abhojyaṃ bhuktvā naiṣpurīṣyaṃ tat saptarātreṇā-vāpyate” [ĀpDh 1.27.3–4] iti || 23.24 ||
saptarātraṃ vā || 23.25 ||
saptarātraṃ vā svayaṃśīrṇāny upabhuñjānaḥ phalāny anatikrāman || 23.25 ||
laśunādiviṣayam idam | yathāha manuḥ |
chatrākaṃ viḍvarāhaṃ ca laśunaṃ grāmakukkuṭam |
palāṇḍuṃ gṛñjanaṃ caiva matyā jagdhvā pated dvijaḥ ||
amatyaitāni ṣaḍ jagdhvā kṛcchraṃ sāntapanaṃ caret || iti | [MDh 5.19] | evaṃ ca sāntapane na vikalpo draṣṭavyaḥ | abuddhipūrve sāntapanaṃ saptarātropavāsaś ca || 23.25 ||
śudhyatīti śeṣaḥ | atha vā nopavaset kiṃ tu svayaṃśīrṇāni svayaṃpatitāni phalāni bhuñjāno ‘natikrāmann asvāduphalopalambhe tadatikrameṇa svāduphalāntaragrahaṇārtham agacchan saptarātram evaṃ kurvañ śudhyati || 23.25 ||
svayaṃ śīrṇāny upayuñjānaḥ phalāny anatikrāman || 23.26 ||
vāśabdo ‘dhikriyate | śudhyed ity asyādhyāhāraḥ | phalāni svayaṃ śīrṇāni kākādibhir apāṭitāni | sthāvarahiṃsāpratiṣedhārthaṃ svayaṃśīrṇagrahaṇam | tāny upayuñjānaḥ anatikrāman guṇadoṣaparīkṣām akurvann ity abhiprāyaḥ | kṣutpramāpaṇam idam | iyam avasthā yāvatā kālena saṃpadyate tāvatā śudhyatīty arthaḥ | abhakṣyāṇām ekaśaphādīnāṃ paśūnaṃ bhakṣaṇe abuddhipūrve manunoktaṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ draṣṭavyam |
jagdhvā tu śuṣkamāṃsāni bhaumāni kavakāni ca |
ajñātaṃ caiva sūnāstham etad eva vrataṃ caret || iti | [MDh 11.155] | buddhipūrve taptakṛcchrasahitaṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ draṣṭavyam | tathāha śāṇḍilyaḥ |
abhakṣyabhakṣaṇe vipraḥ paśūnāṃ buddhipūrvakam |
taptakṛcchreṇa sahitaṃ carec cāndrāyaṇavratam ||
iti || 23.26 ||
prāk pañcanakhebhyaḥ chardanaṃ ghṛtaprāśanaṃ ca || 23.27 || — || 23.26 ||
pañcanakhebhyaḥ prāg yad abhojyam uktaṃ tad abhyavahāre chardanaṃ ghṛtaprāśanam ca prāyaścittam, na pūrvavat prāyaścittāntaram api | niṣpurīṣībhāva ity anenaiva siddhe ārambhasāmarthyāc caśabdāt kvacid upavāsaś ca | asamāso doṣāpekṣayā vyastasamastakriyārthaḥ | tatra prāg abhiśastādibhyo ghṛtaprāśanamātram | ghṛtaprāśane cānyāhārapratiṣedhaḥ | tatprāśanaṃ ca yāvatā kāyāplavamātraṃ bhavati | prāg apāṅktyādibhyaś chardanaghṛtaprāśane, prāg gokṣīrāder ekarātropavāsaḥ, prāk pañcanakhebhyaś chardanamātram ity evaṃ kalpyam | apañcanakhānāṃ tūktam “śvāpadoṣṭrakharāṇāṃ cāṅgasya” [GDh 23.5] iti | abuddhipūrve sakṛdbhojana idam | buddhipūrve manunoktaṃ taptakṛcchraṃ draṣṭavyam |
bhuktvāto ‘nyatamasyānnam amatyā tu tryahaṃ kṣapet |
matyā bhuktvā caret kṛcchraṃ retoviṇmūtram eva vā || iti | [MDh 4.222] | atra abuddhipūrve chardanāsaṃbhave tryahopavāso draṣṭavyaḥ | eteṣām uktānāṃ caturvidhānāṃ buddhipūrvābhyāse paśulaśunādivarjitānāṃ pratilomapatitasūtaka-śāvānnavarjitānāṃ ca manunoktaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha |
abhojyānāṃ tu bhuktvānnaṃ strīśūdrocchiṣṭam eva ca |
jagdhvā māṃsam abhakṣyaṃ ca saptarātraṃ yavān pibet || iti | [MDh 11.152] | aṅgirasoktaṃ vā |
abhakṣyāṇām apeyānām alehyānām ca bhakṣaṇe |
retomūtrapurīṣāṇāṃ prāyaścittaṃ kathaṃ bhavet ||
padmodumbarabilvānāṃ kuśāśvatthapalāśayoḥ |
eteṣām udakaṃ pītvā saptarātreṇa śudhyati ||
ity abhakṣyabhakṣaṇaprāyaścittam | śaktasya kṛcchro draṣṭavyaḥ | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “śva-kākāvalīḍhaśūdrocchiṣṭabhojaneṣv atikṛcchraḥ, kṛcchra itareṣu” [VaDh 14.33] iti | laśunādīnām abhyāse manunoktaṃ yaticāndrāyaṇam draṣṭavyam |
yaticāndrāyaṇaṃ vāpi śeṣeṣūpavased ahaḥ || iti | [MDh 5.20] | paśūnām abhyāse cāndrāyaṇatrayaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha prajāpatiḥ |
cāndrāyaṇatrayaṃ kuryād abhakṣyapaśubhakṣaṇe |
ekam eva sakṛt kṛtvā cared āha prajāpatiḥ || iti |
patito dviprakāraḥ – patito mahāpatita iti | mahāpatito mahāpātakī | yathāha manuḥ |
brahmahatyā surāpānaṃ steyaṃ gurvaṅganāgamaḥ |
mahānti pātakāny āhuḥ saṃyogaṃ caiva taiḥ saha || iti | [MDh 11.53] | etebhyo ‘nyaḥ patitaḥ, tasya pātityaṃ dvijātikarmabhyo hāniḥ | tatra patita abhojyānnamadhye parigaṇitaḥ, aspṛśyatvād evetarasya siddhatvāt | evaṃ ca patito ‘spṛśyo na bhavati | yathāha uśanā: “patitopapātakāv abhojyānnau mahāpātako ‘spṛśyaś ca” iti | evaṃ ca mahāpatakina abhojyānnamadhye parigaṇitatvāt prāyaścittāntaraṃ draṣṭavyam | tatra pratilomamahāpātakinor akāmataḥ sakṛdbhojane atikṛcchro draṣṭavyaḥ | yathāha hārītaḥ |
yad annaṃ pratilomasya śūdrajasyottamastriyāḥ |
mahāpātakinaś caiva yad annaṃ strīkṛtaghnayoḥ ||
ārūḍhapatitasyaiva sagotrābhartur eva ca |
pāṣaṇḍam āśritānāṃ ca yateś caiva tathaiva ca ||
atikṛcchraṃ cared bhuktvā pramādād brāhmaṇaḥ sakṛt |
matyā cāndrāyaṇaṃ kuryād āmaṃ ced ardham eva ca ||
abhojane tadardhaṃ ca triguṇaṃ sahabhojane |
caturguṇaṃ taducchiṣṭe pānīye ‘rdhārdham eva ca || iti | evaṃ cāmaṃ ced ardham evetyādi sarvatra draṣṭavyam | amatyābhyāse ‘pi tathaiva śrūyate |
kṛcchrābdapādam ucchiṣṭam abhyāse ‘jñānabhojane |
matyābhyāse tathā kuryāt triṃśat kṛcchraṃ dvijottamaḥ || iti | tatra caṇḍālādīnāṃ matyābhyāse tatsāmyam eva draṣṭavyam | yathāha manuḥ: “caṇḍālāntyastriyo gatvā” [MDh 11.175] ityādi | mahāpātakaviṣaye ‘py āpastamboktaṃ draṣṭavyam:
adhonābhy uparijānv ācchādya triṣavaṇam upaspṛśan akṣāralavaṇaṃ bhuñjāno dvādaśa varṣāṇi nāgāraṃ praviśet | atha siddhir atha saṃprayogaḥ syād āryair etad evānyeṣām api patanīyānām | iti | [ĀpDh 1.28.11–14] | pratilomānulomaviṣaye sarvatra prāyaścittaṃ kalpyam | pratilomānāṃ śūdraprabhavānām anulomānāṃ śūdrāyāṃ dvijātyutpannānāṃ ca śūdrānnabhojanavad draṣṭavyam | yathāha śāṇḍilyaḥ |
śūdrasya śūdrajasyaiva vyutkramāt tatstriyām api |
utpannasya dvijātīnāṃ samam annam akalpayan || iti |
śūdrānnabhojane tu prajāpatir āha |
brāhmaṇasyaiva śūdrānnam abhojyaṃ parikīrtitam |
sakṛd bhuktvā tad ajñānāt brahmakūrcaṃ sakṛt pibet ||
abhyāse tu tad abhyasyed āmam annam agarhitam |
matyā trirātraṃ kurvīta kṛcchram abhyāsakṛc caret || iti | sūtakaśāvānnabhojane ‘pi smṛtyantaroktaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha gargaḥ |
śāve ca sūtake caiva matyā bhuktvaindavaṃ caret |
matyābhyāse tataḥ kuryāt kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyanottaram ||
tryahaṃ tv akāmataḥ kuryād abhyāse kṛcchram eva ca || iti |
etad api brāhmaṇasya brāhmaṇāśauce | kṣatriyādīnām āśauce ‘pi tatrivoktam |
dviguṇaṃ triguṇaṃ caiva caturguṇam athāpi ca |
kṣatraviṭśūdrajātīnām āśauce parikīrtitam || iti |
tatra brāhmaṇasya brāhmaṇāśauce yat prāyaścittam uktaṃ tad eva kṣatriyādīnām api draṣṭavyam | dviguṇatriguṇakrameṇādhikaṃ hīnajātiṣu draṣṭavyam | uttamajātiṣu tatkrameṇaiva hīnam | tathā tatraivābhihitam |
samānajātiṣu sarveṣāṃ vipravan niṣkrayaḥ smṛtaḥ |
kramād vṛddhaṃ kramād dhīnaṃ hīnajātyuttameṣv api || iti | sarvatrāśaucottarakālam eva prāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam,
yady annam atti teṣāṃ yaḥ sa daśāhena śudhyati | [MDh 5.102] iti liṅgāt | tatra niyogād bhuktasya vasiṣṭhoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
āśauce yas tu śūdrasya sūtake cāpi bhuktavān |
krimibhir bhūtvā svadehānte tāṃ viṣṭhām upajīvati ||
anirdaśāhe ca śave niyogāt bhuktavān dvijaḥ |
sa gacchen narakaṃ ghoraṃ tiryagyoniṣu jāyate || iti | [VaDh 4.31–32] | “dvādaśa māsān dvādaśārdhamāsān vā anaśnan saṃhitādhyayanam adhīyānaḥ pūto bhavatīti vijñāyate” [VaDh 4.33] iti | tatraivaṃ kalpyam – śūdrāśauce bhuktasya dvādaśa māsān vaiśyasya daśa māsān, kṣatriyasyāṣṭau, brāhmaṇasya dvādaśārdhamāsān iti | evaṃ laśunādīnāṃ pṛthag upalabdhatvāt tadvarjitānām eva manunoktaṃ saptarātraṃ yavāgūpānaṃ draṣṭavyam | upapātakamadhye pāṭho ‘pi tadvad asaṃvyavahārārthaṃ na prāyaścittapraveśārtham avakīrṇyādivat śāntyudakapraveśārthaṃ vā | buddhipūrvābhyāse surāpānasamaṃ prāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam,
garhitānnādyayor jagdhiḥ surāpānasamāni ṣaṭ | [MDh 11.56] iti manuvacanāt | tad api cāndrāyaṇadvayam | yathāha gargaḥ |
abhakṣyāṇām abhojyānāṃ kāmato bhojane kṛte |
cāndrāyaṇadvayaṃ kuryāt surāpānasamaṃ hi tat || iti | bhojayitur dviguṇaṃ draṣṭavyam, “bhojayitur dvigunaṃ daṇḍaḥ” iti kātyāyanenoktatvāt || 23.27 ||
tatraivābhojyaprakaraṇe pañcanakhāś ca śalyaketyādibhir aṣṭabhiḥ sūtrair yāny abhojyāny uktāni tebhyaḥ prāg yāny abhojyāni “nityam abhojyam” [GDh 17.8] ityādibhir ekonaviṃśatisūtrair uktāni teṣu bhukteṣu chardayitvā ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati | evaṃ ca pūrvakaṃ prāyaścittadvayaṃ svabhāvaduṣṭeṣu pañcanakhādiṣv evāvatiṣṭhate | atra viṣṇuḥ: “malānāṃ madyānām anyatarasya prāśane cāndrāyaṇaṃ kuryāl laśunapalāṇḍukagṛñjana-tajjaviḍvarāhagrāmakukkuṭanaramāṃsabhakṣaṇe ca sarveṣv eteṣu dvijātīnāṃ prāyaścittaṃ punaḥ saṃskāraḥ [ViDh 51.2–4] | bṛhaspatiḥ |
alehyānām apeyānām abhakṣyāṇāṃ ca bhakṣaṇe |
retomūtrapurīṣāṇāṃ śuddhiś cāndrāyaṇaṃ smṛtam ||
aṅgirāḥ |
alehyānām apeyānām abhakṣyāṇāṃ ca bhakṣaṇe |
retomūtrapurīṣāṇām ṛṣikṛcchro viśodhanam ||
padmodumbarabilvānāṃ kuśaparṇapalāśayoḥ |
eteṣām udakaṃ pītvā taptenaiva viśudhyati ||
kāśyapaḥ:
laśunapalāṇḍugṛñjanakukkuṭabhakṣaṇe medaḥśukrapāne ‘yājyayājane ‘bhojyabhojane ‘bhakṣyabhakṣaṇe ‘gamyāgamane caivaṃ prāyaścittaṃ brāhmaṇebhyo nivedya ṣaḍrātropoṣitaś cīrṇānte prācyām udīcyāṃ diśi gatvā yatra grāmyapaśūnāṃ śabdo na śrūyate tasmin deśe ‘gniṃ prajvālya brahmāsanam āstīrya tatpraṇītena vidhinā punaḥ saṃskāram arhati |
sumantuḥ:
laśunapalāṇḍugṛñjanabhakṣaṇe vīraśrāddhe sūtikābhojyānnamadhumāṃsamūtra-reto’medhyābhakṣyabhakṣaṇe sāvitryaṣṭasahasreṇa mūrdhni saṃpātān avanayet | etāny evāturasya bhiṣakkriyāyām apratiṣiddhāni bhavanti | yāni cānyāny evaṃprakārāṇi teṣv apy adoṣaḥ |
palāṇḍuṃ laśunaṃ caiva gṛñjanaṃ kavakaṃ tathā |
catvāry ajñānato jagdhvā taptakṛcchraṃ cared dvijaḥ || manus tu |
chatrākaṃ viḍvarāhaṃ ca laśunaṃ grāmakukkuṭam |
palāṇḍuṃ gṛñjanaṃ caiva matyā bhuktvā bhaved [pated?] dvijaḥ ||
amatyaitāni ṣaḍ jagdhvā kṛcchraṃ sāntapanaṃ caret |
yaticāndrāyaṇaṃ vāpi śeṣeṣūpavased ahaḥ ||
saṃvatsarasyaikam api caret kṛcchraṃ dvijottamaḥ |
ajñātabhuktaśuddhyarthaṃ jñātasya tu viśeṣataḥ || [MDh 5.19–21]
śātātapaḥ: “laśunapalāṇḍugṛñjanakusumbhaśara-kavakāmedhyabhakṣaṇe taptakṛcchraḥ” | viṣṇuḥ: “vṛntākakavakāśane sāntapanam” [ViDh 51.34] | paiṭhīnasiḥ: “laśunapalāṇḍugṛñjanabhakṣaṇe prājāpatyam” | devalaḥ: “abhakṣyabhakṣaṇe kṛcchram” | paiṭhīnasiḥ: “abhakṣyabhakṣaṇe taptakṛcchram” | saṃvartaḥ |
abhojyabhojanaṃ kṛtvā brahmakṣatraviśāṃ gaṇaḥ |
gomūtrayāvakāhāraḥ saptarātreṇa śudhyati ||
bṛhaspatiḥ|
pītvā śuklakaṣāyāṇi bhuktvā cānnaṃ vigarhitam |
bhaved aprayato vipraḥ karmaṇaḥ syād adhogatiḥ ||
viṣṇuḥ: “dadhivarjitāni sarvaśuklāni [-śuktāni?] cātra prāsyopavaset” [cf. ViDh 51.42] | iti prakṛtam | śaṅkhaḥ |
lohitān vṛkṣaniryāsān vraścanaprabhavāṃs tathā |
bhuktvā ṛbīsapakvaṃ ca trirātraṃ tu vratī bhavet ||
śaṅkhalikhitau: “sarvāsāṃ dvistanīnāṃ kṣīraprāśane ‘jāvarjam etad eva | atra ṣaḍrātram abhojanaṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ ca” iti prakṛtam | anirdaśāvigokṣīraprāśane tadahar abhojanaṃ sacailasnānaṃ ca | śātātapaḥ |
uṣṭrīkṣīram avikṣīram annaṃ ca mṛtisūtake |
corasyānnaṃ navaśrāddhe bhuktvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || paiṭhīnasiḥ: “avikharoṣṭramānuṣadugdhaprāśane punar upanayanaṃ prājāpatyaṃ ca” | baudhāyanaḥ: “aveḥ payaḥpāne kṛcchro ‘nyatra gavyāt | gavi trirātropavāsaḥ” [BDh 1.12.12–13] | śaṅkhaḥ |
anirdaśāyā goḥ kṣīram ājaṃ māhiṣam eva ca |
goś ca kṣīraṃ vivatsāyāḥ pītvā pakṣavrataṃ caret ||
kṣīrāṇi yāny apeyāni tadvikārāśane budhaḥ |
saptarātravrataṃ kuryād yad etat parikīrtitam ||
sumantuḥ: “ekaśaphoṣṭrasyandinyavistrīkṣīraprāśane gomahiṣyajānāṃ cānirdaśāhānāṃ kṣīraprāśane trirātraṃ yāvakas triṣavaṇaṃ ca” | viṣṇuḥ: “go’jāvimahiṣīvarjaṃ sarvapayāṃsi ca tāny apy anirdaśāhāni syandinīsaṃdhinīvivatsākṣīraṃ cāmedhyabhujaś ca kṣīraṃ prāśyopavaset” [ViDh 51.38–41] iti prakṛtam | hārītaḥ: “anuktānāṃ sattvānāṃ bhakṣaṇe ‘tikṛcchro grāmyāṇāṃ cāndrāyaṇam” | yamaḥ |
varāhaikaśaphānāṃ ca kākakukkuṭayos tathā |
kravyādānāṃ ca sarveṣām abhakṣyā ye ca kīrtitāḥ ||
māṃsamūtrapurīṣāṇi prāśya gomāṃsam eva ca |
śvagomāyukapīnāṃ ca taptakṛcchraṃ viśodhanam ||
upoṣya dvādaśāhaṃ vā kūṣmāṇḍair juhuyād ghṛtam ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ: “śvakukkuṭagrāmyasūkarakākagṛdhrabhāsa-vāyasapārāvatamānuṣakākolūkānāṃ māṃsādane saptarātram upavāso niṣpurīṣībhāvo ghṛtaprāśanaṃ punaḥ saṃskāraś ca kāryaḥ” [VaDh 23.30] |
biḍālakākākhūcchiṣṭaṃ jagdhvā ca nakulasya ca |
keśakīṭāvapannaṃ ca pibed brahmasuvarcalām || [= MDh 11.159]
keśakīṭāvapannaṃ ca strībhiḥ svādaṃs tathaiva ca |
śvodakyābhyāṃ ca saṃspṛṣṭaṃ pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
yamaḥ |
mākṣikaṃ phāṇitaṃ śākaṃ gorasaṃ lavaṇaṃ ghṛtam |
etāni hastadattāni bhuktvā sāntapanaṃ caret ||
śaṅkhaḥ |
ekapaṅktyupaviṣṭānāṃ viṣamaṃ yaḥ prayacchati |
yaś caivāśnāty ayaṃ sarvaḥ kuryād brahmahaṇi vratam ||
yamaḥ |
brāhmaṇakṣatriyaviśāṃ śūḍrāṇāṃ sahabhojanam |
prājāpatyaṃ taptakṛcchram atikṛcchraṃ tathaiva ca ||
cāndrāyaṇam iti proktaṃ prāyaścittaṃ krameṇa tu ||
śātātapaḥ |
yo ‘gṛhītvā vivāhāgniṃ gṛhastha iti manyate |
