annaṃ tasya na bhoktavyaṃ vṛthāpāko hi sa smṛtaḥ ||
vṛthāpākasya bhuktvānnaṃ prāyaścittaṃ cared dvijaḥ |
prāṇāyāmaṃ trir abhyasya ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati ||
aṅgirāḥ |
brahmakṣatraviśāṃ bhuktvā na doṣo ‘sty agnihotriṇām |
sūtake śāva āśauce asthisaṃcayanāt param ||
cāṇḍālaḥ śvapacaḥ kṣattā sūto vaidehakas tathā |
māgadhāyogavau caiva saptaite ‘ntyāvasāyinaḥ ||
antyāvasāyinām annam aśnīyād yas tu kāmataḥ |
sa tu cāndrāyaṇaṃ kuryāt taptakṛcchram athāpi vā ||
yamaḥ |
brāhmaṇānnaṃ dadac chūdraḥ śūdrānnaṃ brāhmaṇo dadat |
ubhāv etāv abhojyānnau bhuktvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || 23.26 ||
alam ativācālatayā | prastutaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ |
ākrośānṛtahiṃsāsu trirātraparamaṃ tapaḥ || 23.28 || — || 23.27 ||
H reads “trirātraṃ paramaṃ”
ākrośe atyantapātakopapātakayuktād anyatra | anṛtaṃ sākṣipratiśrutyādānādau | hiṃsāyāṃ sthāvarāṇām, jaṅgamānām uktatvāt | trirātraparamaṃ sarvaṃ bahurātram iti yāvat | ata ekarātraṃ dvirātraṃ trirātram iti kalpyam | tapo brahmacaryādy uktam | tat prāyaścittam | tatra brāhmaṇākrośe trirātram, kṣatriyākrośe dvirātram, vaiśyākrośa ekarātram, śūdrākośe ‘har iti | evam ākrośaviṣaye draṣṭavyam | anṛte ‘pi brāhmaṇādyarthe kramād draṣṭavyam | hiṃsāyāṃ ca phalavṛkṣe dvirātram, upajīvyacchāyāvṛkṣe ekarātram, evamādīnām aharmātram iti | idam api prayojanāntareṇa svāmyanujñayā vinā kṛtasyeti |
anye vyācakṣate – ākrośe dhvanimātreṇābhiśastena kenacit saṃmukham | saṃmukhābhiśaste tu vasiṣṭhoktaṃ draṣṭavyam: “brāhmaṇam anṛtenābhiśasya” ityādi | tatra pātakadhvanau trirātraṃ pātakasame dvirātram upapātaka ekarātram anyatrāharmātram iti | hiṃsāśabdena ca hiṃsāyāṃ kṛtavyavasāyina ucyante | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ |
jīvann ātmatyāgī kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caret trirātraṃ copavasen nityaṃ snigdhena vāsasā prāṇān ātmani saṃyamya triḥ paṭhed aghamarṣaṇam iti | api caitena kalpena gāyatrīṃ parivartayed api vāgnim upasamādhāya kūśmāṇḍair juhuyāt ghṛtam ||
iti | [VaDh 23.19–21] | tatra brāhmaṇādīnāṃ śastrādinā vraṇādi kṛtvā jīvataḥ kṛcchraṃ buddhimātre trirātopavāsaḥ, kṣatriyasya vraṇādi kṛtvā jīvataḥ jalārdravāsasaḥ trirātram aghamarṣaṇapāṭho gāyatrījapo vā buddhimātre trirātraṃ tapomātram, vaiśyasya vraṇādi kṛtvā jīvato jalārdravāsasḥ haviṣyaṃ bhuktvā nityaṃ tris triḥ kūśmāṇḍair homaḥ buddhipūrve trirātraṃ tapomātram idam, śūdrasya vraṇādi kṛtvā jīvata ahorātraṃ buddhimātre ‘harmātram ity evaṃ kalpyam | anṛtaśabdārtha eṣām api |
apare vyācakṣate – ākrośaśabdena daṇḍapāruṣyam ucyata iti |
tad ayuktam, brāhmaṇasya tāvat brāhmaṇadaṇḍapāruṣyam uktaṃ prāyaścittam: “abhikruddhāvagūraṇam” [GDh 21.20] ityādi | tenaiva brāhmaṇasyārdhaṃ kṣatriyasyety anenaiva sarveṣāṃ kalpayituṃ śakyam | ato vākpāruṣyaviṣaya evedam | tatrāpi śrotriyasyāśrotriyākrośa idam | aśrotriyasya śrotriyākrośe tu “śataṃ kṣatriyo brāhmaṇākrośe” [GDh 12.8] ityādi daṇḍānurūpaṃ parikalpyam | kṣatriyādīnāṃ svajātyākrośe ‘pi daṇḍānurūpaṃ kalpyam | daṇḍas tu manunoktaḥ |
asavarṇe dvijātīnāṃ dvādaśaiva vyatikrame |
padeṣv avacanīyeṣu tad eva dviguṇaṃ bhavet || iti | [MDh 8.269] | evaṃ śrotriyasyāpi prāyaścittānurūpo daṇḍaḥ parikalpyaḥ | yatra prāyaścittam eva vidhīyate daṇḍo noktaḥ tatra daṇḍaparikalpanā | yatra tu daṇḍa evokto na prāyaścittaṃ tatra prāyaścittaparikalpanā kartavyeti | evaṃ cāvacanīyapadeṣv api prāyaścittaṃ siddham | avacanīyam api kuṣṭhādibhir bahubhir ajātair abhiśaṃsanam, na pātakopapātakaviṣayam, tatra prāyaścittasya gurutvadarśanāt | anṛtadoṣaprāyaścittam apy āhitāgner arthaviṣaya idam | tathāha prajāpatiḥ |
anṛte somapaḥ kuryāt trirātraṃ paramaṃ tapaḥ |
pūrṇāhutiṃ vā juhuyāt saptateti ghṛtena svāhā || iti |
anāhitāgner arthaviṣaye “vāṅmanasor apacāre vyāhṛtayaḥ pañca” [GDh 25.12] iti yad vakṣyati tad draṣṭavyam | tad rahasyam iti cet, na, “prāyaścittam aviśeṣāt” [GDh 25.9] ity uktatvāt rahasye prakāśe na bhavatīti | anarthaviṣaye dakṣiṇaśravaṇasparśanam | yathāha vyāghraḥ |
anṛtoktau ṣṭhīvane ca dantasparśe kṣute tathā |
patitānāṃ ca saṃbhāṣe dakṣiṇaṃ śravaṇaṃ spṛśet || iti | sākṣibhāve tv anṛtenoktāv āhitāgnīnām anyeṣāṃ ca pātakasamatvam eva | yad uktaṃ “kauṭasākṣyam” [GDh 21.10] ityādi | pratiśrutyānṛtoktau hārīta āha |
pratiśrutyānṛtaṃ brūyān mithyā satyam athāpi vā |
sa taptakṛcchasahitaṃ carec cāndrāyaṇaṃ vratam || iti | brahmacāriṇo ‘pi viśeṣo draṣṭavyaḥ | yathāha gargaḥ |
trirātram ekarātraṃ vā brahmacāry anṛte caret |
madhumāṃsāśane kṛcchraṃ śavanirharaṇe tathā || iti | tatrārthaviṣaye trirātram, itaratraikarātram | māṃsasyābhakṣyasya bhakṣaṇe tu taptapūrvam idaṃ kartavyam | avakīrṇiprāyaścittavat abuddhipūrvaviṣayaṃ cet, buddhipūrvaviṣaye tu punaḥsaṃskāraś ca | tathāha prajāpatiḥ |
māṃsaṃ bhuktvā brahmacārī punaḥsaṃskāram ācaret |
abhyāse tv aindavaṃ caiva naiṣṭhiko dviguṇaṃ caret ||
vanasthaḥ triguṇaṃ kuryād yatiḥ kuryā caturguṇam |
māṃsāśane ‘nṛtoktau ca śavanirharaṇe tathā || iti |
śavanirharaṇaṃ tu brahmacāriṇa upādhyāyādivarjitānām | yathāha manuḥ |
ācāryaṃ svam upādhyāyaṃ pitaraṃ mātaraṃ gurum |
nirhṛtya tu vratī pretaṃ na vratena viyujyate || iti | [MDh 5.91] | tatra svaśabdaḥ pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyata ity uktam | tataḥ svam ācāryam eva, nācāryasyācāryam, evaṃ sarvatra,
guror gurau saṃnihite guruvad vṛttir iṣyate | [MDh 2.205] ity evaṃ mā bhūd iti | tataś ca tannirharaṇe vrataviyogo bhavaty eveti | tatra kṛcchraṃ kṛtvā punaḥsaṃskāra iti draṣṭavyam | ācāryādau vrataviyogābhāvād evāśaucasyāpy abhāvaḥ siddhaḥ, “śāvam āśaucaṃ daśarātram anṛtvigdīkṣita-brahmacāriṇāṃ sapiṇḍānām” [GDh 14.1] ity uktatvāt | daśāhād arvāg api piṇḍanirvāpaṇakāle aśucir bhavati | tathā śāṇḍilyaḥ |
brahmacārī yadā kuryāt piṇḍanirharaṇaṃ pituḥ |
tāvat kalam aśucaṃ syāt tataḥ snātvā viśudhyati || iti | asnātvā bhojane prajāpatyuktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
asnātvaiva yadā bhuṅkte piṇḍaṃ datvā pitur vratī |
spṛṣṭvā śavam udakyāṃ vā caṇḍālaṃ sūtikāṃ tathā ||
akāmatas trirātraṃ syāt buddhyā sāntapanaṃ caret || iti | gṛhasthādīnāṃ ca sūtikādisparśane bhojane smṛtyantaroktaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha vyāghraḥ |
aspṛśyasparśanaṃ kṛtvā yadā bhuṅkte gṛhāśrami |
akāmatas trirātraṃ syāt ṣaḍrātraṃ kāmataś caret ||
yatiś caiva vanasthaś ca kuryātāṃ tau tad eva tu || iti | yatra tu brahmacāryādīnāṃ viśeṣo nāsti tatra sarveṣāṃ samānam eva draṣṭavyam | yathāha uśanā |
caṇḍālaśvapacau spṛṣṭvā viṇmūtrocchiṣṭam eva ca |
trirātreṇa viśuddhiḥ syāt bhuktvocciṣṭaṃ ṣaḍ ācaret || iti | tathā vyāghro ‘pi |
caṇḍālodakyāsaṃspṛṣṭau snānam eva samācaret |
tenocchiṣṭena saṃspṛṣṭaḥ trirātraṃ tu samācaret || iti | tathā yamo ‘pi |
caṇḍālodakabhāṇḍeṣu yaḥ pibet tṛṣito jalam |
tatkṣaṇāt kṣipate yas tu prājāpatyaṃ samācaret ||
yadi na kṣipet toyaṃ śarīre yasya jīryate |
prājāpatyaṃ na dātavyaṃ kṛcchraṃ sāntapanaṃ caret || iti | tathā vyāso ‘pi |
caṇḍālabhāṇḍasaṃspṛṣṭaṃ pibet toyam akāmataḥ |
ahorātroṣitaḥ snātvā pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
tataḥ snātvā tathā kuryāt kāmāt sāntapanaṃ caret |
teṣāṃ taṭāke kūpe ca tathaivoktaṃ manīṣibhiḥ || iti |
tathā vyāghro ‘pi |
viṇmūtrocchiṣṭakūpasthā apaḥ prāśya dvijātayaḥ |
trirātreṇaiva śudhyanti kumbhe sāntapanaṃ smṛtam || iti | tathā śātātapo ‘pi |
mūtroccāraṃ dvijaḥ kṛtvā akṛtvā śaucam ātmanaḥ |
mohād bhuktvā trirātraṃ tu buddhyā sāntapanaṃ caret || iti | tathā vṛddhavasiṣṭho ‘pi |
ekapaṅktigato bhuṅkte na spṛśed itaraṃ yadi |
bhasmanā kṛtamaryādas tasya paṅktir na duṣyati || iti | tathā śaṅkhaḥ: “antarudakamūtrapurīṣakaraṇe sacelasnānaṃ mahāvyāhṛtihomaś ca” iti | tathā tatraivāparam api “retomūtrapurīṣāṇy udake kṛtvā trirātropoṣita idam āpaḥ pravahata iti japet” iti | tatraiva “duḥsvapnāriṣṭa-darśane ghṛtaṃ hiraṇyaṃ dadyāt” iti | evam anyāny api smṛtyantarāṇi draṣṭavyāni || 23.28 ||
mahāpātakopapātakayuktād anyatrākrośe satāsatā vā dośeṇātivāde sākṣyādiviṣayād anyatrānṛte tatroktatvāt | prāṇibhyo ‘nyatra hiṃsāyām, prāṇiṣūktatvāt | eteṣu nimitteṣu paramaṃ tapaḥ parameṇa trirātram anaśanaṃ brahmacaryaṃ kartavyam | paramagrahaṇād ekarātrāder api lābhaḥ | tatra brāhmaṇa ākrośe trirātram, kṣatriya dvirātram, vaiśya ekarātram, śūdre ‘har iti vyavasthā |
anṛte ‘py evam | phalāphalādyapekṣayā brāhmaṇādisvāmikeṣu vṛkṣādiṣu hiṃsāyām apy evam | atra prajāpatiḥ |
anṛte somapaḥ kuryāt trirātraṃ paramaṃ tapaḥ |
pūrṇāhutiṃ vā juhuyāt sapta te agna ity ṛcā ||
iti |
anṛtoktau ṣṭhīvane ca dantasparśana eva ca |
patitānāṃ ca saṃbhāṣe dakṣiṇaṃ śravaṇaṃ spṛśet || iti | idaṃ parihāsādinimittānṛtaviṣayam | hārītaḥ |
pratyāśrutyānṛtaṃ bhrūyān mithyā satyam athāpi vā |
sa taptakṛcchrasahitaṃ carec cāndrāyaṇavratam ||
prajāpatiḥ |
māṃsaṃ bhuktvā brahmacārī punaḥ saṃskāram arhati |
abhyāsa aindavaṃ caiva naiṣṭhiko dviguṇaṃ caret ||
vanasthas triguṇaṃ kuryād yatiḥ kuryāc caturguṇam |
māṃsāśane ‘nṛtoktau ca śavanirharaṇe tathā || iti | ||23.27 ||
ākrośe viśeṣaḥ |
satyavāk ced vāruṇīmānavībhir
homaḥ || 23.29 ||
satyavākye vāruṇīmānavībhir
homaḥ | 23.28 ||
yadi tu satyavākyenāvaruṣṭo bhavati satyenākrośati vā | evaṃ ca pūrvam asatyeneti gamyate | tato vāruṇīmānavībhiḥ prajāpatidevatābhiś ca ājyena, pūrvoktaṃ vā trirātraṃ homaḥ kāryaḥ | aviśeṣāt sakṛd eva homaḥ | vāruṇībhis tisṛbhis tathā mānavībhiś ca | bahuvacanaprayogād vikalpitābhiḥ samuccitābhiś ca | tatra śrotriyākrośe aśrotriyasya samuccitābhiḥ viparīte vikalpitābhir iti draṣṭavyam || 23.29 ||
ākrośe satyavākye sati vāruṇībhir mānavībhiś cājyahomaḥ kartavyaḥ | trirātraṃ paramam ity eva | “yat kiṃcedam,” “imaṃ me varuṇa,” “tat tvā yāmi,” “avate heḍa” iti vāruṇyaḥ | “agnir ukthe purohita” ity ārabhyādhyāyaparisamāpter mānavya ṛca ekonaṣaṣṭir manunā dṛṣṭāḥ | tāsv antyāś catasro “makṣū devavata” ityādyās taittirīyake saumāraudryamiṣṭau dhāyyatvena viniyuktāḥ | “asāv āditya” ity asminn anuvāke mānavī ṛcau dhāyye kuryād iti | sūtrakāro ‘py āha: “mānavī ṛcā dhāyye makṣū devavata” ity etāsāṃ dve iti | tatrāntyābhir ābhiś catasṛbhir homa ity eke | anye tu ṛgvedapāṭhitābhiḥ sarvābhir ṛgbhir iti || 23.28 ||
vivāhamaithunanarmārtasaṃyogeṣv adoṣam eke ‘nṛtam || 23.30 || — || 23.29 ||
vivāhakāle kanyāgateṣu varagateṣu vā avalakṣaṇeṣu śobhanakathane nānṛtakathanadoṣa ity abhiprāyaḥ | maithune gotraskhalanādau | narma parihāsaḥ | ārtena duḥkhitena ca saṃyoge yat tad duḥkhaśamanāyocyate, eteṣu nimitteṣu anṛtābhidhāne prāyaścittābhāvo doṣābhāvād ity eke manyante | gautamas tv eteṣv api doṣo ‘stīti manyate | tatra niyamasthasya gautamābhiprāyo draṣṭavyaḥ, itarasyaikīyaṃ matam iti || 23.30 ||
vivāhakāle kanyāvarayor asatsv api guṇeṣu kathiteṣv idaṃ te dāsyāmīti pratiśrutyāpradāne ca na dośaḥ | tathā maithunasaṃyoga idaṃ te dāsyāmīty uktvā maithune kṛte tasyādāne ‘pi na doṣaḥ | narma parihāsas tatsaṃyoge ‘nṛtavacane na doṣaḥ | tad yathā bhoktukāmaṃ gṛham āgataṃ śyālādikaṃ pratyucyate – ehi manya odanaṃ bhokṣyase bhuktaḥ so ‘tithibhir ity evaṃprāyam | ārtasaṃyoga ārtasya duḥkhopaśamāyānṛtavacane na doṣaḥ | tenaiteṣu nimitteṣv anṛtavacaneṣu na prāyaścittam iti || 23.29 ||
na tu khalu gurvartheṣu || 23.31 || — || 23.30 ||
guruprayojaneṣv avivāhādiṣv apy anṛtaṃ na vadet | khalv iti nipāto ‘vadhāraṇārthaḥ | na kadācid apy ekīymatenāpi | tuśabdo viśeṣārthaḥ | mātulādigurvartheṣv api na vadet viśeṣataḥ ācāryārtheṣv iti || 23.31 ||
guruprayojaneṣu vivāhādiṣv apy anrtaṃ na vaktavyam || 23.30 ||
kiṃ punaḥ tatra kāraṇam? idam eva |
kasmād yataḥ |
sapta pūruṣān itaś ca parataś ca hanti manasāpi guror anṛtaṃ vadann alpeṣv
apy artheṣu || 23.32 || — 23.31 ||
sapta āgāminaḥ putrādīn itaḥ parataś cātītān api pitrādīn hanti pīḍayati pāpena yojayati | manasāpi gurvartham anṛtaṃ cintayann api, kim u vaktavyaṃ vadann ity arthaḥ | alpakeṣv api prayojaneṣu, kim uta mahatsu || 23.32 ||
ita ity ātmānaṃ nirdiśati | ātmānam ārabhya sapta puruṣān putrapautrādīn parataś ca sapta puruṣān pitṛpitāmahādīn hanti pīḍayati pāpena yojayatīti | manasāpi guror anṛtaṃ cintayann alpeṣv api prayojaneṣu kim aṅga mahatsu vācā vadann iti

antāvasāyinīgamane kṛcchrābdaḥ || 23.33 || — || 23.32 ||
antāvasāyinī vyākhyātā “yaś cāntāvasāyibhiḥ saha saṃviśet” [GDh 20.1] iti yatra | tasyāṃ maithuna ācarite sakṛdgamane kṛcchrābdaḥ | saṃvatasraṃ prājāpatyavidhināvasthānam ity arthaḥ || 23.33 ||
antyāvasāyinīnāṃ gamane maithunācaraṇe kṛcchrābdaḥ prāyaścittaṃ saṃvatsaraṃ prājāpatyavidhināvasthānam | buddhipūrva idam || 23.32 ||
amatyā dvādaśarātraḥ || 23.34 || — || 23.33 ||
evaṃ ca pūrvaṃ buddhipūrva ity avagamyate | kṛcchra iti vaktavye dvādaśarātragrahaṇaṃ parākopasaṃ-grahārtham | tathāha aṅgirāḥ |
antyajānāṃ tu gamane bhojane ca pramāpaṇe |
parākeṇa viśuddhiḥ syāt bhagavān aṅgiro ‘bravīt || iti | idam api retaḥsekāt prāg evoparatasya | kutaḥ? “retaḥ siktvā svayoniṣu” [MDh 11.170], iti pūrvakālakriyāsmaraṇāt | tasmāt prāyaścittalāghavam avagamyata iti | evaṃ ca kumārīgamanādāv apy ardhadaṇḍaprāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam | gamane tu nirvṛtte vasiṣṭhoktaṃ draṣṭavyam: “dvādaśarātram abbhakṣo dvādaśarātram upavaset | aśvamedhāvabhṛtaṃ vā gacchet | etenaiva caṇḍālīvyavāyo vyākhyātaḥ” [VaDh 23.38, 40–41] iti | buddhipūrve ‘bhyāse gurutalpaprāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam | tatrāpi samaśabdopādānāt manunoktaṃ draṣṭavyam: “sahasraṃ tv antyajāṃ striyam” [MDh 8.385] iti daṇḍavidhānāt sahasradaṇḍatulyatvāc cāndrāyaṇatrayaṃ draṣṭavyam | tatrābuddhipūrve dvitīyagamane yāvat prāyaścittaṃ tāvad evāgarbhotpatteḥ draṣṭavyam, nādhikakalpanāsti | kutaḥ?
saṃvatsarābhiśastasya duṣṭasya dviguṇo damaḥ |
vrātyayā saha saṃvāse caṇḍālyā tāvad eva ca || [MDh 8.373] iti liṅgāt | kūśmāṇḍahomo vā draṣṭavyaḥ | yathāha prajāpatiḥ |
sarvastrīṇāṃ ca saṃyogo gamane ca dvijottamaḥ |
prāyaścittād asaṃtuṣṭaḥ kūśmāṇḍair juhuyād ghṛtam || iti | garbhotpattau satyāṃ prāyaścittābhāvaḥ | yathāha śāṇḍilyaḥ |
antyastrīṣu prasūtasya dvijasyānicchayāpi ca |
niṣkṛtir nocyate tasya jātyā caṇḍāla eva saḥ || iti |
buddhipūrve ‘bhyāse ca prāyaścittābhāvaḥ |
caṇḍālāntyastriyo gatvā bhuktvā ca pratigṛhya ca |
pataty ajñānato vipro jñānāt sāmyaṃ tu gacchati || [MDh 11.175] iti manuvacanāt | atrānnabhojane yad uktaṃ prāyaścittaṃ tadardhaṃ pratigrahe ‘pi kalpyam | kutaḥ?
pakvād apakvam ardhaṃ syād yad anyat bhakṣaṇāgatam |
tasmād ardham idaṃ proktaṃ yad anyad dravyam ucyate || iti vyāghravacanāt || 23.34 ||
kṛcchre prakṛte dvādaśarātragrahaṇaṃ parākopasaṃgrahaṇārtham | tathā ca –
antyajānāṃ tu gamane bhojane ca pramāpaṇe |
parākeṇa viśuddhaḥ syād bhagavān aṅgirābravīt || iti | idam api retaḥsekāt prāg evoparatasya | ūrdhvaṃ tu vāsiṣṭham: “dvādaśarātram abbhakṣo dvādaśarātram upavased aśvamedhāvabhṛthaṃ vā gacchet | etena cāṇḍālīvyavāyo vyākhyātaḥ” [VaDh 23.38, 40–41]
iti || 23.33 ||
udakyāgamane trirātras trirātraḥ || 23.35 || — || 23.34 ||
striyā rajasvalāyā gamane trirātram abhojanam | gamanādhikāre punar gamanagrahaṇaṃ svadārād anyatra tadadhikapraveśārtham | svadāre cāsyaiva praveśārtham | tatraiva pratipattavyam – abuddhipūrve tu sakṛdgamane śātātapoktaṃ draṣṭavyam: “anudakamūtrapurīṣagrahaṇe śvakākasparśane sacelasnānaṃ mahāvyāhṛtihomaś ca rajasvalāgamane caitad evam” iti | abuddhipūrve ‘bhyāse ca vasiṣṭhoktaṃ draṣṭavyam: “rajasvalāgamane śuklaṃ vṛṣabhaṃ dadyāt kṛṣṇaliṅgam” iti | buddhipūrve tu sakṛdgamana idam | abhyāse tu manunoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
amānuṣīṣu govarjam udakyāyām ayoniṣu |
retaḥ siktvā jale caiva kṛcchraṃ sāntapanaṃ caret || iti | [MDh 11.173] | atra kṣatriyāyāṃ dviguṇaṃ vaiśyāyāṃ triguṇam iti draṣṭavyam | tathā brāhmaṇasyārdhaṃ kṣatriyasyetyādi veditavyam | gamanaṃ prati kūśmāṇḍahomaś ca draṣṭavyaḥ | dviruktir adhyāyaparisamāptyarthā || 23.35 ||
udakyāgamane sati brahmacaryānaśanadinā prāyaścittena trirātro gamayitavyaḥ | buddhipūrve sakṛdgamana idam | abhyāse mānavam |
amānuṣīṣu govarjam udakyāyām ayoniṣu |
retaḥ siktvā jale caiva kṛcchraṃ sāntapanaṃ caret || iti | [MDh 11.173] | abuddhipūrve sakṛdgamane śātātapoktam: “anudakamūtrapurīṣakaraṇe ca kākasparśane sacailasnānaṃ mahāvyāhṛtibhir homaś ca | rajasvalāgamane caivam” iti | abhyāse vāsiṣṭham: “rajasvalāgamane śuklam ṛṣabhaṃ dadyāt kṛṣṇapiṅga[la?]m” iti | [dviruktis uktārthā] || 23.33 ||
atha caturviṃśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
āviṣkṛtainasāṃ prāyaścittam uktam | anāviṣkṛtainasāṃ tu prāyaścittaṃ vaktavyam ity ata āha |
rahasyaṃ prāyaścittaṃ vakṣyate \
rahasyaṃ prāyaścittam avikhyātadoṣasya || 24.1 ||
rahasyam aprakāśaṃ kartavyaṃ yathānyair na jñāyate | prāyaścittagrahaṇam alpenāpi mahataḥ pāpasya niṣkṛtir bhavatīty evamartham | avikhyātadoṣasya lokenāvijñātainasaḥ | yenāntareṇa yat kartuṃ na śakyate tasmād anyair avijñātainasa ity arthaḥ | pāradāryapatitasaṃprayogādau na kevalaṃ kartur eva vijñānam, asaṃbhavāt | kathaṃ punar aprakāśe pāpe ‘lpadoṣatvam, na punar viṣabhakṣaṇādivad ekarūpateti | nātropapattir anveṣṭavyā, vacanagamyatvāt, matipūrvāmatipūrvabheda iva | atha vā avaśyakartavyatvād idam ucyate | yo hi prakāśaṃ pāpaṃ karoti sa ubhayaloke nirapekṣaḥ pravartate, tasya bahutaraḥ saṃkalpo bhavati, tataś ca doṣabhūyastvam, anyeṣāṃ pravṛttau hetubhāvaṃ ca pratipadyate gatānugatiko hi prāyaśo loka iti | tataś ca prakāśe lokanindānivṛttiḥ prāyaścittaṃ ca kartavye itaratra tu pāpakṣapaṇamātram eveti || 24.1 ||
yasya pāpasya doṣaḥ parair na vikhyātas tasya prāyaścittaṃ rahasyaṃ bhavati | yathā parair na jñāyate tathā kartavyam iti yāvat | yair vinā yat pātakaṃ kartuṃ na śakyate tadvyatiriktair jñātatvaṃ niṣidhyate | tena pāradārye patitasaṃvāse ca tair jñātatve ‘pi vakṣyamāṇaṃ bhavaty eva || 24.1 ||
caturṛcaṃ taratsamandīty apsu japed apratigrāhyaṃ pratijighṛkṣan
pratigṛhya vā || 24.2 ||
“tarat sa mandi dhāvati” ity etāś catasra ṛco ‘ghamarṣaṇarūpeṇāpsu japet, anyathā anyair jñāyata iti | trirātraṃ japo draṣṭavyaḥ | tathāha manuḥ |
pratigṛhyāpratigrāhyaṃ bhuktvā cānnaṃ vigarhitam |
japaṃs taratsamadhīyaṃ pūyate mānavas tryahāt || iti | [MDh 11.253] | haviṣyabhojane ca draṣṭavyam – apratigrāhyasya jātikarmaduṣṭasya yad dravyaṃ tat pratijighṛkṣan pratigrahītum icchan | evaṃ ca bruvann etad darśayati kāmakārakṛte ‘pi pāpe rahasyam asti icchāmātre ‘py apratigrāhyasya doṣo ‘stīti | “pratiṣiddhaṃ vāṅmanasor apacāre” [GDh 25.12] iti yad vakṣyati tasmād anyatra draṣṭavyam | pratigṛhya vā etad eva kuryāt | vāśabdād abhojyaṃ bhuktvā ca | tatra yadā pūrvam apratigrāhyo ‘yam iti  na jānāti tadā pratigṛhya japet, itaratra pūrvam iti draṣṭavyam | atrābhojyabhojane mūtrādivarjitasya draṣṭavyam, tatra śaṅkhenoktatvāt: “retomūtrapurīṣaprāśane oṃkāreṇābhimantryāpaḥ pibet” iti | yatra japaviśeṣo noktaḥ tatraikādaśāvaro draṣṭavyaḥ | tatra buddhipūrvābuddhipūrvabhede etāvān viśeṣaḥ – ‘buddhipūrvaṃ kṛto mayāpacāras tasyedaṃ prāyaścittaṃ bhaved’ ity evaṃ saṃkalpya kartavyam, tāvatā ca gurur bhavatīti | tathāha āpastambaḥ: “yaḥ pramatto hanti prāptaṃ doṣaphalaṃ saha saṃkalpena bhūyaḥ” [ĀpDh 1.29.2–3] iti | abuddhipūrve tu saṃkalpena vinā prāyaścittamātram eva kartavyam iti | tathā vasiṣṭho ‘pi pāpasya dhyānena prāyaścittasya gauravaṃ darśayati: “manasā pāpaṃ dhyātvoṃpūrvā vyāhṛtīr aghamarṣaṇaṃ vā paṭhet triḥ” [VaDh 23.23] iti | tataś ca buddhipūrvābuddhipūrvabhedena prāyaścittakalpanā | abhyāse ‘pi tad evābhyaset,
japādipūraṇaṃ kuryāt khyātadoṣo dvijottamaḥ |
rahaḥ kṛtasya pāpasya tat tad evābhyaset tathā ||
iti prajāpativacanāt || 24.2 ||
jātiduṣṭasya vā karmaduṣṭasya vā puruṣasya svabhūtaṃ svayaṃduṣṭaṃ ca kṛṣṇājinādy apratigrāhyam | gatyabhāvāt pratijighṛkṣan pratigrahītum icchaṃs taratsamandīdhāvatīti caturṛcaṃ sūktam apsu japet | nābhidadhne jale sthita ity eke | nimagna ity anye | pratigṛhya vā pratigrahāt paścād vā japet | evaṃ tulyavad vikalpaḥ | anye pratigrahāt pūrvam evāpratigrāhyam iti jñāte prāg japaḥ | paścāj jñāte paścāj japa iti | atra manuḥ |
pratigṛhyāpratigrāhyaṃ bhuktvā cānnaṃ vigarhitam |
japaṃs taratsamandīyaṃ mucyate mānavas tryahāt || iti | [MDh 11.253] | prajāpatiḥ |
japādipūraṇā kuryāt khyātadoṣo dvijottamaḥ |

idam abhyāsaviṣayam || 24.2 ||
abhojyaṃ bubhukṣamāṇaḥ pṛthivīm āvapet || 24.3 ||
abhojyaṃ caturvidhaṃ pūrvoktam | tad bubhukṣamāṇo bhoktum icchan | āpatpradarśanārthaḥ bubhukṣamāṇa ity asyopanyāsaḥ | tataś ca tadupabhogam antareṇa yadi jīvanaṃ na saṃbhavatīti draṣṭavyam | pṛthivīm āvapet prakṣipet |
apare tu “pṛthivyām āvaped” iti paṭhanti | vyācakṣate ca pṛthivyāṃ prakṣiped iti |
tad ayuktam, evaṃ saty anyair vijñāyata
iti || 24.3 ||
“nityam abhojyaṃ keśakīṭāvapannam” [GDh 17.8–9] ity uktam | yadi gatyabhāve tad eva bhoktum icchati tadā pṛthivīm āvapen mṛdaṃ prakṣipet tato bhuñjīta

ṛtvantarāramaṇa udakopasparśanāc chuddim eke || 24.4 ||
ekeśabdasaṃbandhārthā dvitīyā | ṛtumadhye āramaṇe uadkyāgamana ity arthaḥ | udakopasparśanāt sacelasnānāc chuddhim eka āhuḥ | gautamas tu sacelaṃ snātvā abliṅgābhir vāruṇībhiś cāpāmārjanam apīcchati | tathā ca vakṣyati: “anārjavapaiśuna” [GDh 25.10] ityādi | āramaṇa ity uktatvāt svadāraviṣayam idam | paradāreṣv etadadhikaṃ paradāraprāyaścittaṃ drṣṭavyam | tad api vakṣyati: “tad āhuḥ katidhāvakīrṇī praviśati” [GDh 25.1] ityādi || 24.4 ||
ṛtumadhya āramaṇa udakyāgamana udakopasparśanāt sacailasnānāc chuddhim āhur eke | “udakyāgamane trirātraḥ” [GDh 23.33] iti prakāśaviṣayam | ekegrahaṇaṃ paratrāpi saṃbadhyate || 24.4 ||
eke ‘strīṣu || 24.5 ||
strīṣu || 24.5 ||
eke astrīṣu vaḍavādyāsu govarjaṃ maithunam āsevya etad evodakopasparśanam icchanti | gautamas tu vakṣyati: “anārjava” [GDh 25.10] ityādau | gavi manunoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
tryahaṃ tūpavased yuktas trir ahno ‘bhyupayann apaḥ |

eke svastrīṣūdakopasparśanam anyatra trirātraṃ manyante | apara āha – strīṣu vaḍavādyāsv api govarjaṃ maithuna ācarita udakopasparśanāc chuddhim eke manyante || 24.5 ||
rahasye brāhmaṇavadha idānīm āha |
atha brāhmaṇavadhe rahasyam |
payovrato vā daśarātraṃ ghṛtena dvitīyam adbhis tṛtīyam || 24.6 ||
payovrato vā daśarātraṃ ghṛtena dvitīyam adbhis tṛtīyaṃ divādiṣv ekabhaktiko jalaklinnavāsā lomāni nakhāni tvacaṃ māṃsaṃ śoṇitaṃ snāyv asthi majjānam iti homā ātmano mukhe mṛtyor āsye juhomīty antataḥ sarveṣāṃ prāyaścittaṃ bhrūṇahatyāyāḥ || 24.6 ||
payovrataḥ kṣīrāhāraḥ | evaṃ prathamadaśarātram, dvitīyaṃ ghṛtena, tṛtīyam adbhir varteta | vāśabdād etat triṃśadrātraṃ haviṣyabhojano vā | śaktito vikalpo draṣṭavyaḥ || 24.6 ||
bhrūṇahatyā brahmahatyā | tasyāḥ prāyaścittam idam ucyate | ādita ekaṃ daśarātraṃ payovrataḥ kṣīrāhāraḥ syāt | dvitīyaṃ daśarātraṃ ghṛtena vartayet | tṛtīyam adbhiḥ | vāśabdād dhaviṣyabhojano vā | śaktito vikalpaḥ | eteṣu divaseṣv ekabhaktikaḥ payaḥprabhṛti kim api pūrvoktaṃ sakṛd evopabhuñjīta | kadā? divādiṣu prātaḥkāleṣu na sāyaṃ na madhyāhne | jalaklinnavāsā eṣu divaseṣv ārdravāsāś ca syāt | tathā homāś cāṣṭau pratyaham ājyena kartavyāḥ | tatra mantrāḥ – lomāni nakhāni tvacaṃ māṃsaṃ śoṇitaṃ snāyv asthi majjānam iti | teṣāṃ sarveṣām ātmano mukhe mṛtyor āsye juhomi svāhety ante prayoktavyam | juhoticodanā svāhākārapradāneti vacanāt | tad yathā | lomāny ātmano mukhe mṛtyor āsye juhomi svāhā, nakhāny ātmano mukhe mṛtyor āsye juhomi svāhety evaṃprakārā homāḥ || 24.6 ||
tasya guṇavidhānam āha |
divādiṣv ekabhaktiko jalaklinnavāsāḥ || 24.7 ||
divādiṣu pūrvāhṇeṣu ekabhaktikaḥ sāyam abhuñjāna ity arthaḥ | bahuvacanāt triṣu daśarātreṣu payovratapakṣa idam | tataś ca haviṣyabhojane dvirbhojanam aviruddham | jalaklinnavāsāḥ ārdravāsāś ca syāt | idam api “tadvrata eva” [GDh 24.12] ityādau na bhavatīti draṣṭavyam, prakāśo mā bhūd iti || 24.7 ||
prāyaścittaṃ bhrūṇahatyāyāḥ || 24.9 ||
etad yathoktaṃ prāyaścittaṃ bhrūṇahatyāyāḥ | prāyaścittādhikāre punaḥ prāyaścittagrahaṇam adṛṣṭārtham evaitat na tu dṛṣṭārtham apīti | bhrūṇagha iti puruṣanirdeśe kartavye pāpanirdeśaḥ sakṛt kriyāyām evaitad iti jñāpanārtham | abhyāse tu gurutaraṃ kalpyam, yan manunoktam |
enasāṃ sthūlasūkṣmāṇāṃ cikīrṣann apanodanam |
avety ṛcaṃ japed abdaṃ yat kiṃcedam itīti ca || [MDh 11.252] || 24.9 ||
atha bhrūṇahatyāyā evānyat prāyaścittam ucyate |

ukto niyamaḥ || 24.7 ||
athānyat prāyaścittaṃ vakṣyate | pūrvaṃ vaikalpikam | pūrvaprāyaścittāt bhrūṇahatyāyāḥ anyad iti | ukto niyamaḥ “payovrato vā” [GDh 24.6] ityādi | viśeṣās tu vidhīyante || 24.10 ||
“payovrato vā” [GDh 24.6] ityādir vakṣyamāṇe ‘pi veditavyaḥ || 24.7 ||
agne tvaṃ pārayeti mahāvyāhṛtibhir juhuyāt kūśmāṇḍaiś cājyam || 24.11 || — || 24.8 ||
“agne tvaṃ pāraya” [RV 1.189.2] ityādinā mantreṇa mahāvyāhṛtibhiś ca bhūrādyaiḥ kūśmāṇḍaiś ca “yad deva devaheḍanam” [GDh 27.7] ityādibhiś cānvahaṃ krameṇa sakṛd ājyaṃ juhuyāt | ājyagrahaṇaṃ siṃhāvalokitanyāyena pūrvatrāpy adhikartavyam | yatra yatra dravyānuddeśas tatra tatra sarvatreti kecit | cakārāt praṇavena ca || 24.11 ||
“agne tvaṃ pārayā” [RV 1,189.2] ity ṛcā mahāvyāhṛtibhir bhūrādibhiḥ kūṣmāṇḍair “yad devā devaheḍanam” [VS 20.14] ityādibhiś ca krameṇa sakṛd ājyaṃ juhuyāt || 24.8 ||
tadvrata eva vā brahmahatyāsurāpāna-steyagurutalpeṣu prāṇāyāmais tānto ‘ghamarṣaṇaṃ japet samam aśvamedhāvabhṛthena || 24.12 ||
tadvrata eva vā brahmahatyāsurāpāna-steyagurutalpeṣu prāṇāyāmais tānto ‘ghmarṣaṇaṃ japan samam aśva-medhāvabhṛthenedaṃ ca prāyaścittam || 24.9 ||
tenaiva coktena “payovrato vā” [GDh 24.6] ityādinā brahmahatyādicaturṣu pāpeṣu prāyaścittaṃ kuryāt | steyaṃ cātra suvarṇasteyaṃ brahmahatyāsurāpānagurutalpasāhacaryāt | prāṇāyāmais tāntaḥ khinnaḥ aghamarṣaṇam “ṛtaṃ ca satyaṃ ca” ityādi japet | samaṃ tulyam etad aśvamedhāvabhṛtena | surāpānādiṣv aśvamedhāvabhṛtasnānaṃ śuddhihetur ity etad anena darśayati | daṇḍāpūpikayāśavmedho ‘pi draṣṭavyaḥ | yathāha manuḥ |
yathāśvamedhaḥ kraturāṭ sarvapāpapraṇodanaḥ |
tathāghamarṣaṇaṃ sūktaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam || [MDh 11.260] iti || 24.12 ||
tadvrata eva vā tenaiva “payovrato vā” [GDh 24.6] ityādinā vratenopetaś caturṣu brahmahatyādiṣu pāpeṣu prāyaścittaṃ kuryāt | prāṇāyāmais tānto mlāno yāvadbhiḥ prāṇāyāmais tānto bhavati tāvadbhiḥ kuryād aghamarṣaṇam | aghamarṣaṇena ṛṣiṇā dṛṣṭam “ṛtaṃ ca satyaṃ ca” ityādināghamarṣaṇaṃ | tac cāśvamedhāvabhṛthena samaṃ tulyam | japann iti vartamānaprayogeṇa pratyaham eva triṃśadrātraṃ vrataṃ kuryāt | atra manuḥ |
yathāśvamedhaḥ kraturāṭ sarvapāpapraṇāśanaḥ |
tathāghamarṣaṇaṃ sūktaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam || [MDh 11.260] || 24.9 ||
sāvitrīṃ vā sahasrakṛtva āvartayet punīte haivātmānam || 24.13 || — || 23.10 ||
H reads “āvartayan”
adhikārāt tadvrata evaiteṣv eva pāpeṣu māsaṃ sāvitrīṃ bahukṛtvo ‘bhyasan śīghraṃ śuddhim āpnoti | heti nipātaḥ vaidikārthatvapradarśanārthaḥ | evety avadhāraṇārthaḥ | vety etad vacanam anyeṣv api pāpeṣu sāvitryabhyāsaḥ śuddhihetur iti pradarśanārtham | tathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
sahasraparamāṃ devīṃ śatamadhyāṃ daśāvarām |
śuddhikāmaḥ prayuñjīta sarvapāpeṣv avasthitaḥ || iti | [VaDh 26.15] | avadhāraṇaṃ pāvanatamatva-pradarśanārthaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha vyāghraḥ |
na sāvitrīsamaṃ japyaṃ na vyāhṛtisamaṃ hutam |
nānnatoyasamaṃ dānaṃ na cāhiṃsāsamaṃ tapaḥ ||
iti || 24.13 ||
tadvrata evety anuvartate | prāṇāyāmais tānta iti ca | sāvitrīṃ sahasrakṛtva āvartayann iti japyamātraṃ bhidyate | anyat samānam | evaṃ kurvann ātmānaṃ punīte brahmahatyādibhyaś caturbhyaḥ śodhayati | heti prasiddhau | evety avadhāraṇe | tataś cānyeṣv api pāpeṣu sāvitryabhyāsaḥ śuddhihetuḥ | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ |
sahasraparamāṃ devīṃ śatamadhyāṃ daśāvarām |

na sāvitrīsamam japyaṃ na vyāhṛtisamaṃ hutam |
nānnatoyasamaṃ dānaṃ na cāhiṃsāsamaṃ tapaḥ || iti || 24.10 ||
prāyaścittāntaram āha |
antarjale vāghamarṣaṇaṃ trir āvartayan sarvapāpebhyo mucyate mucyate || 24.14 ||
tadvrata evodakasyāntas triṃśadrātram aghamarṣaṇaṃ trir abhyasan sarvasmāt pāpāt jñānājñānakṛtāt mucyate śudhyatīty arthaḥ || 24.14 ||
tadvrata evodakasyāntarnimagnas triṃśadrātram aghamarṣaṇaṃ trir abhyasya sarvasmāt pāpāj jñānakṛtād ajñānakṛtāc ca mucyate | dviruktiś ca vyākhyātā || 24.11 ||
atha pañcaviṃśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
uktāni mahāpātakeṣu rahasyāni | athedānīm upapāta-keṣu vaktavyāni | tatrāvakīrṇinaḥ upapātakitvāt prāyaścittasyānyatrātideśāc ca tasya pūrvam ucyate | tatra nindātiśayayajñāpanārthā śrutir upanyasyate |
uktāni mahāpātakeṣu rahasyaprāyaścittāni | athopapātakeṣu vakṣyan prathamam avakīrṇinaḥ prāyaścittaṃ vaktuṃ tasya nindātiśayapradarśanāya śrutim udāharati |
tad āhuḥ katidhāvakīrṇī praviśatīti || 25.1 ||
taddoṣagurutvam asya paśyanto viśiṣṭā āhuḥ – kathidhā katiprakāram | avakīrṇiśabdo hy aviplutasya vācakaḥ, raktasyeti yāvat | tena kvāsya prāṇādi gacchatīty abhiprāyaḥ || 25.1 ||
prāṇena pañcavṛttinā maruto vāyūny praviśati | indraṃ balena praviśati | vṛttasvādhyāyasaṃpadbrahma-varcasena bṛhaspatiḥ | itareṇa sarveṇa cakṣurādinendri-yavargeṇāgnim eva praviśati | evam alpāyur nirutsāho brahmavarcasahīnaś cakṣurādihīnaś cāvakīrṇī bhavati | ataś caritavyaṃ prāyaścittam || 25.2 ||
evaṃ saṃśayam utpādya svayam evāha |
atrottaram |
marutaḥ prāṇenendraṃ balena bṛhaspatiṃ brahmavarcasenāgnim evetareṇa
sarveṇeti || 25.2 ||
marutaḥ prāṇenendre balena bṛhaspatiṃ brahmavarcasenāgnim ivetareṇa
sarveṇeti || 25.2 ||
maruto vāyūn prāṇena pañcavṛttinā praviśati, indraṃ balena utsāhaśaktyā, bṛhaspatim adhītaśrutyādijanitena tejasā, agnim itareṇa | evakārān nānyaṃ manaādi | sarvagrahaṇāc cakṣuḥśrotrādinā | aviplato rikto ‘lpāyur nirutsāho brahmavarcasahīnaś cakṣurādihīnaś ca saṃpadyate | seyam udvegajananārthaṃ prāyaścittaprasaṅgārthaṃ vā śrutir udāhṛtā || 25.2 ||
prāṇena pañcavṛttinā maruto vāyūny praviśati | indraṃ balena praviśati | vṛttasvādhyāyasaṃpadbrahma-varcasena bṛhaspatiḥ | itareṇa sarveṇa cakṣurādinendri-yavargeṇāgnim eva praviśati | evam alpāyur nirutsāho brahmavarcasahīnaś cakṣurādihīnaś cāvakīrṇī bhavati | ataś caritavyaṃ prāyaścittam || 25.2 ||
idānīṃ prāyaścittam āha |
tadānīm āha |
so ‘māvāsyāyāṃ niśy agnim upasamādhāya prāyaścittājyāhutīr juhoti || 25.3 ||
avakīrṇīti prastutatvāt sa ity anarthaka iti cet, rahasi khaṇḍitasyaiva parāmarśārthaḥ, anyathā śrutyudāharaṇāt sarveṣāṃ brahmacāriṇām idaṃ prāyaścittam ity āśaṅkā syād iti | amāvāsyāyāṃ kṛṣṇaparvaṇi niśi ardharātra iti draṣṭavyam, anyathā prakāśaḥ syād iti | agnim upasamādhāya kasmiṃścit kāṣṭhe mathitvety arthaḥ | itarathā juhotivacanād evāgneḥ siddhatvād avacanīyam evaitat syāt | prāyaścittagrahaṇaṃ vyatikramānantaraṃ prāyaścittānuṣṭhānajñāpanārtham, smṛtyantaroktasya vā
sāvitrīṃ ca japen nityaṃ pavitrāṇi ca śaktitaḥ | [MDh 11.225] ityevamādeḥ sāmānyasya prāyaścitta-vidheḥ prāpaṇārtham | ājyagrahaṇaṃ ca niyamārtham | tathā ca gṛhyasmṛtiḥ: “catasra ājyaprakṛtayo bhavanty ūdhasyaṃ vā bāhyaṃ dadhi payo vā” ity āhuti-grahaṇam avahitena homārtham || 25.3 ||
so ‘vakīrṇy amāvāsyāyāṃ niśy ardharātre gṛhyoktena mārgeṇāgniṃ pratiṣṭhāpyopasamādhāyājyabhāgānte prāyaścittarūpā ājyāhutīr juhoti || 25.3 ||
tatra mantrau |
kāmāvakīrṇo ‘smy avakīrṇo ‘smi kāma kāmāya svāhā | kāmābhidugdho ‘smy abhidugdho ‘smi kāma kāmāya svāheti || 25.4 ||
kāmāvakīrṇo ‘smy avakīrṇo ‘smi kāma kamāya svāhā | kāmābhidugdho ‘smy abhidugdho ‘smi kāma kāmāya svāheti samidham ādhāyānuparyukṣya yajñavāstu kṛtvopotthāya samāsiñcatv ity etayā trir upatiṣṭheta || 25.4 ||
sakalaḥ mantrapāṭhaḥ pratyakṣaśrutau tathopalabhyamānatvāt || 25.4 ||
homānta ekāṃ samidhaṃ tūṣṇīm ādhāya “adite ‘nv amaṃsthāḥ” [ĀpGṛ 1.2.8] ityādibhir agnim anuparyukṣati | tato yajñavāstu karoti | atra chandogānāṃ gṛhye sviṣṭakṛto ‘nantaraṃ paṭhyate: “samidham ādhāya darbhān ājye havīṃṣi ca trir avadhāyāgramadhyamūlāny “aktaṃ rihāṇā vi yantu vayaḥ” [TaitS 1.1.13.1] ity abhyukṣyāgnāv anuprahared yaḥ (yo?) bhūtānām adhipatī rudras tanticaro vṛṣā paśūn asmākaṃ mā hiṃsīr etad astu hutaṃ tava svāheti tad yajñavāstu sarvatra kuryād iti | tad etad yajñavāstu kṛtvopotthāyāgnisamīpe sthitvā samāsiñcatu, ity etayā ca trir agnim upatiṣṭheta || 25.4 ||
samidham ādhāyānuparyukṣya yajñavāstu kṛtvopotthāya saṃ mā siñcantv ity etayā trir upatiṣṭhet || 25.5 ||
mantradvayahomānta ekāṃ samidhaṃ tūṣṇīm eva hutvā tataḥ paryukṣya “adite ‘nvamaṃsthāḥ” ity evamādibhiḥ | tato yajñavāstu kṛtvā tat chandogānāṃ gṛhye paribhāṣitam | tatrāgisamīpe paścād utthāya “saṃ mā siñcantu” ity etayā prakṛtam agniṃ trir upatiṣṭhet
|| 25.5 ||
agnyupasthānastutyarthaṃ vaidikārthavādam āha |
trirupasthānasyārthavādaḥ |
trayo vā ime lokā eṣāṃ lokānām abhijityā abhikrāntyā iti || 25.6 || — || 25.5 ||
H reads “traya ime”
trayo lokā ime bhūrbhuvaḥsvarlokākhyāḥ | teṣām abhijitiḥ apracyavanam abhikrāntis tatraivotpattiḥ | abhijityā abhikrāntyā iti ca bruvan akṛtaprāyaścittasya avakīrṇinas triṣv api lokeṣv anavakāśaṃ darśayti | vaiśabdo ‘narthakaḥ, “anarthakāni vaidikākṣarāṇī” ity uktatvāt | “ime lokāḥ” iti pratyakṣeṇa nirdeśa ādarārthaḥ || 25.6 ||
trayo hi lokā bhūr bhuvaḥ svar iti | teṣām abhijitir bhogayogyatāpādanam | abhikrāntis tatraivādhipatyenādhiṣṭhāyāvasthānam | tadartham evaṃ kartavyam iti saṃbandhaḥ || 25.5 ||
etad evaikeṣāṃ karmādhikṛtya yo pūta iva syāt sa itthaṃ juhuyād ittham
anumantrayeta || 25.7 ||
etad evaikeṣāṃ karmādhikṛtya yo ‘prayata iva syāt sa itthaṃ juhuyād ittham anumantrayeta varo dakṣiṇeti prāyaścittam aviśeṣāt || 25.6 ||
etad eva karmādhikṛtya kṛtvety arthaḥ | ekeṣāṃ matena yo pūta iva syāt | adhikārād rahasyaprāyaścitte ‘pi kṛte yo pūtam ivātmānaṃ manyeta sa etam eva juhuyāt evam evāgnim upatiṣṭheta || 25.7 ||
yo ‘pūta iva syād anyo ‘py ātmānam apūtam iva manyate na kevalam avakīrṇī so ‘py etad evoktaṃ karmādhikṛtyetthaṃ juhuyād ittham anumantrayeta homam upasthānaṃ caivaṃ kuryāt | varo dakṣiṇā | gaur vai varaḥ | sā svayaṃkartṛkatvād brahmaṇe deyeti śravaṇaviśeṣāt | aviśeṣeṇa sarveṣām upapātakinām idaṃ prāyaścittam ity ekeṣāṃ matam || 25.6 ||
varo dakṣiṇeti || 25.8 ||
ante karmaṇo deyaḥ, svayaṃkartṛkatvād anyasmai brāhmaṇāya deyā | gauravān mahāpātakaviṣayaṃ draṣṭavyam | gautamasya tu tasyaiva prāyaścittasyāvartanam || 25.8 ||
prāyaścittam aviśeṣāt || 25.9 ||
upapātakināṃ sarveṣāṃ sāmānyam uktam eva prāyaścittam | kecid uparitanasūtraśeṣatvena vyācakṣate – prāyaścittaṃ vakṣyamāṇam aviśeṣād rahasye prakāśe ceti | tatra prakāśe vihitānāṃ śeṣatvena varṇanīyam, rahasye tu svātantryarūpeṇa | prāyaścitta-grahaṇam alpe ‘pi nānādaraḥ kārya iti || 25.9 ||
anārjavapaiśunapratiṣiddhācārānādyaprāśaneṣu śūdrāyāṃ ca retaḥ siktvāyonau ca doṣavati karmaṇy abhisaṃdhipūrve ‘py abliṅgābhir apa upaspṛśed vāruṇībhiḥ || 25.10 ||
anārjavapaiśunapratiṣiddhācārānādyaprāśaneṣu śūdrāyāṃ ca retaḥ siktvāyonau ca doṣavati ca karmaṇy api saṃdhipūrve ‘bliṅgābhir apa upaspṛśed vāruṇībhir anyair vā
pavitraiḥ || 25.7 ||
anārjavam anṛjutvaṃ mānasaṃ karma śāṭhyaṃ vā | paiśunaṃ paraparivādaḥ vācikam | pratiṣiddhācāro niyamalopaḥ kāyikam | anādyasyānekavidhasyopa-bhogaḥ anādyasya prāśanam | śūdrāyāṃ ca retaḥ siktvā maithunaṃ samāsevya | pṛthagupadeśaḥ parigṛhītāyām apīty evamarthaḥ | asyopapātakaprāyaścittam eva yady api bhavati, tathāpy āhatyopadeśād idam eveti draṣṭavyam | evaṃ sāmānyaviśeṣabhāvam ālocya sarvatra vyavasthā kartavyā | caśabdo rajasvalāyām upasaṃgrahārthaḥ | ayonau ca āsyādau | caśabdāt prayatnotsarge ca | doṣavati ca karmaṇi parapīḍāhetau karmaṇi kṛte | caśabdād vihitākaraṇe ca | abhisaṃdhipūrve ‘pi tādarthyenopakalpite ‘pīty arthaḥ | anādyaprāśanādi yady api pratiṣiddhasevanam, tathāpi yatnato varjanārthas teṣāṃ pṛthagupadeśaḥ, prāyaścittagauravārtho vā | tataś ca bahubhir mārjanaṃ draṣṭavyam | doṣavat karma steyam ity eke | tatra suvarṇasteyād anyad iti draṣṭavyam, tasya “brahmahatyāsurāpāna” [GDh 24.12] ity uktatvāt | hiṃsety apare | tatrāpi pātakopapātakaṃ varjayitveti draṣṭavyam, tayor uktatvāt | abhisaṃdhipūrve ‘pīty apiśabdo ‘bhyāse ‘pīti kecid vyācakṣate | tad ayuktam, pratyabhyāsaṃ prāyaścittāvṛtter uktatvāt | anabhisaṃdhipūrve ‘pīty evamarthaṃ tatra laghutvaṃ mā bhūd iti | abliṅgābhiḥ abdevatābhir āpohiṣṭhetyādikābhiḥ | bahuvacanāt tryavarābhiḥ | vāruṇībhiḥ varuṇadevatābhiḥ “imaṃ me varuṇa” ityevamādibhiḥ | pūrvaṃ snātvāpo mūrdhni nikṣipet | visamāsanirdeśo vikalpārthaḥ | tataś cābliṅgābhir vāruṇībhir veti draṣṭavyam || 25.10 ||
anārjavaṃ śāṭhyam | paiśunaṃ paradoṣasūcanam | pratiṣiddhācāro niṣiddhānuṣṭhānam | anādyam abhakṣyaṃ tasya prāśanam | eteṣu śūdrāyāṃ retaḥ siktvāyonau cāsyādiṣu vā retaḥ siktvā, doṣavati karmaṇi parapīḍātmake steyātmake ca saṃdhipūrve buddhipūrve, apiśabdād abuddhipūrve kṛte ‘bliṅgābhir “āpo hi ṣṭhā mayobhuvaḥ” [RV 10.9.1] iti tisṛbhir “hiraṇyavarṇāḥ śucayaḥ pāvakāḥ” [TS 5.6.1.1] iti catasṛbhiḥ, vāruṇībhir “yat kiṃcedam imaṃ me varuṇa” [RV 7.89.5], “tat tvā yāmi” [RV 1.24.11], “ava te heḍaḥ” [RV 1.24.14] ity etābhir anyair vā pavitraiḥ pavamānaḥ “suvarjana” ityādibhir apa upaspṛśet pūrvaṃ snātvā paścād añjalinā mūrdhni mantrair etair apaḥ kṣipet | yady apy anādyaprāśanam api pratiṣiddhācāras tathāpi pṛthagupādānāt teṣu bahubhir mārjanam anādyaprāśane yathāsaṃbhavaṃ draṣṭavyam || 25.7 ||
anyair vā pavitraiḥ || 25.11 ||
anyair aghamarṣaṇādibhir vā pavitrāṇām eva grahaṇaṃ yathā syād iti pavitragrahaṇam | saṃbhavato vikalpaḥ
|| 25.11 ||
pratiṣiddhavāṅmanasor apacāre vyāhṛtayaḥ pañca || 25.12 ||
pratiṣiddhavāṅmanasāpacāre vyāhṛtayaḥ pañca satyāntāḥ || 25.8 ||
pratiṣiddhāpacāre vāṅmanasor apacāre ca trayāṇāṃ pūrvaprāyaścittam | viśeṣārtham idam – vyāhṛtayo bhūrādyāḥ puruṣasatyāntāḥ pañca abliṅgābhir mārjanānantaram ādityopasthānarūpeṇaikādaśavāram udāhartavyāḥ || 25.12 ||
pratiṣiddhaviṣaye yo vāṅmanasayor apacāraḥ kutsitā pravṛttis tatra vyāhṛtayaḥ pañca japyā bhūrādayaḥ satyāntāḥ prathame ‘dhyāya uktāḥ | vāṅmanasor iti pāṭho ‘smabhyaṃ na rocate | acatureti samāsāntavidhiprasaṅgāt, pratiṣiddhagrahaṇasya ca duranvayatvāt || 25.8 ||
sarvāsv apo vācāmed ahaś ca mādityaś ca punātv iti sāyam || 25.13 ||
sarvāsv apo vācāmed ahaś ca mādityaś ca punātv iti prātā rātriś ca mā varuṇaś ca punātv iti sāyam || 25.9 ||
sarvāsu kriyāsu anārjavādikarmasv ity arthaḥ | sarvāsu vyāhṛtiṣu pratyekam ācamanam iti kecit | tad ayuktam, sarvābhir iti prasaṅgāt | anantarāṇāṃ trayāṇām eva mā bhūd iti sarvagrahaṇam | abgrahaṇaṃ kṣārādinivṛttyartham | evaṃ sāyaṃ prātar abhimantrya pratipāpam ācāmet | saṃbhavato vikalpaḥ || 25.13 ||
sarvāsu pāpakriyāsv anārjavādiṣv ābhyāṃ mantrābhyām apo ‘bhimantryācāmed “ahaś ca” iti prātaḥ pibed “rātriś ca” iti sāyaṃ pibet || 25.9 ||
aṣṭau vā samidha ādadhyād devakṛtasyeti hutvaiva sarvasmād enaso
mucyate mucyate || 25.14 || — || 25.10 ||
aṣṭau vā samidha ādadhyāt “devakṛtasyainaso ‘vayajanam asi svāhā | manuṣyakṛtasyainaso ‘vayajanam asi svāhā | pitṛkṛtasyainaso ‘vayajanam asi svāhā | ātmakṛtasyainaso ‘vayajanam asi svāhā | anyakṛtasyainaso ‘vayajanam asi svāhā | yad diva ca naktaṃ cainaś cakṛma tasyāvayajanam asi svāhā | enasa enaso ‘vayajanam asi svāhā” ity etair mantrair hutvaiva anyad akṛtvety arthaḥ | asya prādhānyakhyāpanārtha evakāraḥ | tataś ca saṃbhave idam eva kartavyam | sarvasmād enasaḥ na kevalam anārjavādibhyaḥ, kiṃ tu ayājyayājanādibhyo ‘py enasaḥ pāpān mucyate || 25.14 ||
atha vā “devakṛtasya” [VS 8.13] ityādibhir mantrair aṣṭau samidha ādadhyāj juhuyāt | hutvaiva sarvasmād enaso na kevalam anārjavādibhyaḥ kiṃ tv ayājyayājanāder apy enaso mucyate | asya homasya mukhyatvapradarśanārtham evakāraḥ | hutvaivānyad akṛtveti | tataś ca sati saṃbhava idam eva jyāyaḥ | “devakṛtasyainaso ‘vayajanam asi svāhā” ityādayo ‘ṣṭau mantrāḥ [dviruktir uktārthā] || 25.10 ||
atha ṣaḍviṃśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
uktaṃ kṛcchrātikṛcchrau cāndrāyaṇam iti sarvaprāyaścittam iti | atra kṛcchrasvarūpanirūpaṇārtham idam āha |
kṛcchrātikṛcchrau cāndrāyaṇam ity uktam | tatra krameṇa kṛcchrādisvarūpam āha |
athātaḥ kṛcchrān vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || 26.1 ||
athaśabdo maṅgalārthaḥ pūrvasyāśubhasaṃkīrtanasya vyavacchedārthaḥ | evaṃ caite dharmārthā api bhavanti | tathāha śaṅkhaḥ: “śaktitaḥ śarīraṃ śodhayed yamaniyamamantravratādibhiḥ” iti | vrataśabdena cātra kṛcchrāṇy abhidhīyanta iti | ataḥśabdo hetau yata evaite maṅgalārthā api bhavanti tato vyākhyāsyāma iti | kṛcchragrahaṇaṃ saṃjñārtham | anvarthasaṃjñā ceyam, kṛcchrād duḥkhāt pāpān mocayatīti || 26.1 ||
athaśabdo ‘dhikārārthaḥ | ataḥśabdo hetau | ya eta ākhyātā na śakyante kartum ataḥ kṛcchrān vyākhyāsyāma iti || 26.1 ||
haviṣyān prātarāśān bhuktvā tisro rātrīr nāśnīyāt || 26.2 ||
haviṣyān śuddhān vrīhyādibhir niṣpannān ghṛtādisaṃyuktān kṣāralavaṇavarjitān prātarāśān grāsān | āśān iti nātisauhityaṃ darśayati | tryaham abhyavahṛtya tatraiva tisro rātrīr nāśnīyāt |
nanu ca sāyaṃprātar bhojanaprāptau satyāṃ prātaraśanavacanād eva rātribhojanapratiṣedhaḥ siddhaḥ |
ucyate – haviṣyān iti viśeṣārambhāt prātarhaviṣyabhojanaṃ sāyaṃ yathāprāptabhojanam ity āśaṅkā syād iti rātrau yathāprāptasyāpi pratiṣedhārthaṃ vacanam || 26.2 ||
yajñiyair varakādivyatiriktair niṣpāditān ghṛtādisaṃyuktān kṣāralavaṇavarjitān prātarāśān | aśnanta [aśyanta?] ity āśā grāsāḥ | divā bhojyān grāsān bhuktvā | “athāparaṃ tryaham” GDh 26.3] iti vakṣyamāṇatvād atrāpi tryaham iti gamyate | tisro rātrīr nāśnīyād iti tasmiṃs tryahe rātryaśanapratiṣedhaḥ śrutyanusāreṇa kṛtaḥ | punar ayaṃ vaktavyaḥ | katham |
sāyaṃ prātar dvijātīnām aśanaṃ śruticoditam |
iti parisaṃkhyānād dvir eva bhojanaṃ prāptam | tatra prātarāśān bhuktvety ukte parisaṃkhyānāt siddhā rātrāv aśananivṛttir yathottaratra divā bhojananivṛttiḥ
|| 26.2 ||
athāparaṃ tryahaṃ naktaṃ bhuñjīta || 26.3 ||
athānantaram aparam api tryaham | aparagrahaṇāc ca pūrvam api tryahasiddhiḥ | prākṛtaṃ haviṣyānnaṃ naktam eva bhuñjīta pūrvavat || 26.3 ||
evaṃ divā haviṣyabhojanena tryahaṃ nītvānantaraṃ tryahaṃ naktam eva bhuñjīta | haviṣyān ity eva || 26.3 ||
athāparaṃ tryahaṃ na kaṃcana yāceta || 26.4 ||
athety adhikārārtham | sakṛdbhojanasyāyācitalabhyasya na kālaviśeṣaḥ | aparagrahaṇaṃ pūrvatryahasādṛśyopapādanārtham | tataś ca haviṣyatvaṃ nātisauhityaṃ cātrāpi draṣṭavyam | na kaṃcana jñātim api prārthayet | evaṃ ca ayācitalabdhasyāpratiṣedhaḥ |
kecid bruvate – bhojane labhyamāne yadi brūyād ahaviṣyaṃ na bhokṣya iti tato ‘rthād dhaviṣyabhojanam iti yācñā kṛtā bhavatīty ato ‘tra haviṣyabhojananiyamo nāstīti |
anye tv āhuḥ – pratigraho ‘nena pratiṣidhyate yājñāyā asvīkṛtasvīkaraṇaviṣayatvān na haviṣyabhojanapratiṣedha iti | evaṃ cātmīyasyāpratiṣedhaḥ | na yāceteti siddhe na kaṃcanety ārambhasāmarthyāt paricārakādiviṣayāpi yācñā na kartavyā || 26.4 ||
atha naktabhojanatryahānantaraṃ tryahaṃ na kaṃcana bandhum api yāceta | yācñāpratiṣedho ‘yam | svadravyasya vāyācitalabdhasyāpratiṣedhaḥ | evam ukte haviṣyaniyamo na prāpnoti | kālaviśeṣāśravaṇād dvirbhojanaṃ ca prāpnoti | na yācetety atrāpi haviṣyān ity evānuvartate | ayācitalabdhe ‘pi sakṛd eva siddham | kutaḥ | athāparam iti vacanasya pūrveṇa sadṛśārthatvāt | tat tu divā naktaṃ vā yatheccham | anye tu “tryaham ayācitavrataḥ” [ĀpDh 1.27.7] ity āpastambīye darśanād ayācitalabdhenaiva tryahaṃ vṛttir na svadravyeṇa | nāpi yācitalabdheneti varṇayanti | anuṣṭhānam apy evam eva || 26.4 ||
athāparaṃ tryaham upavaset || 26.5 ||
athaśabda uktārthaḥ | anantaraṃ tryahaṃ nirāhāraḥ syāt | ihāparagrahaṇaṃ prakārāntareṇāpi tryahaprayogaprāpaṇārtham | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
ahaḥ prātar ahar naktam ahar ekam ayācitam |
ahaḥ parākaṃ tatraiva evaṃ caturahau parau ||
anugrahārthaṃ viprāṇāṃ manur dharmabhṛtāṃ varaḥ |
bālavṛddhāturāṇāṃ ca śiśukṛcchram uvāca ha || iti | [VaDh 23.43] | upavāsaṃ kartum aśaktasya bhāradvājoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
prājāpatyaṃ caran vipro yady aśakto dine dine |
viprān pañcāvarān śuddhān bhojayet samyag arcitān || iti || 26.5 ||
spaṣṭam | evam ayaṃ dvādaśāhasādhyaḥ kṛcchraḥ | vasiṣṭhena prakārāntaram api darśitam |
ahaḥ prātar ahar naktam ahar ekam ayācitam |
ahaś copavased ekam evaṃ caturahau parau ||
anugrahārthaṃ viprāṇāṃ manur dharmabhṛtāṃ varaḥ |
bālavṛddhāturāṇāṃ ca śiśukṛcchram uvāca ha || iti | [VaDh 23.43]
bharadvājaḥ |
prājāpatyaṃ caran vipro yady aśakto dine dine |
viprān pañcāvarāñ śuddhān bhojayet samyag arcitān || iti | yasmin dine ‘śaktis tatraivam, dināntareṣu pūrvavat | tatrāpy aśakto brāhmaṇabhojanam upavāsadineṣv aśakto vā brāhmaṇabhojanaṃ datvā haviṣyān samyag bhuñjīta || 26.5 ||
idānīṃ guṇavidhim āha |
atha kṛcchrasya guṇavidhiḥ |
tiṣṭhed ahani rātrāv āsīta kṣiprakāmaḥ || 26.6 ||
bhojanādyaviruddheṣu kāleṣu ahani tiṣṭhet, rātrāv āsīta | evaṃ ca nidrāyā avaśyabhāvitvād āsīna eva nidrāṃ seveteti draṣṭavyam | diva rātrau ca kṣiprakāmaś ca syāt | kāma āsthā | sarvadhānubhavam utpannam api nāśayed ity arthaḥ | atha vā sarvaprayatnena śīghraṃ śuddhaḥ syām iti satataṃ saṃjātamatiḥ syāt |
kecid vyācakṣate – śīghraṃ śuddhikāmaḥ tiṣṭhed ahani rātrāv āsīta, śanaiḥ śaktitaḥ śuddhiṃ karomīti yasya matiḥ syāt tasyāniyama iti | evaṃ ca guṇabāhulyāt prāyaścittagurutvam anumīyate | tathā ca prājāpatyadvayasthāna idaṃ gautamoktam itikartavyatāsahitaṃ prājāpatyaṃ draṣṭavyam | prājāpatyasthānamātre itikartavyatārahitam | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “tryahaṃ prātas tathā sāyam ayācitṃ parāka iti kṛcchraḥ” [VaDh 24.2] iti |
anye bruvate – yeṣāṃ mantrādhikāro nāsti strīśūdrādīnāṃ teṣāṃ mantrarahitaṃ vasiṣṭhādyuktena mārgeṇa draṣṭavyam | yeṣāṃ tadadhikāro ‘sti teṣām idam iti |
apare “akṣiprakāmaḥ” iti paṭhanti, vyācakṣate ca – akṣiprakāma evaṃ prājāpatyaṃ kuryāt kṣiprakāmas tu vasiṣṭhoktaṃ kuryād iti | tathā cāha |
atha cet tvarate kartuṃ divasaṃ mārutāśanaḥ |
rātrau sthito jale vyuṣṭaḥ prājāpatyena tatsamam ||
sāvitryaṣṭasahasraṃ tu japyaṃ kṛtvotthite ravau |
mucyate pātakaiḥ sarvair yadi na bhrūṇahā bhavet || [VaDh 27.17–18] || 26.6 ||
yaḥ kāmayeta kṣipraṃ śudhyeyam iti sa tiṣṭhann evāhar nayet | bhojanādyavirodhena rātrāv āsīta | svāpo ‘py āsīnasyaiva | vasiṣṭhas tu kṣiprakāmasya prakārāntaram āha |
atha cet tvarate kartuṃ divasaṃ mārutāśanaḥ |
rātrau caiva jale tiṣṭhet prājāpatyena tat samam ||
sāvitryaṣṭasahasraṃ tu japyaṃ kṛtvotthite ravau |
mucyate pātakaiḥ sarvair yadi na bhrūṇahā bhavet || [VaDh 27.17–18] || 26.6 ||
satyaṃ vadet || 26.7 ||
satyaṃ yathābhūtaṃ vadet | vivāhādiṣv apy ekeṣāṃ pakṣam āśritya anṛtaṃ na vaktavyam ity abhiprāyaḥ | evaṃ ca satyavacanenāpi pāpakṣapaṇaṃ bhavatīty avagamyate || 26.7 ||
satyaṃ yathādṛṣṭam | vivāhādiviṣaye ‘pi satyam eva vadet || 26.7 ||
anāryair na saṃbhāṣeta || 26.8 ||
anāryā dvijātibhyo ‘nye varṇāḥ | varṇāpaśadāś ca | bahuvacanāt strīpuṃnapuṃsakaiś ca || 26.8 ||
dvijātivyatiriktair liṅgasyāvivakṣitatvāt tatstrībhir api na saṃbhāṣeta || 26.8 ||
rauravayodhājaye nityaṃ prayuñjīta || 26.9 ||
rauravayaudhājape nityaṃ prayuñjīta || 26.9 ||
rauravayodhājaye sāmanī “punānaḥ soma dhāraya” ity asyām ṛci gīyete | nityam ity asya sarvatrānuṣaṅgo draṣṭavyaḥ “tiṣṭhed ahani” [GDh 26.6] ityādau, japed iti vaktavye prayuñjīteti vacanāt | svādhyāyarūpeṇa nityaśabdād anusaṃdheyam ity eke | punaḥ punar bahuśa ity anye || 26.9 ||
rauravayaudhājape sāmanī | “punānaḥ soma dhāraya” [RV 9.63.28] ity asyām ṛci gīte | nityaṃ pratyahaṃ prayuñjīta gāyet | apara āha – nityaṃ punaḥ prayuñjīteti || 26.9 ||
anusavanam udakopasparśanam āpohiṣṭheti tisṛbhiḥ pavitravatībhir mārjayīta hiraṇyavarṇāḥ śucaya ity aṣṭābhiḥ || 26.10 ||
anusavanam udakopasparśanam āpo hi ṣṭheti tisṛbhiḥ pavitravatībhir mārjayīta hiraṇya-varṇāḥ śucayaḥ pāvakā ity aṣṭābhiḥ || 26.10 ||
anusavanaṃ pūrvāhṇamadhyāhnāparāhṇeṣu udakopasparśanaṃ snānam upaśabdaprayogāt | āpohiṣṭhetyādibhis tisṛbhiḥ pavitravatībhiḥ “pavitraṃ te vitatam” ityādibhiś catasṛbhir eva, anuktasaṃkhyātvāt tritvaṃ vinā bahuvacanasyākṛtārthatvāc ca | “hiraṇyavarṇāḥ śucayaḥ” ity aṣṭābhiś ca mārjayīta salilam abhimantrya mūrdhani prakṣipet | anye karaṇavibhaktinirdeśād ebhir eva snānam iti varṇayanti | mārjanaśabdasya madhyopadeśād anyaiś ca pavitraiḥ || 26.10 ||
udakopasparśanaṃ snānam | tadanuvasanaṃ triṣu savaneṣu kartavyam | tadanantaraṃ ca mārjanam āpo hi ṣṭhetyādibhiḥ | pavamānaḥ “suvarjanaḥ” ity anuvāke yā ṛcas tāḥ pavitravatyaḥ, liṅgasamavāyāt | tābhiś ca taittirīye pañcame kāṇḍe ṣaṣṭhe praśne “hiraṇyavarṇāḥ śucayaḥ pāvakāḥ” ityādyā ṛcaḥ paṭhyante | tā daś bhavanti | tatrāṣṭābhiḥ | yadi tu śākhāntare kvacid aṣṭāv eva paṭhyante tatas tā eva grāhyāḥ || 26.10 ||
athodakatarpaṇam || 26.11 ||
atha anantaram, anusavanaprāpaṇārthaṃ tasyopādānam | evaṃ ca naimittikeṣv api snāneṣūdakatarpaṇam astity avagamyate | tathāha bhṛguḥ |
nityaṃ naimittikaṃ kāmyaṃ trividhaṃ snānam ucyate |
tarpaṇaṃ trividhasyāsyāpy eṣā eva vidhiḥ sadā || iti |
udakopasparśanam iti yatra udakādhikāre punar udakagrahaṇam udakasthasyāpi tarpaṇaprāpaṇārtham | tathā ca smṛtyantare viruddham abhidhāya punaḥ samarthitam | yathā |
nodakeṣu na pātreṣu na kruddho naikapāṇinā |
nopatiṣṭhati tat toyaṃ yad bhūmyāṃ na pradīyate ||
tathā |
udake nodakaṃ kuryāt pitṛbhyas tu kadācana |
uttīrya tu śucau deśe kuryād udakatarpaṇam ||
ity abhidhāyoktam |
āpo devagaṇāḥ sarve āpa pitṛgaṇāḥ smṛtāḥ |
tasmād apsu jalaṃ deyaṃ pitṝṇāṃ dattam akṣayam ||
tathā |
devātānāṃ pitṝṇāṃ ca jale dadyāj jalāñjalim |
asaṃskṛtapramītānāṃ sthale dadyāj jalāñjalim ||
iti || 26.11 ||
atha mārjanānantaram udakena tarpaṇaṃ kartavyam
|| 26.11 ||
tat punaḥ kebhyaḥ kathaṃ vety ata āha |
tatra mantrāḥ |
namo hamāya mohamāya maṃhamāya dhūnvate tāpasāya punarvasave namaḥ | namo mauñjyāyormyāya vasuvindāya sarvavindāya namaḥ | namaḥ pārāya supārāya mahāpārāya pāradāya pārayiṣṇave namaḥ | namo rudrāya paśupataye mahate devāya tryambakāyaikacarāyādhipataye harāya śarvāyeśānāyogrāya vajriṇe ghṛṇine karpardine namaḥ | namaḥ sūryāyādityāya namaḥ | namo nīlagrīvāya śitikaṇṭhāya namaḥ | namaḥ kṛṣṇāya piṅgalāya namaḥ | namo jyeṣṭhāya śreṣṭhāya vṛddhāyendrāya harikeśāyordhvaretase namaḥ | namaḥ satyāya pāvakāya pāvakavarṇāya kāmāya kāmarūpiṇe namaḥ | namo dīptāya dīptarūpiṇe namaḥ | namas tīkṣṇāya tīkṣṇarūpiṇe namaḥ | namaḥ saumyāya supuruṣāya mahāpuruṣāya madhyamapuruṣāyottamapuruṣāya brahmacāriṇe namaḥ | namaś candralalāṭāya kṛttivāsase nama iti || 26.12 ||
namo ‘hamāya mohamāya maṃhamāya dhunvate tāpasāya punarvasave namaḥ | namo mauñjyāyorvyāya vasuvindāya sārvavindāya namaḥ | namaḥ pārāya supārāya mahāpārāya vārayiṣṇave namaḥ | namo rudrāya paśupataye mahate devāya tryambakāyaikacarāyādhipataye harāya śarvāyeśānāyogrāya vajriṇe ghṛṇine kapardine namaḥ | namaḥ sūryāyādityāya namaḥ | namo nīlagrīvāya śitikaṇṭhāya namaḥ | namaḥ kṛṣṇāya piṅgalāya namaḥ | namo jyeṣṭhāya śreṣṭhāya vṛddhāyendrāya harikeśāyordhvaretase namaḥ | namaḥ satyāya pāvakāya pāvakavarṇāya kāmāya kāmarūpiṇe namaḥ | namo dīptāya dīptarūpiṇe namaḥ | namas tīkṣṇāya tīkṣṇarūpiṇe namaḥ | namaḥ sobhyāya supuruṣāya mahāpuruṣāya madhyamapuruṣāyottamapuruṣāya brahmacāriṇe namaḥ | namaś candralalāṭāya kṛttivāsase namaḥ || 26.12 ||
nāyam eko mantraḥ, etā evājyāhutaya iti bahuvacananirdeśāt | kiṃ tarhi trayodaśaite mantrā namaskārādayo namaskārāntāś ca draṣṭavyāḥ, śrutau tathaiva pāṭhdarśanāt | tataś caitair namaskārāntair mantair udakatarpaṇaṃ kartavyam |
anye punar āhuḥ – saṃpradānavibhaktyantāḥ ṣaṭpañcāśad ete mantrā iti |
apare namaskārādaya eva na namaskārāntās tathāpāṭhadarśanād ity āhuḥ || || 26.12 ||
nāyam eko mantraḥ, etāś cājyāhutaya iti bahuvacana-nirdeśāt | kiṃ tarhi | trayodaśaite mantrāḥ | namaskārā-dayo namaskārāntāś ca sarve | tatra prathame caturthyantāni ṣaḍ devasya nāmāni | dvitīye catvāri | tathā tṛtīye | caturthe trayodaśa | mahate devāyeti mahādevapadam eva vyastam uktam | pañcamādiṣu triṣu dve | aṣṭame ṣaṭ | namave pañca | daśame dve | tathaikādaśe | dvādaśe ṣaṭ | iti ṣaṭpañcāśad devanāmāni | ebhir mantrais tarpaṇam anusavanam || 26.12 ||
etad evādityopasthānam || 26.13 ||
etad eva yajuḥ ādityopasthānam |
nanu ca upasthānaśabdasya kriyāvacanatvād yajuṣā saha sāmānādhikaraṇyānupapattiḥ, ata eva vaktavyam etair evādityopasthānam iti |
naiṣa doṣaḥ – upatiṣṭhate ‘nenety upasthānam ity evaṃ yajur evābhidhīyata ity adoṣaḥ | tataś cādityopasthānam ity arthāt kriyācodaneti | evaśabda udakatarpaṇe ‘py ayam eva mantra iti sūcanārthaḥ | itarathā etad ādityopasthānam ity ukte udakatarpaṇaṃ gṛhyoktena mantreṇa prasajyeteti | evaṃ ca nityād adhikam idaṃ draṣṭavyam, yathāsamāmnāyaprayogārtho vā | evaṃ codakatarpaṇādy anyathety avagamyate | raudreṇa mantreṇādityastutir ayukteti cet, naiṣa doṣaḥ | na hi vacanasyātibhāra ity atibhārābhāvād rudrāṃśatvāc cādityasya | tathā purāṇa uktam: “kṣitijalapavanahutāśanayajamānākāśasomasūryākhyā mūrtayo bhagavataḥ śarvasyāṣṭau” iti | etad eva viruddham, tatraivādityo viṣṇur iti darśanād iti cet, dvayor abhedatvād adoṣaḥ | tathā ca bhagavadgītāsu “rudrāṇāṃ śaṃkaraś cāsmi” [BhG 10.23] iti | tarpaṇānantaram upadeśāt trir ādityopasthānam anusavanaṃ draṣṭavyam |
anye vyācakṣate – “athodakatarpaṇam ādityopasthānaṃ ca” iti vaktavye pṛthagyogakaraṇāt pratyahaṃ sakṛd evādityopasthānam iti |
apare prāyaścittād anyatrāpi praveśārthaṃ pṛthagyogakaraṇam iti | vacanavad ekavacanatvād uktabahutve ‘pi na virodhaḥ || 26.13 ||
āditya upasthīyate yena tad ādityasyopasthānam | etena kṛtsnena mantreṇāditya upastheya ity uktaṃ bhavati | etad apy anusavanaṃ pratyaham | sakṛd ity anye, pṛthag yogakaraṇāt | anyathāthodakatarpaṇam ādityo-pasthānaṃ cety ekam eva yogam akariṣyat || 26.13 ||
etā evājyāhutayaḥ || 26.14 ||
ājyāhutayaś ceti vaktavye etā evety abhidhānam āhuti-bahutvapratiṣedhārtham | atas trayodaśaitā ājyāhutayo draṣṭavyāḥ | pūrvavat samāmnāyaprayogārtha eva-śabdaḥ | tataś ca namaskārādayo mantrāḥ svāhākārān-tāś ca draṣṭavyāḥ | kutaḥ? āhutigrahaṇasāmarthyāt | yady api puṃliṅgā mantrāḥ, tathāpy āhutisāmānādhi-karaṇyād etā iti strīliṅganirdeśaḥ |
kecid vyācakṣate – etāḥ saṃpradānavibhaktayaḥ ṣaṭpañcāśad ājyāhutaya iti, saṃpradānavibhaktiparāmarśārthaḥ strīliṅganideśa iti | etad api sakṛt sakṛd ādityopasthānavad anusavanaṃ draṣṭavyam, ānantaryānabhidhānāt | evaṃ dvādaśarātraṃ varteta || 26.14 ||
etā iti mantram api parāmṛśati | etac chandasyāhuti-sāmānādhikaraṇyāt strīliṅgatā | etair eva trayodaśabhir mantrair ājyam api hotavyam ity uktaṃ bhavati | tatra “juhoticodanā svāhākārapradānā” iti svāhākārān tair homaḥ pratyahaṃ sakṛt kartavyaḥ || 26.14 ||
dvādaśarātrasyānte caruṃ śrapayitvaitābhyo devatābhyo juhuyāt || 26.15 ||
dvādaśarātrasyānte, na pratidinam | trayodaśe vāhani, ṣaṣṭhīnirdeśāt | caruṇā juhuyād ity eva siddhe śrapayitvāgrahaṇam utpūya śrapaṇam abhidhāryāvaropaṇam ity evamartham | juhuyād iti homavidhyartham | atra svagṛhyoktena sthālīpākavidhānena draṣṭavyam | “etābhyo devatābhyaḥ” iti na vaktavyam, vakṣyamāṇatvād eva siddham iti cet, dhyānapūrvārtha upadeśa ity adoṣaḥ
|| 26.15 ||
evam uktena prakāreṇa dvādaśarātraṃ nītvā tadante trayodaśe ‘hani gṛhyoktena mārgeṇa caruṃ śrapayitvaitābhyo vakṣyamāṇābhyo devatābhyo juhuyāt || 26.15 ||

etenaiva prājāpatyavidhinā atikṛcchro ‘py atiśayapīḍotpādako vyākhyātaḥ | kṛcchrān iti bahuvacanād evaitat siddham iti cet, ucyate – agniṣṭomavat prathamasya prakṛtitvakhyāpanārtham abhidhānam iti | tataḥ sāntapanādāv api “tiṣṭhed ahani” [GDh 26.6] ityādyavirodhakalpanā kartavyā | tatsvarūpaṃ tu smṛtyantarād avagantavyam | tathāha bodhāyanaḥ |
yatātmano ‘pramattasya dvādaśāham abhojanam |
parāko nāma kṛcchro ‘yaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanaḥ ||
gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ kṣīraṃ dadhi sarpiḥ kuśodakam |
ekarātropavāsaś ca kṛcchraṃ sāntapanaṃ smṛtam ||
gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ caiva kṣīraṃ dadhi ghṛtaṃ tathā |
pañcarātraṃ tadāhāraḥ pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
gomūtrādibhir abhyastam ekaikaṃ tritrisaptakam |
mahāsāntapanaṃ kṛcchraṃ vadanti brahmavādinaḥ ||
kaṇapiṇyākatakrāṇi tathā cāmānilāśanaḥ |

pauṣabhādrapadajyeṣṭhā ārdrākāśātapāśrayāḥ |
trīn śuklān mucyate pāpāt patanīyād ṛte dvijaḥ ||

retomūtrapurīṣāṇāṃ prāśane ‘bhojyabhojane |

mucyate sarvapāpebhyo ity etad vacanaṃ satām ||
mantramārgapramāṇaṃ ca vidhānaṃ samudāhṛtam |

prathamaṃ caritvā śuciḥ pūtaḥ karmaṇyo bhavati || 26.20 || — || 26.21 ||
prathamaṃ prājāpatyaṃ caritvā śucir apagatavihitākaraṇa-nimittadoṣaḥ pūtaḥ pratiṣiddhācaraṇajanitādharmakṣayaś ca, karmaṇyaḥ kāmyanityakarmayogyaś ca bhavati saṃpadyate |
nanu ca idam anarthakam, prāyaścittavidhānād evaitasya siddhatvāt, kuryād ity atra pāpakṣapaṇahetutvasya pratipāditatvāt | tatrāpi prathamād ity eva siddhatvāc caritvāgrahaṇam apy anarthakam | tathā ca śuciḥ pūta ity anenādharmāvāntarabhedakathanaṃ mandaphalam, pūta ity etāvataivobhasiddheḥ | tathā karmaṇya ity etad api | yadi tāvad apetādharmatvena karmādhikārābhprāyaḥ tato ‘rthasiddhatvād avacanīyam | atha viśiṣṭataraphalaprāptyabhiprāyaḥ, tad apy ayuktam | yasya karmaṇo yat phalaṃ tad eva, na phalātiśayo bhavatīti |
atrocyate – dehāntarakṛtobhayavidhādharmakṣapaṇasādhanatvajñāpanārtho ‘yam ārambhaḥ | caritvāgrahaṇaṃ sakṛtprayogeṇa bhavatīti jñāpanārtham, upariṣṭāt “yad anyan mahāpātakebhyaḥ” [GDh 26.21] ity abhidhānāt, pratiṣiddhasevana eva prāyaśaḥ prāyaścittavidhānāt | pūtaśabdena ca pratiṣiddhaviṣaya evāśaṅkā syād iti śucigrahaṇam | tasya ca jñātasyeti draṣṭavyam, ajñātasya vedoditānām ity uktatvāt |
nanv etad api sarvaprāyaścittābhidhānāt siddham, tasya buddhipūrvābuddhipūrvakṛtānāṃ sāmānyatvāt |
ucyate – nirdiṣṭaviṣayam idam | anirdiṣtaviṣayaṃ tad iti | evaṃ pratiṣiddhaviṣaye ‘pi draṣṭavyam | karmaṇya ity etad apy adharmasaṃbandhe sati karmaphalapratibandho bhavatīty asyārthasya jñāpanārtham || 26.20 ||

uktaṃ kṛcchātikṛcchrau cāndrāyaṇam iti | tatra kṛcchrātikṛcchravidhir uktaḥ | adhunā cāndrāyaṇavidhivivakṣayāha |
athātaś cāndrāyaṇam || 27.1 ||
vyākhyāsyāmaḥ iti śeṣaḥ | athaśabdo ‘dhikārārthaḥ | kṛcchrātikṛcchraphalasya tattulyatvād anantaroktaṃ na bhavatīti | athaḥśabdo hetau, yata etat phalasaṃyuktam iti | cāndrāyaṇam iti saṃjñā | anvarthatvāt candragativaśāt bhavatīti | jātyām ekavacanam | evaṃ ca smṛtyantaroktasya yaticāndrāyaṇādeḥ sarvaprāyaścittatvaṃ jñāpayati | tathāha manuḥ |

caturaḥ prātar aśnīyād dvijaḥ piṇḍān kṛtāhnikaḥ |
caturo ‘stamite sūrye śiśucāndrāyaṇaṃ caret ||
yathā kathaṃcit piṇḍānāṃ tisro ‘śītiḥ samāhitaḥ |
māsenāśnan haviṣyasya candrasyaiti salokatām ||
ityādi || 27.1 ||
pūrvavad vyākhyeyam | candraprāptinimittabhūtaṃ karma cāndrāyaṇam | tathā cānte vakṣyati – candramasaḥ salokatām āpnotīti || 27.1 ||
tasyokto vidhiḥ kṛcchre || 27.2 ||
tasya sarvaprakārasya, tacchabdopādānāt | ukto vidhiḥ itikartavyatā | kṛcchre prathame “tiṣṭhed ahani” [GDh 26.6] ityādi || 27.2 ||
“tiṣṭhed ahani” [GDh 26.6] ityadiko yo vidhiḥ kṛcchra uktaḥ sa cāndrāyaṇasyāpi draṣṭavyaḥ || 27.2 ||
yas tv iha viśeṣaḥ sa ucyate |
yas tu viśeṣaḥ sa ucyate |
vapanaṃ vrataṃ cet || 27.3 ||
vapanaṃ keśādyapanayanaṃ kartavyaṃ vrataṃ cet | prāyaścittārtham asyaitad bhavati cet | vrataśabdena smṛtyantare prāyaścittam uktam iti | yathāha manuḥ |
etair dvijātayaḥ śodhyā vratair āviṣkṛtainasaḥ || iti | [MDh 11.226] evaṃ ca yady adṛṣṭārthaṃ tadānīṃ vapanam antareṇāpi tad bhavatīti jñāpitam |
anye tu brahmacārivratam āhuḥ, “caturo vratino ‘pi” [MDh 11.121] ityādau tathā darśanāt | asmin pakṣe gṛhasthasya tadantareṇāpi bhavati | ayaṃ nyāyaḥ kṛcchrādāv api draṣṭavyaḥ || 27.3 ||
vratam iti prāyaścittam āha,
etair dvijātayaḥ śodhyā vratair āviṣkṛtainasaḥ | [MDh 11.226] ityādau darśanāt | yadi prāyaścittārthaṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ kriyate tadā vapanam api kartavyam | aviśeṣe ‘pi puruṣāṇām eva | “tad eva striyāḥ keśavapanavarjam” [BDh 2.2.45] iti baudhāyanasmaraṇam | cāndrāyaṇe vapanavidhānāt kṛcchre prāyaścittārthe ‘pi na bhavati | vrataṃ cared iti vacanād adṛṣṭārthe karmaṇyatārthe ca cāndrāyaṇe na vapanam || 27.3 ||
svarūpābhidhānārtham āha |
śvobhūtāṃ paurṇamāsīm upavaset || 27.4 ||
upavāso bhaktatyāgaḥ | taṃ caturdaśyāṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ || 27.4 ||
śvaḥ paurṇamāsī bhavitety avagamya pūrvedyuś caturdaśyām upavaset | upavāso bhojanalopaḥ  || 27.4 ||
ā pyāyasva saṃ te payāṃsi navo nava iti caitābhis tarpaṇam ājyahomo haviṣaś cānumantraṇam || 27.5 ||
ā pyāyasva saṃ te payāṃsi navo nava iti caitābhis tarpaṇam ājyahomo haviṣaś cānu-mantraṇam upasthānaṃ candramasaḥ || 27.5 ||
“ā pyāyasva sametu te saṃ te payāṃsi samuyantu vājāḥ, navo navo bhavati jāyamānaḥ” ity etābhis tisṛbhir udakatarpaṇaṃ ghṛtena ca homo haviṣaś ca vakṣyamāṇasyānumantraṇaṃ kartavyam |
tatraika āhuḥ – aṅgabhūyastve phalabhūyastvābhyupagamāt kṛcchravidhyatideśāc ca kṛcchre raudreṇa yajuṣā ya udakatarpaṇādaya upadiṣṭāḥ taiḥ saha samuccīyanta iti |
tad ayuktam | “atideśaprāptaṃ hi pratyakṣaśrutena nivartyate” iti nyāyān nivṛttir eva nyāyyeti | atha kim etābhiḥ yathāsaṃkhyenaikaikaṃ kartavyam, uta samuccitābhiḥ sarvam |
anya āhuḥ – ājyahoma ity ekavacananirdeśād ekaikaṃ kuryāt, samuccaye hi mantrabahutvāt karmabahutvam iti homapṛthaktve ca bahuvacanaṃ prasajyeteti || 27.5 ||
āpyāyasvetyādibhir mantrais tarpaṇādīni catvāri karmāṇi kartavyāni | vaiṣamyād yathāsaṃkhyaṃ na bhavati | tatra tarpaṇahomau pratimantraṃ bhavataḥ | anumantraṇam upasthānaṃ ca samuccayena | kṛcchravidhyatideśād raudreṇa ya udakatarpaṇādayaḥ prāptās teṣāṃ ca samuccaya ity eke | upadiṣṭair atidiṣṭānāṃ bādha ity anye || 27.5 ||
upasthānaṃ ca candramasaḥ || 27.6 ||
caśabdād etābhir eva kartavyam | yady asau na dṛśyate tadā ādityasya kāryam, “suṣumnaḥ sūryaraśmiḥ” iti mantraliṅgadarśanāt | atra bhinnārthatvāt kṛcchravidhy-atideśaprāptam ādityopasthānaṃ nivartate || 27.6 ||
yad devā devaheḍanam iti ca catasṛbhir juhuyāt || 27.7 || — || 27.6 ||
“yad devā devaheḍanam” iti ṛgbhiḥ | anādeśād ājyaṃ juhuyāt | strīliṅgasaṃkhyānirdeśa ṛgarthaḥ, yajūṃṣy apy evamādīni yajurvede paṭhyanta iti tannivṛttyartham | ataḥ pūrveṇa sahitāḥ pañcājyāhutayaḥ sapta vā
|| 27.7 ||
“yad devā devaheḍanam” [VS 20.14] ity anuvāka āditaś catasṛbhir ṛgbhir anādeśād ājyaṃ juhuyāt | pūrvābhis tisṛbhiś ceti saptājyāhutayaḥ || 27.6 ||
devakṛtasyeti cānte samidbhiḥ || 27.8 || — || 27.7 ||
“devakṛtasya” ityādaya uktāḥ [GDh 25.14] | tair mantrair aṣṭau samiddhomān kuryāt | ante cāndrāyaṇasamāptau | caśabdād ādau ca || 27.8 ||
ājyahomānte “devakṛtasya” [VS 8.13] ityādibhiḥ pūrvoktair aṣṭabhir mantraiḥ samidbhir homaḥ kartavyaḥ | upadeśakramād eva siddhe ‘ntagrahaṇaṃ prāptānuvādaḥ | anye punaś cāndrāyaṇānta iti vyācakṣate | teṣāṃ caśabdo na saṃgacchate || 27.7 ||
oṃ bhūr bhuvaḥ svar tapaḥ satyaṃ yaśaḥ śrīr ūrgiḍ aujas tejaḥ puruṣo dharmaḥ śiva ity etair grāsānumantraṇam || 27.9 ||
oṃ bhūr bhuvaḥ svas tapaḥ satyaṃ yaśaḥ śrīr ūrgiḍ aujas tejo varcaḥ puruṣo dharmaḥ śiva ity etair grāsānumantraṇaṃ pratimantraṃ manasā || 27.8 ||
praṇavādayaḥ pañcadaśaite mantrāḥ | teṣām ekaikenānupūrvyā grāsānumantraṇaṃ kartavyam | pūrvaṃ haviṣo ‘numantraṇam uktam | yadā cāntyaḥ piṇḍo vyāvartate tadāntyamantro ‘pīti mantrānto draṣṭavyaḥ || 27.9 ||
praṇavādayaḥ pañcadaśa mantrās teṣām ekaikena mantreṇaikaikasya grāsasya manasānumantraṇaṃ kartavyam | anumantraṇakrameṇa bhojanam | yadā tu nyūnā grāsās tadā yāvad grāsam ādito mantrā grāhyāḥ | antato lupyante | grāsānumantraṇam iti vacanān naite bhojanamantrāḥ | tataś ca prāṇāhutimantrāṇām anivṛttiḥ | yadā catvāro grāsās tadā dvābhyāṃ pūrvaṃ yadā trayo dvābhyāṃ dvābhyāṃ pūrvau yadā dvau dvābhyāṃ pūrvam uttaraṃ tribhiḥ | sarvair ekam | haviṣaś cānumantraṇam iti pūrvoktam iha tu grāsānumantraṇam iti prāṇāhutimantrāś ca sthitāḥ | tatra prayogaḥ – sarvaṃ bhojyaṃ pātre nidhāyāpyāyasvetyadibhir anumantrya grāsān kṛtvā praṇavādibhiḥ krameṇānumantrya prāṇāhutīḥ kṛtvā prāśnīyād iti || 27.8 ||
pratimantraṃ manasā namaḥ svāheti vā sarvān || 27.10 ||
namaḥ svāheti vā sarvān || 27.9 ||
mantraṃ mantraṃ prati namaskāraṃ svāhākāraṃ vānte kṛtvā sarvān grāsān manasānumantrayet | icchāto vikalpaḥ sarvagrahaṇād vibhaktānām anumantraṇam, na punar avibhaktānām | anumantraṇamantrāś caite na bhakṣaṇamantrāḥ | ato ‘nnam evam abhimantrya bhuñjīta || 27.10 ||
atha vā sarvān eva grāsān namaḥ svāhety anumantrayet | namaḥ svāhety anayor vikalpaḥ | samudito mantra ity anye || 27.9 ||
grāsapramāṇam āsyāvikāreṇa || 27.11 || — 27.10 ||
yāvatā grāsena mukhaṃ na vikriyate, nātivivṛtam ity arthaḥ, tāvad eva pramāṇaṃ syāt || 27.11 ||
yāvatpramāṇe grāse grasyamāna āsyam avikṛtaṃ bhavati tāvat tasya pramāṇam || 27.10 ||
carubhaikṣasaktukaṇayāvakaśākapayodadhighṛtamūlaphalodakāni havīṃṣy
uttarottaraṃ praśastāni || 27.12 || — || 27.11 ||
dvādaśaitāni dravyāṇi | eteṣāṃ havirdravyāṇāṃ yad yad uttaraṃ tat tat pūrvasmāt pūrvasmāt praśastataram, pīḍātiśayahetutvāt | carur haviṣyaṃ svayaṃ śrapayitavyam | bhaikṣaṃ yasyopapadyate brahmacāryānītaṃ vā | śeṣāṇi prasiddhāni | havirgrahaṇaṃ haviṣa iva saṃskārārtham | tato lavaṇaguḍādivarjanasiddhiḥ | tatra dravyāṇāṃ puṭikayā grāsakalpanā, tatra “guruṣu gurūṇi” [GDh 19.19] ity anena nyāyena kalpanā kartavyā || 27.12 ||
haviṣyair upakalpito navasrāvito viśadasiddhaudanaś caruḥ | bhaikṣaṃ brahmacāriṇā śiṣyādinā svayam ānītam, gṛhasthasya bhikṣācaraṇaniṣedhāt | cūrṇīkṛtā lājāḥ saktavaḥ | kaṇāḥ phalīkaraṇāni | yāvakaḥ pūrvam uktaḥ | anyāni prasiddhāni | dvādaśaitāni havīṃṣi | teṣu ca pūrvasmāt pūrvasmād uttaram uttaraṃ praśastam | tatra dravāṇāṃ patrapuṭādinā grāsakalpanā | tapāṃsi cainaḥsu guruṣu gurūṇi laghuṣu laghūni || 27.11 ||
paurṇamāsyāṃ pañcadaśa grāsān bhuktvaikāpacayenāparapakṣam
aśnīyāt || 27.13 || — || 27.12 ||
caturdaśyām upavāsaṃ kṛtvā eteṣām anyatamasya haviṣaḥ pañcadaśyāṃ pañcadaśagrāsān aśitvā tataḥ pratipadam ārabhya ekaikāhny ā kṛṣṇapakṣam aśnīyāt | aparapakṣagrahaṇaṃ vyāptyartham | sakalaṃ pakṣam anena prakāreṇa vyāpnuyāt, na tv aniyamena saṃkhyāṃ pūrayed iti | sakṛdbhojanārthaṃ vā gamyamānasya punarvacanam || 27.13 ||
evaṃ caturdaśyām upoṣyāparedyuḥ pañcadaśyāṃ pañcadaśa grāsān aśitvā tataḥ param ekāpacayena dvirvacane saty artha spaṣṭo bhavati pratyaham ekaikāpacayeneti | sarvam evāparapakṣam aśnīyāt | tithihrāse kramaprāpte navamībhojane yadā prātaḥ pañca nāḍyo navamī, aparedyuś ca daśamī nāsti tadā pūrvedur āgatāyām eva navamyāṃ nava grāsān bhuktvāparedyur ekādaśīprāptān ekādaśa grāsān bhuñjīta | daśamīprāptānāṃ daśagrāsānāṃ lopaḥ | evaṃ tithivṛddhāv ekādaśīgrāse prāpte yadā ṣaḍviṃśatināḍikā divā daśamī catasra ekādaśī, aparedyu rātrāv api kiyaty apy ekādaśī tadā pūrvedyur ekādaśyāṃ praniviṣṭāyām ekādaśa grāsān bhuktvāparedyur api tān evaikādaśa bhuñjīta | tasyāparedyur dvādaśeti prayogaḥ |
yathā kathaṃcit piṇḍānāṃ tisro ‘śītīḥ samāhitaḥ |
māsenāśnan haviṣyasya candrasyeti salokatām || [MDh 11.220] iti mānave cāndrāyaṇāntaraṃ vidhīyate | na punar upacayāpacayarūpa ukte cāndrāyaṇe piṇḍasaṃkhyāniyamaḥ | tathā ca yājñavalkyena spaṣṭam uktam |
yathā kathaṃcit piṇḍānāṃ catvāriṃśac chatadvayam |
māsenaikena bhuñjīta cāndrāyaṇam athāparam || iti [YDh 3.324] || 27.12 ||
amāvāsyāyām upoṣya ekoccayena pūrvapakṣam || 27.14 ||
amāvāsyāyām upoṣyaikopacayena pūrvapakṣam || 27.13 ||
tato ‘māvāsyāyām upavāsaṃ kṛtvā pratipatprabhṛty ekaikasyoccayena vṛddhyā śuklapakṣam aśnīyāt | tathā paurṇamāsyāṃ pañcadaśa grāsā bhavanti | madhyatanutvāt pipīlikāmadhyam idam || 27.14 ||
evam ekāpacayena grasyamāneṣu caturdaśyām eko grāso bhavati | amāvāsyāyām upavāsaḥ | amāvāsyāyām upoṣya pūrvpakṣapratipady ekaṃ grāsam aśitvaikaikopacayenaikaikagrāsavṛddhyā kṛtsnam eva pūrvapakṣaṃ aśnīyāt | paurṇamāsyāṃ pañcadaśa bhavanti | tad etat tanumadhyatvāt pipīlikāmadhyaṃ cāndrāyaṇam || 27.13 ||
viparītam ekeṣām || 27.15 || — || 27.14 ||
ekeṣām ācāryāṇāṃ matena viparītam idam eva bhavati | caturdaśyām upavāsaṃ kṛtvāmāvāsyāyām ekaṃ piṇḍam aśnīyāt | tataḥ pratipadam ārabhyaikaikavṛddhyā śuklapakṣam aśnīyāt | tathā ca caturdaśyāṃ pañcadaśa bhavanti | tataḥ paurṇamāsyām upavāsaḥ | pratipadi pañcadaśa grāsāḥ | tata ekaikāhny ā kṛṣṇapakṣam aśnīyād iti|
anye tv anyathā viparītam ācakṣate – paurṇamāsyām upoṣya pratipadam ārabhaikaikāpacayenāparapakṣam aśnīyāt, tato ‘māvāsyāprabhṛty ekaikoccayena pūrvapakṣam aśnīyād iti | madhyapṛthutvād yavamadhyam idam | tasmāt pūrvokto ‘yaṃ ca pipīlikāmadhyaḥ || 27.15 ||
ekeṣām ācāryāṇāṃ matenedam eva vidhānaṃ viparītaṃ bhavati | amāvāsyāyām upoṣyaikopacayena pūrvapakṣam aśitvā kṛṣṇapratipadam ārabhyaikāpa-cayenāparapakṣam aśnīyāc caturdaśyām eko grāso bhavati | amāvāsyāyām upavāsaḥ | tad etat sthūlama-dhyatvād yavamadhyaṃ cāndrāyaṇam || 27.14 ||
eṣa cāndrāyaṇo māsaḥ || 27.16 || — || 27.15 ||
candro yena hrāsavṛddhibhyām anugamyate tac cāndrāyaṇam | tatsahacarito ‘pi māsaḥ cāndrāyaṇa ity ucyate || 27.16 ||
evaṃ māsasādhyaṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ tadyogād eṣa māsaś cāndrāyaṇaḥ | yady apy ukte prakāre pipīlikāmadhye dvātriṃśad ahāni yavamadhye caikatriṃśat tathāpi na vaikenākṣareṇeti nyāyenaiṣa māsa ity uktam || 27.15 ||
etam āptvā vipāpo vipāpmā sarvam eno
hanti || 27.17 ||
evam āptvā vipāpo vipāpmā sarvam eno
hanti || 27.16 ||
etam evaṃvidhaṃ cāndrāyaṇam āptvā vipāpaḥ vihitākaraṇanimittasyainasaḥ kṣayeṇa | vipāpmā pratiṣiddhācaraṇanimittakilbiṣavināśāt | anyad api dehāntaragatam api sarvaprakāram eno hanti | evaṃ ca vihitākaraṇe ca kuṣṭhādyupaghāte sati cāndrāyanaṃ kartavyam iti sūcitaṃ bhavati || 27.17 ||
evam evaṃvidhaṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ māsam āptvā māsasādhyam etad vrataṃ kṛtvā vipāpo vihitākaraṇajanyapāpahīno bhavati | vipāpmā niṣiddhācaraṇabhavapāpahīnaḥ | sarvam eno hanti yac cānyaj janmāntarārjitaṃ sūkṣmam enas tad api sarvaṃ hanti || 27.16 ||
dvitīyam āptvā daśa pūrvān daśāparān ātmānaṃ caikaviṃśaṃ paṅktiṃ ca
punāti || 27.18 || — || 27.17 ||
nigadavyākhyānaṃ sūtram || 27.18 ||
dvāv āptveti vaktavye dvitīyam iti vacanaṃ nairantaryārthaṃ dvitīyaṃ māsaṃ nirantaram āptveti | kathaṃ punar nairantaryasya saṃbhavaḥ | yāvatā pipīlikāmadhye śvobhūtāṃ paurṇamāsīm upavased ity uktaṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ pañcadaśa grāsān bhuktveti ca tathāmāvāsyāyām upoṣyaikaikopacayena pūrvapakṣam aśnīyād iti taddvitīyapaurṇamāsyantaḥ sa prayogaḥ | tadanantaraṃ dvitīyasyārambhe caturdaśyām upavāsaḥ | pañcadaśyāṃ pañcadaśa grāsān iti ca nopapadyate | tasmād evam atra vaktavyam – nātra dvayoś cāndrāyaṇayor vidhānam | kiṃ tarhi | māsadvayasādhyam ekaṃ cāndrāyaṇam | tasyaiṣa phalavidhiḥ | tasyādau caturdaśyām upavāsas tṛtīye paurṇamāsyantaś ca prayogaḥ | madhye yathoktam | dvitīyā ca paurṇamāsī tantreṇa prathamasyāntyā dvitīyasyādyā | evaṃ yavamadhye dvitīyāmāvāsyā | etena saṃvatsaraṃ vāptveti vyākhyātam || 27.17 ||
saṃvatsaraṃ tv āptvā candramasaḥ salokatām āpnoty āpnoti || 27.19 ||
saṃvatsaraṃ cāptvā candrasamaḥ salokatām āpnoti salokatām āpnoti || 27.18 ||
tuśabdo ‘vyavahitārthaḥ | salokatāṃ sālokyaṃ gacchati || 27.19 ||
yas tu saṃvatsaram avyavadhānena cāndrāyaṇavrataṃ carati sa candramasaḥ sālokyam āpnoti | dviruktir vyākhyātā | atra manuḥ |
aṣṭāv aṣṭau samaśnīyāt piṇḍān madhaṃdine sthite |
niyatātmā haviṣyasya yaticāndrāyaṇaṃ caran ||
caturaḥ prātar aśnīyād dvijaḥ piṇḍān samāhitaḥ |
caturo ‘stamite sūrye śiśucāndrāyaṇaṃ caran || iti | [MDh 11.218–219], “yathā kathaṃcit piṇḍānām” [MDh 11.220] iti ca || 27.18 ||
atha aṣṭāviṃśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
idānīṃ “svāmī rikthakraya-” [GDh 10.38] iti, “rikthabhāja ṛṇam” [GDh 12.37] iti ca sūcitaṃ dāyavibhāgam āha |
atha dāyavibhāgaḥ |
ūrdhvaṃ pituḥ putrā rikthaṃ bhajeran || 28.1 ||
nanu kasmād itaravat prāyaścittāt prāg anabhidhānam |
tatraika āhuḥ – yadi putrā pitaraṃ balād vibhāgāya pravartayanti, pitā vā putrāṇāṃ karmādhikāre sati yathāprāptaṃ vibhāgaṃ na karoti, tato ‘nayoḥ prāyaścittaṃ nāstīty evamartham iti |
na punar etad vicāraṇīyaṃ yena na kvacid api vyatikrame prāyaścittābhāva iṣyate | ataḥ prāyaścittagauravārtham evam abhidhānam |
sūtram idānīṃ vivriyate — ūrdhvaṃ jīvanāt pituḥ prete tasminn ity arthaḥ | ekaśeṣaś ca draṣṭavyaḥ, “pitā mātrā” iti | tathāha manuḥ |
ūrdhvaṃ pituś ca mātuś ca sametya bhrātaraḥ saha |
bhajeran paitṛkaṃ riktham anīśās te hi jīvatoḥ || iti | [MDh 9.104] | pitṛśabdasya saṃbandhiśabdatvāt putragrahaṇaṃ gamyamānam api kriyate | puruṣāṇām eva vibhāga ity evamartham | tathā ca strīṇāṃ tāvat taittirīyakaśrutau śrūyate: “tasmāt striyo nirindriyā adāyādīḥ” [TaitS 6.5.8.10] iti | tathā napuṃsakānāṃ vasiṣṭha āha: “anaṃśās tv āśramāntaragatāḥ klībonmattapatitāś ca” [VaDh 17.52] iti | “jīvati vecchati” [GDh 28.2] iti vacanād ūrdhvam iti gamyamānam api abhidhīyate | ekoddiṣṭādisaṃskārakaraṇād ūrdhvam iti pratipādanārtham | rikthaṃ paitṛkaṃ dravyaṃ kṛṣyādilabdhaṃ rūḍhyā |
“vibhajeran” | atra vicāryate – svatve sati vibhāgaḥ, uta vibhāge sati svatvam?
atra kecid āhuḥ vibhāge satīti |
tad ayuktam, sarveṣāṃ vibhāgaprāpteḥ | vacanāt putrāṇām eva niyamya iti cet, “anīśās te jīvatoḥ” [MDh 9.104] iti liṅgāt | kutaḥ? ūrdhvaṃ pituś ca mātuś ceti niyamād eva prāgavibhāge siddhe – punar īśatvamātrapratiṣedhāt svatvāstitvaṃ jñāpayati | itarathā anarthakaḥ pratiṣedhaḥ prāptyabhāvāt | tasmāt svatve saty eva vibhāgaḥ | evaṃ tarhi jātaputrasya putrasaṃkramitatvāt parasvādānaṃ prāpnotīti cet, jātivad ubhayatra vartata ity adoṣaḥ | īśatvapratiṣedhāt tu putrāṇāṃ dānābhāvo ‘rthasiddhaḥ || 28.1 ||
ūrdhvaṃ pitur pitari mṛte tadīyaṃ rikthaṃ svagṛhakṣetradāsagavāśvasvarṇādikaṃ putrā bhajeran, putrās tatra bhāginaḥ | putrāṇāṃ tatsvāmitvam ity uktaṃ bhavati | ūrdhvaṃ pitur iti vacanāj jīvati tasmin na tatra putrāṇāṃ svāmyam | tathā ca manuḥ |
ūrdhvaṃ pituś ca mātuś ca sametya bhrātaraḥ saha |
bhajeran paitṛkaṃ riktham anīśās te hi jīvatoḥ || iti | [MDh 9.104] | pitṛśabdasya saṃbandhiśabdatvād eva siddhe putragrahaṇaṃ niyamārtham | tena pitur ūrdhvaṃ vibhajatāṃ mātāpy aṃśaṃ samaṃ hared ityādivacanajātam ācāryasyābhimataṃ na bhavati | putrā evaṃ sarvaṃ dhanādikaṃ gṛhītvā mātaraṃ yathāvad rekṣeyur iti manyate | śrūyate ca: “tasmāt striyo nirindriyā adāyādāḥ” iti | manur apy āha |
pitā rakṣati kaumāre bhartā rakṣati yauvane |
putrās tu sthavirībhāve na strī svātantryam arhati || iti [MDh 9.3] || 28.1 ||
nivṛtte rajasi mātur jīvati vecchati || 28.2 ||
H reads “cecchati”
uparatarajaskāyāṃ vā mātari jīvaty api pitari tadanujñayā vibhāgaḥ, icchā ca pituḥ na putrāṇām, jīvatīti sāmānādhikaraṇyāt | so ‘yaṃ vibhāgaḥ putrāṇāṃ karmādhikārāpekṣayā draṣṭavyaḥ || 28.2 ||
atha vā jīvaty api pitari putrā rikthaṃ bhajerann iti | icchati sati | tadanujñayety arthaḥ | tasya kālaḥ – nivṛtte rajasi mātuḥ | uparatarajaskāyāṃ nivṛttaprasavāyām ity uktaṃ bhavati || 28.2 ||
sarvaṃ vā pūrvajasyetarān bibhṛyāt pitṛvat || 28.3 ||
jyeṣṭha eva vā sarvaṃ dhanam ādāya itarān yavīyaso bhrātṝn bibhṛyāt poṣayet na vibhāgaṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ | pitṛvat yathā pitā bibharti | ayam api yavīyasāṃ karmānuṣṭhānasāmarthyābhāve jyeṣṭhavṛttitve sati draṣṭavyaḥ || 28.3 ||
jyeṣṭha eva sarvaṃ dhanaṃ svīkṛtya gṛhītvetarān kaniṣṭhān bibhṛyāt | te ‘pi tasmin putravad bhajeran
|| 28.3 ||
vibhāge tu dharmavṛddhiḥ || 28.4 ||
tuśabdo hetvarthaḥ | yasmāt pratyekam atithidānādikarmānuṣṭhānena dharmavṛddhis tasmād vibhāga eva yuktatara iti | arbhakāvasthāyām api bhinnabuddhitve sati vibhāgaḥ kartavya ity evamartha upadeśaḥ | vṛddhivacanāc cāṃśapradātur api dharmo ‘sty eveti jñāpayati || 28.4 ||
tuśabdaḥ pakṣaṃ vyāvartayati | naitad evaṃ jyeṣṭha eva bibhṛyād iti | yad uktaṃ vibhāga eva jyāyān yatas tatra dharmavṛddhiḥ | yathāha bṛhaspatiḥ |
ekapākena vasatāṃ pitṛdevadvijārcanam |
ekaṃ bhaved vibhaktānāṃ tad eva syād gṛhe gṛhe || 28.4 ||
adhunā kathaṃ punaḥ saṃvibhājya ity āha |
adhunā pitur ūrdhvaṃ jīvati ca tasmin vibhāgaprakāram āha |
viṃśatibhāgo jyeṣṭhasya mithunam ubhayatodadyukto ratho govṛśaḥ || 28.5 ||
sarvasmāt pitṛdhanād viṃśatitamo bhāgo jyeṣṭhasya | mithunaṃ gomithunaṃ prasiddham | ubhayatodataḥ aśvāśvataragardabhāḥ, teṣāṃ yathāsaṃbhavam anyatamābhyāṃ yukto rathaḥ | govṛṣaḥ prasiddhaḥ | ayam uddhāraḥ sati saṃbhave jyeṣṭhasya || 28.5 ||
sarvasmāt pitṛdhanād viṃśatitamo bhāgaḥ mithunaṃ gavādiṣu strīpuṃsayor yugmam | ubahatodantā aśvāśvataragardabhās teṣām anyatamābhyāṃ yukto rathaḥ govṛṣaḥ puṃgavaḥ | ayam uddhāro jyeṣṭhasya
|| 28.5 ||
kāṇakhorakūṭavaṇṭā madhyamasyānekāś cet || 28.6 ||
H reads “vaṇeṭā”
kāṇaḥ prasiddhaḥ | khoro vṛddhaḥ | khoṭa iti pāṭhe tu pādavikalaḥ | kūṭaḥ śṛṅgavikalaḥ | vaṇṭo vilopitavāladhiḥ | aviśeṣitatvāt gavāśvādīnām api sarveṣāṃ yathāsaṃbhavo draṣṭavyaḥ | madhyamasyāyam uddhāraḥ, te ca yadi bahavo bhavanti | itareṣām api santīty arthaḥ || 28.6 ||
kāṇa ekanetraḥ | vikalāṅga iti yāvat | khoro vṛddhaḥ | khoṭa iti pāṭhe vikalapādaḥ | kūṭaḥ śṛṅgahīnaḥ | vaṇeṭo vikalavāladhiḥ | gavāśvādiṣu ya evaṃrūpaḥ sa madhyamasyoddhāraḥ | sa ca kāṇādir yady aneko bhavati | itareṣām apy asti coditi || 28.6 ||
avir dhānyāyasī gṛham anoyuktaṃ catuṣpadāṃ caikaikaṃ yavīyasaḥ || 28.7 ||
aviḥ prasiddhaḥ | ekavacanād ekam, dhānyaṃ vrīhyādi | ayo lohaḥ | ānurūpyeṇa dhānyāyasoḥ kalpanārthaṃ dvivacanam | evaṃ ca na sarvaṃ gṛhṇīyāt | gṛham ekaṃ śakaṭayuktaṃ catuṣpadāṃ ca gavādīnām ekaikaṃ pṛthak pṛthag ānurūpyeṇa yavīyasaḥ kaniṣṭhā gṛhṇīyuḥ | ayaṃ kaniṣṭhoddhāraḥ || 28.7 ||
avir ūrṇāyuḥ | jātāv ekavacanam | yāvanto ‘vayaḥ, ekasya catuṣpadāṃ caikaikam ity eva siddhatvāt | apara āha: yady api pitur eka evāvis tathāpi sa yavīyasaḥ | catuṣpadāṃ caikaikam iti tu bahuviṣayam iti | dhānyaṃ vrīhyādi | aya āyasaṃ dātrādi | dhānyam ayaś ceti dhānyāyasī | etad ubhayaṃ yāvat kiṃcid gṛhe | gṛhaṃ yatrāsyate | anaḥ śakaṭaṃ yuktaṃ bāhyābhyām | catuṣpadāṃ ca gavādīnām ekam iṣṭaṃ gṛhṇīyāt | ayaṃ kanīyasa uddhāraḥ | ayaṃ ca sarvakanīyasaḥ | itareṣām uddhāro yo madhyamasya || 28.7 ||
samadhetarat sarvam || 28.8 ||
samadhā cetarat sarvam || 28.8 ||
evam uddhṛtoddhāraśiṣṭaṃ samaṃ vibhaktavyam |
nanu ca vibhāge samadhaiva sarvasmin prāpte vacanād uddhāra ukte śeṣaṃ samadhaiva prāpnotīti nārtho ‘nena sūtreṇa |
naiṣa doṣaḥ, yadi hi pitā vibhāgaṃ karoti tadoddhārapratiṣedhārtham idam iti |
anye tu pitur uddhārapratiṣedhārtham idam iti bruvate | ayaṃ ca prakāro jyeṣṭhe kaniṣṭhe ca guṇavati draṣṭavyaḥ || 28.8 ||
itarad uddhṛtaśiṣṭaṃ sarvaṃ sarve samadhā gṛhṇīyuḥ | samam ity arthaḥ | dvidhā bahudhetyādau dṛṣṭo dhāpratyayaḥ prayuktaḥ || 28.8 ||
dvyaṃśī vā pūrvajasya || 28.9 ||

ekaikam itareṣām || 28.10 ||

H reads “labhate”

kalpāntareṣu bahuṣu kṣetrādiṣv ekaikaṃ dhanarūpaṃ jyeṣṭhānupūrvyād gṛhṇīyuḥ | kāmyaṃ yasya yad iṣṭaṃ sa tad gṛhṇīyād iti | sarveṣv iṣṭaṃ jyeṣṭas tadrahiteṣv iṣṭam anantara iti | ayam uddhāraḥ sarveṣām || 28.9 ||

naikaśaphadvipadānām || 28.13 ||
naikaśaphadvipadām || 28.11 ||
aśvādīnāṃ stryādīnāṃ ca na daśa daśa grāhyā ity arthaḥ | yadi sarve nirguṇās tadānīṃ samam eva vibhāgo draṣṭavyaḥ | atra viśeṣānupādānāt, smṛtyantare ca dvirukteḥ pūrvasya tatprayojanatvāt | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “atha bhrātṝṇāṃ dāyavibhāgaḥ” [VaDh 17.40] iti | yadā kecit guṇavantaḥ kecin nirguṇās tadā jyeṣṭho daśamam uddhāraṃ gṛhītvā anyat samaṃ vibhajet | yathāha uśanā: “jyeṣṭhasya daśamoddhāraḥ kaniṣṭhasyābhipreto bhāgaḥ” iti | asmin deśe ayam eva vibhāgo draṣṭavyaḥ, samācārāt || 28.13 ||
ekaśaphānām aśvādīnāṃ dvipadāṃ dāsyādīnāṃ ca daśakaṃ na gṛhṇīyuḥ | kiṃ tu pūrvoktam aikaikam eveti | dvipādānām iti pāṭhe pādaśabdena samānarthaḥ padaśabdaḥ | evam ekamātṛkāṇāṃ soddhāro vibhāga uktaḥ || 28.11 ||

jyeṣṭhasyeti vartate | jyeṣṭhāyāḥ putraś ca bhavati yo jyeṣṭhasya bhavati tasya pañcadaśa gāva ṛṣabhaś caika uddhāraḥ | samam anyat || 28.13 ||

H reads “yavīyasām”
sama evāyaṃ vibhāgo bhavati | ajyaiṣṭhineyena kaniṣṭhāyāṃ jātena jyeṣṭhena saha yavīyasaḥ jyeṣṭhāyāḥ putrasya kanīyasaḥ | ekasya mātṛto jyaiṣṭhyam anyasya janmataḥ | ayam api guṇavadviṣayo draṣṭavyaḥ || 28.16 ||
jyeṣṭhasyeti vatate | tac cājyaiṣṭhineyenety anena sāmānādhikaraṇyāt tṛtīyāntaṃ saṃpadyate | ajyaiṣṭhineyena kaniṣṭhāyāṃ jātena jyeṣṭhena saha yavīyasāṃ jyaiṣṭhineyānāṃ samo vā vibhāgaḥ | ekasya janmato jyaiṣṭhyam anyeṣāṃ mātṛta iti || 28.14 ||
pratimātṛ vā svavarge bhāgaviśeṣaḥ || 28.17 || — || 28.15 ||
H reads “svasvavarge”
yadā tu bahvyaḥ samānajātīyā asamānāpatyāś ca jāyā bhavati, tadā putrāśrayasya vibhāgasya duṣkaratvān mātṛta eva vibhajyate | sve sve varge bhāgaviśeṣo ‘pi draṣṭavyaḥ | sa ukto “viṃśatibhāgo jyeṣṭhasya” [GDh 24.5] ityādinā || 28.17 ||

iti saṃvādya || 28.18 || — || 28.16 ||
pitā na tu mātāpi | utsṛjet arpayet na tu dadyāt śāstreṇa putratvāt,
pāṇigrāhaṇikā mantrā kanyāsv eva pratiṣṭhitāḥ | [MDh 8.226] iti coktatvāt | putrikāṃ duhitaram | anapatyaḥ avidyamānaputraḥ | āgneyīṃ prājāpatyāṃ ceṣṭiṃ kṛtvā anāhitāgniś cet sthālīpākavidhānena āhutidvayaṃ hutveti draṣṭavyam | tato dānakāle ‘smadartham apatyaṃ yad asyāṃ bhaviṣyatīty evaṃ vareṇa saha saṃvādaṃ kṛtvā | tata eva pratipāditāyāṃ yaḥ putraḥ sa mātāmahasya putro bhavati, notpādayitur ity abhiprāyaḥ || 28.18 ||
pitā nāma tām utsṛjed dadyāt | bhāvisaṃjñānirdeśo ‘yam | yathā yūpaṃ chinattīti | putrikāṃ bhaviṣyantī duhitaram anapatyo ‘putro ‘gniṃ prajāpatiṃ ceṣṭvāgnaye svāhā prajāpataye svāhety ājyabhāgān anantaram aupāsana ājyena hutvāsmadartham apatyam iti saṃvādya yasmai dadāti tena saṃvādaṃ kārayitvā | tatra prakāro vasiṣṭhena darśitaḥ |
abhrātṛkāṃ pradāsyāmi tubhyaṃ kanyām alaṃkṛtām |
asyāṃ janiṣyate putraḥ sa me putro bhaved iti || [VaDh 17.17] | evaṃ dattā sā putrikā tasyāṃ jāto mātāmahasyaiva putro notpādayituḥ | ata eva manuḥ |
mātuḥ prathamataḥ piṇḍaṃ nirvapet putrikāsutaḥ |
dvitīyaṃ tu pitus tasyās tṛtīyaṃ tu pituḥ pituḥ || iti | [MDh 9.140] | evaṃ sarve garbhāḥ putrikāpy eṣā pituḥ putrapratinidhiḥ | “ive pratikṛtau” [Pāṇ 5.3.96] saṃjñāyām kann iti | saiva ca rikthagrāhiṇī | tathā ca manuḥ |

abhisaṃdhimātrāt putrikety ekeṣām || 28.19 || — || 28.17 ||
