piṇḍasaṃbandhāḥ sapiṇḍāḥ, tadabhāve gotrasaṃbandhāḥ sagotrāḥ, tadabhāve ṛṣisaṃbandhāḥ samānārṣeyāḥ | evam āsannasyābhāve viprakṛṣṭas taddravyabhāg iti || 28.21 ||
yasya putrikārūpam apy apatyaṃ nāsti so ‘napatyaḥ | tasya rikthaṃ piṇḍādisaṃbandhā bhajeran strī vā | piṇḍasaṃbandhāḥ sapiṇḍāḥ | gotrasaṃbandhāḥ sagotrāḥ | hārītasya hārīta itivat | ṛṣisaṃbandhāḥ samānapravarāḥ haritakutsapiśaṅgaśaṅkhadarbha-haimagavāḥ parasparam | evam anyatrāpi | tatra sapiṇḍādyāḥ pratyāsattikrameṇa gṛhṇīyuḥ | tathā ca āpastambaḥ: “putrābhāve yaḥ pratyāsannaḥ sapiṇḍaḥ” [ĀpDh 2.14.2] iti | tad yathā – pitā mātā ca sodaryas tatputrā bhinnodarā bhrātaras tatputrāḥ pitṛvya ityādi | sapiṇḍābhāve sagotrās tadabhāve samānapravarāḥ | strī tu sarvaiḥ sagorādibhiḥ samuccīyate | yadā sapiṇḍādayo gṛhṇanti tadā taiḥ saha patny apy ekam aṃśaṃ haret | tathā |
pitur ūrdhvaṃ vibhajatāṃ mātāpy aṃśaṃ samaṃ haret | iti | [YDh 2.123] | ata eva strī pṛthaṅnirdiṣṭā | sapiṇḍādayaḥ samānena | patnīdāyas tv ācāryasya pakṣe na bhavati | manur api |
nirindriyā adāyādā striyo nityam iti sthitiḥ || iti | [MDh 9.18] | atra sapiṇḍādyabhāve bṛhaspatiḥ |
anyatra brāhmaṇāt kiṃ tu rājā dharmaparāyaṇaḥ |
tat strīṇāṃ jīvanaṃ dadyād eṣa dāyavidhiḥ smrtaḥ ||
annārthaṃ taṇḍulaprastham aparāhṇe tu sendhanam |
vasanaṃ tripaṇakrītaṃ deyam ekaṃ trimāsataḥ ||
etāvad eva sādhvīnāṃ coditaṃ vidhināśanam || iti |
tad evaṃ manu-bṛhaspatibhyāṃ patnīdāyasyātyantābhāva uktaḥ | yājñavalkyena tu patnīdāyaḥ sa uktaḥ: “patnī dutaraś ca” [YDh 2.135] ityādi | atra vyāsaḥ |
dvisahasrapaṇo dāyaḥ patnyai deyo dhanasya tu |
yac ca bhartrā dhanaṃ dattaṃ sā yathākāmam āpnuyāt || iti | ācāryeṇa tu sapiṇḍādisamāṃśagrahaṇam uktam | tatra sarvam eva dhanaṃ sapiṇḍādyā gṛhītvā striyo yāvajjīvaṃ rakṣeyur iti mukhyaḥ kalpaḥ | tadasaṃbhave ‘śanavasanayoḥ paryāptaṃ dhanakṣetrādikam aṃśatvena vyapohya śeṣaṃ gṛhṇīyuḥ | tathā ca bṛhaspatinā patnīdāyaṃ pratiṣiddhyānta uktam |
vasanasyāśanasyaiva tathaiva rajatasya ca |
trayaṃ vyapohya tacchiṣṭaṃ dāyādānāṃ prakalpayet ||
dhūmāvasārikaṃ dravyaṃ sahāyās tān ataḥ purā |
tathaivāśanavāsāṃsi vigaṇayya dhane mṛtā || iti ||
|| 28.19 ||
strī cānapatyasya || 28.22 ||
putrikāputrasyāpy abhāva etat | kutaḥ? “putrikām anapatyaḥ” [GDh 28.18] ity anapatyādhikāre punar anapatyagrahaṇāt | te yadaiva piṇḍādisaṃbandhā rikataṃ bhajeran tadā stry api bhāgaṃ labheta | caśabdād ācāryaḥ śiṣyaś ca, sāmarthyāt sarvābhāve | tad āha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “tadabhāve ṛtvigācāryau” iti
|| 28.22 ||
bījaṃ vā lipseta || 28.23 || — || 28.20 ||
yad vā – sapiṇḍādibhyo bījam icched apatyam utpādayed ity arthaḥ | icchāto vikalpaḥ niyogato vā
|| 28.23 ||
atha vā strī sapiṇḍādibhyo bījaṃ lipseta | apatyam utpādayed ity uktaṃ bhavati | asmin pakṣe tu na sapiṇḍādyā dhanaṃ gṛhṇīyur eṣyanto ‘patyasyārthāya rakṣeyuḥ || 28.20 ||
asmin pakṣe viśeṣaḥ |
devaravatyām anyajātam abhāgam || 28.24 || — || 28.21 ||

|| 28.24 ||
devare vidyamāne yady anyato bījaṃ lipseta tatas tasyāṃ jātam apatyam abhāgaṃ bhāgarahitam | na tasya dhanagrahaṇam asti | asati tu devare ‘nyato jātam apy apatyaṃ sabhāgam eva || 28.21 ||
strīdhanaṃ duhitṝṇām aprattānām apratiṣṭhitānāṃ ca || 28.25 || — || 28.22 ||
strīdhanaṃ ṣaṭprakāram | yathāha manuḥ |
adhyagnyadhyāvāhanikaṃ dattaṃ ca prītikarmaṇi |
mātṛbhrātṛpitṛprāpaṃ ṣaḍvidhaṃ strīdhanaṃ smṛtam || iti | [MDh 9.194] | yac cānyat tayā karmaṇopāttaṃ tat tasyāṃ mṛtāyāṃ tasyā eva duhitṝṇām adattānām anapatyānāṃ ca bhavati |
anya āhuḥ – apratiṣṭhitānāṃ putrāṇām akṛtavivāhānām iti |
apare niḥsvānām iti |
caśabdāt bhartuś ca | atra adattānāṃ duhitṝṇām abhāve putrā niḥsvā gṛhṇīyuḥ, tadabhāve akṛtavivāhāḥ, tadabhāve anapatyāḥ striyaḥ, sarveṣām abhāve bharteti kramo draṣṭavyaḥ || 28.25 ||
pitṛmātṛsutabhrātṛdattam adhyagny upāgatam |
ādhivedanikādyaṃ ca strīdhanaṃ parikīrtitam || [YDh 2.143] iti yājñavalkyaḥ | tat strīdhanaṃ tasyāṃ mṛtāyāṃ duhitṝṇām aprattānāṃ bhavati | yadi sarvā api prattā apratiṣṭhitānāṃ bhavati | prattāsu pratiṣṭhitāḥ kāścit kāścid apratiṣṭhitāḥ | prattāprattāsamavāye ‘prattā gṛhṇāti | pratiṣṭhitāpratiṣṭhitāsamavāye ‘pratiṣṭhitā gṛhṇāti | yadā prasūtādidhanaṃ tadā sarvāsāṃ bhavati | eṣā mātur ūrdhvaṃ jīvantyāṃ pitṛkulalabdhasya strīdhanasya gatiḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
mātus tu yautakaṃ yat syāt kumārībhāga eva saḥ | [MDh 9.131] | vasiṣṭhaś ca “mātuḥ pāriṇeyaṃ striyo vibhajeran” [VaDh 17.46] iti | yat tu śākkhalikhitābhyām uktam: “samaṃ sarve sodaryā mātṛkaṃ dravyam arhāḥ strīkumāryaś ca” iti | tadbhartṛkulalabdheḥ prattāsu duhitṛṣu | tatra prattāviṣaye prabhūtatame mānavam |
jananyāṃ saṃsthitāyāṃ tu samaṃ sarve sahodarāḥ |
bhajeran mātṛkaṃ riktaṃ bhaginyaś ca sanābhayaḥ ||
yās tv āsāṃ syur duhitaras tāsām api yathārhataḥ |
mātāmahyā dhanāt kiṃcid pradeyaṃ prītipūrvakam || iti | [MDh 9.192–193] | tatraivālpe dhane bārhaspatyam |
strīdhanaṃ tadapatyānāṃ duhitṝṇāṃ tadāśinī |
aprattā cet samūḍhā sā labheta tu samātṛkam || iti ||
|| 28.22 ||
bhaginīśulkaṃ sodaryāṇām ūrdhvaṃ mātuḥ || 28.26 || — || 28.23 ||
bhaginīpradānanimittaṃ yad dravyaṃ labdham ārṣāsuravivāhayoḥ tat sodaryāṇām ūrdhvaṃ mātuḥ, tasyāṃ mṛtāyām ity arthaḥ | evaṃ ca pitur abhāve mātaiva tasyeṣṭa ity uktaṃ bhavati || 28.2 ||
bhaginīpradānanimittaṃ pitrā yad gṛhītaṃ dravyam āsurārṣavivāhayos tasmin mṛte tasyā bhaginyā eva sodaryā bhrātaras teṣāṃ bhavati | tac ca mātur ūrdhvaṃ jīvantyāṃ mātari tasyā eva na tu mṛtasya pitur etat svam iti | tatra ye bhāgino bhinnodarā bhrātaro mātṛsapatnī ceti te sarve ‘ṃśaṃ na gṛhṇīyur iti | yatra vivāhasamaye bhartrādikulena bhaginyādyau dattam ābharaṇakṣetrādikaṃ tat tasyā eva | mṛtāyāṃ ca tasyām aprajasi yājñavalkyenoktam |
bandhudattaṃ tathā śulkam anvādheyakam eva ca |
aprajāyām atītāyāṃ bāndhavās tad avāpnuyuḥ ||

pūrvaṃ caike || 28.27 || — || 28.24 ||

asaṃsṛṣṭivibhāgaḥ pretānāṃ jyeṣṭhasya || 28.28 || — || 28.25 ||
asaṃsṛṣṭino vibhaktāḥ santaḥ pṛthak pṛthak yadā bhrātaro vyavaharanti teṣāṃ yaḥ praiti tasya anapatyasya | kutaḥ? anapatyādhikārāt | yo bhāgaḥ saḥ svasvajyeṣṭhasyaiva bhavati na sarveṣāṃ, sa pitṛsama iti | dvaimātṛkāṇāṃ samānajātīyānām asamānajātīyānām abhinnamātṛkāṇāṃ ca yo bhāgaḥ saḥ svasvavargajyeṣṭhasyaiva bhavati || 28.28 ||
asaṃsṛṣṭino vibhaktabhrātaraḥ | vibhaktavyo vibhāgaḥ | asaṃsṛṣṭināṃ vibhāgo ‘saṃsṛṣṭivibhāgaḥ | pretānām ity etad upasarjanībhūtānām apy asaṃsṛṣṭināṃ viśeṣaṇam | anapatyasya ceti vartate | asaṃsṛṣṭināṃ vibhaktānām anapatyānāṃ bhrātṝṇāṃ pretānāṃ yo vibhāgo vibhaktavyo dhanādiḥ sa jyeṣṭhasya bhrātur bhavati netareṣāṃ bhrātṝṇāṃ nāpi patnyā na ca pitror ity ācāryasya pakṣaḥ | tathā ca śaṅkhalikhita-patiṭhīnasī: “aputrasya svaryātasya bhrātṛgāmi dravyaṃ tadabhāve mātāpitarau haretāṃ patnī vā jyeṣṭhā sagotraśiṣyasya brahmacāriṇaś ca” iti | manus tu |

yas tu vibhakto ‘pi punaḥ kāryavaśāt saṃsṛjyate sa eva tadbhāgabhāg bhavati | punaḥ pretagrahaṇaṃ sodaryārtham anyeṣāṃ yathāprāptam eveti || 28.29 ||
bhrātrādibhiḥ saṃsṛṣṭaṃ dhanaṃ yasya sa saṃsṛṣṭī sādhāraṇadhano ‘vibhakto vibhajya saṃsṛṣṭaś ca,

pitṛvyeṇātha vā prītyā sa tatsaṃsṛṣṭa ucyate ||

vibhaktajaḥ pitryam eva || 28.30 || — || 28.27 ||

|| 28.30 ||

svayam ārjitam avaidyebhyo vaidyaḥ kāmaṃ na dadyāt || 28.31 || — || 28.28 ||
H reads “arjitam”
yat tu pitur dravyam anupaghnatā svayam upāttaṃ bhavati, vaidyagrahaṇād vā pratigrahādinā, tad itarebhyaḥ kāmam icchāto na dadyāt | yathāha manuḥ |
anupaghnan pitur dravyaṃ śrameṇa yad upārjayet |
svayam īhitalabdaṃ tan nākāmo datum arhati ||
vidyādhanaṃ tu yad yasya tat tasyaiva dhanaṃ bhavet || [MDh 9.206, 208] ityādi || 28.31 ||
vidyām adhīta iti vaidyaḥ | svayam arjitaṃ vidyārahitebhyo bhrātṛbhyaḥ kāmaṃ na dadyāt | adāne ‘pi na pratyavāyo dāne tv abhyudaya iti || 28.28 ||
avaidyāḥ samaṃ vibhajeran || 28.32 || — || 28.29 ||
yat tu mūrkhāḥ kṛṣyādinopārjayeyuḥ tat samam eva vibhajeyuḥ | tathā cāha |
avaidyānāṃ tu sarveṣām īhātaś ced dhanaṃ bhavet |

yadā tu sarve bhrātaro mūrkhāḥ kṛṣyādinopārjayeyus tadā samaṃ vibhajeran | vaidyenāpi kṛṣyādinā yad arjitaṃ na vidayā labdhaṃ yadi pitṛdravyāvirodhi tatra sāmyam eva | tatra sūtradvayam api caitad bhrātṛviṣayam eva | pitari tu jīvati viduṣāviduṣā vāvibhaktenārjitaṃ pitur eva |

rikthabhājaḥ || 28.33 || — || 28.30 ||
aurasaḥ svasya bhāryāyāṃ svayam utpādyate | tathāha manuḥ |
svakṣetre saṃskṛtāyāṃ tu svayam utpādayet tu yam |
tam aurasaṃ vijānīyāt putraṃ prathamakalpitam || iti | [MDh 9.166] | kṣetrajo yo niyogadvāreṇotpādyate | yathāha manuḥ |
yas talpajaḥ pramītasya klībasya vyādhitasya vā |

mātā pitā vā dadyātāṃ yam adbhiḥ putram āpadi |

sadṛśaṃ tu prakuyād yaṃ guṇadoṣavivarjitam |
putraṃ putraguṇair yuktaṃ sa vijñeyas tu kṛtrimaḥ || iti | [MDh 9.169] | gūḍhotpanno nigūḍham utpāditaḥ | yatra bījī na jñāyate so ‘yaṃ bījina avijñānān mātṛjātīyaḥ | evaṃ sahoḍhakānīnāv api |
utpadyate gṛhe yasya na vijñāyeta kasya saḥ |
sa gṛhe gūḍha utpannaḥ tasya syād yasya talpajaḥ || iti | [MDh 9.170] | apaviddho yaḥ parityakto ‘nyena gṛhyate vijñānena | yathāha |
mātāpitṛbhyām utsṛṣṭaṃ tayor anyatareṇa vā |

auraso dharmapatnījaḥ | atra yājñavalkyaḥ |

sahoḍhaḥ sagarbhāyām ūḍhāyām utpadyate | yathāha manuḥ |

utpādayet punar bhūtvā sa paunarbhava ucyate ||
sā ced akṣatayoniḥ syāt gatapratyāgatāpi vā |
paunarbhavena bhartrā sā punaḥ saṃskāram arhati || iti | [MDh 9.175–76] | putrikāputraḥ pūrvoktaḥ | svayaṃdatto yaḥ svayam evātmānaṃ pratipādayati | tathāha |
mātāpitṛvihīno yaḥ tyakto vā syād akāraṇāt |
ātmānaṃ sparśayed yasmai svayaṃdattas tu sa smṛtaḥ || iti | [MDh 9.177] | krītaḥ dhanena svīkṛtaḥ | yathāha manuḥ |
krīṇīyād yas tv apatyārthaṃ mātāpitror yam antikāt |
sa krītakaḥ sutas tasya sadṛśo ‘sadṛśo ‘pi vā || iti | [MDh 9.174] | ete gotrabhājaḥ udakadānādinā putrārthaṃ kurvantīty arthaḥ | evaṃ caite na rikthabhājaḥ, pūrve tu rikthabhājo gotrabhājaś ca | kuta etat? aurasena sahābhidhānāt smṛtyantarāc ca |
aurasaḥ kṣetrajaś caiva dattaḥ kṛtrima eva ca |
gūḍhotpanno ‘paviddhaś ca dāyādā bāndhavāś ca ṣaṭ || iti | [MDh 9.159] | bāndhavā gotrabhāja ity arthaḥ | putratvād eva gotrabhāktve prāpte tadvacanam iha rikthabhāktvapratiṣedhārtham | aurasādisadbhāve ca teṣāṃ rikthabhāktvapratiṣedhād aurasādisadbhāve caite grāsācchādanamātram arhanti putratvād itareṣām | yathāha |
sarveṣām api tu nyāyyaṃ dātuṃ śaktyā manīṣiṇā |
grāsācchādanam atyantaṃ patito hy adadad bhavet || iti | [MDh 9.202] | tadabhāve tu bhavaty eva rikthabhāktvam, putrapratinidhitvāt | yathāha |
kṣetrajādīn sutān etān ekādaśa yathoditān |
putrapratinidhīn āhuḥ kriyālopān manīṣiṇaḥ || iti | [MDh 9.180] | pratinidhiś ca mukhyadravyābhāve samagraphala eva bhavati, somābhāve pūtīkavat | putrikāputrasyehābhidhānaṃ gotrabhāktva-prāpaṇārtham | rikthabhāktvaṃ tv aurasasadbhāve ‘pi bhavaty eva | yathāha manuḥ |
putrikāyāṃ kṛtāyāṃ tu yadi putro ‘nujāyate |
samas tatra vibhāgaḥ syāt jyeṣṭhatā nāsti hi striyāḥ || iti | [MDh 9.134] | śūdrāputrasyehānabhidhānam anaurasatvād arikthabhāktvād vā | tathāha |
brāhmaṇakṣatriyaviśāṃ śūdrāputro na rikthabhāk |
yad evāsya pitā didyāt tad evāsya dhanaṃ bhavet || [MDh 9.155] || 28.34 ||
pitṛveśmani kanyā tu yaṃ putraṃ janayed iha |
taṃ kānīnaṃ vaden nāmnā voḍhuḥ kanyāsamudbhavam || iti | [MDh 9.172]
atra vasiṣṭhaḥ |
aprattā duhitā yasya putraṃ vindeta tulayataḥ |
pautrī mātāmahas tena dadyāt piṇḍaṃ hared dhanam || iti | [VaDh 17.23] | yājñavalkyaḥ |
kānīnaḥ kanyakājāto mātāmahasuto yataḥ || iti | [YDh 2.129] | tatrāprattāyām eva mṛtāyāṃ mātāmahasya putraḥ pautro vā | ūḍhāyāṃ voḍhuḥ | atra manuḥ |
yā garbhiṇī saṃskriyate jñātājñātāpi vā satī |
voḍuḥ sa garbho bhavati sahoḍha iti cocyate ||
yā patyā vā parityaktā vidhavā vā svecchayā |
utpādayed punarbhūtvā sa paunarbhava ucyate || [MDh 9.173, 175] | putrikāputraḥ pūrvam evoktaḥ | manuḥ |
mātāpitṛvihīno yas tyakto vā syād akāraṇāt |
ātmānaṃ sparśayed yasmai svayaṃdattas tu sa smṛtaḥ ||
krīṇīyād yas tv apatyārthaṃ mātāpitror yam antikāt |
sa krītakaḥ sutas tasya sadṛśo ‘sadṛśo ‘pi vā || iti | [MDh 9.177,174] | ete tu gotrabhājo gotram eva kevalaṃ bhajante na riktham | pūrve tu rikthabhājo gotrabhājaś caurasena sahābhidhānāt | sarve caite sajātīyāḥ,
sajātīyeṣv ayaṃ proktas tanayeṣu mayā vidhiḥ | [YDh 2.133] iti yājñavalkyavacanāt || 28.31 ||
caturthāṃśino vaurasādyabhāve || 28.35 ||
caturthāṃśina aurasādyabhāve || 28.32 ||
ṣaṇṇām aurasādyānām abhāve kānīnādayaś caturthāṃśabhājaḥ, samastabhājo va, vāśabdāt | tathāha śaṅkhaḥ: “upārjitaṃ jyeṣṭhāya tadabhāve kṣetrajaputrikāputrayos tayor apy abhāve trayāṇām itareṣāṃ teṣām apy abhāve ṣaḍbhyaḥ” iti |
anye tu “aurasādyabhāve” ity etad uttarasūtrārtham āhuḥ | tataś ca kānīnādayaś caturbhāgārhā jīvanamātrasaṃbandhino veti || 28.35 ||
atha vā naite kānīnādayo na rikthabhājaḥ kiṃ tu caturthāṃśinaḥ | pitṛdhanasya caturtham aṃśaṃ bhajeran | pūrvoktānāṃ ṣaṇṇām aurasādīnām abhāve | bhāve tu ta eva bhajeran | caturthāṃśavyatiriktaṃ ca sapiṇḍā gṛhṇīyuḥ | yad atra putrikāputrasyaurasādy-abhāve ‘pi caturthāṃśabhāktvam uktaṃ tad apakṛṣṭaputrikāputraviṣayam | yo hīnavarṇāyā bhāryāyā duhitaraṃ putrikāṃ karoti tatrāpy abhisaṃdhimātreṇa tatputraviṣayam ity arthaḥ | atra manuḥ |
putrikāyāṃ kṛtāyāṃ tu yadi putro ‘nu jāyate |
samas tatra vibhāgaḥ syāj jyeṣṭhatā nāsti hi striyaḥ || [MDh 9.134] iti |
ṣaṣṭhaṃ tu kṣetrajasyāṃśaṃ pradadyāt paitṛkād dhanāt |
auraso vibhajan dāyaṃ pitryaṃ pañcamam eva vā ||
aurasakṣetrajau putrau pitṛrikthasya bhāginau |
daśāpare tu kramaśo gotrarikthāṃśabhāginaḥ || [MDh 9.164–165] iti ca | atra dattaputra-grahaṇānantaraṃ vasiṣṭhaḥ: “yasmiṃś cet pratigṛhīta aurasaḥ putra utpadyate caturthabhāgabhāgī syāt” iti | atra kātyāyanaḥ |
utpanne tv aurase putre tṛtīyāṃśaharāḥ sutāḥ |
savarṇā asavarṇās tu grāsācchādanabhāginaḥ || iti |
atra bṛhaspatiḥ |
eka evaurasaḥ pitrye dhane svāmī prakīrtitaḥ |
tattulyaḥ putrikāputro bhartavyās tv apare smṛtāḥ ||
kṣetrajādyāḥ sutās tv anye pañcaṣaṭsaptabhāginaḥ || iti | hārītaḥ: “vibhajiṣyamāṇa ekaviṃśaṃ kānīnāya dadyād, viṃśaṃ paunarbhavāyaikonaviṃśaṃ dvyāmuṣyāyaṇāyāṣṭādaśaṃ kṣetrajāya saptadaśaṃ putrikāputrāyetarān aurasāya” iti | yājñavalkyo dvādaśa putrān anukramyāha |
piṇḍado ‘ṃśaharaś caiṣāṃ pūrvābhāve paraḥ paraḥ | iti | [YDh 2.132]
manur api |
śreyasaḥ śreyaso ‘bhāve yavīyān riktham arhati | iti | [MDh 9.184] | nārado ‘pi |
kramād ete pravartante mṛte pitari taddhane |
jyāyaso jyāyaso ‘bhāve jaghanyas tad avāpnuyāt || iti | vasiṣṭho ‘pi: “yasya tu pūrveṣāṃ ca na kaścid dāyādaḥ syād ete tasya dāyaṃ hareyuḥ” [VaDh 17.39] iti | atraurasaḥ putrikāputraḥ kṣetrajaḥ kānīno guḍhotpanno ‘paviddhaḥ sahoḍhaḥ paunarbhavo dattaḥ svayamupāgataḥ kṛtakaḥ krīta iti krameṇa putrān abhidhāya devalaḥ |
ete dvādaśa putrās tu santatyartham udāhṛtāḥ |
ātmajāḥ parajāś caiva labdhā yādṛcchikās tathā ||
teṣāṃ ṣaḍ bandhudāyādāḥ pūrvaṃ ye pitur eva ṣaṭ |
viśeṣaś cāpi putrāṇām ānupūrvyād viśiṣyate ||
sarve ‘py anaurasasyaite putrā dāyaharāḥ smṛtāḥ |
aurase punar utpanne teṣu jyaiṣṭhyaṃ na gacchati ||
teṣāṃ savarṇā ye putrās te tṛtīyāṃśabhāginaḥ |
hīnāḥ samupajīveyur grāsācchādanasaṃbhṛtāḥ || iti |
bandhudāyādā iti bandhūnāṃ sapiṇḍānām apy ete dāyaṃ hareyur na kevalaṃ pitur eva | itare pitur eveti | eṣa eva smṛtyantareṣv api bandhudāyādaśabdasyārthaḥ | tad evam –
aurasaḥ putrikā bījikṣetriṇau putrikāsutaḥ |
paunarbhavaś ca kānīnaḥ sahoḍho gūḍhasaṃbhavaḥ ||
dattakrītasvayaṃdattāḥ kṛttrimaś cāpaviddhakaḥ |
yatra kva cotpāditaś ca putrākhyā daśa pañca ca ||
anenaiva krameṇaiṣāṃ pūrvābhāve paraḥ paraḥ |
piṇḍado ‘ṃśaharaś ceti yuktā guṇavaśā sthitiḥ || iti ||

savarṇāputrāṇāṃ vibhāga uktaḥ | asavarṇāsutānāṃ tu viśeṣam āha |
uktaḥ savarṇaputrāṇāṃ vibhāgaḥ | atha kramavivāheṣv asavarṇāputreṣu viśeṣam āha |
brāhmaṇasya rājanyāputro jyeṣṭho guṇasaṃpannas tulyabhāk || 28.36 || — || 28.33 ||
brāhmaṇasya rājanyāputro jyeṣṭho guṇasaṃpannaś ca yaḥ sa brāhmaṇīputreṇa yavīyasā tulyabhāk || 28.36 ||
brāhmaṇasya rājanyāyāṃ jātaḥ putro yadi guṇasaṃpanno jyeṣṭhaś ca bhavati tadā brāhmaṇīputreṇa yavīyasā tulyabhāk | ekasya vayasā jyaiṣṭhyam aparasya jātyeti || 28.33 ||
jyeṣṭhāṃśahīnam anyat || 28.37 || — || 28.34 ||
jyeṣṭhāṃśo ya uddhāraḥ pūrvokto “viṃśatibhāgo jyeṣṭhasya” [GDh 28.5]ityādinā tadvirahitam anyad dravyaṃ samaṃ labheta, uddhāro na bhavatīty arthaḥ | yady api dravyaparo nirdeśaḥ, tathāpi jyeṣṭhāṃśa-pratiṣedhamātram evātra vivakṣitam, ārambha-sāmarthyāt | anyathā hi “guṇasaṃpanno jyeṣṭhabhāk” ity evaṃ vaktavyam āsīd iti guṇahīnaḥ nāsau tulyabhāk | tatra manūktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
caturo ‘ṃśān hared vipras trīn aṃśān kṣatriyāsutaḥ |
vaiśyāputro hared dvyaṃśam aṃśaṃ śūdrāsuto haret || iti | [MDh 9.153] | śūdrāputrasya pratiṣedha ukta iti cet, ucyate – kṣatayonyām utpannasya pratiṣedhaḥ | akṣatayonyām utpannasya aṃśabhāktvam iti | paunar-bhavādidvāreṇa kṣatayonyām api saṃbhavaty eveti | yeṣām aurasādyabhāva iti śeṣaḥ | te aurasādy-abhāve tulyabhāktvam asya vyācakṣate | teṣu satsu smṛtyanta-roktaṃ draṣṭavyam | tathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “tryaṃśaṃ brāhmaṇyāḥ putro haret dvyaṃśaṃ rājanyāyāḥ putraḥ samam itare bhajeran” [VaDh 17.48–50] iti | vṛttāpekṣayā viṣayavikalpo draṣṭavyaḥ || 28.37 ||
viṃśatibhāgo jyeṣṭhasyetyādir ya uddhāraḥ pūrvam uktas tadvyatiriktam anyad vibhajeteti prakaraṇād gamyate | guṇahīne jyeṣṭhe ca rājanyāputre mānavam |
sarvaṃ vā rikthajātaṃ tad daśadhātra vibhajya tu |
dharmyaṃ vibhāgaṃ kurvīta vidhānena tu dharmavit ||
caturo ‘ṃśān hared vipras trīn aṃśān kṣatriyāsutaḥ |
vaiśyāputro hared dvyaṃśam ekaṃ śūdrāsuto haret || iti [MDh 9.152–153] || 28.34 ||
rājanyāvaiśyāputrasamavāye yathā sa brāhmaṇīputreṇa || 28.38 || — || 28.35 ||
yadā vaiśyāputro guṇasaṃpanno jyeṣṭhaḥ, kṣatriyāsuto yavīyān, brāhmaṇīsutas tu nasty eva, tadā yathā sa brāhmaṇīputreṇa kṣatriyāputras tulyabhāk evaṃ kṣatriyāputreṇa vaiśyāputras tulyabhāk jyeṣṭhāṁśa-hīnaḥ | evaṃ ca kṣatriyāputrasya jyeṣṭhasya asmin viṣaye jyeṣṭhāṃśo ‘stīti jñāpitaṃ bhavati || 28.38 ||
yadā brāhmaṇīputras tu nāste tadā rājanyāputro brāhmaṇīputreṇa samavāye yathā tulyabhāk, evaṃ kṣatriyāputreṇa vaiśyāputras tulyabhāk || 28.35 ||
kṣatriyāc cet || 28.39 || — || 28.36 ||
vaiśyāputro jyeṣṭho guṇasaṃpannas tulyabhāg iti vartate | kṣatriyād api vaiśyāputro jyeṣṭhaś cet kṣatriyāputreṇa yavīyasā tulyabhāg bhavatīty arthaḥ | evaṃ vaiśyāc chūdrāputrasya draṣṭavyam ity eke | tad ayuktaṃ manunoktatvāt || 28.39 ||
cecchabdaś caśabdasyārthe | kṣatriyāc cotpannayoḥ putrayoḥ samavāye vaiśyāputro jyeṣṭho guṇa-saṃpannaḥ kṣatriyāputreṇa yavīyasā tulyabhāk | evaṃ vaiśyād utpannasya śūdrāputrasyāpy eke manyante draṣṭavyam iti | nety anye ‘nuktatvāt || 28.36 ||
śūdrāputro ‘py anapatyasya śuśrūṣuś cel labheta vṛttimūlam
antevāsividhinā || 28.40 || — || 28.37 ||
avidyamānadvijātiputrasya brāhmaṇasyāpi śūdrayonyām utpannaḥ yāvatā kṛṣyādikarmānuṣṭhāna-samartho bhavati tāvanmātraṃ labheta sa yadi śiṣyavac chuśrūṣāparo bhavati | bhrātaraṃ tadbhāryāṃ ca gurum ivānuvartetety arthaḥ, antevādividheḥ śuśrūṣāviśeṣaṇatvāt | caturthabhāge tadapatye vṛttītyādi, kutaḥ? kṣatayonyā grahaṇād iti cet, garhārthāpiśabdahetor asyāṃśabhāktvaṃ ca || 28.40 ||
brāhmaṇasyeti vartate | anapatyasyāvidyamāna-dvijātiputrasya brāhmaṇasya śūdrāputro ‘pi vṛttimūlaṃ labheta | yāvatā kṛṣyādikarmasamartho bhavati tāval labheta | sa yady antevāsividhinā śuśrūṣur bhavati | yathā śiṣya ācāryaṃ śuśrūṣate tathā śuśrūṣuś ced iti | evaṃ kṣatriyavaiśyayor api śūdrāputro vṛttimūlaṃ labheta || 28.37 ||
sarvarṇāputro ‘py anyāyavṛtto na labhetaikeṣām || 28.41 || — || 28.38 ||
H reads “anyāyyavṛtto”
samānavarṇāputo ‘pi, apiśabdāj jyeṣṭho ‘pi durvṛttaḥ patata iti yāvat | na labheta ity eke manyante | tathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “anaṃśās tv āśramāntaragatāḥ klībonmatta-patitāś ca” [VaDh 17.52–53] iti | labhetety ācāryaḥ | śakyavineyatvāpekṣayā vikalpaḥ || 28.41 ||
yas tv anyāyyavṛtto ‘dharmeṇa dravyāṇi pratipādayati veśyādibhyaḥ prayacchati [sa] savarṇāputro ‘py apiśabdāj jyeṣṭho ‘pi dāyaṃ na labhetety ekeṣāṃ matam | tathā ca āpastambaḥ: “yas tv adharmeṇa dravyāṇi pratipādayati jyeṣṭho ‘pi tam abhāgaṃ kurvīta” [ĀpDh 2.14.15] iti || || 28.38 ||
śrotriyā brāhmaṇasyānapatyasya rikthaṃ bhajeran || 28.42 || — || 28.39 ||

apatyaṃ jaḍasya bhāgārham || 28.45 || — 28.42 ||
jaḍasya ya apatyaṃ tad bhāgayogyaṃ bhavati | arhagrahaṇād yadi yogyaṃ bhavati, na tu jaḍāpatyatayaiva | jaḍāpatyam iti vaktavye visamāsaḥ klībādīnāṃ kṣetrajāpatyaprāpaṇarthaḥ | tathāha manuḥ |
yady arthitā tu dāraiḥ syāt klībādīnāṃ kathaṃcana |
teṣām utpannatantūnām apatyaṃ bhāgam arhati || iti | [MDh 9.203] | asmād eva liṅgāt teṣāṃ yathā-saṃbhavaṃ saṃskārānumānam | anyathā sāvitrī-patitatve sati tadapatyasyātyayogyatvam eveti | kṛta-prāyaścittasya vā draṣṭavyam | evaṃ ca yat pūrvam api patitasyāpy anujñātaṃ tad api kṛtaprāyaścittasya draṣṭavyam | evaṃ bruvann etat jñāpayati – vibhāgotta-rakālam api tad apatyasya bhāgo dātavya iti || 28.45 ||

udakayogakṣemakṛtānnneṣv avibhāgaḥ || 28.47 || — || 28.44 ||
udakaṃ kūpādikam | yogaḥ iṣṭam, kṣemaḥ pūrtam | tathāha lokākṣiḥ |
kṣemaṃ pūrtaṃ yogam iṣṭam ity āhus tattvadarśinaḥ |
avibhājye tu te prokte śayanaṃ tv annam eva ca || iti |
kṛtānne utsavārtham upakalpite | kṛtagrahaṇāt taṇḍulādau na doṣaḥ | eteṣu vibhāgo nāsti | tatra śayanavat taṇḍulādīny ekasyaiva dātavyānīti kecit | jyeṣṭhasya kaniṣṭhasya vecchanti | anyāni samudāya eva sthāpyānīti draṣṭavyam || 28.47 ||
udakaṃ kūpādi | yogakṣemāv iṣṭāpūrte | tathā ca laugākṣiḥ |
yogaḥ pūrtaṃ kṣema iṣṭā ity āhus tattvadarśinaḥ |
avibhājye tu te prokte śayanaṃ cānnam eva ca || iti |
kṛtānne tūtsavādiṣu kalpite prabhūte ‘pi | eteṣu vibhāgo na kartavyaḥ | yathāvasthiteṣv eva sodaryānurūpena bhogaḥ || 28.44 ||
strīṣu ca saṃyuktāsu || 28.48 || — || 28.45 ||
yāś ca striyo maithunopabhogārthena parigṛhītāḥ paralokavītabhayaiḥ pitṛbhiḥ | nanu ca doṣābhāvaḥ kasmān na gṛhyata iti cet, “pratilome vadhaḥ puṃsām” [GDh 28.46] ity anena prāyaścittaśravaṇāt | tāsu ca putrair na vibhāgaḥ | tāsāṃ vṛddhānām aśanācchādanaṃ dātavyam, anyābhir gantavyam ity abhiprāyaḥ | cakārād anyad apy ekatvād aśakyam | yathāha manuḥ |
vastraṃ patram alaṃkāraṃ kṛtānnam udakaṃ striyaḥ |
yogakṣemaṃ pracāraṃ ca na vibhājyaṃ pracakṣate || [MDh 9.219] iti || 28.48 ||
yāś ca striyo dāsyo bhrātrādiṣu kenacit saṃyuktā upabhogaparigṛhītās tās tasyaiva | yady anyāḥ santy anyatrānyeṣāṃ bhāgaḥ | yadi na santi tadā dravyeṇa sāmyam āpādanīyam | yadā punar ekaiva dāsya-saṃyuktā ca tadā paryāyeṇa karma karotu || 28.45 ||
anājñāte daśāvaraiḥ śiṣṭair ūhavadbhir alubdaiḥ praśastaṃ kāryam || 28.49 || — || 28.46 ||
ājñātaṃ jñātaṃ tadviparītam anājñātam, tasmin yathāvad anavagate vā saṃdigdhe vā daśāvaraiḥ sarvālpair ity arthaḥ | śiṣṭair avagatavedārthaiḥ | tathāha manuḥ |
dharmeṇādhigato yais tu vedaḥ saparibṛṃhaṇaḥ |
te śiṣṭā brāhmaṇā jñeyāḥ śrutipratyakṣahetavaḥ || iti | [MDh 12.109] | ūhavadbhir ūhāpohasamarthaiḥ mīmāṃsādiśāstrakṛtabuddhibhir ity arthaḥ | alubdhaiḥ vītatṛṣṇaiḥ praśastaṃ stutam, idam atra yuktam iti | tat kāryam anuṣṭheyam | tatsaṃnidhāne kartavyam ity abhiprāyaḥ || 28.49 ||
jñāyata ivājñātam | tadviparītam anājñātam | yo ‘rtho yathāvad avijñātaḥ saṃdigdho vā tatrānājñāte daśāvarair daśabhyo ‘nyūnaiḥ śiṣṭaiḥ |
dharmeṇādhigato yais tu vedaḥ saparibṛṃhaṇaḥ |
te śiṣṭā brāhmaṇā jñeyāḥ śrutipratyakṣahetavaḥ || [MDh 12.109] iti manunoktaiḥ | ūhavidbhir ūhāpohakuśalaiḥ | alubdhair utkocādiṣu niḥspṛhaiḥ | evaṃbhūtair brāhmaṇair yat praśastaṃ stutam idam atra yuktam iti tat kāryaṃ kartuṃ yuktam || 28.46 ||
ke punas te daśāvarās tān āha |
catvāraś caturṇāṃ pāragā vedānāṃ prāguttamāḥ traya āśramiṇaḥ pṛthag dharmavidas traya etān daśāvarān pariṣad ity ācakṣate || 28.50 || — || 28.47 ||
H reads “prāg uttamāt”
brāhmaṇā eva, dharmapravacane teṣām evādhikārāt | tathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “brāhmaṇo dharmān prabrūyāt” [VaDh 1.40] iti | catvāraḥ, naikaś caturvedo ‘pi | caturṇāṃ vedānāṃ pāragāḥ sarahasyam adhyetāraḥ vedānāṃ sāṅgānām | uttamāḥ prāk traya āśramiṇaḥ tṛtīyādhyāya uktāḥ | brāhmaṇagrahaṇaṃ mā bhūd ity āśramagrahaṇam | ete ‘pi vedārthajñā eva parigṛhyante, ajñānām anadhikārāt | svadharmānuṣṭhānāpekṣayā tv evam uktam | pṛthagdharmaśāstrajñāḥ trayaḥ, naikaḥ sarvadharmajño ‘pi | te ca vedavittve saty āśramitve ca tātparyam aṅgīkṛtya pṛthag ucyante | etān daśāvarān pariṣad iti śiṣṭā ācakṣate | etad api smṛtyantareṇa sahaikavākyatvārtham | tad apy eṣām asaṃbhave tryavarādīnām upasaṃgrahārtham | tathā ca manuḥ |
daśāvarā vā pariṣad yaṃ dharmaṃ parikalpayet |
tryavarā vāpi vṛttasthāḥ taṃ dharmaṃ na vicālayet || iti | [MDh 12.110] | tathā vasiṣṭho ‘py āha |
catvāro vā trayo vāpi yaṃ brūyur vedapāragāḥ |
sa dharma iti vijñeyo netareṣāṃ sahasraśaḥ || iti | [VaDh 3.7] | tatra bahutvāsaṃbhave svalpaṃ grāhyam iti draṣṭavyam || 28.50 ||
caturṇāṃ vedānāṃ pāragāḥ sāṅgānām adhyetāro ‘rthajñāś ca | evaṃbhūtās catvāro na cāturvaivya ekaḥ | āśramiṇas tṛtīye ‘dhyāya uktā brahmacārī gṛhastho bhikṣur vāikhānasa iti | teṣūttamād vaikānasāt pūrve traya āśramiṇaḥ | pṛthag dharmaśāstravidas trayaḥ | pṛthaggrahaṇam ekam eva dharmaśāstraṃ viduṣāṃ trayāṇāṃ grahaṇaṃ mā bhūd iti | tān etān daśāvarān pariṣad ity ācakṣate dharmajñāḥ || 28.47 ||
asaṃbhave tv eteṣāṃ śrotriyo vedavic chiṣṭo vipratipattau yad āha || 28.51 || — || 28.48 ||
tat kāryam iti śeṣaḥ | tuśabdo ‘vadhāraṇe | asaṃbhava eva eteṣām, na yathāsaṃbhavaṃ katipayānām apy asaṃbhava iti | aśrotriyo vety abhāvavikalpena vāśabdenaiva siddhau evam upadeśaḥ pūrvoktānāṃ sarveṣām asaṃbhave, na daśāvarāsaṃbhavamātra iti | śrotriyaḥ samastavedapāragaḥ, na smṛtyantarokta-mantrabrāhmaṇādhyāyī bahupāṭhasyābhīṣṭatvāt | paricchede vedavit tadarthajñaḥ | vināpi ca granthaprakarṣeṇa tadarthajñānasya darśanāc chrotriya-grahaṇam | śiṣṭaḥ svadharmāvasthitaḥ | vipratipattau abhiprāyabhede eko ‘pi pramāṇam || 28.51 ||
eteṣāṃ vyastānāṃ samastānāṃ ca bahūnām asaṃbhave śrotriyaḥ sāṅgasya vedasyādhyetā | vedavit tadarthajñaḥ | śiṣṭaḥ svadharmanirataḥ | evaṃbhūtaḥ eko ‘pi vipratipattiviṣaye yad āhedam atra yuktam idaṃ kāryam iti tat kāryam | tathā ca manuḥ |
eko ‘pi vedavid dharmaṃ yaṃ vyavasyet samāhitaḥ |
sa dharmaḥ paramo jñeyo nājñānām udito ‘yutaiḥ || iti | [MDh 12.113] || 28.48 ||
atra kāraṇam āha |
kasmāt punar ekasyāpi śrotriyasya vedavidaḥ śiṣṭasya vacanaṃ kartavyam ity ata āha |
yato yamaprabhavau bhūtānām || 28.52 ||
yato ‘yam aprabhavo bhūtānāṃ hiṃsānugrahayogeṣu || 28.49 ||
yasmād vedāt, yamaprabhavau, yamaśabdena saṃsāranivṛttir ucyate, prabhavaśabdena tasyaiva pravṛttiḥ, ubhayasādhanasya ca vedād adhigamaḥ | tena vedaḥ pramāṇam vipratipattau | yathāha manuḥ |
cāturvarṇyaṃ trayo lokāś catvāraś cāśramāḥ pṛthak |
bhūtaṃ bhavyaṃ bhaviṣyac ca sarvaṃ vedāt prasiddhim ait || [MDh 12.97] | ādau ca yamagrahaṇaṃ saṃsāranivṛttiḥ paramapuruṣārtha iti jñāpanārtham
|| 28.52 ||
prabhavaty asmād iti prabhavaḥ kāraṇam | tan na vidyate yasya so ‘prabhavaḥ | yasmād ayaṃ bhūtānāṃ hiṃsānugrahayogeṣu daṇḍaprāyaścittādiṣv agṛhyamāṇakāraṇaḥ kevalaṃ śāstranetras tasmād ekasyāpi vacanam anuṣṭheyam iti | apara āha – prabhavanaṃ prabhavaḥ prabhutvaṃ tad yasya nāsti so ‘prabhavaḥ | na hy asau śāstranirapekṣaḥ svatantraḥ kiṃcid anugṛhṇāti nigṛhṇāti vā | tasmād asya vacanam anuṣṭheyam iti || 28.49 ||
kiṃ ca |
hiṃsānugrahayogeṣu || 28.53 ||
veda eva pramāṇam iti vākyaśeṣaḥ | hiṃsāsaṃbandhe — ete paśvādayo yajñe hiṃsyā ity atra, anugrahayoge — sarve brāhmaṇādayo 'nugrāhyā ity atrāpi veda eva pramāṇam | taddvāreṇa ca vedavid ity abhiprāyaḥ
|| 28.54 ||
sāṃprathaṃ dharmavidhaḥ phalam āha |
sāṃprathaṃ ye kevalaṃ dharmam anutiṣṭhanti tebhyo jñātvānutiṣṭhan viśiṣṭa ity āha |
dharmināṃ viśeṣeṇa svargaṃ lokaṃ dharmavid āpnoti
jñānābhiniveśābhyām || 28.54 || — || 28.50 ||
dharmī kevaladharmī | viśeṣeṇa ā pralayād ity abhyprāyaḥ | dharmavit jñātvānuṣṭhāyī | kutaḥ? jñānagrahaṇāt | abhiniveśaḥ tadanuṣṭhānam | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “dhārmikaḥ” [VaDh] ityādi || 28.54 ||
dharmiṇo dharmavanto dhārmikāḥ | teṣāṃ madhye yo dharmavid dharmaśāstraṃ yāvato ‘rthato ‘dhītya dharmaṃ tattvato vetti saḥ | jñānābhiniveṣābhyām | jñānaṃ samarthāvagatiḥ abhiniveśas tātparyeṇānu-ṣṭhānam | jñānenābhiniveśena na kevalānuṣṭhātṛbhyo viśeṣeṇa svargaṃ lokam āpnoti || 28.50 ||
iti dharmo dharmaḥ || 28.55 || — || 28.51 ||
“vedo dharmamūlam” (GDh 1.1) ity ārabhya etatpartyantaṃ dharmasaṃjñā gautamasyeti vākyaśeṣaḥ || 28.55 ||
so ‘yam ādito “vedo dharmamūlam” (GDh 1.1) ity ārabhyaivamanto dharmaḥ uktaḥ | dviruktiḥ śāstraparisamāptyarthā || 28.51 ||
iti purāṇamaskarīye gautamabhāṣye aṣṭāviṃśo ‘dhyāyaḥ |
samāptaṃ cedaṃ gautamīyaṃ nibandhanaṃmaskarīyaṃ bhāṣyam |
iti śrīgautamīyavṛttau haradattaviracitāyāṃ mitākṣarāyāṃ tṛtīyapraśne daśamo ‘dhyāyaḥ ||
samāpto ‘yaṃ granthaḥ ||
